sarvasiddhipradāṃ sarvavidyāmantraviśodhinīm | praṇamāmi 
jagattārāṃ sārāñca mantrasiddhaye |
ityevañca varārohe ! stotraṃ siddhikaraṃ param | paṭhitvā 
mokṣamāpnoti satyaṃ vai girinandini ! | kujavāre 
caturdaśyāmamāyāṃ jīvavāsare |
śukre niśi gate stotraṃ paṭhitvā mokṣamāpnuyāt | tripakṣe 
mantrasiddhiḥ syāt stotrapāṭhāddhi śaṅkari ! | caturdaśyāṃ 
niśābhāge śanibhaumadine tathā | niśāmukhe paṭhet stotraṃ 
mantrasiddhimavāpnuyāt | kevalaṃ stotrapāṭhāddhi 
mantrasiddhiranuttamā | jāgartti satataṃ caṇḍi ! 
stotrapāṭhādbhujaṅginī ||

iti muṇḍamālātantre ekādaśapaṭale mahāvidyāstotraṃ samāptam ||



atha kavacam || sadāśiva ṛṣirdevi ! uṣṇik chanda udīritam | 
viniyogaśca deveśi ! satataṃ mantrasiddhaye | mastakaṃ pārvatī pātu 
pātu pañcānanapriyā | keśaṃ mukhaṃ pātu caṇḍī bhāratī 
rudhirapriyā | kaṇṭhaṃ pātu p. 748) stanaṃ pātu kapālaṃ pātu caiva 
hi |
kālī karālavadanā vicitrā citraghaṇṭinī |
vakṣomūlaṃ nābhimūlaṃ durgā tripurasundarī | samastaṃ pātu me 
tārā sarvāṅgī sarvameva ca | viśveśvarī pṛṣṭhadeśaṃ netraṃ pātu 
maheśvarī |
hṛtpadmaṃ kālikā pātu kaṇṭhaṃ pātu nabhogatam | nārāyaṇī 
guhyadeśaṃ meḍhraṃ meḍhreśvarī tathā | pādayugmaṃ jayā pātu 
nityaṃ māṃ caṇḍikāvatu | jīvaṃ māṃ pārvatī pātu mātaṅgī 
pātu sarvadā | chinnā dhūmā ca bhīmā ca bhaye pātu jale vane | 
kaumārī caiva vārāhī nārasiṃhī yaśo mama | pātu nityaṃ 
bhadrakālī śmaśānālayavāsinī | udare sarvadā pātu sarvāṇī 
sarvamaṅgalā | jaganmātā jayaṃ pātu nityaṃ kailāsavāsinī | 
śivapriyā sutaṃ pātu sutaṃ parvatanandinī | trailokyaṃ pātu bagalā 
bhuvanaṃ bhuvanuśvarī | sarvāṅgaṃ sarvanilayā pātu nityañca 
pārvatī |
cāmuṇḍā pātu me romakūpaṃ sarvārthasādhinī | brahmāṇḍaṃ 
me mahāvidyā pātu nityaṃ manoharā |
liṅgaṃ liṅgeśvarī pātu mahāpīṭhe maheśvarī | sadāśivapriyā 
pātu nityaṃ pātu sureśvarī | gaurī me sandhideśañca pātu vai 
tripureśvarī | sureśvarī sadā pātu śmaśāne ca śave'vatu | kumbhake 
recake caiva pūrake kāmamandire | kāmākhyā kāmanilayaṃ pātu 
durgā maheśvarī |
ḍākinī kākinī pātu nityaṃ me śākinī tathā | hākinī lākinī pātu 
rākiṇī pātu sarvadā | jvālāmukhī sadā pātu mukhamadhye 
śivāvatu | tāriṇī vibhave pātu bhavānī ca bhave'vatu | 
trailokyamohinī pātu sarvāṅgaṃ vijane'vatu | rājakule mahāghāte 
saṃgrāme śatrusaṅkaṭe | pracaṇḍā sādhakaṃ māñca pātu 
bhairavamohinī | śrīrājamohinā pātu rājadvāre vipattiṣu | 
sampatpradā bhairavī ca pātu bālā balaṃ mama | nityaṃ māṃ 
śambhuvanitā pātu māṃ tripurāntakā | ityevaṃ kathitaṃ devi ! 
rahasyaṃ sarvakālikam | bhuktidaṃ muktidaṃ

p. 749) saukhyasarvasampatpradāyakam | yaḥ pathet prātarutthāya 
sādhakendro bhavedbhuvi |
kujavāre caturdaśyāmamāyāṃ mandavāsare | gurau guruṃ 
samabhyarccya yaḥ paṭhet sādhakottamaḥ | sa yāti bhavanaṃ devyāḥ 
satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
evaṃ yadi varārohe ! paṭhedbhaktiparāyaḥ |
mantrasiddhirbhavettasya cācirānnātra saṃśayaḥ | satyaṃ 
lakṣapuraścaryyāphalaṃ prāpya śivāṃ vrajet | śivamārgaṃ 
rājamārgaṃ prāpya jīvaḥ śivo bhavet |
paṭhitvā kavacaṃ stotraṃ muktimāpnoti niścitam | paṭhitvā kavacaṃ 
stotraṃ daśavidyā yajed yadi | vidyāsiddhirmantrasiddhirbhavatyeva na 
saṃśayaḥ | kharasiddhiścitāsiddhirdurlabhā dharaṇotale | 
ayatnasulabhā siddhistāmbūlānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
niśāmukhe niśāyāñca mahākāle niśāntake | paṭhedbhaktyā 
maheśāni ! gāṇapatyaṃ labhettu saḥ || iti muṇḍamālātantre 
ekādaśapaṭale mahāvidyākavacaṃ samāptam ||

śmaśānakālikādīnāṃ pūjādikantu 
tantrasāre'nusandheyam || atha śmaśānakālikākavacam || 
kailāsaśikharāruḍhaṃ śaṅkaraṃ varadaṃ śivam | devī papraccha 
sarvajñaṃ sarvadevamaheśvaram | bhagavan ! sarvadeveśa ! devānāṃ 
bhogada ! prabho ! | prabrūhi me mahādeva ! gopyaṃ yadyapi ca prabho ! 
| śatrūṇāṃ yena nāśaḥ syādātmano rakṣaṇaṃ bhavet | 
paramaiśvaryamatulaṃ labhed yena hi tadvada ||

śrībhairava uvāca

vakṣyāmi te mahādevi ! sarvadharmavidāṃ vare | adbhutaṃ kavacaṃ 
devyāḥ sarvakāmaprasādhakam | viśeṣataḥ śatrunāśakaraṃ 
rakṣākaraṃ nṛṇām |
sarvāniṣṭapraśamanamabhicāravināśakam |
sukhadaṃ bhogadañcaiva vaśīkaraṇamuttamam | śatrusaṅghāḥ 
kṣayaṃ yānti bhavanti vyādhipīḍitāḥ |
duḥkhino jvariṇaścaiva svābhīṣṭapracyutāstathā || asya 
śrīkālikākavacasya bhairava ṛṣirgāyatrī cahndaḥ śrīkālikā devatā 

p. 750) śatrusaṅghanāśe viniyogaḥ | oṃ dhyāyet kālīṃ 
mahāmāyāṃ trinetrāṃ bahurūpiṇīm | caturbhujāṃ lolajihvāṃ 
pūrṇacandranibhānanām | nīlotpaladalaprakhyāṃ 
śatrusaṅghavidāriṇīm | naramuṇḍaṃ tathā khaḍgaṃ kamalañca 
varaṃ tathā | bibhrāṇāṃ raktavadanāṃ daṃṣṭrālīghorarūpiṇīm | 
aṭṭāṭṭahāsaniratāṃ sarvadā ca digambarīm | śavāsanasthitāṃ 
devīṃ muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitām | iti dhyātvā mahādevīṃ tatastu 
kavacaṃ paṭhet | oṃ kālikā ghorarūpāḍhyā sarvakāmapradā 
śubhā | sarvataḥ saṃstutā devī śatrunāśaṃ karotu me | hrīṃ 
hrīṃsvarūpiṇī caiva hrāṃ hrāṃ huṃ rūpiṇī tathā | hīṃ hāṃ 
kṣauṃ kṣauṃsvarūpā sā sadā śatrūn praśāstu me | śrīṃ hrīṃ 
aiṃrūpiṇī devī bhavabandhavimocinī | hasakalahrīṃsvarūpāsau 
ripūn sā hantu sarvadā | yayā śumbho hato daityo niśumbhaśca 
mahāsuraḥ | vairināśāya vande tāṃ kālikāṃ śaṅkarapriyām | 
brāhmī śaivī vaiṣṇavī ca vārāhī nārasiṃhikā | kaumāryaindī ca 
cāmuṇḍā khādayantu mama dviṣaḥ | sureśvarī ghorarūpā 
caṇḍamuṇḍavināśinī |
muṇḍamālāvṛtāṅgī ca sarvataḥ pātu māṃ sadā | hrīṃ hrīṃ 
kālike ! ghoradaṃṣṭre ! rudhirapriye ! rudhirarucirapūrṇavaktre ! 
rudhirāvṛtastani ! |
mamāmukaśatrūn khādaya khādaya cchedaya cchedaya hiṃsaya 
hiṃsaya māraya māraya bhindi bhindi chindi chindi uccāṭaya uccāṭaya 
drāvaya drāvaya svāhā || vāṃ vīṃ kālikāyai madīyaśatruṃ 
samarpayāmi svāhā | oṃ jaya jaya kiri kiri kiṭi kiṭi kaṭṭa kaṭṭa 
mardaya mardaya mohaya mohaya hara hara mamāmukaripūn dhvaṃsaya 
dhvaṃsaya bhakṣa bhakṣa troṭaya troṭaya yātudhāni ! cāmuṇḍe ! 
sarvajanān rājño rājapuruṣān striyo mama vaśyāḥ kuru kuru tanu 
tanu dhānyaṃ dhanamaśvān gajān ratnāni divyakāminīḥ putrān 
rājaśriyaṃ dehi dehi yaccha yaccha kṣāṃ kṣīṃ kṣūṃ kṣaiṃ

p. 751) kṣauṃ kṣaḥ svāhā | ityetat kavacaṃ divyaṃ kathitaṃ 
śambhunā purā | ye paṭhanti sadā teṣāṃ dhruvaṃ naśyanti śatravaḥ 
| vairiṇaḥ pralayaṃ yānti vyādhitāśca bhavanti hi | dhanahīnāḥ 
putrahīnāḥ śatravastasya sarvadā | sahasrapaṭhanāt siddhiḥ kavacasya 
bhavettadā | tataḥ kāryāṇi siddhyanti taddhi śaṅkarabhāṣitam | 
śmaśānāṅgāramādāya cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ | pādodakena 
piṣṭvā ca likhellauhaśalākayā | bhūmau śatrūn 
hīnarūpānuttarāśirasastathā | hastaṃ dattvā taddhṛdaye kavacantu 
svayaṃ paṭhet |
śatroḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāntu kuryānmantreṇa mantravit | 
hanyādastraprahāreṇa śatrurgacched yamakṣayam | 
jvalitāṅgāratāpeṇa jvarī bhavati tādṛśaḥ |
proñchanairvāmapādena daridro bhavati dhruvam | vairināśakaraṃ 
proktaṃ kavacaṃ vaśyakārakam | paramaiśvaryadañcaiva 
putrapautrādivṛddhidam | prabhātasamaye caiva pūjākāle viśeṣataḥ | 
sāyaṃkāle tathā pāṭhāt sarvasiddhirbhaveddhruvam ||
śatruruccāṭanaṃ yāti deśācca vicyuto bhavet | paścāt 
kiṅkaratāmeti satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || śatrunāśakare ! devi ! 
sarvasampatkare ! śubhe ! | sarvadevastute ! devi ! kālike ! tvāṃ 
namāmyaham ||

iti rudrajāmale vairiharaṇaṃ nāma śmaśānakālikākavacaṃ 
samāptam ||



dakṣiṇācārasyāpi śāktaviṣayakatvāt śāktapraśaṃsā 
ucyate ||
atha śāktapraśaṃsā || muṇḍamālātantre dvitīyapaṭale || 
svarge marttye ca pātāle nāsti śāktāt paraḥ priyaḥ | saurāṇāṃ 
gāṇapatyānāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ tathaiva ca | tadante caiva śāktāḥ 
syuḥ kramaśaḥ kramaśaḥ priye ! |
śṛṇu devi ! varārohe ! nāsti śāktāt paro janaḥ | śākto'pi 
śaṅkaraḥ sākṣāt paraṃbrahmasvarūpabhāk | ārādhitā yena kālī 
tārā tribhuvaneśvarī | ṣoḍaśī caiva mātaṅgī chinnā ca 
bagalāmukhī | ārādhitā maheśāni ! sa śivo nātra saṃśayaḥ |

p. 752) atigopyaṃ maheśāni ! śāktānāṃ paramaṃ padam | yo 
jānāti mahīmadhye sa śivo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
brahmādyarccitapādābjaṃ yo bhajet satataṃ mudā | 
sayātyacirakālena muktimandirameva hi || tṛtīyapaṭale || 
pārvatīcaraṇadvandvabhajanāt kiṅkaro bhavet | svargabhogaśca 
mokṣaśca śāktānāṃ na bhavet kimu | śāktānāñcaiva nindāṃ ye 
kurvanti hi narādhamāḥ | teṣāṃ lohitapānaṃ vai kurvanti 
bhairavīgaṇāḥ | bhairavāścaiva bhairavyaḥ sadā hiṃsanti pāmarān |
śāktān hiṃsanti garjanti nindanti bahujalpakāḥ | chinatti teṣāṃ 
deveśi ! śirāṃsi haravallabhā | śāktānāmuttamo nāsti svarge 
marttye rasātale | śāktastu śaṅkaro jñeyastrinetraścandraśekharaḥ |
svayaṃ gaṅgādharo bhūtvā viharet kṣitimaṇḍale || caturthapaṭale || 
devīṃ prati śivavākyam || madaṃśāścaiva ye bhūtāste śaivā 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | tvadaṃśāścaiva śāktāśca satyaṃ vai girinandini 
! | bahuvarṣasahasrānte śaivāḥ śaktiparāyaṇāḥ | śāktāśca 
śaṅkarā devi ! yasya kasya kulodbhavāḥ | cāṇḍālā brāhmaṇāḥ 
śūdrāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyasambhavāḥ | ete śāktā jagaddhātri ! na 
manuṣyāḥ kadācana | paśyanti mānuṣān loke kevalaṃ 
carmacakṣuṣā || tathā saptamapaṭale'pi ||

śiva uvāca ||

brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyairvaiśyaiḥ śūdraireva ca jātibhiḥ | 
vāmamārgaprabhāveṇa karttavyaṃ japapūjanam | ye śāktā 
brāhmaṇā devi ! kṣatriyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ | veśyāśca 
brāhmaṇā devi ! sarve śudrāśca brāhmaṇāḥ | brāhmaṇāḥ 
śaṅkarāścaṇḍi ! trinetrāścandraśekharāḥ || nirvāṇatantre 
tṛtīyapaṭale || śāktā eva dvijāḥ sarve na śaivā na ca vaiṣṇavāḥ | 
upāsate yato devīṃ gāyatrīṃ paramākṣarīm | gandharvatantre 
dvitīyapaṭale || śaktyupāsanānantaramanyopāsananiṣedho yathā || 
tripurāmadhikṛtya | asyāścārādhanaṃ kṛtvā

p. 753) nānyasyārādhanaṃ caret | anyasya smaraṇāddevi ! yoginī 
śāpamālabhet || iti śāktapraśaṃsā ||

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ pañcame bhaktikāṇḍe 
mahāvidyādikathanarūpaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paricchedaḥ |



kumārīpūjanaratetyādinā pūrvalikhitaṃ 
kumārīpūjāvidhinā cākāṅkṣitaṃ prāptāvasareṇedānīṃ 
taducyate mayā | atha kumārīpūjā || tatra kumārīnirūpaṇaṃ 
rudrajāmalottarakhaṇḍīyaṣaṣṭhapaṭale ||
ekavarṣā bhavet sandhyā dvivarṣā ca sarasvatī | trivarṣā ca tridhā 
mūrttiścaturvarṣā ca kālikā | subhagā pañcavarṣā tu ṣaṣṭhavarṣā 
bhavedumā |
saptabhirmālinī sākṣādaṣṭavarṣā tu kubjikā | navabhiḥ 
kālasandarbhā daśabhiścāparājitā | ekādaśe tu rudrāṇī 
dvādaśābde tu bhairavī | trayodaśe mahālakṣmīrdvisaptā 
pīṭhanāyikā | kṣetrajñā pañcadaśabhiḥ ṣoḍaśe cāmbikā matā | 
evaṃ krameṇa saṃgṛhya yāvat puṣpaṃ na jāyate | 
pratipadādipūrṇāntaṃ vṛddhibhedena pūjayet || ekavarṣetyādisarvaṃ 
vṛhannīlatantre'pi kevalaṃ ṣaḍvarṣā ca umā bhavet | dvisaptā 
nāyikā smṛtā | ṣoḍaśe carcakā mateti pāṭhabhedo nāmabhedaśceti | 
kubjikātantre saptamapaṭale tu viśeṣa ukto yathā || pañcavarṣāṃ 
samārabhya yāvad dvādaśavārṣikī |
kumārī sā bhaveddevi ! nijarūpaprakāśinī |
ṣaḍvarṣāñca samārabhya yāvat sā navavārṣikī | tāvaccaiva 
maheśāni ! sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhaye | aṣṭavarṣāṃ samārabhya 
yāvattrayodaśābdikī | kulajāṃ tāṃ vijānīyāttatra pūjāṃ 
samācaret |
daśavarṣāṃ samārabhya yāvat ṣoḍaśavārṣikī | yuvatīṃ tāṃ 
vijānīyāddevatāṃ tāṃ vicintayet | annaṃ vastraṃ tathā nīraṃ 
kumāryai yo dadāti hi |
annaṃ merusamaṃ devi ! jalañca sāgaropamam | vastraiḥ 
koṭimahasrābdaṃ śivaloke mahīyate | pūjopakaraṇānīha kumāryai 
yo dadāti hi | santuṣṭā devatā tasya putratvenānukalpyate || viśvasāre 

p. 754) tu || aṣṭavarṣā tu sā kanyā bhavedgaurī varānane ! | navavarṣā 
rohiṇī sā daśavarṣā tu kanyakā | ata ūrddhaṃ mahāmāyā bhavet 
saiva rajasvalā | ārabhya dvādaśābdācca yāvadviṃśatisaṃkhyakam 
| sukumārā ca sā proktā sarvatantrasamanvitā | 
yānapāṣāṇadhātūnāṃ tejorūpeṇa saṃsthitā | jīvajantuṣu deveśi 
| kiṃ vaktavyamataḥ param | yatra nāsti mahāmāyā tatra kiñcinna 
vidyate || ityuktam ||
tat pūjāphalaṃ yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe saptadaśapaṭale || 
kumārīpūjanaphalaṃ vaktuṃ nārhāmi sundari ! | jihvākoṭisahasraistu 
vaktrakoṭiśatairapi |
tasmāttāṃ pūjayedbālāṃ sarvajātisamudbhavām | jātibhedo na 
karttavyaḥ kumārīpūjane śive ! | jātibhedānmaheśāni ! narakānna 
nivarttate |
vicikitsāparo mantrī dhruvaṃ sa pātakī bhavet | devībuddhyā 
mahābhaktastasmāttāṃ paripūjayet | sarvavidyāsvarūpā hi kumārī 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | yadi bhāgyavaśāddevi ! veśyākulasamudbhavām | 
kumārīṃ labhate kānte | sarvasvenāpi sādhakaḥ | yatnataḥ 
pūjayettāntu svarṇaraupyādibhirmudā |
tadā tasya mahāsiddhirjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 
kumārīpūjāsthānaṃ phalamapi tatraiva || kumārīpūjyate yatra sa 
deśaḥ kṣitipāvanaḥ | mahāpuṇyatamo bhūyāt samantāt 
krośapañcakam | jāmale tatraiva || mahāparvasu sarveṣu viśeṣācca 
pavitrake | mahānavamyāṃ deveśi ! kumārīñca prapūjayet | tasmāt 
ṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ yuvatīti pracakṣate |
tatra bhāvaprakāśaḥ syāt sa bhāvaḥ paramo mataḥ | rakṣitavyāḥ 
prayatnena akṣatāstāḥ pracintayet | mahāpūjādikaṃ kṛtvā 
vastrālaṅkārabhojanaiḥ | pūjayenmandabhāgyo'pi labhate 
jayamaṅgalam | anyeṣāṃ kathanenātha prayojanamahāphalam | 
vidhinā pūjayed yastu divyavīrapaśusthitaḥ |
bhāvatraye mahāsaukhyaṃ divyakarmaṇi satphalam || tathā || 
aśeṣakulasampannāṃ nānājātisamudbhavām | nānādeśodbhavāṃ 
vāpi saguṇāguṇasaṃyutām | dvitīyavatsarādūrddhaṃ yāvat 
syādaṣṭamābdakam | tāvajjaptvā pūjayitvā kanyāṃ 
sundaramohinīm | divyabhāvasthitaḥ sākṣāttantramantraphalaṃ labhet | 
kumārī pūjanādeva kumārībhojanādibhiḥ | ekadvitryādivījānāṃ 
phaladā nātra saṃśayaḥ | tābhyaḥ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ dattvā 
anulepādikaṃ tathā | bālapriyañca naivedyaṃ dattvā 
tadbhāvabhāvitaḥ |
mṛdā tadaṅgamātmānaṃ bālabhāvaviceṣṭitaḥ | 
atipriyakathālāpakrīḍākautūhalānvitaḥ |
yathārthaṃ tat priyaṃ tatra kṛtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet | kanyā 
sarvasamṛddhiḥ syāt kanyā sarvaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ | homaṃ 
mantrārcanaṃ nityakriyāṃ kaulikasatkriyām | 
nānāphalamahādharmaṃ kumārīpūjanaṃ vinā | tattatkarmaphalaṃ 
nātha prāpnoti sādhakottamaḥ | phalaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ vīraḥ kumārī 
pūjayā labhet |
kusumāñjalipūrṇañca kanyāyai kulapaṇḍitaḥ | dadāti yadi tat 
puṣpaṃ koṭimeruhiraṇyavat | taddānajaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ kṣaṇādeva 
samālabhet |
kumārī bhojitā yena trailokyaṃ tena bhojitam || kālītantre 
ekādaśapaṭale || kumārīpūjanaṃ kuryāt sarvakarmaphalāptaye || 
rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe saptamapaṭale || atha pūjāṃ pravakṣyāmi 
kumāryā atidurllabham | ityabhidhāya || pūjāsthānaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ 
devālayamathāpi vā | naṭīkanyāṃ hīnakanyāṃ tathā 
kāpālikanyakām |
rajakasyāpi kanyāñca tathā nāpitakanyakām | gopālakanyakāñcaiva 
brāhmaṇasyāpi kanyakām | śūdrakanyāṃ vaidyakanyāṃ tathā 
vaṇikakanyakām |
caṇḍālakanyakāṃ vāpi yatra kutrāśrame sthitām | suhṛdvargasya 
kanyāñca samānīya prayatnataḥ | pūjayet 
paramānandairātmadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ |
mama pūjāṃ yaḥ karoti pratyahaṃ śuddhabhaktitaḥ | tasyā vaśyaṃ 

p. 756) kumārīṇāṃ pūjanaṃ bhojanaṃ raveḥ |
tejorūpaṃ vidhoścāgneḥ sarvabhāve prakāśate | 
tatpūjanāttadālāpāt bhojanādapi tacchabhāt | mama prītirbhavet 
sākṣāddevatā guptasaṃsthitā | bālabhairavadevasya kāminī 
vaṭukasya ca |
matputtrasya sarvalokapūjitasya mahaujasaḥ |
pūjābhirvividhairdravyaiḥ kumāri devapūjitā | kumārī devatā 
proktā sarvato jagadīśvarī | pūjārthaṃ sarvalokasya samānīya 
sureśvarāḥ pūjayanti mahādevīṃ guptabhāvanivāsinīm | sadā 
bhojanavāñchāḍhyā mānyā santuṣṭahāsinī | vṛthā na rauti sā 
devī kumārī devanāyikā | sarasvatīsvarūpā sā pūjyate 
sarvanāyakaiḥ | śivabhaktairviṣṇubhaktaistathānyadevapūja-kaiḥ | 
sarvalokaiḥ pūjitā sā cāvaśyaṃ pūjyate vudhaiḥ | pūjayā labhate 
pūjāṃ pūjayā labhate śriyam | pūjayā dhanamāpnoti pūjayā 
labhate mahīm | pūjayā labhate lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīṃ mahaujasam | 
mahāvidyā prasīdanti sarve devā na saṃśayaḥ | 
kālabhairavabrahmendrā brāhmaṇā brahmavedinaḥ | rudrāśca 
devavargāśca vaiṣṇavā brahmarūpiṇaḥ |
avatārāśca dvibhujā vaiṣṇavāśca caturbhujāḥ | anye 
dikpāladevāśca carācaragurustathā | nānāvidyāratāḥ sarve 
dānavāḥ kūṭaśālinaḥ | upasargasthitā ye ye te te tuṣṭā na 
saṃśayaḥ | yadyahaṃ śuddhirūpā hi anyaloke ca kā kathā |
kumārīpūjanaṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet | mahāśāntirbhavet 
kṣipraṃ sarvapuṇyaphalapradam || vṛhannīlatantre || mahābhayāni 
durbhikṣādyutpātāni kuleśvari ! | duḥsvapnamapamṛtyuñca 
yaccānyat duḥkhadaṃ nṝṇām | kumārīpūjanādeva na tāni 
prabhavanti hi |
nityaṃ krameṇa deveśi ! pūjayedvidhipūrvakam | ghnanti vighnān 
pūjitāśca bhayaṃ śatrūn mahotkaṭān |
grahā rogāḥ kṣayaṃ yānti bhūtavetālapannagāḥ || rudrajāmale 
tathā || tattanmantrasamullekhāt kṣaṇāt puṇyāyutaṃ labhet | mantreṇa 

p. 757) puṭitaṃ kṛtvā japtvā siddhiśvaro bhavet | yadyat 
prakāramuddiśya vadāmi surasundara ! |
tattat kāryañca karttavyaṃ bhinnabuddhiṃ na kārayet | tathā | śṛṇu 
nātha ! kulārthaṃ me kumārīpūjane manum | 
mahāvidyāmahāmantraṃ siddhimantraṃ na saṃśayaḥ | 
etanmantraprabhāveṇa jīvanmukto bhavet sudhīḥ | ante devīpadaṃ yāti 
satyamānandabhairava ! | aihike sukhasampattiṃ madhumatyāḥ 
prasādakam |
avaśyaṃ prāpnuyānmarttyo viśrāmaṃ kuru śaṅkara ! | vāgbhavena 
vapuḥkṣobhaṃ māyāvīje guṇāṣṭakam | śriyo vīje śriyo lābho 
māyāvīje ripoḥ kṣayaḥ | bhairaveṇa tu vījena khecaratvaṃ surādibhiḥ 
| kumārikā hyahaṃ nātha ! sadā tvaṃ hi kumārakaḥ |
śatamaṣṭottarāṃ vāpi ekāṃ vā paripūjayet | pūjitāḥ 
pratipūjyante nirvahantyavamānitāḥ | kumārī yoginī sākṣāt kumārī 
paradevatā | asurā duṣṭanāgāśca ye ye duṣṭagrahā api |
bhūtavetālagandharvā ḍākinī yakṣarākṣasāḥ | yāścānyā 
devatāḥ sarvā bhūrbhuvaḥ svaśca bhairavāḥ |
pṛthivyādīni sarvāṇi brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaram | brahmā viṣṇuśca 
rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ | te tuṣṭāḥ sarvatuṣṭāśca yastu 
kanyāṃ prapūjayet |
vidhiyuktāṃ kumārīṃ tu bhojayeccaiva bhairava ! | pādyārghyañca 
tathā dhūpaṃ kuṅkumaṃ candanaṃ śubham |
bhaktibhāvena sampūjya kumārībhyo nivedayet || 
kumārīpūjākramastu || tathā ||
ānīya sundarīṃ nārīṃ kumārīṃ varanāyikām | 
ratnālaṅkārasaṃyuktāṃ śaṅkhavastrādiśobhitām | vāgbhavena 
jalaṃ nātha tannāmnā paridāpayet | devībuddhyā sadā dhyātvā 
pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ | māyāvījena tannāmnā pādyaṃ 
dadyāttathā prabho ! | lakṣmīvījena cārghyantu kūrccavījena 
candanam | māyāvījena puṣpāṇi kumāryai dāpayet sudhīḥ | 
sadāśivena mantreṇa dhūpadīpau mahattamau | dattvā 
ṣaḍaṅgamantreṇa pūjayeddevanāyakam | tatprakāraṃ

p. 758) mahādeva ! śṛṇuṣvānandarūpadhṛk | mahātejomayaṃ 
śubhraṃ hṛdayaṃ hastadakṣiṇaiḥ | vibhāvya prapaṭhed natvā 
tanmantraṃ śṛṇu śaṅkara ! | ādau vāgbhavamuccārya māyāṃ 
lakṣmīñca kūrccakam | pretavījaṃ tato brūyāt savisargendukīlakam | 
kulaśabdaṃ samuccāryaṃ kumārike tato vadet | hṛdayāya namaḥ 
procya tataḥ śirasi bhāvayet | śuklavarṇaṃ sarvamayaṃ vījamuccārya 
saṃnyaset | hakāraṃ vāgbhavādyañca vakāraṃ vāgbhavātmakam | 
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ vāgbhavañca dviṭhāntaṃ śirase padam | 
vahnijāyāvadhirmantro nyaset śirasi sādhakaḥ | śikhāmadhye 
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ nīlāñjanacayaprabham | vibhāvya sanyasenmantrī 
kumārīkulasiddhaye | ādau praṇavamuddhṛtya tadante viṣṇusundarī |
śikhāyai padamuddhṛtya vaṣaṭkāraṃ tato vadet | tataḥ kavacamadhye 
ca balavantaṃ sutejasam | prathamāruṇasaṅkāśaṃ dhyātvā cāru 
kalevaram | vāgbhavañca samuccārya kulaśabdaṃ tato vadet | 
vāgīśvaripadaṃ paścāt kavacāya tato vadet | 
tārakabrahmaśabdañca kavacanyāsajālakam | tato netratraye dhyātvā 
mahāvījaṃ mahāprabham | raktavarṇaṃ koṭikoṭi 
javāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitam | virājitaṃ koṭipuṇyārjitatejasi bhāskare | 
vāgbhavaṃ hi samuccārya kuleśvaripadaṃ tataḥ | netratrayāya 
śabdānte vauṣaḍ locanamantrakam | tataḥ sādhakamantro ca 
vāmahastatale tathā | madhyamātarjanībhyāñca tāladvayamupācaret | 
tanmantraṃ koṭisūryograjyotsnājālasamaprabham |
mahākāśodbhavaṃ śabdamahograparipīḍanam | māyāvījaṃ 
tathāstrāya padamuddhṛtya yatnataḥ |
phaḍante cāntamuddhṛtya mahāmantraṃ prakīrttitam | tatastasyāḥ 
kuṇḍaviledhyātvā ca parivāravān | pūjayed yatnato mantrī 
tejasā'mṛtavārayā | tarpayet pūjayedbhaktyā bhairavaṃ 
bālabhairavam |
devatābhiḥ pūjayitvā parivārān krameṇa tu | tato vāgbhavamuccārya 

p. 759) siddhajayāya śabdataḥ | pūrvaṃ padaṃ samuccārya vaktrāya 
nama īritam | tato vāgbhavamuccārya jayāya śabdamuddharet |
uttare vaktramuddhṛtya caturthyantaṃ namaḥ padam | tato 
vāgbhavamāyāśrīvījamuccārya yatnataḥ |
kubjike paścimānte ca vaktrāya nama īritam |
tato vāgbhavamuccārya kālikepadamuccaret |
dakṣavaktrāya śabdānte namo mantraḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 
etanmantrākṣarānnātha ! samuccārya kuleśvara ! | pūjayitvā 
krameṇaiva bhāskaraṃ paripūjayet | caṇḍaṃ dikpāladevañca 
sandhyādīn paripūjayet |
vīrabhadrā mahākanyā kaulinīṃ kulagāminīm | aṣṭādaśabhujāṃ 
kālīṃ caṇḍadurgāṃ prapūjayet | naivedyādīn samānīya 
nānābhaktyānurājitam | dugdhaṃ ghanāvṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ pakvānnaṃ 
pakvasatphalam | yadyatkālopayogyañca sarvadā madhumiśritam | 
pañcatattvaṃ kuladravyaṃ nijakalyāṇavarddhanam | nānādravyañca 
naivedyaṃ svasvakalpoktasādhitam | kumārībhyo nivedyaivaṃ 
nānāsaurabhaśobhitam |
śītalaṃ jalamānīya dadyāttābhyo mahāsudhīḥ | tato hitaṃ 
mahāmantraṃ kumāryāścāti durlabham | adha vāgmī samūlañca 
japtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet |
samarpya prāṇavāyūnāṃ dhāraṇaṃ kārayet svayam | 
aṣṭāṅgādipraṇāmañca kurvan stotraṃ paṭhannamet || 
praṇāmamantro'pi tatraiva || namāmi kulakāminīṃ 
paramabhāgyasandāyinīm |
kumāraraticāturīṃ sakalasiddhimānandinīm | pravālaguṭikāsrajaṃ 
rajatarāgavastrānvitām | hiraṇyatulabhūṣaṇāṃ bhuvanavāk 
kumārīṃ bhaje | iti mantreṇa sannamya tāriṇīṃ paripūjayet | śivaṃ 
gaṇeśaṃ sampūjya praṇamet sādhakottamaḥ | dakṣiṇāṃ 
vidhivaddatvā kumārībhyaḥ krameṇa tu | vivāhayet svayaṃ kanyāṃ 
brahmahatyāṃ vinaśyati | yo yaśca puṇyakāle tu kanyādānaṃ 
samāpayet |
bhuktimuktiphalaṃ tasya saubhāgyaṃ sarvasampadaḥ | rudraloke

p. 760) vasennityaṃ trinetro bhagavān haraḥ | tīrthakoṭisahasrāṇi 
aśvamedhaśatāni ca | tatphalaṃ labhate marttyo yastu kanyāṃ 
vivāhayet | bālukā sāgare yāvat tāvadabdasahasrakam | ekaikaṃ 
kulamudhṛtya rudraloke mahīyate | tattadiṣṭadevatāyāḥ prītaye tuṣṭaye 
sudhīḥ |
kanyādānaṃ samāhṛtya muktimāpnoti bhairava ! | 
tattaddvitīyakanyāyāstattadbuddhyā ca sādhakaḥ | vibhāvya 
śivarūpatvaṃ sampradānīyakanyake | pūrṇarūpaṃ śivaṃ dhyātvā 
varaṃ sarvāṅgasundaram |
tejomayaṃ yaśaḥkāntaṃ kālabhairavarūpiṇam | vaṭukeśaṃ 
mahādevaṃ varayet sādhakāgraṇīḥ |
bālarūpāñca trailokyasundarīṃ varavarṇinīm | 
nānālaṅkāranamrāṅgīṃ bhadravidyāprakāśinīm | cāruhāsyāṃ 
mahānandahṛdayāṃ śubhadāṃ śubhām |
dhyātvā dvādaśapatrābje pūrṇacandranibhānanām | sampradānaṃ 
samānīya tattanmantreṇa dāpayet | iti kumārīdānakramaphalam ||

atha kumārīmantrapuraścaraṇam || aṣṭamapaṭale || atha vakṣye 
mahādevyāḥ kumāryā japahomakam | lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ japaṃ kṛtvā 
māyāṃ vāvāgbhavaṃ manum | kālīvījaṃ vāpi nātha ! athavā 
kāmavījakam | sadāśivena puṭitaṃ vinducandravibhūṣitam | athavā 
praṇavenāpi puṭitaṃ tridaśeśvara ! | lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ krameṇaiva 
japtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet | japettanmantraṃ deveśa ! 
lakṣasaṃkhyāvidhānataḥ | taddaśāṃśena juhuyād 
ghṛtāktavilvapatrakaiḥ | athavā śvetapuṣpaiśca 
kundapuṣpairmahāphalam |
evaṃ krameṇa juhuyāt karavīraprasūnakaiḥ |
ghṛtāktaiḥ kevalairvāpi candanāgurumiśritaiḥ | haviṣyāśī 
divābhāge rātrau pūjāparo bhavet |
nijapūjāmaśeṣaistu kuladravyaiḥ prapūjayet | ravisaṃkhyaṃ japettatra 
paramānandarūpadhṛk | japānte 
juhuyānmantrīmaduktadravyasaṃyutaiḥ |
tataḥ prāṇātmakaṃ vāyuṃ śodhayitvā punaḥ punaḥ | 
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā cāṣṭāṅgaiḥ praṇamenmudā |

p. 761) praṇāmasamaye nātha ! idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhed yadi | kavacasya 
tathā pāṭhyaṃ kumārīṇāmathāpi vā | nijadevyā mahāstotraṃ 
pīṭhāṅgaṃ kavacaṃ tataḥ |
kumārīṇāmahaṃdeva ! sahasraṃ nāma sāṣṭakam | paṭhitvā 
siddhimāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | tadaśaktau nijadevyāḥ 
sahasranāmamaṅgalam | aṣṭottaraṃ paṭhennātha ! siddhikāṅkṣī na 
saṃśayaḥ |
tāsāṃ stotraṃ divyanātha ! śṛṇusarvatra maṅgalam | akasmāt 
siddhimāpnoti paṭhitvā sādhakottamaḥ | agre saṃsthāpya tāḥ sarvā 
ratnakoṭisuśītalāḥ | tatastotraṃ paṭheddhīmān samāhitamanā vaśī | 
mahādivyācārarato vīrabhāvolvaṇo'pi vā |
evaṃ krameṇa prapaṭhedbhaktibhāvaparāyaṇaḥ |
mahāvidyā mahāsevā bhaktiśraddhāplutārpitaḥ | mahājñānī 
bhavet kṣipraṃ vāñchāsiddhimavāpnuyāt ||

atha kumārīpūjāprayogaḥ || tatra ekavarṣāvadhi 
ṣoḍaśavarṣaparyantamajātapuṣpā kumārīsaṃjñā tatra 
varṣakrameṇa nāmāni yathā || sandhyā || 1 || sarasvatī || 2 || tridhā 
mūrttiḥ || 3 || kālikā || 4 || subhagā || 5 || umā || 6 || mālinī || 7 || kubjikā 
|| 8 || kālasandarbhā || 9 || aparājitā || 10 || rudrāṇī || 11 || bhairavī || 12 || 
mahālakṣmīḥ || 13 || pīṭhanāyikā || 14 || kṣetrajñā || 15 || ambikā || 16 
|| pratipadādipañcadaśyantaṃ vṛddhikrameṇa pūjanīyāḥ | kumārī tu 
sarvajātīyaiva praśasyā | naṭīkanyā kāpālikakanyā rajakakanyā 
nāpitakanyā gopālakanyā brāhmaṇakanyā śūdrakanyā 
vaidyakanyā vaṇikkanyā caṇḍālakanyā suhṛdvargakanyā | 
kumārīmāsane samupaveśya dhyāyed yathā || bālarūpāñca 
trailokyasundarīṃ varavarṇinīm | nānālaṅkāranamrāṅgīṃ 
bhadravidyāprakāśinīm | cāruhāsyāṃ mahānandahṛdayāṃ 
śubhadāṃ śubhām |
evaṃ dhyātvātmaśirasi puṣpaṃ dattvā mānasopacāreṇa sampūjya 
punardhyātvā kumāryaṅge dattvā idamāsanam aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ huṃ 

p. 762) hsauḥ sandhyāyai kumāryai namaḥ || evaṃ 
pūrvavījānyuccārya sarasvatyai kumāryai nama 
ityādivatsarānukrameṇa caturthyante na nāmrā āsanādibhiḥ 
ṣoḍaśairupacāraiḥ sampūjayet | etajjalaṃ aiṃ sandhyāyai namaḥ | 
etatpādyaṃ hrīṃ sandhyāyai namaḥ | evamābharaṇam | eva gandhaḥ 
huṃ sandhyāyai namaḥ | etāni puṣpāṇi hrīṃ sandhyāyai vauṣaṭ | eṣa  
dhūpaḥ hsauḥ sandhyāyai namaḥ | evaṃ dīpaḥ | etannaivedyaṃ aiṃ 
hrī/ śrī/ hu/ hauḥ sandhyāyai nivedayāmi | śaṅkhamālyakajjvala 
kuṅkumādikamanena namo'ntena mantreṇa deyam | sarvatra 
caturthyantanāmānte kumāryai iti viśeṣaṇaṃ dātavyam | tataḥ 
ṣaḍaṅgapūjā tatkramastu kumāryā hṛdayaṃ dakṣiṇahastena dhṛtvā 
mahātejomayaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ vibhāvya aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hu/ hsauḥ 
kulakumārike hṛdayāya namaḥ | śiraḥ śuklavarṇaṃ sarvamayaṃ 
vibhāvya haiṃ vaiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ aiṃ śirase svāhā | śikhāṃ 
nīlāñjanacayaprabhāṃ vibhāvya oṃ śrīṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | kavacaṃ 
prathamāruṇasaṅkāśaṃ sutejaskaṃ vibhāvya aiṃ kulavāgīśvari 
kavacāya hu/ | tato netratrayaṃ sarvavījamayaṃ mahāprabhaṃ 
raktavarṇaṃ koṭi koṭi javāpuṣpojjvalaṃ vibhāvya aiṃ kuleśvari 
netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | hrīṃ astrāya phaḍiti vāmahastatale 
tarjanīmadhyamābhyāṃ tāladvayaṃ dadyāt | tatastasyāḥ kuṇḍa vile 
saparivāraṃ bālabhairavaṃ dhyātvā ete gandhapuṣpe oṃ 
saparivārabālabhairavāya namaḥ iti pūjayet | ete gandhapuṣpe aiṃ 
siddhajayāya pūrvavaktrāya namaḥ | evaṃ jayāyottaravaktrāya | aiṃ 
hrīṃ śrīṃ kubjike paścimavaktrāya | aiṃ kālike dakṣiṇavaktrāya | 
oṃ bhāskarāya pratyekaṃ dikpālebhyaḥ vīrabhadrāyai 
mahākanyāyai kaulinyai kalanāminyai aṣṭādaśabhujāyai kālyai 
caṇḍadurgāyai oṃkārādinamo'ntena pūjayet | tataḥ 
kumārīmūlamantraṃ svīyamantraṃ vā yathāśakti japtvā

bhairavyu vāca ||

devendrādaya indukoṭikiraṇāṃ vārāṇasīvāsinīṃ vidyāṃ 
vāgbhavakāminīṃ trinayanā sūkṣmakriyāgāminīm ||

bhāvyāṃ devagaṇaiḥ śivendrayatibhirmokṣārthibhirvālikāṃ

bālāṃ bālakapūjitāṃ guṇayutāṃ vidyāvatāṃ mokṣadāṃ

nirarthakāya vapuṣe hitvā kumārīkalāṃ
mahyaṃ mātṛkumārike ca trividhā mūrtyā ca tejomayī || 4 ||

hālāhālakarālikāṃ kulapathollāsaikavījodvahāṃ

valakumārike ! śirasi śuklāmbhoruhe sambhaje || 5 ||

sadā ravidale bālendravidyāṃ satīṃ sākṣāt siddhikarīṃ kumāri 
vimale tvāmādyarūpeśvarīm || 6 ||

yogīśaṃ bhuvaneśvaraṃ priyakaraṃ śrīkālasandarbhadaṃ

vidyāntāmaparājitāṃ madanamodamattānanāṃ

bhavennaraḥ | paśureva mahāvīro divyo bhavati niścitam |
kramaśo hyaṣṭasiddhiḥ syādvāgmī bhavati naścitim | sarvā vidyāḥ

p. 768) kāmavījasvarūpā māṃ tapasvinī |
rasanāgraṃ tathā pātu vāgdevī mālinī mama | tālvagraṃ 
kāmarūpā ca dantāgraṃ kubjikā'vatu | devī praṇavarūpā sā pātu 
nityaṃ śiro mama | oṣṭhādharaṃ śaktivījātmikā svāhā svarūpiṇī 
galadeśaṃ mahāraudrī pātu me cāparājitā | kṣrauṃvījaṃ me sadā 
kaṇṭhaṃ rudrāṇī svāhayānvitā | hṛdayaṃ ṣoḍaśī vidyā pātu 
ṣoḍaśasusvarā | dau vāhū pātu sarvatra mahālakṣmīḥ pradhānikā |
sarvamantrasvarūpā me codaraṃ pīṭhanāyikā | pārśvayugmaṃ 
tathā pātu hṛddevī vāgbhavātmikā | kaiśorī kaṭideśaṃ me 
māyāvījasvarūpiṇī | jaṅghāyugmaṃ jayantī me yoginī 
kallukāvṛtā | sarvāṅgamambikā devī pātu mantrārthagāminī | 
keśāgraṃ kamalā deva ! nāsāgraṃ naramohinī | civukaṃ

mantrayantraprakāśinī |

p. 769) kavacasya prasādataḥ | sarvasiddhīśvaro yogī jñānī bhavati

dadāti siddhimuttamām |
paṭhed yaḥ śṛṇuyādvāpi sa bhavet kalpapādapaḥ |

p. 770) prapaṭhanāmānandasindhūdbhavam |
lokānāṃ kalikālaghorakaluṣadhvaṃsaikahetuṃ jayaṃ ye lokāḥ 
prapaṭhanti dharmamatulaṃ mokṣaṃ vrajanti kṣaṇāt ||

iti rudrajāmale uttaratantre mahātantroddīpane kumāryupacaryāvinyāse 
kumārīkavacollāse siddhamantraprakaraṇe  
bhāvanirṇayakumārīkavacaṃ samāptam ||



śaktaścet tadā daśamapaṭaloktaṃ kumārīsahasranāma paṭhet 
tadatra granthagauravabhiyā na likhitam ||

atha brāhmaṇapraśaṃsā || matsyasūkte tṛtīyacaraṇe 
prathamapaṭale || brāhmaṇā jaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ nirjalaṃ 
sārvakālikam | yeṣāṃ vākyodakenaiva śudhyanti malinā janāḥ || 
yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe trayodaśapaṭale || sarvadevamayo vipro 
brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakaḥ | brahmatejaḥ samudbhūtaḥ sadāprākṛtiko 
dvijaḥ | brāhmaṇairbhujyate yatra tatra bhuṅkte hariḥ svayam | tatra 
brahmā ca rudraśca khacarā ṛṣayo muniḥ | pitaro devatāḥ sarve 
bhuñjate nātra saṃśayaḥ | sarvadevamayo viprastasmāttaṃ 
nāvamānayet || saptamapaṭale || ato'vidyaṃ savidyaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ 
sarvadā bhajet | santuṣṭe brāhmaṇe devi ! santuṣṭāḥ sarvadā surāḥ |
vituṣṭe brāhmaṇe devi ! vituṣṭā vayameva hi |
yadya kāryaśataṃ devi ! brāhmaṇaḥ samupācaret | ātmano 
hitakāmena tathāpi taṃ na codvijet | nāvamānañca karttavyaṃ 
sarvadā surasundari ! brāhmaṇaḥ sarvadevātmā sākṣāttejomayo hi 
saḥ ||
trayodaśapaṭale || brāhmaṇañca kumārīñca śaktimagniṃ śrutiñca 
gām | nityamicchanti te devāḥ yajituṃ karmabhūmiṣu || vṛhannīlatantre 
saptamapaṭale || adhyāpakāya yo vṛttiṃ dattvādhyāpayati dvijān | kiṃ 
na tyaktaṃ bhavettena dharmārthakāmamicchatā | puṇyāt puṇyataraṃ 
puṇyaṃ jānīhi sarvasammatam ||
yoginītantre tathā || brahmaśāpo

p. 771) durādharṣaḥ ko'pyatastaṃ na kopayet |
vāgvajraṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hi sadā jānīhi kāmini ! || 
trayodaśapaṭale || bhavedyasya brahmaśāpo niṣkṛtistasya dūrataḥ |
takṣakeṇāpi daṣṭasya pratikāro hi tatkṣaṇāt | 
brahmaśāpapraśaptasya kalpānte syāt pratikriyā || kalpasaṃkhyā tu 
tatraivoktā ||
kalpamekaṃ maheśāni ! kalo varṣaśatatrayam | narakānniṣkṛtirnāsti 
tasya bhāvānnasaṃśayaḥ | evaṃ tadvaṃśajāḥ sarve 
pīḍyante'harniśaṃ priye ! | nānāvidhamahotpātairyāvat syāt 
sāptapauruṣam | tasmāttu brāhmaṇaṃ devi ! nāvamanyet kutracit || iti 
brāhmaṇapraśaṃsā ||

atha tīrthayātrā || yoginītantre madhyabhāge dvitīyapaṭale || 

śrībhagavānuvāca ||

nityaṃ nirvarttya svagṛhe pitṝnnāndīmukhāmapi | abhyarcya 
vidhivadbhaktyā paścādyātrāṃ samācaret |
uttare ca gate śukre sānukūle śubhagrahe |
gurupitroranujñāto brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ || bhojayitvā dvijān 
sapta tato yātrāṃ samācaret | siṃhe dhanuṣi meṣe ca na gacchet 
pūrvapīṭhakam | ghaṭe ca manmathe kumbhe na gacchet paścimaṃ 
budhaḥ |
vṛṣe'ṅganāyāṃ makare na gaccheddakṣiṇālayam || karkaṭe 
kīṭamīne ca na gaccheduttaraṃ sudhīḥ | cāpa-gokanyakāyāñca na 
gacchedvahnikoṇakam || makare karkaṭe mīne nairṛtyāṃ parivarjjayet |
vāyavyāṃ kumbhameṣe ca cāpe caiva vivarjayet | siṃhe mīne karkaṭe 
ca aiśānyāñca vivarjjayet || etat śūlaṃ vijānīyād yoginīṃ śṛṇu 
śaṅkari ! |
na gacchenmandavāre ca pūrvadeśaṃ mama priye | paścimaṃ 
saviturvāre dakṣiṇaṃ budhavāsare || kujāhe cottaraṃ deśaṃ candre 
cāgnidiśaṃ priye ! | jīvavāre ca naiṛtyāṃ vāyavyāṃ bhṛguvāsare | 
śanivāre tathaiśānyāṃ some caiva viśeṣataḥ |
pūrvadeśaṃ maheśāni ! pratipannavamīṣu ca || na gacched yātriko 
yātrāṃ yoginīsammukhī yataḥ | caturddaśīṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhīṃ 
paścime tu vivarjayet || trayodaśīṃ pañcamīñca na 
gaccheddakṣiṇāṃ diśam |
dvitīyāṃ daśamīñcaiva yakṣadeśaṃ vivarjayet | pūrṇimāṃ 
saptamīñcaiva vāyavyāṃ sarvathā tyajet | na gacchecca 
tathaiśānyāmamāvāsyāṃ tathāṣṭamīm ||
viṣkumbhaḥ prītirāyuṣmān pratipatsu vivarjayet | saubhāgyaṃ 
śobhanañcaiva dvitīyāmatigaṇḍakam | sukarmā ca dhṛtiścaiva 
tṛtīyāṃ parivarjayet | gaṇḍo vṛddhirdhruvaścaiva vyāghātaśca 
tathaiva ca || caturthāṃ varjayeddevi pañcamyāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ tathā | 
vajrasiddhivyatīpāte ṣaṣṭhyāṃ jānīhi śaṅkari ! || varīyān 
parighaścaiva saptamyāṃ parivarjayet |
aṣṭamyāṃ śivasiddhiñca sādhyañca parivarjayet || navamyāṃ 
śubhaśakrañca daśamyāṃ brahmameva vā | ekādaśyāṃ 
tathaindrantu dvādaśyāṃ vaidhṛtiṃ tyajet |
pratipadi kṛttikāñca dvitīyāṃ jyeṣṭhasaṃjñitām || ayātrike tu 
nakṣatre prasthānaṃ na kadācana | yātrāyāntu na duṣyeta karaṇasya 
ca cintanam || janmamāse janmadine janmanakṣatrake tathā | 
aṣṭamyāñca navamyāñca sadā yātrāṃ vivarjayet || āpatkāle ca 
yātrāyāṃ uṣāsampaddhi paścime || godhulīsamaye caiva pūrvadeśe 
maheśvari ! | madhyāhne dakṣiṇe caiva cāparāhṇe tathottare | 
aiśānyāñca tathā rātrau naiṛtyāṃ sandhyayordvayoḥ |
madhyandine tathāgneye vāyavyāṃ prātareva hi | vāruṇādiṣu yoge 
vā yathākāle samācaret | vinā jātadinaṃ tatra aṣṭamīṃ navamīṃ 
vinā | prācīdiśaṃ samāgacchan yātrāṃ kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ |
paścime prāṅmukhaḥ kuryāt dakṣiṇe paścimāmukhaḥ | uttare 
dakṣiṇamukho yātrāṃ kuryāt susiddhaye | kusumaṃ yāvakaḥ 
śaṅkho bherī bhūdevabhūmipāḥ | gāvaśchatraṃ ratho yānaṃ 
dakṣiṇe subhagāḥ smṛtāḥ | siddhānnaṃ māṃsapiṇḍañca 
bhaktabhājanajantavaḥ |
mṛtpiṇḍaṃ kuṇapāhārāḥ sarvāśca dakṣiṇe śubhāḥ | kanyā vai 
mithune veśyā pūrṇakumbhāstriyastathā | pakṣiṇaḥ

p. 773) paśavaścaiva caranto vāmataḥ śubhāḥ | agre dadhiphale yātrā 
śubhadā parikīrttitā | tathā krauñcamayūrāśca yugmaṃ gacchanti 
gacchataḥ | tadā siddhiṃ vijānīyādanyathā vighnamādiśet | gṛdhrāḥ 
śyenāśca cillāśca pārśve gacchanti vai yadā | na kuryād yātriko 
yātrāṃ bādhake vacasi sthite || pañcamapaṭale || kāmarūpamadhye || 
janārdanacihnaśailaṃ nirūpya || uttare tasya śailasya aiśānyāṃ 
virajasya ca | dakṣiṇe caiva śailasya paścime śaubhraliṅgakam | 
etanmadhyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ āgastyaṃ nāma vai param | trayaṃ 
śatamitaṃ kṣetramaśāmbhavamidaṃ smṛtam | etadgayāṃ 
vijānīyādanyallohityamucyate | tatra piṇḍapradānena pitṝṇāṃ 
paramā gatiḥ |
janārdanasya haste ca svādu piṇḍaṃ samarpayet | eṣa piṇḍo mayā 
dattastava haste janārdana ! | paraloke prabho ! mahyaṃ tvaṃ hi dātā 
bhaviṣyasi | kaliśeṣasya pūrve tu dhanuraṣṭapramāṇataḥ | sā śilā 
pretabhāvena pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇāya ca |
tatra piṇḍapradānena na preto jāyate kvacit |
cakratīrthasya cāgneye dhanurdvandvapramāṇataḥ | liṅgaṃ lolaṃ 
paraṃ tīrthaṃ tiladugdhaiḥ pratarpayet |
janārdanaṃ tato vīkṣya mucyate vai ṛṇatrayāt | kalidvāparayoḥ 
sandhau dhanurarddhapramāṇataḥ | śukreṇa sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ 
śukreśaṃ nāmataḥ śrutam | devaṃ śukreśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā ko na 
mucyeta bandhanāt | goleśvaraṃ tathā dṛṣṭvā mucyate brahmahatyayā 
| aṅgāreśañca siddheśaṃ gajādityaṃ gajaṃ tathā | 
mārkaṇḍeyeśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā pitṝṇāmanṛṇo bhavet | 
gayāgoleśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā snātvā devaṃ janārdanam | etena kiṃ na 
vihitaṃ nṛṇāṃ sukṛtikāraṇam |
brahmalokaṃ nayantīha puruṣāneka viṃśatim | pṛthivyāṃ yāni 
tīrthāni cāsamudrasarāṃsi ca | cakratīrthe ca gacchanti vāramekaṃ 
dine dine | pṛthivyāñca gayā puṇyaṃ gayāyāṃ kūpakaṃ tathā |
kūpādaṣṭaguṇā devi ! śreṣṭhā mātṛgayā

p. 774) śubhe ! | putro mātṛgayāṃ gatvā hyanṛṇo bhavati kṣaṇān |
gayāyāṃ piṇḍadānena pitṝṇāmanṛṇo bhavet | gayāntaṃ 
piṇḍadānañca gayāntaṃ tīrthameva ca || tathā || atrāgastye 
mahākṣetre svakaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadettuyaḥ | māsadvayādhikaṃ 
varṣamāyuṣo varddhate kramāt | pitrośca jīvatoḥ putro na 
kuryādaurddhadehikam | bahuputre tvekaputre putre vā yogasevite |
janmakuṣṭhayute putre aputre vā maheśvari! | catvāriṃśatpare devi ! 
svayamātmakriyāṃ caret | svahaste tu vṛṣotsargaṃ yaḥ 
karotyaurddhadehikam | paralokagate devi ! akṣayaṃ tadapi smṛtam |
dampatyorjīvatoḥ kuryāt vṛṣotsargadvayaṃ sadā | pitrośca jīvatoḥ 
putro na kuryādaurddhadehikām | udyatastu gayāṃ gantuṃ śrāddhaṃ 
kṛtvā vidhānataḥ | vidhāya karpaṭīveśaṃ grāmasya ca pradakṣiṇam |
tato grāmāntaraṃ gatvā śrāddhaśeṣasya bhojanam | kṛtvā 
pradakṣiṇaṃ gacchet pratigrahavivarjitaḥ | gṛhāccāratnimātrañca 
agastyagamanaṃ prati | svargārohaṇasopānaṃ pitṝṇāntu pade pade |
divā rātrau sarvadā ca āgastye śrāddhakṛd bhavet | nirāmiṣaṃ 
kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā ca viṣṇuvarjitam | śapanti pitarastasya 
nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ | āmaśrāddhe cāmamāṃsaṃ 
pradadyādavicārayan | pitaro'dhomukhāstasya tiṣṭhanti niyataṃ priye ! 
| samiṣañca kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ bhuṅkte yastu nirāmiṣam | 
tāmisranarakaṃ gacchet pitṛbhiḥ saha nānyathā | nirāmiṣam kṛtaṃ 
yena kuryāt śrāddhaṃ nirāmiṣam | kuryādāmiṣabhuk śrāddhaṃ 
sāmiṣantu varānane ! || matsyasūkte ṣaḍviṃśatipaṭale'pi || na 
kuryādāmiṣairhīnaṃ varṣaśrāddhaṃ maheśvari ! | parvaśrāddhaṃ 
gayāśrāddhamāmiṣeṇa praśasyate | tīrthaśrāddhaṃ 
nāndīmukhaṃ kuryādāmiṣavarjitam | nirāmiṣakṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ 
yastvaśnīyācca sāmiṣam | nirāśāḥ pitaro yānti śāpaṃ datvā 
mudāruṇam | śūdrairnimantrite vipre śrāddhayajñotsaveṣu ca |

p. 775) brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayedyastu bhuṅkte viṣṭāṃ sa śaṅkari ! |
māhiṣaṃ vṛhadājañca aiṇañca camaraṃ tathā | godhāṃ 
kūrmañca śallañca śāśakaṃ śaukaraṃ tathā | vārāhañca tathā 
meṣaṃ śrāddhe deyāni sarvaśaḥ | akalau tu gavāṃ māṃsaṃ 
sārameyañca taddvayam | hīnendriyañca chagalamakṛṣṇaṃ tañca 
varjayet | kṛṣṇachāgasya māṃsena mārgeṇaikena ṣaṭsamāḥ |
cāmareṇa śataṃ varṣaṃ sahasraṃ godhikā priye ! | ṣaṇmāsañca 
varārohe ! kurmaṇa māsamātrakam | śaukareṇa tu ṣaṇmāsaṃ 
śāśakena navaiva tu | dvāviṃśacchālvakenaiva 
kṣudravārāhakaistathā | śatābdaṃ cāmareṇaiva sthūlamāṃsantu 
varjayet | yatra varjyaṃ bhavet puṃbhiścaturbhiḥ ṣaḍbhireva ca |
atisthūlamiti proktaṃ tasmāttat pūrvasūribhiḥ | mahiṣasya ca gavyasya 
varāhasya mama priye ! | mārgasya vṛhadājasya sthūlañcāpi 
praśasyate | khaḍgaṃ pāñcanakhaṃ bhakṣyaṃ kāpotaṃ 
khaḍgasaṃyutam | caturmukhaṃ cāviśālaṃ varjayecca mama priye ! |
godhikāṃ svarṇakhaḍgañca cāmaraṃ kṛṣṇameva ca | varjayet 
kūrmakaṃ vidvān yadi cakreṇa cihnitam | siṃhatuṇḍaṃ rohitañca 
rājīvaṃ citrakaṃ tathā | mahāśaṅkaṃ prauṣṭhikañca matsyañca 
pārvatīyakam | vṛhadrohitamatsyañca vṛhatprauṣṭhīkameva ca |
vṛhacchallañca citrañca śrāddhe yatnena yojayet | matsyāṃśca 
śalkahīnāṃśca sarpākārāṃśca varjayet | śalkahīnasya madhye tu 
pradeyaṃ kavacadvayam | potodhānādikaṃ yacca vikṛtākārakañca 
yat | sarpāsyān pīvarāṃścaiva vaḍālīñca vivarjayet | tathā | 
dhūmapakvañca kadalaṃ śarkarāṃ kīṭasaṃyutām | mahiṣasya 
ghṛtaṃ kṣīramājaṃ śrāddhe vivarjayet | nārikelañca tālañca 
kharjaraṃ pīnakaṃ tathā | takraṃ ghṛtaṃ vinā kṣīraṃ prayatnena 
vivarjayet | dīpaṃ tyajet vastravarttyā pratyakṣaṃ tailameva ca |
kusumbhaṃ nālikāśākaṃ mālatīkusumaṃ tathā | vṛddhiśrāddhe 
paṅkajañca karavīrāṇivarjayet | ekajātīyapātre tu dadyādannaṃ 
samāhitaḥ | daive tu prathamaṃ dadyāt pitṛpātre tadantaram |

p. 776) pitṛśeṣantu daive'nnaṃ nava dadyāt kadācana | annaṃ na 
kṣālayet kvāpi āmaśrāddhe niragnike | vṛddhau tu kṣālayedannaṃ 
saṃkrame grahaṇeṣu ca | etadvacanaṃ smārttenāpi dhṛtam | 
aṣṭamuṣṭipramāṇena brāhmaṇe naiva saṃkramāt | tato'dhikañca 
nyūnañca na dadyāt śrāddhakarmaṇi | yaḥ śrāddhaṃ padmapātre 
ca karoti sumanohare | varṣāṇāntu śataṃ sāgraṃ tṛptirbhavati 
niścitam | aśvatthasya cchade devi ! brahmapatre ca śaṅkari ! |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ jāyate tṛptiranantāṃ śatapatrake | māsaikaṃ tāmrapātre 
ca rukmapātre tu vatsaram | raupye daśaguṇaṃ proktaṃ khaḍgapātre 
śatottaram | ekajātīyapātre tu mṛtāhe śrāddhakarmaṇi | pārvaṇe ca 
tathā vṛddhau pṛthagjātiñca yojayet | saṃvatsaraṃ bhavettāvad 
brīhīṃścaiva niyojayet | varṣādbhavati yo vrīhivrate śrāddhe 
vivarjayet | dhānyaṃ varṣāsamudbhūtaṃ tilaṃ śrāvaṇikañca yat |
yajñādau ca tathāśrāddhe dvisvinnaṃ parivarjayet | ṣaṣṭi dhānyaṃ 
rājadhānyaṃ vṛhaddhānyañca ballabham | somadhānyaṃ 
śīghradhānyaṃ vaṅgāniraktapāṇikam | ketakīṃkalaviṅkañca 
dhānyaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ tathā | mādhavañca pradīpañca 
viṣṇudhānyañca vallabham | bhogadhānyamaśokañca nāgākhyaṃ 
pañcakaṃ tathā | dhānyāni śrāddhayogyāni deveṣu ca niyojayet |
godhūmaiśca yavaiścaiva cāpūpaiśca maheśvari ! | nīvārāṃśca 
tathā śrāddhe devadhānyaṃ tathā param | vasante ropitaṃ dhānyaṃ 
yatnena ca vivarjayet | tadannabhakṣaṇādeva pāpaṃ saṃkramate 
nṛṇām | bhakṣaṇe śrāvaṇānnasya daridraścābhijāyate | bhakṣaṇe 
somadhānyasya vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | bhakṣaṇe 
vṛddhadhānyasya vibhavo jāyate kila | rājadhānyaślakṣṇadhānya 
bhakṣaṇādviṣṇulokabhāk raktadhānyaudanaṃ bhuktvā 
vijayaśrīmavāpnuyāt | nārāyaṇaṃ mādhavasya bhaogaśca mama 
sundari ! | dhānyatrayodaśaṃ bhuktvā naraḥ khyātimavāpnuyāt |

p. 777) nimantrito brāhmaṇaśca yadi śrāddhe nahi vrajet | dāruṇaṃ 
narakaṃ yāti pitṛbhiḥ saha śāśvatam || iti 
tīrthayātrādiśrāddhakathanam ||

atha kāśīmāhātmyam || yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe 
ṣoḍaśapaṭale || tripurāsurajanyatatparājitanirjarastavānantaram |
pṛthivīñca rathaṃ kṛtvā cakre candradivākarau | brahmāṇaṃ 
sārathiṃ kṛtvā vedānnrajjuṃstathaiva ca | devān kṛtvā rathāṅgāni 
amāṃścaiva tathā punaḥ | dhanuḥ kṛtvā sumeruñca jyāñca kṛtvā 
tu vāsukim | viśvañca sakalaṃ kṛtvā rathasthaṃ sa carācaram |
vāṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ vidhāyaiva tripuraṃ bhasmasāt kṛtam | carācareṇa 
sahitaṃ devadaityādibhiḥ saha | mayā tatra maheśāni ! punaḥsṛṣṭiḥ 
kṛtā tataḥ | yatra bhasmakṛtaṃ devi ! jagadetaccarācaram | 
mahāśmaśānaṃ tadviddhi sarveṣāṃ layakāraṇam | mṛtānāṃ 
sarvadevānāṃ tejastatra vyavasthitam | pañcakrośātmakaṃ bhūtvā 
tejastu jagatāṃ tathā | nirmāya māyayā dehaṃ traipuraṃ bhasma 
vakṣasi | tatra mṛtyucchalenāhaṃ tuṣṭāva parameśvarīm |
tattejasi mahākālyāṃ parāṃ caitanyarūpīṇīm | tatastejasi kālī sā 
prādurbhūtā parā kalā | mahasā dīpyamānantu tejaḥ kālīti 
kīrttitam | mukhamātraṃ samādṛṣṭaṃ mahākālyāstu tejasi |
ato gaurīmukhaṃ nāma munibhiḥ parigīyate | taddṛṣṭvā parameśāni ! 
ānando mama jāyate ! ānandakānanaṃ tasmādgīyate devatādibhiḥ |
kālīmayaṃ hi tattejaḥ sakalaṃ saṃ vabhūva ha | yathā 
sāgarajāto'mbhaḥśīkaraḥ sāgaro bhavet | tathāmarāditejo hi 
kālītejo vabhuva ha | yathā nānājalaṃ devi ! gaṅgāyāṃ patitaṃ 
yadi | gaṅgaiva jāyate sarvaṃ tathā tejaḥ sureśvari ! | sarvaṃ 
kālyabhavat pūrṇaṃ nāsti bhedo maheśvari ! | ataḥ kāśī bhavet 
kālī niścitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ | pañcakrośātmikā 
kāśībrahmatejomayī śritā | arddhacandrātmikā devi ! dṛśyate

p. 778) sarvajātibhiḥ | iyaṃ tvamākalā kāśī kṣayodayavivarjitā |
tatra vindu mahādevi ! maṇikarṇi virājate | taccandrāmṛtamevātra 
gaṅgāṃ jānīhi kāmini ! | yāni kāni ca tīrthāni tatra kālyāṃ 
maheśvari ! | sarvaṃ tadamṛtaṃ devi ! jānīhi surasundari ! | 
tāmasomaniśaṃ devi ! śirasā dhārayāmyaham | ato hi śaṅkaratvaṃ 
me niścitaṃ satyameva hi | tāṃ kālīṃ śirasādhāya 
pañcakrośamayīṃ sadā | aharniśaṃ pūjayāmi 
paramānandavṛṃhitaḥ | atoviśveśvaratvaṃ me sadaiva nātra 
saṃśayaḥ | brahmaviṣṇvādikānāñca ya īśvaraḥ sureśvari ! |
viśveśvaraḥ sa eva syānnāparaḥ parameśvari ! | kevalānandavān 
bhūtvā pūjayāmi parāṃ sadā | tatra tasyāḥ kṛpā jātā 
vācyamānā śarīriṇām |

devyuvāca |

bho deva ! paramānanda ! mamānandaḥ kṛtastvayā | ataḥ kāśyāṃ 
mṛtānāṃ tvamānandaṃ dehi sarvadā |

īśvara uvāca ||

iti śrutvā vacastasyā magno'hamamṛtārṇave | dadāmi paramaṃ 
brahma mumūrṣoḥ karṇagocare | vārāṇasyāṃ sadā devi ! sthitvā 
dhyāyan parāṃ śive ! | jale shale cāntarīkṣe vārāṇasyāṃ mṛtāstu 
ye | dadāmi paramaṃ brahma teṣāṃ hi karṇagocare | hitvā hi sakalaṃ 
karma sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ hi te || prayānti brahma nirvāṇaṃ 
mamopadeśataḥ kṣaṇāt | tatsarvaṃ sukṛtaṃ karma duṣkṛtaṃ vā 
maheśvari ! | bhavedbhasma mahākālyāḥ prasādājjñānayogataḥ |
kāśīlagnaṃ hi yatkiñcit kāśī bhavati tatkṣaṇāt | 
kāśīsparśanamātreṇa kāśyāntu mṛtyumeti saḥ | tajjanmani 
mahādevi ! athavā parajanmani | satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ 
satyameva sureśvari ! | vahnitejo dahet tūlaṃ sparśamātrāt kṣaṇaṃ 
yathā | śūlo karma dahetkāśītejaḥsparśāt kṣaṇāttathā |
tūlarāśiṃ dahedvahniḥ kiñcitkālād yathā śive ! | tathā

p. 779) dahetkarmarāśiṃ kāśījanmaikato nṛṇām | kāśīsthānaṃ 
puṇyacayaṃ kiṃ vāhaṃ kathayāmi te | api cet tvatsamā nārī 
matsamaḥ puruṣo'sti cet | aṇḍajāḥ svedajāścaiva udbhijjāśca 
jarāyujāḥ | te sarve muktimāyānti kāśyāñcedbhāgyatomṛtāḥ |
iyaṃ vārāṇasī devi ! mahātejomayī śubhā | yugabhedājjanaireva 
dṛśyate hi caturvidhā ! kṛte ratnamayī kāśī tretāyāṃ svarṇajā 
smṛtā | dvāpare sā śilārūpā kalau bhūmimayī śubhā | nātaḥ 
parataraṃ kṣetraṃ triṣu lokeṣu vidyate | satyaṃ satyaṃ mahādevi ! 
śapathena vadāmi te | saṃsārabahulo devi ! muktimicchati yaḥ punaḥ |
māmeva śaraṇaṃ gatvā tiṣṭhet kāśyāṃ sa yantritaḥ | sa eva patito 
jñānī sa eva kulapāvanaḥ | prāṇānte'pi mahādevi ! kāśyā na 
niḥsareddvijaḥ | sa eva paramo mūrkhaḥ sa eva kulanāśanaḥ | vṛthaiva 
mūrkhalokena kāśīṃ prāpya tyajed yadi | bahubhirjanmabhiḥ 
puṇyairyadi kāśīṃ labhet punaḥ | tadā naiva tyajet kāśīṃ 
prāṇānte'pi kadācana | anāyāsena saṃsārasāgaraṃ yastitīrṣati |
sa gacchet khalu kenāpi mama vārāṇasīṃ purīm | annaṃ 
dadyādannapūrṇā jñānaṃ dadyāt sarasvatī | prāṇānte 
muktidātāhaṃ kāśyānyaṃ bhāvanā kimu ||  
vṛndāvanakāñcyādimāhātmyastvatra granthagauravabhiyā na 
likhitam | anveṣakairyoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe pañcadaśapaṭalādiṣu 
tadanneṣyamiti ||

śrīprāṇatoṣaṇyāṃ bhaktikāṇḍābhidhānaṃ 
saptaparicchedānvitaṃ pañcamakāṇḍaṃ samāptam ||



ṣaṣṭhasya jñānakāṇḍasya nirghaṇṭo'tra vidhīyate | ādau 
pañcamahāyogo yamādistadanantaram | ṣaḍaṅgayogakathanaṃ

p. 780) yamānāṃ mukhyakīrttanam | niyamānāṃ tathā 
mukhyamāsanaṃ tadanantaram | yogavidyā yogavighnā maṭhakriyā 
tataḥ param | prāṇāyāmasya bhedaśca kumbhakasya tataḥ param |
prāṇāyamakriyākālāścatvāraḥ parikīrttitāḥ | 
śreṣṭhamadhyādhamatvena prāṇasiddheśca lakṣaṇam | śramodakena 
tenaiva tanumārjanajaṃ phalam | nāḍīśuddheśca cihnāni 
kumbhasiddhiphalaṃ tataḥ | varjanīyāni yoge ca tataḥ siddhikarāṇi ca |
apathyapathye yogasya tato'vasthā manonmanī | kumbhakāḥ saptadhā 
bhinnā netyādi tadanantaram | karmāṇi phalayuktāni dhautyādīni tataḥ 
param | sādhakābhyāsajaphalaṃ tadvighnakīrttanaṃ tataḥ |
ghaṭāvasthā tatphalañca tadvighnañca tataḥ param | tataśca 
mudrādaśakaṃ saphalañca nirūpitam | 
etadādyaparicchedasamāpanamataḥ param | pṛthvyādidhāraṇaṃ tasya 
phalaṃ dhyānaṃ tataḥ param |
samādhistasyāpi phalaṃ prāṇāyāmaphalāntaram | tithibhede 
vāmadakṣanāsāvāyugatikramaḥ | viparītagaterdoṣo nirgame 
copaveśane | vāyoḥ karmaviśeṣe tu phalañca vāmadakṣayoḥ |
vāyoḥ karmaviśeṣe'pi phalaṃ praśne ca diksthiteḥ | phalaṃ 
pūrṇaśūnyabhedādgatirnāḍyāstathaiva ca | aṅgabhede kāryabhede 
pañcabhūtādayastataḥ | teṣāṃ śubhāśubhe caiva tatkālasya 
nirūpaṇam | bhūtajñānaṃ tadavasthāḥ pañca bālyādibhedataḥ |
tatphalañca vārabhede vāmadakṣaphalaṃ tataḥ | diggatirdhamanī 
vāyau svasvanāḍīphalaṃ tataḥ | viparīte vaiparītyaṃ phalasya 
tadanantaram | vṛhannāḍīpade yātrā śubhadā vātavedataḥ |
yātrāpadāni ca vahannāḍīhastasya mārjanam | tatphalaṃ 
pūrṇariktāṅgabhede karmanirūpaṇam | vāmanāsāśritaṃ karma 
dakṣanāsāśritaṃ tathā || suṣumnāśritakāryāṇi 
bhūtarūpādivedanam | bhūtagatiḥ pañcabhūtabhede kāryasya 
nirṇayaḥ | praśnasya nirṇayo bhūtaiḥ śubhāśubhanirūpaṇam |
mūlādi cintāvijñānaṃ bhūtaistātkālikī tathā | avasthendoḥ

p. 781) siddhikālaḥ pṛthvyavasthānirūpaṇam | 
tattvairnakṣatravijñānaṃ nisargagatinirṇayaḥ | gamanādyairgaterbhedo 
vāyostat nyūnakāraṇām | phalañca tadvadūrddhvādau śvāsabhede 
jayājayau | tattvenāstrasya ghaṭanaṃ tattvairyuddhe jayādikam |
praveśakāle lābhādirhānirniḥsaraṇe tathā | śūnye na 
ghātanirṇātirbhūtena sthānanirṇayaḥ | jayādinirṇayaścaiva 
vaśīkaraṇameva ca | garbhajñānaṃ vatsarasya śubhāśubhaphalaṃ 
tataḥ | nidānādiparijñānaṃ rogasya nirṇayastataḥ | 
mṛtyujīvanavijñānaṃ kālajñānaṃ tataḥ param | 
varṣatrayādivijñānaṃ jīvanasya svakasya ca | 
arundhatyādivijñānamāyurvṛddhikriyā tataḥ | 
svarajñānapraśaṃseti paricchedadvitīyakam || rājayogapraśaṃsā ca 
tadbhedastithisaṃkhyakaḥ | bhedānāṃ lakṣaṇañcaiva lakṣayogastataḥ 
param | tasya pañcavidho bhedo rājayogasya lakṣaṇam | haṭayogaśca 
dvividho jñānayogastadantaram | vāhyalakṣaṃ 
tatphalañcākāśapañcakalakṣaṇam | 
ṣoḍaśādhāranirṇītiraṣṭāṅgavivṛtistataḥ | 
piṇḍabrahmāṇḍayoraikyaṃ piṇḍamadhye jagatsthitiḥ | 
rājayogasiddhiphalaṃ padmasaṅketamadbhutam | 
avyaktāderguṇāścaiva yogādeśastataḥ param | yogādhikārī yogasya 
siddhikāraṇameva ca | yogabhedānmantrayogo layayogasya vistṛtiḥ |
kālapuruṣavijñānaṃ tatphalaṃ tadanantaram | rasanāsādhanaṃ tasya 
phalaṃ nānāvidhaṃ tataḥ | paricchedatṛtīyasya samāptikīrttanaṃ 
tataḥ | sthūlasūkṣmakrameṇaiva brahmāṇḍasya nirūpaṇam |
sarvadevāśrayo meruḥ satyalokastadūrddhvataḥ | adho rasātalaṃ tasya 
bhuvanānyevameva ca | ādhārapadmavistāraḥ svādhiṣṭhānasya 
varṇanam | tatra vaikuṇṭhagolokavarṇanaṃ tadanantaram | 
maṇipūravarṇanañcānāhatasya ca varṇanam | viśuddhavarṇanaṃ 
tasmādājñākhyavarṇanaṃ tataḥ | sahasrāravarṇanañcādho vaktre 
gurudevayoḥ | sthitivījañca ṣaṭcakraprakārāntaravarṇanam |

p. 782) paricchedacaturthasya samāpanamiti smṛtam || jñānaprakāraṃ 
jñānasya dvaividhyaṃ dvaitavarjitam | jñānaṃ 
tatphalanirdeśo'dvaitajñānākṛtistataḥ | tattvajñānaṃ 
tatpraśaṃsātmanaḥ sākṣitvanirṇayaḥ | rathitvañca rathāditvaṃ 
śarīrādestataḥ param | bhoktṛmuktatvanirṇītiruttamādinirūpaṇam |
brahmajñasya dhyānapūjādyabhāvastadanantaram | ātmanirūpaṇaṃ 
jñānaṃ trividhaṃ jñānakāraṇam | nirvāṇasādhakaṃ karma 
śivavījanirūpaṇam | pāśanirūpaṇaṃ jīvātmābhedadarśanaṃ 
tataḥ | ātmano janmamaraṇādyabhāvadarśanaṃ punaḥ | 
piṇḍapadādinirṇītistantra śravaṇajaṃ phalam | vidyātattvaṃ 
brahmavidyā praśaṃsārjitapuṇyataḥ | brahmajñānaṃ 
paramātmanirṇayastadanantaram | bhikṣuniṣṭhaiva sā vidyā brahmaṇo 
jagadudbhavaḥ | brahmajñānaprāptiphalaṃ 
brahmamantrapraśaṃsanam | brahmamantroddhṛtirmantrārthacaitanye 
tadarcanā | brahmastotraṃ tatkavacaṃ tannaivedyaphalaṃ tataḥ |
tadarcakapraśaṃsā ca tanmantrasya puraskriyā | anyānyapi ca vedyāni 
yathāsthānaṃ manīṣibhiḥ | paricchedapañcamasya samāpanamiti 
smṛtam | mātrādicakraviṃśañca bālādicakramadhyagam | tasya 
vyākhyānametaddhi ṣaṣṭhacchedasamāpanam | sarvatobhadracakrañca 
tadvyākhyā prakriyāyutā | cakraṃ śatapadaṃ tasmāt tadvyākhyā 
tadanantaram | praśnapravacane vāṇī praṇāmo mantra eva ca |
praśnasya kathane tasya saṅketastadanantaram | prastāracakraṃ tasyāpi 
vyākhyānaṃ tadanantaram | paricchedasaptamasya samāpanamidaṃ 
kṛtam | yathāsthānamanyadapi jñeyaṃ dhīmadbhiratra tu ||
idaṃ vistṛtasaptabhirjñānakāṇḍaṃ paricchedarūpaiḥ prasūnaiḥ 
suramyam | budhālokatāṃ gandhamugdho'nuvāraṃ phalaṃ kintu 
gurvāsyalabhyaṃ nu vividdhi | kṣamadhvaṃ paṇḍitā ! doṣaṃ 
parapiṇḍopajīvinaḥ | mamāśudhādikaṃ sarvaṃ śodhyaṃ 
yuṣmābhiruttamaḥ || ādeṣṭṭasatvaratayā punarīkṣituṃ 
naitacchakyamuttamavaco racitaṃ mamedam | vijñāpayāni satataṃ
p. 783) parivikṣya sarvaṃ ghorā ! bhavadbhiratisūkṣmadhiyā 
viśodhyam ||

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe ṣaṭtriṃśatpaṭale | yathā ||

śrī-ānandabhairavyuvāca ||

triguṇajñānavikalo na paśyati divāniśam | divā rātrau jñānahetau 
na paśyati kalevaram | iti kṛtyā hi maraṇaṃ nṛṇāṃ janmani janmani |
tajjanmakṣayahetośca mama yogaṃ samabhyaset | 
pañcāmarāmahāyogaṃ kṛtvā syādamaro naraḥ | tat prakāraṃ 
śṛṇu prāṇavallabha ! priyadaśana ! | tava bhāvena kathaye na kutravada 
śaṅkara ! | yadi no kasya nikaṭe kathyate yogasādhanam | vighnā ghorā 
vasantye va gātre yogādikaṃ katham | 
yogayogādbhavenmokṣastatprakāśāddhi nāśanam | ato na darśayed 
yogaṃ yadīcchedātmano hitam | kṛtvā pañcāmarāyogaṃ pratyahaṃ 
bhaktisaṃyutaḥ | paṭhet śrīkuṇḍalīdevīsahasranāma cāṣṭakam |
mahāyogī bhavennātha ! śvāsena nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
pañcāmarāyogavidyā sarvavidyāprakāśinī kṛtvā 
pañcāmarāsiddhiṃ tato'ṣṭāṅgādidhāraṇā | ādau 
pañcāmarāsiddhistato'lpayogasādhanam | tatprakāraṃ pravakṣyāmi 
sāvadhānāvadhāraya | ahaṃ jānāmi saṃsāre kevalaṃ yogapaṇḍitā 
| tatsākṣiṇī hyahaṃ nātha ! bhaktānāmudayāya ca | idānīṃ kathaye 
te'hamamarāyogasādhanam | evaṃ kṛtvā nityarūpī 
yogānāmaṅkurāyate | yogakālena puruṣaḥ siddhimāpnoti niścitam |
pañcāmarāvidhānāni krameṇa śṛṇu śaṅkara ! | netīyogaṃ hi 
siddhānāṃ mahākaphavināśanam | dantīyogaṃ pravakṣyāmi 
hṛdayagranthibhedanam | dhautīyogaṃ tataḥ paścāt sarvaṃ 
malavināśanam | netīyogaṃ hi paramaṃ sarvāṅgodaracālanam |
kṣālanaṃ paramaṃ yogaṃ nāḍīnāṃ kṣālanaṃ smṛtam | evaṃ 
pañcāmarāyogaṃ yamināmatigocaram | yamaniyamakāle tu 
pañcāmarākriyāṃ yajet | amarāsādhanādeva amaratvaṃ

p. 784) bhavedvaram | etatkaraṇakāle ca tathā pañcāmarāśanam |
pañcāmarābhakṣaṇena amaro yogasiddhibhāk | tāni dravyāṇi 
vakṣyāmi tavāgre parameśvara ! | yena hīnā na sidhyanti 
kalpakoṭiśatena ca | ekā tu amarā dūrvā tasyā granthiṃ samānayet |
anyā tu vijayādevī siddhirūpā sarasvatī | anyā tu vilvapatrasthā 
śivasantoṣakāriṇī | anya tu yogasiddhārthe nirguṇī cāmarā matā |
anyā tu kālatulasī śrīviṣṇoḥ priyatoṣaṇī | etāḥ pañcāmarā 
jñeyā yogasādhanakarmaṇi | etāsāṃ dviguṇaṃ grāhyaṃ 
vijayāpatramuttamam | brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā sarvatra 
pūjitā | etaddravyāṇi saṃgṛhya bhakṣayeccūrṇamuttamam |
taccūrṇabhakṣasamaye etanmantrādipañcakam | paṭhitvā bhakṣaṇaṃ 
kṛtvā naro mucyeta saṅkaṭāt | oṃ hare amarapuṣṭe ! 
tvamamṛtodbhavasambhave ! | amaraṃ māṃ sadā bhadre ! kuruṣva 
tvaṃ harapriye ! | oṃ dūrvāyai svāhā | iti dūrvāyāḥ || 
punarvijayāmantreṇa śodhayet sarvakanyakāḥ || oṃ saṃvide brahma 
ityādi || oṃ brāhmaṇyai namaḥ svāhā || oṃ siddhimūlakare ! 
devītyādi | arddhaṃ rājaputtravaśaṅkari ! rājakanye ! triśūlini! |
ai/ kṣattriyāyai namaḥ svāhā | oṃ ajñānendhanetyādi | hrīṃ 
vaiśyāyai namaḥ svāhā | oṃ namasyāmītyādi | śrīṃ śūdrāyai 
namaḥ svāhā | oṃ kāyasiddhikare ! devi! vilvapatranivāsini ! | 
amaratvaṃ sadā dehi śivatulyaṃ kuruṣva mām | oṃ śivadāyai namaḥ 
svāhā | oṃ nirguṇḍi ! parameśāni ! yogānāmadhidevate ! |
sā māṃ rakṣatu amare ! bhāvasiddhiprade ! namaḥ | oṃ śokāpahāyai 
namaḥ svāhā | oṃ viṣṇoḥ priye ! mahāmāye ! kālajvālanivāsini ! |
tulasi ! māṃ sadā rakṣa māmekamamaraṃ kuru | oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ aiṃ 
hrīṃ amarāyai namaḥ svāhā | punarekatra āhṛtya sarvāsāṃ 
śodhanaṃ caret | oṃ amṛte ! amṛtodbhave ! ityādi | dhenumudrāṃ

p. 785) yonimudrāṃ matsyamudrāṃ pradarśayet | 
tattvamudrākrameṇaiva tarpaṇaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ | amantrakaṃ 
saptavāraṃ gurunāmnā śire'rpayet | saptavāramiṣṭadevyā nāmnā 
mūrdhni pradāpayet | tatastarpaṇamākuryāt tarpayāmi namo namaḥ |
etadvākyasya pūrve tu iṣṭamantraṃ samuccaret | paradevatāṃ 
samuddhṛtya sarvādyapraṇavaṃ smṛtam | tato mukhe prajuhuyāt 
kuṇḍānāṃ nāmapūrvakam | oṃ vada vadetyādi 
sarvasattvavaśaṅkari | śatrukaṇṭhatriśūlini ! svāhā | etaduktvā 
āśramau ca vaśī sādhakasattamaḥ | pañcāmarārasānandaḥ 
kulācāravidhipriyaḥ | sthiracetā bhaved yogo yadi pañcāmarāgataḥ |

dattātreyasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamādhyāye || yamaśca 
niyamaścaiva āsanañca tataḥ param | prāṇāyāmaścaturthaḥ syāt 
pratyāhāraśca pañcamaḥ | ṣaṣṭhī tu dhāraṇā proktā dhyānaṃ 
saptamamucyate | samādhiraṣṭamaḥ proktaḥ sarvapuṇyaphalapradaḥ |
evamaṣṭāṅgayogañca yājñavalkyādayo viduḥ | kalasādyāstathā 
siddhā haṭaṃ kuryustathā yathā || niruttaratantre caturthapaṭale || 
āsanaṃ prāṇasaṃrodhaḥ pratyāhāraśca dhāraṇā |
dhyānaṃ samādhiretāni yogāṅgāni vadanti ṣaṭ | 
prāṇāyāmadviṣaṭkena pratyāhāraḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 
pratyāhāradviṣaṭkena jāyate dhāraṇā śubhā | 
dhāraṇādvādaśaproktaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyānaviśāradaiḥ |
dhyānadvādaśakaireva samādhirabhidhīyate | yat samādhau paraṃ 
jyotiruttaraṃ viśvatomukham | tasmin dṛṣṭe kriyā kācid yātāyātaṃ 
na vidyate || dattātreyasaṃhitāyām | yamā ye daśa samproktā 
ṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ | laghvāhārastu teṣveko mukhyo bhavati nāpare |
ahiṃsā niyameṣveko mukhyo bhavati nāpare | caturaśītilakṣeṣu 
tvāsaneṣūttamaṃ śuṛṇu | ādināthena samproktaṃ 
padmāsanamihocyate | uttānau caraṇau kṛtvā ūrusaṃsthau prayatnataḥ 
|
p. 786) ūrumadhye tathottānau pāṇī kṛtvā tato dṛśau | nāsāgre 
vinyasedvaktraṃ dantamūlañca jihvayā | uttolya civukaṃ vakṣaḥ 
saṃsthāpya pavanaṃ śanaiḥ | yathāśaktyā samākṛṣya 
pūrayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ | yathāśaktyā samākṛṣya dhārayedudaraṃ 
śanaiḥ | yathāśaktyā tataḥ paścādrecayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ | idaṃ 
padmāsanaṃ proktaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam ||  grahajāmale 
trayodaśapaṭale || ūrvorupari meḍhrānte ubhe pādatale tathā |
padmāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarvapāpapraṇāśanam | tathā |
yuvāvastho'pi vṛddho'pi vyādhito vā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | abhyāsāt 
siddhimāpnoti yoge sarvasya tantratā | brāhmaṇaḥ śramaṇo vāpi 
vṛddho vāpyahito'pi vā | kāpāliko vā cārvākaḥ śraddhayā sahitaḥ 
sudhīḥ | yogābhyāsarato nityaṃ sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt | kriyāyuktaḥ 
sa siddhaḥ syādakriyasya kathaṃ bhavet | śāstrasya pāṭhamātreṇa 
kathaṃ siddhiḥ prajāyate | muṇḍo vā daṇḍadhārī vā 
kāṣāyavasano'pi vā | nārāyaṇavado vāpi jaṭilo bhasmalepanaḥ |
namaḥ śivāyavādī vā brahvarccāpūjako'pi vā | 
sthānadvādaśapūjyo vā bahuvatsalabhāṣitaḥ | kriyāhīno'thavā 
krūraḥ kathaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt | na veśadhāraṇaṃ siddheḥ 
kāraṇaṃ na ca tattathā | kriyaiva kāraṇaṃ siddheḥ satyametattu 
sāṅkṛte | śiśnodarārthaṃ yogasya kathaṃ vā veśadhāriṇaḥ |
annapānavihīnāstu vañcayanti janān kila | 
uccāvacairvipralambhairyajante kṛśamānavāḥ | yoginī vayamityevaṃ 
mukhabhogaparāyaṇāḥ | śanaistathāvidhānajñān 
yogābhyāsavivarjitān | kṛtārthān vacanaireva varjayedveśadhāriṇaḥ |
ete cāviṣṭabhūtāḥ syuryogābhyāsasya sarvadā | varjayettān 
prayatnena na īdṛśī siddhidā kriyā | prathamābhyāsakāleṣu 
praveśasya sadā mune ! | ālasyaṃ prathamo vighno dvitīyastu 
prakathyate | pūrvoktadhūrttagoṣṭhī ca tṛtīyo mantrasādhanam |

p. 787) caturtho dhātuvādaśca pañcamaḥ pāpavādakaḥ | evaṃ 
bahuvidhā vighnā mṛgatṛṣṇāsamā mukhe | sthirā manasi jāyante 
tāstu jñātvā tyajet sudhīḥ | tathā | suśobhanaṃ maṭhaṃ kuryāt 
sūkṣmadvārantu nirvraṇam | suṣṭhu liptaṃ gomayena sudhayā vā 
prayatnataḥ | dine dine susaṃmṛṣṭaṃ sammārjanyāpyatandritaiḥ |
vāsitañca sugandhena dhūpitaṃ guggulādibhiḥ || grahajāmale 
dvādaśapaṭale ||

īśvara uvāca ||

haṭavidyā parā gopyā yogināṃ siddhimicchatām | devī vīryavatī 
guptā nirvīryā ca prakāśitā | suvāhye dhārmike deśe subhikṣe 
nirupadrave | ekāntaṃ maṭhamadhye ca sthātavyaṃ haṭayoginām |
svalpadvāramarandhragarttapiṭakaṃ nātyuccanīcāyataṃ 
samyaggomayasāndraliptamamalaṃ niḥśeṣavāhyojjhitam | vāhye 
maṇḍapakūpavediracitaṃ prākārasaṃveṣṭitaṃ proktaṃ 
yogamaṭhasya lakṣaṇamidaṃ saṃsāranistāriṇi ! || evaṃvidhamaṭhe 
sthitvā sarvacintāvivarjitaḥ | gurupadiṣṭamārgeṇa haṭayogaṃ 
samabhyaset || dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || 
malamūtrādibhirdoṣairaṣṭādaśabhireva ca | varjito dārasambandhaṃ 
vastraṃ vā'jinameva ca | nānyatrāstaraṇāsīnaḥ 
paraśaṅkāvivarjitaḥ | tasmin śastaṃ samāstīrya āsanaṃ 
viṣṭarādikam | tatropaviśya bhedhāvī padmāsanasamanvitaḥ |
samakāyaḥ prāñjaliśca praṇamya ceṣṭadevatām | tato 
dakṣiṇahastasyāpyaṅgaṣṭhena tu piṅgalām | nirudhya 
pūrayedvāyumiḍayā tu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | yathāśaktyā nirodhena 
tataḥ kuryācca kumbhakam | tatastyajet piṅgalayā śanaireva na vegataḥ |
punaḥ piṅgalayākṛṣ.ya pūrayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ | dhārayitvā 
yathāśakti recayenmārutaṃ śanaiḥ | yayā tyajettayāpūrya 
dhārayedavirodhataḥ | evaṃ prātaḥ samāsīnaḥ 
kuryādviṃśatikumbhakān | kumbhakaḥ sahito nāma 
sarvagrahavivarjitaḥ | madhyāhne'pi tathā kuryāt 
punarviṃśatikumbhakān | evaṃ sāyaṃ prakurvīta

p. 788) punaviṃśatikumbhakān | kurvīta rekakumbhābhyāṃ sahitān 
prativāsaram | kuryādevaṃ caturvāramanālasyo dine dine | evaṃ 
māsatrayaṃ kuryānnāḍīśuddhistato bhavet || grahajāmale 
trayodaśapaṭale || prāṇāyāmastridhā prokto recakumbhakapūrakaiḥ |
sahitaḥ kevalaśceti kumbhako dvividho mataḥ | recaścāpūrya yaḥ 
kāryaḥ sa vai sahitakumbhakaḥ | yāvatkevalasiddhiḥ syāt sahitaḥ 
tāvadabhyaset | recakaṃ pūrakaṃ tyaktvā sukhaṃ yadvāyudhāraṇam 
| prāṇāyāmo'yamityuktaḥ sa vai kevalakumbhakaḥ | 
gurupadiṣṭamārgeṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ samācaret | yāvadvāyuḥ sthito 
dehe tāvajjīvitamucyate | maraṇaṃ tasya niṣkrāntistato vāyuṃ 
nibandhayet | malākulāsunāḍīṣu māruto naiva madhyagaḥ | kathaṃ 
syāttanmalībhāvaḥ kāyaśuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet | śuddhimeti yadā 
sarvaṃ nāḍīcakraṃ malākulam | tadaiva jāyate yogī 
prāṇasaṃgrahaṇe kṣamaḥ | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryānnityaṃ 
sāttvikayā dhiyā | tathā suṣumnāpārśvasthā malāḥ śoṣaṃ 
prayānti hi | baddhapadmāsano yogī prāṇaṃ candreṇa pūrayet |
dhārayitvā yathāśakti punaḥ sūryeṇa recayet | prāṇaṃ sūryeṇa 
cākṛṣya pūjayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ | vidhivat kumbhakaṃ kṛtvā 
punaścandreṇa recayet | yena tyajecca tenaiva pūrayedanirodhataḥ |
recayecca tato'nyena recayecca na vegataḥ | candreṇa vāmanāsayā 
sūryeṇa dakṣiṇanāsayā  ityarthaḥ | prāṇaṃ cediḍayā 
pibenniyamitaṃ bhūyo'nyathā recayet pītvā piṅgalayā 
samīraṇamatho baddhvā tyajedvāmayā | sūryācandramasoranena 
vidhinā vimbadvayaṃ dhyāyataḥ śuddhā nāḍigaṇā bhavanti 
yāmino māsatrayādūrddhvataḥ || prātarmadhyandinaṃ sāyaṃ 
madhyarātre ca kumbhakān | caturaśītiparyantaṃ caturvāraṃ 
samabhyaset | prāṇāyāme mahādharmo yogino mokṣadāyakaḥ |
prāṇāyāmo divārātrau devi ! jalaṃ parityajet | adhame dvādaśī 
mātrā madhyame dviguṇā smṛtā | uttame triguṇā jñeyā

p. 789) prāṇāyāmasya nirṇaye | kanīyasi bhavet svedaḥ kampo bhavati 
madhyame | uttiṣṭhatyuttame prāṇī baddhapadmāsano muhuḥ | jalena 
śramajātena gātramārjanamācaret | dṛḍhatālayutā dīptistena 
gātrasya jāyate | yadā tu nāḍīśuddhiḥ syāttadā cihnāni vāhyataḥ |
jāyante yogino dehe tāni vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ | śarīralaghutā 
dīptirjaṭharāgnivivarddhanam | kṛśatvañca śarīrasya tasya jāyeta 
niścitam | pūrvoktakāle kurvanti paramābhyāsameva ca | tataḥ paraṃ 
yatheṣṭantu śaktiḥ syādvāyudhāraṇe | yatheṣṭadhāraṇādvāyoḥ 
siddhiḥ kumbhasya kevalā | kevale kumbhake siddhe recapūrakavarjite |
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcit triṣu lokeṣu didyate ||

atha kumbhakasiddhiphalam || prasvedo jāyate pūrvaṃ mardanaṃ 
tena kārayet | tatastu dhāraṇādvāyoḥ krameṇaiva śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | 
kampo bhavati dehasya āsanasthasya yoginaḥ | 
tato'dhikatarābhyāsāddārdurī jāyate dhruvam | yathaiva darduro 
gacchedutpatyotplutya bhūtale | padmāsanasthito yogī tathā gacchati 
bhūtale | tato'dhikatarābhyāsādbhūmityāgaśca jāyate | 
padmāsanasthito vāsau bhūmimutsṛjya varttate | nirādhāro vicitraṃ hi 
tadā sāmarthyamudvahet | alpaṃ vā bahu vā bhuktvā yogau na vyathate 
kvacit | alpamūtrapurīṣe tu alpanidrā ca jāyate | krimayo vṛścikā 
nānāsvedo durgandhatā tanoḥ | etāni sarvakhyātasya na jāyate 
kadācana | abhyāsakāle prathame śastaṃ kṣīrājyabhojanam | 
tato'bhyāse sthirībhūte na tādṛṅniyamagrahaḥ | yathā siṃho gajo 
vyāghro bhavedvaśyaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | tathaiva sevito vāyuranyathā 
hanti sādhakam | prāṇāyāmeṇa yuktena sarvarogakṣayo bhavet | 
ayuktābhyāsayogena sarvarogasamudbhavaḥ | hikkā śvāsaśca 
kāsaśca śiraḥ karṇākṣivedanā | bhavanti vividhā doṣāḥ pavanasya 
vyatikramāt || iti vāyuvyatikramadoṣaḥ || 

dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || tathā varjyāni vakṣyāmi 
yogavighnakarāṇi ca | lavaṇaṃ sarṣapañcānnamuṣṇaṃ rūkṣañca 
nindakam | atīva bhojanaṃ tyājyaṃ striyā saṅgamanaṃ bahu | 
agnisevā tu santyājyā dhūrttagoṣṭho viśeṣataḥ || grahajāmale | 
atyāhāraḥ pravāsaśca prajalpo niyamagrahaḥ | janasaṅgaśca 
laulyañca ṣaḍbhiryogaḥ praṇaśyati || iti varjanīyāni || 
utsāhānniścayāddhairyāttattvajñānācca sāhasāt | 
janasaṅgaparityāgāt ṣaḍbhiryogo hi sidhyati | tathā | 
kaṭvamlatiktalavaṇoṣṇaharītaśākāḥ 
sauvīratailabahusarṣapamatsyamadyāḥ | 
ājādimāṃsadadhitakrakulatthakolāḥ 
pinyākahiṅgulasunādimapathyamasmin || bhojanasahitaṃ vidyāt 
punarapyuṣṇīkṛtaṃ bhojyam | atilavaṇāśanapalalakandaśākotkaṭaṃ 
duṣṭam || varjayed durjanaprītiṃ vahnitāpasya sevanam | prātaḥ 
snānopavāsādi kāyakleśavidhiṃ tathā || ityapathyāni || 

tathā | godhūmaśālikalāyayavaṣaṣṭikānnaṃ 
kṣīrājyakhaṇḍanavanītasitāmadhūni | 
śuṇṭhīkaṇākaṇaphalādikapañcaśākaṃ dugdhādidivyamudakañca 
yamīndrapathyam || miṣṭaṃ sumadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ ramyaṃ 
dhātuprarohaṇam | mano'bhilaṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ yogī bhojanamācaret ||  
iti pathyāni || 

dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || upāyañca pravakṣyāmi śīghraṃ 
yogasya siddhaye | kṣīraṃ ghṛtañca miṣṭānnaṃ mitāhāraśca 
śasyate | ṣaṭkarmabhirgatasthaulyaḥ kaphamedomalārttigaḥ | 
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryādanāyāsena sidhyati | prāṇāyāmaireva 
sarve viśuṣyanti malāḥ priye ! | medaḥśleṣmādikaṃ nyasya 
karmaṣaṭkaṃ na sammatam | sumanaskātmako bhāvo yadi vāyuṃ

p. 791) samabhyaset | ṣaṭkarmayogamāpnoti pavanābhyāsatatparaḥ | 
brahmādayo'pi tridaśāḥ pavanābhyāsatatparāḥ | ataḥ siddhiṃ 
gatāste ca tasmātpavanamabhyaset | yāvadbaddho maruddehe 
tāvaccittaṃ nirāśayam | trāṭakañcedbhruvormadhye 
tāvatkālabhayaṃ kutaḥ | vidhivat prāṇasaṃrodhānnāḍīcakre 
viśoṣite | suṣumnāvadanaṃ bhittvā mukhādviśati mārutaḥ | mārute 
madhyasañcāre manaḥsthairyaṃ prajāyate | yo manaḥsusthirībhāvaḥ 
saivāvasthā manonmanī | tatsiddhaye viśeṣajñaścitrān kurvīta 
kumbhakān | vicitrakumbhakābhyāsādvicitrāṃ siddhimāpnuyāt || 
atha trāṭakam || nirīkṣenniścaladṛśā sūkṣmalakṣyaṃ prayatnataḥ | 
aśrusampātaparyantaṃ trāṭakaṃ tanmaheśvari ! | yatnatastrāṭakaṃ 
gopyaṃ tathā hāṭakapeṭikā || vicitrakumbhakāstu | 
sūryabhedanamuḍḍākhyaṃ tamā śītkāraśītalī | bhastrikā 
bhrāmarī mūrcchā plāvanī sapta kumbhakāḥ | ādau sūryabhedanam 
|| āsane sukhade yogī baddhvā muktāsanaṃ tataḥ | dakṣanāḍyā 
samākṛṣya vahnisthaṃ pavanaṃ śanaiḥ | ākeśāgrānnakhāgrādvā 
nirodhāvadhi kumbhayet | tataḥ śanaiḥ savyanāḍyā recayet pavanaṃ 
sudhīḥ || asya phalam || kapālaśodhanaṃ vātadoṣaghnaṃ 
kṛmidoṣaham | punaḥ punaridaṃ kāryaṃ sūryabhedanamuttamam || 1 || 

atha uḍḍākhyam || mukhaṃ saṃyamya nāsābhyāṃ cākṛṣya 
pavanaṃ śanaiḥ | yathā lagati kaṇṭhānte hṛdayāvadhi sasvanaḥ | 
pūrvavat kumbhayet prāṇaṃ recayediḍayā tataḥ || phalam ||  
sleṣmarogaharañcaitadanalairdīptabandhanam | 
nāḍījalodarādhātugaṇḍādoṣavināśanam | gacchatā tiṣṭhatā 
kāryamuḍḍākhyaṃ kumbhakantvidam || 2 || 

atha śītkāraḥ || śītkārañca sadā vaktre ghrāṇe caiva vijṛmbhakam | 
evamabhyāsayogena kāmadevo dvitīyakaḥ || phalam || 
yoginīcakramāsādya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ | na kṣudhā na tṛṣā 
nidrā naiva mūrcchā prajāyate | bhavet svacchandadehaśca

p. 792) sarvopadravavarjitaḥ | anena ca vinā satyaṃ yogīndro 
bhūmimaṇḍale | rasanātālumūlena yaḥ prāṇaṃ satataṃ pibet | 
yatnādūrddhvaṃ bhavet siddhiḥ sarvarogakṣayo bhavet || 3 || 

atha śītalī || jihvayā vāyumākṛṣ.ya pūrvavat kumbhakāditaḥ | 
śanaistu ghrāṇarandhrābhyāṃ recayedanilaṃ priye ! || phalam || 
gulmaplīhādikān doṣān jvaraṃ retaḥkṣayaṃ sudhām | tṛṣṇāñca 
śītalī nāma kumbhako'yaṃ nihanti vai || 4 || 

atha bhastrikā || samyak padmāsanaṃ baddhvā samagrīvodaraṃ priye ! | 
mukhaṃ saṃyamya yatnena prāṇaṃ ghrāṇena recayet | tathaiva 
svaśarīrasthaṃ pavanaṃ cālayet sphuṭam | tathā lagati 
hṛtkaṇṭhakapāle śvasanastataḥ | vegena pūrayet kiñcit 
hṛtpadmāvadhi mārutam | punarvirecayettadvat pūrayecca punaḥ punaḥ | 
yathaiva lauhakārasya bhastrāṃ vegena cālayet | tathaiva 
svaśarīrasthaṃ pavanaṃ cālayecchanaiḥ | yadā śramo bhavet pūrṇo 
bandhane ca tadā laghu | kārayennāsikāmadhyaṃ tarjanībhyāṃ vinā 
dṛḍham | kumbhakaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā recayediḍayānilam || phalam || 
vātapittaśleṣmaharaṃ śarīrāgnivivarddhanam | 
kuṇḍalīrodhanañcakre bhāraghnaṃ śubhadaṃ śuci | 
brahmanāḍīmukhe saṃsthakaphaghnaṃ malanāśanam | 
sabhyaṅmātraṃ samudbhūtaṃ granthitrayavibhedanam | viśeṣeṇaiva 
karttavyaṃ bhastrākhyaṃ kumbhakaṃ tvidam || 5 || 

atha bhrāmarī || vedodghoṣaṃ pūrakaṃ bhṛṅganādaṃ 
bhṛṅgīnādaṃ recakaṃ mandamandam | 
yogīndrāṇāmevamabhyāsayogāccite jātā kācidānandavallī || 6 | 

atha mūrcchā || pūrakānte gāḍhataraṃ baddhvā jālandharaṃ 
śanaiḥ | recayenmūrcchanākhyo'yaṃ manomūrcchā sukhapradā || 7 
|| 

atha plāvanī || antaḥpravarttitādhāramarutā pūritodaram | 
sākṣātpayasyagādhe'pi plavate padmapatravat || 8 || 

rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe || netīyogavidhānāni śṛṇuṣva

p. 793) vīrapūjita! | yena sarvamastakasthakaphānāṃ dāhanaṃ bhavet | 
sūkṣmasūtraṃ dṛḍhataraṃ pradadyānnāsikāvile | mukharandhre 
samānīya sandhāyainaṃ samāśrayet | punaḥ punaḥ sadā yogī 
yātāyātena gharṣayet | krameṇa varddhanaṃ kuryāt sūtrasya 
parameśvara ! | netīyogena nāsāyā randhraṃ nirmalakaṃ bhavet | 
vāyorgamanakāle tu mahāsukhamiti prabho ! || 1 || 

dantīyogaṃ tataḥ paścāt kuryāt sādhakasattamaḥ | dantadhāvanakāle 
tu yogametaṃ prakāśayet | dantadhāvanakāṣṭhantu 
sārddhahastaikasambhavam | nātisthūlaṃ nātisūkṣmaṃ navīnaṃ 
namramuttamam | apakvaṃ yatnato grāhyaṃ mṛṇālasadṛśaṃ tarum | 
gṛhītvā dantakāṣṭhaṃ vat prātaḥkāle prabhakṣayet | 
dantakāṣṭhāgrabhāgañca kaniṣṭhāṅguliparva ca | evaṃ 
dantāvalībhyāñca carvaṇaṃ sundarañcaret | tat prakṣālya ca nīreṇa 
śanairnirgamamācaret | śanaiḥ śanaiḥ prakarttavyaṃ 
kāyavākcittaśodhanam | yāvanna yāti kāṣṭhāgraṃ nābhimūle 
tvanākulam | tāvat sūkṣmataraṃ grāhyamavaśyaṃ pratyahañcaret | 
hṛdaye jalacakrañca yāvat khaṇḍaṃ na jāyate | tāvatkālaṃ sarvadine 
prabhāte radasādhanam | hṛdaye kaphabhāṇḍasyaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ 
jāyate dhruvam | pavanāgamane saukhyaṃ prāpnoti yoganirbharam | 
khecaratvaṃ lavaṇajamamlatvaṃ kumbhakodbhavam | miṣṭatvaṃ 
śākadadhyannaṃ dvivāraṃ rātribhojanam | avaśyaṃ yojayed yogī 
yadi yogamihecchati | ekabhāgaṃ svasvavījaṃ dvibhāgaṃ taṇḍulaṃ 
matam | uttamaṃ pakvamāhṛtya ghṛtadugdhena bhakṣayet | athavā 
kevalaṃ dugdhaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ | kuṇḍalīṃ 
kularūpāñca dugdhena paritarpayet | kuṇḍalītarpaṇaṃ yogī yadi 
jānāti śaṅkara ! | anāyāsena yogī syāt sa jñānīndro 
bhaveddhruvam || 

saptatriṃśatpaṭale ||
 
ānandabhairavyuvāca

dhautīyogaṃ pravakhyāmi yat kṛtvā nirmalo bhavet | atyantaguhyaṃ 
yogañca samāptikāraṇaṃ nṛṇām | yadi no kurute yogaṃ tadā 
maraṇamāpnuyāt | dhautīyogaṃ vinā nātha ! kaḥ sidhyati mahītale | 
sūkṣmāt sūkṣmataraṃ vastraṃ dvātriṃśaddhastamānataḥ | 
ekahastakrameṇaiva yaḥ karoti śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | 
yāvaddvātriṃśaddhastañca tāvatkālaṃ kriyāñcaret | 
etatkriyāprayogeṇa yogī bhavati tatkṣaṇāt | krameṇa mantrī siddhaḥ 
syāt kālajālaṃ vaśaṃ nayet | etanmadhye cāsanāni śarīrasthāni 
cācaret | dṛḍhāsane yogasiddhiriti tantrārthanirṇayaḥ | siddhe manau 
parāvāptiḥ pañcayogāsanena ca | pārśve cāṣṭāṅgulaṃ vastraṃ 
dīrghaṃ dvātriṃśadīśvara ! | etat sūkṣmaṃ suvasanaṃ gṛhītvā 
kārayed yatī | jitendriyaḥ sadā kuryāt jñānadhyānaniṣevaṇam | 
anācāreṇa hāniḥ syādindriyāṇāṃ balena ca | 
mahāpātakamukhyānāṃ saṅgadoṣeṇa hānayaḥ | sambhavanti 
mahādeva ! tava yogyaṃ sukarma ca | vṛddho vā yauvanastho vā bālo 
vā yatra tatra vā ekavarṇāddīrghajīvau syādamaro lokavallabhaḥ | 
mantrasiddhiriṣṭasiddhiḥ sa siddhīnāmadhīśvaraḥ | śanaiḥ śanaiḥ 
sadā kuryāt kāladoṣavināśanam | hṛdayagranthibhedena 
sarvāvayavavarddhanam | dhautīyogodbhavaṃ kāmaṃ 
mahāmaraṇakāraṇam | tasya tyāgaṃ yaḥ karoti sadaiva devavikramaḥ | 
dautīyogānantaraṃ hi neḍaṇīkarma cācaret | neḍaṇīyogamātreṇa 
āsane neḍaṇopamaḥ | neḍaṇīsādhanādeva cirajīvī nirāmayaḥ | 
tatkāraṇaṃ pravakṣyāmi sāvadhānā'vadhāraya | bhuktvā 
mudgānnapakvañca vāraikaṃ pratipālayet | pratipālayet svodarañca 
kaṭhināśāvivarjitaḥ | punaḥ punaścālanañca kuryāt 
svodaramadhyakam | kulālacakravat kuryād bhramaṇañcodarasya ca | 
sarvāṅgacālanādeva kuṇḍalīśakticālanam | cālanāt 
kuṇḍalīdevyāścetanā sā bhavet prabho ! | etasyānantaraṃ deva !

p. 795) kṣālanaṃ parikīrttitam | nāḍīnāṃ kṣālanādeva 
sarvavidyānidhirbhavet | vāyusiddhirbhavettasya pañcabhūtasya 
siddhibhāk | muṇḍāsanaṃ hi sarvatra sarvadā kārayedbudhaḥ | 
ūrddhvapadmāsanaṃ kṛtvā adhohaste japaṃ caret | tadā 
tridinamākarttuṃ samartho muṇḍikāsanam | tadā hi sarvanāḍyaśca 
vaśībhūtā na saṃśayaḥ | nāḍīkṣālanayogena siddhimāpnoti 
sādhakaḥ neḍaṇīṃ yo na jānāti sa kathaṃ karttumudyataḥ | sa dhīro 
mānasacaro matimān sa jitendriyaḥ | yo neḍaṇīyogasāraṃ 
karttumudyamapāragaḥ | sa cāvaśyaṃ kṣālanañca 
kuryānnāḍyādisādhanam | neḍaṇīyogamārgeṇa 
nāḍīkṣālanatatparaḥ | bhavatyeva mahākāla ! rājarājeśvaro yathā | 
kevalaṃ prāṇavāyośca dhāraṇāt kṣālanaṃ bhavet | vinā 
kṣālanayogena dehaśuddhirna jāyate | kṣālanaṃ nāḍikādīnāṃ 
kaphapittādināśanam | karoti yatnato yogī muṇḍāsanānilāśanāt | 
vāyugrahaṇamevaṃ hi neḍaṇīvaśakālake | na kuryāt kevalaṃ nātha 
! anyakāle sadācaret | yāvanneḍaṇīṃ na jānāti tavadvāyuṃ na 
sampibet | bahutaraṃ na saṃgrāhyaṃ vāyorogamalādikṛt | kevalaṃ 
śvāsagaṇanaṃ yāvanneḍaṇī na siddhyati | pañcāmarāsādhanācca 
pañcavāyuvaśo bhavet || 

grahajāmale || medaḥśleṣmādhikaḥ pūrvaṃ ṣaṭkarmāṇi 
samācaret | anyathā nācarettāni doṣāṇāmapyabhāvataḥ | dhautī ca 
gajakariṇī vastī laulī natī tahā | kapālabhātī caitāni ṣaṭ karmāṇi 
maheśvari! | karmaṣaṭkamidaṃ gopyaṃ ghaṭaśodhanakāraṇam | 
vicitraguṇamādhāya pūjyate yogipuṅgavaḥ || tatra dhautī || 
caturaṅgulavistāraṃ hastapañcadaśena tu | gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa 
siktaṃ vastraṃ śanairgraset | tataḥ pratyāhareccaitat kṣālanaṃ 
dhautakarma tat | kāsaḥ śvāsaḥ plīhā kuṣṭhaṃ kapharogāśca 
viṃśatiḥ | dhautīkarmaprasādena śudhyante ca na saṃśayaḥ || 1 || 


p. 796) atha gajakariṇī || udaragatapadārthamudvamantī 
pavanamapānamudīrya kaṇṭhakāle | kramaparicayasya 
pāyumārgādgajakariṇīti nigadyate sā || 2 || 

atha vastī || nābhinimnajale pāyuṃ nyasya calotkaṭāsanam | 
ādhārānañjanaṃ kuryāt kṣālanaṃ vastikarma tat | gulmaplīhodarā 
rogā vātapittakaphodbhavāḥ | vastikarmaprabhāveṇa sarvarogakṣayo 
bhavet | dhātvindriyāntaḥkaraṇaprasādaṃ dadyācca kāntiṃ 
dahanapradīptim | aśeṣadoṣopacayaṃ nihanyādabhyasyamānaṃ 
janavastikarma || 3 || 

atha laulī || bhūmyādāvativegena tundaṃ savyāpasavyataḥ | natāṃso 
bhrāmayedeṣā laulī syāt parameśvari ! || phalam || 
mandāgnisandīpanapācakādisandīpakānandakarī sadaiva | 
aśeṣadoṣāmayaśoṣaṇī ca haṭhakriyā mauliriyañca laulī || 4 || 

atha natī || sūtraṃ vitastimātrantu nāsānāle praveśayet | mukhena 
gaṇayeccaiṣā natī syāt parameśvari ! || phalam || kapālaśodhanī 
kaṇṭhyā divyadṛṣṭipradāyinī | ya ūrddhvaṃ jāyate rogo 
namayatyāśu taṃ natī || evañca netī natīti nāmamātrabhedaḥ |  netī 
tu pūrvamuktā || 5 ||

atha kapālabhātī || bhastreva lauhakāre ca karṇapūrau sasambhramau | 
kapālabhātī vikhyātā kaphadoṣavnināśinī | iti kapālabhātī || 6 || 

dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || tato'dhikatarābhyāsādbalamutpadyate 
bhṛśam | yena bhūcarasiddhiḥ syād bhūcarāṇāṃ jaye kṣamaḥ | 
vyāghro lulāpo vanyo vā ṛkṣo vā gaja eva vā | siṃho vā mriyate tena 
yogino hastatāḍanāt | kandarpasya yathā rūpaṃ tathā tasyāpi yoginaḥ 
| tasmin kāle mahāvigho yoginaḥ syāt pramādataḥ | tadrūpavaśagā 
nāryaḥ kāṅkṣante tasya saṅgamam | yadi saṅgaṃ karotyeva 
vindustasya vinaśyati | ātmakṣayo vinduhīnādasāmarthyañca jāyate | 

p. 797) tasmāt strīṇāṃ saṅgavarjaṃ kuryādatyantamādarāt | 
yoginastasya siddhiḥ syāt satataṃ vindudhāraṇāt | tasmāt 
sarvaprayatnena rakṣyo vindurhiyoginā | tato rahasyamāviṣṭaḥ 
praṇavaṃ plutamātrayā | japet pūrvārjitānāṃ hi pāpānāṃ 
nāśahetave | sarvavighnaharaścāyaṃ praṇavaḥ sarvadoṣahā | 
evamabhyāsayogena siddhirārambhasambhavā | tato bhaved 
ghaṭāvasthā pavanābhyāsinaḥ sadā | prāṇāpānau mano vāyu 
rjīvātmaparamātmakau | anyonyasyāvirodhena evaṃ saṅghaṭate yathā 
| tadvad ghaṭadvayāvasthā prasiddhā yogināṃ smṛtā | tatra cihnāni 
yāni syustāni vakṣyāmi kānicit | pūrvaṃ yaḥ 
kathito'bhyāsaścaturddhā taṃ parityajet | divā vā yadi vā rātrau 
māsamātraṃ samabhyaset | ekavāraṃ pratidinaṃ kuryāt 
kevalakumbhakam | pratyāhāro hi evaṃ syādevaṃ kuryurhi yoginaḥ | 
indriyāṇīndriyārthebhyo yat pratyāharate sphuṭam | yogī 
kumbhakamāsthāya pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate | yadyat paśyati 
cakṣurbhyāṃ tattadātmani bhāvayet | yadyat śṛṇoti karṇābhyāṃ 
tattadātmani bhāvayet | jihvayā yadrasayati tattadātmani bhāvayet | 
tvacā yadaṅgaṃ spṛśati tattadātmani bhāvayet | evaṃ 
jñānendriyāṇāṃ hi tatsaṃkhyābhistu saṅkṣayet | yāmaṃ yāmaṃ 
pratidinaṃ yogī yatnādatandritaḥ | tadā vicitrasāmarthyaṃ jāyate 
yogino dhruvam | dūraśrutirdūradṛṣṭiḥ kṣaṇāddūragatistathā | 
vāksiddhiḥ kāmacāratvaṃ tathā'dṛśyapadaṃ bhavet | 
malamūtropalepena lauhānāṃ svarṇatā tathā | khecaratvaṃ tathā 
syācca satatābhyāsayogataḥ | tadā buddhimatā bhāvyaṃ yoginā 
yogasiddhaye | ete vighnā mahāsiddhyai na rametteṣu buddhimān | na 
śīlayettu kasmaicit svasāmarthyaṃ hi sarvadā | 
kadāciddarśayenmarttyo bhaktiyuktāya vā punaḥ | yathā mūrkho 
yathā mūḍho yathā vadhira eva vā | tathā vartteta lokeṣu

p. 798) svasāmarthasya guptaye | nocet śiṣyā hi bahavo bhavantyeva na 
saṃśayaḥ | svasvakāryeṣu yogīndraṃ prārthayante janāḥ sadā | 
tatkarmakaraṇavyagraḥ svābhyāse śithilo bhavet | abhyāsena vihīnastu 
tato laukikatāṃ vrajet | avismṛtya gurorvākyamabhyāsāttadaharniśam | 
evaṃ bhavedghaṭāvasthā sahajābhyāsayogataḥ | anabhyāsena 
yogo'yaṃ vṛthā goṣṭhyā na sidhyati | tasmāt sarvaprayatnena 
yogametaṃ samabhyaset | tataḥ paricayāvasthā jāyate'bhyāsayogataḥ | 
vāyuḥ paricito yatnādātmanā saha kuṇḍalīn | bodhayitvā 
suṣumnāyāṃ praviśedavirodhataḥ | vāyunā saha cittantu praviśettu 
mahāpatham | mahāpathaṃ śmaśānañca suṣumnāpyekameva hi | 
nānā matāntare bhedāḥ phalabhedo na vidyate | varttamānaṃ 
bhaviṣyañca bhūtārthañcāpi vetyasau | yasya cittaṃ sapavanaṃ 
suṣumnāṃ praviśeddivā | bhavyābhavye ca vijñāya yogī rahasi 
yatnataḥ | pañcadhā dhāraṇaṃ kuryāt tattadbhūtabhayāpaham || 

grahajāmale caturdaśapaṭale || saśailavanadhātrīṇāṃ 
yathādhāro'hināyakaḥ | sarveṣāṃ haṭhatantrāṇāṃ tathādhārā hi 
kuṇḍalī || ahināyako'nantaḥ || suptā guruprasādena yadā jāgartti 
kuṇḍalī | tadā padmāni sarvāṇi bhidyante granthayo'pi ca | prāṇasya 
śūnyapadavī tadā rājapathāyate | tadā cittaṃ nirālambaṃ tadā 
kālasya vañcanam || śūnyapadavī suṣumnā tatraivoktā yathā || 
suṣumnā śūnyapadavī brahmarandhraṃ mahāpathaḥ | śmaśānaṃ 
śaṅkhinī madhyamārgaścetyekavācakam || rājapathāyata ityanena 
sukhagamanaṃ vyajyate || tasmātsarvaprayatnena prabodhayitumīśvarīm 
| brahmarandhramukhe suptāṃ mudrābhyāsaṃ samācaret | 
mahāmudrā mahābandho mahāvedaśca khecarī | uḍḍīyānaṃ 
mūlabandho bandho jālandharābhidhaḥ | karaṇaṃ viparītākhyaṃ

p. 799) vijrinīśakticālanam | idantu mudrādaśakaṃ 
jarāmaraṇanāśanam | deveśi ! kathitaṃ 
divyamaṣṭaiśvaryapradāyakam | vallabhaṃ sarvasiddhānāṃ 
durlabhaṃ marutāmapi | gopanīyaṃ prayatnena yathā ratnakaraṇḍakam 
| kasya cinnaiva vaktavyaṃ kulastrīsurataṃ yathā || 

atha mahāmudrā || dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || mahāmudrāṃ 
pravakṣyāmi bhairavoktāṃ samādarāt | pārṣṇiṃ vāmasya pādasya 
yonisthāne niyojayet | prasādya dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ hastābhyāṃ 
dhārayeddṛḍhaṃ | civukaṃ hṛdaye nyasya pūrayedvāyunā punaḥ | 
kumbhakena yathāśakti dhārayitvā ca recayet | vāmāṅgena 
samabhyasya dakṣiṇāṅgena cābhyaset | prasāritastu yaḥ 
pādastamūrūpari vinyaset || grahajāmale || pādamūlena vāmena 
yoniṃ sampīḍya dakṣiṇam | pādaṃ prasāritaṃ kṛtvā karābhyāṃ 
dhārayed dṛḍham | kaṇṭhe vaktraṃ samāropya 
dhārayedvāyumūrddhataḥ | yathā daṇḍāhataḥ sarpo daṇḍākāraḥ 
prajāyate | ṛjvībhūtā tathā śaktiḥ kuṇḍalī sahasā bhavet | tadāsau 
maraṇāvasthā jāyate dvipuṭīśritā | ataḥ śanaiḥ śanaireva 
recayettaṃ na vegataḥ | iyaṃ khalu mahāmudrā tava snehāt 
prakāśyate || phalam || mahākleśādayo doṣāḥ kṣīyante 
maraṇādayaḥ | mahāmudrā tu tenaiva samākhyātā maheśvari ! | 
candrāṅgena samabhyasya sūryāṅgena samabhyaset | yāvatsaṃkhyā 
bhavettulyā tato mudrāṃ visarjayet | na hi pathyamapathyaṃ vā rasāḥ 
sarve'pi nīrasāḥ | api bhuktaṃ viṣaṃ ghoraṃ pīyūṣamiva jīryati | 
kṣayakuṣṭhagudāvarttā gudaplīhapurogamāḥ | tasya doṣāḥ kṣayaṃ 
yānti mahāmudrāñca yo'bhyaset | kathiteyaṃ mahāmudrā 
jarāmaraṇanāśinī | gopanīyā prayatnena na deyā yasya kasyacit || 

atha mahābandhaḥ || ayameva mahābandho mudrāṃ baddhvā 
yamabhyaset | mahābandhasthito bhūmau sphicau santāḍayecchanaiḥ |

p. 800) ayameva mahābandhaḥ siddhairabhyasyate naraiḥ | pārṣṇiṃ 
vāmasya pādasya yonisthāne niyojayet | vāmorūpari saṃsthāpya 
dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ tathā | pūrayitvā mukhe pāpa hṛdaye cibukaṃ 
dṛḍham | niṣpīḍya yonimākuñcya manomadhye niyojayet | recayecca 
śanairevaṃ mahābandho'yamucyate | amuṃ yogī mahābandhaṃ 
mahāsiddhipradāyakam | vāmāṅgañca samabhyasya dakṣāṅge 
punarabhyaset | ayañca sarvanāḍīnāmūrddhaṃ gatinirodhakaḥ | 
triveṇīsaṅgamaṃ dhatte kedāraṃ prāpayenmanaḥ | 
rūpalāvaṇyasampannā yathā strī puruṣaṃ vinā | 
mahāmudrāmahābandhau niṣphalau vedhavarjitau || 2 || 

atha mahāvedhaḥ || mahābandhasthito yogī kṛtvā 
prakaramekadhā | vāyunā gatimāvṛtya vidhṛtaṃ kaṇṭhamudrayā | 
samastayukto yo bhūmau sphicau santāḍayecchanaiḥ | puṭadvayaṃ 
samākṛṣya vāyuścarati satvaraḥ | somasūryāgnisandagdho jāyate 
cāmṛtāya vai | mṛtāvasthā samutpannā tato mṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ | 
rājadaṇḍaśca jihvāyāṃ bandhaḥ śasto bhaveddhitaḥ | 
mahāvedhasamābhyāso mahāsiddhipradāyakaḥ | bandhatrayaṃ 
mahāguhyaṃ jarāmaraṇanāśanam | vahnivṛddhikaraṃ 
caivāṇimādyaṣṭaguṇapradam | abādhaṃ harate pāpaṃ yāmaṃ 
yāmaṃ dine dine | puṇyasadbhārasabhāvaṃ pāpaughātiharaṃ sadā | 
samyak śikṣāvatāmeva svapnaṃ prathamasādhane | vahniṃ 
strīpathasevanamādau majjanamācaret || 3 || 

atha khecarī || nā'śanaṃ siddhasadṛśaṃ na kumbhaḥ 
kevalopamaḥ | na khecarīsamā mudrā na nādasadṛśo layaḥ | chedanaṃ 
cālanaṃ dehe kalākrameṇa barddhayettāvat | sā yāvad bhrūmadhyaṃ 
spṛśati sākṣāt khecarīsiddhiḥ || dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || antaḥ 
kapālavivare jihvāṃ vyāvṛtya bandhayet | bhrūmadhyadṛṣṭirapyeṣā 
mudrā bhavati khecarī || grahajāmale || snuhīpatranibhaṃ śastraṃ 
sutīkṣṇaṃ sigdhanirmalam | maryādāyai tatastena romamātraṃ 
samucchinet | kṛtvā saindhavapathyābhyāṃ cūrṇitābhyāṃ 
pravarttate || pathyā harītakī || punaḥ saptadine parāpte romamātraṃ 
samucchinet | evaṃ krameṇa ṣaṇmāsaṃ nityayuktaḥ samācaret | 
ṣaṇmāsādrasanāmūlaśirābandho vinaśyati || yoginī 
veṇuvalkaladhāreṇedānīṃ rasanāmūlamevaṃ krameṇa chindanti || 
atha vāgīśvarīdhāma śiro vastreṇa veṣṭayet | śanairutkarṣayed yogī 
kālavelāvidhānavit | vāgīśvarīdhāma jihvā tasyāḥ 
śiro'grabhāgaḥ | vastrapramāṇamapi tatraiva || vitastipramitaṃ 
dairghyaṃ vistāraṃ caturaṅgulam | mṛdulaṃ dhavalaṃ proktaṃ 
veṣṭanālambanakṣaṇam punaḥ ṣaṇmāsamātreṇa punaḥ 
saṅkarṣaṇāt priye ! | bhrūmadhyāvadhi varddheta 
tiryakkarṇavilāvadhi | adhastaccivukaṃ mūlaṃ prayāti kramakāritā | 
krośādūrddhaṃ caṅkramati tiryak saṅkhyāvadhi priye ! | punaḥ 
saṃvatsare devi ! dvitīye caiva līlayā | brahmarandhrata āvṛtya tiṣṭhet 
pavanavandite | satālumūlaṃ saṅghṛṣya saptavāsaramātmani | 
pūrvoktena prakāreṇa malaṃ sarvaṃ viśodhayet | aṅgulyagreṇa 
saṅghṛṣya jihvāṃ tatra niveśayet | śanaiḥ śanairmastakāntaṃ 
mahāvajrakavāṭabhit | pūrvaṃ vijayatāṃ vidyā vikhyātā 
bhuvanatraye | asyāṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ kurvīta tathā ṣaṭcakrabhinnayā | khe 
nirastasakalakriyākramayācitā carati | śāśvatodaye 
saśirobhūsamavāyakāriṇī || krameṇaiva prakarttavyābhyāsena 
varavarṇini ! | yugapadvilaye tasya śarīraṃ vilayaṃ vrajet | 
tasmācchanaiḥ śanaiḥ kāryo'bhyāsinā yogakaḥ priye ! | evaṃ
