varṣatrayaṃ kṛtvā brahmadvāraṃ viśeddhruvam | ṣaṭcakrāṇi 
vibhedya śaktibhujagīṃ protthāpya mūlasthitāṃ bhittvā 
granthitamañca paścimaśirāḥ prākārarūpaṃ mahat | nītvā 
prāṇamahaḥ śiro'pi vimalaṃ nirmathya cittena tat talliṅgaṃ

p. 802) pibatendumaṇḍalagalanmuktaḥ sa sākṣācchivaḥ || 
nityaṃ yastūrddhajihvo yadi pibati pumān saptadhārāmṛtaughaṃ 
susvādaṃ śītalāṅgaṃ duritabhayaharaṃ kṣutpipāśānivāram | 
piṇḍasthairyaṃ hi tasmānmṛḍa iva nivasanmṛtyurogāttarettu 
daurbhāgyaṃ yāti nāśaṃ prasarati sakalaṃ tritrikālo bhramitvā || 
kapālakuhare jihvā praviṣṭā viparītagā | bhruvorantargatā 
dṛṣṭirmudrā bhavati khecarī | kalāṃ parāṅmukhīṃ kṛtvā tripathe 
parivarttayet | sā bhavet khecarīmudrā vyomacakraṃ taducyate | 
rasanāmūrddhagāṃ kṛtvā kṣaṇārddhaṃ yastu tiṣṭhati | 
viṣayānmucyate yogī vyādhimṛtyujarādibhiḥ | na rogamaraṇaṃ tasya, 
na nidrā na kṣudhā tṛṣā | na ca mūrcchā bhavettasya yo mudrāṃ 
vetti khecarīm | piḍyate  na sa rogeṇa lipyate na sa karmaṇā | bādhyate 
na sa kālena yasya mudrāsti khecarī | cittaṃ carati khe yasmāt jihvā 
carati khe gatā | tenaiṣā khecarī nāma mudrāsananamaskṛtā | 
khecaryāmuditaṃ yena vivaraṃ lambikorddhakam | tasya na kṣarate 
vinduḥ kāminyāśleṣitasya ca | calito'pi yadā vinduḥ samprāpto 
yonimaṇḍale | vrajettūrddhaṃ haṭhācchaktyā nibaddho yonimudrayā | 
ūrddhvajihvaḥ sthiro bhūtvā somapānaṃ karoti yaḥ | māsārddhena 
na sandeho mṛtyuṃ jayati yogavān | indhanāni yathā vahnistena 
varttiñca dīpakaḥ | tathā somalatāpūrṇaṃ dehaṃ dehī na muñcati | 
nityaṃ somakalāpūrṇaṃ dehaṃ dehī na muñcati | nityaṃ 
somakalāpūrṇaṃ śarīraṃ yasya yoginaḥ | takṣakeṇāpi daṣṭasya 
viṣaṃ tasya na sarpati | tasmādidaṃ prakurvīta nityayuktaḥ samāhitaḥ | 
rasanāṃ śeṣayedūrddhvaṃ pibedamaravāruṇīm | kulīnaṃ tamahaṃ 
vande itare kulaghātakāḥ | gośabdenoditā jihvā tatpraveśo hi tāluni | 
gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ tattu mahāpātakanāśanam | jihvāpraveśadaṃ 
bhūtavāyunā sthāpitaṃ khalu | candrād galati saṃsāraṃ

p. 803) tatsyādamaravāruṇī | mūrddhnaḥ ṣoḍaśapatrapadmagalitaṃ 
prāṇādavāptaṃ haṭhādūrddhvāsyo rasanāṃ niyamya vivare 
śaktiṃ parāṃ cintayet | tatkallolakalājalañca vimalaṃ dhārāmṛtaṃ 
yaḥ pibet nirdoṣaḥ sa mṛṇālakomalavapuryogī ciraṃ jīvati || 
cumbantī yadi lambikāgramaniśaṃ jihvā rasasyandinī sakṣārā 
kaṭukātha dugdhasadṛśī kṣīrājyatulyāthavā | vyādhīnāṃ 
haraṇaṃ jarāntakaraṇaṃ śāstrāṅgamodgīraṇaṃ tasya 
syādamaratvamaṣṭaguṇavat siddhāṅganākarṣaṇam || ekaṃ 
sṛṣṭimayaṃ vījaṃ ekā mudrā ca khecarī | eko devo nirālambo 
hyekāvasthā manonmanī | suciraṃ jñānajanakaṃ 
pañcatattvasamanvitam | tiṣṭhantī khecarīmudrā tasmin śūnye 
nirañjane || 4 ||
 
atha mūlabandhaḥ || pārṣṇibhāgena sampīḍya 
yonimākuñcayedgudam | apānamūrddhvamutthāpya 
mūlabandho'yamucyate | adhogatamapānañca ūrddhaṅge kurute 
haṭhāt | ākuñcanena taṃ vacmi mūlabandhaṃ maheśvari ! | 
yonipārṣṇī tu sampīḍya vāyumākuñcayedbalāt | vāraṃ vāraṃ 
tathā corddhaṃ samāyāti samīraṇaḥ | prāṇādinodito 
vindurmūlaṃbandhena caikataḥ | tato yogasya saṃsiddhiḥ pramā tatra 
na saṃśayaḥ | prāṇāpānau ca pavanau  mūlabandhena caikatām | 
gatvā yogasya saṃsiddhiṃ gacchato nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
apānaprāṇayoraikyāt kṣayānmūtrapurīṣayoḥ | yuvā bhavati 
vṛddho'pi satataṃ mūlabandhanāt | apāne corddhvage yāte samprāpte 
yonimaṇḍale | tadānanā śikhā dīrghā varddhate vāyunā hatā | tato 
yātaṃ baddhayonau prāṇeṣu ca svarūpakam | tailābhyaktaṃ 
pradīpantu jvalano dehagastathā | tena kuṇḍalinī suptā santaptā 
saṃvivarddhate | daṇḍāhatā bhujaṅgīva niśvasya ṛjutāṃ vrajet | 
vilaṃ praviṣṭaiva tato brahmanāḍyantaraṃ vrajet | tasmānnityam 
mūlabandhaḥ karttavyaḥ parameśvari ! || 5 || 

p. 804) atha uḍḍīyānam || baddho yena suṣumnāyāṃ prāṇa uḍḍīyate 
tataḥ | tasmāduḍḍīyanākhyo'yaṃ jñātavyaḥ parameśvari ! | 
uḍḍīnaṃ kurute yasmādadhiśrānto mahākhagaḥ | uḍḍīyānaṃ 
tadeva syāt tatra bandho nigadyate | udare paścime bhālaṃ 
nābherūrddhvañca kārayet | uḍḍīyāno hyasau bandho 
mṛtyumātaṅgakeśarī | uḍḍīyānantu sahajaṃ kathyate parameśvari ! | 
abhyāsena vitandrastu vṛddho'pi taruṇo bhavet | 
nābherūrddhvamadhaścāpi tāluṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ | 
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsato mṛtyuṃ jayatyeva na saṃśayaḥ | sati vajrāsane 
pādau karābhyāṃ dhārayed dṛḍham | phalgudeśasamīpe ca bandhaṃ 
tatra prapīḍayet | paścime tālumudare kārayeddhṛdadaye gale | śanaiḥ 
śanairyathā prāṇastundasiddhiṃ sa gacchati | sarveṣāmeva 
bandhānāmuttamo'pyuḍḍiyānakaḥ | udḍīyāne maheśāni ! muktiḥ 
svābhāvikī bhavet || dattātreyasaṃhitāyāñca || uḍḍīyānañca 
sahajaṃ munibhiḥ kathitaṃ sadā | abhyased yastu sattvastho vṛddho'pi 
taruṇāyate | nābherūrddhvamadhaścāpi prāṇaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ | 
ṣaṇmāsamabhyasenmṛtyuṃ jayatyeva na saṃśayaḥ | mūlabandhastu 
yo nityamabhyaset sa tu yogavit || 6 || 

atha jālandharabandhaḥ || grahajāmale || kaṇṭhamākuñcya 
hṛdaye sthāpayeccivukaṃ dṛḍham | bandho 
jālandharākhyo'yamamṛtāvyayakārakaḥ || dattātreyasaṃhitāyāñca 
| kaṇṭhamākuñcya hṛdaye mārutaṃ dhārayed dṛḍham | jālandhare 
bandha eṣa hyamṛtadvayapāragaḥ | nābhisthāgnikapālasthaṃ 
sahasrakamalacyutam | amṛtaṃ sarvadāsrāvaṃ vindutvaṃ yāti 
dehinām | yathāgniśca tadamṛtaṃ na pibecca  pibet svayam | yāti 
paścimamārgeṇa evamabhyāsataḥ sadā | amṛtaṃ kurute dehaṃ 
jālandharamato'bhyaset || grahajāmale || badhnāti hi śirājālamadhye 
gāmi na bhojanam | ato jālandharo bandhaḥ kaṇṭhe 
duḥkhaughanāśanaḥ | jālandhare kṛte bandhe kaṇṭhe saṅkocane kṛte | 

p. 805) na pīyūṣaṃ pacatyagnau na ca vāyuḥ pradhāvati | 
kaṇṭhasaṅkocanenaiva dve nāḍyau kumbhayed dṛḍham | 
madhyacakramidaṃ jñeyaṃ ṣoḍaśādhārabandhanam | 
bandhatrayamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mahāsiddhaniṣevitam | sarveṣāṃ 
haṭhatantrāṇāṃ kuryāt sādhanamīśvari ! | atha ākuñcanenāśu 
kaṇṭhasaṅkocane kṛte | madhye paścimatāluḥ syāt prāṇastu 
madhyanāḍigaḥ | brahmasthānasthito bodhaḥ prayāti pavanālayam | 
tato na jāyate mṛtyurnāsya jarādikaṃ tathā | mūlasthānaṃ 
samākuñcya uḍḍānākhyantu kārayet | iḍāñca piṅgalāṃ baddhvā 
vāhayet  paścimāṃ diśam | anenaiva vidhānena prāpnoti pavano 
layam | tato na jāyate mṛtyurjarārogādikaṃ tathā || 

atha viparītakaraṇam | yatkiñcit sravate candrādamṛtaṃ 
divyarūpiṇam | tatsarvaṃ grasate sūryastena piṇḍo jarāyutaḥ | tatrāsti 
karaṇaṃ divyaṃ sūryasya mukhabandhanam | gurupadeśato jñeyaṃ na 
ca śāstrārthakoṭibhiḥ | ūrddhvaṃ nābhiradhastālurūrddhvaṃ 
bhānuradhaḥ śaśī | karaṇaṃ viparītākhyaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam 
| nityamabhyāsasaṃyuktaṃ jaṭharāgnivivarddhanam | svalpāhāro 
nirāhāraḥ kṣudhārtto balahā bhavet | āhāraṃ bahulaṃ tasya 
sampādya sādhakasya tu | aplāhāro yadi bhavedagnirdehaṃ dahet 
kṣaṇāt | adhaḥ śirāścorddhapādaḥ kṣaṇaṃ syāt prathame dine | 
kṣaṇācca kiñcidadhikamabhyaseddhi dine dine | balitaṃ palitañcaiva 
ṣaṇmāsāddhi vināśayet | māsamātrantu yo nityamabhyaset sa tu 
karṇajit || dattātreyasaṃhitāyāmapi || pakṣiṇā gudamāyujya 
vāmamārgaṃ caredbalāt | vāraṃ vāraṃ yathā corddhvaṃ 
samāyāti samīraṇaḥ | prāṇāpānau nādavindū mūlabandhena 
caikatām | mahāyogasya saṃsiddhiṃ yāti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ | 
karaṇaṃ viparītākhyaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam | 
nityamabhyāsayuktasya jaṭharāgnivivarddhanam | āhārā bahavastasya 
svalpādyāḥ sāaṅkṛte ! dhruvam | alpāhāro yadi bhavedagnirdāhaṃ 

p. 806) karoti vai | ūrddhvaṃ rāhuradhaścandrastathā dṛṣṭiśca 
sāṅkṛte ! | adhaḥśirāścorddhvapādaḥ kṣaṇaṃ syāt prathame dine | 
kṣaṇāttu kiñcidadhikamabhyasettu dine dine | baliśca palitañcaiva 
ṣaṇmāsorddhvaṃ na dṛśyate | yāmamātrantu yo nityamabhyaset sa tu 
yogavit || 

atha vajroṇī || vajroṇī cāmaroṇī ca sayonī ca tridhā mataḥ | 
tattāsāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye karttavyañca viśeṣataḥ | svecchayā 
varttamāno'pi yogoktaniyamaṃ vinā | vajroṇīṃ yo vijānāti sa yogī 
siddhibhājanaḥ | adhobhāge gataṃ vindumabhyāsenorddhvamāharet | 
calitastu svakaṃ vindumūrddhvamākṛṣya rakṣayet | tatra vastudvayaṃ 
vakṣye durlabhaṃ yasya kasyacit | kṣīrañcaiva dvitīyañca nārī ca 
vaśavarttinī | meḍhreṇa ca śanaiḥ samyak kṛtvākuñcanamabhyaset | 
puruṣo vāpi nārī vā vajroṇī siddhimāpnuyāt | prayatnataḥ 
śarājālaṃ phutkāraiḥ kambu varddhayet | śanaiḥ śanaiḥ 
prakuryācca vāyusañcārakāraṇāt | bhāryābhagagataṃ 
vindumabhyāsenorddhvamāharet | balitaṃ palitaṃ vinduṃ 
vindumākṛṣya rakṣayet | evanatu rakṣayan vinduṃ mṛtyu jayati 
yogavān | maraṇaṃ vindupātena jīvitaṃ vindudhāraṇāt | 
sugandhiryogino deho jāyate vindudhāraṇāt | yāvadvinduḥ sthito dehe 
tāvanmṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ | malāyattaṃ balaṃ puṃsāṃ 
śukrāyattañca jīvitam | tasmācchukraṃ malañcaiva rakṣaṇīyaṃ 
prayatnataḥ | ṛtumatyā rajo'pyevaṃ svayaṃ vinduñca rakṣayet | 
meḍhrādākarṣayedūrddhvaṃ samyagabhyāsayogataḥ 
sahayonyamaroṇī ca vajroṇyā eva bhedataḥ | vajroṇī 
maithunādūrddhvaṃ strīpuṃsoḥ sāṅgalepanam | āsīnayoḥ 
sukhenaiva sūkṣmavyāpārayoḥ kṣaṇāt | sahayoniriyaṃ proktā 
śraddhayā parameśvari ! | pittolvaṇatvāt prathamāñca dhārāṃ 
vihāya niḥsāratayāntyadhārām | niṣevate śitalamadhyadhārāṃ

p. 807) kaphoṇikaiḥ khaṇḍamateramaryaḥ | amarīṃ yaḥ pibennityaṃ 
nyāsaṃ kurvan dine dine | vajroṇīmabhyasedevamamaroṇīti kathyate | 
yakṣaṇākuñcanenorddhvaṃ yā rajaḥ sā hi yoginī | atītānāgataṃ 
jñātaṃ khecarī ca prajāyate | dehasiddhiñca labhate 
vajroṇyabhyāsayogataḥ | ayaṃ śubhakaro yogo bhogān bhuṅkte 
vimuktidaḥ || 9 || 

atha śakticālanam || kuṇḍalāṅgī kuṇḍalinī bhujaṅgī 
śaktirīśvarī | kuṇḍalyarundhatī devī śabdāḥ paryāyavācakāḥ | 
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye bālā raṇḍā tapasvinī | balādākṛṣya 
gṛhnīyāttadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam | pucchaṃ pragṛhya bhujagīṃ 
suptāmudbodhayedapi | nidrāṃ vihāya sā ṛjvī ūrddhvamuttiṣṭhate 
haṭhāt | vajrāsanasthito yogī cālayitvā tu kuṇḍalīm | 
kuryādanantaraṃ bhastrāṃ kuṇḍalīmāśu bodhayet | 
mṛtyuvajragatasyāpi tato mṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ | 
muhūrttadvayaparyantaṃ nirbhayaṃ cālanādasau | ūrddhvamākarṣate 
kiñcit suṣumnāṃ kuṇḍalīgatām | tena kuṇḍalinī tasyāḥ 
suṣumnāyāḥ samudgatāḥ | jahāti nityaṃ prāṇo'yaṃ suṣumnāṃ 
brajati svanaḥ | tasmāt sañcālayennityaṃ śabdagarbhāmarundhatīm | 
yasyāḥ sañcālanenaiva rogī rogairvimucyate | yena sañcālitā śaktiḥ 
sa yogī siddhibhājanaḥ | kimatra bahunoktena kālaṃ jayati līlayā | 
abhyāsānniḥsṛtāṃ caindrīṃ saha nityaṃ samāśrayet | 
dhārayettūttamāṅge ca divyadṛṣṭipradāyikām | iti mudrā daśa 
proktāstava snehānmaheśvari ! | ekaikā cāśu yamināṃ 
mahāsiddhipradāyinī | rājayogaṃ vinā pṛthvī rājayogaṃ vinā śilā 
| rājayogaṃ vinā nidrā vicitrāpi na rājate | mārutasya vidhiṃ sarvaṃ 
manoyuktaṃ manīṣiṇām | śivāpi madhyamā nāḍī dṛḍhābhyāse ca 
yoginām | āsane prāṇasaṃyoge mudrābhiḥ sakalā bhavet |

p. 808) upasanne'pi mudrāṇāṃ rājayogaḥ samudravat | 
rudrāṇāñcāparā mudrā tadā siddhiṃ prayacchati || 

iti prāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ ṣaṣṭhe jñānakāṇḍe 
pañcāmarādimudrādaśakarūpagranthikathanaṃ nāma prathamaḥ 
paricchedaḥ || 




dattātreyasaṃhitāyām || pañcadhāraṇānantaram || pṛthivīdhāraṃ 
vakṣye pārthivebhyo bhayāvaham | nābheradho gudasyorddhaṃ 
ghaṭikāḥ pañca dhārayet | vāyuṃ tato bhavet pṛthvīdhāraṇaṃ 
tadbhayāpaham | pṛthivīsambhavāttasya na mṛtyuryogino bhavet | 
nābhisthāne tato vāyuṃ dhārayet pañca nāḍikāḥ | tato jalabhayaṃ 
nāsti jalamṛtyurna yoginaḥ | nābhyūrddhamaṇḍale vāyuṃ dhārayet 
pañca nāḍikāḥ | āgneyī dhāraṇā seyaṃ na mṛtyustasya vahninā | 
na dahyate śarīraṃ hi prakṣipte vahnikuṇḍake | nābhibhruvorhi madhye 
tu prādeśadvayasammite | dhārayet pañca ghaṭikā vāyuṃ saiva hi 
vāyavavī | dhāraṇāttasya vāyostu yogino na bhayaṃ bhavet | 
bhrūmabhyādupariṣṭāttu dhārayet pañca nāḍikāḥ | vāyuṃ yogī 
prayatnena seyamākāśadhāraṇā | ākāśadhāraṇaṃ kurvanmṛtyuṃ 
jayati tattvataḥ | yatra tatra sthito vāpi sukhamatyantamaśnute | evañca 
dhāraṇāḥ pañca kuryādyogī vidhānataḥ | tato dṛḍhaśarīrasya 
mṛtyurasya na vidyate | ityevaṃ pañcabhūtānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ yaḥ 
samabhyaset | brahmaṇaḥ pralaye vāpi tasya mṛtyurna vidyate | 
samabhyasettadā dhyānaṃ ghaṭikāḥ ṣaṣṭimeva ca | vāyuṃ nirudhya 
tāṃ dhyāyeddevatāmiṣṭasādhinīm | saguṇadhyānametat 
syādaṇimādisukhapradam | nirguṇaṃ khamiva dhyāyenmokṣamārgaḥ 
pravarttate | nirguṇadhyānasampannaḥ samādhiñca samabhyaset | 
dinadvādaśakenaiva samādhiṃ samavāpnuyāt | vāyuṃ nirudhya 
medhāvī jīvanmukto bhaveddhruvam | samādhiḥ samatāvasthā

p. 809) jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ | yadi syāddehamutsraṣṭumicchā 
cedutsṛjet svayam | paraṃ brahmaṇi līyeta tyaktvā karma 
śubhāśubham | atha nocet samutsṛṣṭaṃ svaśarīraṃ yadi priyam | 
sarvalokeṣu vicaredaṇimādiguṇānvitaḥ | kadācit svecchayā devo 
bhūtvā svarge'pi sañcaret | manuṣyo vāpi yakṣo vā svecchayāpi 
kṣaṇādbhavet | siṃho vyāghro gajo vāpi syādicchāto'nyajanmatām | 
yatheṣṭameva varttetayogī vidvānmaheśvaraḥ | kavimārgopayuktaṃ te 
sāṅkṛte'ṣṭāṅgayogataḥ | siddhānāṃ kapilādīnāṃ mataṃ 
vakṣye tataḥ param | abhyāsabhedāttadbhedena phalaṃ tatsamaṃ tathā | 
ityanantaraṃ jalandharādiruktaḥ | tena dattātreyamate jalandharādiṃ 
vināpi yogī bhavatīti sudhībhirvibhāvyāvagantavyam | pakṣiṇā 
gudamāyujya vāmamārgaṃ caredbalāt | vāraṃ vāraṃ yathā 
corddhaṃ samāyāti samīraṇaḥ | prāṇāpānau nādavindū 
mūlabandhena caikatām | mahāyogasya saṃsiddhiṃ yāti nāstyatra 
saṃśayaḥ | karaṇaṃ viparītākhyaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam | 
nityamabhyāsayuktasya jaṭharāgnivivarddhanam | āhārā bahavastasya 
sampādyāḥ sāṅkṛte ! dhruvam | alpahāro yadi bhavedagnirdāhaṃ 
karoti vai | ūrddhvaṃ rāhuradhaścandra tathā dṛṣṭiśca sāṅkṛte ! | 
adhaḥśirāścorddhapādaḥ kṣaṇāt syāt prathame deine | kṣaṇāttu 
kiñcidadhikamabhyasettu dine dine | baliśca palitañcaiva 
ṣaṇmāsorddhvaṃ na dṛśyate | yāmamātrantu yo nityamabhyaset sa tu 
yogavit | vajroṇīṃ te pravakṣyāmi yogibhirgopitaṃ sadā | atīva 
tadrahasyaṃ hi na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit | labhyate yadi tasyaikaṃ 
yogasiddhikaraṃ param | kṣīramaṅgarasaṃ ceti dvayorādyañca 
labhyate | tṛtīyaṃ balināṃ puṃsāṃ strībhyaḥ sādhyamupāgataḥ | 
yogābhyāsaratā strī ca puṃsāṃ yatnena sādhayet | puṃsāṃ strī vā 
tadanyadvā strītvaṃ puṃstvamapekṣayā | sa

p. 810) prayojanamātraikasādhanāt siddhimāpnuyāt | galito yadi 
vindustu mūrddhni cākṛṣ.ya dhārayet | evaṃ hi rakṣite vindau mṛtyaṃ 
jayati tattvataḥ | maraṇaṃ vindupātena jīvanaṃ vindurakṣaṇāt | 
vindurakṣāprasādena sarve sidhyanti yoginaḥ | amaroṇistu yathā syāt 
sahayonistato yathā | tadabhyāsakramo jñeyaḥ siddhānāṃ 
sampradāyataḥ | etaiḥ sarvaistu kathitairabhyaset kālayogataḥ | tato 
bhavedrājayogo nāntarībhavati dhruvam | na diṅmātreṇa siddhiḥ 
syādabhyāso naiva jāyate | rājayogapadaṃ prāpya 
sarvasaṅkalpabhañjakam | sarvaṃ kuryānna vā kuryād yathāruci 
viceṣṭitam | tathāntarā ca yogena niṣpannā yoginaḥ kriyā | tadāvasthā 
hi naśyantī bhaktimuktipradā bhavet || 

prāṇāyāmasya phalāntarantu grahayāmale || ādau candraḥ site 
pakṣe bhāskarastu sitetare | pratipatkramato hānistrīṇi kṛtvodayatyayam 
|| tripurāsāramuccaye tu || amāvāsyāvadhyudayamāha yathā || 
ārabhya darśaṃ prathamāmudeti vāme puṭe trīṇi dināni devaḥ | 
vāmetare trīṇi tato dināni pūrṇāṃ tithiṃ yāvadathaivamevam || 
ekasya pakṣasya viparyayeṇa rogābhibhūtirbhavatīha puṃsām | 
pakṣadvaye bandusuhṛdvipattiḥ pakṣatraye vyatyayato mṛtiḥ syāt || 
brahmajāmale || candrodaye yadā sūryaścandraḥ sūryodaye yadā | 
aśubhaṃ hānirudvegaḥ śubhaṃ sarvaṃ niyojayet | candrodaye 
vāmanāḍyāṃ vāyugamananirūpitasamaye evaṃ sūryodaye 
dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ vāyugamananirūpitasamaye karmaviśeṣaḥ | 
nāsikayorvāyoḥ praveśanirgamābhyāñca śubhatvamapi tatraiva || 
yātrādāne vivāhe ca vastrālaṅkāradhāraṇe | śubhakarmaṣu sarveṣu 
praveśe ca śaśī śubhaḥ | vigrahadyūtayuddheṣu 
snānabhojanamaithune | vyavahāre bhaye bhaṅge bhānunāḍī 
praśasyate | mohanaṃ śāntikañcaiva divyauṣadhirasāyanam | 

p. 811) vidyārambhaḥ sthiraṃ kāryaṃ karttavyañca niśākare | 
dūrayuddhe jayī candraḥ samāsanne divākaraḥ | vahannāḍīpade caiva 
yātrā bhavati siddhidā || yasyāṃ nāsikāyāṃ 
prāṇavāyorgatirvakṣyate tadaṃśīyapādaprasāraṇapūrvikā yātrā 
siddhidā bhavatītyarthaḥ | śayane ca prasaṅge vā yuvatyāliṅgane'pi 
vā | yaḥ sūryeṇa pibeccandraṃ sa bhavenmakaradhvajaḥ || sūryeṇa 
dakṣiṇanāsikāsthaprāṇavāyunā candraṃ nāryā 
vāmanāsikāgataprāṇavāyuṃ pibati tatsaṅgamakāle 
svīyaniśvāsamatiniviṣṭaḥ sannākarṣayati | tatphalaṃ tatraiva | 
candracārī viṣaṃ hanti sūryo bālāṃ vaśaṃ nayet | suṣumnāyāṃ 
bhavenmokṣa eko devastridhā mataḥ | bhuktamātreṇa mandāgnī 
strīṇāṃ vaśye'tha karmaṇi | śayanaṃ sūryavāhena karttavyaṃ 
sarvadā budhaiḥ | śānte śoke vivāde ca jvarite mūrcchite tathā | 
sajjanasyāpi bodhārthe candracāraṃ pravāhayet | bhuktamātrādau 
vāmapārśvena śrāntādiṣu dakṣiṇapārśvena śayanaṃ kāryamiti 
tātparyam | caturdikṣūrddhamadhastāccānyatarasthānamāśritya yadi 
dūtaḥ pṛcchati tadā kasyāṃ nāḍyāṃ vāyugatiḥ śubhetyāha || 
ūrddhvavāmāgrato dūto jñeyo vāmapathasthitaḥ | pṛṣṭhe dakṣe 
tathādhastāt sūryavāhagato mataḥ | pūrṇanāḍīgato dūto yat 
pṛcchati śubhāśubham | tatsarvaṃ siddhimāpnoti śūnye śūnyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | ādau śūnyagataḥ pṛcchet paścāt pūrṇo viśed yadi | 
tadā sarvārthasiddhiḥ syāditi jānīhi niścitam || idānīṃ 
prāṇavāyoḥ praveśanirgamābhyāṃ viśeṣamāha || praveśakāle 
yadbhūto vāñchati svaprayojanam | tatsarvaṃ siddhimāpnoti nirgame 
nāsti sundaram || karmaviśeṣe nāḍīviśeṣagatimāha || māraṇaṃ 
mohanaṃ stambhaṃ vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ tathā | 
preraṇākarṣaṇakṣobhaṃ bhānunāḍyudaye kuru | śāntikaṃ 
pauṣṭikaṃ kṣemaṃ divyauṣadhirasāyanam | yogābhyāsādikarmāṇi 
karttavyāni niśākare || tripurāsārasamuccaye || bhūtānāmudayaṃ

p. 812) dvayorapi budhaḥ saṃlakṣayet pakṣayostatrādau vasudhodayaṃ 
nihitadhīrghrāṇasya daṇḍaspṛśi | deve'dhaḥspṛśi vāriṇo 
hutavahasyorddhvā gatiścodayaṃ tiryaksaṃspṛśi mārutasya paritaḥ 
pṛṣṭhe marudvartmanaḥ || deve prāṇavāyau || phalaṃ jāmale || 
pṛthvījale śubhe tattve tejo miśraphalodayam | hānimṛtyukarau 
puṃsāmubhayau vyomamārutau | 

karmaviśeṣe bhūtodayaphalam tripurāsāre || 
vaśyastambhanayoḥ praśasta udayo bhūmerjalasyodayaḥ śastaḥ 
śāntikapauṣṭikādiṣu śubheṣvahnāya vahneḥ punaḥ | 
śatrorbhāraṇadāraṇādikaraṇe uccāṭanocchedayorvāyoḥ 
śāntikanirviṣīkaraṇayorvyomno hitaścodayaḥ || 
kiyatkālamekanāḍīgato vāyurbhavati tatrāpi pañcabhūtodayaḥ kena 
jñeya ityapekṣāyāṃ nāḍīgatikālaṃ sthānakrameṇa 
bhūtodayañcāha jāmale || ekaikasya kalāḥ pañca kremeṇaivodayanti 
tāḥ | pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca | madhye pṛthvī 
tvadhaścāpa ūrddhve vahati cānalaḥ | tiryagvāyupravāhaśca nabho 
vahati saṅkrame || prapañcasāre'pi || puṭayorubhayośca 
daṇḍasaṃsthā pṛthivī toyamadhaḥ kṛśānurūrddhvam | pavanastvatha 
pārśvago'pi madhye gaganaṃ bhūtagatistanūdbhaveyam || tatrāpi 
bālyādyavasthābhedamāha || rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe 
ekādaśapaṭale || svakīyanāsikāgrasthaṃ pañcamaṃ parikīrttitam | 
yannāsāpuṭamadhye tu vāyurbhramati bhairava ! | 
tannāsāpyūrddhamadhye tu bhāvābhāvaṃ vicārayet | ākāśaṃ 
vāyurūpaṃ hi taijasaṃ prabho ! | pārthivaṃ kramaśo jñeyaṃ 
bālyāstādikrameṇa tu | vāmodaye śubhā vāmā dakṣiṇe puruṣaḥ 
śubhaḥ | vāyūnāṃ gamanaṃ jñeyaṃ gaganāvadhireva ca | kevalaṃ 
madhyadeśe tu gamanaṃ pavanasya ca | tadākāśaṃ 
vijānīyādbālyabhāvaṃ prakīrttitam | tiryaggatistu nāsāgre 
vāyorudayameva ca | kevalaṃ bhramaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvamaṅgalameva 

p. 813) ca | kiśoraṃ tadvijānīyādvāyo tiryaggatau vibho ! | 
kevalārddhanāsikāgre vāyurgacchati daṇḍavat | tattaijasaṃ 
vijānīyāttejasā balavān bhavet | yauvanaṃ tadvijānīyāt 
karmasiddhirbhaveddhruvam | yadāvāpya gacchatīha 
nāsāpuṭamanākulam | jalodayaṃ vijānīyāt tadā vyāmohameva ca | 
tadvṛddhagatabhāvaśca vilambo'dhikaceṣṭayā | prāpnoti paramāṃ 
prītiṃ vāruṇārṇodaye'ruṇam | yadadho gacchati kṣipraṃ 
kiñcidūrddhvamagocaram | yadā karoti prāgbālaṃ tadā rogād 
balodayaḥ pṛthivyā unnataṃ bhāgyaṃ rogārttiparipīḍitam | astamitaṃ 
mahādeva ! anulomavilomataḥ | pavano gacchati kṣipraṃ 
vāmadakṣiṇabhedataḥ | vāmanāsāpuṭaṃ yāti pṛthivī jalameva ca | 
sadā phalāphalaṃ dātumuditā kulamaṇḍale | tayorvai vāyavī śaktiḥ 
phalabhāgaṃ tadā labhet | ityavasthābhedatatphalakathanaṃ tatraiva || 
yadyevaṃ vāmabhāge tu bālāyāḥ praśnakarmaṇi | yadi tatra pumān 
praśnaṃ karoti vāmagāminī | tadā rogamavāpnoti 
karmahānirbhaveddhruvam | yadi vāyūdayo vāme dakṣiṇe puruṣaḥ 
sthitaḥ | tadā kuphalamāpnoti dravyāgamanadurlabham | 
akasmāddravyahāniḥ syānmanogataphalāpaham | suhṛdbhaṅgaṃ 
vivādañca bhinne bhinnodayāchubham | kevalaṃ varuṇasyaiva puruṣo 
dakṣiṇe śubhaḥ | aśubhaṃ pṛthivīdakṣe bhedo'yaṃ varadurlabhaḥ | 
sadodayaṃ dakṣiṇe ca vāyostaijasa eva ca | ākāśasya 
vijānīyācchubhāśubhaphalaṃ prabho ! | yadi bhāgyavaśādeva 
vāyormandagatirbhavet | dakṣanāsāmadhyadeśe tadā vāmodayaṃ 
śubham | tadā vāme vicārañca vāyutejaḥsvarasya ca | jñātvodayaṃ 
vijānīyānmiśre hāniḥ svare bhayam | evaṃ svabhuvanāgāre yadi 
gacchati vāyavī | tasmin kāle pumān vāmadakṣabhāgasthasammukhaḥ 
| tadā kanyādānaphalaṃ yathā prāpnoti mānavaḥ tadā

p. 814) vāyuprasādena prāpnoti dhanamuttamam | deśāntaraśubhā 
vārttā āyāti puttrasampadaḥ | iti strīpuṃsabhedena phalakathanam || 

sūkṣmasvarodaye || guruśukrabudhendūnāṃ vāsare 
vāmanāḍikāḥ | sidhyanti sarvakāryeṣu kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ 
arkāṅgārakasaurāṇāṃ vāsare dakṣanāḍikā | smarttavyā 
cirakāmeṇa śuklenaivodayaḥ sthitaḥ | kramādekaikanāḍyāstu 
tattvānāṃ pṛthagudbhavaḥ | ahorātrasya madhye tu jñeyā 
dvādaśasaṃkramāḥ | vṛṣakarkaṭakanyālimṛgamīnaniśākare | meṣe 
siṃhe ca dhanuṣi tulāyāṃ mithune ghaṭe | udayo dakṣiṇe jñeyaḥ 
śubhāśubhavinirṇayaḥ | iti vārādibhedena 
nāḍīviśeṣaphalakathanam || tiṣṭhet pūrvottare candro bhānuḥ 
paścimadakṣiṇe | dakṣanāḍyāḥ pravāhe tu na gaccheddakṣapaścime | 
iti nāḍīviśeṣeṇa digjñānam || vāmācārapravāhe tu na gacchet 
pūrva uttare | paripanthī bhavettasya gato'sau na nivarttate | tasmādatra na 
gantavyaṃ budhaiḥ sarvahitaiḥ śubhaiḥ | tadā tatra tu 
saṅghātamṛtyureva na saṃśayaḥ || iti vahannāḍīkasya 
digviśeṣagamananindā || śuklapakṣe dvitīyāyāmarke vahati 
candramāḥ | pradṛśyate mahā/llābhaḥ puṃsaḥ saumyaṃ prajāyate | 
sūryodaye yadā sūryaścandre candrodayo bhavet | sidhyanti 
sarvakāryāṇi divārātrigatānyapi || iti svasvanāḍīphalakathanam || 
candrakārye yadā sūryaḥ sūryaścandrodaye bhavet | udvegaḥ kalaho 
hāniḥ śubhaṃ sarvaṃ nidhārayet || iti viparītanindā || sūryasya vāhe 
pravadanti jālam ekāsane yuktamanasthirañca | śvāsena yuktasya tu 
śītaraśmeḥ pravāhakāle phalamanyadasyām || yadi pratyūṣakāle tu 
viparītodayo bhavet | candrasthāne sthite cārke ravisthāne ca candramāḥ 
| prathame mānasodvegaṃ dhanahāniṃ dvitīyake | tṛtīye gamanaṃ 
jñeyamiṣṭanāśaṃ caturthake | pañcame rājyavibhavaṃ ṣaṣṭhe 
sarvārthanāśanam | saptame vyādhiduḥkhāni aṣṭame mṛtyumṛcchati || 

p. 815) iti dinabhedena viparītaphalam || kālatrayaṃ vināniṣṭaṃ 
viparītaṃ yadā bhavet | tadā duṣṭaphalaṃ proktaṃ 
kiñcinnyūne'tiśobhanam | prātarmadhyāhnayoścandraḥ sāyaṃkāle 
divākaraḥ | tadā nityaṃ jayolābho viparītantu duḥkhadam || iti 
kālabhedena kālaphalam | vāme vā dakṣiṇe vāpi yatrāpyākramyate 
svaraḥ | kṛtvā tatpadamādyañca yātrā bhavati siddhidā || iti 
vahannāḍīkasya yātrāphalam || candraḥ sampadakāryāṇi ravintu 
viṣamaṃ sadā | pūrṇapādaṃ puraḥ kṛtvā yātrā bhavati siddhidā | 
saptapādāḥ śanau śukre jñātavyāśca vicakṣaṇaiḥ | candro 
ravipadaṃ rudra ! kujamandaṃ tathaiva ca | sārddhaṃ sadā gurupadau 
jñātavyau ca vicakṣaṇaiḥ || iti vārabhede pādayātrābhedaḥ || 
lokānāṃ śīghraṃ gantuñca kṛśalāyāṅgamiṣyate | paradale yathā 
grāhyo hāniśca kalahāgame | yadaṅgaṃ vahate nāḍī grāhyaṃ 
gatikaraṃ nṛṇām | candracāre catuṣpādaṃ pañcapādañca bhāskare 
|| iti vārabhede pādayātrābhedaḥ ||

	evañca gamanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sādhayedbhuvanatrayam | 
yatrāṅge carate vāyustadaṅgasya karastathā | suptotthitamukhaṃ 
śreṣṭhaṃ labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam | na hāniḥ kalaho naiva 
kaṇṭakairnāpi bhidyate | nivarttate sukhenava sarvopadravavarjitaḥ || iti 
vahannāḍīhastena mukhamārjanaphalam | gururbandhurnṛpaścātmā 
anye'pi śubhadāyinaḥ | pūrṇāṅge khalu karttavyā 
kāryasiddhirmanīṣibhiḥ | airī caurāmvuvarṇādyā 
anyeṣāñcādivigrahaḥ | karttavyā khalu riktāyāṃ jayalābhaḥ 
sukhārthibhiḥ | dūradeśe nidhātavyaṃ gamanantu himadyuteḥ | tebhyo 
na deyo dīpte ca bhavanāvarttanañca na | tatkiñcit pūrvamuddiṣṭaṃ 
lābhādisamarāgamam | tatsarvaṃ pūrvanāḍīṣu jāyate nirvikalpakam 
| śūnyaṃ nāḍīviparītaṃ yat pūrvaṃ pratipāditam | jāyate

p. 816) nānyathā caiva yathā sarvajñabhāṣitam | havyavāhe 
khaloccāṭe devavidyādivañcake | kupitaḥ svādviṭho vādyāḥ 
pūrṇasthāḥ syurbhayaṅkarāḥ || iti pūrṇariktaphalam || dūrādhvani 
śubhaścandro nirvighne'bhīṣṭasiddhidaḥ | praveśakāryahetuṣu 
sūryaḥ śīghraṃ praśasyate | candracārī viṣaṃ hanti sūrye bālā 
vaśaṃ nayet | suṣumnāyāṃ bhavenmokṣa eko devaḥ sthirāsthitaḥ | sa 
yogī yogatā nāḍī yogasthāne'pi yogyatā | kāryānubandhanaṃ 
jīvaṃ kathamūrddhvaṃ samācaret | śubhāśubhāni kāryāṇi 
kriyate'harniśaṃ yadā | tadā kāryānurodhena kāryaṃ 
nāḍīpracālanam || iti nāḍīcālanakāraṇam | cālanantu guruto 
jñātavyam ||
 
atheḍāsthirakarmāṇi dūrādhvagamane tathā | āśrame 
harmyaprāsāde vastūnāṃ saṅgrahe'pi ca | 
vāpīkūpataḍāgādipratiṣṭhānte tu devayoḥ | yātrādāne vivāhe ca 
vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇe | śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikañcaiva 
divyauṣadhirasāyanam | svāmisandarśane yātrāvāṇijyānāñca 
saṅgrahe | gṛhapraveśe sevāyāṃ kṛṣīṇāṃ vījavāpane | 
śubhakarmāṇi sandhau ca nirgame vā śubhaḥ śaśī || iti karmaviśeṣe 
karmanāḍīphalam || vidyārambhādikāryeṣu bāndhavānāñca 
darśane | janamukhe ca dharme ca dīkṣāyāṃ mantrasādhane | 
kālavyādhicikitsāyāṃ svāmisambodhane tathā | gajāśvārohaṇe 
dhanvigajāśvānāñca bandhane | paropakaraṇe caiva nidhīnāṃ 
sthāpane tathā | gītavādyādinṛtyādyairgītaśāstravicāraṇe | 
puragrāmanivāse ca tilakacchatradhāraṇe | śāntiśoke viṣāde ca 
jvarite mūrcchite'pi vā | svajane svāmisambandhe 
dhānyāderdārusaṅgrahe | striyā ānandabhūṣāṇāṃ 
viṣṭarāgamane tathā | gurupūjāviṣādānāñcālanañca varānane ! | 
iḍāyāṃ siddhidaṃ proktaṃ yogābhyāsādikarma ca || iti karmaviśeṣe 
nāḍī || tatrāpi varjayedvāyusteja ākāśameva ca |

p. 817) sarvakarmāṇi sidhyanti divā rātrau gatāni ca | sarvatra 
śubhakāryeṣu candracāraḥ praśasyate |
 
atha piṅgalā | kaṭhinakrūravidyānāṃ paṭhane pāṭhane tathā | 
strīsaṅgaveśyāgamane mahānaukādirohaṇe | iṣṭakārye surāpāne 
vīramantra upāsane | bahuladhvaṃsadeśādiviṣadānādivairiṇām | 
śāstrābhyāse ca gamane mṛgādipaśuvikrame | 
iṣṭakākāṣṭhapāṣāṇe ratnagharṣaṇadāruṇe | gītābhyāse 
yantratantre rdargaparvatarohaṇe | dyūte 
cauragajāśvādirathavāhanasādhane | vyāyāme māraṇoccāṭe 
ṣaṭkarmādiṣu sādhane | rakṣiṇīyakṣavetālaviśvabhūtādisaṃgrahe | 
kharoṣṭramahiṣādīnāṃ gajasyārohaṇe tathā | nadījalaughataraṇe 
bheṣaje lekhane maṇeḥ | māraṇaṃ mohanaṃ 
stambhavidveṣoccāṭanaṃ vaśam | prāṇa ākarṣaṇe dāne 
cakravikrayaṇe tathā | khaḍgahaste vairiyuddhe bhoge vā rājadarśane | 
bhojane vyavahāre ca krūre dīpte raviḥ śubhaḥ | bhuktamātre ca 
mandāgnau strīṇāṃ vaśyārthakarmaṇi | śayanaṃ sūryabāhena 
karttavyaṃ sarvadā budhaiḥ | krūrāṇi sarvakarmāṇi carāṇi vividhāni 
ca | tāni sidhyanti sūryeṇa nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | iti karmaviśeṣe 
piṅgalāphalam ||

atha suṣumnā || kṣaṇaṃ vāme dakṣe yadā vahati mārutaḥ | 
suṣumnā sā ca vijñeyā sarvakāryaharā śubhā | tasyāṃ nāḍyāṃ 
sthito vahnirjvalanti kālarūpiṇaḥ | viṣamaṃ taṃ vijānīyāt 
sarvakāryavināśanam | yadānukramamullaṅghya tasyāṃ nāḍyāṃ 
dvayaṃ vahet | tadā tasya vijānīyādaśubhaṃ samupasthitam | kṣaṇam 
vāme kṣaṇaṃ vāyurviṣamaṃ bhāvamādiśet | viparītaphalaṃ 
jñeyaṃ jñātavyañca varānane ! | ubhayoreva sañcārī viṣamaṃ 
taṃ vidurbudhāḥ | na kuryāt krūrasaumyāni tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ 
bhavet | jīvite māraṇapraśne lābhālābhau jayājayau | viṣame

p. 818) vaiparītyasya sa smarejjagadīśvaram | īśvaraṃ cintitaṃ 
kāryaṃ yogābhyāsādikarma ca | anyat kiñcinna karttavyaṃ 
jayalābhasukhādibhiḥ | sūryeṇa vahamānāyāṃ suṣumnāyāṃ 
muhurmuhuḥ | śāpaṃ dadyādvaraṃ dadyāt sarvadā ca tadanyathā | 
nāḍyasaṃkramaṇe kāle tattvasaṃkramaṇe tathā | śubhaṃ kiñcinna 
karttavyaṃ puṇyadānādi koṭidhā ! viṣamadvitīyāyāṃ tristho'pi 
manasāpi na cintayet | yātrāhānikarī tasya mṛtyukāle na saṃśayaḥ | 
puronāsorddhataścandro dakṣorddhe pṛṣṭhato raviḥ | 
pūrṇāriktaviveko'yaṃ jñātavyo deśikaiḥ sadā | 
ūrddhvavāmāgrato dūto jñeyo vāmapathi sthitaḥ | pṛṣṭhe dakṣe 
tathādhastāt sūryavāho gato mataḥ | anādi viṣamaṃ 
sandhinirāhāraṃ nirākulam | paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ vilīyante sā sandhyā 
sandhirucyate | viṣavat sandhikāpraśnaḥ sā sandhyā sandhirucyate | na 
vedaṃ vedamityāha vedo vedo nigadyate | paramātmā hi yatrāsti sa 
vedo veda ucyate | iti nāḍītrayabhedanirṇayaḥ || 

śrīdevyuvāca || 

devadeva ! mahādeva ! sarvasaṃsāratāraka ! | sthitaṃ tvadīyahṛdaye 
rahasyaṃ vada me prabho ! |

śrīśiva uvāca ||

svarajñānarahasyantu na kiñcidiṣṭadevatā | svarajñānarato yogī sa 
yogī paramo mataḥ | pañcatattvādbhavet sṛṣṭistattve tattvaṃ vilīyate | 
pañcatattvāt paraṃ tattvaṃ tattvātmānaṃ nirañjanam | 
tattvānāṃ nāma vijñeyaṃ siddhiyogena yoginaḥ | bhūtānāṃ 
duṣṭacihnāni jānanti hi surottamāḥ | pṛthivyāpastathā tejo 
vāyurākāśa eva ca | pañcabhūtātmakaṃ sarvaṃ yo jānāti sa 
pūjakaḥ | sarvalokasya jīvena na bhedaṃ bhinnatattvakam | 
bhūrllokasatyaparyantaṃ nāḍībhedaṃ pṛthak pṛthak | bhūrloko 
nābhiḥ satyaṃ mastakaṃ tatra tallokāvasthānāt | vāme vā dakṣiṇe

p. 819) vāpi udayaśca prakīrttitaḥ | aṣṭadhā dhātuvijñānaṃ śṛṇu 
vakṣyāmi sundari ! | prathame tattvasaṃkhyāyāṃ dvitīye 
śvāsasandhiṣu | tṛtīye svaracihnāni caturthe prāṇameva ca | 
pañcatattvasya varṇaḥ syāt ṣaṣṭhe ca sthānameva ca | saptame 
svādusaṃyuktaṃ aṣṭame gatilakṣaṇam | evamaṣṭavidhaṃ prāṇaṃ 
viṣamaṃ  tat carācaram | svarātparataraṃ devi ! nānyat sthātuṃ 
kadācana | nirīkṣitavyaṃ yatnena yadā pratyūṣakālataḥ | kālasya 
vañcanārthāya karma kurvanti yoginaḥ | 
śrutyoraṅguṣṭhayormadhyāṅgulyornāsāpuṭadvaye | vadane 
prāntagopānte'ṅgulaṃ deśe dṛgadvayoḥ | asyā bhūtapṛthivyādi 
tattvajñānaṃ bhavet kramāt | 
pītaśvetāruṇaśyāmavindubhirnirupādhikam | darpaṇe ca samālokya 
tatra śvāsaṃ vinikṣipet | ākārantu vijānīyāttattvabhedaṃ 
vicakṣaṇaḥ | iti bhūtaprakriyā || caturasramarddhacandraṃ kauṇyaṃ 
varttulasammitam | vindubhistu na vijñeyaṃ sākāraistattvalakṣaṇam | 
madhye arddhañca paścādarddhañca vahate'nalaḥ | 
tīrthavāyupravāhañca nabho vahati saṃkrame | iti 
bhūtajñānaprakāraḥ | āpaḥ śvetāḥ kṣitiḥ pītā raktavarṇo 
hutāśanaḥ | mārutā nīlajīmūtā ākāśaṃ sarvavarṇakam | iti 
bhūtarūpāṇi | skandhadvaye sthito vahnirnābhimūle prabhañjanaḥ | 
jānudeśe kṣitistoyaṃ pādānte mastake nabhaḥ || iti 
sthānaviśeṣasthatattvena bhūtajñānam || mahīyaṃ madhurasvāduḥ 
kāṣāyaṃ madhuraṃ jalam | tiktaṃ tejaḥ samīro'mlamākāśaṃ 
kaṭukaṃ tathā | iti bhūtarasāḥ | aṣṭāṅgulaṃ 
vahedvāyuranalaścaturaṅgulam | dvādaśāṅgulaṃ māheyaṃ 
ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ vāriṇaḥ | iti bhūtagatiḥ | ūrddhamṛtyuradhaḥ 
śāntistiryaguccāṭanaṃ tathā | madhye stambhaṃ vijānīyānnabhaḥ 
sarvatra madhyamam | pṛthivyāṃ sthirakarmāṇi carakarmāṇi vāruṇe | 
tejasi krūrakarmāṇi māraṇoccāṭane'nile | vyomnikiñcinna

p. 820) karttavyaṃ madhyamo yogasevayā | iti bhūtaviśeṣe 
ṣaṭkarmaviśeṣaḥ | śūnye tu sarvakāryeṣu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | 
pṛthvījalābhyāṃ siddhiḥ syānmṛtyuvahnau kṣayo'nile | nabhasyaṃ 
niṣphalaṃ sarvaṃ jñātavyaṃ tattvavedibhiḥ | ciralābhā kṣitirjñeyā 
tatkṣaṇāttoyatattvataḥ | hāniḥ syādvahnivātābhyāṃ nabhasyaṃ 
viphalaṃ labhet | pītaśyoṇairmadhyavāhī tanupīvaravaddhṛtiḥ | 
kavoṣṇaḥ pārthivo vāyuḥ sthirakāryaprasādhakaḥ | adhovāhī 
gurudhvānaḥ śīghragaḥ śīṇabhāsitaḥ | yaḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulo vāyuḥ 
sa pāpaḥ śubhakarmakṛt | āvarttagaśca coṣṇaśca 
svarṇābhaścaturaṅgulaḥ | ūrddhavāho ca yaḥ krūrakarmakārī ca 
taijasaḥ | uṣṇaśītaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇastiryaggāmyaṣṭamāṅgalaḥ | vāyuḥ 
pavanasamo jñeyaścarakarmasu siddhidaḥ | yaḥ samīrasya pavanaḥ 
sarvatattvaguṇāvahaḥ | āvarttaṃ taṃ vijānīyād yogināṃ 
yogadāyakam | pītañcaiva catuṣkoṇaṃ madhuraṃ madhyamāśritaḥ | 
bhogadaṃ pārthivaṃ tattvaṃ pravāhe dvādaśāṅgulam | 
śvetamarddhendusaṅkāśaṃ svādukāṣāyamādakam | 
lābhakṛdvāruṇaṃ tattvaṃ pravāhe ṣoḍaśāṅgulam | 
raktatrikoṇatiktaḥ syādūrddhamārgapravāhakaḥ | dīptañca taijasaṃ 
tattvaṃ pravāhe caturaṅgulam | nīlañca varttulākāraṃ svādvamlaṃ 
tīryagāśritam | capalaṃ mārutaṃ tattvaṃ pravāhe'ṣṭāṅgulaṃ 
smṛtam | varṇākāraṃ svāduvahamavyakta sarvagāminam | mokṣadaṃ 
nābhasaṃ tattvaṃ sarvakāryeṣu niṣphalam || ityaṅguniviśeṣagatyā 
bhūtatatphalam ||
 
pṛthvījale śubhe tattva tejo miśraphalodayam | hānimṛtyukarau 
puṃsāmubhau ca vyomamārutau | apūrvapaścime pṛthvī tejaśca 
dakṣiṇe tathā | vāyuśca uttare jñeyo madhyakoṇagataṃ nabhaḥ | 
candre pṛthvījale syātāṃ sūrye agniryadā bhavet | tadā 
siddhirnasandehaḥ saumye saumyeṣu karmaṣu | lābhaḥ pṛthvīkṛto na 
syānniśāyāṃ lābhakṛjjalam | vahnau mṛtyuḥ kṣitau vāyurnabhaḥ

p. 821) sthānaṃ dahet kvacit | jīvitavye jaye lābhe kṛṣyāñca 
dhanakarmaṇi | mantrārthī yuddhapraśne ca gamanāgamane tathā | 
āyāti vāruṇe śatrustatrasthe'pi śubhaḥ kṣitau | prayāti vāyuto 
nityaṃ hānirmṛtyurnabho'nile || iti bhūtaviśeṣe phalaviśeṣaḥ ||
 
pṛthivyāṃ mūlacintā syāt mūlamākāśato vahet | tejasi 
dhātucintā syāt jīvaḥ syājjalavātayoḥ || iti bhūtaviśeṣeṇa 
mūlajīvādinirṇayaḥ || pṛthivyāṃ bahupādaḥ syād dvipādasyaiva 
vāyave | tejasi ca catuṣpādo nabhasaḥ pādavarjitaḥ | iti bhūtaviśeṣeṇa 
bahupādādinirṇayaḥ | kuje vahnīravau pṛthvī saurirāpaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 
vāyusthale sthito rāhurdakṣarandhrapravāhakaḥ | jale candro budha 
pṛthvī gurau vātaḥ site'nilaḥ | vāmanāḍyāṃ sthitāḥ sarve 
sarvakāryeṣu niścitam | pārthive mūlavijñānaṃ jīvajñānaṃ tathā 
jale | agnau ca dhātuvijñānaṃ vyomni śūnyaṃ vinirdiśet || iti 
bhūtabhedena dhātvādi || tuṣṭipuṣṭiratikrīḍājapahāsyaṃ dharājale | 
vāyau tejaśi suptajño yattat kalpaṃ pravāsinām | tato mṛtyuśca 
ākāśe candrāvasthāḥ prakīrttitāḥ | dvādaśaitāḥ prayatnena 
jñātavyā deśikaiḥ sadā | pūrvasyāṃ paścimāyāṃ vai 
uttarasyāṃ yathākramam | pṛthivyādīni tattvāni baliṣṭhāni vinirdiśet 
| pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśa eva ca || pṛthivī palapañcāśat 
catvāriṃśadapastathā | tejastriṃśadvijānīyādvāyuśca 
viṃśatirnabhaḥ | nabho'pi viṃśatipalamiti 
kākākṣigolakanyāyenobhayatra sambadhyate || pṛthivī cirakāle ca 
lābhaścāpo bhavet kṣaṇāt | jāyate pavanaistulyaḥ siddhayo'gnau 
bhaviṣyati | iti tattvena praśnaphalakālanirṇayaḥ || pṛthvīpañca apāṃ 
vedāguṇāstejo dvivāyavaḥ | nabhaścaikaguṇaścaiva 
tattvajñānamidaṃ bhavet | iti pañcabhūteṣu

p. 822) śabdādiguṇaparimāṇam || yatkārakṛt prasphuṭitā vidīrṇā 
atītā dharā | dadāti sarvakāryeṣu avasthāsadṛśaṃ phalam | iti 
pṛthivyavasthā | ghaniṣṭhā revatī jyeṣṭhānurādhā śravaṇā tathā | 
abhijiccottarāṣāḍhā pṛthvītattvamudāhṛtam | pūrvāṣāḍhā 
tathāśleṣā mūlārdrā caiva rohiṇī | uttarabhādrapadā toyaṃ 
āpastattvantvabhīṣṭadam | bharaṇī kṛttikā puṣyā maghā pūrvā ca 
phalgunī | pūrvabhādrapadāsvātī tejastattvamiti priye ! | 
viśākhottaraphalgunyau hastā citrā punarvasuḥ | aśvinī mṛgaśīrṣā 
ca vāyustattvamudāhṛtam || iti pañcabhūtanakṣatrāṇi || 
bahannāḍīsthito bhūto yadi pṛcchecchubhāśubham | tat sarvaṃ 
siddhimāyāti śūnye śūnyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | iti 
pūrṇanāḍyādigatasya praśnaphalam || tattvairāmo jayaṃ prāptaḥ 
satattve ca dhanañjayaḥ | kauravā nihatāḥ sarve 
mūrddhatattvaviparyyaye | janmāntarasya saṃskārāt prasādādathavā 
guroḥ | kaiścittu jñāyate tattvaṃ vāsanāvimalātmabhiḥ | 
kāyanagaramadhye tu māruto rakṣapālakaḥ | praveśe daśabhiḥ prokto 
nirgame dvādaśāṅgulam | gamane ca caturviṃśaṃ netravedāśca 
dhāvane | dhāvane javena gamane | maithune pañcaṣaṣṭiśca śayane ca 
śatāṅgulam | prāṇasya sā gatirdevi ! svabhāvād dvādaśāṅgulā | 
bhojane vacane caiva gatiraṣṭādaśāṅgulā || iti kāryaviśeṣe 
prāṇānāṃ vahirgatinirṇayaḥ ||
 
ekāṅgulikṛte nyūne prāṇaniṣkāmatā matā | ānandastu 
dvitīye ca kaviśaktistṛtīyake | vācāśaktiścaturthe tu dūradṛṣṭistu 
pañcame | ṣaṣṭhe ākāśagamanaṃ khaṇḍaveśca saptame | aṣṭame 
siddhayaścāṣṭau navame nidhanaṃ na ca | daśame daśamūrttiḥ 
syācchāyānāśa ekādaśe | dvādaśe haṃsacāraśca 
gaṅgāmṛtarasaṃ pibet | ānakhāgraṃ prāṇapūrakasya bhakṣyañca 

p. 823) bhojanam | evaṃ prāṇavidhiḥ proktaḥ sarvakāryaphalapradaḥ | 
jñātavyo guruvākyena na vidyāśāstrakoṭibhiḥ | prātaścandro raviḥ 
sāyaṃ yadi daivena labhyate | madhyāhne madhyarātrau ca paraṃ 
tattvaṃ pravarttate | dūrayuddhe jayī candraḥ samāsanne divākaraḥ | 
yātrādāne vivāhe ca praveśe nagarādike | śubhakāryeṣu sarveṣu 
candracāraḥ praśasyate || ayanatithidineśastūpatattvena yukto yadi vahati 
kadāciddaivayogenapuṃsaḥ | sa jayati ripusainyaṃ stambhamātre 
svareṇa prabhavati sa ca vijñaḥ keśavasyāpi lokam || jīvavakṣe 
jīvavakṣaṃ jīvāṅge paridhāya ca | jīvo jayati yo yuddhe jīvo jayati 
medinīm | bhūmau jale ca karttavyaṃ gamanaṃ śastrikarmasu | 
vahnivāyupradīpteṣu punarnaiva bhaviṣyati | iti tattvaviśeṣeṇa gateḥ 
śubhāśubhaphalam ||

	jīvena śastraṃ badhnīyāt jīvenaiva vikāśayet | jīve ca 
prakṣipecchastraṃ yuddhe jayati sarvathā | ākarṣe prāṇapavanaḥ 
samāse hetuvāhanaḥ | samottare padaṃ dadyāt sarvakāryāṇi sādhayet 
| apūrṇe śatrusaṃgrāme pūṇavasu vanaṃ tathā kurute 
pūrṇatattvastho jayatyeko vasundharām | yā nāḍī vahate cāṅge 
tadvāme vyādhidevatā | sanmukhe'pīdṛśī teṣāṃ 
sarvakāryaphalapradā | ādau ca kurute mudrāṃ paścādyuddhaṃ 
samācaret | sarvamudrā kṛtā yena teṣāṃ siddhirna saṃśayaḥ | 
yantrapravāhe'pi yathā sūryāvāhe bhaṭāḥ samāḥ | jāte hi 
yodhakāmaḥ samīraṇatattvayorvādam | sa prāpita eṣu nītasya na 
prātaḥkāladrīṭā | yadi sambahe vāyuyuddhaṃ yadā tadā sadāpetaḥ 
|| jayatyeva na sandehaḥ śatruśca yadi cāgrataḥ | yatra nāḍyāṃ 
vahedvāyustadaṃśaṃ prāṇameva ca | ākṛṣya gacchet karṇāntaṃ 
jayatyeva purandaram | pratipakṣaṃ yadārabhya pūrṇo 
jñeyo'bhirakṣitam | na tasyā ripubhiḥ śaktirbaliṣṭhairapi hanyate | 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhye pādāṅguṣṭhe tathā dhanuḥ |

p. 824) yuddhakāle ca karttavyaṃ lakṣayodhajayo bhavet | niśākare 
ravau cāre madhye yasya samīraṇaḥ | sthito rakṣeddigantāni 
jayākāṅkṣī naraḥ sadā | śvāsapraveśakāle tu dūto jalpati 
vāñchitam | tasyārthāḥ siddhimāyānti nirgameṇaiva sundari ! || iti 
śvāsapraveśakāle praśnaphalam ||
 
lābhādīni svakāryāṇi pṛṣṭāni kīrttitāni ca | sajīve sati 
sidhyanti hānirniḥsaraṇe bhavet | naro dakṣaḥ svakīyaśca striyā 
vāmaḥ praśasyate | kumbhako yuddhakāle ca trayo nāḍī striyo gatiḥ | 
hakārasya sakārasya vinā bhedaṃ svaraḥ katham | so'haṃ haṃsaḥ 
padenaiva jīvo japati sarvadā | vāme vā yadi vā dakṣe yadā pṛcchati 
pṛcchakaḥ | tatra ghāto na jāyeta śūnye ghātaṃ vinirdiśet | 
bhūrātmano bhavedghātaḥ padasthānaṃ jale bhavet | 
ūrusthale'gnirbhavanaṃ kaṭisthānena vāyunā | 
śiraḥsthātmakhatattvena jñātavyo ghātanirṇayaḥ | evaṃ pañcavidho 
ghātaḥ svarapraśne prakāśitaḥ | iti tattvena ghātasthānanirṇayaḥ || 
yuddhodaye kṛtapraśne pūrṇasya prathamo jayaḥ | rikte caiva dvitīyeṣu 
jayī bhavati nānyathā | pūrṇanāḍīgatapraśne śūnye gatatadagrataḥ | 
śūnyasthāne'grataḥ śatrurmriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | vāmācāre 
samā nāsā yasya tasya jayo bhavet | pṛcchako dakṣiṇe bhāge vijayo 
dakṣiṇā śirā | yadi pṛcchati candrasthastadā sandhānamādiśet | 
pṛcched yadā tu sūryasthastadā jānīhi vigraham || iti 
vāmadakṣiṇabhedena praśne sandhivigrahakathanam ||
 
	pārthive ca bhaved yuddhaṃ sandhirbhavati vāruṇe | jayo hi 
taijase bhaṅgo mṛtyurvāyau ca nābhase | iti tattvaviśeṣeṇa 
yuddhādijñānam || 

nivarttikāpramādādvā yadā na jñāyate'nilaḥ | praśnakāle 
yadi kuryād baddhayuddhena buddhimān | niścalāṃ dhāraṇāṃ 
jñātvā puṣpahaste nipātayet | pūrṇāṅge puṣpapatanaṃ śūnye vā 

p. 825) tatphalaṃ vadet | tatphalaṃ pūrṇariktaphalam | tiṣṭhannupaviśan 
vāpi prāṇamākarṣayannijam | manobhaṅgaḥ kṛto yena sarvakāryeṣu 
jīvati | jīvena sthāpayedvāyurjīvenāvasthayet punaḥ | jīvena kriyate 
nityaṃ yuddhe jayati sarvathā | svarajñānī phalādagryaṃ niṣphalaṃ 
koṭidhā bhavet | iha loke pareṇaiva svarajñānī balī sadā | 
daśaśatāyutaṃ lakṣmīrddeśādhipacalaṃ kvacit | śatakratuḥ 
surendrāṇāṃ balaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet |

śrīdevyuvāca |

parasparaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ yuddhe prokto jayastathā | same yuddhe 
samutpanne manuṣyāṇāṃ kathaṃ jayaḥ |

śrīśiva uvāca |

dhyāyettattvaṃ sthire jīve juhuyāt sthirasaṅgamam | 
iṣṭasiddhirbhavettasya mahālābho jayastathā | nirākāraṃ 
samutpannaṃ sākāraṃ sakalaṃ jagat | tasyākāraṃ nirākāraṃ 
jñāne bhavati tanmayam || iti jñānayuddhavijayaprakaraṇam ||

	atha vaśīkaraṇam ||

śrīdevyuvāca |

narayuddhaṃ himayuddhaṃ tvayoktañca maheśvara ! | idānīṃ vada 
devaśa ! vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |

śrīśiva uvāca |

candrasūryeṇa candrasya sthāpayejjīvamaṇḍale | ājanmavaśagā 
vāmā kathito'yaṃ tapodhanaiḥ | jīvena gṛhyate jīvā jīvo jīvasya 
dīyate | jāvasthānagato jīvo bālājīvāśukārakaḥ | 
rātryastayāmavelāyāṃ prasupte kāminījane | brahmavīja pibedyastu 
bālājīvaharastu saḥ | aṣṭākṣaraṃ japitvā tu tasmin kāle rate sati | tat 
kṣaṇāddīyate candro mohamāyāti kāminī | samaye vā prasaṅge vā 
yuvatyāliṅgane'pi vā | tatkṣaṇāddāpayedāśu mohayet kāminīntu 
saḥ | yaḥ sūryeṇa pibeccandraṃ sa bhavenmakaradhvajaḥ | śiva 
āliṅgyate śaktyā prasaṅge dakṣiṇe'pi vā | sapta nava trayaḥ pañca 
ravau saṅgastu sūryake | candre divāturīyāṣṭe vaśyā bhavati kāminī 
|| sūryācandrau samau kṛtvā sarvākṛtyadharoṣṭhayauḥ |

p. 826) mahāpadme mukhaṃ procya vāraṃ vāramidaṃ caret | 
adhyānamiti pādasya yāvannidrāvaśaṃ gatā | 
paścājjāgratabelāyāṃ coṣya te galacakṣuṣī | anena vidhinā kāmī 
vaśayet sarvakāminīḥ | idaṃ vacanamanyasmin ityājñā parameśvari 
! || iti vaśīkaraṇam || 

atha gabhaprakaraṇam | ṛtukāle bhavennārī pañcame'hni yadā 
śuciḥ | sūryācandramasau yogo jīvena putrasambhavaḥ | śaṅkhāvalī 
gavāṃ dugdhaṃ pṛthvadhī vahate yadā | bhartturagre vadedvākyaṃ 
garbhaṃ dehi tribhirvacaḥ | ṛtusnātā pibennārī ṛtudānantu yojayet | 
rūpalāvaṇyasampannaṃ narasiṃhaṃ prasūyate | 
suṣumnāsūryagandhena ṛtujñānantu yojayet | aṅgahīnaḥ 
pumāṃstatra jāyate kṛmivigrahaḥ | viṣamāṅge divā rātrau 
viṣamāṅge dinādhipaḥ | candranetrāgnitattveṣu bandhyā 
puttramavāpnuyāt | ratyārambhe raviḥ puṃsāṃ ratyante ca 
sudhākaraḥ | anena karmayogena nādaṇḍe devadaṇḍakaḥ | 
ratyārambhe raviḥ puṃsāṃ striyāścaiva sudhākaraḥ | ubhayoḥ 
saṅgame prāpte bandhyā puttramavāpnuyāt || iti 
puttrakanyājanmanidānam || candranāḍī vahet praśne garbhe kanyā 
yadā bhavet | sūrye bhavettadā putro dvayorgarbhā vihanyate | candraḥ 
strī puruṣaḥ sūryo madhyamārge napuṃsakaḥ | śūnye śūnyaṃ 
yuge yugmaṃ garbhapātaśca saṃkrame | sūryabhāge kṛte 
putraścandravāre tu kanyakā | viṣame garbhapātaḥ syāddvayoratha 
napuṃsakaḥ | tattvairatha vijānīyāt kathitaṃ tattu sundari ! || iti praśne 
garbhe putrādijñānam || garbhādhānaṃ mārute syāt suduḥkhī divye 
khyāto vāruṇe saukhyayuktaḥ | garbhasrāvaḥ svalpajīvī ca vahnau 
bhogī bhavyaḥ pārthive'narthayuktaḥ || dhanavān saukhyayuktaśca 
bhogavān garbhasaṃsthitaḥ | sottamo varuṇe tattve vyomni garbho 
vinaśyati | māhendre jāyate putro varuṇe duhitā bhavet | śeṣeṣu

p. 827) garbhahāniḥ syājjātamātrasya vā mṛtiḥ | ravimadhye 
gataścandraścandramadhye gato raviḥ | jñātavyo gurutaḥ śīghraṃ na 
vidyāśāstrakoṭibhiḥ || iti garbhaprakaraṇam ||

atha saṃvatsaraprakaraṇam | caitraśuklapratipadi 
prātastattvavibhedataḥ | paśyedbhāvī kṣaṇo yogī dakṣiṇe 
cottarāyaṇe | candrodayasya velāyāṃ vahamāno'ṭha tattvataḥ | 
pṛthivyāpastathā vāyuḥ subhikṣaṃ sarvaśasyagam | tejovyomni 
bhayaṃ ghoraṃ durbhikṣaṃ kālatattvataḥ | evaṃ tattvaphalaṃ 
jñeyaṃ varṣe māse dine tathā | madhyamo bhavati krūro duṣṭaḥ 
sarvatra karmaṣu | deśabhaṅgo mahārogaḥ kleśakaṣṭādiduḥkhadaḥ | 
meṣasaṃkrāntivelāyāṃ svarabhedaṃ vicārayet | saṃvatsaraphalaṃ 
brūyāllokānāṃ tattvacintakaḥ | pṛthivyādigatattvānāṃ 
dinamāsābdajaṃ phalam | bhojanañca tathā duṣṭaṃ 
vyomamārutavahnibhiḥ | subhikṣaṃ vā pravṛddhiḥ syād bahuśasyā 
vasundharā | bahuvṛṣṭistathā saukhyaṃ pṛthvītattvaṃ vahed yadi | 
durbhikṣaṃ rāṣṭrabhaṅgaḥ syād vegotpattiḥ sudāruṇā | 
alpādalpatarā vṛṣṭiragnitattvaṃ vahed yadi | utpātopadravau 
bhītiralpavṛṣṭiḥ syurītayaḥ | meṣasaṃkrāntivelāyāṃ vāyutattvaṃ 
vahed yadi | meṣasaṃkrāntivelāyāṃ vyomatattvaṃ vahed yadi | tatrāpi 
sūribhirjñeyaḥ śasyādīnāmupadravaḥ | pūrvapūrvatarasyaiva 
śvāsatattvena siddhidaḥ | sūryaścandro yathābhūte saṃgrahe 
sarvasiddhidaḥ | viṣame vahnitattvasya jñāyate kevalaṃ nabhaḥ | tat 
kuryādvastusaṃgrāhī dvimāse ca mahārghyatā | ravau saṃkramaṇe 
nāḍī galate ca prasarpati | so'nile vahniyoge ca rauravaṃ jagatītale || iti 
saṃvatsaraprakaraṇam ||
 
atha rogaprakaraṇam | mahītale sarogañca jale ca jalamātaraḥ | 
asau śūnyagato dūtaḥ paścāt pūrṇe viśet yadi | mūrcchito'pi 
dhruvaṃ jīved yadarthaṃ paripṛcchati | yasminnaṅge sthito 
jīvastatrasthaḥ paripṛcchati | tadā jīvati jīvo'sau yadi rogairupadrutaḥ | 
dakṣiṇe yadi vā vāme duḥkharaudrākṣaro vadet | tadā jīvati jīvo'sau 
candraḥ samaphalaḥ kramāt | jīvākāre ca vahnyātmā jīvākāraṃ 
vilokya ca | jīvastho jīvitapraśne tasya syāt jīvitaṃ phalam | 
vāmācārastadā dakṣe praveśe yatra vāhane | tatrasthaḥ pṛcchate 
dūtastasya siddhirna saṃśayaḥ | praśne cādhaḥ sthito jīvo nyūnaṃ 
jīvo hi jīvati | ūrddhācārasthito jīvo jīvo yāti yamālayam | 
viparītākṣarapraśnaṃ riktāyāṃ pṛcchako yadi | viparyayaśca 
vijñeyo viṣamasthodaye sati | candrasthāne sthito jīvaḥ sūryasthāne ca 
pṛcchakaḥ | tadā prāṇavimukto'sau yadi rogairupadrutaḥ | 
piṅgalāyāṃ sthito jīvo vāme dūtastu pṛcchati | tadāpi mriyate rogī 
yadi trātā maheśvaraḥ | ekasya bhūtasya viparyayeṇa 
rogābhibhūtirbhavatīha puṃsām | tayordvayorbandhasuhṛdvipattiḥ 
pakṣatrayavyatyayato mṛtiḥ syāt ||

atha kālajñānam | māsādau vatsarādau tu pakṣādau ca 
yathākramam | jñeyaṃ kālaṃ parīkṣet vāyucārabalāt sudhīḥ | 
pañcabhūtātmakaṃ dīpaṃ śaśisnehena siñcitam | rakṣayet 
sūryavātena tena jīvaḥ sthiro bhavet | mārutaṃ bandhayitvā tu 
sūryaṃ bandhayate yadi | abhyāsājīvito jīvaḥ sūryakāle'pi vañcate 
| gaganāt sravate candraḥ kāyapadmāni siñcayet | śaśāṅkaṃ 
vārayedrātrau divācāryo divākaraḥ | ityabhāse tato nityaṃ sa yogī 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | ahorātraṃ yadaikatvaṃ vahate yasya mārutaḥ | tadā 
tasya bhavedāyuḥ sampūrṇaṃ vatsaratrayam | ahorātradvayaṃ yasya 
piṅgalāyāḥ sadāgatiḥ | tasya varṣadvayaṃ proktaṃ jīvitaṃ 
tattvavedibhiḥ | trivāraṃ vahate yasya piṅgalāyāṃ sadāgatiḥ | tadā 
saṃvatsaraṃ cāyuḥ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ | sadāgatirvāyuḥ | rātrau 

p. 829) candro divā sūryo vahed yasya nirantaram | jānīyāttasya vai 
mṛtyuḥ ṣaṇmāsābhyantare bhavet | ekādiṣoḍaśāhāni yadi 
bhānurnirantaram | vahedasya ca vai mṛtyuḥ śeṣāhena ca māsike | 
sampūrṇaṃ vahate sūryacandramāśca na dṛśyate | pakṣena jāyate 
mṛtyuḥ kālajñānena bhāṣitam | sampūrṇaṃ vahate candraḥ sūryo 
naiva ca dṛśyate | māsena jāyate mṛtyuḥ kālajñānena bhāṣitam | 
mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ yasyaiva samakāle pravarttate | tadāsau calito jñeyo 
daśāhe mriyate dhruvam | arundhatīṃ dhruvañcaiva viṣṇostrīṇi 
padāni ca | gatāyuṣo na paśyanti caturthaṃ mātṛmaṇḍalam | arundhatī 
bhavejjihvā dhruvo nāsāgrameva ca | bhruvau viṣṇupadaṃ jñeyaṃ 
tārakaṃ mātṛmaṇḍalam | navadhruvaṃ saptaghoṣaṃ pañcatattvāṃ 
trināsikām | jihvāmekavilāṃ dṛṣṭvā mriyate vā naro dhruvam | 
koṇamatsyāṅgatulyantu kiñcitpiṇḍaṃ nirīkṣayet | yadā na dṛśyate 
vindurdaśāhena naro mṛtaḥ || iti kālajñānam ||

atha nāḍījñānam | iḍā gaṅgetivijñānaṃ piṅgalā 
yamunānadī | madhye sarasvatījñeyā prayāgo yogatastribhiḥ | ādau 
sādhanamākhyātaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam | baddhapadmāsano yogī 
vidadhyāduḍḍiyānakam | pūrakaṃ kumbhakañcaiva recakañca 
tṛtīyakam | jñātavyaṃ yogibhirnityaṃ dehasaṃsiddhihetave | pūrakaṃ 
kurute pṛthvī dhātusāmyaṃ tathaiva ca | kumbhakaṃ stambhanaṃ 
kuryāt jīvarakṣāvivarddhanam | recako harate pāpaṃ 
kumbhādyogapadaṃ vrajet | paścāt saṃgrāmavattiṣṭhennatu bandhe ca 
kārayet | kumbhayet sahajaṃ vāyuryathāśaktyā prayatnataḥ | recayet 
candramārgeṇa sūryeṇa pūrayet sudhīḥ | candramāḥ pibate sūryaṃ 
sūryaḥ pibati candrakam | anyonyakālabhāvena jīvedācandratārakam | 

p. 830) yatrāṅge vahate nāḍī tannāḍībodhanaṃ kuru | 
mukhabaddhamunmocayan vai parānāñāya teṣu vā | 
mukhanāsikakarṇānāmaṅgulībhirniruddhyet | tattvodayamiti jñeyaṃ 
sammukhīkaraṇaṃ priye ! | tasya rūpagataṃ nedaṃ 
maṇḍalīlakṣaṇantvidam | yo vetti mānavo loke sa kṣudro'pica yogavit | 
nirāśīrniṣphalo yogī na kiñcidapi cintayet | vāsanāmunmanīkṛtya 
kālaṃ jayati līlayā | yasya yā śaṃsitā śaktiḥ siddhasya 
guṇagahvaram | baddhapadmāsanastho hi gudavāyuṃ niruddhayet | 
vimuñceta prāṇakādyaṃ kumbhakatrayamānile | śaktyā prāṇe 
niruddhe vivaramupagataṃ brahmacakre ca nityā nikṣepākāśamārge 
śivacaraṇaratā yānti te ke'pi dhanyāḥ || etajjānāti yo yogī etat 
paṭhati nityaśaḥ | sarvaduḥkhavinirmukto labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam | 
svarajñānaṃ śive yasya lakṣmīḥ padatale bhavet | prāṇavat 
sarvadevānāṃ brahmāṇḍe bhāskaro yathā | 
martyaloke'yamāpūjyaḥ svarajñānī pūmānapi | nāḍītrayaṃ 
vijānāti tattvajñānaṃ tathaiva ca | naiva tena bhavettūlyaṃ 
lakṣakoṭirasāyanam | ekākṣarapradātāraṃ nāḍībhedena bhedakam | 
pṛthivyāṃ nāsti taddravyaṃ yaddattvā cāṛṇī bhavet | svaratattvaṃ 
tathā yuddhaṃ divyavaśyatrayantathā | evaṃ pravarttateloke 
prasiddhaṃ siddhiyogibhiḥ | etat tantraṃ mahābhāge ! paṭhanāt 
siddhidāyakam | susthāsanaṃ samāsīno nīrajāyatalocane ! | cintayet 
paramātmānaṃ yo vadet sa bhaviṣyati ||
 
iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ ṣaṣṭhe jñānakāṇḍe 
svarajñānarūpaśākhākathanaṃ nāma dvitīyaḥ paricchedaḥ || 


atha rājayogaḥ || yogasvarodaye |

īśvara uvāca |

rājayogaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu sarvatra siddhidam | guhyādguhyataraṃ 
devi ! nānādharmaṃ parātparam ! rājayogena deveśi ! nṛpapūjyo 
bhavennaraḥ | rājayogī cirāyuśca aṣṭaiśvaryamayo bhavet || 
pañcadaśaprakāro'yaṃ rājayogaḥ || kriyāyogo jñānayogaḥ 
karmayogo haṭhastathā | dhyānayogo mantrayoga urayogaśca vāsanā | 
rājatyetad brahmavaśīva ebhiśca pañcadaśadhā | idānīṃ 
lakṣaṇañcaiṣāṃ kathayāmi śṛṇu priye ! | 
dhyānapūjādānayajñajapahomādikā kriyā | kriyāmuktimayo yogaḥ 
sapiṇḍisiddhidāyakaḥ | yatkāromīti saṅkalpaṃ kāryārambhe manaḥ 
sadā | tatsāṅgācaraṇaṃ kurvan kriyāyogarato bhavet | 
kṣamāvivekavairāgyaśāntisantoṣanispṛhāḥ | etanmuktiyuto yo'sau 
kriyāyogo nigadyate | mātsaryaṃ mamatā māyā hiṃsā ca 
madagarvitā | kāmaḥ krodho  bhayaṃ lajjā lobho mohastathā'śuciḥ | 
rāgadveṣau ghṛṇālasyaśrāntidambhakṣamābhramāḥ | yasyaitāni na 
vidyante kriyāyogī sa ucyate | sa eva muktaḥ sa jñānī caṇḍi ! nāśena 
īśvaraḥ | kriyāmuktikaro yo'sau rājayogaḥ sa muktidaḥ | 
jñānayogaṃ pravakṣyāmi tajjñānī śivatāṃ vrajet | paṭhanāt 
smaraṇādvyānānmaṇḍanāt brahmasādhakaḥ | 
tadbhedasyaikasandhānamaṣṭaiśvaryamayo bhavet | tritīrthaṃ yatra 
nāḍī ca tripuṇyaṃ parameśvari ! | svadehe yo na jānāti sa yogī 
nāmadhārakaḥ | navacakraṃ kalādhāraṃ trilakṣaṃ vyomapañcakam 
| svadehe yo na jānāti sa yogī nāmadhārakaḥ | eṣo'sya viśvarūpasya 
rājayogo mato budhaiḥ | viśeṣaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇu caikamanāḥ 
sati ! | mūlakande sthale caikā nāḍī tejasvatī parā | gudorddhe sā 
tribhāgābhūdiḍā nāma śaśiprabhā | śaktirūpāmahānāḍī 
dhyānāt sarvārthadāyinī | dakṣiṇe'pi kulākhyeti puṃrūpā 
sūryavigrahā | madhyabhāge suṣumnākhyā brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikā 
| śuddhacittena sā vijñā vidyutkoṭisamaprabhā |

p. 832) bhuktimuktipradā dhyānādaṇimādiguṇapradā | suṣumnāntaḥ 
samāśritya navacakraṃ yathā śṛṇu | mūlādhāraṃ catuṣpatraṃ 
gudorddhe varttate mahat | tanmadhye svarṇapīṭhe tu trikoṇaṃ 
maṇḍalaṃ param | tatra vahniśikhākārā mūrttiḥ sarvatra siddhidā | 
asyā dhyānaṃ manomadhye vinā pīṭhena vāṅmayam | 
sarvaśāstrāṇisaṅkarṣaṃ sadā sphurati yogavit | liṅgamūle tu 
pīṭhābhaṃ svādhiṣṭhānantu ṣaḍdalam | tanmadhye bālasūryābhaṃ 
mahajjyotiḥ susiddhidam | dhyānācca varddhate āyuḥ 
kandarpasamatāṃ vrajet | tṛtīyaṃ nābhideśe tu digdalaṃ 
paramādbhutam | mahāmeghaprabhaṃ tattu koṭividyutsamanvitam | 
kalpāntāgnisamaṃ jyotistanmadhye saṃsthitaṃ svayam | tasya 
dhyānāccirāyuḥ syādarogo jagatāṃ varaḥ | sarvapāpavinirmukto 
jagatkṣobhakaro mahān | anāhatamaṣṭapīṭhaṃ caturthakamalaṃ hṛdi | 
sūryapatraṃ mahājyotirmahāsūkṣmantu cākṣuṣam | sūryapatraṃ 
dvādaśadalam | tanmadhye'ṣṭadalaṃ padmamūrddhavaktraṃ 
mahāprabham | prāṇavāyoḥ sthalañcāsya liṅgākārantu karṇikā | 
kālikākhyā karṇikeyaṃ asyā madhye tu kuṇḍalī | padmavatyāḥ 
prabhāṅguṣṭhapramāṇā ratnasannibhā | tasyāsaṅgī jīva iti ananto 
balarūpataḥ | asya dhyānaṃ jagadvaśyaṃ khecarīsarvago bhavet | 
bhavanti vaśyā devādyāścintākartturna cānyathā | iṣṭāniṣṭo 
bhavedvaśyaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | iṣṭasiddhirbhavettasya 
sarvajñādiguṇodayaḥ | kalāpatraṃ pañcamantu viśuddhaṃ 
kaṇṭhadeśataḥ | asya madhye pumānekaḥ koṭicandrasamaprabhaḥ | 
naśyantyasādhyarogā hi sahasrāyuśca cintanāt | ājñākhyaṃ 
ṣaṣṭhakaṃ cakraṃ bhruvormadhye dvipatrakam | agnijvālānibhaṃ 
jyotiḥ puṃsaḥ strīto vivarjitam | dhyānāccāsya 
sarvasiddhirajarāmaratāṃ vrajet | catuḥṣaṣṭidalaṃ tālumadhye 
cakrantu madhyamam | pīyūṣapūrṇaṃ koṭīndusannibhaṃ

p. 833) cāmṛtasthalī | tanmadhye ghaṭikāsaṃjñā karṇikā 
raktasannibhā | saha cendukalā tatrāmṛtadhārāṃ sravatyasau | 
etaddhyātvāmṛtaiḥ snātvā sadā yogāt pramucyate | 
unmādajvarapittādidāhaśūlādivedanāḥ | naśyanti ca śiroduḥkhaṃ 
jāḍyabhāvo'pi naśyati | sadyodhyānādbhuktaviśvaṃ 
jihvājāḍyañca naśyati | brahmarandhre'ṣṭamaṃ cakraṃ śatapatraṃ 
mahāprabham | jālandharaṃ nāma pīṭhaṃ etattu parikīrttitam | 
siddhapuṃsaḥ sthalaṃ jñātvā agnidhūmanibhā śikhā | 
ādimadhyāntahīnā strīpuṃmūrttivarttate parā | antajñānī 
bhaveddhyānādākāśe'pi samāgamaḥ | nirantaraṃ sarvavettā 
ityūcāno mahān bhavet | jaganmadhye sthito 
janturjagadbādhāvivarjitaḥ | navamantu mahāśūnyaṃ cakrantu 
tatparātparam | tadupari paraṃ kiñcinnāsti kiñcinmahāparam | 
mahācakraṃ siddhacakraṃ pūrṇagauryādisaṃjñakam | tanmadhye 
varttate padmaṃ sahasradalamadbhutam | ūrddhvavaktraṃ mahāvaktre 
varṇaśobhāpadaṃ mahat | sarvakalyāṇasampūrṇamasya tulyaṃ na 
vidyate parimāṇaṃ vaktamasya manasā vacasā na hi | trikoṇakarṇikā 
tatra varttate jagadīśvari ! | kalā saptadaśī tatra varttate parameśvari ! | 
nirañjanakalā sā tu koṭisūryasamaprabhā | koṭicandraprabhā caiva 
śītoṣṇādivivarjitā | asya dhyānāt sādhakasya manoduḥkhaṃ 
bhavenna hi | anantaparamānandasthānaṃ jñeyaṃ tadūrddhvataḥ | 
ūrddhvagatakalā tatra tasya dhyānādbhavediti | iti siddhirājayogaṃ 
strīṇāṃ bhogaṃ mahāsukham | gītavādyavinodādi saśivaṃ 
varddhate kṣitau | dhyānaṃ nirantarañcāsya puṇyapāpe sthire na hi | 
nijarūpasya dṛṣṭiḥ syāddūrasyārthañca paśyati || 

sukhasādhyaṃ lakṣayogamidānīṃ śṛṇu pārvati ! | pañcadhā 
lakṣayogaśca ūrddhalakṣādibhedataḥ | ūrddhalakṣaḥ adholakṣo 
vāhyalakṣastathaiva ca | madhyalakṣastathā jñeyo'nantalakṣastathaiva 

p. 834) ca | lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu caiṣāṃ hi phalaṃ jñātvā maheśvari ! | 
ākāśe dṛṣṭimāsthāya mana ūrddhantu kārayet | ūrddhalakṣo 
bhavedeṣa parameśasya caikatā | nāsikopari deveśi ! 
dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ | dṛṣṭiḥ sthirā tu karttavyā adho 
lakṣamimaṃ bhaja | athavā nāsikāgre tu sthirā dṛṣṭiriyaṃ bhavet | 
sthirā dṛṣṭiścirāyuḥ syāttathāsau sthiradṛṣṭimān | vāhyalakṣaṃ 
svayaṃ jñeyaṃ yāti tattvanivāsinām | kāmināntu 
vahirdṛṣṭiścintādiṣu susiddhidā | eṣā vāhyamadhyalakṣā iṣṭacintā 
nirākulam | antarlakṣaṃ śṛṇu śukradigvidigādivarjitam | 
calajjāgratsuṣupteṣu bhojaneṣu ca sarvadā | sarvāvasthāsu deveśi ! 
cittaṃ śūnye niyojayet | karttākārayitā śunyaḥ mūrttimān śūnya 
īśvaraḥ | harṣaśokaghaṭastho'yaṃ janma mṛtyu labhet svayam | 
ghaṭasthācintyayormūrttirhatacintāsvarūpadhṛk | viṣayaṃ 
viṣavadduṣṭaṃ tyaktvā jñātvā tu mārutam | saṃjñāśūnyamanā 
bhūtvā puṇyapāpairna lipyate | bāhyamabhyantaraṃ khaṃ hi 
antarlakṣamiti smṛtam | etaddyānāt sadā kiñcit duḥkhaṃ na 
syācchivo bhavet | śūnyantu saccidānandaṃ niḥśabdaṃ 
brahmaśabditam | saśabdaṃ jñeyamākāśamiti bhedadvayantviha | 
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi rājayogasya lakṣaṇam | rājayoge kṛte puṃbhiḥ 
siddhicihnaṃ bhavediti | paripūrṇaṃ bhaveccittaṃ jagatstho'pi 
jagadvahiḥ | na kṣobho janma mṛtyuśca na duḥkhaṃ na sukhaṃ tathā | 
bhedābhedau manaḥsthau na jñānaṃ śīlaṃ kulaṃ tathā | 
prakāśakuśasambandhiprasaṅgo'yaṃ nirantaram | 
sarvaprakāśako'sau tu naṣṭabhedādireva ca | asya jāterna cihnañca 
niṣkalo'yaṃ nirañjanaḥ | ananto'yaṃ mahājyotirvāñchāṃbhogaṃ 
dadāti ca | asya citte nānurāgo virāgo na bhavediti | rājya prāpte'pi no 
harṣo hānau duḥkhaṃ bhavennahi | kvacidvastuni deśasya niḥsvane

p. 835) keṣu kutracit | vidyāvidyāmitraśatrau samā dṛṣṭiśca sarvaśaḥ 
| bhogāsaktādikarttṛtvena mano no bhavet khavat | lokamadhye bhavet 
karttā manomadhye'pi niṣkriyaḥ | eṣo'pi rājayogīti sukhe duḥkhe 
samastathā | harṣaśokau na jātveṣāṃ nodvego lokasaṅgame | 
nityollāse nirākāre nirāsane nirātmani | manasā niścalo bhūtvā 
sadā tiṣṭhet samo'pi ca | yathākāśe bhraman vāyurākāśaṃ vrajate 
svayam | tathākāśe mano līnaṃ rājayogakriyā matā | 
jagatsaṃsarganirlepaṃ padmapatrajalaṃ yathā ||

idānīṃ haṭhayogastu kathyate haṭhasiddhidaḥ | kṛtvāsanaṃ 
pavanāśaṃ śarīre rogahārakam | pūrakaṃ kumbhakañcaiva recakaṃ 
vāyunā bhajet | itthaṃ kramotkramaṃ jñātvā pavanaṃ sādhayet 
sadā | dhautyādikarmaṣaṭkañca prakuryāddhaṭhasādhakaḥ | 
etannāḍyāntu deveśi ! vāyupūrṇaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | tato mano 
niścalaṃ syāttata ānanda eva hi | haṭhayogānna kālaḥ 
syānmanonāśo bhaved yadi | idānīṃ haṭhayogasya dvitīyaṃ 
bhedamacchṛṇu | ākāśe nāsikāgre tu sūryakoṭisamaṃ smaret | 
śvetaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇamityādirūpataḥ | evaṃ dhyātvā 
cirāyuḥ syādaṅgājananavarjitam | śivatulyo mahātmāsau 
haṭhayogaprasādataḥ | haṭhājjyotirmayo bhūtvā hyantareṇa śivo 
bhavet | ato'yaṃ haṭhayogaḥ syāt siddhidaḥ siddhasevitaḥ | idānīṃ 
jñānayogasya lakṣaṇaṃ kathyate śive ! | yajjñātvā 
jñānasampūrṇaḥ śivaḥ syānna punarbhavaḥ | ekameva jagat 
paśyedviśvātmā viśvabhāvanaḥ | iti kṛtvā tu vai yukto 
jñānayogaṃ samācaret | yatra tatra sthito vāpi sarvajñānamayaṃ 
jagat | ya evamasti bodhena so'pi jñānādhikāravān | prāpnoti 
śāmbhavīmantrān sadā nityaparāyaṇaḥ | yathā nyagrodhavījaṃ hi 
kṣitau vapturdrumāyate | ādāvekastato'nekaḥ

p. 836) svabhāvācchādanādibhiḥ | varddhate'harniśaṃ vṛkṣaḥ 
patrapallavavistṛtaḥ | snehapuṣpaphalairvījairvistāro'yaṃ svabhāvataḥ 
| tathāsau nirmalo nityo nirvikāro nirañjanaḥ | eko'nekaḥ khayaṃ 
bhūyān sādhanād bahudhā sthitaḥ | pañcatattvamayo 
buddhimāyāhaṅkāravikriyaḥ | evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ viśvaṃ 
lokālokasuvistaram | ekameva na cānvo'sti yo jānāti sa tattvavit | 
sthāvarāḥ parvatādyā hi jaṅgamāḥ khecarādayaḥ | 
jaṅgamasthāvarākāraḥ saṃsāraḥ syāt sa īśvaraḥ | svabhāvalīlayā 
bhāti śūnye'sau śūnyabuddhitaḥ | yaddṛṣṭaṃ viṣayaṃ vastu 
taddṛśyamiti kathyate | yo dṛṣṭātītaḥ so'dṛśyastadā dṛṣṭaṃ hi 
manyate | svatanūbhedamevantu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhasaṅkulam | 
yatnāddūraṃ parityajya jñānayogo bhavet sudhīḥ | jñānasaṃyoga 
ekastu ekastu jñānayogavān | ato hi jñānato'bhinnaṃ jñeyaṃ 
jñānāt pṛthak pṛthak | dūrīkṛtyaivamāpṛthvī bhedavākyena 
darśanāt  | jñānayogī bhaved yena jñānayogastu caikakaḥ | evaṃ 
jñānānmaheśāni ! kālajit śivatāṃ vrajet | svabhāvabhedametat 
śṛṇu devi ! prayatnataḥ | yacchrutvā sarvabodhaḥ syāt muktidaḥ 
siddhivāñchitaḥ | ātmano vā pṛthivyādyāḥ svabhāvaḥ 
kiñciducyate | ātmaiva pṛthivī dhātrī komalā ca kvaciddṛḍhā | 
kvacinmanoharā sā ca vimalā ca malāmalā | durgandhā ca sugandhā 
ca nirgandhā gandhamohinī | svarṇarūpā dhāturūpā citrā ratnamayī 
parā | kvacit śvetā kvacidraktā kvacit pītā ca kṛṣṇalā | ūrvarā 
ūrvarā sā tu viṣāmṛtamayī sadā | tathā ca 
devagandharvakinnarādyāḥ khagādayaḥ | sukhasampiṇḍito rogī 
tathaiva krodhaśāntadhīḥ | aśeṣarūpabalito nānābuddhirataḥ svayam 
| devatattvaṃ bhūtaśaktyā jīvasaṃjñā bhramātmikā | jñānayogī 
nirvikāro nistāpa eka īśvaraḥ | ātmaikamūrttimān bhūtvā nirvikalpo 

p. 837) nirañjanaḥ | sukhī duḥkhī mohayukto'nantacetāḥ svabhāvataḥ 
| strīpuṃrūpī mahān so hi parasparavimohitaḥ | amanaskaḥ 
svīyabhāvāt jñānayogī nirākulaḥ | srakcandanādivāmāsu 
svabhāvādbhogamicchukaḥ | sukhātmakaiḥ sukhasthāya 
paraduḥkhāya saccitiḥ | ātmanā manasā padbhyāṃ 
puttrādiratnasampadau | asārasāraṇāttarhi jñānadhīrmanasā bhavet | 
ātmāvivekamāgamya calaccittaṃ mahākulam | viṣayāndhatamo 
dṛṣṭvā no vetti paramātmanaḥ | amāyātmā tattvātītaḥ 
satsandhānavivarjitaḥ | sukhī duḥkhī janma mṛtyuṃ yāti satyaṃ punaḥ 
punaḥ | vairāgyādidhanaṃ tyaktvā viṣavad duḥkhakṛddhiyaḥ | 
koṭisūryasamātmeti jñānayogādvimucyate | ravī tejo ghṛtaṃ dugdhe 
tile tailaṃ svabhāvataḥ | śaśamindau kule śākhaṃ kṣāre ca lavaṇaṃ 
yathā | tathā brahmaṇi saṃsāro hyakhaṇḍaparipūrvake | idānīṃ 
vāhyalakṣāṇi siddhidāni śṛṇu priye ! | dhāraṇākhyā tu caitāni 
jñātavyāni viśeṣataḥ | līlayā bhāvayellīnaṃ jyotiḥpūrṇaṃ 
mahāparam | athavā tatra deveśi ! dhūmrākāraṃ ṣaḍaṅgulam | 
athavāṣṭāṅgulaṃ raktaṃ nāsikopari lakṣayet | 
dvādaśāṅgulamānaṃ vā pṛthvītattvantu pītabham | lakṣayedathavā 
tatra koṭisūryasamaprabham | tejaḥ puñjaṃ mahākāśaṃ 
tattaddhyānācchivo bhavet | ākāśamadhye ākāśoparito 
dṛṣṭisusthiram | kṛtvā dhyānādvinā sūryaṃ caṇḍasūryantu paśyati 
| athavā lakṣametattu kartturvahiḥ śivopari | ūrddhvaṃ 
saptadaśāṅgulyaṃ pramāṇaṃ tejasā prabham | athavā 
pṛthivītattvaṃ taptakāñcanasannibham | dṛṣṭiragre tu karttavyaṃ 
lakṣametadyatātmanām | uktānāṃ yasya kasyaiva ekaśaḥ karaṇaṃ 
priye ! | balīpalitahīnaḥ syādauṣadhena vinā tathā | sarvarogāṇi 
naśyanti mitravacca vaśī ripuḥ | jīvedvarṣasahasrantu sarvalokeṣu 
pūjitaḥ | jihvāgre prabhavedvidyā vinā śāstrāvalokanāt |

p. 838) mūlakandotthatalato brahmanāḍīsamudbhavā | śvetavarṇā 
brahmarandhraparyantameva tiṣṭhati | eṣā tu brahmarandhrākhyā 
tanmadhye varttate parā | padmatantumamākārā koṭisūryataḍitprabhā 
| calatyūrddhaṃ mahāmūrttirasya dhyānādbhavecchivaḥ | 
aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhistu samagreṇa prasīdati | lalāṭopari vā dhyātvā 
candraṃ vā jyotirīśvaram | nāśayet kuṣṭharogādīn mahāyuṣmān 
śivaḥ paraḥ | bhruvormadhye'thavā dhyātvā arkantu teja īśvaram | 
sthiradṛṣṭau rājapūjyo jīvanmuktaḥ śivo yathā | 
ātmānamātmarūpaṃ hi dhyātvā yo niṣkriyo bhavet | 
nirāśīryatatattvo'yaṃ itaro na nṛpasthitiḥ |

	idānīṃ śṛṇu nāḍīnāṃ bhedaṃ vakṣyāmi siddhidam | 
meruvāhye iḍānāmnī piṅgalayā samanvitā | suṣumnā 
bhānumārgeṇa brahmadvārāvadhi sthitā | sarasvatī sugandhā tu 
gāndhārī hastijihvakā | jñātavyā karṇayormadhye netrayośca 
tathāntimā | pūṣā cālambuṣā ceti mūlasthā kutracit tathā | 
liṅgadvārādiḍāmārge brahmasthānāvadhi priye ! | nāḍyantaṃ 
pratilomeṣu sahasrāṇāṃ dvisaptatiḥ | idānīṃ dehamadhyasthāḥ 
kathyante daśa vāyavaḥ | kāryakāraṇabhāvena kathyante tāni 
cihnataḥ | prāṇavāyurhṛdi sthitvā śvāsocchvāsaṃ karoti saḥ | 
asikāntaṃ pītamīśaṃ karoti yogasaṃjñakaḥ | apāno gudadeśasthaḥ 
karotyākuñcanaṃ sa tu | stambhanañca tathāpānaḥ samāno 
nābhimaṇḍale | toṣakādipoṣakantu nāḍīnāṃ rūcidāyakaḥ | 
dīptāgnimadhye'pi tathā samānākhyā mahāparā | tālumadhye 
udānastu aśnāti pibatīti ca | śarīraṃ sakalaṃ vyāpya vyānavāyuḥ 
pratiṣṭhitaḥ | śarīre cālanaṃ teṣu karoti sthāpayatyapi | netramadhye 
kūrmanāmā nimeṣonmeṣakṛdayam | udgāre nāga ākhyātaḥ 
ūrddhavāyuḥ pracālane | kṛkaraḥ kṣutkaro jñeyo devadatto

p. 839) vijṛmbhaṇe | dhanañcayaḥ saccidākāro mṛtadehaṃ na muñcati 
| yadyapi sargakāṇḍe sarvametaduktaṃ tathāpi 
kāryakāraṇabhāvajñāpanāya punarnirdiṣṭamiti na punaruktam | 

	idānīṃ madhyalakṣantu kathyate siddhikārakam | śvetaṃ 
raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ dhūmrākārantu nīlabham | 
agnijvālāsamānābhā vidyutpuñjasamaprabhā | 
ādityamaṇḍalākāramathavā candramaṇḍalam | jvaladākāśatulyaṃ 
vā bhāvayedrūpamātmanaḥ | etajjyotirmayaṃ dehaṃ manomadhye tu 
lakṣayet | eteṣāñca kṛte lakṣe nānāduḥkhaṃ praṇaśyati | manasastu 
malo yāti mahānando bhavettataḥ | kathyate tu devyadhunākāśaṃ 
pañcabhirlakṣaṇaiḥ | ākāśantu mahākāśaṃ parākāśaṃ 
parātparam | tattvākāśaṃ sūryakāśamākāśaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam | 
savāhyābhyantare nityaṃ nirākāśantu nirmalam | karttavyaṃ 
lakṣamākāśaṃ sādhayet sādhanaṃ vinā | ghanāntarālasadṛśaṃ 
parākāśaṃ tathaiva ca | kalpāntāgnisamaṃ jyotirmahākāśaṃ 
smarettathā | koṭikoṭipradīpābhaṃ tattvākāśaṃ smarettathā | 
sūryākāśaṃ tathā koṭisūryavindusamaṃ smaret | savāhyābhyantare 
caivamākāśaṃ lakṣayettu yaḥ | śivavadviharedviśve 
pāpapuṇyavivarjitaḥ | eteṣāñcaiva lakṣeṇa karmadvārā'ghamāharet 
| navacakraṃ kalādhāraṃ dvilakṣaṃ vyomapañcakam | samagraṃ yo 
na jānāti sa yogī nāmadhārakaḥ | ṣoḍaśādhārabhedantu śṛṇu devi 
! viśeṣataḥ | aṅguṣṭhapādayostejaḥ salakṣasthiradṛṣṭimān | 
pādāṅguṣṭhe ya ādhāraḥ prathamo yogatattvataḥ | dvitīyaṃ 
pādamūlantu pādamūlaparaṃ sa vai | pādasya pārṣṇī saṃsthāpya 
balavān prabhavenmuniḥ | pādamūle'thavā pādāṅguṣṭhamūlaṃ 
vidhārayet | tṛtīyantu gudādhāro gudasaṅkocanakriyā | 
vikāśākuñcanaṃ tasya sthiravāyau ca mṛtyujit | liṅgādhāraṃ 
caturthantu liṅgasaṅkocanantu ca | liṅgasaṅkocanābhyāsāt

p. 840) paścimādaṇḍamadhyagaḥ | vajranāḍīti tanmadhye 
punarabhyasayaṃ stathā | sañcāro vāyumanasoratisañcāra iti tridhā | 
granthitrayavibhedastu tadbhedo brahmamārgataḥ | brahmapadmo 
vāyupūrṇo bhūtvā tiṣṭhati yogirāṭ | vīryastambho bhavettena 
sādhayettu sadā yuvā | mūlādhāre brahmapadme ṣaṭpadme ca tathā 
tathā | pañcamaṃ jaṭharādhāraṃ tadā bandhayati kramāt | mṛtyunā 
bhaṅgasiddho'yaṃ mṛtyoreva kṣayaṅkaraḥ | anena 
paścimādūrddhaṃ vāyuḥ kuryādviśāladhīḥ | bandho'yaṃ 
buddhimanasoḥ pañcamādhārakālajit | nābhyādhāro bhavet 
ṣaṣṭhastatra prāṇaṃ samabhyaset | svayamutpadyate nādo nādato 
muktidantataḥ | saptamo hṛdayādhārastasmin vāyunibandhanāt | 
ūrddhaktrāṇi padmāni vikasanti mahān bhavet | 
kaṇṭhādhāro'ṣṭamastatra kaṇṭhasaṅkocalakṣaṇaḥ | jālandharākhyo 
bandhaḥ syāttasmin sati maruddṛḍhaḥ | navamo ghaṇṭikādhārastatra 
jihvāgramagrataḥ | sampivatyamṛtaṃ tasmād yogajinmṛtyujitparaḥ | 
daśamastālukādhārastatra jihvāgrataḥ kṛte | calane dohane caiva jihvā 
jaḍati lambitā | nāsikāprāptajihveyaṃ tālulagnā bhavettataḥ | 
ekādaśī bhavejjihvā talajādhāra īśvari ! | jihvāgramathane tasmin 
pānīyaṃ madhuraṃ bhavet | tatpīteṣu kavirgītijyotiśchandovidāṃ 
varaḥ | dantādhāro dvādaśeti sarvarogakṣayaṅkaraḥ | 
dhārayeddantayormadhye jihvāgrañca balādapi | 
dhṛtvārddhaghaṭikāmātraṃ sarvarogantu nāśayet | nāsādhārastato 
jñeyo nāsālakṣastrayodaśaḥ | manaḥsthirakaro yastu vāyusthirakaro 
mahān | nāsāpuṭe sthirā dṛṣṭirādhāro'yaṃ caturddaśaḥ | 
kṛte'smin svīyatejaḥ syāt pratyakṣaṃ ṣaṭtrimāsataḥ | pārthivaṃ 
truṭati kṣipraṃ pratyakṣaṃ svīyatejasā | pañcadaśo bhruvormadhye 
sthiradṛṣṭistathā dhruvam | asmin dṛṣṭiḥ sthirā koṭiḥ kiraṇāni 
sphuranti hi | netrādhāraḥ ṣoḍaśo'yamaṅgulyagreṇa cālayet |

p. 841) pṛthvīmadhye tu yatkiñcidvarttate jaṭharānalaḥ | pratyakṣaṃ 
tadbhavetsarvaṃ tadābhyāsānna saṃśayaḥ | sarvajñaḥ prabhavettena 
iti ādhāraṣoḍaśaḥ ||

idānīṃ yogamaṣṭāṅgaṃ śṛṇu lakṣaṇasaṃyutam | yamaśca 
niyamaścaiva cāsanaṃ prāṇasaṃyamaḥ | pratyāhāro dhāraṇā ca 
samādhiśca viśeṣataḥ | aṣṭāṅgayoga ebhistu caiteṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ 
śṛṇu | śāntiḥ santoṣa āhāro nidrālpā manaso damaḥ | 
śūnyāntaḥkaraṇañceti yamā iti prakīrttitāḥ | cāpalyantu dūre 
tyaktvā manaḥsthairyaṃ vidhāya ca | ekatra melanaṃ nityaṃ 
prāṇamātreṇa sā matiḥ | sadodāsīnabhāvastu sarvatrecchāvivarjanam 
| yathālābhena santuṣṭaḥ parameśvaramānasaḥ | mānadānaparityāga 
ete tu niyamā iti | āsanāni ca tāvanti yāvanto jīvajantavaḥ | 
prāṇāyāmastridhā ceti bahudhā prathamaṃ śṛṇu | āsane 
prāṇasaṃyāme na śaktāḥ sukumārakāḥ | mahāpuṇyaprabhāveṇa 
śakyate tu mahātmanā | iḍāṃ śaśiprabhāṃ dhyātvā mandendunā 
tu pūrayet | pūrayitvā yathāśakti dhyānayogī tu kumbhayet | 
mahājyotirmano bhūtvā vāyuḥ pūrṇakalevaraḥ | śaktitrāsantu 
santrāsya recayedvāyumarhitaḥ | piṅgalāmarkavarṇāntu 
tyajeddhyātvā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ | ayaṃ pataṅgaḥ 
kāṣṭhāgnipratyāsena punaḥ punaḥ | kṛtvā kalevaraṃ śuddhaṃ 
kuryādyatnairmahātmanā | mano nivārya saṃsāre viṣayakārye 
tathaiva ca | manovikārabhavañcaiva tyaktvā śūnyamayo bhavet | 
pratyahāro bhavatyeva sarvanindācamatkṛtaḥ | dhyānantu dvividhaṃ 
proktaṃ sthūlasūkṣmavibhedataḥ | sthūlaṃ mantramayaṃ viddhi 
sūkṣmantu mantravarjjitam | samādhirniścalā buddhiḥ 
śvāsocchvāsādivarjitaḥ | piṇḍabrahmāṇḍayoraikyaṃ 
śṛṇvidānīṃ prayatnataḥ | brahmāṇḍe santi ye cāṇḍāḥ 
piṇḍamadhye'pi te sthitāḥ | talaṃ pādāṅguṣṭhatale tasyopari

p. 842) talātalam | mahātalaṃ gulphayormadhye gulphopari rasātalam | 
sutalaṃ jaṅghayormadhye vitalaṃ jānumadhyakam | 
ūrvormadhye'talaṃ proktaṃ saptapātālamīritam | talaṃ talātalañceti 
mahātalarasātalam | saptapātālametattu sutalaṃ vitalātalam | idānīṃ 
piṇḍamadhye tu saptalokaṃ śṛṇu priye ! | mūlādhāre tu bhūrloko 
liṅgāgre tu bhuvastataḥ | svarloko liṅgamūle tu merumūle mahastathā 
| merucchidre janoloko merunāḍyāṃ tapastathā | kamale marttyalokastu 
iti lokaḥ pṛthak pṛthak | bhūrbhuvaḥ svarmahaśceti janaścaiva 
tapastathā | saptamaḥ satyalokastu saptaloka iti smṛtaḥ | saptalokaistu 
pātālairbhuvanāni caturdaśa | atha brahmāṇḍamadhyasthāścatvāro 
lokapālakāḥ | piṇḍamadhye tu tān jñātvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet | 
indro brahmā viṣṇurīśaścatvāraścātmadevatāḥ | mūlādhāre 
catuṣpatre gajārūḍho mahāniti | sṛṣṭikarttā ca tatraiva svādhiṣṭhāne 
mahān hariḥ | maṇipūre śūlapāṇiraṣṭasiddhīśvaro mahān | 
tāludvāre tālumadhye lalāṭe vakṣakaṇṭhake | śṛṅgāṭikā kapāle ca 
lambikā brahmarandhrake | navacakramūrddhvacakrañca 
trikūṭetyekaviṃśatiḥ | brahmāṇḍāni vasantīti jñātavyāni 
prayatnataḥ | sapta dvīpāni kathyante'dhunā tāni śṛṇu priye ! | 
jambūdvīpastu majjāyāṃ śākadvīpastu madhyamaḥ | śālmadvīpaḥ 
śiromadhye māṃsamadhye kuśastathā | tvaci krauñco lomamadhye 
gomayadvīpa īritaḥ | nakhamadhye tathā śvetaḥ saptadvīpā 
vasundharā | jambūḥ śākastathā śālmaḥ kuśaḥ krauñcaśca 
gomayaḥ | śvetaḥ sapteti khaṇḍāni saptakhaṇḍairvasundharā | 
guptānyetāni rūpāṇi dehamadhye sthirāṇi ca | samudrāḥ sapta 
kathyante piṇḍamadhye vyavasthitāḥ | 
lavaṇekṣusurāsarpirdadhidugdhajalāntakāḥ | lavaṇaṃ svedamadhye tu 
ikṣūrakte madhu tvaci | sarpirmedo vasā madhye dadhi kṣīraṃ lalāṭake 
|
p. 843) vīryamadhye'mṛto jñeyaḥ pāde kūrmaḥ sthito mahān | 
idānīntu navadvāre nava khaṇḍāni saṃśṛṇu | pāyvādau bhārataṃ 
khaṇḍaṃ kāśmīraṃ trikamaṇḍalum | dvijakhaṇḍamekapādaṃ 
khaṇḍaṃ vakṣye samaṇḍalam | kaivarttaṃ garttagāndhāraṃ 
navakhaṇḍamiti sthitam | idānīṃ parvatāścāṣṭau kathyante śṛṇu 
yatnataḥ | merudaṇḍe sumerustu pīṭhamadhye himālayaḥ | 
vāmaskandhe tathā dakṣe malayo mandarācalaḥ | vindhyastu dakṣiṇe 
karṇe vāme maināka īśvari ! | lalāṭe madhyadeśe tu śrīśailaḥ 
parameśvari ! | tathā brahmakapāṭasthaḥ kailāsaḥ parvato mahān | 
sumerurhimavān vindhyo malayo mandarastathā | śrīśailo 
mainākaśceti kailāso'ṣṭau ca parvatāḥ | apare parvatāḥ sarve 
aṅgulīmadhyavāsinaḥ | śarīre navanāḍīsthā narmadā ca maheśvari 
! | iḍāyāṃ yamunā devi ! piṅgalāyāṃ sarasvatī | suṣumnāyāṃ 
vahedgaṅgā cānyonyāsu ca nāḍiṣu | gaṅgā sarasvatī godā 
narmadā yamunā tathā | kāverī candrabhāgā ca vitastā ca iḍāvatī | 
dvisaptatisahasreṣu nadīnadaparisravaḥ | itastato dehamadhye ṛkṣaśca 
saptaviṃśatiḥ | yogāśca rāśayaścaiva grahāśca tithayastathā | 
laharīṣu mīnamanī cāvāhanaṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā | sarvāṅgeṣu ca 
deveśi ! samagraṃ ṛkṣamaṇḍalam | trayastriṃśatkoṭayastu nivasanti ca 
devatāḥ | tathā pīṭhāni sarvāṇi dehamadhye sthitāni ca | hṛdaye 
vyomamadhye tu anantādyāstu vāsukiḥ | udare vyomamadhye tu pare 
nāgā vasanti hi | gandharvakinnarāḥ śūrā vidyādharāpsarādayaḥ | 
anekatīrthavarṇāśca guhyakāśca vasanti hi | anantasiddhayo buddhyā 
prakāśo varttate hṛdi | meghasya maṇḍalaṃ jñeyam aśrupāte tathaiva 
ca | candrārkau netrayormadhye jaṅghā lomasu sākṣiṇaḥ | 
tṛṇagulmādikañcāpi viśvarūpaṃ smarettataḥ | 
samagradarśanānmuktaḥ svargabhogañca matsukham |

p. 844) tadetaccintayā yāti rogaśokavivarjjitaḥ | yatkarmā karmaṇā 
śaṅkā manomadhye bhavedvahiḥ | tatkarmakaraṇaṃ muktirityāha 
bhagavān śivaḥ | yasya darśanamātreṇa rogaśokavivarjitaḥ | 
paramānandacittaḥ syāttapasvī caiva kīrttitaḥ | saptadvīpā 
bhaveddṛṣṭā tattvajñānaṃ tato bhavet | sarvabhāvaṃ vijānīyād 
vajradeho bhavettathā | sarpadaṣṭe viṣaṃ na syāt kṣudhā nidrā tṛṣā 
tathā | uṣṇatā śītatā ceti vāksiddhiḥ syānna saṃśayaḥ | 
vidyutpāte'pi dehasya kvaciddhānirna jāyate | tato'sau vāyuyogī 
syāddṛṣṭvā pṛthvīkulānvitaḥ | aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhiḥ 
syānmahāpadmodayastathā | āgacchanti samīpe ca nidhayo nātra 
saṃśayaḥ | yatrecchā gamanaṃ tatra svarge marttyerasātale | 
sphuratyājñākhyaḥ sarvatra samīpe parameśvaraḥ | kāraṇe hāraṇe 
śakto rakṣaṇe'pi ca pārvati ! | ātmamadhye mano nityaṃ nirjane nivaset 
sudhīḥ | kṛtvātmamanasoraikyaṃ prāpnoti paramaṃ padam | candraḥ 
sūryaḥ sthiro yāvat tāvaddehasthitistathā | tāvadekaṃ samābhāṣya 
prāpnoti ca sadāgatiḥ | sa bhavet kavitā dhīrā niścalā śāntireva ca | 
gurupādaprasādena tadaikyaṃ yāti siddhibhāk | adhunā kamalānāntu 
śṛṇu saṅketamadbhutam | anekākārabhedotthaṃ kaṃ svarūpantu 
nirmalam | kamalaṃ tena vikhyātaṃ trividhaṃ tattvadehakam | 
tatrādhāraścatuṣpatre sattvarajastamodayaḥ | etadbhāvasthitaścātmā 
sādhvasādhukaro bhavet | asmin sati sthire citte yamo vandīva gacchati | 
anāhato dvitīyaṃ yatkathyate śṛṇu śraddhayā | anāhate mahāpīṭhe 
caturasrasamanvitam | varttate'ṣṭadalaṃ padmamadhovaktrantu satpuram | 
sparśaśabdarūparasagandhā buddhirmanastathā | ahaṅkāraḥ 
kramādete tatrāṣṭadalasaṃsthitāḥ | saparyā pṛthagākārā varttate 
tatra niścitam | dhyānādātmaprakāśo'sya prakāśaṃ kamalaṃ tataḥ 
|
p. 845) yathā sūryaprakāśena ūrddhvavaktraṃ prakāśitam | 
ātmadhyānāt sadā tatra āyurvṛddhirddine dine | śaktiprasannatā 
syācca rogaśokavivarjitaḥ | yasya mudrābhyāsaśālī samyak siddhā 
ca khecarī | cidānandamayaṃ cittaṃ cetanā candrikānvitā | 
paramātmā mahāsūryaḥ sūrya ekaḥ prakāśakaḥ | 
prakāśānandayoraikyaṃ karttavyañca nirantaram | dīptastathā 
mahājyotīravirbhāti paraṃ padam | sadoditaṃ manaḥsūryaṃ 
candrajyotirivekṣate | tanmadhye nābhikarteti karaṇaṃ nā kulākulam | 
avyaktantu paraṃ tattvamanityaṃ varttate sadā | eko nāma pumānasti 
tasmāttasmāt paraṃ padam | tasmāttu paramaṃ śūnyaṃ tasmāt 
syāttu nirañjanam | ete pañcaguṇopetāḥ kathyante tadguṇaṃ yathā | 
nirguṇatvaṃ nirmalatvaṃ paripūrṇatvameva ca | vyāpakatvaṃ 
kevalatvaṃ ānandasya guṇā iti | nirākāratvanityatvanijatvañca 
nirañjanam | nirṇiketanatā ceti tatpadasyeti tadguṇāḥ | 
līnatāśīrṇatāmūrcchātoyamaṇḍalatā iti | guṇāḥ pañca 
samākhyātāḥ śūnyasya paramasya vai | svabhāvaṃ sahajaṃ satyaṃ 
śāntiḥ śāntisvarūpataḥ | iti | nirañjanaguṇāḥ pañca etajjñānī 
maheśvaraḥ | vidyotpattistadānīntu kathyate śṛṇu yatnataḥ | 
ānandaparamātmeti paramānanda ekataḥ | 
prabodhaparamānandacittotpattiprabodhavān | cidudayāt prakāśaśca 
eṣāṃ pañca tathaiva ca | avināśyo'kṣayo'bhedo'dāhyo hyakhādya 
eva ca | ete pañca guṇāḥ proktā anādo nādavairiṇā | 
kiraṇasphūrttivisphūrttiharṣavat paramātmanā | tetu pañca prakāreṇa 
guṇāḥ pañca prakīrttitāḥ | vicāraśca prabhollāsā vibhāvaśca 
layastathā | prabodhasya guṇāḥ pañca kīrttyante tena hetunā | 
abhyāsakartṛkamanāḥ sarvatattvaprabhā tathā | cidudayasya pañceti 
guṇā jñeyā viśeṣataḥ | bodhanaṃ samayatvañca vismṛtiḥ

p. 846) sakalaprabhā | prakāśasya guṇāḥ pañcacaite jñānakarāḥ 
śubhāḥ | etajjñāne tataścaiṣāṃ jñānamutpadyate mahat | 
ākāśāt pavano vāyostejastejasa eva ca | jalaṃ jalāttathā pṛthvī 
eṣāṃ pañcaguṇāstathā | agocarādvayānantagrāhyameṣāṃ 
tathātmanaḥ | sañcāraścālanaṃ śeṣe pañcadhūmrābhamambare | 
uṣmaprakāśaraktābhajvālādāhastu tejasā | prakāśādeva 
śaithilyamadhutā śvetatajjale | sthūlasākārakāṭhinyagandhaṃ 
pātamṛdau tathā | mahābhūtāni pañceti dehamadhye'dhunā śṛṇu | 
mahābhūtāni pañceti pṛthvītejo marut khakam | eteṣāñca tathā 
pañcaguṇasthānaṃ śṛṇu priye ! | asthi māṃsaṃ loma nāḍī tvak ceti 
pṛthivīguṇāḥ | kṣudhātṛṣṇālasyanidrā glāniśca pañca vāriṇaḥ | 
rogo lajjā bhayodvegau dhāraṇā ca marudguṇāḥ | etajjñānenaiva 
teṣāṃ buddhirutpadyate śubhā | yadyapi sargakāṇḍe 
pṛthvyāderguṇā uktāstathāpyetajjñānenetyanena 
kāryakāraṇabhāvadarśanāya punarucyante | mano 
buddhirahaṅkāraścittaṃ caitanyameva ca | ete pañcaprakārāśca 
antaḥkaraṇasambhavāḥ | mananāmananaṃ jñeyaṃ buddhyādipañca 
pañca tu | vivekaśāntisantoṣakṣamāvairāgyateti ca | ete pañcaguṇā 
buddherahaṅkāraguṇān śṛṇu | ahambhāvamahañcādiyugāntaṃ 
hiṃsanaṃ tathā | vṛttiḥ smṛtirmatistyājyaṃ nirāśaṃ caitikā guṇāḥ 
| niḥspṛhatā dveṣatādhairyaṃ vimarṣacintanaṃ tathā | 
citterguṇāstrayo jīvaguṇān śṛṇu maheśvari ! | āsthā śraddhā 
kṛpā bhaktiḥ satyaṃ sattvaguṇā iti | tyāgo bhogaśca śraddhā ca 
sārthavastuspṛhā tathā | raso pañcaguṇāḥ caite tāmasasya guṇān 
śṛṇu | pramodaḥ svādakalahau vivādo bhrāntivarddhanam | 
vañcanañca tathā śokastāmasasya guṇā ime | svapnajāgratsuṣuptāni 
caitanyaṃ jīvakā guṇāḥ | etādṛśi sati tattvaṃ caitanyāttadbhavediti | 

p. 847) prakṛtīcchā kriyā māyā vacaḥ pañca guṇā iti | āśā 
tṛṣṇāspṛhākāṅkṣāmithyāntaṃ prakṛteriti | unmādo vāsanā 
vāñchā cekṣitā ca guṇāḥ priye ! | śaraṇaṃ satkulācāraḥ 
kāryaniścaya ucyate | idaṃ yogarahasyañca na vācyaṃ 
mūrkhasannidhau ||
 
yogadeśastu tatraiva || utpātarahite deśe kaṇṭakādivivarjite | 
abhyasyate sadā yogaḥ samaḥ syāt sukhaduḥkhayoḥ | surājani 
samāśritya karttavyo nirupadrave | deśe tu sarvaśasyāḍhye 
lobhamohavivarjite | stutirnindā na karttavyā sādhunā satyavādinā || 
yogānadhikāriṇamāha tatraiva || manomadhye dayā nāsti sadā yaḥ 
kalahapriyaḥ | svakāryalobhane śīlo gurukāryaparāṅmukhaḥ | etasmai 
ca na dātavyaṃ vaktavyaṃ tasya sannidhau | nañubhayatra sambadhyate 
na vaktavyamityarthaḥ | yogādhikāriṇo'pi tatraiva || 
bhāvābhāvavinirmuktaḥ sarvagrahavivarjitaḥ | sadānandamayo yogī 
sadābhyāsī sadā bhavet | viruddhe duḥkhadeśe ca 
virūpe'tibhayānake | etadaniṣṭasaṃsparśe nyūnādhike balādhike | 
evambhūtasya karmāṇi saṅkalparahitāni ca | evaṃ gacchan svapan 
paśyan pāpapuṇyairna lipyate | utpannatattvabodhaḥ syāt sadā śīlasya 
sarvadā | pare dṛṣṭivilaṃ na syād vividhāni mṛtāni ca | 
antaḥkaraṇametasyayogino niṣkriyaṃ tu sa | sarvadā sahajastasya 
niṣkalādhyātmavādinaḥ | yadā prayatnaniṣpādyaṃ grāhyaṃ 
sarvamakāraṇam | yasya citte haṭhaṃ nāsti sahajānandanirbharam | 
karttavyā ca sthirā dṛṣṭistenaiva paramaṃ sthiram | manaḥ sthirañca 
tenaiva karttavyaṃ tatparaṃ padam | idānīṃ yogamāhātmyaṃ kathyate 
yadbhavettataḥ | guroranugrahācchāstrapāṭhādācāratastathā | 
vedāntārtharahasyārthasarvajñānādupāsanāt | 
āsanāddhāraṇāddhyānāllayaṣaṭkarmasādhanāt | 
āsanāccaturaśītivairāgyatyāgasambhavāt |

p. 848) haṭhayogādvarauṣadhyāt mudrāsādhanamānataḥ | 
vanavāsādbahukleśāttathā mantrādisādhanāt | 
bahudānatapastīrthasevanād dānaśikṣaṇāt | sandhyātrayagraheṇātha 
ṣaḍadarśagrahaṇāttathā | śiromṇḍagato nyāsād yogatattvañca 
vidyate | gurupādodakaṃ śiṣṭasevinā satyavādinā | 
kanyāstrādidṛṣṭipātaharṣagativivarttanāt | prasādāt sadguroḥ samyak 
prāpnoti paramaṃ padam | na guroradhikaṃ tattvaṃ yattasmāt paramaṃ 
padam | nimeṣārddhena tasyaiva ājñāpālanato bhavet | 
mahānandaśataprāptistasmai śrīgurave namaḥ | 
nānāvikalpavibhrāntināśañca kurute tu yaḥ | sadguruḥ sa tu vijñeyo 
na tu vairaprakalpakaḥ | ata eva maheśāni ! sadguruḥ śiva āditaḥ | 
satyavādī ca sacchīlo gurubhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ | svalpācāraratātmā yo 
dānādiśīlasaṃyutaḥ | kāpaṭyalobhavinyāsau 
mahāvaṃśasamudbhavaḥ | īdṛśaḥ sadgurustasya saṅgatau yatnavān 
bhavet | tadeva manasaḥ śāntiṃ prāpnoti paramaṃ padam || 

dattātreyasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamādhyaye tu || yogo hi bahudhā 
brahman ! tatsarvam kathayāmi te | mantrayogo layaścaiva 
haṭhayogastathaiva ca | rājayogaśca sarveṣāṃ yogānāmuttamaḥ 
smṛtaḥ || iti yogabhedaḥ ||

ārambhaśca ghaṭaścaiva tathā paricayaḥ smṛtaḥ | 
niṣpattiścetyavasthā ca caturddhā parikīrttitā | iti yogāvasthā | 
eteṣāṃ vistaraṃvakṣye itthaṃ śrotumihecchasi | aṅgeṣu 
mātṛkānyāsapūrvaṃ mantraṃ japan sudhīḥ | evañca mantrasiddhiḥ 
syānmantrayogaḥ sa ucyate | mṛdustatrādhikārī syāddvādaśābdaistu 
sādhanāt | prāyeṇa labhate jñānaṃ siddhiścaivāṇimādikā | 
alpabuddhirayaṃ yogaḥ sevate sādhakādhamaḥ | mantrayogaśca yaḥ 
prokto yogānāmadhamaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti mantrayogaḥ || 
layayogaścittayogāt saṅketaiśca prajāyate | ādināthena 
saṅketānantakoṭiḥ prakīrttitā |

sāṅkṛtiruvāca |

 bhagavannādināthasya ko'yaṃ rūpaḥ sa ucyatām ||

dattātreya uvāca ||

devadevasya nāmedaṃ ādināthaśca bhairavaḥ | svaśarīraiśca 
devo'yaṃ līlayā ca viriñcibhaḥ | śrīkaṇṭhaparvate gauryā saha 
prathamanāyakaḥ | mahendraparvate caiva kadalīvanagocare | girikūṭe 
citrakūṭe sapādaniyute girau | kṛpayaikaikasaṃyuktaṃ śavarebhye'pi 
dattavān | tāni sarvāṇi vaktuṃ hi na śaknomi tu vistarāt | kānicit 
kathayiṣyāmi sahajābhyāsataḥ sukham | tiṣṭhan gacchan svapan 
bhuñjan śūnyaṃ dhyāyedaharniśam | ayameko hi saṅketa 
ādināthena bhāṣitaḥ | nāsāgradṛṣṭimātreṇa paramaḥ parikīrttitaḥ | 
śivaḥ padme tu bhargasya dhyānaṃ mṛtyuñjayaḥ paraḥ | hu/ | 
bhrūmadhye dṛṣṭimātreṇa paraḥ saṅketa ucyate | 
vibhūtilepalābho'pi uttamaḥ parikīrttitaḥ | svasya 
dakṣiṇapādasyāṅguṣṭhena layamuttamam | uttamaḥ śaravadbhūmau 
śayanañcoktamuttamam | śithile nirjane deśe kuryāccet 
siddhimāpnuyāt | evañca bahusaṅketān kathayāmāsa śaṅkaraḥ | 
saṅketairbahubhiḥ puṇyairyasya cittaṃ layaṃ gatam | sa eva layayogaḥ 
syād haṭhayogaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu | iti || atra sarvayogottamatvenādau 
haṭhayoga uktaḥ | mantrayogastu sādhanarūpatayā yogaprakaraṇena 
likhita iti | śivāgame pañcamapaṭale ||
 
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kālapūruṣalakṣaṇam | yeta 
vijñānamātreṇa trikālajño bhavennaraḥ | kālodarasthitaścāpi 
yenopāyena lakṣyate | tadvadāmi samāsena yathoddiṣṭaṃ śivāgame | 
ekānte nirjane gatvā kṛtvādityantu pṛṣṭhataḥ | nirīkṣettu nijāṃ 
chāyāṃ kaṇṭhadeśe samāhitaḥ | tataścākāśaṃ vīkṣeta tatra 
paśyati śaṅkaram | oṃ hrīṃ paramātmane namaḥ | aṣṭottaraśataṃ 
japtvā tataḥ paśyati śaṅkaram | śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ 
nānārūpadharaṃ haram | ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena bhūdharāṇāṃ 
patirbhavet | varṣadvayena he nātha ! karttā harttā svayaṃ prabhuḥ |

p. 850) trilokatvamavāpnoti paramānandadāyakam | 
santatābhyāsayogena nāsti kiñcit sudurlabham | tadrūpaṃ 
kṛṣṇavarṇañca yaḥ paśyedvyomni nirmale | 
ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyumāpnoti sa yogī nātra saṃśayaḥ | pīte 
vyādhistathā rakte nīle hatyāṃ vinirdiśet | 
nānāvarṇasvarūpe'sminnudvego jāyate mahān | 
pādagulphodarāṇāñca vināśo mṛtyumādiśet | arddhavarṣeṇa 
varṣeṇa kramādvarṣadvayena ca | vinaṣṭe dakṣiṇe bāhau 
svabandhurmriyate dhruvam | vāmabāhau tathā bhāryā vinaśyati na 
saṃśayaḥ | śirodakṣiṇabāhūnāṃ vināśe mṛtyumādiśet | 
aśiromāsamaraṇaṃ vinā jaṅghe dinairnaraḥ | aṣṭābhiḥ 
skandhanāśe tu śarīrārddhe tu tatkṣaṇāt | yogārṇave || prātaḥ 
pṛṭhagate ravāvanimiṣaṃ chāyāṃ gate svāṃ ciraṃ dṛṣṭvorddhaṃ
