nayanena yaṃ sitanaraṃ chāyānaraṃ paśyati | 
tatkarṇāṃsakarāsyapārśvahṛdayābhāve kṣaṇe'rkāśvadik 
bhūvāṇākṣisamāḥ śirovigamato māsāṃstvasau jīvati |

jñānasāre dvitīyādhyāye || rasanāmarddhagāṃ kṛtvā 
kalārddhaṃ yo'vatiṣṭhate | kṣaṇena mucyate devi ! 
vyādhimṛtyujarādibhiḥ | rasanā tvantare nityaṃ yāvadbrahmavilaṃ 
gatā | amṛtaṃ rasanāgreṇa pīyamānaṃ vicintayet | māsārddhaṃ 
jīyate mṛtyuḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ hi pārvati ! | sarvavyādhivinirmukto 
yogīndro nātra saṃśayaḥ | rasanātālumūle ca vāyuṃ pītvā śanaiḥ 
śanaiḥ | ṣaṇmāsābhyantare devi ! mahāyogī bhaviṣyati | 
rasanāsvādasambhedaṃ viśeṣaṃ śṛṇu pārvati !  kṣīreṇa naśyati 
vyādhiḥ kaṭukaṇḍuvināśanam | susvādena mahādevi ! 
bālapāpavināśanam | ghṛtasvādanamātreṇa amaratvaṃ labhennaraḥ | 
madhusvādarasāccaiva śāstramudgirate sa tu | 
miṣṭānnakhaṇḍakhādyāni laḍḍūkasvādavarttini |

p. 851) evamādīnyanekāni kāmadhenurupasthitā | divyakanyāṃ 
ramennityamākṛṣṭirjāyate sadā | aṇimādiguṇopeta ūrddharetāḥ 
prajāyate | hikkāṃ dadyāt sadā vaktre ghrāṇañcaiva vijṛmbhate | 
evamabhyāsayogena kāmadevo dvitīyakaḥ | yoginīgaṇamāsādya 
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ | na kṣudhā na tṛṣā nidrālasyaṃ devi ! 
prajāyate | bhavecca svargagāmīva sarvadoṣavivarjitaḥ || 

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ ṣaṣṭhe jñānakāṇḍe 
rājayogarūpapallavakathanaṃ nāma tṛtīyaḥ paricchedaḥ || 



yogajñānārthaṃ kṣudrabrahmāṇḍatvena śarīramuktam | 
nirvāṇatantre daśamapaṭale yathā || kathitaṃ vāhyadeśasya 
brahmāṇḍasya tu lakṣaṇam | tanmadhye varttate sākṣādbhuvanāni 
caturdaśa | tadeva vigrahaṃ devi ! mahābrahmāṇḍamadhyagam | evaṃ 
bahuvidhaṃ devi ! tatra brahmāṇḍake sthitam | vṛhadbrahmāṇḍe ye 
sarve te'pi yasya śarīriṇaḥ | pṛthivyāṃ te'pi varttante 
jantorākāravigrahāḥ mahābrahmāṇḍamadhye tu 
vṛhadbrahmāṇḍameva ca | tanmadhye jantavo devi ! tanmadhye 
bhuvanāni ca | dṛṣṭimātreṇa bhedo'sti sthūlasūkṣmādi bhedataḥ | 
mahābrahmāṇḍake yadyat prakāraṃ parameśvari ! tattatsarvaṃ hi 
deveśi ! vṛhadbrahmāṇḍamadhyataḥ || caturthapaṭale || śṛṇu devi ! 
pravakṣyāmi vṛhadbrahmāṇḍalakṣaṇam | meruḥ parvatamadhye ca 
sarvadevāśrayaḥ priye ! | mahādhīrā nadī tatra madhyadeśe tu sā 
sthitā | sumeroścorddhadeśe ca satyaloko varānane ! | adhobhāge 
maheśāni ! pratiṣṭhati rasātalam | evaṃ krame merumadhye bhuvanāni 
caturdaśa | pātālasaptakasyorddhe brahmapadmaṃ maheśvari ! | 
ādhāracakraṃ tat padmaṃ dharāmadhye caturdalam | padmamadhye 
vījakośe kṣiticakraṃ manoharam | balayākārarūpeṇa samudrāḥ sapta 
saṃsthitāḥ | jambūdvīpaṃ madhyadeśe catuṣkoṇaṃ manoharam |

p. 852) trikoṇaṃ madanāgāraṃ kandarpaścādhidevatā | indrarūpaṃ 
hi laṃbījaṃ gajendravāhanaṃ śive ! | trikoṇe madanāgāre 
liṅgarūpī maheśvaraḥ | māyāśaktirmaheśāni ! 
bhujagākārarūpiṇī | tayaiva veṣṭitaṃ liṅgaṃ sārddhatribalayākṛti | 
liṅgacchidraṃ svavaktreṇa samācchādya sadā sthitam | indravījaṃ 
varārohe ! liṅgasya vāmadeśake | susiddhaṃ brahmasadanaṃ nādopari 
susundaram | tatraiva nivased brahmā sṛṣṭikarttā prajāpatiḥ | 
vāmabhāge ca sāvitrī vedamātā sureśvarī | tasyāḥ 
prasādamāsādya sṛṣṭiṃ vitanute sadā | yadrūpaṃ brahmasadanaṃ 
lakṣayojanavistṛtam tat sarvaṃ parameśāni ! ṛgvedākhyā mayoditam | 
trikoṇaṃ parameśāni ! dviṣaṣṭikuṇḍalāhivat | tadeva parvataṃ 
proktaṃ sarvadevāśrayaṃ hi tat | trikoṇamadhye tadvāhye 
paśyāpūrvaṃ varānane ! | sthāvaraṃ parvataṃ paśya kīṭaṃ 
paśumanuttamam | khagaṃ nagādikaṃ devi ! nāsti kiṃ pṛthivītale | 
trikoṇavāhye girije ! parvataṃ bahurūpakam | nīlācalaṃ 
mandarākhyaṃ parvataṃ candraśekharaṃ | himālayaṃ suvelañca 
malayaṃ bhasmaparvatam | catuṣkoṇe vaseddevi ! etat saptakulācalam | 
tṛṇagulmalatādīni nānārūpāṇi tatra vai | jāyante ca mriyante ca 
pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā || iti mūlādhārakathanam || 

pañcamapaṭale ||

śiva uvāca ||

etat padmasyorddhadeśe bhīmākhyaṃ paṅkajaṃ śubham | 
patraṣaṭkaṃ tathā vṛttaṃ caturdvāravibhūṣitam | padmamadhye 
vījakoṣe bhuvolokaṃ manoharam | sindūrasadṛśaṃ raktavarṇena 
bhūṣitaṃ tadā | tasyorddhe nivasedviṣṇuḥ śrīvāṇyau vāmadakṣiṇe 
| brahmaṇā sṛjyate lokaḥ pālyate cakrapāṇinā | vaikuṇṭhaṃ nāma tat 
svargaṃ nānādevālayaṃ hi tat | vaikuṇṭhasya dakṣabhāge golokaṃ 
sarvamohanam | tatraiva rādhikā devī dvibhujo muralīdharaḥ | 
nāradādyaiḥ suragaṇaiḥ śobhitaṃ vedapāragaiḥ | 
vaikuṇṭhasadṛśaṃ sthānaṃ nāsti jñāne ca māmake | agre madhye 
tathā vāme jyotiṣāṃ paripaśyati | nānābhogayutāḥ sarve 
nānāratnopaśobhitāḥ | indrādyā devatāḥ sarvā yathā sarvaṃ 
prapaśyati | tathaiva bhūmigāḥ sarve tiṣṭhanti stutihetave | 
mahātattvamayaṃ lokaṃ vedabāhuvirājitam | pītāmbaraṃ 
śāntamūrtti vanamālāvirājitam | evaṃ sarvaṃ bhaktajanaṃ 
vaikuṇṭhe copaśobhitam | viṣṇuśabdaṃ viṣṇugaṇaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ 
viṣṇurūpakam | viṣṇunāma vinā nāsti vaikuṇṭhe parameśvari ! | 
yadrūpaṃ golakaṃ dhāmaṃ tadrūpaṃ nāsti māmake | jñāne vā 
cakṣuṣoḥ kiṃ vā dhyānayogena vidyate | śuddhatattvamayaṃ devi ! 
nānādevena śobhitam | madhyadeśe golakākhyaṃ 
śrīviṣṇorlobhamandiram | śrīviṣṇoḥ sattvarūpasya yat sthalaṃ 
cittamohanam | tasya sthānasya mahātmyaṃ kiṃ mayā kathyate'dhunā 
| tatraiva satataṃ bhāti dvibhujo muralīdharaḥ | tadā sattvamayo 
viṣṇurbhuvanaṃ pāti niścitam | vaiṣṇavasya mahāmokṣo yatraiva 
parameśvari ! | iti sthānasya māhātmyaṃ saṃkṣepeṇa mayoditam | 
vistāreṇa na śaknomi janmāntaraśatena ca | vījakoṣasya vāhye tu 
veṣṭitaṃ toyamaṇḍalam | pramāṇaṃ sundaraṃ toyaṃ yathā 
kṣīrodasāgaram | dhūmrasya jyotiṣākāraṃ koṭicandrasamaprabham | 
balayākārarūpeṇa suśubhraṃ toyamaṇḍalam | gaṅgādisaritaḥ 
sarvāstathaiva bhānti sundari ! | indrādidevatāḥ sarvāḥ stūyamānā 
nirantaram | gandharvayakṣanāgādikuṣmāṇḍā bhairavāstathā | 
nānāsukhavilāsena sadā caikāgracetasaḥ | viṣṇugānaṃ prakurvanti 
stutibhaktiparāyaṇāḥ | vedagānaṃ prakurvanti caturvaktreṇa vedhasā | 
mālavādyāśca ṣaḍrāgāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadrāgiṇī tathā | vedagāne ca 
bhāvanto mūrttimantaḥ sadaiva hi | mālavenaiva rāgeṇa sāmagānaṃ 
sadā priye ! | mallāreṇa sadā pūrvaṃ vasantena tathā punaḥ | hillolena 
yajuḥ pāṭhaṃ sadā kurvanti vedhasā | karṇāṭenaiva ṛgvedaṃ 
śrīrāgeṇa tathā śive ! | nirdiṣṭapāṭhametattu anirdiṣṭamataḥ param | 
atraiva santi te rāgāḥ sahasrāṇi ca ṣoḍaśa | murārermularīgānāt 
sarvastālaḥ prajāyate | tena tālena rāgeṇa sadā gāyanti vedhasā | 
tadrāgasya vibhāgaṃ hi kurvanti munayo janāḥ vasantādyāśca 
ṛtavastiṣṭhanti tatra santatam | nānāṛtuprasūnena bhūṣitaṃ 
muralīdharam | tatraiva rādhikā deva nānāsukhavilāsinī | vadantī 
muralīgānaṃ kuru kānta ! pramohaṇam | yena śabdena kāmasya 
utpattirjāyate sadā | tadrāgañcaiva tattālaṃ kuru gānaṃ prayatnataḥ | 
evamānandasaṃyuktā mahāveśavilāsinī | vāmabhāge sadā bhāti 
rādhikā bhaktavatsalā | tathā | ādau rādhāṃ tataḥ kṛṣṇaṃ japanti 
ye ca mānavāḥ | sadgatiṃ caiva teṣāṃ hi dāsyāmi nātra saṃśayaḥ 
| guruṇā bhāvamārgeṇa mantramārgeṇa caiva hi | ye janā māṃ 
bhajantyeva te narā matsamāḥ sadā | yā nārī māmabhedena bhajate 
puruṣaṃ tathā | tatsamānā ca sā nārī jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
bhaktyā vāpyathavā'.bhaktyā japanti yugalaṃ yadi tava bhaktyā 
pradāsyāmi madgatiṃ śṛṇu rādhike ! || iti svādhiṣṭhānakathanam ||

ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || etatpadmasyorddhadeśe mahāpadmaṃ 
sudurlabham | daśapatraṃ nīlavarṇaṃ sahajaṃ ghorarūpakam | 
ḍādiphāntaiḥ sacandraiśca paṅkajañcātiśobhanam || tanmadhye 
vījakoṣe nivasati satataṃ vahnivījaṃ susiddham vāhye tat 
traipurākhyaṃ navatapananibhaṃ svastikaṃ tattribhāge || 
svarlokākhyamidaṃ devi ! sarvadevaprapūjitam | sākāraṃ 
vahnivījañca sadaiva meṣavāhanam | rudrālayaṃ hi tatraiva 
mahāmohasya nāśanam | bhadrakālī mahāvidyā vāmabhāge 
suśobhitā | bhadrakālī mahāvidyā sadā saṃhārakāriṇī |

p. 855) brahmaṇā sṛjyate lokaḥ pālyate viṣṇurūpiṇā | paro devo 
rudrarūpaḥ sadā saṃhārakārakaḥ | saṃharedrudrarūpastu 
bhadrakālikayā saha | rudrasya bhāṣaṇāddevi ! kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale 
| yadrūpaṃ kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ golokaṃ sarvamohanam | tasmādvai 
sarvatobhāve rudralokaṃ caturguṇam | mahāmokṣapradaṃ nityaṃ 
rudraṃ bhasmāṅgabhūṣaṇam | bhdrakālī mahāvidyā rudrasya 
vāmadeśake | tataḥ kālīṃ mahāvidyāṃ sadaiva muralīdharaḥ | 
ārādhya vahuyatnena vaikuṇṭhādhipatirbhavet | golokādhipatirdevi ! 
stutibhaktiparāyaṇaḥ | kālīpādaprasādena sa bhavellokapālakaḥ | 
lokānāṃ rakṣanārthāya sastrīko muralīdharaḥ | samārādhya 
bhadrakālīṃ goloke nivaset sadā | prasādaṃ kālikāyāśca viṣṇunā 
bhujyate sadā | ataśca pālako viṣṇurmahāsattvaparāyaṇaḥ || iti 
maṇipūrakathanam ||

	saptamapaṭale || etatpadmasyorddhadeśe vimalaṃ padmamuttamam 
| śobhitaṃ dvādaśiḥ patraiḥ śoṇabandhūkasannibham | vāñchanti 
riktaphaladaṃ siddhisindūrasodaram | padmamadhye vījakoṣe 
ṣaṭkoṇamaṇḍalaṃ śubham | maṇḍalasya madhyadeśe vāyuvījaṃ 
manoharam | savījaṃ vāyuvījena vedabāhuvirājitam | lokatrayasya 
īśānamīśvaraṃ sarvapūjitam | yā vidyā bhuvaneśānī triṣu lokeṣu 
pūjitā | īśvarasya vāmabhāge sā devī paritiṣṭhati | maharllokamidaṃ 
bhadre ! pūjāsthānaṃ sureśvari ! || tatraiva mānasaṃ yogaṃ kurute 
yogavidvaraḥ | sindrūrāraktacārvaṅgī sphāṭikairnirmitā yataḥ | 
ataśca mānavāḥ sarve jyotiṣaṃ paripaśyati | sarvāvayavasaṃyuktā 
devāstiṣṭhanti santatam | bhūmigāḥ paripaśyanti cakrākāraṃ hi 
taijasam | svarlokagāminaḥ sarve paripaśyanti sākṛtim | aṅgabhedaṃ na 
paśyanti sthūlarūpanirīkṣaṇam | yathaiva bhūmigā lokāḥ prasaranti 
mahītale | tathaiva devatāḥ sarvāḥ svarga tiṣṭhanti pārvati ! | 

p. 856) bhūrloke nivasedbrahmā bhuvoloke janārddanaḥ | svarloke 
nivasecchambhuḥ sadā saṃhārakārakaḥ | brahmādīnāñca īśānaḥ 
sarvakarttā ca īśvaraḥ | sarvasvāmisvarūpaśca sarvakartā ca īśvaraḥ 
| golokaṃ kathitaṃ devi ! yadrūpaṃ śobhitaṃ sadā | 
tasmācchataguṇaṃ devi ! maharlokaṃ susundaram | vistīrṇañca 
śataguṇaṃ sarvaṃ śataguṇaṃ śive ! | maharlokasya māhātmyaṃ 
kiṃ vaktuṃ śakyate mayā | maharloke vasedyo hi 
sāmānyabhāvatatparaḥ | tasmādeva śataikāśaṃ goloke 
muralīdharam | tadājñāṃ prāpya sahasā sṛjyate padmayoninā | 
tadājñayā pāti lokān dvibhujo muralīdharaḥ | evaṃ hi rudrarūpeṇa 
saṃharatyakhilaṃ jagat | sarvakarttā yato devo hyata eva maheśvari ! | 
īśvaraḥ sarvakarttā ca nirguṇaścācalaḥ śivaḥ | bhuvaneśīṃ 
samāsādya sarvasvāmī ca īśvaraḥ | ata eva maheśāni ! sa eva 
mokṣadāyakaḥ | viśvamātā ca sā devī viśvapālanakāriṇī | 
mokṣadā sarvalokānāṃ muktidā viśvamātṛkā | bhuvaneśīṃ vinā 
īśaḥ kiñcit kartuṃ na śakyate | ata eva hi sā devī mokṣadā 
sarvarūpiṇī | ityanāhatakathanam | 

	aṣṭamapaṭale ||

śaṅkara uvāca |

asyorddhve nirmalaṃ padmaṃ sarvamohanakāraṇam | ṣoḍaśaiḥ 
patrakairyuktaṃ mohāndhākāranāśanam | dhūmamadhye yathā 
vahnistathā jyotirmayaṃ priye ! | padmamadhye varāṭe ca janolokaṃ 
susundaram | mahāmohāndhaśamanaṃ tadvāhye candramaṇḍalam | 
devavṛndairgāthakaiśca munibhiḥ pariśobhitam | golokasya 
lakṣaguṇamidaṃ sthānaṃ sudurlabham | dairghyeṇa ca mayoktañca 
vistīrṇañca tathā punaḥ | sarvaṃ lakṣaguṇaṃ devi ! golokānnātra 
saṃśayaḥ | vījakoṣe maṇidvīpe ṣaṭkoṇaṃ yantramuttamam | 
yantramadhye ca vṛṣabhaṃ mahāsiṃhārddhadehakam | 

p. 857) tasyopari mahāgaurī dakṣabhāge sadāśivaḥ | trinetraṃ 
pañcavaktraśca prativaktre trilocanaḥ | vibhūtibhūṣitāṅgaśca 
rajatādrisahodaraḥ | vyāghracarmadharo devo'ṇimādibhirvibhūṣitaḥ | 
lokānāmiṣṭadātā ca lokānāṃ bhayanāśanaḥ | lokānāṃ 
bhuktijanako lokānāṃ muktidāyakaḥ | sadānandakaro 
devaścārddhanārīśvaro vibhuḥ | kvacit jyotirmayo devaḥ 
kvacidākāravarjitaḥ | devānāṃ pūjyarūpaśca devānāṃ 
svāmirūpakaḥ | bhaktasya muktido nityaṃ viṣṇutvadāyakaḥ prabhuḥ | 
vilvapatraiḥ pūjakasya nijasāyujyadāyakaḥ | golokādhipatiṃ kṛtvā 
bhaktaṃ rakṣati yaḥ śivaḥ | tasya devasya māhātmyaṃ vistāreṇa ca 
caṇḍike ! | yā gaurī lokamātā ca brahmārddhāṅgasvarūpiṇī | 
triguṇā sā mahādevī guṇaikena pinākadhṛk | tasyāḥ saṅgaṃ 
samāsādya sarvakartā sadāśivaḥ || iti viśuddhasthānakathanam || 

navamapaṭale ||

śaṅkara uvāca ||

etat padmasyorddhvadeśe jñānapadmaṃ sudurlabham | 
padmadvayasamāyuktaṃ pūrṇacandrasya maṇḍalam | padmamadhye 
vījakoṣe smareccintāmaṇeḥ purīm | tanmadhye navakoṇañca yantraṃ 
paramadurlabham | śambhuvījaṃ hi tanmadhye sākāraṃ 
haṃsarūpakam | haṃsaḥ paraṃ brahmarūpaḥ sākāraḥ śivarūpakaḥ 
| tāracañcūrvarārohe ! nirgamāgamapakṣavān | 
śivaśaktipadadvandvaṃ vindutrayavilocanam | vihāraścāsya haṃsasya 
hemapaṅkajapūjite | evaṃ haṃso maṇidvīpe tasya kroḍe paraḥ śivaḥ | 
vāmabhāge siddhakālī sadānandasvarūpiṇī | tasyāḥ 
prasādamāsādya sarvakarttā maheśvaraḥ | tapolokamidaṃ bhadre ! 
sarvalokasya durlabham | yatra brahmādayo devā dhyānaṃ kurvanti 
sarvadā | manasāpi na labhyeta yogena tapasā na ca | tapolokaṃ 
golokasya caturlakṣaṇaṃ śive ! | brahmalokeṣu ye devā vaikuṇṭhe ye 
surādayaḥ | śambhuloke vased yo hi te ca bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |

p. 858) tapasāpi na labhyeta tapolokagatiḥ śive ! | tapolokasamo nāsti 
lokamadhye sulocane ! | sālokyaṃ hi maharloke sārūpyaṃ janalokake | 
sāyujyaṃ ca tapoloke nirvāṇaṃ hi tadūrddhvake | tato brahmādayo 
devāstapolokārthinaḥ sadā | iti te kathitaṃ kānte ! kramaṣaṭkasya 
lakṣaṇam | yajjñānādamaratvañca jīvanmuktaśca sādhakaḥ | 
yajjñātvā jananīgarbhaṃ na viśettu kadācana || iti 
jñānasthānakathanam ||
 
	daśamapaṭale ||

śaṅkara uvāca |

jñānapadmasyorddhvadeśe sahasradalapaṅkajam | adhovaktraṃ 
mahāpadmaṃ sumerormadhyasaṃsthitam | śuklaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ 
kṛṣṇaṃ haritameva ca | vicitracitrarūpeṇa nānāvarṇena śobhitam | 
śuklaṃ kṣaṇāt kṣaṇādraktaṃ kṣaṇāt pītaṃ suśobhitam | yasmin 
kṣaṇe śuklavarṇaṃ haritaṃ varṇamuttamam | citrarūpañca cārvaṅgi 
! dhatte kasmin kṣaṇe kṣaṇe | evaṃ nānāvidhaṃ devi ! tat padmaṃ 
śobhitaṃ sadā | yathaiva dhāma golokaṃ pratipatre tathaiva hi | 
golokādhipatistatra bhaktibhāvaparāyaṇaḥ | kailāsādhipatirdevi ! 
dhyānayogaṃ sadābhyaset | evaṃ brahmādayo devā 
indrādyāstridiveśvarāḥ | stutibhaktiparāḥ sarve dīnabhāve sadā 
sthitāḥ | lakṣaṃ lakṣaṃ maheśāni ! tatraiva muralīdharaḥ | 
śatalakṣaṃ tatra rudro brahmā lakṣaśataṃ priye ! | pratyahaṃ 
parameśāni ! brahmāṇḍā bahavo'bhavan | tanmadhye sthāpayed 
brahmā tatraiva kamalāpatim | śivaṃ bahuvidhākaraṃ tatraiva 
sthāpayettataḥ | evaṃ hi parameśāni ! nānāśaktiṃ pravinyaset | 
pratibrahmāṇḍamadhye tu brahmādidevatātrayam | nānāśaktiyutaṃ 
kṛtvā brahmāṇḍasthāpanañcaret | brahmapadme pṛthivyāntu varttante 
mānuṣādayaḥ | te sarve devi ! brahmāṇḍastanmadhye bhuvanāni ca | 

p. 859) pātālasaptakaṃ tatra tatraiva svargasaptakam | evaṃ cakre 
sarvadehe bhuvanāni caturddaśa | pratidehaṃ pareśāni ! brahmāṇḍaṃ 
nātra saṃśayaḥ || tathā | sthānaṃ bahuvidhākāraṃ sarvadehasya 
cāśrayam | tanmadhye satyalokañca mahārudrasya kāraṇam | 
daśakāntena svarṇena nirmitaṃ cakrapāṇinā | dikṣu toyamaṇḍalañca 
tathā pūrṇendumaṇḍalam | paritaḥ pārijātāni madhye kalpadrumaḥ 
punaḥ | kalpadrumasya nikaṭe jyotirmandirameva ca | oṃ 
udyadādityasaṅkāśaṃ caturdvāravibhūṣitam | 
mandavādyasamāyuktaṃ gandhapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam | tanmadhye vedikā 
devi ! ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ priye ! | mahākālī paramātmā 
caṇakākārarūpataḥ | māyayācchāditātmāsau tanmadhye 
samabhāgataḥ | mahārudraḥ sa evātmā mahāviṣṇuḥ sa eva hi | 
mahābrahmā sa evātmā nāmamātravibhedakaḥ | 
ekamūrttistrināmāni brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ | nānābhāve mano 
yasya tasya mokṣo na vidyate | brahmāṇḍāstatra jāyante lakṣaṃ 
lakṣaṃ sulocane ! | tatra brahmā tatra viṣṇustatra rudraḥ pravinyaset | 
evaṃ brahmāṇḍanirmāṇaṃ kṛtvā viṣṇuḥ sanātanaḥ | sa 
jīvamūrttiṃ nirmāya tathā jantośca vigraham | evaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ 
vividhaṃ nityaṃ sṛjati nirguṇam | nirguṇe viṣṇurūpaśca 
siddhikāraṇameva hi | kecidvadanti sa brahmā kaiścidviṣṇuḥ 
prakathyate | kecidrudro mahāpūrva ekadevo nirañjanaḥ | 
ādyāśaktiyuto devaścaṇakākārarūpakaḥ | indrajālasya dīpābhaṃ 
candrasūryāgnirūpakam | mahājyotirnirākāraṃ golokaṃ sumukhaṃ 
tvatha | satyaloke vījakoṣe cintāmaṇigṛhe śubhe | dhyāyennirañjanaṃ 
devi ! ratnasiṃhāsanopari | tasyāntike nijaguruṃ 
pūjādhyānaparāyaṇaḥ | sakāntaṃ pūjayeddevi ! rajatācalasodaram 
suraktāṃ cāruvadanāṃ svaprakāśasvarūpiṇīm | evaṃ 
kāntāyutaṃ devaṃ svamūrdhnisthaṃ vicintayet | yathā

p. 860) darpaṇamadhye tu paripaśyanti parvatam | sahasrāre mahāpadme 
tathā devaṃ vicintayet | evaṃ brahmamayaṃ hyetat paraṃ 
mokṣamayaṃ priye ! | nirguṇasyālayaṃ sākṣānmahākālālayaṃ 
śive ! | yatraiva varttate nityaṃ nirākāraśca niścalaḥ | yasya rūpaṃ 
parānandaṃ parāparajagatpatiḥ | nityānandaparā devī kālī 
kālaprakāśinī | ādyāśaktirmahākālī devanirvāṇakāriṇī | jāyate 
ca kṣitau vṛkṣo yathā pṛthvyāṃ vilīyate | toyāttu vudvudaṃ jātaṃ 
yathā toye vilīyate | jalade taḍidutpannā līyate ca yathā ghane | tathā 
brahmādayo devāḥ kālikāyā bhavanti | tathā pralayakāle tu 
punastasyāṃ pralīyate | śaktijñānaṃ vinā devi ! muktirhāsyāya 
kalpyate | ekāṃśena bhavedbrahmā ekāṃśena janārddanaḥ | 
ekāṃśena bhavecchambhuḥ kālikāyāḥ sulocane ! | aparā sā 
mahākālī nadyādīnāṃ samudravat | goṣpade ca tathā toyaṃ 
brahmādyā devatāstathā | goṣpadaṃ kiṃ vijānīyāt samudrasya 
jalaṃ śive ! | tena brahmā na jānāti viṣṇuḥ kiṃ vetti śaṅkaram | 
sṛṣṭikarttā yathā kālyā janyante ca surādayaḥ | tathā pralayakāle tu 
punastasyāṃ pralīyate | ato nirvāṇadā kālī pumān svargapradāyakaḥ 
| dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi sthāne saṃsthitaśca raveḥ sutaḥ | kālīnāmnā 
palāyeta bhītiyuktaḥ samantataḥ | ataḥ sā dakṣiṇānāmnī trīṣu 
lokeṣu gīyate | nirguṇaḥ puruṣaḥ kālyā sṛjyate lupyate yataḥ | ataḥ sā 
dakṣiṇānāmnī triṣu lokeṣu gīyate | vaśyavidyā mahāvidyā kālikā 
jagadambikā | sākārarūpā sā kālī caṇakākārarūpiṇī | 
hastapādādirahitā somasūryāgnirūpiṇī | tasyāḥ sthānaṃ kathitaṃ 
satyalokaṃ varānane ! | yat sthānaṃ sarvadevasya prārthanīyaṃ 
sadānaghe ! | tasya sthānasya māhātmyaṃ kiṃ mayā kathyate'dhunā 
|
p. 861) sahasradalapadmasya caikapatre sulocane ! | sahasraṃ golakaṃ 
dhāma tato vaktuṃ na śakyate | janmāntarasahasreṇa jihvākoṭiśatena 
ca | tadvaktuṃ na hi śaknomi ekapatrasya śobhanam | atastvaṃ hi 
varārohe ! viratā bhāva pārvati ! | kiñjalkamadhye yā śobhā kiṃ 
vaktuṃ śaktyate mayā | tanmadhye karṇikā devi ! tanmadhye 
vījakoṣakam | vījakoṣasya madhye tu sudhāsāgara uttamaḥ | 
lakṣayojanavistāraḥ suśobhaṃ toyamuttamam | tanmadhye tu 
maṇidvīpaṃ sahasrayojanaṃ priye ! | paritaḥ pārijātāśca 
kadambavanamuttamam | madhye kalpadrumastatra jyotirmandiramuttamam 
| caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ hemaprākārabhūṣitam | 
mandavādyasamāyuktaṃ gandhadhūpasamanvitam | 
devakanyāsahasrāṇi paricaryāparāṇi ca | tanmadhye vedikā devi ! 
pañcāśadakṣarātmikā | tasyopari maheśāni ! ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ 
śive ! | mahākālī mahārudraścaṇakākārarūpakaḥ | indrajālasya 
dīpābhaṃ mahājyotiḥ sanātanam | śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ 
caturthollāsadhṛtasamayācāratantre ||

devyuvāca ||

devadeva ! mahābāho ! sṛṣṭisthitilayātmaka ! | mūrdhni padmaṃ 
sahasrāraṃ raktavarṇamadhomukham | tasya madhyasthitaṃ 
dhyāyedguruṃ śāntaṃ saśaktikam | mūlādhāre mahāśaktiṃ 
kuṇḍalīrūpadhāriṇīm | adhovaktre krameṇaiva sarvapadmeṣu 
bhāvanā | tadā kathaṃ bhavettatra cintanaṃ gurudevayoḥ | ādhāre ca 
sthitastatra adhobhāge kathaṃ bhavet ||

mahādeva uvāca |

yathā yuktaṃ tvayā devi ! kathitaṃ vīravandite ! | evamevātra sandeho 
jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | kathyate parameśāni ! 
sandehacchedakāraṇam | tāni padmāni deveśi ! suṣumnāntaḥsthitāni 
ca | paraṃbrahmasvarūpāṇi śabdabrahmamayāni ca | tat sarvaṃ 
paṅkajaṃ devi ! sarvatomukhameva ca | pravṛttiśca nivṛttiśca dvau

p. 862) bhāvau jīvasaṃsthitau | pravṛttimārgaḥ saṃsārī nivṛttiḥ 
paramātmani | pravṛttibhāvacintāyā madhye vaktraṇi cintayet | 
nivṛttiyogamārgeṇa sadaivorddhvamukhāni ca | evameva bhāvabhedād 
hṛtsandeho na jāyate || 

śrītattvacintāmaṇau tu viśeṣa ukto yathā || merorvāhyapradeśe 
śaśimihiraśire | savyadakṣeṇa sanne madhye nāḍī suṣumnā 
tritayaguṇamayī candrasūryāgnirūpā | 
dhustūrasmerapuṣpagrathitatamavapuḥ kandamadhyācirasthā 
vajrākhyā meḍhradeśocchirasi pariṇatā madhyame syājjvalantī || 
tanmadhye citriṇī sā praṇavavilasitā yogināṃ yogagamyā 
lūtātantūpameyā sakalasarasijānmerumadhyāntarāle | bhittvā 
dedīpyate tadgrathanaracanayā śuddhabodhaprabodhā tanmadhye 
brahmanāḍī haramukhakuharādādidevāntarāsthā || 
vidyunmālāvilāsā munimanasi lasattanturūpā susūkṣmā 
śuddhajñānaprabodhā sakalasukhalasacchuddhabhāvādyabhāvā | 
brahmadvāraṃ tadāsye pravilasitasudhāsāraramyapradeśam 
granthisthānaṃ tadetadvadanamiti suṣumnākhyanāḍyā lapanti || 
athādhārapadmaṃ suṣumnāsyalagnaṃ dhvajādhogudorddhaṃ 
catuḥśoṇapatram | 
adhovaktramudyatsuvarṇābharamyairvakārādiśāntairyutaṃ 
vedavarṇaiḥ || amuṣmin dharāyāścatuṣkoṇacakraṃ 
samudbhāsiśūlāṣṭakairāvṛtaṃ tat | lasatpītavarṇaṃ 
taḍitkomalāṅgaṃ tadambhaḥsamāste dharāyāḥ svavījam || 
caturbāhubhūṣaṃ gajendrādiruḍhaṃ tadaṅke 
navīnārkatulyaprakāśam | śiśuḥ sṛṣṭikārī 
lasadvedabāhurmukhāmbhojalakṣmīścaturbhāgavedaḥ || vasedatra 
devī ca ḍākinyābhikhyā lasadvedabāhūjjvalā raktanetrā | 
samānoditānekasūryaprakāśā prakāśaṃ vahantī sadā 
śuddhabuddheḥ || vajrākhyā vaktradeśādvilasati satataṃ 
karṇikāmadhyasaṃsthaṃ koṇaṃ tat traipurākhyaṃ taḍidiva vilasat 
komalaṃ kāmarūpam | kandarpo nāma vāyurvilasati satataṃ tasya 
madhye samantāt jīveśo bandhujīvaprakaramapi hasan koṭisūrya 
prakāśaḥ || tanmadhye liṅgarūpau drutakamalakaṇākomalaḥ 
paścimāsyo jñānadhyānaprakāśaḥ prathamakiśalayākārarūpaḥ 
svayambhūḥ | 
udyatpūrṇenduviśvaprakarakaracayasnigdhasantānahāsī kāśīvāsī 
vilāsī vilasati saridāvartarūpaprakāśī || tasyorddhe 
viṣatantuśokavilasatsūkṣmā jaganmohinī brahmadvāramukhaṃ 
mukhena madhuraṃ sañchādayantī svayam | śaṅkhāvarttanibhā 
navīnacapalāmālāvilāsāspadā suptā sarpasamā śivopari 
lasatsārddhatrivṛttāvṛtiḥ || kūjantī kulakuṇḍalī ca madhuraṃ 
mattālimālāsphuṭaṃ vācaṃ 
komalavākyabandharacanābhedāvibhedakramaiḥ | 
śvāsocchāsavibhañjanena jagatāṃ jīvo yayā dhāryate sā 
mūlāmbujagahvare vilasati proddāmadīptāvaliḥ || tanmadhye paramā 
kalātikuśalā sūkṣmātisūkṣmā parā nityānandaparasparāticapalā 
mālālasaddīdhitiḥ | brahmāṇḍādikaṭāhameva sakalaṃ yadbhāsayā 
bhāsate seyaṃ śrīparameśvarī vijayate nityaprabodhodayā || 
dhyātvaitanmūlapadmāntarapathavilasatkoṭisūryaprakāśam 
vācāmīśo narendraḥ sa bhavati sahasā sarvavidyāvinodī | ārogyaṃ 
tasya nityaṃ niravadhi sa mahānandaṃcittātmarātmā 
vākyairvākyaprabandhaiḥ | sakalasuragurun sevate śuddhaśīlaḥ | 
sindūrapūrarucirāruṇapadmamanyat sauṣumnamadhyaghaṭitaṃ 
dhvajamūladeśe | aṅgacchadaiḥ parivṛtaṃ taḍidābhavarṇairvādyaiḥ 
savindulasitaiśca purandarāntaiḥ || tasyāntare 
pravilasadviyadaprakāśamambhojamaṇḍalamatho varuṇasya tasya | 
arddhendurūpalasitaṃ śaradinduśubhraṃ vaṅkāravījamamalaṃ 
makarādhirūḍhaḥ || tasyāṅkadeśaśayito harireva 
pāyānnīlaprakāśaruciraśriyamādadhānaḥ | pītāmbaraḥ 
prathamayauvanagarbhadhārī śrīvatsakaustubhadharo dhṛtavedabāhuḥ || 

p. 864) atraiva bhāti satataṃ khalu rākiṇī sā nīlāmbu 
jodarasahodarakāntiśobhā | 
nānāyudhodyatalasatsatatāṅgalakṣmīrdivyāmbarābharaṇabhūṣita-
mattacittā ||
 
atha jñānapraśaṃsā || nigamakalpadrume tṛtīyapaṭale || 
jñānena labhate mokṣaṃ jñānena pāpanāśanam | jñānena 
vīrakarmā ca jñānena paśubhāvanam | jñānena divyabhāvī ca 
tasmājjñānaṃ viśiṣyate | jñānāt pavitraṃ sarvañca jñānenaiva 
pavitrakam | yathāgninā dahet sarvaṃ kāṣṭhagulmalatādikam | tathā 
jñānena dahyante sarvakarmaphalāni ca | jñānañca dvividhañcaiva 
bhedābhedavibhedataḥ | bhedajñānena yatkāryaṃ puṇyapāpaṃ yuge 
yuge | abhedajñānamānena pūrvakarmādi dahyate | aparañca na 
bhūyeta abhedī muktitāṃ vrajet | anyathā lakṣayugairvā na muktiṃ 
svargatādikam | labhate sarvadā śambhorbhedajñānī na saṃśayaḥ | 
abhedasya kramaḥ kutra niścitaḥ kena karmaṇā | bhedaḥ paśūnāṃ 
bhedo hi divyabhāva udāhṛtaḥ | bhedābhedavido vīraḥ krame 
caivākramaṃ nyaset | krameṇa yadi kurvīta kāmanārahitaścaran | tadā 
kaivalyaphaladaṃ kāryaṃ jñeyaṃ tathākramam | kāmanā yadi 
badhnīyāt parā vāpyaparāpi vā | krameṇāpyapakṛṣṭā sā 
paśupāśaniyantritā | ekaṃ dvayaṃ vyutkramaṃ vā śodhitañca 
prayatnataḥ | bhedavaryā naro mokṣī bhedabuddhyā svakarmabhāk || 

athādvaitajñānam || muṇḍamālātantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 
yāvannāmātmabhāvaśca tāvadevaṃ pṛthagvidham | tāvat kriyā 
pṛthagbhāvā tāvannānāvidhā matā | tāvadbhinnāśca devāśca 
brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ | gaṇeśaśca dineśaśca vahnirvaruṇa eva ca 
| kuberaścāpi digpālā etatsarvaṃ pṛthak pṛthak | tāvannānāvidhā 
ceṣṭā strīpuṃnapuṃsakātmikā | tāvadvilvadalaṃ bhinnaṃ deveśi ! 
tulasīdalāt | tāvajjavādroṇakṛṣṇā karavīrāṇi bhūtale | vibhinnāni 
ca deveśi ! satyaṃ vai tulasīdalāt | tāvaddivyaśca vīraśca tāvattu

p. 865) paśubhāvakaḥ | tāvattantre bhedabuddhistāvaddeve pṛthak 
kriyā | harau hare bhavedbuddhirjāyate jagadambike ! | karālavadanā 
kālī śrīmadekajaṭā śivā | ṣoḍaśī bhairavī chinnā bhinnā ca 
bhuvaneśvarī | chinnā bhinnā tvannapūrṇā bhinnā ca vagalāmukhī | 
mātaṅgī kamalā bhinnā bhinnā vāṇī ca rādhikā | bhinnā ceṣṭā 
kriyā bhinnā bhinna ācārasaṃgrahaḥ | yāvannaikyaṃ pādapadme 
bhavānyā naiva jāyate | advaite tāriṇīpādapadme paramapāvane | 
jñānasāre samutpanne hṛtpadmanilaye tathā | aikyaṃ bhavati 
cārvaṅgi ! sarvajīveṣu śaṅkari ! | nanu jīveṣvaikyamiti kathamuktaṃ 
yāvataikyajñānasamutpattau satyāṃ 
śivaśaktisarvajīvānāmevaikyaṃ tāriṇīpādapadme ityapi na 
vaktumucitaṃ tadānīṃ sevyasevakatvabhāvāditi cet satyam | 
mumukṣūn prati jñānamiśrabhaktyupadeśārthamidamuktam | ata eva 
bhaktiravyabhicariṇītyagre vakṣyati | niścitaikajñānaṃ muktaṃ prati 
nopadeśāpekṣā tasya sarvathā niścintatvāditi || na ca pāpaṃ na vā 
puṇyaṃ na svargo narakaṃ na ca | na sukhaṃ nāpi duḥkhañca na 
rogebhyo bhayaṃ tathā | na bhayaṃ nāpi śokaśca sarvaṃ 
brahmamayaṃ jagat | brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā vaidyajā 
śūdrajāntyajā | tathaiva tāriṇī vidyā yathā vidyā tathā tathā | 
evaṃ jñānaṃ maheśāni ! yadā vai jāyate priye ! | tadaiva vidyā 
deveśi ! vidyāvidyāvirodhinī | jāyate nātra sandeho brahmānandamayo 
bhavet | advaitañca guṇātītaṃ nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ paraḥ | 
paramānandasaṃyukto muktiṃ yāsyati niścitam | iti satyaṃ punaḥ 
satyaṃ satyaṃ vacmi varānane ! | tattvajñānāt paraṃ nāsti nāsti 
devaḥ sadāśivāt | tadaivācirakālena so'haṃjñānaṃ prajāyate | 
nānātvabuddhiṃ kṛtvā vai sāttvikīṃ paramātmikām | gṛhītvā ca 
varārohe ! jāyate paramārthavit | jñānāt parataraṃ nāsti nāsti nāsti 

p. 866) varānane ! | labdhvā hi tattvaṃ paramaṃ mucyate dehabandhanāt 
|| tathā || nānātantre pṛthak ceṣṭā mayoktā girinandini ! | 
aikyajñānaṃ yadā devi ! tadā siddhimavāpnuyāt | sthāvare jaṅgame 
caiva yadā tulyaṃ mano bhavet | kiṃ na sidhyati viśveśi ! paratreha ca 
pārvati ! | evaṃ bhuktiśca muktiśca jāyate jagadambike ! | 
tattvajñānaṃ tathaivānte ato nirvāṇamāpnuyāt | ekādaśapaṭale || 
yāvannānātvameva syāt tāvadbhinnaṃ mahītale | tāvajjātiśca 
gotrañca tāvannāma pṛthagvidham | tāvalliṅgaṃ pṛthak sarvaṃ 
varṇānāṃ pṛthageva hi | tāvanmitravipakṣau ca tāvat 
kalatrabāndhavau | tāvat pṛthagvidhā pūjā mantrayantrārthanādibhiḥ 
| tāvat puṇyaṃ tāvadeva pāpaṃ puṇyavivarddhakam | tāvat 
tvañcāpyahamayamiyañca jāyate priye ! | yāvanna jāyate caṇḍi ! 
vidyāvidyāvirodhinī | avidyānāśinī vidyā vidyāvidyāvivarddhinī | 
yā tāriṇī mahāvidyā vidyāvidyāvirodhinī | ata eva varārohe ! 
vidyāmutpādya bhūtale | nirvāṇaṃ mokṣamāpnoti satyaṃ 
tripurasundari ! | śrīdurgācaraṇāmbhoje bhaktiravyabhicāriṇī | tadaiva 
jāyate brahmajñānaṃ brahmasudurlabham | brahmā viṣṇuśca 
rudraśca vāsavādyā divaukasaḥ | bhairavāścaiva gandharvā 
vidyābhyāsasamutsukāḥ | brahmavidyāsamāvidyā 
brahmavidyāsamā kriyā | brahmavidyāsamaṃ jñānaṃ nāsti nāsti 
kadācana ||

jagati cātmadvayaṃ pravṛttinivṛttibhedena karmakāṇḍaṃ 
tatsanyāsaśca | tathā ca || vājasaneyasaṃhitopaniṣadaḥ | 
pūjyapādaśaṅkarācāryakṛtabhāṣyadhṛtataittarīyake | imau dvāveva 
panthānāvanuniṣkrāntatarau bhavataḥ | kriyāpathaścaiva purastāt 
tayoḥ sannyāsa evetyavocaditi | dvāvimāvatha panthānau yatra vedāḥ 
pratiṣṭhitāḥ | pravṛttilakṣaṇo dharmo nivṛttau ca vibhāṣitaḥ || iti | 

p. 867) ataḥ kāmyakāṇḍe bhaṇḍikāṇḍe ca pravṛttimārgiṇaḥ 
puruṣārthasiddhaye saguṇasthūlamūrttīśvaropāsanā bahuvidhā 
nirūpitā | idānīntu nivṛttimārgiṇāṃ 
sūkṣmatamanirguṇeśvaropāsanārthamātmatattvamucyate | yathā 
mahānirvāṇatantre caturddaśollāse || brahmāditṛṇaparyantaṃ 
māyayā kalpitaṃ jagat | satyamekaṃ paraṃ brahma viditvaivaṃ sukhī 
bhavet | vihāya nāmarūpāṇi nitye brahmaṇi niṣkale | pariniścitatattvo 
yaḥ sa muktaḥ karmabandhanāt | na 
muktirjapanāddhomādupavāsaśatairapi | brahmaivāhamiti jñātvā 
mukto bhavati dehabhṛt || kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe'pi || kṣaṇaṃ 
brahmāhasmīti yaḥ kuryādātmacintanam | koṭijanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ 
tatkṣaṇāttasya naśyati || mahānirvāṇatantre || ātmā sākṣī vibhuḥ 
pūrṇaḥ satyo'dvaitaḥ parātparaḥ | dehastho'pi na dehastho 
jñātvaivaṃ muktibhāg bhavet || 

nanvātmā yadi sākṣitvenābhimatastadā ya upādhikṛtaḥ 
saṃsārī vidyāvidyayoradhikṛtaḥ saṃsāragamanāya mokṣagamanāya 
ca tasya sādharaṇo rathaḥ kathaṃ kalpitaḥ | yathā kaṭhopaniṣadi 
tṛtīyavallyām || ātmānaṃ rathinaṃ viddhi śarīraṃ rathameva tu | 
buddhintu sārathiṃ viddhi manaḥ pragrahameva ca | indriyāṇi 
hayānāhurviṣayāṃsteṣu gocarān | ātmendriyamanoyuktaṃ 
bhoktetyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || iti | etāṃ vyācakṣante bhagavantaḥ 
śaṅkarācāryāḥ | rathinaṃ rathasvāminaṃ viddhi jānīhi | 
rathabaddhahayasthānīyairindriyairākṛṣyamāṇatvāt śarīrasya 
rathatvam | buddhintu adhyavasāyalakṣaṇāṃ sārathiṃ viddhi 
buddhinetṛpradhānatvāt śarīrasya sārathinetṛpradhāna iva rathaḥ | 
manaḥ saṅkalpavikalpādilakṣaṇaṃ pragrahaṃ raśanāṃ viddhi 
manasā hi gṛhītāni śrotrādīni karaṇāni pravarttante raśanāya vā | 
indriyāṇi cakṣurādīni hayānāhuḥ | rathakalpanākuśalāḥ 
śarīrarathākarṣaṇasāmānyāt teṣu indriyeṣu parikalpiteṣu gocarān 

p. 868) mārgān rūpādīn viṣayān viddhi | ātmendriyamanoyuktaṃ 
śarīrendriyamanobhiḥ sahitaṃ saṃyuktamātmānaṃ bhokteti 
saṃsārītyāhurmanīṣiṇo vevekinaḥ | nahi kevalasyātmano 
bhoktṛtvamasti budhyādyupādhikṛtameva tasya bhoktṛtvam | satyaṃ 
dehastho'pi na dehastha ityanenātmano nirliptatvaṃ darśinamato 
nirliptasya sākṣitvaṃ vinā bhoktṛtvaṃ ghaṭate ityabhiprāyeṇātmā 
sākṣītyuktam | tarhi sarvatra śarīreṣu 
ekasyaivātmano'vasthānanirdiṣṭatvaṃ śiṣṭatvaṃ vā kasya kena vā 
bhavatītyapekṣāyāmuttaramāha saivopaniṣat | yastvavijñānavān 
bhavatyayuktena manasā tadā | tasyendriyāṇyavaśyāni duṣṭāśvā 
iva sāratheḥ | yastu vijñānavān bhavati yuktena manasā sadā | 
tasyendriyāṇi vaśyāni sadaśvā iva sāratheḥ | atastarhi 
caitanyarūpasyātmanaḥ sākṣitvamātramabhimatañcettadā mokṣaḥ 
saṃsāro vā kasyeti sandehaṃ nirākaroti saiva | yastu vijñānavān 
bhavati samanaskaḥ sadā śuciḥ | sa tu tatpadamāpnoti yasmādbhūyo na 
jāyate | yastvavijñānavān bhavatyamanaskaḥ sadā'śuciḥ | na sa 
tatpadamāpnoti saṃsārañcādhigacchati | bālakrīḍitavat sarvaṃ 
rūpanāmādikalpanam | vihāya brahmaniṣṭho yaḥ sa mukto nātra 
saṃśayaḥ | manasā kalpitā mūrttirnṛṇāṃ cenmokṣabādhinī | 
svapnalabdhena rājyena rājāno mānavāstathā | 
mṛcchilādhātudārvādimūrttāvīśvarabuddhayaḥ | kliśyantastapasā 
jñānaṃ vinā mokṣaṃ na yānti vai | āhārasaṃyamakliṣṭā 
yatheṣṭāhāratundilāḥ | brahmajñānavihīnāścenniṣkṛtiṃ te vrajanti 
kim | vāyuparṇakaṇātoyavratīno mokṣabhāginaḥ | santi cet pannagā 
muktāḥ paśupakṣijalecarāḥ | uttamo brahmasadbhāvo 
dhyānabhāvastu madhyamaḥ | stutirjapo'dhamo bhāvo vahiḥ 
pūjā'dhamādhamā | yoge jīvātmanoraikyaṃ pūjanaṃ sevakeśayoḥ 
|
p. 869) sarvaṃ brahmeti viduṣo na yogo na ca pūjanam || nanu yogā 
bahuvidhāstūktā eva kathaṃ jīvātmanoraikyamātraṃ yoga ityucyate 
iti cet satyam | te yogāḥ prāṇāyāmādikarmarūpatayā gauṇā eva 
mukhyayogastu jīvātmanoraikyameva | tathā ca kulārṇve || na 
padmāsanato yogo na nāsāgranirīkṣaṇāt | aikyaṃ 
jīvātmanorāhuryogaṃ yogaviśāradāḥ || iti || mahānirvāṇe || 
brahmajñānaṃ paraṃ jñānaṃ yasya citte virājate | kiṃ tasya 
japayajñādyaistapobhirniyamavrataiḥ | satyaṃ 
vijñānamānandamekaṃ brahmeti paśyataḥ | svabhāvād 
brahmabhūtasya kiṃ pūjādhyānadhāraṇāḥ | na pāpaṃ naiva 
sukṛtaṃ na svargo na punarbhavaḥ | nāpi dhyeyo na vā dhyātā sarvaṃ 
brahmeti jānataḥ | ayamātmā sadā mukto nirliptaḥ sarvavastuṣu | kiṃ 
tasya bandhanaṃ kasmānmuktimicchanti durdhiyaḥ | svayaṃ viracitaṃ 
viśvamavitarkyaṃ surairapi | rājate tatra tatraiva hyapraviṣṭaḥ 
praviṣṭavat | vahirantaryathākāśaṃ vastūnāmeva sarvaśaḥ | tathaiva 
bhāti tadrūpo hyātmā sākṣī svarūpataḥ | na bālyamasti ca jarā 
nātmano yauvanaṃ januḥ | sadaikarūpaścinmātro vikāraparivarjitaḥ | 
janmayauvanavārddhakyaṃ dehasyaiva na cātmanaḥ | paśyanto'pi na 
paśyanti māyāprākṛtabuddhayaḥ | yathā śarāvatoyasthaṃ raviṃ 
paśyatyanekadhā | tathaiva māyayā dehe bahudhātmānamīkṣate || 
gandharva tantre pañcamapaṭale || nityaḥ sarvagato hyātmā kūṭastho 
doṣavarjitaḥ | ekaḥ sambhidyate bhrāntyā mayayā na svarūpataḥ | 
tasmāddvaitañca me nāsti na prapañco na saṃsmṛtiḥ | yathākāśo 
ghaṭākāśo mahākāśa itīritaḥ | tathā bhrāntirdvidhā proktā 
hyātmā jīveśvarātmanā | nāhaṃ deho na ca prāṇo nendriyāṇi 
tathaiva ca | na mano naiva buddhiśca naiva cittamahaṅkṛtiḥ | nāhaṃ

p. 870) pṛthvī na salilaṃ na ca vahnistathānilaḥ | na cākāśo na 
śabdaśca na ca sparśastathā rasaḥ | na hi gandho na rūpañca na 
māyāhaṃ na saṃsṛtiḥ | sadā sākṣisvarūpātmā brahma caivāsmi 
kevalam | so'haṃ brahma na saṃsārī na matto'nyat kadācana | iti 
vidyāt svamātmānaṃ samādhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ | samādhau 
śuddharūpo'sau turīyasvara eva hi | turīyāṃśaḥ parā 
śaktirvindvātmā brahmakevalam | jīvamīśvarabhāvena vidyāt 
so'hamatidhruvam | yathā phenataraṅgādi samudrādutthitaṃ punaḥ | 
samudre līyate tadvajjagadātmani līyate | yasyaivaṃ paramātmā na 
pṛthagbhūtaḥ prakāśitaḥ | sa yāti paramaṃ bhāvaṃ svayaṃ 
sākṣāt parāmṛtam | yadā manasi caitanyaṃ bhāti sarvatragaṃ sadā | 
yogibhyo vyavadhānena tadā sampadyate svayam | yadā sarvāṇi 
bhūtāni svātmanyevābhipaśyati | sarvabhūteṣu cātmānaṃ brahma 
sampadyate tadā | yadā sarvāṇi bhūtāni samādhistho na paśyati | 
ekībhūtaḥ pareṇāsau tadā bhavati kevalam | tadā 
janmajarāduḥkhavyādhīnāmeva bheṣajam | kevalaṃ 
brahmavijñānaṃ jāyate'sau tadā śivaḥ | tasmādvijñānato 
muktirnānyathā bhāvakoṭibhiḥ | mantrauṣadhibalairyadvajīryate 
bhakṣitaṃ viṣam | tadvat sarvāṇi karmāṇi jīryanti jñāninaḥ kṣaṇāt 
| dehābhimāne galite vidite paramātmani | yatra yatra mano yāti tatra 
yatra samādhayaḥ | ahaṃ brahma na cānyo'smin mukto'hamiti 
bhāvayet | saccidānandarūpo'haṃ nityamuktasvabhāvavān | evaṃ 
samādhiyukto yaḥ sa samādhāya kalpyate | sadāśivo'hamityuktvā 
svecchayā vihared yatiḥ | lipyate na sa pāpena badhyate na ca karmaṇā | 
yathāgnirvidrutasvarṇamālinyaṃ dahati kṣaṇāt | tathā 
brahmārpitātmāsau sarvakarmāṇi nāśayet | ātmasthāṃ devatāṃ 
tyaktvā vahirdevo vicintyate | karasthaṃ kaustubhaṃ tyaktvā bhramate 
kācatṛṣṇayā ||

p. 871) muṇḍamālātantre ekādaśapaṭale || jñānañca dvividhaṃ 
jñeyaṃ durjñeyaṃ manasāpi ca | jñānaṃ paramatattvākhyaṃ 
jñānaṃ jñeyārthasādhanam | anyadvibhrāntiviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ 
sādhāraṇaṃ matam | evañca trividhaṃ śeṣamadhamaṃ tattvavarjitam 
| tattvajñānaṃ varārohe ! yogīndrāṇāñca durlabham | vinā 
tattvaparijñānād viphalaṃ pūjanaṃ japaḥ | eko devaśca eko'haṃ 
nātmā bhinnaḥ śarīrataḥ | ghaṭātpaṭāt maheśāni ! 
kālacakrānmahīruhāt | evaṃ jñānaṃ tattvamayaṃ tadā 
mukto'cireṇa tu | nānākāraṇamevāsya pūjanaṃ dhyānameva ca | 
sevanañcaiva tīrthānāṃ śaraṇaṃ tāriṇīpadam | smaraṇaṃ 
manurājasya bhramaṇaṃ vai kulācale | satsaṅgaḥ sevanaṃ viṣṇoḥ 
śaṅkarasyāpi pūjanam | kālikāpādayugalabhajanaṃ 
jñānakāraṇam || kriyāyā api muktisādhanatvaṃ darśayati 
dvitīyapaṭale | bhaktyā ca kriyayā caṇḍi ! pūjayedyastu kālikām | 
jīvaḥ śivatvaṃ labhate satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || tathā || sadā 
kriyā prakarttavyā kriyayā siddhimuttamām | prāpnoti 
sādhakaśreṣṭhaścāt eva na ca tyajet | etattu vacanaṃ 
gṛhasthaparamagrespaṣṭībhaviṣyati prathamapaṭale || kva yamaḥ kva tapo 
viṣṇuḥ kva kaliḥ karmahiṃsakaḥ | sarvañca mānasaṃ kleśaṃ sadā 
satyaṃ vibhāvayet | evaṃ vidhānamāsādya prajapedbhāvayet sudhīḥ | 
so'cireṇaiva kālena śivatvaṃ labhate janaḥ || 
śivajīvayorupādhimātrabheda iti yat prāguktaṃ tadeva 
spaṣṭīkariṣyate yathā tatraiva || jīvaḥ śivaḥ śivo devaḥ sa jīvaḥ 
kevalaḥ śivaḥ | pāśabaddho bhavejjīvaḥ pāśamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ | 
pāśastu kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe || ghṛṇā lajjā bhayaṃ śoko 
jugupsā ceti pañcamī | kulaṃ śīlaṃ tathā jātiraṣṭau pāśāḥ 
prakīrttitāḥ | pāśabaddho bhavejjīvaḥ pāśamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 
muṇḍamālātantre tṛtīyapaṭale || tuṣeṇa baddho vrīhi syāttuṣābhāve 
tu taṇḍulaḥ | karmabaddho bhavejjīvaḥ karmamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ ||

p. 872) tathā | toye kṣiptaṃ haviryadvattatra kṣiptaṃ na pūrvavat | 
pṛthattayā guṇebhyaḥ syāttadvadātmā ihocyate | kṣīreṇa sahitaṃ 
toyaṃ kṣīrameva yathā bhavet | aviśeṣo 
bhavettadvajjīvātmaparamātmanoḥ | yathā candrārkayorvimbau 
jalapūrṇaghaṭeṣu ca | ghaṭe bhagne jaleṣveva pralīnau candrasūryakau | 
na jāyate na mriyate ātmā tu paramaḥ śivaḥ | ātmajñānaṃ 
samāsādya saṃsārārṇavadustaram | taratyeva sadā caṇḍi ! jīvaḥ 
śivatvamālabhet | kaṭhopaniṣadi dvitīyavallyāmapi || na jāyate mriyate 
vā vipaścinnāyaṃ kutaścinna babhūva kaścit | ajo nityaḥ 
śāśvato'yaṃ purāṇo na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre || 
bhagavadgītāyāmapyetat | kevalaṃ vipaścidityatra kadāciditi nāyaṃ 
bhūtvā bhavitā vā na bhūya iti ca dvitīyacaraṇe viśeṣaḥ || tathā 
ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || jīvaḥ śivastu vijñeyo viśveśaḥ sarvadāratiḥ | 
brahmatattvaṃ varārohe ! devānāmapi durlabham || tathā || jīvaḥ 
śivatvaṃ labhate jñānāttu varavarṇini ! | vicitraṃ gurupādābjaṃ 
pārvatyāḥ śaṅkarasya ca | bhajedekavidhānena jīvanmuktaḥ sa eva hi | 
piṇḍe yuktāḥ pade yuktā rūpe yuktā varānane ! | guṇātīteṣu ye 
yuktāste muktā nātra saṃśayaḥ | na piṇḍaṃ na padaṃ rūpaṃ na 
jānāmi surottama ! | kathyatāmityādidevīpraśnānantaram ||
 
śiva uvāca ||

piṇḍaṃ kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ pado'haṃ samprarttitaḥ | rūpañcāpi 
varārohe ! dhyānameva na saṃśayaḥ | mahākuṇḍalinīṃ śaktiṃ ye 
bhajanti bhujaṅginīm | sa kṛtārthaḥ sa dhanyaśca sa divyo vīrasattamaḥ 
| tathā || ye divyāḥ sādhakendrāśca ye vīrāḥ sādhakottamāḥ 
paśavaḥ paśavo jñeyāḥ sarvaśāstrārthakovidāḥ | sādhako 
muktimāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ varānane ! | śṛṇu devi ! jagaddhātri !

p. 873) sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalam | tantraṃ śṛṇuyāddeveśi ! 
brahmanirvāṇamāpnuyāt | tantraśravaṇe kathaṃ brahmāptiriti 
nāśaṅkanīyam || paramārthasvarūpāmnāyāhvayatantraśāstrasya 
tattvajñānadarśakatvena brahmavidyātvādvedādīnāṃ na tathātvam 
| tathā ca muṇḍakopaniṣat | dve vidye veditavye iti isma yad brahmavido 
vadanti parā caivāparā ca | tatra parā ṛgvedo yajurvedaḥ 
sāmavedo'tharvavedaḥ śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ niruktaṃ chando 
jyotiṣamiti | athāparā yayā tadakṣaramadhigamyate | nanu 
ṛgvedādivāhyādvedādivāhyācet parā vidyā tarhi kathamupaniṣat 
parā vidyā syānmokṣasādhanañca | yā vedavāhyāḥ smṛtaya iti 
smaranti kudṛṣṭitvānniṣphalatvādanādeyatvāt | upaniṣadaśca 
ṛgvedādivāhyatvaṃ syāt | ṛgvedāditve ca 
pṛthakkaraṇamanarthakamapyapare'pi na vedyaviṣayavijñānasya 
vivakṣitatvādupaniṣadvedyākṣaraviṣayaṃ hi vedyākṣaraviṣayaṃ hi 
vijñānamiha parāvidyeti prādhānyena vivakṣitam | 
nopaniyacchabdarāśiḥ | vedaśabdena tu sarvatra rāśirvivakṣitaḥ | 
śabdarāśyadhigame'pi yattu āntaramantareṇa 
gurvabhigamanādilakṣaṇaṃ vairāgyañca nākṣarādigamaḥ 
sambhavatīti pṛthakkaraṇaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ parā vidyeti || 

brahmajñānapraśaṃsāmāha || gāyatrītantre prathamapaṭale || 
tāvadvidyā bhavet sarvā yāvajjñānaṃ na jāyate | 
brahmajñānapadaṃ jñātvā sarvāścaiva tirohitāḥ | 
vedaśāstrapurāṇāni sāmānyagaṇikā iva | yā punaḥ śāmbhavī 
vidyā guptā kulabadhūriva | dehasthāḥ sarvavidyāśca dehasthāḥ 
sarvadevatāḥ | dehasthasarvatīrthāṇi labhante guruvākyataḥ | 
adhyātmavidyā hi nṛṇāṃ saukhyamokṣakarī bhavet | karmākarma 
tathā jāpyametat sarvaṃ nivarttate || ata eva kulārṇave || abhyasya

p. 874) sarvaśāstrāṇi tattvaṃ jñātvā prayatnataḥ | palālamiva 
thānyārthī sarvaśāstrāṇi santyajet || gītāyāmapi || traiguṇyaviṣayā 
vedā nistraiguṇyo bhavārjuna ityādi | gāyatrītantre || kāṣṭhamadhye 
yathā vahniḥ puṣpe gandhaḥ payo ghṛtam | dehamadhye tathā devaḥ 
puṇyapāpavivarjitaḥ | ātmatattvajñānasya 
bahujanmārjitapuṇyaphalatvaṃ darśayati kaṭhopaniṣadi dvitīyavallī | 
śravaṇāyāpi bahubhiryo na labhyaḥ śṛṇvanto'pi bahavo yaṃ na 
viduḥ | āścaryo'sya vaktā kuśalo'sya labdhā āścaryo jñātā 
kuśalānuśiṣṭaḥ | na nareṇāvareṇa prokta eṣa suvijñeyo bahudhā 
cintyamānaḥ | ananyaprokte na gīratra nāstyaṇīyān 
hyatarkyamanupramāṇāt || naiṣā tarkeṇa matirāpaneyā 
proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭhaḥ | yāṃ tvamāpaḥ 
satyavṛtirvatāsi tvādṛṅno bhūyānnāciketaḥ ! praṣṭā || 
jānāmyahaṃ śevadhirityanityam nahyadhruvaiḥ prāpyate dhruvaṃ tat | 
tato mayā nāciketaścito'gniranityairdravyaiḥ prāptavānasmi nityam || 
kāmasyāptiṃ jagataḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ kratoranantyamabhayasya pāram | 
stomamahadurugāyaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ dṛṣṭvā dhṛtyā dhīro 
nāciketo'tyasrākṣīḥ || taṃ durddarśaṃ gūḍhamanupraviṣṭaṃ 
guhāhitaṃ gahvareṣṭhaṃ purāṇam | adhyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ 
matvā dhīro harṣaśokau jahāti || itacchrutvā samparigṛhya marttyaḥ 
pragṛhya dharmyamanumetamāpya | sa modate modanīyaṃ hi labdhvā 
vivṛtaṃ padma naciketasaṃmanye || anyatra 
dharmādanyatrādharmādanyatrāsmāt kṛtākṛtāt | anyatra bhūtācca 
bhavyācca yattat paśyasi tadvada || sarve vedā yatpadamāmananti 
tapāṃsi sarvāṇi ca yadvadanti | yadicchanto brahmacaryaṃ caranti tatte 
padaṃ saṅgraheṇa bravīmyom || ityetat || nityātantre aṣṭamapaṭale || 

devyuvāca || 

p. 875) devadeva ! mahādeva ! jagannistāratāraka ! | paramātmeti kiṃ 
nāma kathayasva maheśvara ! |

īśvara uvāca ||

sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā bhadre ! kathayāmi śṛṇusva tat | yasya 
vijñānamātreṇa jīvanmukto bhavennaraḥ | virajākhye maheśāni ! 
mahāpadmavarāṭake | vaikharīparamāyogāt puṣkare tīrtharājake | 
nirākāraṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ paraṃbrahmasvarūpakam | sarvajño nirguṇo 
nityo'dvayaḥ sarvapratiṣṭhitaḥ | mahākuṇḍalinīyogāt yadā tejomayo 
bhavet | tadaiva cintanīyo'sau paramātmā sureśvari ! | jīvādyāḥ 
parameśāni ! tadaṃśāḥ parikīrttitāḥ || 

mahānirvāṇatantre dvitīyollāse ||

devyuvāca ||

kathitaṃ yat paraṃ brahma parameśaṃ parāt param | yasyopāsanato 
marttyo bhuktiṃ muktiñca vindati | kenopāyena bhagavan ! paramātmā 
prasīdati | kiṃ tasya sādhanaṃ deva ! mantraḥ ko vā prakīrttitaḥ | kiṃ 
dhyānaṃ kiṃ vidhānañca pareśasya parātmanaḥ | tattvena 
śrotumicchāmi kṛpayā kathaya prabho ! ||

sadāśiva uvāca |

tava snehena vakṣyāmi mama prāṇādhikaṃ param | jñeyaṃ bhavati 
tadbrahma saccidviśvamayaṃ param | yathātathasvarūpeṇa 
lakṣaṇairvā maheśvari ! | sattāmātraṃ 
nirviśeṣamavāṅmanasagocaram | asattrilokīsadbhāvaṃ svarūpaṃ 
brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam | samādhiyogaistadvedyaṃ sarvatra samadṛṣṭibhiḥ | 
dvandvātītairnirvikalpairdehātmādhyāsavarjitaiḥ | yato viśvaṃ 
sadudbhūtaṃ yena jātañca tiṣṭhati | yasmin sarvāṇi līyante jñeyaṃ 
tadbrahma lakṣaṇaiḥ || brahmajñānantu sannyāsināmeva na 
gṛhasthānām | tathā ca muṇḍakabhāṣye prathame 
śrīśaṅkarācāryapādāḥ || jñānamātre yadyapi 
sarvāśramiṇāmadhikārastathāpi sannyāsaniṣṭhā eva 
brahmavidyāmokṣasādhanaṃ na karmasahiteti bhaikṣacaryāṃ carantaḥ 
sannyāsayogāditi ca bruvan darśayati | vidyākarmavirodhācca nahi

p. 876) brahmātmaikatvadarśanena saha kama svapne'pisampādayituṃ 
śakyam | vidyāyāḥ kālaviśeṣābhāvānniyatanimittatvāt 
kālakarmasaṅkocānupapattiḥ | yattu gṛhastheṣu 
brahmavidyāpradānakarttṛtvādiliṅgaṃ na tat sthitaṃ nyāyaṃ 
bādhitumutsahate | nahi vidhiśatenāpi tamaḥprakāśayorekatra 
sambhavaḥ śakyate karttumityantenāhuḥ | tarhi vakṣyamāṇamasmin 
dharme syādityupakramya mātāpitroḥ sevanatatpara iti | kathamuktaṃ 
gṛhasthasyaiva mātṛpitṛsevanasambhavāditi cenna | asmin dharme 
ārabhyamāṇe iti vaktavyamanyathā paropakāranirata iti na saṅgacchate 
| brahmaikaniṣṭhasyātmaparajñānābhāvāditi || brahmaṇo 
jagatsṛṣṭimāha muṇḍakopaniṣat || yathorṇanābhiḥ sṛjate gṛhṇate ca 
yathā pṛthivyāmoṣadhayaḥ sambhavanti | yatheśataḥ puruṣāt 
keśalomāni tathā kṣarāt sambhavatīha viśvam || 
ātmajñānaprakārastu tṛtīyamuṇḍakopaniṣat || sa vedaitat paraṃ 
brahmadhāma yatra viśvaṃ nihitaṃ bhāti śubhram | upāsate puruṣaṃ 
ye hyakāmāste śubhrametadativarttanti dhīrāḥ || kāmān yaḥ 
kāmayate manyamānaḥ sa kāmabhirjāyate tatra tatra | 
paryāptakāmasya kṛtātmanastu ihaiva sarve pravilīyanti kāmāḥ || 
nāyamātmā pravacanena labhyo na medhayā na bahunā śrutena | 
yamevaiṣa vṛṇute tena labhyastasyaiva ātmā vṛṇute tanuṃ svām || 
nāyamātmā balahīnena labhyo na ca pramādāttapaso vāpyaliṅgāt | 
etairupāyairyatate yastu vidvāṃstasyaiṣa ātmā viśate brahmadhāma || 

brahmajñānaprāptiphalamāha va || samprāpyainamṛṣayo sai 
jñānatṛptāḥ kṛtātmāno vītarāgāḥ praśāntāḥ te sarvagaṃ 
sarvataḥ prāpya dhīrā yuktātmānaḥ sarvamevāviśanti || 

p. 877) vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ sannyāsayogādyatayaḥ 
śuddhasattvāḥ | te brahmalokeṣu parāntakāle parāmṛtāḥ 
parimucyanti sarve || sannyāsināṃ brahmavidyeti yat prāguktaṃ tadatra 
suspaṣṭīkṛtamupaniṣadeti || tathā || gatāḥ kalāḥ 
pañcadaśapratiṣṭhā devāśca sarve pratidevatāsu | karmāṇi 
vijñānamayaśca ātmā pare'vyaye sarva ekībhavanti || yathā nadyaḥ 
syandamānāḥ samudre'staṃ gacchanti nāmarūpe vihāya | tathā 
vidvānnāmarūpādvimuktaḥ parātparaṃ puruṣamupaiti divyam || sa yo 
havaitat paramaṃ brahma veda brahmaiva bhavati | nā syādbrahmavitkule 
bhavati tarati śokaṃ tarati pāpmānaṃ guhāgranthibhyo vimukto'mṛto 
bhavati || mahānirvāṇatantre'pi || bahujanmārjitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ 
sadgururyadi labhyate | tadā tadvaktrato labdhvā 
janmasāphalyamāpnuyāt | caturvargaṃ kare kṛtvā paratreha ca modate | 
sa dhanyaḥ sa kṛtārthaśca sa kṛtī sa ca dhārmikaḥ | sa snātaḥ 
sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ | sarvaśāstreṣu niṣṇātaḥ 
sarvalokapratiṣṭhitaḥ | yasya karṇapathopānte prāpto mantro 
mahāmaṇiḥ | dhanyā mātā pitā tasya pavitraṃ tat kulaṃ śive ! | 
pitarastasya santuṣṭā modante tridaśaiḥ saha | gāyanti gāyanīṃ 
gāthāṃ pulakāṅkitavigrahāḥ | asmatkule kulaśreṣṭho jāto 
brahmopadeśikaḥ | kimasmākaṃ gayāpiṇḍaiḥ kiṃ 
tīrthaśrāddhatarpaṇaiḥ | kiṃ dānaiḥ kiṃ japairhomaiḥ 
kimanyairbahusādhanaiḥ | vayamakṣayatṛptāḥ smaḥ satputtrasyāsya 
sādhanāt | śṛṇu devi ! jagadvandye ! satyaṃ satyaṃ mayocyate | 
varabrahmopāsakānāṃ kimanyaiḥ sādhanāntaraiḥ | 
mantragrahaṇamātreṇa dehī brahmamayī bhavet | brahmabhūtasya 
deveśi ! kimavāpyaṃ jagattraye | kiṃ kurvanti grahā ruṣṭā 
vetālāpaṭakādayaḥ | tasya darśanamātreṇa palāyante 
parāṅmukhāḥ | racito brahmamantreṇa prāvṛto brahmatejasā | 

p. 878) kiṃ bibheti gṛhādibhyo mārttaṇḍa iva cāparaḥ | taṃ dṛṣṭvā 
bhayamāpannāḥ siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā gajāḥ | vidravanti ca 
naśyanti pataṅgā iva pāvake | na tasya duritaṃ kiñcid 
brahmaniṣṭhasya dehinaḥ | tasya pūtasya śuddhasya sarvaprāṇihitasya 
ca | ko vopadravamanvicchedātmāpaghātakaṃ vinā | ye druhyanti 
khalāḥ pāpāḥ parabrahmopadeśinaḥ | svadrohaṃ te prakurvnti 
nātirikto yataḥ smṛtaḥ | sa tu sarvahitaḥ sādhuḥ sarveṣāṃ 
priyakārakaḥ | tasyāniṣṭe kṛte devi ! ko vā syānnirupadravaḥ | 
svarūpabuddhyā yadvedyaṃ tadeva lakṣaṇaiḥ śive ! | 
lakṣaṇairāptumicchūnāṃ vihitaṃ tatra sādhanam | tat sādhanaṃ 
pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāvahitā priye ! | tatrādau kathayāmyādye ! 
mantroddhāraṃ maheśituḥ | praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya 
saccitpadamudāharet | ekaṃ padānte brahmeti mantroddhāraḥ 
prakīrttitaḥ | sandhikrameṇa militaḥ saptārṇo'yaṃ manurmataḥ | 
tārahīnena deveśi ! ṣaḍvarṇo'yaṃ manurmataḥ || 
etanmantrapraśaṃsāpi tattaiva || sarvamantrottamaḥ 
sākṣāddharmārthakāmamokṣadaḥ | mātrasiddhādyapekṣāsti 
nārimitrādidūṣaṇam | na tithirna ca nakṣatraṃ na ca rāśigaṇastathā | 
kulākulānāṃ niyamo na saṃskāro'tra vidyate | sarvathā 
siddhimantro'yaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || mantrārthaṃ 
mantracaitanyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || mantrārthaṃ 
mantracaitanyaṃ yo na jānāti sādhaḥ | śatalakṣaprajapte'pi tasya 
mantro na sidhyati | ato'syārthañca caitanyaṃ kathayāmi śṛṇu priye ! | 
akāreṇa jagatpātā saṃharttāsyādukārakaḥ | makāreṇa jagatsraṣṭā 
praṇavārtha udāhṛtaḥ | svacchandena sadāsthāyi ciccaitanyaṃ 
prakīrttitam | ekamadvaitamīśāni ! vṛhatvādbrahmagīyate | 
mantrārthaḥ kathito devi ! sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhidaḥ | 
mantracaitanyametaddhi tadadhiṣṭhātṛdevatā | tajjñānaṃ parameśāni 
! bhaktānāṃ siddhidāyakam | tasyādhiṣṭhātṛ deveśi ! sarvavyāpi

p. 879) sanātanam | avitarkyaṃ nirākāraṃ vācātītaṃ nirañjanam | 
vāṅmāyā kamalādyena tārahīnena pārvati ! | dīyate vividhā vidyā 
māyāśrīsarvatomukhī | tāreṇa tārahīnena pratyekaṃ sakalaṃ padam 
| yugmayugmakrameṇāpi mantro'yaṃ vividho bhavet ||
 
atha brahmamantropāsanāprakārastu tatraiva || brāhme muhūrtte 
utthāya praṇamya brahmadaṃ gurum | dhyātvā ca paramaṃ brahma 
yathāśakti manuṃ smaret | pūrvavat praṇamedbrahma 
prātaḥkṛtyamidaṃ smṛtam || tathā | atha sandhyāvidhiṃ vakṣye 
brahmamantrasya śāmbhavi ! | yāṃ kṛtvā brahmasampattiṃ labhate 
bhuvi mānavaḥ | prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne yathādeśe yathāsane | 
pūrvavat paramabrahma dhyātvā sādhakasattamaḥ | aṣṭottaraśataṃ 
devi ! gāyatrījapamācaret | japaṃ samarpya vidhivat pūrvavat praṇamet 
sudhīḥ | eṣā sandhyā mayā proktā sarvathā brahmasādhanam | 
gāyatrīṃ śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm | parameśvaraṃ 
ṅe'ntamuktvā vidmahe tadanantaram | paratattvāya parato dhīmahīti 
vadet priye ! | tadanantaramīśāni ! tanno brahma pracodayāt | iyaṃ 
śrībrahmagāyatrī caturvargapradāyinī | yajanaṃ pūjanañcaiva 
snānaṃ pānañca bhojanam | yadyat karma prakurvīta brahmamantreṇa 
sādhayet | tat saditi vadeddevi ! prārambhe sarvakarmaṇām | 
brahmārpaṇamastu vākyaṃ pānabhojanakarmaṇoḥ | ṛṣiḥ sadāśivo 
hyasya chando'nuṣṭuvudāhṛtam | devatā paramaṃ brahma 
sarvāntaryāmi nirguṇam | caturvargaphalāvāptyai viniyogaḥ 
prakīrttitaḥ | aṅganyāsakaranyāsau kathayāmi śṛṇu priye ! | tāraṃ 
saccidekamiti brahmeti sakalaṃ tataḥ | 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyānāmikāsu maheśvari ! | kaniṣṭhayoḥ 
karatalapṛṣṭhayoḥ suravandite ! | 
namaḥsvāhāvaṣaḍvauṣaṭphaḍantaiśca yathākramam |

p. 880) nyasennyāsoktavidhinā sādhakaḥ susamāhitaḥ | 
hṛdādipādaparyantamevamevaṃ vidhīyate | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ

dakṣahastasya pārvati ! | vāmanāsāpuṭaṃ dhṛtvā dakṣanāsāpuṭena 
ca | pūrayet pavanaṃ mantrī mūlamantramidaṃ japan | aṅguṣṭhena 
dakṣanāsāṃ dhṛtvā kumbhakayogataḥ | japeddvātriṃśatāvṛttyā

manum | vāmanāsāpuṭe'pyevaṃ pūrakumbhakarecakam |

hariharavidhivedyaṃ yogibhirdhyānagamyam |

dadyādvāyutattvaṃ dīpaṃ taijasamarpayet | naivedyaṃ toyatattvena 
pradadyāt paramātmane | tato japtvā mahāmantraṃ manasā 
sādhakottamaḥ | samarpya brahmaṇe paścādvahiḥ pūjāṃ samārabhet 
| upasthitāni dravyāṇi gandhapuṣpādikāni ca vastrālaṅkaraṇādīni 
bhakṣyapeyāni yāni ca | mantreṇānena saṃśodhya dhyātvā brahma 
sanātanam | nimīlya netre matimānarpayet paramātmane | 
brahmārpaṇaṃ brahmahavirbrahmāgnī brahmaṇā hutam | brahmaiva 
tena gantavyaṃ brahmakarmasamādhinā | tato netre samunmīlya japtvā 
mūlaṃ svaśaktitaḥ | tajjapaṃ brahmasāt kṛtvā stotrañca kavacaṃ 
paṭhet | stotraṃ śṛṇu maheśāni ! brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ | yat 
śrutvā sādhako devi ! brahmasāyujyamaśnute | namaste sate 
sarvalokāśrayāya namaste cite viśvarūpātmakāya | 
namo'dvaitatattvāya muktipradāya namo brahmaṇe vyāpine nirguṇāya 
||
p. 881) tvamekaṃ śaraṇyaṃ tvamekaṃ vareṇyaṃ tvamekaṃ

p. 882) parameśasya nāvāhanavisarjjane | sarvatra sarvakāleṣu

bubhukṣitaḥ | pūjayet paramātmānaṃ sadā nirmalamānasaḥ | anena 
brahmamantreṇa bhakṣyapeyādikaṃ caret | dīyate parameśāya tadeva 
pāvanaṃ mahat | gaṅgātoye śivādau ca sparśadoṣo'pi varttate |

p. 883) vimukto'pāvanīmukhe | yatra kutra mṛto jñānī tatraiva 
mokṣamāpnuyāt || 

niśvāsataḥ pañcabhūtāvasthāḥ pūrvaṃ nirūpitāḥ 
idānīṃ varṇabhedenāvasthāpañcakaṃ nirūpayan varṇataḥ 
prayojanāntaramapi darśitavān | 
narapatijayacaryāsvarodayadhṛtabrahmajāmale || mātṛkāyāṃ svarāḥ 
proktāḥ svarāḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyayā | teṣāṃ dvāvantimautyājyau 
catvāraśca napuṃsakāḥ | śeṣā daśasvarānteṣu syādekaiko dvike 
dvike | jñeyā ataḥ svarādyāśca hrasvāḥ pañcasvarodaye | svarā hi 
mātṛkoccārā mātrāvyāptaṃ jagattrayam | tasmāt svarodbhavaṃ 
sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram | akārādisvarāḥ pañca brahmādyāḥ 
pañca devatāḥ | nivṛttyādyāḥ kalāḥ pañca icchādyaṃ 
śaktipañcakam | māyādyāścakrabhedāśca dharādyaṃ 
bhūtapañcakam | gandhādyā viṣayāste ca kāmavāṇā itīritāḥ | 
piṇḍaṃ padaṃ tathā rūpaṃ rūpātītaṃ nirañjanam | svarabhede 
sthitaṃ jñānaṃ jñāyate gurutaḥ sadā | akārādisvarāḥ pñca 
teṣāmaṣṭau bhidāstvamī | mātrā varṇo graho jīvo rāśirbhaṃ 
piṇḍayogakau | prasupto bhāṣate yena yena gacchati śabditaḥ | tatra 
nāmnādyavarṇe yā mātrā mātrāsvaraḥ sa hi || iti mātrāsvaracakram 
|| kādihāntān likhedvarṇān svarādho ṅañaṇojjhitān | 
tiryakpaṃktikrameṇaiva pañcatriṃśatprakoṣṭhake | naranāmādimo 
varṇo yasmāt svarādadhaḥ sthitaḥ | sa svarastasya varṇasya varṇasvara 
ihocyate | na proktā ṅañaṇā varṇā nāmādau santi te nahi | 
cedbhavanti tadā jñeyā jagaḍāste yathākramam | yadi nāmni 
bhavedvarṇaḥ saṃyuktākṣaralakṣaṇaḥ | grāhyastasyādimo varṇa 
ityuktaṃ brahmajāmale || iti varṇasvaracakram || asvare meṣasiṃhāliriḥ 
kanyāyugmakarkaṭāḥ | usvare ca dhanurmīnau esvare ca tulāvṛṣau | 
osvare mṛgakumbhau ca rāśiśambhūgrahasvarāḥ | svarādhaḥ

p. 884) sthāpayet kheṭaṃ rāśeryo yasya nāyakaḥ || iti grahasvaracakram

p. 892) mātrāsvaro'kārastasyaikā saṅkhyā varṇasvara ekārastasya

p. 898) tatsthatvāt prasaṅgena tadanantarodayaṃ saṅkathayati | asyeti || 
asya tithyantarodayasyāntarodayā ghaṭikārddhamānato 
daṇḍārddhapramāṇamiti || iti svaraḥ ||

tithyantarodayamāneneti | tithyantarodayamānena pūrvakathitena 
ghaṭikāsvare ghaṭīsvarasya ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyorabhedāt udayo jñeyo 
jñātavyaḥ | ādijāmale upajīvyagranthe evaṃ vidhānena prakāreṇa

ca saptapañcāśat 57 palāni bhuktāni tatrāntarodayo 
ghaṭikārddhamānena dvitīyasyākārasya palaṃ yāvaditi ghaṭīsvaraḥ || 
antarodayādikaṃ yathā bhavati tatkathayati || dvādaśābdeti || 
dvādaśābdādisvarāṇādyantā aṣṭau svarāḥ saṃsthānāt 
svabhogasamatāt svakālataḥ svabhogakālena dvādaśābdavarṣādinā 
udayāstaṃ tatra svabhogamānena ekādaśodayairantaḥ punarudayante 
ityanuyujyate | ata evāntarodayajijñāsāyāmekādaśena yo yatra bhāgo

kṛtamiti || varṣeti | atrāntarodayasaṃjñake svarodaye pañcaprakāreṇa 
kramāt kālamānaṃ samayamānaṃ mayoktaṃ varṣaṃ māso 
dinanāḍīpalāni ceti yathā dvādaśavārṣike varṣādipañcāpi 
vārṣikasvaramāsādi ayane dināni ṛtau dināni pakṣe dināni dine 
daṇḍādi ghaṭikāyāṃ palādīti || gatasamaye 
bhuktavarttamānasvarajñānārthamāha dvādaśābdeti |

svarabhogeṇa pratyekamekādaśalabdhāntarodayamānena bhuktaṃ

anusaraṇīyā || 


	uditasyeti | uditasya svarasya nāmavarṇamiliteṣu 
svasvakālapramāṇataḥ svasvabhogyasamayaprāmāṇyāt bālādikā 
avasthāḥ pañca syuḥ || avasthāṃ kathayati || ādyo bāla iti nijāvasthā 
bālatvādyanusāreṇa phaladā yathā bālāḥ kāryāḥ 
kṣamāsyathetyādi || kiṃ lābha ityādi || naṣṭe gatadravye daṣṭe 
sarpādidaṣṭe rujānvite saroge eteṣu kāryeṣu bālasvaro niṣiddhaḥ | 
yathā yasya bālasvarodayastadānīṃ dravyanāśe sapradaṃśe saroge 
ca niṣiddhaṃ phalamityarthaḥ | vivāhādiṣu śubhakāryeṣu śubhadaḥ | 
yātrāyāṃ yuddhe ca dīkṣāmantropadeśe ca tatra mṛtyusvarodayeṣu 
bālasvarāvadhikaspaṣṭasvarodayo yogasvarādyadhikanāma aṣṭau 
svarāsteṣāṃ teṣāṃ bālāntatadabadhikā dvādaśābdādi śiṣṭaṃ 
tadantarodayaḥ bālādipañcasvaravyavasthetyagre vyaktībhaviṣyati || 
svarāṇāṃ nyūnādhikabalamāha || mṛta iti || mṛtāddhṛto balī tataḥ 
kumāra iti dhyeyam | ayamarthaḥ | ekasya mṛtasvarodayaḥ aparasya 
vṛddhādisvarastadā pūrvāpekṣayā paro janaḥ saṅgrāme jayam yo 
yāsyati | yasya bālasvaramadhikṛtya yaḥ pañcame sthāne svaraḥ sa 
tasya mṛtyudāyaka iti mṛtatvenābhidhīyate | tṛtīye yuddhasvarodaye 
sakalakriyāsu vṛddhiḥ | śeṣā bālakumāravṛddhā baddhā mṛtyuriti || 
hīnasvare sati mṛtyurbhavati uditasvarādhikye jayaḥ | sāmye sāmyaṃ 
samatā yodhayoḥ sandhānaṃ sandhiḥ jayaparājayau vā samau || 
ekasvara iti || eka ubhayoryodhayorbālavaśe tu tadā sāmyaṃ 
jayabhaṅgau samau | dvitīye'rddhaphalaṃ bālāpekṣayā 
kumāre'rddhajayaḥ yūni pūrṇaphalaṃ vṛddhe baddhaḥ bandhanaṃ 
parācchatrorjayaśca || śatroriti || śatroḥ mṛtyusvare pañcamasvare udite 
svakīyayūni tasmin tatra jayo niścitaḥ | nāmajāśca svarotpattiṃ

p. 900) dvādaśādyaṣṭasvareṣu pūrvaṃ pratijñātuṃ darśayati || 
tatkāla iti || tatkāle ghaṭīsvare mātrāsvare udetīti saṅgrāhyaḥ | tathā 
dine tithau varṇasvaramupakramya bālādikavyavasthāpakṣe 
pakṣasvarodaye grahasvarodaya iti jñeyaḥ | māse māsasvare 
jīvasvarodayaḥ jīvasvarāvadhikā bālādicintā || ṛtau rāśyaṃśasvare 
rāśisvarādhikā bālādivyavasthā || tadyathā || naraharināmasvare 
cintā ṣaṇmāse ayanasvare'pi * *? sambhavo nakṣatrasvaraḥ 
tadavadhikā bālādicintā | abde varṣa *? piṇḍataḥ 
piṇḍasvarādbālādivyavasthā | dvādaśavārṣike dvādaśābdike 
yogasvara iti tadavadhikā bālādivyavasthā || tadyathā 
rāmatoṣaṇanāmni mātrāsvarokāraḥ varṇasvara ekāraḥ 
grahasvarakāraḥ jīvasvarakāraḥ rāśisvarakāraḥ piṇḍasvarakāraḥ 
yogasvarakāraḥ ityaṣṭau nāmajā mātrāsvarādyā yogāstāḥ 
prathamaṃ likhitāḥ | ghaṭyādidvādaśābdāntākṛṣṭāvādau 
sthūlakrameṇa tato'ntarodayakrameṇa likhitāḥ | yatra sthūle sūkṣme 
bālādijijñāsā bhavati atredaṃ vyavaharaṇīyam | yathā 
mātrāsvarakāra iti sthūlakramoditaḥ ghaṭīsvarakāraḥ sa ca 
kārāvadhikaḥ pañcamaḥ | antarodayaghaṭīsvare kārodayaḥ 
mātrāsvarakāraḥ || iti bālasvarodayaḥ || evaṃ 
varṇasvarakārastadavadhikaḥ pratipadyakāra uditaḥ sa ca pañcamo 
niṣiddhaḥ | atrāntarodayakārodayaḥ savarṇāvadhikaścaturtho vṛddha 
iti phaladaḥ grahasvarokārastadavadhikapakṣasvare 
sthūlakrameṇoditakāraḥ sa ca kāradvitīyaḥ kumāro bhavati 
antarodaye'pi kāraḥ so'pi kāro dvitīyaḥ kumāra iti tatphaladaḥ | evaṃ 
jīvasvaro'kārā tasya māse udayaḥ māsasvarastu kāraḥ sa 
cākārāvadhikasthūlakrameṇa svarāntarodayaḥ sa ca kārāddvitīyaḥ 
iti kumāraphaladaḥ | evaṃ rāśisvarakāraḥ tasya gatāvudaya

p. 901) ṛtusvarakāraḥ sa ca rāśisvarāt tṛtīyaḥ || iti svarodayaḥ | kāra 
uditaḥ jīvarāśisvaraḥ iti bālodayaḥ evaṃ nakṣatrasvarakāraḥ 
tasyānayane udayaḥ sa ca rāśisvarāttṛtīya iti svarodayaḥ | antarodayaḥ 
kārodayaḥ sa ca nakṣatrasvarāttṛtīyaḥ iti yuvasvaraḥ | evaṃ 
piṇḍasvarakārastasya vārṣikasvara udayaḥ | vārṣikasvarakāraḥ sa 
piṇḍasvarāt pañcamaḥ antarodaye kārodayaḥ sa ca 
piṇḍasvarāccaturtho vṛddhaḥ | evaṃ yogasvarakārastasya 
dvādaśavārṣike udayaḥ | dvādaśavārṣike svarakāro 
yogasvarāddvitīyaḥ | kumāraḥ antarodayaḥ kāraḥ sa eva yogasvaraḥ | 
iti bālodayaḥ | mātrādiyogāntāstu svarāṇāṃ nāmajānāṃ 
kālajavaiparītyakramanyastaghaṭīsvarādidvādaśavārṣikasvareṣu 
aṣṭasvarasamutpattikramanirasanaṃ hṛdā paribhāvanīyamiti || atra 
nāmajāṣṭasvareṣviti prāśastyamavaśyāpekṣaṇīyam | 
varṇasvarasyopadarśayati sarvakālebalī varṇa iti | varṇo varṇasvaraḥ 
sarvakālabalī sarvasmin kāle dvādaśābdarūpe balī valavān 
svaphaladaḥ | atra saṃśayo na | hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇamāha sarvavyāpīti 
sarveṣāṃ mātrāsvaragrahādīnāṃ vyāpī vyāpakaḥ 
teṣāmetadvyāpyatvāt yato varṇasvarabalenātibalino'pi tasya teṣāmapi 
nairapekṣyeṇa phalavatvamiti tasmāddhetoḥ sarvaprayatnena 
śubhāśubhaṃ vīkṣyam || yadā padā || yathā hastipadacihne 
anyapadacihnāni praviṣṭāni tathā mātrāsvarāḥ kārādayaḥ 
saptavarṇaphalodayasthāḥ phaladānārthaṃ praviṣṭāḥ | 
varṇaphalodayasthā iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ | varṇaphalaṃ puraskṛtya 
udayasthāḥ varṇabalena balina ityarthaḥ | tatra viśeṣamāha | sādhanaṃ 
varṇabale yuddhe viśeṣato varṇabalaṃ grāhyam | yogena yogasvareṇa 
śarīrasya jñānasambhavaṃ yogaṃ sādhayet | āṇavaṃ 
aṇimādisiddhibhavam | śāmbhavaṃ śambhuviṣayakaṃ śākteyaṃ

p. 902) gauryādibhavam || iti svaracakravivaraṇam | varṇeti 
varṇasvarādhikapṛthakprakāśitatithinakṣatrādīnāṃ 
bālādivyavasthāsu darśayannāha tithivārāviti | tithiḥ nandādiḥ | 
vāro maṅgalādiḥ | pṛthak pṛthak bhinnakrameṇa sarvaṃ 
sambhāṣitaṃ tat sarvamekatra sarvasvarasthāne sthitaṃ tasminna 
varttulīkṛtya varṇasvarodayaṃ kuryāt | ayamarthaḥ | akārādipañcako 
varṇaḥ svarastadadhaḥ svaracakratithivāranakṣatrabale nyastāni tāni 
bālasvarasya bālatve bālādikumārāditve kumārādiphaladāni yathā 
śrīrāmatoṣaṇanāmni kāropasthite ikāro varṇasvaro 
bālastadadhaścakrāntarasthitā dvitīyāsaptamīdvādaśītithayo 
bālāḥ | evaṃ tadadho budhacandrau vārau tau bālau 
tadadhaścakrāntarasthāni nakṣatrāṇi bālāni 
tadagrimatithivāranakṣatrāṇi kumārādīni vyavaharaṇīyāni | 
evamanyanāmnāpi vyavahāraḥ karttavyaḥ ||

yasyeti | yasya puṃso nāmādikaṃ varṇaṃ nāmādyākṣaraṃ 
tithivārau nakṣatrañca mṛtaṃ tadīyapañcamasvarodayā yasmin dine 
taddinaṃ tasyābhimatakriyāsu varjayet | yadṛkṣaṃ yaddinaṃ taddinaṃ 
tasya hānimṛtyukaram | aneneti || anena svarayogeṇa yadā 
śatrormṛtasvarodayaḥ svasya bālādyudayo viśeṣaḥ 
kumārayuvodayastadā śatrūṇāṃ sāvarṇādikaṃ 
mantrādikriyāñca taddine budhaḥ paṇḍitaḥ sādhayet kuryādityarthaḥ 
|| iti tithivāranakṣatrasvaracakravivaraṇam || bālādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ 
phalānyāha bāleti || bālasvarādikāvasthā bhānusaṅkhyā bhavanti 
tāśca svabhogapramāṇakrameṇodayantu ghaṭikākhye dine pakṣe 
māse ṛtau ayane varṣe dvādaśavārṣike dvādaśa bhāgāḥ | ayane 
ṣaṇmāse pakṣe dvādaśabhāgaḥ varṣe māso dvādaśabhāgaḥ 
dvādaṣavarṣe ekavarṣaṃ prati kramāt evaṃ bhogapramāṇāt 
svasvāvasthā bhavanti | bhuktasvarapramāṇantviti | yasya

p. 903) bālādisvarasyāvasthā ghaṭīsvarādau jñātumiṣyate tena 
yadbhuktaṃ tat pūrvaṃ pratyekaṃ palīkṛtya 
svasvabhogadvādaśabhāgena vibhajet | bhāge hṛte yallabdhaṃ 
tatsaṅkhyāgatāvasthāśeṣo varttamānāvasthaśeṣe tu 
gatagamyakaraṇāt gate ṣaṣṭyādyuddhṛte gatasamayaḥ gamye 
ṣaṣṭyādyuddhṛte gamyasamayo varttamānāvasthā āyāti | kramaṇikā 
yathā | nāmno yuvatithiḥ tṛtīyā tasya gatadaṇḍaḥ | atra 
dvādaśāvasthājijñāsāyāṃ bhuktadaṇḍaṃ palīkṛtya palāni tatra 
saṃyojya vṛttatithidvādaśabhāgena palīkṛtya tat pañcadaṇḍena 
vṛttam anena bhāge gṛhīte labdham | catasro'vasthāgatāḥ 
pañcamīvarttamānā bhāgaśeṣāḥ | asmit bhāgahārake pātite 
bhogyaṃ ubhayatra ṣaṣṭyā haraṇāt labdhapalam | etena 
pañcamāvasthāyāṃ gataṃ gamyañca sārddhadaṇḍadvayātmakam | 
yatra tu dinamāsasambhāvanā tatra daṇḍe ṣaṣṭyuddhṛte māsa iti 
prakṛte ca dvādaśāvasthā madhye vā pañcamāvasthā kathitāsti sā 
aśanaphaladā evamanyeṣu ghaṭīsvarādisvarāvasthāḥ kāryāḥ 
avasthāślokāstu granthe likhyante || śūlā ca bālā śiśuhāsikā ca 
kumārikā yauvanarājyadā ca | kleśapramodā jaritā tathāsau mṛtā ca 
bālasvarajādyavasthāḥ || 1 || svasthābhṛtā meghanigharṣavṛddhā 
mahodayā śāntikarī na ca strī | vṛddhā samā sā tu guṇodayā ca 
maṅgalyadā dvādaśikā kumāre || 2 || utsāhadhairyograjarā balā ca 
maṅgalyayogā ca sakāmapuṣṭiḥ | śākhī ca sihā badhaneśvarī ca 
śāntā bhidhā dvādaśikā yuvākhye || 3 || vaikalyaśeṣā khalu yā ca 
moghācyutikṣamā duḥkhitarātrinidrā | buddhiprasaṅgā ca tathā ca 
caṇḍā jarā mṛtā dvādaśikā ca vṛddhiḥ || 4 || cintā ca baddhvā 
pratighātakī ca śeṣo mahījvālabhavābdhidā ca | prāṇī kirā 
bhedakarī vadādyā mṛtyukṣayā dvādaśamṛtyujāśca || 5 || 

p. 904) evaṃ ṣaṣṭiravasthā syuravasthāpañcakaṃ sadā | tāsu 
sarvāsu vijñeyaṃ svanāmasadṛśaṃ phalam | prathamaślokāt 
śiśuhāsikā ca yauvanarājyadā ca meghanigharṣā vṛddhā ca 
guṇodayā ca || 5 || utsāhadhairyograjavā ca bālā sakāmapuṣṭiḥ 
vaikalyaśeṣā ca duḥkhito rātrirnidrā ca mahījavā ca bhāvavahnidā 
ca || ityavasthā || pūrvasminniti || pūrvasmin pradeśe asvarasvāmī 
evamaṣṭasvikārādayaḥ madhye okāraḥ nandāyāmasvaraḥ 
prācyāmudeti tatrāśanisvaro dakṣiṇā ityādi || yāmyam || yattithau 
yaḥ svaraḥ yasyāṃ sarvadā udayaṃ yāti tatpañcamīṃ diśaṃ 
sarvakāryeṣu varjayet | viśeṣato yātrāyāṃ yathā ikārasvaro 
dvitīyāsaptamīdvādaśīṣu dakṣiṇā diguṇeti tatpañcamī dik 
pūrvāśā mantavyā | tatra mṛtasvarasyoditattvāt madhyadik vyavasthā 
kathaṃ kāryā iti saṃśaye pratipat ṣaṣṭhyakādaśīṣu akārodayaḥ 
pūrvadiśīti tatpañcamapraveśo madhyaṃ 
prāsādādābūrddhādhaḥsthitagṛhādi na gacchediti sambandhanīyam 
|| 

pṛcchata iti || svarasya dik krameṇoditasya pañcamīṃ diśaṃ 
lakṣīkṛtya avasthāsu pṛcchako yadi praśnaṃ karoti tadā hānyādikaṃ 
jāyate atra saṃśayo na | tathā ca dvitīyākārasya dakṣiṇadiśyudayāt 
pūrvadigastamitā tadarati sthite pṛcchake phalameva vaktavyamityarthaḥ 
|| iti diksvaravivaraṇam || tithisvare antarodayasthabālādisvarasya 
praśnāt phalamumapadarśyāha tithyādāviti || tithyādau tithyapakrame 
tithisvarādyārabhya bālasvarādiko ghaṭīsvara udayaṃ yāti | tatra 
praśne sati tasya bālādisvarasya balaṃ vadāmi tadudayaprakāramāha 
tithīti || titheryāvatī ghaṭī daṇḍarūpā bhuktā tatsaṅkhyā ṣaṣṭyā 
saṅguṇya palamayīṃ kṛtvā tatra ṛkṣavahnihṛte

p. 905) saptaviṃśatyadhikaśatatrayahṛte yallabdhaṃ 
tattithisvarāvadhike bhuktaḥ svaraḥ śeṣe tatkālasambhavaḥ | 
tātkālikasvaro jñātavyaḥ | sa eva titherantarodayaghaṭīsvara iti 
dātavyaḥ | titherantarodayavarṇasvarakramikā bālādivyavasthā 
ghaṭīsvare tu mātrāsvarādbālādivyavasthetyukteravyavasthitaḥ 
pakṣaḥ | phalabhāgitvamupadarśayati yamuddiśyeti || yaṃ 
janamuddiśya praśnaḥ kṛtastasya phalaṃ prajāyate phalabhāgitvaṃ 
bhavati tannāmno bālādisvaravaśenetyarthaḥ | yatra kiñcinnāma 
noddiśyate prakāśyate tatra praṣṭureva śubhamaśubhañca phalaṃ 
vakṣyamāṇaṃ vaktavyam | etat phalaṃ ślokapañcakenāha 
kumāretyādi sphuṭam ||

viṣamasamabhedena praśnāntaramāha ojasvarā iti || ojāḥ 
prathamatṛtīrapañcamāḥ puruṣasvarāḥ syuḥ yugmasvarān 
ikārokārān striya iti viduḥ | evamanyat svasvare svapakṣīyasvare 
puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ balaṃ jāyate | tathā ca puṃsvare varttamāne 
bālādau pumān balavān adhīne tu puttrotpattiḥ | evaṃ strīṣu 
anyadāha garbhārtha iti | garbhapraśne puṃsvare puttrotpattiḥ same 
kanyotpattiḥ yugmaviṣayasvarāvadhisvaranirvyūhe praśne yugmañca 
bhavati naṣṭasvarāvasāne kṣayo bhavati garbhanāśo vācyaḥ | 
saṃkrame svaraprārambhe māturmṛtyurbhavati | 
yugmagarbhayogyatāyogyamāha dyunāḍīsvarayoriti dyurdivasaḥ nāḍī 
ghaṭī tattannirūpitandharau dinasvaraghaṭīsvarau tayoḥ satoḥ 
puṃyugmaṃ puruṣadvayam | samandharayoḥ kanyādvayam puṃstriyoḥ 
eko viṣamaḥ svaro dvitīyaḥ samasvaraḥ tadā puṃstrī kanyā puttraśca 
iti garbhayugmagarbhavigaye praśne vinirdiśet vadediti tātkālikasvaraḥ 
|| janmarkṣa iti || janmakṣe janmanakṣatre janmapādā 
janmanakṣatrapādikā tadvaṇa śatapadacakrokta kṣetre 
pādaprakārasvākṣare yo'kṣaro varṇaṃsvaraḥ yathā 
kekīhahītyaksaracatuṣṭayaṃ punarvasupādikācatuṣṭayasya tena

p. 906) prathamadvitīyapādikayoḥ kakāraḥ tasya varṇasvaro'kāraḥ 
tṛtīyacaturthayorhakārastasya varṇasvara okārastatpādikayorjātasya 
varṇasvaro nāḍīsvara ihāhvayaḥ prāṇīnāṃ svabhāvaḥ 
svabhāvasiddha ityarthaḥ || naḍīsvaraprayojanaṃ janmanyāha ca paleti | 
tithisvaro viśiṣya tadantarodayasvaro vā 
nāḍīsvarāvadhikabālādisvare ślokapañcakena phalaṃ sphuṭam || 
tatkālasvare jātakanirṇayaḥ ||

atha yodhayoryuddhe ko jayī syādityāha | kakārādijakārāntā 
varṇāstathā pañca hrasvasvarāḥ kakhagaghacachajajhaṭaṭha ḍaḍhata 
thadadha a i u e o ete site śuklapakṣe balinaḥ syuḥ | śeṣā 
dhanapaphababhamayaralavaśaṣasaha ā ī ū ai au ete sarve sitetare 
kṛṣṇapakṣe balinaḥ syuḥ || ekasvara iti || yatrobhayaḥ svaraḥ ekaḥ 
pṛthakpakṣayorvarṇānāmādau bhavati tatra pakṣabalaṃ grāhyaṃ 
śuklakṛṣṇavibhedataḥ | yathā 
karmugharmuramukayorekapakṣākṣarayornāmādivarṇasvaraḥ 
kakāraḥ śuklapakṣe vakāraḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe'stīti tatra pakṣe tau 
pratyekaṃ jayinau śuklakṛṣṇabhedāditi svaraikyayā vācā 
ekapakṣāntarānyataratāmāha || ekapakṣākṣara iti || 
dvayoryodhayorekapakṣākṣarayornāmādivarṇasvaraḥ kakāraḥ | 
ekaścettadā śuklapakṣe gauro gauravarṇaḥ | apare kṛṣṇapakṣe 
kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ śyāmavarṇaḥ niścitaṃ jayati yathā 
karmurgauravarṇaḥ gharmuḥ sitetaraḥ śyāmavarṇaḥ 
svaraścobhayoreko'kāraḥ pañcaikañca tatra karmurgauratvena 
śuklapakṣe jayī taditaraśyāmavarṇatvāt kṛṣṇapakṣe jayīti ||

yasminniti || yasmin ekapakṣākṣare eka eva svaraḥ yodho gaurau 
kṛṣṇau vā hrasvadīrghau bhavataḥ tatra yadi hrasvo yo varṇasvare 
likhanakrameṇa svarāsannākṣaraḥ san sa tadā jayī bhavati |

p. 907) yastayordīrghaḥ sa yadi svarādbhavākṣarastadā jayī 
bhavatītyarthaḥ || pramāṇeti || pramāṇaṃ hrasvādi nāmajākhyā 
varṇā gauravarṇādayaḥ | teṣāmaikyaṃ samatā yodhayoryadi bhavati 
tatra yāyisthāyivibhedato yuddhabalaṃ jñeyam | etadeva vyaktikṛtya 
vyavasthāpayati yodhayoriti | yodhayoḥ sarvabhedena pramāṇādinā 
pūrvaślokakathitenaikye samatāyāṃ yūni mārake ca svare udite sati 
yāyī prathamodyato jayī | tathā tenaiva prakāreṇa bāle vṛddhe antime 
mṛte ca svare sthāyī prativādī jayī | bālatithitrayoparisthite 
saptākṣareṣvakṣarabhedena tithiṣu pratyekaṃ phalamāha ādyatitheriti | 
pratipatṣaṣṭhyekādaśīnāmupari akachaḍadhabhaba iti saptākṣarāṇi 
santi | teṣāṃ tā eva tithayo bālāstāsu ādyatitheḥ 
pratipadastayorvarṇā akārakakārachakārāḥ  evaṃ śeṣayoḥ 
ṣaṣṭhyekādaśyorddvau varṇau matau | tataḥ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ 
ḍakāradhakārau ekādaśyāṃ bhakārabakārau evaṃ tithisvare 
tithitraye varṇasaṅkhyā jñeyā || varṇatithyādīti evaṃ yasyāstitheryo 
varṇastasya sā tithirbhavati | tadādyekādyanukrameṇa tithitrayasya 
yamahādistathā mṛtiriti māsatrayaṃ yasya kakārasya 
pratipajjanmanāmnī ṣaṣṭhyahādināmnī ekādaśī mṛtināmikā | atra 
pratyekaṃ phalamāha śrījanmanītyādi || iti 
svaraprakāraviśeṣanirṇayaḥ ||

	asmin dine puruṣasya kiṃ phalaṃ syāditi svarabhedena 
vicārayannāha yaduktamiti | tadahaṃ dinaphalaṃ svarādakārādisvarāt 
mātrādināmakāt dinaphalaṃ vakṣye | kīdṛśaṃ puṃsāṃ 
dharmādiprakāśanaṃ puṃsāmityupalakṣaṇam anyeṣāmapi yad 
jāmale jāmalādau tanme guruprasādato gurupadeśājjñātam | 
prakāramupadarśayannāha yogeti || 
yogasvarapiṇḍasvararāśisvarajīvasvaragrahasvaravarṇasvaranakṣatra-
svara mātrāsvarāstān aṣṭau svarānnām utpādya sthāpayet

p. 908) tiryakpaṃktikrameṇa likhet | tasyeti || tasya yogasvarāderadhastāt 
dvādaśābdādikālajān 
dvādaśābdābdāyanattumāsapakṣadinaghaṭīsambhavānaṣṭau 
svarānnāmādau likhet kathambhūtān svarabhogena samāyuktān 
sthūlakrameṇāntarodayamānena ca viśiṣya sahitān | bhogamāha 
bhogāścaikādaśāṃśakaḥ sthūlamānaṃ pratyekamekādaśena yo 
bhogaḥ prāpyate vārṣādi yo yasmin yaḥ sa bhogotpattirityarthaḥ | 
yathā dvādaśavārṣike varṣamekamādau dinadvayaṃ 
lokābdhināḍikā aṣṭatriṃśat  1 | 1 | 2 | 43 | tatpalāni cetyādi 
yathāyathamaṣṭānīkānīti tatraiva yogasvarādikāntasvarāṇāṃ 
svarādhikasvareṣu bālādivyavasthā | yathottareti | svaravedibhirete 
svarā yathottarabalā jñātavyāḥ | ke te yogādikā mātrāntā aṣṭau 
nāmajā nāmata utpāditāḥ tathā tenaiva prakāreṇa phalodayāścāpi 
dvādaśābdādikā ghaṭīparyantā sarvayoditā yathottarabalā 
jñātavyāḥ  yathā yogasvarāt piṇḍasvaro balī piṇḍānnakṣatrasvara 
evaṃ krameṇāpi tathā dvādaśābdikasvarādvārṣiko 
vārṣikādayanasvara evaṃ krameṇāgrato'pi jñātavyamityarthaḥ ||

akārādīti || akārādikramāt a i u e o evaṃ krameṇa likhitvā 
tadadhastāt vedāścatvārastatpramāṇikāścatuḥsaṅkhyā vinyasya 
svarajñairviśeṣopakā matā svīkṛtā ityarthaḥ | prayojanantu 
asyāṣṭasvareṣu pratyekamata eva catuḥsaṅkhyāvinyāsaḥ 
śubhāśubhakoṭidvaye vibhāsyata iti pūrvapratijñātānāṃ 
nāmajāṣṭasvarāṇāṃ dvādaśābdikakālajāṃ bālādyavasthāṃ 
sūcayan kutaḥ kutra sā cintanīyetyāha yogāditi | yogāt yogasvarāt 
bālabhūtāt dvādaśavarṣe bālādyavasthāḥ syuḥ | ayamarthaḥ 
dvādaśavārṣikasvare yaḥ svaro'kārādiṣu uditaḥ sa 
yogasvaramānamadhikṛtya bālādisvaraiḥ sthale viśiṣyāntarodaye 
sūkṣme vyavasthāpanīya iti evamagre'pi saptāvasthāḥ kathitā

p. 909) varṣairbālā boddhavyāḥ || tad yathā piṇḍataḥ 
piṇḍasvarādābdike vārṣikasvarādbhasvarāt nakṣatrasvarāt ayane 
makarakarkaṭādirāśiṣu dvayorāśisvarāt 
ṛtusvarājjīvasvarānmāsasvare avasthā bālādyavasthā grahāt 
grahasvarāt pakṣasvare varṇāt varṇasvarāttithisvare mātrāsvarāt 
ghaṭīsvare bālādyavasthā cintanīyeti ||

dvādaśābdika iti || akārādisvare a i u e o 
evaṃrūpadvādaśābdikakālaḥ samayaḥ sthitaḥ sa yadi 
pañcāvasthāsu śubhaḥ syāt pañcāvasthāsu akārādikramasthitāsu 
aśubhado niṣiddhaphaladaḥ antarodayakrameṇa na syāt tadā śubhaḥ 
syāt avasthāsu pañcāvasthāsu 
svarodayavarṣamāsadinanāḍīpalarūpāsu na śubhaḥ syāttadā śubho 
na syāt | yadvā pañcāvasthāśubho'pi pañcāvasthāyāṃ 
bālādikrameṇa varṣamāsadinetyādikrameṇa śubho'pi yadi 
dinacaryāyāṃ kathitaprakāreṇābhadrakarāśiravaśiṣyate tadā 
śubho na syāt | yadā pañcāvasthāśubhaḥ pañcasvavasthāsu 
aśubhaḥ śubhagrahaḥ na śubho'śubhagrahaḥ syāt tadā phalaṃ 
vācyaṃ pariśeṣakrameṇa ityabhiprāyaḥ | yathā ye bālādisvarā 
antarodayagatāsteṣāmastarāśipatiraśubhaḥ śubhaśca 
taduparisthitasvareṣu viṃśopakāraṃ parikalpya 
śubhāśubhakoṭidvayāṅkamālikhya svalpasya bahuṣu pātanāt 
pariśeṣeṇa yadi śubhagraharāśistadā śubhaṃ anyathā aśubhaṃ 
sāmānyato jñātvā viṃśatyetyādiprakāreṇa varttamānasvaraṃ 
nirūpya tadadhaḥsthagrahavaśena phalaṃ vācyaṃ pariśeṣe 
śubhagrahakoṭiḥ viṃśatyetyādikrameṇāśubhakoṭistadā 
miśraphalaṃ vācyaṃ pariśeṣe tu śubhagrahakoṭistadā miśraphalaṃ 
vācyaṃ lābhālābhau dhātumūlajīvādhipagrahavaśena 
tattaddravyāṇāmiha paripāṭī cintanīyā | 
prāguktadinaphalavyavasthārthaṃ bālādiṣu vyavasthāmāha svarāviti 
| vṛddhāstamau vṛddhamṛtau svarau duṣṭau niṣiddhaphaladau tathā

p. 910) bālāpūrvaphalaṃ bālasvaraprathamārddhaṃ tathā 
duṣṭamityarthaḥ | śeṣaṃ sārddhadvayaṃ 
bālottarārddhasahitakumārayūnoriti tatra ityanena prakāreṇa 
śubhāśubhaṃ jñeyam ||

dvādaśa iti || svāntarodayasaṃsthitā ye dvādaśavārṣikādyā 
aṣṭau te śubhāḥ bālottarārddhakumārayuvānaḥ svarā 
viṃśopasaṅkhyāsahitā ekata ekadeśe sthāpyā aśubhā 
bālapūrvārddhamṛtavṛddhasvarā viṃśopakāḥ sthāpyāḥ | 
viṃśopakāḥ pratyekaṃ kumārādīnāṃ catvāraḥ | bālo 
miśraphalatvenobhayatra dvisaṅkhyā tatra prathamārddhasamaye 
aśubhadviḥ || 42 || uttarārddhasamaye śubhadviḥ || 42 || śubhāśubheti || 
śubharūpasyāśubhasvarūsya ca rāśiyugmasya madhyataḥ 
ekasmādasmin pātite śeṣe śubharāśayaḥ śubharāśau vā 
svarūpaṃ taddinajaṃ phalaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || rogādikasya phalaṃ 
kuto vā syādityākāṅkṣāyāmāha viṃśatyeti | śeṣe pātitaśeṣe 
viṃśatyā tāḍite guṇite catuḥṣaṣṭivibhājite catuḥṣaṣṭyā 
hṛtabhāge labdhaṃ tattatra teṣāṃ madhye viṃśopakāḥ śubhāḥ 
śubhaprakāśakāḥ | yathā ekalābhe yogataḥ dvilābhe piṇḍataḥ 
ekamaṣṭaparyantena mātrāntāt phalam | tattatkathitāditi pañcasvareṣu 
pratyekaṃ catuḥsaṅkhyā vinyasya viṃśatisaṅkhyā bhavati tena 
viṃśatyā guṇaḥ yogādimātrāntā svarāṇāmaṣṭasaṅkhyā 
dvādaśābdādighaṭyantasvarāṇāmapyaṣṭasaṅkhyā 
ato'ṣtābhiraṣṭaguṇanāccatuḥṣaṣṭisaṅkhyā 
bhāgahāraketyabhiprāyaḥ || catuḥpañcavibhājite iti netrapāṭhaḥ | 
tatra catuḥpañcāśatā bhāgo grāhya iti ||

yogādisaṅkhyālābhe ekādikrameṇa kutaḥ phalaṃ syādityāha 
|| yoga iti || yogalabdhe ekasaṅkhyālābhe svakarmmataḥ svavyāpārāt 
piṇḍe dvisaṅkhyālābhe śarīrāt svakīyādeva me nakṣatre 
tatsaṅkhyālabdhe suhṛjjanāt rāśau catuḥsaṅkhyāto labdhe 
svakulataḥ svakulajanāt jīve pañcasaṅkhyāto labdhe svavittāt

p. 911) svadhanāt svacittādipāṭhe svamānasāt sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ vā 
bhavatīti | grahato grahe svasaṅkhyāto labdhe ripoḥ śatrujanāt grahata 
ityatra sarve bhaktikaṣṭamiti | varṇe saptasaṅkhyāto labdhe svasvāmitaḥ 
sevyāt sarveṣāmaṣṭasaṅkhyāto labdhe aṣṭalabdhāyāṃ strījanāt 
śubhaṃ phalaṃ aśubhaṃ phalaṃ vā bhavatīti prakāraḥ 
sāmānyataḥ prāpto'rddhavyaktīkaraṇāya likhyate | yasya 
devadattādināmno dinaphalaṃ jñātumiṣyate tannāmno 
mātrādyaṣṭasvarānutpādya vyutkrameṇa yogasvaramupakramya 
mātrāntā tiryakkrameṇālikhya dvādaśābdikaghaṭyantān 
sthūlakrameṇa viśiṣyāntarodayakrameṇāṣṭasvarānutpādya 
tadhadho'nukrameṇa likhitvā tatra yogasvarāditaḥ pratyekaṃ 
bālakumārādivyavasthāṃ vidhāya pañcasvaraviṃśopakaṃ 
pratyekaṃ catuḥsaṅkhyāvyavasthitamatra likhet | tad yathā 
bālottarārddha samaye dvayaṃ kumāre yūni ca pratyekaṃ catuścatuḥ 
kṛtvā śubharāśiṃ parikalpya likhet | bālapūrvārddha samaye 
dvayaṃ vṛddhe mṛte ca catuścatuḥ saṅkhyāmaśubhakoṭau likhitvā 
śubhāśubharāśyorantare yatrāvaśiṣyate tannāmasadṛśaṃ phalaṃ 
bhavati | tatra yadavaśiṣyate tadviṃśage saḍguṇa catuḥṣaṣṭyā 
haraṇāt yallabdhaṃ tatsaṃkhyāyogādiṣu te bhuktasvarāḥ 
tadutpattijñānārthaṃ sakalasādhāraṇādhikaśata 100 
śakavarṣīyaśuklapratipadi dvādaśābdādyaṣṭasvarāḥ 
svāntarodayasthā gatagamyarūpāḥ pūrvaṃ likhitāḥ | tathā 
dinadarśanārthaṃ likhyante yathā śrīrāmatoṣaṇanāmno 
mātrādyaṣṭasvarā vyutkrameṇa yogādikāḥ yadyapyādau 
yogasvarādidvādaśābdādisvarūpeṣūtpattistena prathamata eva svarā 
vyavasthāpayitumarhati | tatrāpi 
mātrādisvarasaptakavyavasthāvyatirekeṇa tadvyavasthā na bhavatīti 
śeṣe tasya kathanamiti tātparyam | atra dvādaśābdādyantarodaye 
yogasvarādyavadhikabālādikavyavasthāyā yogasvarakārastadavadhiko 
dvādaśābde uditakāro bālastasyottarārddhamiti śubharāśau 
dvisaṃkhyāpiṇḍasvara ekārastadavadhiko varṣa uditakāro 
vṛddhastasyāśubhakoṭī catuḥsaṃkhyā | 
nakṣatrasvarakārastadavadhiko'yane udita ekāro yuvā śubharāśau 
tasya catuḥsaṃkhyā | rāśisvarakārastadavadhika ṛtau uditakāro 
bālastasyottarārddhamiti śubharāśau dvisaṃkhyā | 
vasvarokārastadavadhiko māsa uditakāraḥ kumārastasya rāśau 
catuḥsaṃkhyā | grahasvaro'kārastadavadhikaḥ pakṣa uditaḥ 
kārastasya śubharāśau catuḥsaṃkhyā | varṇasvarakārastadavadhiko 
dine uditakāro'tyaśubharāśau tasya catuḥsaṃkhyā | mātrā 
svarakārastadavadhiko ghaṭyuditakāro bālastasyottarārddhamiti 
śubharāśau dvisaṃkhyā | etāvatā bālatayottarārddhatvena 
ṣaṭsaṃkhyā kumāradvayasya cāṣṭau yūnaḥ kārasya catvāraḥ 
varttulīkaraṇādaṣṭādaśa 18 | śubharāśau hvatvamadyāṣṭau ca 
bālatrayasya bhuktapūrvārddhasya ṣaṭ 6 | 
varttulīkaraṇādaśubharāśau caturdaśasaṃkhyā 14 | 
rāśidvaye'dhikarāśeranyo rāśiḥ pātanīyaḥ | prakṛte 
yūnaścāṣṭamarāśeradhikaśubharāśau pātanādavaśeṣaḥ | 
avaśeṣeṇa śubharāśisthitau phalaṃ śubhaṃ syāt | śeṣe 
catuḥsaṃkhyāyāṃ viṃśatiguṇitāyāṃ vṛttaṃ 80 | atra 
catuḥṣaṣṭiharaṇāllabdhaśeṣaḥ | evaṃ dvitīyo varttamānaḥ piṇḍaḥ 
svaraḥ śubhaḥ śubhaphalaṃ śarīraṃ cintanīyaṃ śarīraṃ mṛtyū 
palakṣaṇaṃ svīyajanādityarthaḥ | evaṃ sādhāraṇe 
dvādaśābdādisamayanirūpitasvare'pi prativyakti nāmajasvarāṇāṃ 
bālakumārādivyavasthāmutpādya yathāyathaṃ karmāṇi dinaphale ca 
karttarvyānīti || evamiti || evamanena prakāreṇa vidvān paṇḍito 
jñātvā śubhāśubhaṃ vā phalaṃ vadet ||

p. 913) sarvatobhadraṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam | ūrddhvagā daśa 
vinyasya tiryagrekhāstathā daśa | ekāśītipadaṃ cakraṃ 81 jāyate 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | akārādisvarāḥ koṣṭhe tvīśādau vidiśi kramāt | 
sṛṣṭimārgeṇa dātavyāḥ ṣoḍaśaiva caturbhramam | kṛttikādīni 
dhiṣṇyāni pūrvāśādau likhedbudhaḥ | 
saptasaptakramādetānyaṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyayā | lekhyā 
avakahaḍāḥ prācyāṃ maṭapavatāśca dakṣiṇe | layabhajakhāśca 
vāruṇyāṃ pasadacalāstathottare | trayastrayo vṛṣādyāśca

sṛṣṭimārgataḥ | śeṣeṣu koṣṭhakeṣvevaṃ nandāditithipañcakam | 
vārāṇāṃ saptakaṃ lekhyaṃ bhaumādiprakrameṇa ca | ravermeṣatule 
prokte candrasya vṛṣavṛścikau | bhaumasya mṛgakarkau ca kanyāmīnau 
budhasya ca | jīvasya karkamakarau mīnakanye sitasya ca | tulāmeṣau ca 
mandasya uccanīcau prakīrttitau | śanyarkarāhuketvārāḥ krūrāḥ 
śeṣāḥ śubhagrahāḥ | krūrayukto budhaḥ krūraḥ 
kṣīṇacandrastathaiva ca | yasminnṛkṣe sthitaḥ kheṭastato vedhatrayaṃ 
bhavet | grahadṛṣṭivaśenātra vāmasammukhadikṣiṇe | bhuktaṃ 
bhogyaṃ tathā krāntaṃ viddhaṃ krūragraheṇa bham | 
śubhāśubheṣu kāryeṣu varjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ | vakrage dakṣiṇā 
dṛṣṭirvāmādṛṣṭiśca śīghrage | madhyacāre tathā madhyā jñeyā 
bhaumādipañcake | sūryamuktā ghaṭīyantre śīghrāścārke 
dvitīyage | samāstṛtīyage jñeyā mandā bhānau caturthage | mandāḥ 
syuḥ pañca ṣaṣṭhe'rke ativakrā nagāṣṭage | navame daśame bhānau 
jāyate kuṭilā gatiḥ | dvādaśaikādaśe sūrye labhante śīghratāṃ 
punaḥ | rāhuketū sadā vakrau śīghragau candrabhāskarau | 
gaterekasvabhāvatvādeṣāṃ sṛṣṭitrayaṃ sadā | krūrā vakrā 
mahākrūrāḥ saumyā vakrā mahāśubhāḥ | syuḥ 
sahajasvabhāvasthāḥ saumyāḥ krūrāśca śīghragāḥ | ghaṅachāḥ 

p. 914) ṣaṇaṭhāścaiva dhaphaḍāḥ sthajhañāstathā | etattrikaṃ 
trikaṃ biddhaṃ biddhaiḥ kaphabhadaiḥ kramāt | ghaṅachā 
raudravedhena ṣaṇaṭhā hastage grahe | dhaphaḍāḥ 
pūrvāṣāḍhāyāṃ khajhañā bhādra uttare | ravau śasau yajau tatra 
ṅañau caiva parasparam | ekena dvitīyaṃ biddhaṃ 
śubhāśubhagrahavyadhe | avarṇādisvaro vedhe naikavedhe 
dvayorvyadhaḥ | yugmasvarātmake vedhe cānusvāravisargayoḥ | 
koṇasthadhiṣṇyayormadhye antyādipādage grahe | asvarādicatuṣkasya 
vedhaḥ pūrvatithestathā | ekādikrūravedhena phalaṃ puṃsāṃ

bhramo'kṣare hāniḥ svare vyādhibhayaṃ tithau | rāśau biddhe

| krūrayuktaḥ punaḥ saumyo jñeyaḥ krūraphalapradaḥ | arkavedhe

vighnadau | candre miśraphalaṃ puṃsāṃ ratilābhaśca bhārgave |

sthānavaśāt svayam | etadeva phalaṃ jñeyaṃ saumyaiḥ 
krūrairviparyaye | sthānavedhasamāyoge yatsaṅkhyaṃ jāyate balaṃ | 
tatsaṅkhyaṃ vedhyavastūnāṃ balaṃ jñātvā phalaṃ vadet | 
vakragrahe phalaṃ dvighnaṃ triguṇaṃ svoccasaṃsthite | svabhāvajaṃ 
phalaṃ śīghre nīcasthe'rddhaphalo grahaḥ | tithirāśyaṃśanakṣatraṃ 
biddhaṃ krūragraheṇa yat | śubhāśubheṣu kāryeṣu varjanīyaṃ 
prayatnataḥ | na nandati vivāhe ca yātrāyāṃ na nivarttate | sa 
rogānmucyate rogī vedhavelākṛtodyamaḥ | rogakāle bhavedvedhaḥ 
krūrakhecarasambhavaḥ | vakragatyā bhavenmṛtyuḥ śīghre

p. 915) cāpyarujānvitaḥ | vedhasthāne bhavedbhaṅgo durge khaṇḍiḥ 
prajāyate | kavipraveśanaṃ tatra yodhāghātaśca tatra vai | yatra

p. 916) mṛtyurna sandeho rogādvāpi raṇe'pi vā | sūryabhāt 
pañcamaṃ dhiṣṇyaṃ jñeyaṃ vidyunmukhābhidham | 
śūlañcāṣṭatamaṃ proktaṃsannipātaṃ caturdaśam | 
ketumaṣṭādaśaṃ vidyādulkā syādekaviṃśatiḥ | dvāviṃśatitame 
kalpastrayoviṃśe ca vajrakam | nirghātaśca caturviṃśe 
uktāścāṣṭāvupagrahāḥ | prasthāne vighnadāḥ proktāḥ 
sarvakāryeṣu sarvadā | janmabhaṃ karma cādhānaṃ vaināśaṃ 
sāmudāyikam | sāṅghātikamidaṃ dhiṣṇyāṣṭakaṃ sarvajanīnakam |

phalaṃ brūhi saumyaiḥ krūraiḥ śubhāśubham | janmabhaṃ 
janmanakṣatraṃ daśamaṃ karmasaṃjñitam | ekaviṃśaṃ 
tathādhānaṃ trayoviṃśaṃ vināśakam | aṣṭādaśañca nakṣatraṃ 
sāmudāyikasaṃjñakam | sāṅghātikañca vijñeyaṃ ṛkṣaṃ 
ṣoḍaśameva hi | śarīraṃ rājyajātaṃ hi jātināmadhanābhidham | 
deśabhaṃ deśanāmarkṣaṃ rājyarkṣañcābhiṣekakam | mṛtyuḥ 
syājjanmabhe biddhe karmabhe kleśa eva ca | ādhānabhe pravāsaḥ 
syād vināśe varṇavigrahaḥ | sāmudāyikabhe'niṣṭaṃ hāniḥ 
sāṅghātike tathā | jātibhe kulanāśaḥ syād 
bandhanañcābhiṣekabhe | deśabhe deśabhaṅgaśca krūro varaṃ 
śubhaiḥ śubham | upagrahasamāyoge mṛtyurbhavati nānyathā | 
bhayaṃ bhaṅgaśca ghātaśca mṛtyubhaṅgayutā mṛtiḥ ||

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi yaduktaṃ brahmajāmale | 
ekāśītipade cakre grahavedhāt śubhāśubham | 
krūrairekādipañcāste yudhi vedhe phalaṃ labhet | tithirdhiṣṇyaṃ 
svarā rāśirvarṇaścaiva tu pañcamaḥ | yaddine bidhyate candrāt 
taddine syāt śubhāśubham | deśaḥ kālastataḥ paṇyamiti 
trīṇyarghyanirṇaye | cintanīyāni vedhāni sarvakālaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
