deśo'tha maṇḍalaṃ sthānamiti deśastridhocyate | varṣaṃ māsaṃ 
dinañceti trikāle'pi ca kathyate | dhāturmūlañca jīvaśca 
paṇyamevaṃ tridhā matam | atha trikatrayasyāsya vakṣyāmi

p. 917) svāmikhecarān | deśeśā rāhumandejyā maṇḍalasvāminaḥ 
punaḥ | ketusūryasitāḥ sthānanāthāścandrāracandrajāḥ | varṣeśā 
rāhuketvārkijīvā māsādhipāḥ punaḥ | bhaumo'rkastu sito 
jñeyaścandraḥ syāddivasādhipaḥ | dhātvīśāḥ sauripotārā jīveśā 
jñendusūrayaḥ | mūleśāḥ ketuśukrārkā iti paṇyādhipā grahāḥ | 
puṃgrahā rāhuketvarkajīvabhūmisutā matāḥ | strīgrahau 
sitaśītāṃśū saurisaumyau napuṃsakau | sitendū sitavarṇeśau 
rakteśau bhaumyabhāskarau | pītau saumyagurū kṛṣṇā 
rāhuketvarkajā matāḥ | grahe vakrodayo yaḥ syādudaye ca balādhikaḥ 
| deśādīnāṃ sa evaikaḥ svāmikheṭastathā mataḥ | vakrodayaḥ 
svaharmyeṣu pūrṇavīryo bhaved grahaḥ | tadagrapṛṣṭhage kheṭe balaṃ 
trairāśikaṃ matam | uccasthe ca balaṃ pūrṇaṃ nīcāṃśasthe balaṃ 
dalam | trairāśikavaśājjñeyamantare tu balaṃ budhaiḥ | evaṃ 
deśādināthānāṃ vedho jñeyo gṛhaṃ prati | suhṛdaḥ śatravo 
madhyāścintanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ | svamitrasamaśatrūṇāṃ 
vedhāddeśādike kramāt | śubho grahaḥ śubhaṃ datte 
catustridvyekapādataḥ | duṣṭaṃ duṣṭagrahaḥ 
kuryādekadvitricatuṣpadaiḥ | biddhaḥ pūrṇadṛśā paśyan tatpādena 
phalaṃ grahaḥ | vidadhātyanyathā jñeyaṃ phala dṛṣṭyanusārataḥ | 
varṇādisvararāśīnāṃ meṣādye rāśimaṇḍale | grahadṛṣṭivaśāt 
so'pi vedho varṇādike mataḥ | svaravarṇāḥ svacakroktāstithivedhasya 
pīḍakāḥ | tithau varṇe ca rāśau ca svadṛṣṭyā dṛṣṭijaṃ phalam | 
aśubho vā śubho vāpi śukro vedhyatithau grahaḥ | sarvaṃ nijaphalaṃ 
datte kṛṣṇapakṣe tu tadbalam | kheṭakasyāṃśako jñeyā pūrṇā 
dṛṣṭiḥ sadā budhaiḥ | dṛṣṭihīne punarvedhe na syāt 
kiñcicchubhāśubham | saumyaḥ pūrṇadṛśā paśyan bidhyan 
varṇādipañcake | phalaṃ viṃśopakān pañca krūraśca caturo diśet | 
vedhyavarṇādike yāvat sthāne vedhe ca yāvatau | dṛṣṭistadanumānena 
vācyā viṃśopakābudhaiḥ | evaṃ viṃśopakā yatra sambhavanti

p. 918) śubhāśubham | tasmādevaṃ vidhānena teṣāṃ jñeyaṃ 
śubhāśubham | varttamānārghyaviṃśāṃśakalpanā teṣu ca kramāt 
| varttamānārghyake dadyādutpādya ca śubhāśubham | 
deśadhvaṃsaḥ prajāpīḍā mṛtiḥ prāṇibadhastathā | yatra dṛṣṭistu 
tatra syād durbhiṣaṃ maṇḍale sphuṭam | akāle'pi phala puṣpaṃ 
vṛkṣāṇāṃ yatra jāyate | svajātimāṃsabhuktiśca durbhikṣaṃ tatra 
rauravam | paracakrāgamastatra durbhikṣañca svarājyake | ṛtau 
viparyayo yatra durbhikṣaṃ maṇḍale bhavet | bhūmikampo rajaḥpāto 
raktapātaśca jāyate | deśe sarvasukhopete vedhādarghyo bhavedbhayam 
| dīpo yathā gṛhasyāntarudvyotayati sarvataḥ | tasyedaṃ 
sarvatobhdracakraṃ jñānaprakāśakam || 

asya vyākhyānaṃ prakriyā saṅketaścocyate ūrddhvageti | 
pūrvapaścimakrameṇa rekhādaśakaṃ vinyasyottaradakṣiṇakrameṇa 
punastatra rekhādaśakaṃ nyaset | etenaikāśītiḥ koṣṭhakāni bhavanti | 
tatra koṣṭhe īśānādikoṇakoṣṭheṣu sṛṣṭimārgeṇādāvīśānakoṇe 
tataścāgnikoṇe tato nairṛte tataśca vāyavye evaṃ caturāvṛttyā 
akārādisvarān likhet | pūrvadigavadhisaptasaptakrameṇa 
kṛttikādīnyaṣṭāviṃśatinakṣatrāṇi caturdikṣu prathamapaṃktau 
likhet | tadadhaḥ paṃktau avakahetyādivarṇaviṃśatikaṃ 
tadadhaḥpaṃktau vṛṣādirāśidvādaśakañca likhet | 
avaśiṣṭakoṣṭhapañcake nandāditithipañcakaṃ likhet | tatreśāne a u 
ḷ o iti svaracatuṣkam | āgneye ā ī ḹ au iti svaracatuṣkam | nairṛte i ṛ e 
aṃ iti svaracatuṣkam | vāyavye ī ṝ ai aḥ iti svaracatuṣkañca likhet | 
tataḥ pūrvadiśi prathame paṃktau kṛttikādīni sapta ṛkṣāṇi 
dvitīyapaṃktau avakahaḍeti tṛtīyapaṃktau vṛṣamithunakarkaṭeti | 
tadadhaḥkoṣṭhe nandāditithiñca likhet | tato dakṣiṇadiśi pūrvavat

p. 919) paṃktikrameṇa madhādīni maṭapavateti siṃhakanyātuleti 
bhadrāñca likhet | paścimāyāṃ diśi pūrvavat tatra nandākoṣṭhe 
ravimaṇḍalau bhadrākoṣṭhe indubudhau jayākoṣṭhe guruṃ 
riktākoṣṭhe śukraṃ pūrṇākoṣṭhe śaniṃ likhet | grahāṇāṃ 
balavijñānāya uccanocasthānamāha ravermeṣa ityādi || grahādhīnā 
hi sakalaśubhāśubhacintetyato grahāṇāṃ krūrasaumyatvamāha 
śanyarketi | śaniravirāhuketumaṅgalāḥ krūrāśca budhaguruśukrāḥ 
śubhāḥ | budhastu krūrayuktaḥ pāpaḥ | evaṃ kṣīṇacandraḥ 
kṣīṇastu jyotiṣe | kṛṣṇāṣṭamīdalādūrddhvaṃ yāvat 
śuklāṣṭamīdalam | tāvadeva śaśī kṣīṇaḥ pūrṇaḥ syādanyataḥ 
śaśī || iti | etatkathanaprayojanamāha yasminniti | yatra cakre 
grahāśrayanakṣatrasya vāmasmmukhadakṣiṇakrameṇa nakṣatreṇa saha 
vedhatrayaṃ bhavet | bhuktagrahāśrayanakṣatre pūrvaṃ bhogyaṃ 
tannakṣatrāt param ākrāntaṃ graheṇādhiṣṭhitam | tatra karma na 
karttavyamityarthaḥ || 

	dṛṣṭiphalaṃ kathayati vakrageti || maṅgalādigrahapañcake 
vakragāmiṇi sati dakṣiṇā dṛṣṭirjñātavyā | atra dakṣiṇavedhaphalaṃ 
vācyaṃ śīghragāmiṇi vāmadṛṣṭirvāmavedhaphalaṃ vācyam | 
madhyacāre prakṛtagatau madhyā sammukhe dṛṣṭirjñeyā tatra 
sammukhavedhaphalaṃ vācyam | grahāṇāṃ 
vakraśīghrādisamayamāha sūryayuktaṃ iti | sūryeṇa saha 
ekarāśisthā grahā aṃśaviśeṣe astā bhavanti | uktañca jyotistattve | 
sūryeṇa yuktā agrimarāśiṃ gatā uditāḥ syuḥ | sūrye dvitīyage 
grahākrāntarāśeragrimarāśigate sūrye tu grahāḥ śīghrāḥ 
śīghragatayo bhavanti | evaṃ krameṇa tṛtīyarāśige sūrye grahāḥ 
samāḥ prakṛtagatayo bhavanti | caturthe sūrye 
mandāścakrapūrvasamagatayo bhavanti | evaṃ pañcamarāśige 
sūrye'pi saptamāṣṭamarāśige sūrye grahā ativakragatayo bhavanti | 
evaṃ navamadaśamarāśige sūrye grahāṇāmakuṭilā sahajā 
gatirjāyate | ekādaśadvādaśarāśige sūrye grahāḥ śīghragatayo

p. 920) bhavanti | eṣā vakraśīghrādigatistu 
maṅgalādipañcakānāmeva | anyeṣāṃ gativiśeṣamāha rāhuketū 
iti | rāhuketucandrasūryāṇāmekagatitvāt sadā dṛṣṭitrayaṃ 
vāmadakṣiṇamadhyadṛṣṭiḥ sadaiva bhavati | krūrā ityādi | 
śunyādigrahā vakragatayaḥ santo mahākrūrā evaṃ saumyāḥ 
śubhagrahā vakrāḥ santo mahāśubhā bhavanti | tathā ca tayoḥ 
śubhāśubhayorvakragatyorddṛṣṭyanukrameṇa śubhaṃ viruddhañca 
phalaṃ bhavati | sahajagatyā prakṛtagatyā svabhāve svarāśau sthitā 
vakrā vā ubhayathāpi saumyāḥ śubhagrahā mahāśubhā bhavanti | 
evaṃ saumyāḥ krūrāśca grahāḥ śīghragāḥ santo'pi śubhā 
bhavanti ka pa bha da ityakṣaracatuṣṭaye biddhe dhṛti gha ṅa 
chetyakṣaratrikaṃ ṣa ṇa ṭheti dha pha ḍeti trikaṃ tha jha ñeti trikaṃ 
yathākramaṃ biddhaṃ bhavati | tathā ca ghakāre biddhe gha ṅa cheti 
varṇatrayaṃ biddhaṃ bhavati | evaṃ pakāre biddhe ṣa ṇa ṭheti 
varṇatrayaṃ bhakāre biddhe dha pha ḍeti varṇatrayaṃ dakāre biddhe 
tha jha ñeti varṇatrayaṃ krameṇa biddhaṃ bhavati | ārdrānakṣatre 
biddhe sati ka gha ṅa cheti varṇacatuṣkaṃ biddham | evaṃ 
hastānakṣatre biddhe pa ṣa ṇa ṭhetyakṣaracatuṣkaṃ 
pūrvāṣāḍhānakṣatre biddhe bha dha pha ḍeti varṇacatuṣkaṃ 
uttarabhādranakṣatre biddhe da tha dha ñeti varṇacatuṣkaṃ kramād 
biddhaṃ bhavati | ravāvityādivacanopāttau ekavibhaktyantau yau dvau 
dvau varṇau staḥ tayormadhye śubhāśubhagrahairekasmin biddhe sati 
aparamapi biddhaṃ bhavati | evamanusvāravisargayorekasmin 
biddhe'paramapi ||

koṇastheti || koṇasthanakṣatrayoḥ 
bharaṇīkṛttikayoraśleṣāmaghayorviśākhānurādhayoḥ 
śravaṇādhaniṣṭhayorantyādyapādagate grahe sati 
pratyekamakārādisvaracatuṣkeṣu pūrṇātitheḥ 
pañcamīdaśamīpaurṇamāsyamāvasyānāṃ vedho bhavati | yathā 
bharaṇyā antyapāde kṛttikāyā ādyapāde yadi graho varttate tadā

p. 921) akārādayaḥ svarāścatvāro biddhāḥ pūrṇātithyā saha 
bhavanti evamanyeṣvapi koṇeṣu jñeyam || eketyādi || ekakrūravedhe 
udvegaḥ dvivedhe bhayaṃ trivedhe hāniścaturvedhe rogaḥ pañcavedhe 
mṛtyuḥ || ṛkṣetyādi || pāpagraheṇa nakṣatre biddhe sati bhramo 
buddhibhraṃśaḥ | anyat sugamam || yuddhe viśeṣamāha eketi | 
nāmavarṇādike ekapāpena biddhe yuddhe bhayaṃ dvābhyāṃ 
dhanahānistribhirbhaṅgaścaturbhirmṛtyurityarthaḥ || 
śubhagrahaphalamāha yatheti || arketyādi || bhṛgusuto maṅgalaḥ sauriḥ 
śaniḥ | candretyādi | yadi candraḥ kṣīṇastadā pāpaphalaṃ yadā 
pūrṇaścandrastadā śubhaphalaṃ sarvaprakāreṇa śubhaphaladaḥ | 
grahāṇāṃ yādṛgbalaṃ tādṛk phalaṃ syāditi balaṃ darśayati 
svakṣetre ityādi sugamam | idañceti sthānabalaṃ 
śubhāśubhagrahāṇāṃ tulyam | phalantu viparyayeṇa jñeyam | yadā 
pāpagrahaḥ pūrṇabalastadā phalaṃ mandaṃ yadā śubhagrahaḥ 
pūrṇabalastadā atīva śubham || tithītyādi || 
tithirāśinavāṃśakrameṇa nakṣatrañca yat krūragraheṇa biddhaṃ 
yattadvarjyaṃ tatra śubhakrama na karttavyamityarthaḥ | vedhaphalamāha 
na nandatītyādi sugamam | biddhanakṣatrādau rājñā durge kṛte 
khaṇḍiḥ khaṇḍanaṃ jāyate tasya bhaṅgo bhavatīti tātparyam | 
kaviḍākārīti prasiddhastasya tasya praveśanaṃ biddhanakṣatradikkṛta 
durge bhavati | evaṃ tatra koṣṭhe kṛte yodhānāmavaghātaśca | atra tu 
yat senāṅganāmādyavarṇo biddhapuruṣasya durgādervā 
pāpagrahabiddho bhavati ityapyūhyam | yatreti yatra pūrvādidiśi 
vṛṣāditrirāśigo ravirbhavati sā digastamitā jñeyā ityarthaḥ | śeṣā 
diśa uditā bhavanti | īśāneti || īśānadiksthāḥ svarāḥ a u ḷ o evaṃ 
rūpāścatvāraḥ prācyāṃ tatra sthitā jñeyāstasyāmastamitāyāṃ 
te'pyastamitā jñeyā ityarthaḥ | evamanyatra nakṣatrāṇīti ||

p. 922) yatra diśi rāśitrayabhogakrameṇa sūryaḥ sthitastatrasthā 
nakṣatravarṇarāśitithivārāḥ sarve'stamitā bhavanti | tatphalamāha 
nakṣatreti | sugamam || yātreti || śubhaṃ yātrādi aśubhaṃ maraṇādi 
astadigabhimukhaṃ narairna kāryamityarthaḥ || astāśāyāmiti || yasya 
puṃso nāmākṣaramastadiśisthaṃ bhavati sa daivahata iva jñeyaḥ | 
karāviti sugamam || padāvabhimatalābhaḥ praśneti | praśnalagnaṃ 
aśubhabiddhañcettadā aśubhaṃ śubhabiddhañcettadā śubhaṃ 
śubhāśubhabiddhañcettadā miśram || grahābiddhetyādi || 
graheṇābiddhaṃ lagnaṃ carastiradvyātmakarūpaṃ 
grahadṛṣṭyanusāreṇa ca yadyat phalaṃ svabhāvajaphalañca 
vācyamityarthaḥ | ayamabhiprāyaḥ || yallagnamasmin cakre 
graheṇābiddhaṃ syāt tasya kendragatadṛṣṭyanusāreṇa phalaṃ 
vācyam | kintu sa graho yadi svābhivyatiriktapāpaḥ syāt tadā 
kāryasyāsiddhirvācyā yadi sa śubhastadā 
kāryasiddhirmiśraścenmiśraphalaṃ vācyam ||

carādisaṃjñā dīpikāyām || carasthiradvyātmakanāmadheyā 
meṣādayo'mī kramaśaḥ pradiṣṭā iti || krūrairityādi || 
pāpagrahairyasya varṇādayaḥ pañcamotkramābhyāṃ 
maṇḍalādikaṃ pāpabiddhañcettadā vinaśyati | maṇḍalaṃ deśaḥ || 
nagaraṃ rājasthitisthānam | durgaṃ koṭādi | devālayaḥ prasiddhaḥ | 
puraṃ rājanāmacihnitarājadhānī | kṛttiketyādi kūrmacakre trīṇi 
trīṇi kṛtvā saptaviṃśatinakṣatrāṇi navabhāgakrameṇāṅgāni 
vyavastheyāni | tatra kṛttikādinakṣatratrike krūrabiddhe sati 
nābhisthadeśā naśyanti evamanyatra | ayamabhiprāyaḥ  kūrmacakre 
sarvatobhadracakre ca yadi pāpabiddhaṃ nakṣatraṃ syāttadā 
tannakṣatrāṅkitadeśā naśyanti mahopadrutā bhavanti | ekacakre 
biddhañcettadā kiñcidupadrutā iti || kṛttikāyāmiti || kṛttikāyāṃ 
biddhāyāṃ brāhmaṇo bidhyati evaṃ puṣye biddhe kṣatriyo bidhyati 
revatyāṃ vaiśyaḥ punarvasau śudraśca | tatra  śubhabiddhe śubhaṃ 

p. 923) pāpabiddhe aśubhaṃ miśre miśraphalam | matāntare 
tvanyavedhenāpi yadāha varāhaḥ | pūrvātrayaṃ sānalamagrajānāṃ 
rājñāntu puṣyeṇa sahottarāṇi | sapauṣṇamaitraṃ pitṛdaivatañca 
prajāpaterbhañca kṛṣīvalānām | ānityahastābhijidaśvibhāni vaṇig 
janānāṃ pravadanti bhāni | mūlatrinetrānilavāruṇāni 
bhānyugrajāteḥ prabhaviṣṇutāyām || saumyenducitrāvasudaivatāni 
sevājanasvāmyamupāgatāni | sarpaṃ viśākhā 
śravaṇābharaṇyaścāṇḍālajāterapi nirdiśanti || taulyamiti || taulyaṃ 
tulanārhadravyaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ ratnaṃ raso ghṛtatailādi | dhānyaṃ 
śasyaṃ mahārghyatāmadhikanyūnatāmityarthaḥ | śubhavedhe 
subhikṣamiti | krūravedha iti sugamam || upagrahamāha sūryabhāditi 
sugamam | nāḍīnakṣatramāha janmani bheti | idaṃ nakṣatraṣaṭkaṃ 
sarvajanasādhāraṇaṃ jñātavyam | jātibhamuktam | deśabhaṃ 
deśanāmādyakṣareṇa śatapadacakre jñātavyam | yasminnṛkṣe 
rājño'bhiṣeko bhavati tannakṣatramabhiṣekabham | tatra 
krūravedhaphalamuktaṃ śubhaphalaṃ vācyam | tithiriti yasmin dine 
śubhāśubhavedhe sati candravedhena tadbidhyate tithyādipañcakeṣu 
yatra yadā candravedhastadā tasya kathitaphalaṃ vācyam | 
tithyādipañcake biddhe candrayogo yadā bhavet | taduktaṃ phalaṃ tadā 
vācyaṃ taddine grahasambhavam || deśa iti || paṇyaṃ vikreyadravyam | 
dhāturiti dhātuḥ svarṇādipṛthivyantaḥ | mūlaṃ vṛkṣādigulmāntam | 
jīvo manuṣyādikīṭaparyantaḥ | deśetyādi rāhuśanivṛhaspatayaḥ 
deśasvarāḥ | keturaviśukrā maṇḍaleśāḥ | candramaṇḍalabudhāḥ 
sthānapatayaḥ | rāhuketuśanivṛhaspatayo varṣādhipāḥ | 
ravibhaumabudhaśukrā māsādhipāḥ | candro divasādhipaḥ | 
śanirāhukujā dhātvadhipāḥ | budhacandravṛhaspatayo jīveśāḥ | 
raviketuśukrāmūleśāḥ | rāhuketuravijīvamaṅgalāḥ 
puruṣādhipāḥ | śukracandrau strīgrahau | śanibudhau napuṃsakau |

p. 924) gṛhe iti | gṛhe svagṛhe svasvarāśau yathā siṃhe sūrya ityādi 
vakre bhuktāṃśeṣu parāvṛtya gamane svaharmyeṣu svagṛheṣu 
tatpūrvaparagṛhe'pi pūrṇabala iti trairāśikaṃ balam | ucceti 
dalamarddham | antare uccanīcamadhye trairāśikavaśādanupātena 
balaṃ jñeyam | anupāto yathā | uccāvadhipratirāśitriṃśāṃśake 
pratyekaṃ daśa daśa vikalā hānirevaṃ 
nīcāvadhipratirāśitriṃśāṃśake pratyekaṃ daśa daśa vikalā 
vṛddhiriti | evamiti | tattatprabhutvaviṣayakadeśādau vedhacintā kāryā 
ityarthaḥ | evaṃ gṛhaṃ prati vedhakā grahāḥ ke suhṛdaḥ ke vā 
śatravaḥ ke vā madhyā iti cintanīyam | yathā maṇḍalasvāmī sūryaḥ 
tasya mitrāṇi candrakujaguravaḥ | tairyadi maṇḍalaṃ biddhaṃ tadā 
śubhaphalam | saumyo budhastasya vedhānna niṣiddhaṃ śaniśukrau 
śatrū tayorvedhādviruddhaphalamevamanyeṣvapi jñeyam | svamitreti 
deśādike deśamaṇḍalapaṇye svasya mitrasya samasya śatrośca 
vedhāt śubhagrahaḥ śubhaphalaṃ dadāti catustridvyekapādakaiḥ | 
tathā ca svavedhe pūrṇaṃ mitravedhe pādonaṃ samavedhe'rddhaṃ 
śatruvedhe pādaphalamiti | duṣṭagraho viruddhaphalaṃ datte tathā ca 
svavedhe pādaphalaṃ mitravedhe arddhaphalaṃ samavedhe pādonaṃ 
śatruvedhe pūrṇam | biddhamiti biddharāśiṃ pūrṇadṛṣṭyā paśyan 
yadrāśyakṣaraṃ yadā biddham karoti tadā pādena phalaṃ vidadhāti 
dṛṣṭyanusārato'tra viparītakramo yathā | pūrṇadṛṣṭyātra 
kapālaphalaṃ tathā pādadṛṣṭyā pūrṇaphalam | arddhadṛṣṭyā 
pādatrayaṃ pādatrayadṛṣṭyā arddham || varṇeti || 
meṣādidvādaśarāśiṣu varṇa ādibhūto yeṣāṃ a i u e o ityeteṣāṃ 
varṇasaṅghaṭanato ravirbudho vā evaṃ krameṇa 
rāśyaṃśakavyavasthitarāśīnāṃ grahadṛṣṭivaśādvedhaphaladaḥ 
so'pi vedho varṇādiko mataḥ | yatrāṃśakavedhastatra 
tannavāṃśavarṇasvarayorapi vedhastatrāpi phalaṃ biddhanavāṃśaka 
rāśau grahasya dṛṣṭivaśena | svaravarṇeti || 

p. 925) eṣāṃ ye ca varṇāste svaracakroktā nāmajāḥ svīyacakre 
kathitāḥ | kakārādināmnaḥ kakāro varṇaḥ akārasvaraḥ evaṃ 
svasvavarṇāḥ pāpagrahabiddhāḥ pīḍakāstithivedhāścaitādṛśāḥ 
pīḍakā eva | evaṃ tithau yo varṇo yaśca rāśistatrāpi 
śubhāśubhagrahavedhāt svadṛṣṭyā pādadṛṣṭikrameṇa dṛṣṭijaṃ 
phalaṃ jñeyam | aśubheti || pāpagrahaḥ śubhagraho vā śuklapakṣe 
tithivedhaṃ kurvan sarvaṃ svakīyaphalaṃ dṛṣṭikalpitaṃ datte 
kṛṣṇapakṣe tu tadarddhamiti | kheṭeti | grahasya aṃśake pūrṇā 
dṛṣṭiḥ paṇḍitaiḥ sadā cintanīyā yato dṛṣṭihīne vedhe punaḥ 
kiñcidapi śubhāśubhaphalaṃ na syāditi hetugarbheṇa yojanā | 
saumyeti || evaṃ prakāreṇa varṇādipañcake 
varṇasvararkṣarāśitithyātmake biddhe sati graho vedhakaḥ 
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ datte | saumya iti || diśi 
sarvatobhadracakrapūrvādau varṇādipañcake pūrṇadṛśā paśyan 
bidhyan pañcaviṃśopakān patitapādaṃ 
pañcapañcaviṃśopakasaṃjñakaṃ phalaṃ śubhagraho dadāti | 
krūragrahaḥ patipādaṃ caturviṃśopakān phalaṃ dadāti | 
vṛṣādirāśitraye avadahaḍā varṇāḥ kṛttikādīni saptabhāni 
siṃhāditraye maṭapababhā varṇā maghādīni saptabhāni 
vṛścikāditraye ṇaṣabhajasvā varṇā anurādhādīni saptabhāni 
kumbhāditraye gaśadacalā varṇā dhaniṣṭhādīni saptabhāni evaṃ 
nakṣatrāṇi varṇāśca rāśīnāṃ jñeyāḥ 

	yadi paśyanti te kheṭāḥ pādadṛṣṭyā prakalpayet | 
biṃśopakāḥ syuḥ pañcaiva pratipādaṃ śubhagrahaiḥ | 
aśubhairvedamitā jñeyāḥ pādadṛṣṭyā vicakṣaṇairiti || vedhe iti  || 
varṇādipañcake vedhe yāvaddṛṣṭibalaṃ sthānavedhe ca yāvatī 
dṛṣṭistadanumānena budhairviṃśopakāvācyā iti | evamiti || evaṃ 
prakāreṇa yatrāśubhe śubhe vā grahe dṛṣṭyādibalabhedena 
viṃśopakā vyavasthākrameṇa sambhavanti śubhagrahaviṃśopakā

p. 926) ekatra dhāryā aśubhagrahaviṃśopakā aparatra dhāryāḥ | 
teṣāṃ madhye parasparaṃ ye yasmānnyūnāste adhike 
pariśodhanīyāḥ | śeṣaṃ yaddṛśyaṃ taccihnitaṃ śubhamaśubhaṃ 
vā phalaṃ vācyam | yatra yāvatī dṛṣṭirityasyārtho vivicya likhyate | 
dvitīye daśame vā vedhe pañcaviṃśopakāḥ | navame pañcame vā 
daśaviṃśopakāḥ | caturthe'ṣṭame vā pratyekaṃ pañcadaśa | 
samasaptake viṃśatiḥ || tathā ca dṛṣṭikathane jyotiḥśāstram | 
pādaikadṛṣṭirdaśame tṛtīye dvipādadṛṣṭirnavapañcake ca | 
dvipādadṛṣṭiścaturaṣṭake ca sampūrṇadṛṣṭiḥ samasaptake ceti | 
sarvakrūragrahāṇāṃ pādavṛddhyā catvāraścatvāro viṃśopakā 
jāyante | iti sampūrṇadṛṣṭau ṣoḍaśaviṃśopakā bhavanti | evaṃ 
krameṇa yatra yatra ye viṃśopakāḥ śubhātmakā aśubhātmakā 
bhavanti tatra śubhagrahaviṃśopakān ekatraiva 
paracakrakrūragrahaviṃśopakān likhitvā yo yatra patati taṃ tatra 
pātayitvā avaśiṣṭau yadi śubhagrahasya tadanumānena śubham | 
pāpagrahasya cettadā aśubhaṃ phalaṃ bhavati ||

varttamānetyādi | teṣu vikreyadravyeṣu 
varttamānadhānyādidravyamūlye 
tannāmanakṣatrasvaravarṇatithirāśibhedena pūrvoktaprakāreṇa 
viṃśāṃśakalpanā anyonyapariśodhanaparyantā kāryā | 
tato'vaśiṣṭaviṃśopakabhāge parikalpanena śubhagrahādiṣve 
kāvaśeṣe viṃśatyaṃśo mūlye hrasati | tadviśeṣe dvau bhāgau 
hrasataḥ | evamadhike cintanīyam | yatra deśādervarṇādipañcakaṃ 
śubhakrūraviṃśopakaśeṣa evaṃ krameṇa 
ekadvitryādiviṃśāntabhāgānmadhye adhikīkriyate | 
mūlyamevādhikaṃ bhavati śubhāśubhañcotpādyam | evaṃ 
yatrādau pāpagrahavedhastatrāśubhaṃ kiñcidvastu vedhādapi 
cintanīyam | yatra deśādervarṇādipañcakaṃ śubhamātreṇa biddhaṃ 
tatrātīva śubham | yatra pāpamātreṇa tatrātīvāśubham | yatra 
bhūtavedhastatra dṛṣṭibhedena viṃśopakān parikalpya

p. 927) śubhāśubhaviṃśopakācchubhamaśubhaphalaṃ vācyam | 
deśa ityādi yatra deśe krūragrahavedhe sati taddṛṣṭiradhikā 
kathitaprakāreṇa sampadyate tasya vināśaḥ | evaṃ tasmin deśe 
prajāpīḍāprakāropadravaḥ | rājavināśo maṇḍale ca durbhikṣam | 
kūrmacakre pāpagrahapīḍā yatra sarvatobhadre'pi pāpagrahapīḍā 
tasyāvaśyaṃ deśādervināśaḥ prajāpīḍādikañca phalaṃ bhavati | 
etatsakalanimittānyupadarśayati | akāle'pītyādi | yatra deśādau 
vṛkṣāṇāmakāle puṣpādyudgamanasamayānyasamaye puṣpaṃ 
phalaṃ vā bhavati tatra svajātimāṃsabhuktirmanuṣyamāṃsaṃ 
manuṣyo bhuṃkte evamanyajātīyānāmapi tatra rauravaṃ 
bhayānakaṃ durbhikṣaṃ yatraiva bhavati tatra paracakrāgamo'pi 
bhavati | surājake śobhanarājñi api deśe durbhikṣam | ṛtorityādi | yatra 
deśe ṛtorviparyayaḥ anyādṛśaḥ kramaḥ 
pauṣādisambhāvitakuhelikādirgrīṣmādau sambhavati yathā 
sādhāraṇakrame śītalasamaye uṣṇatā grīṣmādau śaityam | anyattu 
dharmavaddhetuviparyayo vā tatrāpi durbhiṣaṃ bhavati | bhūkampa 
ityādi | sarvasukhopetadeśe vedhāt pāpagrahakṛto dīrghabhūmikampo 
rajaḥpāto dhūlīvṛṣṭiḥ raktavṛṣṭirvā jāyate || dīpo yatheti | idaṃ 
sarvatobhadracakraṃ tathā jñānaprakāśakam | yathā dīpo 
gṛhasyāntarmadhyaṃ dyotayati | yathā dīpadyotavaśena 
gṛhāntarvarttidravyajñānaṃ bhavati tathāsmāt sarvatobhadracakrāt 
sarvaviṣayakajñānamutpadyate ||

cakraṃ śatapadaṃ vakṣye ṛkṣāṃśākṣarasambhavam | 
nāmādivarṇato jñeyā ṛkṣarāśyaṃśakāstathā | 
tiryagūrddhvagatā rekhā rudrasaṅkhyā likhedbudhaḥ | jāyate 
koṣṭhakānāntu śatamekaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | vinyasyāvakahaḍādīni 
rudrādividiśi kramāt | pañca pañca krameṇaiva śuddhavarṇān 
niyojayet | pañcasvarasamāyogādekaikaṃ pañcadhā kuru | 

p. 928) kuryāt kupubhudusthāne trīṇi trīṇyarakṣarāṇi ca | 
kughaṅasthā bhavet stambhe raudre tvīśānayogake | puṣaṇaṭho bhavet 
stambhe haste āgneyasaṃjñite | bhavedbhudhaphaḍhāḥ stambhe 
pūrvāṣāḍhe ca nairṛte | hathajhañā bhavet stambhe vāyavye bhādra 
uttare | evaṃ stambhacatuṣkañca jñātavyaṃ svaravedibhiḥ dhiṣṇyāni 
kṛttikādīni pratyekaṃ caturakṣaraiḥ | sābhijityaṃ śakastatra śataikaṃ 
dvādaśādhikam | yadṛkṣāṃśakakoṣṭhasthaḥ krūraḥ saumyo'pi vā 
grahaḥ | saumyavedhe śubhaṃ jñeyamaśubhaṃ krūrakhecaraiḥ | 
miśrairmiśraphalaṃ tatra nirvedhena śubhāśubham | yaduktaṃ 
sarvatobhadre grahopagrahavedhataḥ | śubhāśuphalaṃ sarvaṃ tadihāpi 
vicintayet || 

asyārthaḥ | śatapadaṃ koṣṭhacakraṃ vakṣye kimbhūtaṃ 
ṛkṣāṃśākṣarasambhavam | ṛkṣasya nakṣatrasya ye'ṃśāḥ 
pādarūpāsteṣāṃ yānyakṣarāṇi vakṣyamāṇāni taiḥ sambhavo 
yasya tat | tattadakṣaraghaṭitamityarthaḥ | nāmādivarṇena 
nakṣatrasambandhāṃśo jñeyaḥ | cakranyāsamāha tiryagityādi | 
rudrasaṃkhyā ekādaśasaṃkhyāḥ | rekhāstiryagūrddhvagatāḥ 
pūrvapaścimakremeṇaikādaśa dakṣiṇottarakrameṇāpyekādaśa 
likhet | īśāṇakoṇamārabhya pūrvapaṃktau 
prathamakoṣṭhapañcakeṣu avakahaḍeti varṇapañcakaṃ 
śuddhamakārasvaramātrayuktaṃ niyojayet likhet | 
evamāgneyakoṇādyārabhya dakṣiṇapaścimottarapaṃktiṣu 
koṣṭhapañcakeṣu maṭaparateti layabhajasveti gaśadacaleti 
varṇapañcakaṃ krameṇa likhet | a-i-u-e-o iti pañcasvarayogena 
likhitavarṇaḥ pañcadhātuḥ | a-i-u-e-o vavivuvevo iti krameṇādho'dho 
likhediti tātparyārthaḥ | likhitamadhye kupubhudusthāne anyāni trīṇi 
trīṇyakṣarāṇi kuryāllikhedityarthaḥ | īśānakoṇāvasthitastambhe 
ārdrānakṣatre kughaṅacha iti varṇacatuṣkam evamagnikoṇe 
hastānakṣatre puṣaṇaṭheti varṇacatuṣkaṃ nairṛtakoṇastambhe

p. 929) pūrvāṣāḍhānakṣatre bhudhaphaḍheti varṇacatuṣkaṃ 
vāyukoṇastambhe uttarabhādrapadanakṣatre hathajhañeti 
varṇacatuṣkam | evaṃ pratyekaṃ varṇacatuṣṭayaṃ mātrādicatuṣṭaye 
vyavasthāpakasvaravedibhiḥ stambhacatuṣṭayaṃ jñātavyam | 
dhiṣṇyānītyādi | nyastākṣareṣvakāramārabhya 
caturbhiścaturbhirakṣaraiḥ sābhijitsahitāni 
kṛttikādīnyaṣṭāviṃśatinakṣatrāṇi bhavanti | tatra pratinakṣatraṃ 
catvāraścatvāro'ṃśā dvādaśādhikaśatasaṅkhyā bhavanti | etena 
a i u e kṛttikā | o va vī vu rohiṇī | ve vo ka ki mṛgaśirāḥ | kugha ṅa 
cha ārdrā | ke ko ha hi punarvasuḥ | hu he ho ḍa puṣyā | ḍi ḍu ḍe ḍo 
aśleṣā | ma mi mu me maghā | mo ṭa ṭi ṭu pūrvaphalgunī | ṭe ṭo pa pi 
uttaraphalgunī | pu ṣa ṇa ṭha hastā | pe po va vi citrā | vyavevota svātī | 
ti tu te to viśākhā | la li lu le anurādhā | no yā ye yu jyeṣṭhā | ye yo 
bha bhi mūlā | bhu dha pha ḍha pūrvāṣāḍhā | bhe bho ja ji 
uttarāṣāḍhā | ju je jo tha abhijit | khi khu khe kho śravaṇā | ga gi ge go 
dhaniṣṭhā | śo śa śi śu śatabhiṣā | śe śo da di pūrvabhādrapadam 
| ha kha jha ña uttarabhādrapadam | de do ca ci revatī | cu ce co la aśvinī 
| li lu la lo bharaṇī | ityaṣṭāviṃśatinakṣatrāṇi ||

	prayojanamupadarśayati yadṛkṣāṃśaka ityādi | krūraḥ saumyo 
vā grahaḥ yasminnakṣatre sthitastasmāt punastiryakkrameṇa puṃsaḥ 
puruṣasya nāmādyakṣaraṃ vedhayet | yadīti yojanīyam | 
vedhaphalamāha saumyavedha ityādi | śubhavedhe śubhaṃ pāpavedhe 
tvaśubhamiti | miśraiḥ śubhapāpairvedhe miśraṃ śubhāśubham | 
vedhaśūnyena kimapīti | sarvatobhadracakre grahato grahavedhato yat 
phalamuktaṃ tadatrāpi | pepīvavīti citrānakṣatraṃ tena 
rāmatoṣaṇanāmnaścitrānakṣatraṃ 
tṛtīyapādāśritatulārāśirevamanyanāmno'pi nakṣatraṃyam | 
praśne tu praṣṭāraṃ dṛṣṭvā oṃ manaścintitavastūnāṃ

p. 930) praṣṭurāsyena bhāṣiṇīm | tāṃ varṇarūpiṇīṃ vande 
bhāratīṃ bhaktavatsalām | ityanena sarasvatīṃ natvā manasā 
śatadhā vāgbhavaṃ svīyeṣṭamantraṃ vā japtvā 
kimarthamatrāgatastvaṃ kathayetyādi pṛcchet | tataḥ praṣṭā 
prathamaṃ yadvadiṣyati tasyādyakṣareṇa lagnanavāṃśakaiḥ jñātvā 
grahavedhavaśena grahadṛṣṭivaśena ca dhātumūlajīvādikaṃ 
varṇākārādikaṃ tacchubhāśubhañca vācyamiti ||

trayodaśorddhvagā rekhāstiryagrekhāstathā daśa | bhaveyuḥ 
koṣṭhakāstatra saṃkhyāyāścottaraṃ śatam | kavargaṃ 
navadhālikhya koṣṭhake prathame'ṣṭame | dvitīye saptame cādyā 
naṭābhyāṃ tasya triṣaṣṭhake | yaśau vargau caturthe ca tathā 
vargañca pañcame | navadvādaśake tādyān śeṣe pādyān 
dvikoṣṭhake | caturakṣarasaṃyoge aśvinyādikrameṇa ca | jñeyā 
navāṃśakā varṇā meṣādau rāśimaṇḍale | bhaumaṃ śukraṃ 
budhaṃ candraṃ bhānuṃ saumyaṃ sitaṃ kujam | guruṃ sauriṃ 
śaniṃ jīvaṃ vidadhyāt koṣṭhakopari | koṣṭhakākṣarato 
jñeyaścandrastatkālasambhavaḥ | tadadhīnaṃ phalaṃ sarvaṃ 
lābhālābhaṃ jayājayam | iṣṭanāḍyā hatādhiṣṇyaiḥ 
ṣaṣṭibhāgāptaśeṣakai | aśvinyādīndubhuktena 
yogāttatkālacandramāḥ | krūrakṣetrākṣare candre na śubhaṃ 
sarvakarmasu | śubhakṣetre śubhantvevaṃ prastāre candranirṇayaḥ | ca 
tu 4 strī 3 munayaḥ 7 sūryāḥ 12 sapta 7 nanda 9 guṇe 3 ṣavaḥ 5 | 
māsāḥ 12 śailāḥ 7 inā 12 stutyā 24 rāśīnāñca dhruvā ime || 
guṇāḥ 3 śailā 7 yugāḥ 4 pañca sapta pañca 5 trayo 3 yugāḥ 4 | 
nāgā 8 vāṇā 5 rasā 6 bhūtā 5 māsāderaṃśakā dhruvāḥ | 
aṃśakenāṃśakaṃ guṇyaṃ dhruvayuktakṛtaṃ punaḥ | svaguṇe 
guṇayet paścāt mūlāṅke bhājayet sudhīḥ | ṣaṣṭi 60 rvāṇā 5 raviḥ 
12 pakṣau 2 vahni 3 rnetraṃ 2 ravi 12 stathā | 
candrabhūsutaśukrāṇāṃ gurujñaravisauriṇām | nāḍīphalau yaśau 

p. 931) vargā vargo dinaphalodayaḥ | kaḥ pakṣeṇa ca māsena ṭatapā 
tvayanābdhakaiḥ | praśnakāle vivāhe ca ghāte janmani saṅkare | 
śaśāṅkasya phalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvaśāstre prakāśitam || iti 
prastāracakram || 

asyārthaḥ prakriyā saṅketaścocyate | trayodaśa ityādi | etena 
ūrddhādhaḥkrameṇa nava koṣṭhāni dvādaśasthāneṣu bhavanti | tatra 
vargakṣemavinyāsaḥ | navāṃśakrameṇa dvādaśasu nava nava 
pādikā bhavanti iti darśayannāha kavargamityādi | kavargaprathame 
meṣe aṣṭame vṛścike ūrddhādhaḥkrameṇa navakoṣṭhakena navadhā 
navaprakāreṇa ka kha ga gha ṅa ka kha ga gha evaṃ vilikhya tathaiva 
dvitīye vṛṣarūpe saptame tulārūpe cādyān cakārādīn vargavarṇān 
likhet | triṣaṣṭhake mithunakanyāsvarūpe ṭādyān ṭakārādīn varṇān 
vilikhya caturthe karkaṭarūpe yaśavargau tathaivāvargaṃ a i u e ai o au 
aṃ aḥ svarūpaṃ pañcame siṃhasvarūpe navadvādaśe 
dhanurmīnasvarūpe tādyān tavargavarṇān śeṣe dvikoṣṭhake 
makarakumbhasvarūpādyān pavargavarṇān likhet | caturakṣareti | ka 
kha ga gha evaṃ krameṇa melanādaśvinyādisaptaviṃśatinakṣatrāṇi 
bhavanti | tatra navabhirnavabhirakṣarairnavāṃśaiḥ 
pādikārūpairdvādaśa rāśayastadaṃśā vyavasthitā iti || bhaumamiti 
|| vidadhyāllikhet | tatkālasambhava eva nakṣatramāne 
saptaviṃśatinakṣatrāṇāmanyatamabhogabhāgasthaścandraḥ 
koṣṭhākṣaratattatpādikārūpanavāṃśastho jñeyaḥ | iṣṭanāḍya iti || 
iṣṭadaṇḍāḥ saptaviṃśatibhiḥ pūritāḥ punaḥ ṣaṣṭyā haraṇena 
labdhamekanakṣatraṃ pañcadaśādyantarapalāvaśeṣe nakṣatrasya 
prathamaprathamapādikā tadupari triṃśāṃśadaṇḍābhyantare 
dvitīyapādikā tadupari pañcacatvāriṃśatpalābhyantare 
tṛtīyapādikā tadupari ṣaṣṭyabhyantare caturthapādike tu tattadavaśeṣe 
tatra tatra tātkālikaścandraḥ sthāpyaḥ | etenaitadāyātaṃ 
ṣaṣṭyāptaṃ yattadbhuktanakṣatraṃ yaccheṣaṃ tadbhujyamānamiti

p. 932) tatra candrabhuktanakṣatrāṅkayogena yadi saptaviṃśatyadhikaṃ 
bhavati tadā saptaviṃśatimapāsya tātkālikaścandraḥ sthāpya iti 
satyudayaṃ gantavyamiti || 

tātkālikīkaraṇaprakāramupadarśayati | iṣṭanāḍyo 
nakṣatrabhuktadaṇḍā dhiṣṇyaiḥ saptaviṃśatyā hatāḥ karttavyāḥ | 
tatra ṣaṣṭibhāgāptaśeṣike adhikaraṇe ṣaṣṭyā haraṇādagre 
bhuktanakṣatrasaṃkhyāṅke śeṣe ca bhujyamānanakṣatraśarīre 
uparyadhaḥkrameṇa ṛte prakṛtakrameṇāśvītyādīndubhukte na 
nakṣatrāṅkena yukto yadi saptaviṃśatimapāsyāvaśiṣṭaṃ 
bhuktanakṣatraṃ śeṣañca 
tadapramāṇanakṣatrabhāgaścandrabhujyamāna evaṃ tatkālacandro 
bhavati | nakṣatrabhogasamayastu trayodaśapalādhikadaṇḍadvayādhika 
ityarthaḥ | apavādastu pale ṣaṣṭinyūnādhikanakṣatramāne sati bhavati | 
tatphalānyupadarśayati krūrakṣetrākṣaramiti || candre 
krūrakṣetrākṣare pāpagraharāśyakṣarasthe sarvakarmasu na śubham | 
śubhakṣetre śubhagraharāśinakṣatre candre sthite sati śubhaṃ 
kālamiti | prastāre cakre candranirṇayaḥ kathitaḥ | kathitaphalaṃ kadā 
syādityādiśaṅkāyāmāha || nāḍīphalāviti || yaśau yavargaśavargau 
nāḍauphalau dinābhyantaraphaladau tatra candreṇaikapādikālaṅghane 
trayastriṃśatpalāni tadupari kāryaṃ siddhirevaṃ 
pratipādamavadhikapādalaṅghane tatpādapavamānaṃ varttulīkṛtya 
daṇḍarūpaḥ samayo vaktavyaḥ | sa ca rāśiśeṣe palatrayanyūnāḥ 
pañca daṇḍāḥ paraṃ bhavanti | avargau dinaphalodaya ekadinena 
kathitaphaladātā kaḥ kavargaḥ pakṣe | cavargo māse | ṭaḥ ṭavarga 
ṛtūn prati | tapau tavargapavargau ayanābdayoḥ | ṣaṇmāse tavargaḥ 
abde pavargaḥ phaladātarīti | praśnakāla iti | eteṣu kāryeṣu 
śaśāṅkasyānena prakāreṇa sarvaśāstreṣu gopiteṣu na 
prakaṭīkṛtapraśnakāle vivāhe kṛtyādau tatkālacandrāt 
śubhāśubhaṃ cintyam || iti prastāracakram || 

p. 933) jayati nikhilamānyaḥ prāṇakṛṣṇaḥ śaraṇyaḥ 
sukṛtavitatakirttyutsargagaṇyo vareṇyaḥ | lipikṛtanavakīrttyākhyeya 
eko'tipuṇyo nirasitaguṇidainyo gauḍabhūmadhyadhanyaḥ || 
śrīrāmatoṣaṇalipipratirūḍhaśākhaṃ 
vyākoṣapuṣpasurabhīkṛtadigvibhāgam | 
sattvaikalabhyamatikarmaṭhagamyabhāvaṃ śrīprāṇakṛṣṇahṛdaye 
mudamātanotu || 

iti śrīprāṇakṛṣṇaviśvāsānumatāyāṃ 
śrīrāmatoṣaṇavidyālaṅkārabhaṭṭācārya viracitāyāṃ 
śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ pañcapuṣpastavakānvitaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ 
jñānakāṇḍaṃ samāptam || 


mahākālollāsaprathitaratiyuddhapraṇayinī galadveṇībandhā 
gaganavasanā svinnavadanā | navāmbhodasnigdhā 
śavavihitaraktekṣaṇayutā hṛdi dhvāntāgāre taḍidiva sadā sā 
sphuratu me || nirguṇākhyasya kāṇḍasya saptamasya vidhīyate | 
nirghaṇṭaḥ prathamastatra paśubhāvādinirṇayaḥ | bhāvatrayaphalaṃ 
pūrvāhṇādau bhāvatrayaṃ tataḥ | paśubhāve siddhibījaṃ 
divyavīrapraśaṃsanam | paśvādilakṣaṇaṃ teṣāṃ 
kṛtyākṛtyavinirṇayaḥ | bhāvatrayasyottamatvādinirṇītistataḥ param | 
ārambhādiprabhedena bhāvatrayanirūpaṇam | bhāvanirūpaṇaṃ 
brahmacaryanirūpaṇaṃ tataḥ | tāmrasya 
dhāraṇaphalamaṅgabhedādanekadhā | āśramatrayamātrantu kalau 
jñeyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ | gṛhasthakṛtyamātithyaṃ pitrārccā 
dāralakṣaṇam | parastrīvarjanaṃ putrakanyādilālanādi ca | 
kṛtyākṛtye gṛhasthasyānyadvijñeyaṃ yathāsthalam | 
bhikṣukāśramakṛtyādi kalau tatpratiṣedhanam | 
daśāvadhūtanāmāni tato'vadhūtanirṇayaḥ | tatkṛtyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ 
teṣāṃ praśaṃsā ca tataḥ param | oṃ tatsaditi nirddeśaphalaṃ tasya 
praśaṃsanam | ācārabhedakathanamiti cchedo'grimaḥ smṛtaḥ | 
pūrṇābhiṣekastasyāpi prayogastadanantaram | abhiṣekāntaraṃ 
vīrapraśaṃsā vīrakṛtyakam | vaidhetarasurāpāṇaniṣiddhaṃ 
śāstrasammatam | dravyābhāve cānukalpavidhānaṃ tadanantaram |

p. 934) asaṃskṛtasurāpāṇanindā viprādhikāritā | tatra 
tatphalasaṃyuktā śāpamuktiphalaṃ tataḥ | rasāsaktau japaphalaṃ 
mānaso bhāva eva ca | dharmaḥ pañcamakārasya nirṇayastadanantaram 
| phalaṃ pañcamakārāṇāṃ tairvinārcananindanam | vaiṣṇavānāṃ 
pañcatattvaṃ pañcatattvasya śodhanam | svayambhūkusumādīnāṃ 
nirūpaṇamataḥ param | tadutpattiprakāraśca teṣāṃ śodhanameva ca | 
kāraṇānāṃ mantrādiśca pātrāṇāṃ sthāpanakramaḥ | tarpaṇasya 
kramaścaiva tattvamudrānirūpaṇam | tarpaṇasthānanirṇīti 
rbhāvabhedakrameṇa tu | tattvaśuddhiriti cchedadvitīyasya samāpanam || 
vīrapūjā tu tatrādau dvāviṃśatyakṣaro manuḥ | ṛṣyādinyasanaṃ 
dhyānaṃ pīṭhapūjādireva ca | tato mahākālapūjā paranindā tataḥ 
param | japārddhena phalaprāpti rvījañca siddhikāraṇam | 
brāhmaṇādivibhedena puraścaryāvidhistataḥ | 
mālāsiddhikramādiśca tataḥ kūlavaṭīkramaḥ | anyānyapi 
sādhanāni prayogatrayaṃ tataḥ param | siddhirna jāyate 
cettatropāyāntarameva ca | dakṣiṇādisādhanañca 
dvitīyāyajanakramaḥ | mahāmantrapraśaṃsā ca mantraśuddhyādireva 
ca | dvitīyānāmaśatakaṃ vīrahomastataḥ param | 
dravyadānaphalañcaiva surābhedaphalaṃ tataḥ | 
mahāśaṅkhaprakaraṇamaśokādinatistataḥ | parvatādau karaṇīyaṃ 
natimantrastataḥ param | śmaśānādikriyānindā teṣāmakaraṇe tataḥ | 
rasāsvādaśca vīrāṇāṃ tatphalaṃ tadanantaram | 
ṣaṭkarmasādhanañcaiva vīrāṇāṃ tadanantaram | tṛtīyaparicchedasya 
samāpanamidaṃ smṛtam | kulācāraḥ kulīnasya lakṣaṇaṃ 
tadanantaram | kulācāro dvidhā kaulakarttavyaṃ tadanantaram | 
kaulanindāniṣedhaśca gṛhāvadhūtanirṇayaḥ | rātrikṛtyaṃ 
kulācāraḥ kaulapraśaṃsanaṃ tataḥ | antaryāgaprakaraṇaṃ 
jñānahomavidhistataḥ | śrīcakrasya kramo lobhapānanindā tataḥ

p. 935) param | ajñāni pānanindādipūjake lakṣaṇādi ca | 
āvaśyakaṃ pañcaśuddhermaṇḍalasya ca lakṣaṇam | tarpaṇe 
yanniṣiddhañca vihitañca tataḥ param | dravyaśuddhyādi ca tato 
gurupaṃktikramastataḥ | kulācārakramaścaiva kupātrādiniṣedhanam | 
asnātādeḥ kuladravyaniṣedhastadanantaram | ucchiṣṭasparśanasyaiva 
pratiṣedhastataḥ param | dravyabhāṇḍoddhāradoṣaścakravighnaḥ 
saśāntikaḥ | prauḍhollāse jñānimattajñānacihnānyataḥ param | 
cakre ca parihāsādivarjjanaṃ pātragopanam | paśusaṅgena kṛtyaṃ yad 
vāgvādādivivarjjanam | paśuśāstravarjanañcāśraddadhānasya 
nindanam | akāmo guruśaktyādāvarccanaṃ devatādhiyā | 
balātkāraniṣedhaścāmamāṃsādyabhivandanam | 
kulaśāstrādinindāyāḥ pratiṣedhastataḥ param | 
ānandarūpakathanamatipānādinindanam | 
sukhārthapānadoṣaścānukalpastadanantaram | dhustūrādiniṣedhaśca 
sarvānukalpa eva ca | pāramparyaṃ vinā pañcatattvasevanagarhaṇam | 
mahāpāpaṃ paśūnāntu pañcatattvaniṣevaṇe | 
cakrānuṣṭhānanirṇītistataśca pātravandanam | pātramānañca 
cakreśaśaktyucchiṣṭavicāraṇam | śāntistotraṃ 
tataścakrasamāptikramanirṇayaḥ | 
ānandastotrakathanamānandollāsanirṇayaḥ | iti cchedacaturthasya 
samāptirabhidhīyate || cakrapañcakakarttavyānuṣṭhānañcakranāma 
ca | cakrādhikārī śrīrājacakraśaktyādinirṇayaḥ | 
mahācakraśaktisudhāśuddhyādinirṇayastataḥ | devacakrasya 
śaktyādinirṇayastadanantaram | vīracakre tathā śaktyādinirṇītistataḥ 
param | mātrādinirṇayaḥ saṅketājñavīravivarjanam | paśucakrasya 
śaktyādinirṇītistadanantaram | kulaśaktiḥ svīyaśaktipūjanaṃ 
prathamaṃ tataḥ | paraśaktyarccanaṃ siddhamantriṇāmeva kevalam | 
śaktisādhanakarmāpi śaktyāṅgapīṭhanirṇayaḥ | tatra tatra 
japaphalaṃ nānāśaktiphalaṃ tataḥ | śakteḥ pūjā ca śīrṣādau

p. 936) japanirṇaya eva ca | liṅgamantrajapādiśca homaḥ 
pūrṇāhutistataḥ | cakre ca kāmanāsiddhikathanaṃ tadanantaram | 
latāsādhanamanyacca śayyāśuddhistataḥ param | 
navaśaktivinirddeśaḥ kāpālikyādilakṣaṇam | veśyāviśeṣanirddeśo 
viparītajape phalam | vayaḥkrameṇa sā śaktiḥ kālyādināmadhāriṇī | 
śyāmādividyāsiddhiśca śaktiviśeṣayogataḥ | tatprayogaścaiva 
yonerdhyānaṃ stotraṃ tataḥ param | kavacaṃ yoniyoge ca japasya 
phalanirṇayaḥ | navapuṣpavinirṇītiḥ śaktiprāśastyaniścayaḥ | 
śaktyarpitaphañcaiva śaktyaṅge devatāsthitaḥ | 
anyallatāsādhanañcārcane'nyadārasaṅgamaḥ | strīdehe 
sarvatīrthāni yonipūjāvidhistataḥ | yonipūjāvidhā 
yonismaraṇādiphalaṃ tataḥ | āvāhanādirahitaṃ yonipūjanameva ca | 
śaktipādodakādeśca pānādijaphalaṃ tataḥ | 
bhagatattvakuntalayormāhātmyaṃ tadanantaram | bandhāśceti 
paricchedapañcamasya samāpanam | jñeyamanyad yathāsthānaṃ 
dhīmadbhirgranthamadhyataḥ || bhāvasāramatiyatnacintanai 
rāmatoṣaṇavinirmitaṃ budha ! | paśya kāṇḍamidameva nirguṇaṃ 
moktumicchasi bhavārṇavādyadi ||

atha bhāvanirṇayaḥ || rudrajāmale uttarakhaṇḍe ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 
paśubhāvasthitāṃ nātha ! devatāṃ śṛṇu vistarāt | durgāpūjāṃ 
viṣṇupūjāṃ śivapūjāñca nityaśaḥ | avaśyaṃ hi yaḥ karoti sa 
paśuruttamaḥ smṛtaḥ | kevalaṃ śivapūjāñca yaḥ karoti sa sādhakaḥ 
| paśūnāṃ madhyataḥ śrīmān śivayā saha cottamaḥ | kevalaṃ 
vaiṣṇavo dhīraḥ paśūnāṃ madhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ | bhūtānāṃ 
devatānāñca sevāṃ kurvanti sarvadā | paśūnāṃ madhyamāḥ 
proktā narakasthā  na saṃśayaḥ | tvatsevāṃ mama sevāñca 
brahmaviṣṇvādisevanam | kṛtvānyasarvabhūtānāṃ nāyikānāṃ 

p. 937) mahāprabho ! | yakṣiṇīnāṃ bhūtinīnāṃ tataḥ sevāṃ 
śubhapradām | yaḥ paśurbrahmakṛṣṇādisevāñca kurute sadā | tathā 
śrītārakabrahmasevāṃ ye vā narottamāḥ | teṣāmasādhyā 
bhūtādidevatā sarvakāmahā | varjjayet paśumārgeṇa 
viṣṇupūjāparo janaḥ | dvitīyapaṭale || nityaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā 
sandhyāvandanaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam | devatādarśanaṃ pīṭhadarśanaṃ 
tīrthadarśanam | gurorājñāpālanañca devatānityapūjanam | 
paśubhāvasthito marttyo mahāsiddhiṃ labheddhruvam || ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 
punarbhāvaṃ paśoreva śṛṇu sādarapūrvakam | akasmāt 
siddhimāpnoti paśurnārāyaṇopamaḥ | vaikuṇṭhanagare yāti 
caturbhujakalevaraḥ | śaṅkacakragadāpadmahasto garuḍavāhanaḥ | 
mahādharmasvarūpo'sau mahāvidyāprasādataḥ | paśubhāvaṃ 
mahābhāvaṃ bhāvānāṃ siddhidaṃ param | ādau bhāvaṃ paśoḥ 
kṛtvā paścādāvaśyakaṃ caret | vīrabhāvaṃ mahābhāvaṃ 
sarvabhāvottamottamam | tatpaścādatisaundaryaṃ divyabhāvaṃ 
mahāphalam || iti bhāvāḥ ||

bhāvaphalamapi tatraiva || phalākāṅkṣī mokṣagāmī 
sarvabhūtahite rataḥ | vidyākāṅkṣī dhanākāṅkṣī ratnākāṅkṣī ca 
yo naraḥ | kuryādbhāvatrayaṃ divyaṃ bhāvasādhanamuttamam | 
bhāvena labhate rājyaṃ dhanaṃ ratnaṃ mahāphalam | vārāṇasyāṃ 
koṭiliṅgapūjanena ca yat phalam | tat phalaṃ labhate marttyaḥ 
kṣaṇādeva na saṃśayaḥ || iti bhāvaphalam ||
 
bhāvaviśeṣaphalamapi tatraiva || ādau daśamadaṇḍena 
paśubhāvamathāpi vā | madhyāhne daśadaṇḍena 
vīrabhāvamudāhṛtam | sāyāhne daśadaṇḍe tu divyabhāvaṃ 
śubhapradam | athavā paśubhāvena yajediṣṭādidevatām | janmāvadhi 
japenmantrī mahāsiddhimavāpnuyāt | pīṭhabrahmaṇamātreṇa 
mahāṣoḍhāśrameṇa ca | paśubhāva sthito mantrī

p. 938) siddhimekāmavāpnuyāt | yadi pūrvaparasthāñca 
mahākaulikadevatām | kulamārgasthito mantrī siddhimāpnoti niścitam | 
yadi vidyāḥ prasīdanti vīrabhāvaṃ tadā bhavet | vīrabhāvaprasādena 
divyabhāvamavāpnuyāt | divyabhāvaṃ vīrabhāvaṃ ye gṛhṇanti 
narottamāḥ | vāñchākalpadrumalatāpatayaste na saṃśayaḥ | 
āśramī dhyānaniṣṭhaśca mantratantraviśāradaḥ | bhūtvā 
bhrāmanmahāpīṭhaṃ sākṣānmukto bhaved yatiḥ | kimanyena 
palenāpi yadi bhāvādikaṃ bhavet | bhāvagrahaṇamātreṇa mama 
jñānī bhavennaraḥ | vākyasiddhirbhavet kṣipraṃ vāṇī 
hṛdayagāminī | nārāyaṇaṃ pravihāya lakṣmīstiṣṭhati mandire | 
mama pūrṇottamā dṛṣṭistasya dehe na saṃśayaḥ | avaśyaṃ 
siddhimāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ sadāśiva ! || tathā | bhāvastu trividho 
deva ! divyavīrapaśukramāt | guravaśca tridhā cātra tathaiva 
mantradevatā | divyabhāvo mahādeva ! śreyān sa sarvasiddhidaḥ | 
dvitīyo madhyamaḥ proktastṛtīyaḥ sarvaninditaḥ | bahujāpāttathā 
homāt kāyakleśādivistaraiḥ | na bhāvena mahādeva ! mantratantrāḥ 
phalapradāḥ || tathā | prathamaṃ divyabhāvantu kaulike śṛṇu yatnataḥ 
| sarvadevārcitāṃ vidyāṃ tejaḥpuñjaprapūritām | tejomayīṃ jagat 
sarvāṃ vibhāvya mūrttikalpanām ||

ekādaśapaṭale || trividhaṃ divyabhāvañca vedāgamavivekajam 
| vedārthamadhamaṃ proktaṃ madhyamañcāgamodbhavam | uttamaṃ 
saphalaṃ proktaṃ vivekollāsasambhavam | tatraiva trividhaṃ bhāvaṃ 
divyavīrapaśukramam | divyavīraikajaṃ proktaṃ 
sarvasiddhipradāyakam | uttamaṃ tadvijānīyādānandarasasāgaram | 
madhyamañcāgamollāsaṃ vīrabhāvaṃ kriyānvitam | vedodbhavaṃ 
phalārthañca paśubhāvaṃ hi cādhamam | sarvanindāsamāvyāptaṃ 
bhāvānāmadhamaṃ paśoḥ | uttame uttamaṃ jñānaṃ 
bhāvasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām | madhyame madhumatyāśca 
matkulāgamasambhavam | akālamṛtyuharaṇaṃ

p. 939) bhāvānāmatidurlabham | vīrabhāvaṃ vinā nātha ! na sidhyati 
kadācana | adhame adhamaṃ vyākhyānindārthavācakaṃ sadā | yadā 
nindāṃ na karoti tadā tatphalamāpnuyāt | paśubhāve'pi siddhiḥ 
syādyadi vedaṃ sadābhyaset | vedārthacintanaṃ nityaṃ 
vedapāṭhadhvanipriyam | sarvanindāvirahitaṃ hiṃsālasyavivarjitam | 
lobhamohakāmakrodhamadamātsarya varjitam | yadi bhāvasthito mantrī 
paśubhāvo'pi siddhibhāk | paśubhāvaṃ mahābhāvaṃ ye jānanti 
mahītale | kimasādhyaṃ mahādeva ! śramābhyāsena yānti tat | 
śramādhīnaṃ jagat sarvaṃ śarmādhīnāśca devatāḥ | 
śramādhīnaṃ jagat sarvaṃ śramādhīnaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ | 
śramādhīnaṃ kulācāraṃ paśubhāvopalakṣaṇam | 
vedārthajñānamātreṇa paśubhāvaṃ kulapriyam | 
smṛtyāgamapurāṇāṇi vedārthavihitāni ca | 

	bhāvāśramaprakāramāha tatraiva || abhyasya sarvaśāstrāṇi 
tattvaṃ jñātvā tu buddhimān | palalānīva dhānyārthī 
sarvaśāstrāṇi santyajet | jñānī bhūtvā bhāvasāramāśrayet 
sādhakottamaḥ vede vedakriyā kāryā maduktavacanādyataḥ | 
paśūnāṃ śramadāhānāmiti lakṣaṇamīritam | āgamārthakriyā 
kāryā sadā matkulaceṣṭayā | vīrāṇāmuddhatānāñca 
maccharīrānugāminām | macceṣṭākulatattvānāmiti lakṣaṇamīritam | 
vivekasūtrasaṃjñānāṃ kriyāsu dṛḍhacetasām | sarvatra 
samabhāvānāṃ bhāvamātraṃ hi sādhanam | sarvatra 
matpadāmbhojasambhavaṃ hi carācaram | dṛṣṭvā yat kurute karma 
cātulaṃ phalasiddhidam | akhaṇḍajñānacittānāmiti bhāvaṃ 
vivekinām | nirmalānandadivyānāmiti lakṣaṇamīritam | divyantu 
trividhaṃ bhāvaṃ yo jānāti mahītale | na naśyati mahāvīraḥ kadācit 
sādhakottamaḥ | krameṇa divyabhāvādīnāśritya sādhayed yadi | 
divyabhāve mahāsiddhiṃ prāpnoti sādhakottamaḥ |

p. 940) divyabhāvaṃ vinā nātha ! matpadāmbhojadarśanam | ya icchati 
mahādeva ! sa mūḍhaḥ sādhakaḥ katham | paśubhāvaṃ hi prathame 
dvitīye vīrabhāvakam | tṛtīye divyabhāvañca iti bhāvatrayaṃ kramāt 
| tatprakāraṃ śṛṇu śiva ! trailokyaparipālana ! | 
bhāvatrayaviśeṣajñaḥ ṣaḍādhārasya bhedinaḥ | 
pañcatattvārthabhāvajño divyācārarataḥ sadā | sa eva bhavati 
śrīmān sādhyānāmādipāragaḥ | śivavadviharelloke 
aṣṭaiśvaryasamanvitaḥ | sarvatra śucibhāvena ānandaghanasādhanam | 
jāpanaṃ dhāraṇaṃ vāpi cittamāyāti yatnataḥ | ekānte nirjane deśe 
siddho bhavati niścitam | tatkālaṃ vījabhāvārthaṃ bhāvamātraṃ hi 
sādhanam | bhāvena labhyate siddhirvīrādanyatra kutracit | rātrau 
sandhyādi sampūjya tāmbulapūritānanaḥ | vijayānandasampūrṇo 
jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ | aikyaṃ citte samādāya 
ānandodrekasambhramaḥ | yo japet sakalāṃ rātriṃ gatabhīrnirjane 
gṛhe | sa bhavet kālikādāso divyānāmuttamottamaḥ | evaṃ 
bhāvatrayaṃ jñātvā yaḥ karma sādhayettataḥ | aṣṭaiśvaryayuto 
bhūtvā sarvajño bhavati dhruvam ||

kubjikātantre saptamapaṭale ca | bhāvaśca trividho devi ! 
divyavīrapaśukramāt | viśvañca devatārūpaṃ bhāvayet surasundari ! 
| strīmayañca jagat sarvaṃ puruṣaṃ śivarūpiṇam | abhede cintayed 
yastu sa eva devatātmakaḥ | devatātmako devatāsvabhāva ityarthaḥ | 
sarvatrābhedacintanena śivaśaktimayatvamakhilasyetyāyātañcettadā 
snānādikaṃ na karttavyamiti śaṅkānirāsāyoktaṃ tatraiva yathā | 
nityasnānaṃ nityadānaṃ trisandhyañca japārcanam nirmalaṃ 
vasanaṃ devi ! paridhānaṃ samācaret | vedaśāstre dṛḍhajñānaṃ 
gurau deve tathaiva ca | mantre caiva dṛḍhajñānaṃ pitṛdevārcanaṃ 
tathā | balivasyaṃ tathā śrāddhaṃ nityakāryaṃ śucismite ! | śatruṃ 
mitrasamaṃ devi ! cintayettu maheśvari ! | annñcaiva maheśāni !

p. 941) sarveṣāṃ parivarjayet | gurorannaṃ maheśāni ! bhoktavyaṃ 
sarvasiddhaye | kadaryañca maheśāni ! niṣṭhuraṃ parivarjayet | 
devatānindakaṃ dṛṣṭvā nālāpañca samācaret | satyañca 
kathayeddevi ! na mithyā ca kadācana | kevalaṃ divyabhāvena pūjayet 
parameśvarīm | gurorārādhanaṃ devi ! pratyahaṃ cintayet sudhīḥ | 
sarvañca devatārūpaṃ parameṣṭhisvarūpakam | ekāgrāme sthito 
nityaṃ trisandhyaṃ pūjayedgurum | gurutulyaṃ maheśāni ! 
namaskuryādvarānane ! | strīṇāṃ pādatalaṃ dṛṣṭvā 
guruvadbhāvayet sadā | śrīkhaṇḍapaṅkaṃ rudhiraṃ bhūṣitaṃ 
sumanoharam | śarīraṃ kārayeddevi ! gandharvadevamuttamam | 
tripuṇḍraṃ bhasmanā vāpi raktacandanakena vā | 
rudrākṣabhūṣaṇaṃ devi ! sarvāṅge ca maheśvari ! | kevalaṃ 
bhairavo bhūtvā yajeddevīṃ sanātanīm | pratyahañca baliṃ dadyād 
devatābhāvasiddhaye | samayāñca gṛhītvā tu pūjādau ca maheśvari 
! | niśīthe pūjanañcaiva karttavyañca maheśvari ! | kulavṛkṣaṃ tathā 
dṛṣṭvā karttavyañca varānane | praṇāmaṃ vandanañcaiva 
pratyahañca maheśvari ! | rātrau caiva yajeddevīṃ na divāpi kadācana 
| rātrau tāmbūlapūrāsyo japenmantraṃ maheśvari ! | sarvañcaiva 
mahādevi ! paradevyai samarpayet || 
paśubhāvakarttavyākarttavyagarbhatadbhāvanindāmāha tatraiva || 
paśubhāvaratā ye ca kevalaṃ paśurūpiṇaḥ | rātrau yantrañca 
manatrañca na spṛśenna japet kvacit | saṃśayo balidāne ca tantre ca 
saṃśayaḥ sadā | mantre cākṣarabuddhiśca aviśvāso gurau sadā | 
pratimāsu śilābuddhirbhedako daivate punaḥ | nirāmiṣeṇa deveśi ! 
devatāyāḥ prapūjanam | ajñānena sadā snānaṃ pratyahaṃ 
devatāḍanam | sarveṣāñcaiva nindāntu yaḥ kuryācca maheśvari ! | sa 
eva paśubhāvena adhamaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || nityātantre prathamapaṭale'pi 
|| rātrau naiva yajeddevīṃ sandhyāyāṃ vāparāhṇake | ṛtukālaṃ 
vinā devi ! ramaṇaṃ parivarjayet | māṃsādikaṃ maheśāni | tyajet

p. 942) pañcasu parvasu | yadanyadvedavihitaṃ kuryānniyamatatparaḥ || 
iti paśubhāvanindā ||

divyavīrayoruttamatvamapi tatraiva || uttamo divyabhāvaśca 
vīrabhāvaśca madhyamaḥ | adhamaḥ paśubhāvaśca devi ! satyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | paśubhāve stitā mantrāḥ kevalaṃ varṇarūpiṇaḥ | 
karttavyañca maheśāni ! devatābhāvabodhanam | tasmāt khalu 
prayatnena vīrabhāvena sādhayet | dṛṣṭvā kulastriyaṃ devi ! 
kulavṛkṣaṃ viśeṣataḥ | kulagehaṃ tathā devi ! namaskuryādvarānane 
! | kulastriyaṃ maheśāni ! tantramantraviśāradām | dūtīṃ 
yāgaratāñcaiva veśyāṃ vā puṃścalīṃ tathā | bṛddhāṃ vā 
yuvatīṃ vāpi namaskuryādvarānane ! ||

picchilātantre daśamapaṭale | divyavīrau mahābhāvāvadhamaḥ 
paśubhāvakaḥ | vaiṣṇavaḥ paśubhāvena pūjayet parameśvaram | 
śaktimantre varārohe ! paśubhāvo bhayānakaḥ | 
divyairvīrairmaheśāni ! jāyate siddhiruttamā | divye vīre na bhedo'sti 
bhedo vīro mahoddhataḥ | divyavīrau pravakṣyāmi sarvabhāvottamau 
matau | vinā śaktiṃ na pūjāsti matsyamāṃsaṃ vinā priye ! | 
mudrāñca maithunañcāpi vinā naiva prapūjayet | strībhagaṃ 
pujanādhāraḥ svarṇarūpyātmakaḥ kuśaḥ | abhāve 
sarvadravyāṇāmanukalpaḥ kalau yuge | athavā parameśāni ! 
mānasaṃ sarvamācaret | snānantu mānasaṃ proktaṃ vaidiko 
mānasaḥ sadā | yatra bhuktvā mahāpūjā mānasaṃ bhojanantu tat | 
svakīyāṃ parakīyāṃ vā mānasantu ramet striyam | mānasaṃ 
madyamāṃsādi svīkuryāt sādhakottamaḥ | svayambhūkusumaṃ 
tadvanmānasaṃ samupācaret | mānasaṃ bhagaromādi mānasaṃ 
bhagapūjanam | sarvantu mānasaṃ kuryāttena sidhyati sādhakaḥ | na 
kalau prakṛtācāraḥ saṃśayātmani naiva saḥ | mānasenaiva bhāvena 
sarvasiddhimupālabhet || nigamakalpadrume dvitīyapaṭale ||

p. 943) paśunāñcaiva vīrāṇāṃ divyānāṃ parameśvara ! | 
ārambhaśca samāptiśca teṣāñcaiva pṛthak pṛṭhak | 
paśubhāvasamāptiśca vīrabhāvāvarabdhakaḥ | divyāvarabdhako 
vīrabhāvanāśaka eva ca | yathā bālyaṃ yauvanañca vṛddhabhāvaḥ 
kramāt priyam | yathā puṣpaṃ phalañcaiva vījañcaiva yathākramam | 
yathā dugdhaṃ navanītaṃ ghṛtañceti maheśvara ! | yathā 
saṅkalpakāryañca dakṣiṇā ceti bharava ! | tathā bhāvatrayaṃ 
jñeyamārambhārambhajanmakam ! ajñānaprabhavaṃ sarvaṃ jagat 
sthāvarajaṅgamam | ajñānarajjubhirbaddhaḥ sṛjāmīha punaḥ punaḥ | 
ajñānañca  kriyāmūlaṃ yāvattattvaṃ na vindati | tattve samudgate 
kiñcit kriyāyāṃ nāsti vāsanā | vāsanārahito yastu hyabhiṣekaḥ 
prapūrvakaḥ | tasyāpi kāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ śṛṇu madvākyasambhavam 
| ata eva maheśāni ! vīrāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ paśuḥ | divyānāṃ 
vīrabhāvaśca tasmāt kiñcinna vidyate | madvākyaṃ tyajataḥ ko'pi 
yadi śuddhiṃ na cācaret | kāryaṃ na vidyate tasya 
yatheṣṭakulabhāginaḥ | ata eva vāmakeśvaratantre ekapañcāśatpaṭale 
|| janmamātraṃ paśubhāvaṃ varṣaṣoḍaśakāvadhi | tataśca 
vīrabhāvastu yāvat pañcāśato bhavet | dvitīyāṃśe 
vīrabhāvastṛtīyo divyabhāvakaḥ | evaṃ bhāvatrayeṇaiva 
bhāvamaikyaṃ bhavet priye ! | aikyajñānāt kulācāro yena devamayo 
bhavet | bhāvo hi mānaso dharmo manasaiva sadābhyaset | āśramiṇo 
bhāvagrahaṇasya 
prāguktatvenāśramajñānākāṅkṣāyāmāśramā nirūpyante | 
tatrāpi brahmacaryasya prathamopasthitatvāt tadevādau nirūpyate || 

nirvāṇatantre caturdaśapaṭale | yathā | brahmārccanaṃ 
pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi ! samāhitā | yasyācaraṇamātreṇa naro 
nārāyaṇo bhavet | gairikaṃ vasanaṃ kuryād devatādhyānatatparaḥ | 
phalamūlāhārarato dugdhaṃ gavyaṃ samāharet | śālyudbhavaṃ na 
gṛhṇiyāt śūdrānītaṃ tathā jalam | ṛtukālaṃ vinā naiva

p. 944) svakāntāgamanaṃ caret | gṛhastho'pi maheśāni ! brahmacārī 
sadā guruḥ | udāsīnaḥ kadācidvai gurukarmādhipo bhavet | tasya 
śiṣyasya kalyāṇaṃ kadācinnāsti caṇḍike ! | nakhalomādikaṃ devi ! 
na tyājyaṃ brahmacāriṇā | sadaiva tu sadā bhāvaṃ sadaiva 
dhyānatatparaḥ | triśūlaṃ dhārayeccaikaṃ triśikhāṃ vāpi dhārayet 
| tāmrayuktañca rudrākṣaṃ karṇayugme niveśayet ||

tāmradhāraṇasya phalamuktam yogasāre dvitīyaparicchede || 
bāhau ca dhāraṇāttāmraṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati | 
hastayordhāraṇādeva sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet | 
karṇayordhāraṇāttāmraṃ koṭitīrthaphalaṃ labhet | kaṇṭhe ca 
dhāraṇāttāmraṃ yogī mokṣamavāpnuyāt || tāmramiti ṣaṣṭhyarthe 
dvitīyā ||

brahmacaryaphalamuktaṃ nirvāṇatantre caturdaśapaṭale || 
uddeśe vidyate devi ! brahmacārī tapodhanaḥ | taddeśe ca sthirā 
lakṣmīrjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || vastutastu kaulasya 
brahmacaryāśramo nāsti || taduktaṃ mahānirvāṇatantre aṣṭamollāse 
||

śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

catvāraḥ kathitā varṇā āśramā api suvrate ! | ācāraścāpi 
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ pṛthak pṛthak | kṛtādau kalikāle tu 
varṇāḥ pañca prakīrttitāḥ | brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiṣyaḥ śūdraḥ 
sāmānya eva ca ! eteṣāṃ sarvavarṇānāmāśramau dvau maheśvari ! 
| teṣāmācāradharmāṃśca śṛṇuṣvādye ! vadāmi te | puraiva 
kathitaṃ tāvat kalisambhavaceṣṭitam | tapaḥsvādhyāyahīnānāṃ 
nṛṇāmalpāyuṣāmapi | kleśaprayāsaśaktānāṃ kuto 
dehapariśramaḥ | brahmacaryāsamo nāsti vānaprastho'pi na priye ! | 
gṛhastho bhikṣukaścaiva āśramau dvau kalau yuge | iti likhitastu sa 
āśramaḥ kvacit kvacit vyavahāradarśanāditi ||


p. 945)	atha gārhasthyam || nirvāṇatantre || atha vakṣye gṛhasthasya 
lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu caṇḍike ! | pāṭho homaścātithīnāṃ sevanaṃ deva 
pūjanam | pitṛśrāddhaṃ kulācāraṃ tattvajñānaṃ samācaret | 
nijakāntā sadā pūjyā nijakāntā hi devatā | mānasaṃ pūjanaṃ 
kuryāt mānasaṃ japamācaret | mānaso hi mahādharmo mānasaṃ 
nāsti pātakam || mahānirvāṇatantre || gṛhasthasya kriyāḥ sarvā 
āgamoktāḥ kalau śive ! | nānyamārgaiḥ kriyāsiddhiḥ kadāpi 
gṛhamedhinām | viprāṇāmitareṣāñca varṇānāmitare kalau | 
ubhayatrāśrame devi ! sarveṣāmadhikāritā | sarveṣāmeva 
saṃskārakarmāṇi śaivavartmanā | karmaṇāmitareṣāñca 
karmaliṅgaṃ pṛthak pṛthak | jātamātro gṛhasthaḥ syāt 
saṃskārādāśramī bhavet | gāhasthyaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād 
yathāvidhi maheśvari ! | tattvajñāne samutpanne vairāgyaṃ jāyate 
yadā | tadā sarvaṃ parityajya sannyāsāśramamāśrayet | 
vidyāmupārjayedbālye dhanadārāṃśca yauvane | prauḍhe 
dharmyāṇi karmāṇi caturthe pravrajet sudhīḥ | mātaraṃ pitaraṃ 
vṛddhaṃ bhāryāñcaiva pativratām | śiśuñca tanayaṃ hitvā 
māvadhūtāśramaṃ vrajet | mātṝḥ pitṛn śiśūn dārān svajanān 
bāndhavānapi | yaḥ pravrajati hitvaitān sa mahāpātakī bhavet | 
mātṛhā pitṛhā sa syāt strībadhī vipraghātakaḥ | asantarpya tu 
pitrādīn yo gacchedbhikṣukāśrame | brāhmaṇo viprabhinnaśca 
svasvavarṇoktasaṃskriyām | śaivena vartmanā kuryādeṣa dharmaḥ 
kalau yuge ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

ko vā dharmo gṛhasthasya bhikṣukasya ca kiṃ vibho ! | viprasya 
viprabhinnānāṃ saṃskārādīni me vada ||

śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

gārhasthyaṃ prathamaṃ dharmaṃ sarveṣāṃ manujanmanām | tadeva 
kathayāmyādau śṛṇukaulini ! tattvataḥ | brahmaniṣṭho gṛhasthaḥ syād 
brahmajñānaparāyaṇaḥ | yadyat karma prakurvīta tadbrahmaṇi

p. 946) samarpayet | na mithyābhāṣaṇaṃ nānyatra cāsādhyaṃ 
samācaret | devatātithipūjāsu gṛhastho nirato bhavet || 

atithiryathā nāradapañcarātre ṣaṣṭhādhyāye || atithiḥ pūjito 
yena viśvañca tena pūjitam | atithiryasya tuṣṭo hi tasya tuṣṭo hariḥ 
svayam  | adhiṣṭhātātithirgehe santataṃ sarvadevatāḥ | tīrthānyenāni 
sarvāṇi puṇyāni ca bratāni ca | tapāṃsi yajñāḥ satyañca śīlaṃ 
dharmaḥ sukarma ca | apūjitairatithibhiḥ sārddhaṃ sarvaiḥ prayānti te | 
atithiryasya bhagnāśo gṛhāt pratinivarttate | pitarastasya devāśca 
puṇyadharmahutāśanāḥ | yaśaḥ pratiṣṭhā lakṣmīścāpīṣṭadevo 
gurustathā | nirāśāḥ pratigacchanti tyaktvā pāpañca pūruṣam | 
strīghnairgoghnaiḥ kṛtaghnaiśca brahmaghnairgurutalpagaiḥ | 
viśvāsaghātibhirduṣṭairmitradrohibhireva ca | satyaghnaiśca 
kṛtaghnaiśca kīrttighnaiḥ pāpibhistathā | ālāpavārakaiścaiva 
kanyāvikrayibhistathā | somāpahāribhiścaiva 
mithyāsākṣyapradātṛbhiḥ | brahmasvahāribhiścaiva tathā sthāpyasya 
hāribhiḥ | vṛṣavāhairdevalaiśca tathaiva grāmayājibhiḥ | 
śūdrānnabhojibhiścaiva śūdraśrāddhāhabhojibhiḥ | 
śrīkṛṣṇavimukhairviprairhiṃsrairnaravighātakaiḥ | gurāvabhaktai 
rogārtaiḥ śaśvanmithyāpravādibhiḥ | viprastrīgāmibhiḥ 
śūdrairmātṛgāmibhireva ca | aśvatthaghātibhiścaiva patnībhiḥ 
patighātibhiḥ | pitṛmātṛghātibhiśca śaraṇāgataghātibhiḥ | 
brāhmaṇakṣatraviṭśūdraiḥ śilāsvarṇāpahāribhiḥ | tulyadoṣo 
bhavatyebhiryasyātithiranarcitaḥ | mātaraṃ pitarañcaiva sākṣāt 
pratyakṣadevatām | sadā gṛhī niṣeveta sadā sarvaprayatnataḥ | 
tuṣṭāyāṃ mātari śive ! tuṣṭe pitari pārvati ! | tava prīti rbhaveddevi ! 
paraṃ brahma prasīdati | tvamādye ! jagatāṃ mātā pitā brahma parāt 
param | yuvayoḥ prīṇanaṃ yat syāt tasmāt kiṃ gṛhiṇāṃ tapaḥ | 
āsanaṃ bhojanaṃ vastraṃ pānaṃ bhajanameva ca | tattat

p. 947) samayamājñāya mātre pitre niyojayet | śrāvayenmṛdulāṃ 
vāṇīṃ sarvadā priyamācaret | pitro rājñānusārī syāt sa putraḥ 
kulapāvanaḥ | auddhatyaṃ parihāsañca tadvarjyaṃ bahubhāṣaṇam | 
pitroragre na kurvīta yadīcchedātmano hitam | mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vīkṣya 
natvottiṣṭhet sasambhramaḥ | vinājñayā nopaviśet saṃsthitaḥ 
pitṛśāsane | vidyādhanamadonmatto yaḥ kuryāt pitṛhelanam | sa yāti 
narakaṃ ghoraṃ sarvadharmavahiṣkṛtaḥ | mātaraṃ pitaraṃ 
putradārānatithisodarān | hitvā gṛhīna bhuñjīyāt prāṇaiḥ 
kaṇṭhagatairapi | vañcayitvā gurun bandhūn yo bhuṅkte 
svodarambhariḥ | ihaiva loke garhyo'sau paratra nārakī bhavet | gṛhastho 
gopayeddārān vidyāmabhyāsayet sutān | gopayet svajanān bandūneṣa 
dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ | jananyā varddhitā deho janakena pravarddhitaḥ | 
svajanaiḥ śikṣitaḥ prītyā so'dhamastān parityajet | eṣāmarthe 
maheśāni ! kṛtvā kaṣṭaśatānyapi | pīṇayet satataṃ yastu dharmo 
hyeṣa sanātanaḥ | sa dhanyaḥ puruṣo loke sa kṛtī paramārthavit | 
brahmaniṣṭhaḥ satyasandho yo bhavedbhuvi mānavaḥ | na bhāryāṃ 
tāḍayet kvāpi mātṛvat pālayet sadā | na tyajedghorakaṣṭe'pi yadi 
sādhvī pativratā | sthiteṣu svīyadāreṣu striyamanyāṃ na saṃspṛśet | 
duṣṭena cetasā vidvānanyathā nārakī bhavet | virale śayanaṃ vāsaṃ 
tyajet prājñaḥ parastriyā | ayuktabhāṣaṇañcaiva striyai śauryaṃ na 
darśayet | dhanena vāsasā premṇā śraddhayāmṛtabhāṣaṇaiḥ | 
satataṃ toṣayeddārānnāpriyaṃ kvacidācaret | utsave lokayātrāyāṃ 
tīrtheṣvanyaniketane | na patnīṃ preṣayet prājñaḥ 
putramātyavivarjitām | yasmin nare maheśāni ! tuṣṭā bhāryā 
pativratā | sarvaṃ dharmaṃ kṛtaṃ tena bhavati priya eva saḥ | 
caturvarṣāvadhi sutān lālayet pālayet pitā | tataḥ ṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ 
guṇān vidyāñca śikṣayet | viṃśatyabdādhikān putrān prerayed

p. 948) gṛhakarmasu | tatastān tulyabhāvena matvā snehaṃ pradarśayet | 
kanyāpyevaṃ pālanīyā śikṣaṇīyātiyatnataḥ | deyā varāya viduṣe 
dhanaratnasamanvitā | evaṃ krameṇa bhrātṝṃśca 
svasṛbhrātṛsutānapi | jñātīn mitrāṇi bhṛtyāṃśca 
pālayettoṣayedgṛhī | tataḥ svadharmaniratānekagrāmanivāsinaḥ | 
abhyāgatānudāsīnān gṛhasthaḥ paripālayet | yadyevaṃ nācareddevi ! 
gṛhastho vibhave sati | paśureva sa vijñeyaḥ sa pāpī lokagarhitaḥ | 
nidrālasyaṃ dehayatnaṃ keśavinyāsameva ca | āsattimaśane vastre 
nātiriktaṃ samācaret | yuktāhāro yuktanidro mitavāṅmitamaithunaḥ | 
svaccho namraḥ śucirddakṣo yuktaḥ syāt sarvakarmasu | śūraḥ śatrau 
vinītaḥ syādbāndhave gurusannidhau | jugupsitānna manyet 
nāvamanyeta māninaḥ | sauhṛdaṃ vyavahārāṃśca pravṛttiṃ 
prakṛtaṃ nṛṇām | sahavāsena tarke ca viditvā viśvasettataḥ | eṣa 
dveṣṭyupari kṣudrāt samayaṃ vīkṣya buddhimān | 
pradarśayedātmabhāvānaiva dhairyaṃ vilaṅghayet | svīyaṃ yaśaḥ 
pauruṣañca guptaye kathitañca yat | kṛtaṃ yadupakārāya dharmajño 
na prakāśayet | jugupsitapravṛttau ca likhite'pi parājayaḥ | guruṇā 
laghunā vāpi yaśasvī na vivādayet | vidyādhanayaśodharmān yatra 
mānamupārjayet | vyasanañcāsatāṃ saṅgaṃ mithyādrohaṃ 
parityajet | avasthānugatāśceṣṭāḥ samayānugatāḥ kriyāḥ | 
tasmādavasthāṃ samayaṃ vīkṣya karma samācaret | yogakṣemarato 
dakṣo dhārmikaḥ priyabāndhavaḥ | mitavāṅmitahāsyaḥ 
syānmānyāgre tu viśeṣataḥ | jitendriyaḥ prasannātmā sucintaḥ 
syāddṛḍhavrataḥ | apramatto dīrghadarśī mātrāsparśān viśodhayet | 
satyaṃ mṛdu priyaṃ dhīro vākyaṃ hitakaraṃ vadet | ātmotkarṣaṃ 
tathā nindāṃ pareṣāṃ parivarjayet | jalāśayaśca vṛkṣaśca 
viśrāmagṛhamadhvani | setuḥ pratiṣṭhito yena teta lokatrayaṃ jitam |

p. 949) santuṣṭau pitarau yasminnanuraktāḥ suhṛjjanāḥ | gāyanti 
yadyaśo lokāstena lokatrayaṃ jitam | satyameva vrataṃ yasya dayā 
dīneṣu sarvathā | kāmakrodhau yasya vaśyau tena lokatrayaṃ jitam | 
śaucantu dvividhaṃ devi ! vāhmābhyantarabhedataḥ | 
brahmaṇyātmārpaṇaṃ yattat śaucamāntarikaṃ smṛtam | adbhirvā 
bhasmanā vāpi malānāmapakarṣaṇam | dehaśuddhirbhavedyena 
vahiḥśaucaṃ taducyate | bhasmātra yājñikaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ mṛtsnāpi 
malavarjitā | nidānte maithunasyānte tyāge ca malamūtrayoḥ | 
bhojanānte male spṛṣṭe vahiḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate | kintvatra bahunoktena 
śocāśaucavidhau śive ! manaḥ pūtaṃ bhavedyena gṛhasthastu 
tadācaret | sandhyā traikālikī kāryā vaidikī tāntrikī kramāt | 
upāsanādibhedena pūjāṃ kuryādyathāvidhi || tathā | arghyadānaṃ 
dineśāya gāyatrījapanaṃ tathā | aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā śataṃ vā 
daśadhāpi vā | japānāṃ niyamo bhadre ! sarvatrāhnikakarmaṇi | 
śūdrasāmanyajātīnāmadhikāro'sti kevalam | āgamoktavidhau devi ! 
sarvasiddhistato bhavet ||

nirvāṇatantre dvitīyapaṭale || gṛhasthasya divyamūrttiṃ 
candanādi-vibhūṣitām | sarveṣāṃ pitṛrūpo'sau gṛhasthaḥ 
sādhurūpakaḥ | sadā dānarataḥ śrīmān sadā yajñaparāyaṇaḥ | 
sthāpayet pañca tattvāni gehamadhye prayatnataḥ | tadaiva 
sarvasiddhīśo bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ | yasminmantre vicāro'pi 
siddhādigaṇadūṣaṇam | tattat sarvaṃ maheśāni ! gṛhasthasya 
muniścitam | suvicārya mahāmantraṃ gṛhṇīyāt sādhakottamaḥ | 
ekākṣare tathā kūṭe mālāmantrādike tathā | vicāro nāsti deveśi ! 
svapnalabdhe tathaiva hi | tathāpi ca gṛhasthasya suvicāryo mahāmanuḥ | 
daṇḍino bhūṣaṇaṃ nāsti sarvamantrasya dīkṣaṇe |

p. 950) gṛhasthāśramamāsādya tattvajñāneṣu yo rataḥ | sa muktaḥ 
sarvapāpebhyaḥ sa tu sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ | annadānena yat puṇyaṃ 
toyadānena yat phalam tattatsarvaṃ gṛhasthasya nānyasya surasundari ! || 
nityātantre aṣṭamapaṭale ||

devyuvāca ||

kathitaṃ parameśāna ! kaulācārasya lakṣaṇam | varttate tatkathaṃ 
nātha ! gṛhastheṣu ca rājasu |

īśvara uvāca |

sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇatrayavibhedataḥ | sarveṣu varttate devi ! 
kauladharmaḥ sanātanaḥ | sattvaṃ syādbhaktiyogena 
sarvotpattyekakāraṇam | ata eva maheśāni ! gṛhasthāḥ 
sāttvikāmatāḥ | rajaḥ syāt karmayogena karmakarttā mahīpatiḥ | ata 
eva maheśāni ! rājā rājasikaḥ smṛtaḥ | tamobhāvasthitaḥ 
śambhuryogīśaḥ parameśvaraḥ | ata eva tamobhāvamāśrayet 
saṅgavarjitaḥ | śakticakraṃ rājacakraṃ vīracakraṃ maheśvari ! | ata 
eva prakarttavyaṃ krameṇa parameśvari ! || kubjikātantre saptamapaṭale 
|| aśokaṃ vilvavṛkṣaṃ ca śivāṃ caiva sureśvari ! dṛṣṭvā 
praṇāmaṃ kuryācca sarvakāmārthasiddhaye | ulūkaṃ 
śaṅkhacillañca gṛdhraṃ caiva sureśvari ! | namaskuryānmahākālīṃ 
yuddheṣu jayamālabhet | catuṣpathaṃ śmaśānañca 
namaskuryādviśeṣataḥ | tantrajñaṃ cāvadhūtañca 
namaskuryādvarānane ! | upāsakaṃ sadā dṛṣṭvā madhuraṃ 
bhāṣaṇaṃ caret | samayāñca vinā devi ! na siddhiḥ syāt kadācana | 
tasmāccaiva prayatnena gṛhṇīyānnātra saṃśayaḥ ||

atha yatyāśramaḥ || nirvāṇatantre trayodaśapaṭale || 
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyukto brāhmaṇo gamanaṃ caret | gatvā ca 
daṇḍinikaṭe praṇameddaṇḍavadbhuvi | tvameva devadeveśa ! tvameva 
trāṇakārakaḥ | tvameva jagatāṃ vastā pāhi māṃ śaraṇāgatam | 
vastetyatrāntarbhūtaṇyartho vivakṣito vastā vāsayiteti yāvat | iti 
śrutvā daṇḍadhārī papraccha sādarājjanam | kastvaṃ kasya 
sutastvaṃ hi kena vāgamanaṃ vada | śrutvā tadvacanaṃ vipraḥ

p. 951) provācātmanivedanam | vipravaṃśe samudbhūto 
hyamuko'haṃ vivekavān | nāsti me tu pitā sākṣānnāsti me 
strīsutādikam | mṛtau ca mātāpitarau mṛtā bhrātādayaḥ sutāḥ | 
paścāt svakāntānāśe tu hyahamatyantatāpavān | ata eva hi bhoḥ 
svāmin ! dehi me paramāśramam | etena pitrādiṣu tiṣṭhatsu 
yatyāśramo na bhavatīti sūcitam | tān vihāya daṇḍasya 
dhartturdoṣakathanagarbhadaṇḍivacanaṃ tatraiva || satyaṃ kuru 
dvijaśreṣṭha ! yaduktaṃ vai mamāntike | mithyābhāṣaṇadoṣeṇa 
brahmavartma vivarjitam | bhavatyeva na sandeho dvija ! matpurato vada | 
sthitāyāṃ yauvanayutakāntāyāṃ parameśvari ! | sarvaṃ hi viphalaṃ 
tasya yaḥ kuryāddaṇḍadhāraṇam | vidyete pitarau devi ! yaḥ 
kuryāddaṇḍadhāraṇam | sannyāso viphalastasya rauravākhyaṃ 
gamiṣyati | vidyate bālabhāvena yasya kāntā sutastathā | 
sannyāsadhāraṇaṃ tasya vṛthā hi parameśvari ! | sa guruścāpi 
śiṣyaśca rauravākhyaṃ prapadyate | ityādi dṛḍhavākyañca śrutvā 
daṇḍī jitendriyaḥ | sannyāsadānaṃ tasyaiva dadyānmuktiñca 
śāśvatīm || ityādinā doṣadarśanena punaḥ 
pitrādimaraṇaniścāyakaṃ dṛḍhavākyaṃ śrutvā tasya 
sannyāsadānamarpayedityanvayaḥ ||

tatprakāramāha | ādau daśākṣaraṃ mantraṃ prathame 
śrāvayedguruḥ ||

śrīśaṅkara uvāca ||

śṛṇu devi ! pravakṣyāmi mantraṃ daśākṣaraṃ param | yasya 
śravaṇamātreṇa mūḍho'pi viṣṇurūpadhṛk | puṃmantro'pi ca 
deveśi ! śaktimantraṃ japed yadi | ajñātvā taṃ mahāmantraṃ sa 
bhavedbrahmarākṣasaḥ | ajñātvā taṃ mahāmantraṃ tattvajñānī 
bhaved yadi | tathāpi narakaṃ gacchediti vedasya sammatam | śṛṇu devi ! 
pravakṣyāmi darśanaṃ mantramuttamam | mayāvījaṃ badhūvījaṃ

p. 952) lakṣmīḥ kālī ca pāśakam | gaganaṃ pakṣivījañca 
vahnikāntā tataḥ priye ! | iti te kathitaṃ caṇḍi ! daśākṣaramanuttamam 
| yajjñānādamaratvañca labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ | asya 
grahaṇamātreṇa naro nārāyaṇo bhavet | asya prasādamātreṇa daṇḍī 
nirvāṇatāṃ vrajet | daṇḍopari japedetaddaśārṇaṃ parameśvari ! | 
asmānmantrānmahāmantrā jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ | sarvo 
devasamācāro daśārṇājjāyate dhruvam | mahāvidyopāsakaśca 
daśārṇaṃ na hi paśyati | iha loke daridraśca pare ca brahmarākṣasaḥ | 
tasmād yatnena deveśi ! daśārṇaṃ śrāvayedguruḥ | kevalaṃ 
śravaṇaṃ kuryānna japet sādhakottamaḥ | śrāvayed yo daśārṇañca 
sa naro gurusattamaḥ | mantradātā yadā devi ! tavaiva sa guruḥ smṛtaḥ | 
daśārṇadātā yo devi ! sa eva paramo guruḥ | yathāmarataraṅgiṇyā 
asamāḥ sakalāpagāḥ | daśārṇasya samo nāsti mantrasya surapūjite ! 
|| tathā || ataḥ param pravakṣyāmi yadrūpaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam | 
sādhurūpo gṛhasthaśca brāhmaṇo brahmavādakaḥ | 
sarvamāyāsamātyaktaḥ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ | jitendriyo jitakrodhaḥ 
samatvaṃ sarvajātiṣu | putre ripau samatvañca sandhyāyāṃ pauṣṭike 
tathā | kāyaprāṇe na sambandho mṛtyaradya yugāntike | 
brahmajñānaṃ vinā jñānaṃ yasya citte manoramam | 
sannyāsadhāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ viprāṇāṃ muktihetave | yo vipro 
dhārayeddaṇḍaṃ saiva nārāyaṇaḥ svayam | caturbhujāḥ prajāyante 
daṇḍadhāraṇamātrataḥ | tat śrutvā ca mahārambhagamanaṃ kārayet 
| krośaṃ vā krośayugmaṃ vā vegena gamanañcaret | guruṇā ha 
śiṣyeṇa pṛṣṭhe pṛṣṭhe vidhānataḥ | tiṣṭha tiṣṭha mahābāho ! māṃ 
tyaktvā nahi gacchatu | śiṣyaḥ paramahaṃsastvaṃ tvatsamo nāsti 
bhūtale | kṣantavyamaparādhaṃ me tvameva viṣṇurūpadhṛk | tvameva 
jagatāṃ vandyastvameva sarvapūjitaḥ | tvameva paramo haṃsastiṣṭha

p. 953) tiṣṭheti mā vraja | sa śiṣyo daṇḍinā devi ! iti vākyaṃ 
vadedyataḥ ataḥ sa paramo haṃsaḥ pathi śete pramohitaḥ | tasyaiva 
darśanārthāya cāntarīkṣe ca devatāḥ | sastrīkāḥ 
saparīvārāścāyānti digvidikṣu ca | etasmin samaye daṇḍī 
sāntvayecchiṣyamuttamam | phutkāraṃ bahuśo datvā mantreṇānena 
suvrataḥ | phutkārairvāyuvegaiśca punaḥ prāṇān prayojayet | 
janmāntarañca tasyaiva tatkṣaṇe jāyate kila | janmāntaraṃ yamālokya 
saṃskāramācaredguruḥ | daṇḍāntike samānīya 
annaprāśanamācaret | amukastvaṃ samābhāṣya puṣpaṃ vahnau 
vinikṣepet | iti nāmnā tu saṃsthāpya mahāsaṃskāramācaret | tato'pi 
daṇḍinā devi ! śiṣyāya jñānahetave | śṛṇu śiṣya ! mahādhīra ! 
madvākyaṃ hṛdaye kuru | janmāntarantu tasyaiva prāṇādhikaṃ 
vikārayet | mṛtadehasvarūpo'yaṃ śarīro'yaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | virato 
bhava sarvatra toṣānnāhāraceṣṭayā | brāhmaṇenaiva yaddattaṃ 
tanmātrabhojanaṃ kuru | pañcatattvaṃ sadā sevyaṃ guptabhāvo 
jitendriyaḥ | sadaiva mānasīṃ pūjāṃ sadā mānasatarpaṇam | 
trisandhyaṃ mānasaṃ yogaṃ nābhikuṇḍe prayatnataḥ | sadaiva 
mānasaṃ bhogaṃ tyāgaṃ kuru prayatnataḥ | ṣaḍvargeṣu jito bhūtvā 
naro nārāyaṇaḥ svayam | bhavatyeva na sandeho 
daṇḍadhāraṇamātrataḥ | pitṛvaṃśe saptadaśa mātṛvaṃśe 
trayodaśa | kāntāyāḥ saptamaścaiva lakṣmīnārāyaṇo bhavet | iti 
śrutvā vacastasya śiṣyaścaivābravīdvacaḥ | yaduktaṃ mayi 
muktyarthaṃ tatkaromi nirantaram | pañcatattvaṃ sadā sevyaṃ 
kasmāllābho vadasva me | yatraiva varttate daṇḍī 
bahuśiṣyasamāvṛtaḥ | tatra gatvā prayatnena bahuśiṣyaṃ viceṣṭaya | 
athavā vīramadhye tu yatnena gamanaṃ cara | tattvajñānaṃ 
gṛhasthasya sannidhāne vrajet kila | sudūramapi gantavyaṃ yatrāste 
kulanāyakaḥ | bhikṣucaryā na ca svārthaṃ devatāyāḥ kṛte punaḥ |

p. 954) ācāryapatnīṃ dṛṣṭvā tu bhikṣāṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ | he 
mātardehi me bhikṣāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ tarpayāmyaham | evamuktvā tato 
daṇḍī mahāsaṃskāramācaret | daṇḍāntike samānīya 
homayedvidhipūrvakam | tato humet karaṃ dhṛtvā ājyairaṣṭāhutīḥ 
punaḥ || viparītakrameṇaiva kuryādaṅgaviśodhanam | tataḥ kuryāt 
prayatnena antaryajñopavītakam | śikhāṃ tasya śikhāṃ manye 
pūgamadhye vidhārayet | mūlena yajñasūtrantu tasminneva niveśayet | 
ghṛtairmṛttikayā pūgaṃ vilipya śodhayettataḥ | tatpūgaṃ 
mūlamantreṇa kuṇḍamadhye vinikṣipet | pūrṇāhutiṃ tato dadyāt 
tatpūgamānayet sudhīḥ | sayajñasūtraṃ saśikhaṃ tatpūgaṃ 
preṣayet priye ! | mūlamantraṃ japettatra gajāntakasahasrakam | 
tataścaśrāvayenmantraṃ kālikāyāśca sundari ! | athavā 
śrāvayenmantraṃ tārāyā durlabhaṃ param | mūlamantraṃ 
samuccārya pūgabhasma prayatnataḥ | bhakṣaṇāt tatkṣaṇe 
sākṣādantaryajñopavītavān | bhavatyeva na sandeho naro nārāyaṇaḥ 
svayam | vilvadaṇḍaṃ samānīya vaṃśasyaikaṃ samānayet | 
vilvadaṇḍe nyasennyāsān kālikāyāḥ prayatnataḥ | jīvanyāsaṃ tataḥ 
kṛtvā daṇḍe devīṃ vicintayet | vilve sthitañca caitanyam vaṃśadaṇḍe 
niyojayet | iṣṭadevīsvarūpo'yaṃ daṇḍaśca parameśvari ! | śiṣyasya 
dakṣiṇe haste daṇḍasthāpanamācaret | kamaṇḍaluṃ samānīya 
vāruṇaṃ prajapet sudhīḥ | vāruṇīṃ nikṣipettatra tato mūlamanuṃ 
japet | aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā tasya vāmakare nyaset | gairikaṃ kapilaṃ 
vastraṃ yatnena paridhāpayet | daṇḍadhāraṇamātreṇa naro nārāyaṇī 
bhavet | adyāvadhi mahāmāyāṃ daṇḍopari vibhāvaya | kuru pūjāṃ 
mahākālyā daṇḍopari hṛdā tataḥ | sākṣānnārāyaṇastvaṃ hi 
dharmādharmaparo bhava | tava mātā pitā svāmī sarvaṃ daṇḍāntike 
sthitam || iti paramāśramagrahaṇam || 


p. 955)	daṇḍino bhikṣācaraṇaprakārastu tatraiva || sudūraṃ gamanaṃ 
gehe gṛhasthasya dvijasya ca | nārāyaṇaṃ samābhāṣya dvāre tasya 
vrajet sudhīḥ | daṇḍigamanānantaraṃ gṛhasthakarttavyamāha tatraiva || 
iti śrutvā gṛhasthaśca puṭāñjaliparo bhavet | dvāre ca daṇḍinaṃ 
dṛṣṭvā praṇamedbrahmaṇo varaḥ | bhikṣāṃ dadyāt prayatnena madhu 
māṃsaṃ vinā priye ! | ādāya bhikṣāṃ deveśi ! cauravadgamanaṃ 
caret || daṇḍino'vasthitiprakāro'pi tatraiva || gṛhasthasyālaye devi ! 
rātrivāsañca kārayet | tathā hi nagaraṃ gatvā trirātraṃ vasatiṃ caret 
| tīrthabhūmiṃ tato gatvā saptarātraṃ vasettataḥ || daṇḍinaṃ dṛṣṭvā 
brāhmaṇasya karttavyamuktaṃ tatraiva || brāhmaṇo daṇḍinaṃ dṛṣṭvā 
praṇamedvidhipūrvakam | toyapūrṇañca deveśi ! kiṃ vā śūnyaṃ 
kamaṇḍalum | dṛṣṭimātreṇa tatpātraṃ pūrṇaṃ kuryāddvijottamaḥ | 
kamaṇḍalau jaladānaphalamapi tatraiva || kuśāgramāṇaṃ vinduñca 
yadi yadyāt kamaṇḍalau | samudrasaptadānasya phalaṃ sa labhate 
dhruvam || jaladānanindāpi tatraiva || dadyānna yadi mohena sa 
bhavedāpadāśrayaḥ | triṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi sa punarviṭapī bhavet | 
iti te kathitaṃ caṇḍi ! sannyāsadhāraṇaṃ param | na vaktavyaṃ 
paśoragre prāṇānte'pi kadācana | vastutastu kalau daṇḍagrahaṇaṃ 
nāsti || dīrghakālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dhāraṇañca kamaṇḍaloḥ | 
ityādibhirvacanairudvāhatattvādidhṛtairniṣiddhatvāt ||

mahānirvāṇatantre aṣṭamollāse'pi || bhikṣuke'pyāśrame devi 
! vedoktaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam | kalau nāstyeva tattvajño yatastat 
śrautasaṃskṛtiḥ | śaivasaṃskāravidhināvadhūtāśramadhāraṇam | 
tadeva kathitaṃ bhadre ! sannyāsagrahaṇaṃ kalau || nāradapañcarātre 
dvitīyarātre ca || kalau ca daṇḍagrahaṇaṃ naiva nirvāṇakāraṇam | 
paraṃ vedaviruddhañca viparītāya kalpyate || ityādibhiḥ

p. 956) pratiṣiddhatvācca || yattu nirvāṇatantre caturdaśapaṭale || 
brāhmaṇena vinānyatra sannyāso nāsti caṇḍike ! | kuryānmohena 
yo'nyatra sa vai pāpāśrayo naraḥ | guptabhāvena deveśi ! śṛṇu 
matprāṇavallabhe ! | sannyāsinā sadā sevyaṃ pañcatattvaṃ varānane 
! | dvādaśābdasya madhye tu yadi mṛtyurna jāyate | daṇḍaṃ toye 
vinikṣipya bhavet paramahaṃsakaḥ | avadhūtācārarato haṃsaḥ 
paramapūrvakaḥ | saiva cānandavikhyāto dvādaśābde sarasvatī || iti 
vacanamapi kalītaraparam | sarasvatīti daśānāmāvadhūtamadhye 
saṃjñāviśeṣaḥ tatpramāṇantu || 
tīrthāśramavanāraṇyagiriparvatasāgaraḥ | sarasvatī bhāratī ca purīti 
daśa kīrttitāḥ || iti || eṣāṃ lakṣaṇavṛhacchaṅkaravijaye || 
vidyāraṇyasvāmidhṛtaṃ yathā | triveṇī saṅgame tīrthe 
tattvamasyādilakṣaṇe | snāyāttattvārthabhāvena tīrthanāmā sa 
ucyate || 1 || āśramagrahaṇe prauḍha āśāpāśavivarjitaḥ | 
yātāyātavinirmukta etadāśramalakṣaṇam || 2 || suramye nirjhare deśe 
vane vāsaṃ karoti yaḥ | āśāpāśavinirmukto vananāmā sa ucyate || 3 
|| araṇye saṃsthito nityamānandanandane vane | tyaktvā sarvamidaṃ 
viśvamānandalakṣaṇaṃ kila || 4 || vāso girivare nityaṃ gītābhyāse hi 
tatparaḥ | gambhīracalabuddhiśca girināmā sa ucyate || 5 || vaset 
parvatamūleṣu prauḍho yo dhyānadhāraṇāt | sārātsāraṃ vijānāti 
parvataḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 6 || vaset sāgaragambhīro vanaratnaparigrahaḥ | 
maryādāñca na laṅgheta sāgaraḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 7 || svarajñānavaśo 
nityaṃ svaravādī kavīśvaraḥ | saṃsārasāgare sārābhijño yo hi 
sarasvatī || 8 || vidyābhareṇa sampūrṇaḥ sarvabhāraṃ parityajet | 
duḥkhabhāraṃ na jānāti bhāratī parikīrttitaḥ || 9 || jñānatattvena 
sampūrṇaḥ pūrṇatattvapade sthitaḥ | padabrahmarato nityaṃ 
purīnāmā sa ucyate || 10 || kevalanirvāṇatantradarśinastvadhunāpi

p. 957) tathācaranti | avadhūtatāpi caturthāśramiṇāmeva || tathā ca 
yonitantre || caturthāśramiṇāṃ madhye avadhūtāśramo mahān | 
tatrāhaṃ kulayogena mahādevatvamāgataḥ ||

athāvadhūtāśramaḥ | mahānirvāṇatantre aṣṭamollāse || 

śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

avadhūtāśramo devi ! kalau sannyāsa ucyate | vidhinā yena karttavyaṃ 
tatsarvaṃ śṛṇu sāmpratam | brahmajñāne samutpanne virate 
sarvakarmaṇi | adhyātmavidyānipuṇaḥ sannyāsāśramamācaret | 
vihāya vṛddhau pitarau śiśuṃ bhāryāṃ pativratām | tyaktvā 
samarthān bandūṃśca pravrajannārakī bhavet | sampādya 
gṛhakarmāṇi paritoṣyāgamānapi | nirmamo nilayādgacchenniṣkāmo 
vijitendriyaḥ | āhūya svajanān bandūn grāmasthān prativāsinaḥ | 
prītyānumatimanvicchedgṛhājjigamiṣurnaraḥ | 
teṣāmanujñāmādāya praṇamya paradevatām | grāmaṃ 
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya nirapekṣo gṛhādi yat | muktaḥ saṃsārapāśairyaḥ 
paramānandavṛṃhitaḥ | kulāvadhūtaṃ brahmajñaṃ gatvā 
samprārthayedidam | gṛhāśrame paraṃ brahman ! mamaitad vigataṃ 
vayaḥ | prasādaṃ kuru me nātha ! sannyāsagrahaṇaṃ prati | 
nivṛttagṛhakarmāṇaṃ vicārya vidhivadguruḥ | śāntaṃ vivekinaṃ 
vīkṣya dvitīyāśramamādiśet | tataḥ śiṣyaḥ kṛtasnāno yatātmā 
vihitāhnikaḥ | ṛṇatrayavimuktyarthaṃ devarṣīṃścārcayet pitṝn | 
devā brahmā ca viṣṇuśca rudraśca sagaṇaiḥ saha | ṛṣayaḥ 
sanakādyāścātryādyā devarṣayastathā | atra ye pitaraḥ pūjyā 
vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tānapi | pitā pitāmahaścaiva prapitāmaha eva ca | 
mātā pitāmahī devi ! tathaiva prapitāmahī | 
mātāmahādayo'pyevaṃ mātāmahyādayo'pi ca | prācyāmṛṣīn 
yajeddevān dakṣiṇasyāṃ pitṝn yajet | mātāmahān pratīcyāntu 
pūjayennyāsakarmaṇi | pūrvādikramato dadyādāsanānāṃ dvayaṃ 
dvayam | devādīn kramatastatrābāhya pūjāṃ samācaret |

p. 958) samarcya vidhivattebhyaḥ piṇḍān dadyāt pṛthak pṛthak | 
piṇḍapradānavidhinā dattvā piṇḍaṃ yathākramam | kṛtāñjalipuṭo 
bhūtvā prārthayet pitṛdevatāḥ | tṛpyadhvaṃ pitaro ! devā ! 
devarṣimātṛkā ! gaṇāḥ ! | guṇātītapade yūyamanṛṇīkurutācirāt | 
ānṛṇyamarthayitvā tu praṇamya ca punaḥ punaḥ | 
ṛṇatrayavinirmuktamātmaśrāddhaṃ prakalpayet | pitāmahyātmaiva 
sarveṣāṃ tatpitā prapitāmahaḥ | ātmanyātmārpaṇārthāya 
kuryādātmakriyāṃ sudhīḥ | uttarābhimukho bhūtvā pūrvavat 
kalpitāsane | āvāhyātmapitṝn devi ! dadyāt piṇḍaṃ samarcayet | 
prāgagrān dakṣiṇāgrāṃśca paścimāgrān yathākramāt | 
piṇḍārthamāstareddarbhānudagrān svasvakarmaṇi | samāpya 
śrādhakarmāṇi gurudarśitavartmanā | 
mumukṣuścittaśuddhyarthamimaṃmantraśataṃ japet | hrīṃ 
tryambakaṃ yajāmahe sugandhiṃ puṣṭivarddhanam | urvārukamiva 
bandhanānmṛtyormukṣīya māmṛtāt | upāsanānusāreṇa vidyāṃ 
maṇḍalapūrvakam | saṃsthāpya kalasaṃ tatra guruḥ pūjāṃ 
samārabhet | tatastu paramaṃ brahma dhyātvā śāmbhavavartmanā | 
vidhāya pūjāṃ brahmajño brahmasthāpanamācaret | 
prāguktasaṃskṛte vahnau saṅkalpoktāhutiṃ guruḥ | dattvā śiṣyaṃ 
samāhūya sākalyaṃ hāvayettu tam | ādau vyāhṛtibhirhutvā 
prāṇahomaṃ prakalpayet | prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaścodānavyānau ca 
vāyavaḥ | tatra homaṃ tataḥ kuryāddehāmedhyasya muktaye | pṛthivī 
salilaṃ vahnirvāyurākāśameva ca | gandho rasaśca rūpañca sparśaḥ 
śabdo yathākramāt | tato vākpāṇipādāśca pāyūpasthau tataḥ 
param | śrotraṃ tvak nayanaṃ jihvā ghrāṇabuddhīndriyāṇi ca | etāni 
me padānte ca śudhyanti padamuccaret | hrīṃ jyotirahaṃ virajā 
vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ dviṭha ityapi | caturviṃśatitattvāni karmāṇi 
vaidikāni ca | hutvāgnau niṣkriyo dehaṃ mṛtavaccintayettataḥ | 

p. 959) vibhāvya mṛtavatkāryaṃ rahitaṃ sarvakarmaṇā | smaran tat 
paramaṃ brahma yajñasūtraṃ samuddharet | aiṃ klīṃ hūṃ iti 
mantreṇa skandhāduttārya tattvavit | yajñasūtraṃ kare dhṛtvā 
paṭhitvā vyāhṛtitrayam | vahnijāyāṃ samuccārya ghṛtāktamanale 
kṣipet | hutvaivamupavītañca kāmavījaṃ samuccaran | chitvā 
śikhāṃ kare kṛtvā ghṛtamadhye niyojayet | brahmaputtri ! śikhe ! tvaṃ 
hi bālarūpā sanātanī | dīyate pāvake sthānaṃ gacchadevi ! namo'stu 
te | kāmaṃ māyāṃ kūrcamantraṃ vahnijāyāmudīrayet | 
śikhāmāśritya pitaro devā devarṣayastathā | 
sarvāṇyāśrayakarmāṇi nivasanti śikhopari | tataḥ santarpya tāḥ 
sarvā devarṣipitṛdevatāḥ | śikhāsūtraparityāgāddehī brahmamayo 
bhavet | yajñasūtraśikhātyāgāt sannyāsaḥ syāddvijanmanām | 
śūdrāṇāmitareṣāñca śikhāṃ hutvaiva saṃskriyā | tato 
muktaśikhāsūtraḥ praṇameddaṇḍavadgurum | gururutthāpya taṃ 
śiṣyaṃ dakṣakarṇe vadadimam | tattvamasi mahāprājña ! haṃsaḥ 
so'haṃ vibhāvaya | nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ svabhāvena sukhaṃ cara | 
tato ghaṭañca vahniñca visṛjya brahmatattvavit | ātmasvarūpaṃ taṃ 
matvā praṇamecchirasā gurum | namastubhyaṃ namo mahyaṃ tubhyaṃ 
mahyaṃ namo namḥ | tvameva tvadahameva viśvarūpa ! namo'stu te | 
brahmamantropāsakānāṃ tattvajñānāṃ jitātmanām | svamantreṇa 
śikhācchedāt sannyāsagrahaṇaṃ bhavet | 
brahmajñānaviśuddhānāṃ kiṃ yajñaiḥ śrāddhapūjanaiḥ | 
svecchācāraparāṇāntu pratyavāyo na vidyate | tato 
nirddvandvarūpo'sau niṣkāmaḥ sthiramānasaḥ | nirmamo 
viharecchiṣyaḥ sākṣādbrahmamayo bhuvi | mukto vidhiniṣedhābhyāṃ 
niryogakṣemamātmavit | sukhaduḥkhasamo dhīro jitātmā vigataspṛhaḥ 
| sthirātmā prāptaduḥkho'pi sukhe prāpte'pi niḥspṛhaḥ | 
sadānandaḥ śuciḥ śānto nirapekṣo nirākulaḥ | nodvejakaḥ

p. 960) syājjīvānāṃ sadā prāṇihite rataḥ | vigatāmarṣabhīrdānto 
niḥsaṅkalpo nirudyamaḥ | śokadveṣavimuktaḥ syācchatrau mitre samo 
bhavet | śītavātātapasahaḥ samo mānāpamānayoḥ | samaḥ 
śubhāśubhe tuṣṭo yadṛcchālābhavastunā | nistraiguṇyo nirvikalpo 
nirlobhaḥ syāttvasañcayī | yathā satyamupāśritya mṛṣā viśvaṃ 
pratiṣṭhati | ātmāśritastathā deho jānannaivaṃ sukhī bhavet | 
indriyāṇyeva kurvanti svaṃ svaṃ karma pṛthak pṛthak | ātmā sākṣī 
vinirlipto jñātvaivaṃ mokṣabhāgbhavet | dhātupratigrahaṃ 
nindāmanṛtaṃ krīḍanaṃ striyā | ratastāgamasūyāñca sannyāsau 
parivarjayet | sarvatra samadṛṣṭiḥ syāt kīṭe daive tathā nare | sarvaṃ 
brahmeti jānīyāt paribrāṭ sarvakarmasu | viprānnaṃ śvapacānnaṃ 
vā yasmāttasmāt samāgatam | deśaṃ kālaṃ tathā 
cānnamaśnīyādavicārayan | adhyātmaśāstrādhyayanaiḥ sadā 
tattvavicāraṇaiḥ | avadhūto nayet kālaṃ svecchācāraparāyaṇaḥ | 
sannyāsināṃ mṛtaṃ kāyaṃ dāhayenna kadācana | sampūjya 
gandhapuṣpādyairnikhanedvāpsu majjayet | aprāptayogamarttyānāṃ 
sadā kāmābhilāṣiṇām | prabhāvājjāyate devi ! pravṛttiḥ 
karmasaṅkule | atrāpi te sānuraktā dhyānārcā japasādhane | 
śreyastadeva jānantastatraiva dṛḍhaniścayaḥ | ataḥ karmavidhānāni 
proktāni cittaśuddhaye | nāmarūpaṃ bahuvidhaṃ tadarthaṃ kalpitaṃ 
mayā | brahmajñānaṃ vinā devi ! karmasannyasanaṃ vinā | kurvan 
kalpaśataṃ karma na bhavenmuktibhājanaḥ | kulāvadhūtastattvajño 
jīvanmukto narākṛtiḥ | sākṣānnārāyaṇaṃ matvā gṛhasthastaṃ 
prapūjayet | yaterdarśanamātreṇa vimuktaḥ sarvapātakāt | 
tīrthavratatapodānasarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet || ityavadhūtāśramaḥ ||

tadbhedāstu caturdaśollāse ||

devyu vāca ||

dvividhāvāśramau proktau gārhastho bhaikṣukastathā |

p. 961) kimidaṃ śrūyate citramavadhūtāścaturvidhāḥ | 
etadveditugicchāmi tattvataḥ kathaya prabho ! | caturvidhāvadhūtānāṃ 
lakṣaṇaṃ saviśeṣataḥ ||

śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

brahmamantropāsakā ye brāhmaṇakṣatriyādayaḥ | gṛhāśrame 
vasanto'pi jñeyāste yatayaḥ priye ! | pūrṇābhiṣekavidhinā 
saṃskṛtā ye ca mānavāḥ | śaivāvadhūtāste jñeyāḥ pūjanīyāḥ 
kulārcite ! | brahmāvadhūtāḥ śaivāśca sāgrimācāravarttinaḥ | 
vidadhyaḥ sarvakarmāṇi śaśvadīritavartmanā | vinā 
brahmārpitañcaite tathā cakrārpitaṃ vinā | niṣiddhamannaṃ toyañca 
na gṛhṇīyuḥ kadācana | brahmāvadhūtakaulānāṃ 
kaulānāmabhiṣekiṇām | prāgeva kathitaṃ dharmmamācārāśca 
varānane ! | snānaṃ sannyasanaṃ pānaṃ dānañca dārarakṣaṇam | 
sarvamāgamamārgeṇa śaivabrahmāvadhūtayoḥ ||

nirvāṇatantre caturdaśapaṭale ||

śrīśaṅkara uvāca ||

śṛṇu devi ! pravakṣyāmi avadhūto yathā bhavet | vīrasya mūrttiṃ 
jānīyāt sadā tattvaparāyaṇaḥ | yadrūpaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ 
sannyāsadhāraṇaṃ param | tadrūpasarvakarmāṇi 
prakuryādvīravallabhaḥ | daṇḍino muṇḍanaṃ 
cāmāvāsyāyāmācaredyathā | tathā caiva prakuryāttu vīrasya 
muṇḍanaṃ priye ! | asaṃskṛtaṃ keśajālamuktālambikacoccayam | 
asthimālāvibhūṣā vā rudrākṣānapi dhārayet | digambaro vā 
vīrendraścāthavā kaupinī bhavet | raktacandanasiktāṅgaṃ 
kuryādbhasmāṅgabhūṣaṇam ||

yogasāre dvitīyaparicchede || nṛkaroṭiṃ vidhāryañca 
kāṣṭhadaṇḍaṃ tathā priye ! paraśuṃ cājinañcaiva yogīva dhārayet 
sadā | khaṭṭāṅgaṃ dhārayedyogī vāsarūpakavāsasī | kapāle 
dhārayeccandraṃ candanādyairviśeṣataḥ | īṣatpiṅgalakaṃ vastraṃ 
dhārayet sarvadā suta | sata iti śivasya sambodhanam || 
sudarśanākhyaṃ yaccakraṃ tattu sandhārayedbudhaḥ | vipañcīṃ 
kapilañcaiva

p. 962) maḍḍuḍiṇḍimajharjharān | vādayan ḍamaruṃ yogī yatra 
kutrāśrame sthitaḥ || nirvāṇatantre || kṣamā dānaṃ tato dhyānaṃ 
bālabhāvena śailaje ! | śivo'haṃ bhairavānando mukto'haṃ 
kulanāyakaḥ | evaṃ bhāvaparo mantro hetuyuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ | 
saṃvidāsevanaṃ kuryāt sadā kāraṇasevanam | bhavet sākṣāt sa 
puruṣaḥ sambhu rūpo na saṃśayaḥ || mahānirvāṇatantre || 
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraḥ sāmānya eva ca | 
kulāvadhūtasaṃskāre pañcānāmadhikāritā || nirvāṇatantre || 
nirvāṇamuktimāpnoti brāhmaṇo vīrabhāvataḥ | avadhūtaḥ 
kṣatriyaśca mahāyogī na saṃśayaḥ | svarūpo'pi bhavedvaiśyaḥ 
śudro'pi saha lokavān | sampūrṇaphalamāpnoti vipro nirvāṇatāṃ 
vrajet | tribhāgaṃ phalamāpnoti kṣatriyo vīrabhāvataḥ | pādadvayantu 
vaiśyasya śudrasya caikapādakam || daśānāmavadhūtānāṃ 
pratyekaṃ nāma prāguktamidānīmapi teṣāṃ 
pradhānatvenāṅgīkaraṇārthaṃ saṃjñākathanaṃ 
garbhalakṣaṇaviśeṣamāha tatraiva || avadhūtasya cākhyānaṃ 
śṛṇuṣva parvatātmaje ! | vanāraṇye bhāratī ca giriśca purireva ca 
ekasthāne tu saṃsthitya iṣṭadhyānādikaṃ caret | yo mantradānaṃ 
tapasā sa vanaḥ parikīrttitaḥ | srastakeśo jaṭājūṭaḥ sadā 
vātūlavadbhavet | antaryogī mahāvīro'raṇyasaṅgaśca śailaje ! | 
nānāśāstreṣu yo vijño nānākarmaviśāradaḥ | 
sadeṣṭadevībhāvena bhāvayedyo hi cāmalām | sa eva bhāratī vīro 
mahājñānī jitendriyaḥ | sadorddhvabāhuryo vīro muktakeśo 
digambaraḥ | sarvatra samabhāvena bhāvayedyo narottamaḥ | iṣṭadevīṃ 
vinā nāsti sa giriḥ parikīrttitaḥ | nānādeśeṣu pīṭheṣu kṣetreṣu 
tīrthabhūmiṣu | bhramaṇaṃ kurute nityaṃ kuryādyatnena pūjanam | 
devatāyāḥ sadā dhyānaṃ śrīguroḥ pūjanaṃ tathā | antaryāgeṣu 
yo niṣṭhaḥ sa vīraḥ purireva ca | avadhūtāśrame devi ! yasya bhaktiḥ 
suniścalā | tasya tuṣṭā bhavet kālī kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale |

p. 963) avadhūtaṃ samālokya śambhuvat pūjanaṃ caret | śaktitaḥ 
pañcatattvāni yatnenaiva nivedayet | aśaktaḥ parameśāni ! bhaktitaḥ 
paritoṣayet || mahānirvāṇatantre || bhaktāvadhūto dvividhaḥ 
pūrṇāpūrṇavibhedataḥ | pūrṇaḥ paramahaṃsākhyaḥ 
parivrāḍaparaḥ smṛtaḥ | kṛtāvadhūtasaṃskāro yadi 
syājjñānadurbalaḥ | tadā lokālaye tiṣṭhannātmānaṃ sa tu śodhayet 
| rakṣan svajāticihnañca kurvan karmāṇi kevalam | sadā brahmaparo 
bhūtvā sādhayejjñānamuttamam ||

muṇḍamālātantre dvitīyapaṭale || avadhūtaḥ śivaḥ 
sākṣādavadhūtaḥ sadāśivaḥ | avadhūtī śivā devī 
avadhūtāśubhaṃ śṛṇu | caturāśramiṇāṃ madhye 
avadhūtāśramo mahān | avadhūtaśca dvividho gṛhasthaśca 
citānugaḥ | sacelaścāpi digvāsā vidhiyonivihāravān | sadāraḥ 
sarvadārastho aṭṭahāso digambaraḥ | gṛhāvadhūto deveśi ! dvitīyastu 
sadāśivaḥ | avadhūtasya oṃ tatsaditi mantreṇa karma karttavyamāha 
mahānirvāṇatantre || oṃ tatsanmantramuccārya so'hasmīti cintayan | 
kuryādātmocitaṃ karma sadā vairāgyamāśritaḥ | kurvan 
karmāṇyanāsakto nalinīdalanīravat | yatetātmānamuddharttuṃ 
tattvajñānavivekataḥ | sarveṣāmīṃtatsaditi nirdeśena 
karmaphalamāha tatraiva || oṃ tatsaditi mantreṇa yo yatkarmasamācaret | 
gṛhastho vāpyudāsīnastasyābhīṣṭāya tadbhavet | 
tanmantrakriyamāṇakarmaṇaḥ sampūrṇatvamapi tatraiva || 
japahomapratiṣṭhā ca saṃskārādyakhilāḥ kriyāḥ | oṃ tatsaditi 
niṣpannāḥ sampūrṇāḥ syurna saṃśayaḥ | kimanyairbahubhirmantraiḥ 
kimanyairbhūrisādhanaiḥ | brāhmyeṇānena mantreṇa sarvakarmāṇi 
sādhayet | sukhasādhyamabāhulyaṃ sampūrṇaphaladāyakam | nāstye 
tasmānmahāmantrādupāyāntaramambike ! | purapradeśe dehe vā
p. 964) likhitvā dhārayedidam | gehe tasya mahātīrthaṃ dehaḥ 
puṇyamayo bhavet | nigamāgamatantrāṇāṃ sārātsārataro manuḥ | 
oṃ tatsaditi deveśi ! tavāgre satyamīritam  | caturvidhānāṃ 
tattvānāmanyevāmapi vastunām | mantrānyaiḥ śodhanenālaṃ 
syāccedetena śodhitam | paśyan sarvatra sadrūpaṃ 
japaṃstatsanmahāmanum | svecchācāraḥ śuddhacittaḥ sa eva bhuvi 
kaularāṭ | japādasya bhavet siddho muktaḥ syādarṇacintanāt | 
sākṣādbrahmamayo dehī sarthamenaṃ japenmanum | tripado'yaṃ 
mahāmantraḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam | sādhanādasya mantrasya 
bhavenmṛtyuñjayaḥ svayam | yugmayugmapadaṃ vāpi pratyekaṃ 
padameva vā | japtvaitasya maheśāni ! sādhakaḥ siddhibhāgbhavet || iti 
mantrapraśaṃsā ||

śaivāvadhutasya sarva karmānadhikāramāha tatraiva || 
śaivāvadhūtasaṃskāravidhūtākhilakarmaṇaḥ | nāpi daive na vā 
pitre nārṣe kṛtye'dhikāritā ||

atha paramahaṃsaḥ || caturṇāmavadhūtānāṃ turīyo haṃsa 
ucyate | trayo'nye yogabhogāḍhyā muktāḥ sarve śivopamāḥ | haṃso 
na kuryāt strīsaṅgaṃ na vidhatte parigraham | prārabdhamaśnan 
viharenniṣedhavidhivarjitaḥ | tyajet svajāticihnāni karmāṇi 
gṛhamedhinām | turīyo vicaret kṣauṇīṃ niḥsaṅkalpo nirudyamaḥ | 
sadātmabhāvasantuṣṭaḥ śokamohavivarjitaḥ | nirniketastitikṣu
