syānniḥsaṅgo nirupadravaḥ | nārpaṇaṃ bhakṣyapeyānāṃ na tasya 
dhyānadhāraṇā | mukto vimukto nirdvandvo haṃsācāraparo yatiḥ | iti 
te kathitaṃ devi ! caturṇāṃ kulayoginām | lakṣaṇaṃ saviśeṣeṇa 
sādhūnāṃ matsvarūpiṇām | eteṣāṃ darśanāt sparśādālāpāt 
paritoṣaṇāt | sarvatīrthaphalāvāptirjāyate manujanmanām || 

p. 965)	atha ācārabhedāḥ || vedācārastu pūrvamuktaḥ | adhunā 
vaiṣṇavādirucyate | nityātantre prathamapaṭale || atha vakṣye 
maheśāni! vaiṣṇavācāramuttamam | yasya vijñānamātrena kālaḥ 
syādvihitāñjaliḥ | vedācārakrameṇaiva sadā niyamatatparaḥ | 
maithunaṃ tatkathālāpaṃ kadācinnaiva kārayet | hiṃsāṃ nindāñca 
kauṭilyaṃ varjayenmāṃsabhojanam | rātrau mālāñca yantrañca 
spṛśennaiva kadācana | viṣṇuṃ samarcayeddevi ! viṣṇau karma 
nivedayet | bhāvayet sarvadā devi ! sarvaṃ viṣṇumayaṃ jagat | iti 
vaiṣṇavācāraḥ || 

	tathā || śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! subhage ! śaivācāraṃ sudurlabham | 
vedācārakrameṇaiva śaive śākte vyavasthitam | tadviśeṣaṃ 
mahādevi ! kevalaṃ paśughātanam | idānīṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi 
dakṣiṇācāramadrije ! | dakṣiṇāmūrttimuninā āśrito'sau yataḥ 
purā | ata eva maheśāni ! dakṣiṇācāra ucyate | 
prāvarttako'yamācāraḥ prathamo divyavīrayoḥ | 
vedācārakrameṇaiva pūjayet parameśvarīm | svīkṛtya vijayāṃ 
rātrau japenmantramananyadhīḥ || tṛtīyapaṭale || divyavīramataṃ bhadre 
! śṛṇu sarvāṅgasundari ! | samāsataḥ pravakṣyāmi 
pañcamācāramuttamam | divaseṣu maheśāni ! brahmacārī 
samāhitaḥ | pañcatattvānukalpena rātrau devīṃ samarccayet | aparaṃ 
śṛṇu vakṣyāmi sārātsāraṃ parātparam | brahmānandamaye jñāne 
tava snehena pārvati ! | vedaśāstrapurāṇeṣu gūḍho 
jñānasamuccayaḥ | kāṣṭhamadhye yathā vahnistathā devi ! 
pratiṣṭhitaḥ | aśvamedhakṛtau naiva vājihatyā yathā bhavet ! tathaiva 
parameśāni ! yajñe doṣo na vidyate | śuddhāśuddhaṃ 
bhavecchaddhaṃ śodhanādeva pārvati ! | etadeva maheśāni ! 
siddhāntācāralakṣaṇam || samayācāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || snātaḥ 
śuklāmbaradharaḥ śuddhaveśadharastathā | devapūjārato nityaṃ 
tathā viṣṇuparo divā | naktaṃ dravyādikaṃ sarvaṃ yathā lābhena 
cottamam | vidhivat kriyate bhaktyā sa sarvañca phalaṃ labhet || 
kulācāraṃ paścādupapādayiṣyati || ityācārabhedāḥ || 
iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ saptame nirguṇakāṇḍe 
paśubhāvādinirṇayādirūpagranthikathanaṃ nāma prathamaḥ 
paricchedaḥ || 


	atha pūrṇābhiṣekaḥ || mahānirvāṇatantre daśamollāse || 

śrīsadāśiva uvāca ||

vidhānametat paramaṃ guptamāsījjagattraye | guptabhāvena kurvanto 
narā mokṣaṃ yayuḥ purā | prabale kalikāle tu prakāśe kulavartmanaḥ 
| naktaṃ vā divase kuryāt saprakāśābhiṣecanam | nābhiṣekaṃ vinā 
kaulaḥ kevalaṃ madyasevanāt | pūrṇābhiṣiktaḥ kaulaḥ 
syāccakrādhīśaḥ kulārcakaḥ | tatrābhiṣekapūrvāhṇe 
sarvavighnopaśāntaye | yathāśaktyupacāreṇa vighneśaṃ 
pūjayedguruḥ | guruścennādhikārī syācchubhapūrṇābhiṣecane | 
tathābhiṣiktakaulena tatsarvaṃ sādhayet priye ! | khāntārṇaṃ 
vindusaṃyuktaṃ vījamasya prakīrttitam | gaṇako'sya ṛṣiśchando 
nīvṛdvighnastu devatā | karttavyakarmaṇo vighnaśāntyarthaṃ 
viniyogitā | ṣaḍdīrghayuktamūlena ṣaḍaṅgāni samācaret | 
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhyāyedgaṇapatiṃ śive ! || 
sindūrābhaṃ trinetraṃ pṛthutarajaṭharaṃ hastapadmairdadhānam 
khaḍgaṃ pāśāṅkuśeṣṭānyaruṇakaralasadvāruṇīpūrṇakumbham | 
bālendūddīptamauliṃ karipativadanaṃ vījapūrārdragaṇḍaṃ 
bhogīndrābaddhabhūṣaṃ bhajata gaṇapatiṃ raktavastrāṅgarāgam || 
dhyātvaivaṃ mānasairiṣṭvā pīṭhaśaktiṃ prapūjayet | tīvrā ca 
jvālinī nandā bhogadā kāmarūpiṇī | ugrā tejasvatī satyā madhye 
vighnavināśinī | pūrvādito'rcayitvaitāḥ pūjayet kamalāsanam | 
punardhyātvā gaṇeśānaṃ pañcatattvopacārakaiḥ | abhyarcya ca 
caturdikṣu gaṇeśaṃ gaṇanāyakam | gaṇanāthaṃ gaṇakrīḍaṃ yajet 
kaulini ! sattamaḥ | ekadanta vakratuṇḍaṃ lambodaragajānanau | 
mahādevañca vikaṭaṃ dhūmrābhaṃ vighnanāśanam | tato 
brāhmīmukhā śaktīrdikpālāṃśca prapūjayet | teṣāmastrāṇi 
sampūjya vighnarājaṃ visarjayet | evaṃ sampūjya 
vighneśamadhivāsanamācaret | bhojayecca pañcatattvairbrahmajñān 
kulasādhakān | tataḥ paradine snātaḥ kṛtanityoditakriyaḥ | 
ājanmakṛtapāpānāṃ kṣayārthaṃ tilakāñcanam | utsṛjet 
kaulatṛptyarthaṃ bhojyaikaikamapi priye ! | arghyaṃ dattvā dineśāya 
brahmaviṣṇunavagrahān | arcayitvā mātṛgaṇān vasudhārāṃ 
prakalpayet | karmaṇo'bhyudayārthāya vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ samācaret | 
tato gatvā guroḥ pārśvaṃ praṇamya prārthayedidam | ehi nāya ! 
kulācāra ! nalinīkulavallabha ! | tvatpadāmbhoruhacchāyāṃ dehi 
mūrdhni kṛpānidhe ! | ājñāṃ dehi mahābhāga ! 
śubhapūrṇābhiṣecane | nirvighnaṃ karmaṇaḥ siddhirmupaimi 
tvatprasādataḥ | śivaśaktyājñayā vatsa ! kuru pūrṇābhiṣecanam | 
manorathamayī siddhirjāyatāṃ śivaśāsanāt | itthamājñāṃ guroḥ 
prāpya sarvopadravaśāntaye | āyulakṣmībalārogyāvāptyai 
saṅkalpamācaret | tatastu kṛtasaṅkalpo vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ | 
kāraṇaiḥ śuddhisahitairabhyarcya vṛṇuyādgurum | gururmanohare gehe 
gairikādivicitrite | citradhvajapatākābhiḥ phalapuṣpeṇa śobhite | 
kiṅkiṇījālamālābhiścandrātapavibhūṣite | 
ghṛtapradīpāvalibhistamoleśavivarjite | 
karpūrasahitairdhūpairyakṣadhūpaiḥ suvāsite | 
vyajanaiścāmarairbarhairdarpaṇādyairalaṅkṛte | sārddhahastamitāṃ 
vedīmuccakaiścaturaṅgulām | racayenmṛṇmayīṃ tatra 
cūrṇairakṣatasambhavaiḥ | maṇḍalaṃ sarvatobhadraṃ vidadhyāt 
śrīgurustataḥ | svasvakalpoktavidhinā kuryādarcāvidhikriyām | kṛtvā 
pūrvoktavidhinā pañcatattvāni śodhayet | saṃśodhya pañcatattvāni 
pūrvakalpitamaṇḍale | svarṇaṃ vā rājataṃ tāmraṃ mṛṇmayaṃ 
ghaṭameva vā | kṣālitaṃ candravījena dadhyakṣatavicarcitam | 
sthāpayedbrahmavījena sindūreṇāṅkayet śriyā |

p. 968) kṣakārādyairakārāntairvarṇairvinduvibhūṣitaiḥ | 
mūlamantraprajāpena pūrayet kāraṇena tam | athavā tīrthatoyena 
śuddhena pāthasāpi vā | navaratnaṃ suvarṇaṃ vā ghaṭamadhye 
vinikṣipet | panasoḍumbarāśvatthavakulāmrasamudbhavam | pallavaṃ 
tanmukhe dadyādvāgbhavena kṛpānidhiḥ | śarāvaṃ mārttikañcāpi 
phalākṣatasamanvitam | ramāṃ māyāṃ samuccārya sthāpayet 
pallavopari | badhnīyād vastrayugmena grīvāṃ tasya varānane ! | 
śaktau raktaṃ śive viṣṇau śvetavāsaḥ prakīrttitam | sthāṃ sthīṃ 
māyāṃ ramāṃ smṛtvā sthirīkṛtya ghaṭāntare | nikṣipya 
pañcatattvāni navapātrāṇi vinyaset | rājataṃ śaktipātraṃ 
syādgurupātraṃ hiraṇmayam | śrīpātrantu mahāśaṅkhaṃ 
tāmrāṇyanyāni kalpayet | pāṣāṇadārulauhānāṃ pātrāṇi 
parivarjjayet | śaktyā prakalpayet pātraṃ mahādevyāḥ prapūjane | 
pātrāṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā gurūn devīṃ pratarpayet | 
tatastvamṛtasampūrṇaghaṭamabhyarcyayet sudhīḥ | darśayitvā 
dhūpadīpau sarvabhūtabaliṃ haret | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā 
dhyātvāvāhya maheśvarīm | svaśaktyā pūjayediṣṭāṃ 
vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet | homantu kṛtvā niṣpādya 
kumārīśaktisādhanam | puṣpacandanavāsobhirarcayet sa guruḥ śive ! | 
anugṛhṇantu kaulā me śiṣyaṃ prati kulavratāḥ | 
pūrṇābhiṣekasaṃskāre bhavadbhiranumanyatām | evaṃ pṛcchati 
cakreśete brūyurgurumādarāt | mahāmāyāprasādena prabhāvāt 
paramātmanaḥ | śiṣyo bhavati pūrṇaste paratattvaparāyaṇaḥ | 
śiṣyeṇa ca gururdevīmarcayitvārcite ghaṭe | kāmaṃ māyāṃ ramāṃ 
japtvā cālayedghaṭamuttamam | uttiṣṭha 
brahmakalasamuttarābhimukhaṃ guruḥ | 
mantrairetairvakṣyamāṇairabhiṣiñcet kṛpānvitaḥ | 
śubhapūrṇābhiṣekasya sadāśiva ṛṣiḥ smṛtaḥ | chando'nuṣṭup 
devatādyā praṇavaṃ vījamīritam | śubhapūrṇābhiṣekārthe 
viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | guruvastvābhiṣiñcantu
p. 969) brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ | 
durgālaśmībhavānyastvāmabhiṣiñcantu mātaraḥ || 1 || ṣoḍaśī 
tāriṇī nityā svāhā mahiṣamardinī | etāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu 
mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 2 || jayadurgā viśālākṣī brahmāṇī ca 
sarasvatī | etāstvāmbhiṣiñcantu bagalā varadā śivā || 3 || nārasiṃhī 
ca vārāhī vaiṣṇavī vanamālinī | indrāṇī vāruṇī raudrī 
tvābhiṣiñcantu śaktayaḥ || 4 || bhairavī bhadrakālī ca tuṣṭiḥ 
puṣṭirumā kṣamā | śraddhā kāntirdayā śāntirabhiṣiñcantu te sadā 
|| 5 || mahākālī mahālakṣmīrmahānīlasarasvatī | ugracaṇḍā 
pracaṇḍā ca abhiṣiñcantu sarvadā || 6 || matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca 
nṛsiṃho vāmanastathā | rāmo bhārgavarāmastvāmabhiṣiñcantu 
vāriṇā || 7 || asitāṅgo ruruścaṇḍaḥ krodhonmattabhayaṅkaraḥ | 
kapālī bhīṣaṇaśca tvāmabhiṣiñcantu vāriṇā || 8 || kālī kapālinī 
kullā kurukullā virodhinī | vipracittā mahogrā tvāmabhiṣiñcantu 
sarvadā || 9 || indro'gniḥ śamano rakṣo varuṇaḥ pavanastathā | 
dhanadaśca maheśānaḥ siñcantu tvāṃ digīśvarāḥ || 10 || raviḥ 
somo maṅgalaśca budho jīvaḥ sitaḥ śaniḥ | rāhuḥ ketuḥ sanakṣatrā 
abhiṣiñcantu te grahāḥ || 11 || nakṣatraṃ karaṇaṃ yogo vārāḥ 
pakṣau dināni ca | ṛturmāso hāyanastvāmabhiṣiñcantu sarvadā || 12 || 
lavaṇekṣusurāsarpirdadhidugdhajalāntakāḥ | 
samudrāstvābhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 13 || anantādyā 
mahānāgāḥ suparṇādyāḥ patatriṇaḥ | taravaḥ kalpavṛkṣādyāḥ 
siñcantu tvāṃ digīśvarāḥ || 14 || pātālabhūtalavyomacāriṇaḥ 
kṣemacāriṇaḥ | pūrṇābhiṣekasantuṣṭā abhiṣiñcantu pāthasā || 15 || 
daurbhāgyaṃ duryaśo rogā daurmanasyaṃ tathā śucaḥ | 
vinaśyantvabhiṣekena kālīvījena tāḍitāḥ || 16 || 
bhūtapretapiśācāśca grahā ye riṣṭakāriṇaḥ | vidrutāste vinaśyantu 
p. 970) ramāvījena tāḍitāḥ || 17 || abhisārakṛtā doṣā 
vairimantrodbhavāśca ye | manovākkāyajā doṣā 
vinaśyantvabhiṣecanāt || 18 || naśyantu vipadaḥ sarvāḥ sampadaḥ santu 
susthirāḥ | abhiṣekeṇa pūrṇena pūrṇāḥ santu manorathāḥ || 19 ||
ityekādhikaviṃśatyā mantraiḥ saṃsiktasādhanam | 
paśormukhāllabdhamantraṃ punaḥ saṃśrāvayedguruḥ || 
pūrvoktanāmnā sambodhya jñāpayan śaktisādhakān | dadyādīn 
kulanāthāntamākhyānaṃ kauliko guruḥ | śrutamantragurau devi ! 
sampūjya nijadevatām | pañcatattvopacāreṇa gurumabhyarcayettataḥ | 
gobhūhiraṇyavāsāṃsi yānālaṅkaraṇāni ca | gurave daksiṇāṃ 
dattvā yajetkaulān śivātmakān | kṛtakaulārcano dhīraḥ 
śānto'tivinayānvitaḥ | śrīguroścaraṇau spṛṣṭvā bhaktyā 
natvedamarthayet | śrīnātha ! jagatāṃ nātha ! mannātha ! 
karuṇānidhe ! | parāmṛtapradānena pūrayāsmanmanoratham | ājñā 
me dīyatāṃ kaulāḥ ! pratyakṣaśivarūpiṇaḥ ! | macchiṣyāya 
vinītāya dadāmi paramāmṛtam | cakreśa ! parameśāna ! 
kaulapaṅkajabhāskara ! | kṛtārthaṃ kuru sacchiṣyaṃ dehyamuṣmaiḥ 
kulāmṛtam | ājñāmādāya kaulānāṃ paramāmṛtapūritam || 
saśuddhikaṃ pānapātraṃ śiṣyahaste samarpayet | hṛdyākṛṣya gurau 
devīṃ sruvasaṃlagnabhasmanā | svasya śiṣyasya kaulānāṃ kūrce ca 
tilakaṃ nyaset || kūrce bhrūmadhye || tataḥ prasādatattvāni kaulebhyaḥ 
pariveśayet | bhaktānuṣṭhānavidhinā vidadhyāt pānabhojanam | iti te 
kathitaṃ devi ! śubhapūrṇābhiṣecanam | brahmajñānaikajananaṃ 
śivatvaphalasādhanam | navarātraṃ saptarātraṃ pañcarātraṃ 
trirātrakam | athavāpyekarātrañca kūryāt pūrṇābhiṣecanam | 
saṃskāre'smin maheśāni ! pañcakalpāḥ prakīrttitāḥ | navarātre 
vidhātavyaṃ sarvatobhadramaṇḍalam | navanāthaṃ saptarātre
p. 971) pañcābjaṃ pañcarātrake | trirātraṃ caikarātre ca 
padmamaṣṭadalaṃ priye ! | maṇḍale sarvatobhadre navanābhe'pi 
sādhakaiḥ | sthāpanīyā navaghaṭāḥ pañcābje pañcasaṅkhyakāḥ | 
naline'ṣṭadale devi ! ghaṭastvekaḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 
aṅgāvaraṇadevāṃśca keśarādiṣu pūjayet | 
pūrṇābhiṣekasiddhānāṃ kaulānāṃ nirmalātmanām | darśanāt 
sparśanādghrāṇādguruśuddhirvidhīyate | śāktairvā vaiṣṇavaiḥ 
śaivaiḥ saurairgāṇapatairapi | kaulavartmāśritaḥ sādhuḥ 
sādhayedatiyatnataḥ | tathā śaktipūjanaṃ syāt pañcatattvādi 
śodhayet | sveṣṭapūjāvidhānena na tu cakreśvaro bhavet || tathā || 
uktāḥ prayogā bahavaḥ karmāṇi vividhāni ca 
brahmaikaniṣṭhakaulasya tyāgānuṣṭhānayoḥ samam | ekameva paraṃ 
brahma jagadāvṛtya tiṣṭhati | sarvaṃ tadanvitaṃ karma jānanto hi 
ratāḥ priye ! | pṛthaktvena yajanto'pi tat prayānti viśanti ca | sarvaṃ 
brahmaṇi sarvatra brahmaiva paryavasyati | jñeyaḥ sa eva satkaulo 
jīvanmukto na saṃśayaḥ ||

atha pūrṇābhiṣekaprayogaḥ || mahānirvāṇatantre || 
abhiṣekapūrvāhe sarvavighnopaśāntyarthaṃ yathāśaktyupacāreṇa 
gururvighneśaṃ pūjayet | gurustatra cennādhikārī tadābhiṣiktakaulaṃ 
gurutvena svīkṛtya tena sarvaṃ kārayet | gamiti gaṇeśasya vījaṃ 
kṛtanityakriya āsane upaviśyācānto'rghya 
sthāpanādibhūtaśuddhyantaṃ karma kṛtvā ṛṣyādinyāsaṃ kuryād 
yathā | gaṇaka ṛṣirnīvṛcchando vighnarājo devatā 
svakarttavyapūrṇābhiṣekakarmaṇaḥ sarvavighnaśāntyarthaṃ jape 
viniyogaḥ | śirasi oṃ gaṇaka ṛṣaye namaḥ | mukhe novṛcchandase 
namaḥ hṛdi vighnarājāya devatāyai namaḥ | gāṃ hṛdayāya nama 
ityādināṅganyāsakaranyāsau vidhāya mūlena prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ 
kṛtvā dhyāyedyathā || sindūrābhaṃ trinetraṃ pṛthutarajaṭharaṃ 
hastapadmairdadhānaṃ khaḍgaṃ 
pāśāṅkuśeṣṭānyaruṇakaralasadvāruṇīpūrṇakumbham |

p. 972) bālendūddīptamauliṃ karipativadanaṃ vījapūrārdragaṇḍaṃ 
bhogīndrābaddhabhūṣaṃ bhajata gaṇapatiṃ raktavastrāṅgarāgam || 
evaṃ dhyātvā mānasopacāreṇa sampūjya yantre ghaṭe vā 
pīṭhaśaktīḥ pūjayedyathā | ete gandhapuṣpe oṃ tīvrāyai namaḥ | 
evaṃ sarvatra praṇavādinamo'ntena jvālinyai nandāyai kāmarupiṇyai 
ugrāyai tejasvatyai satyāyai madhye vighnanāśinyai pūrvādikrameṇa 
pūjayet | tat ete gandhapuṣpe oṃ kamalāsanāya namaḥ | punardhyātvā 
ghaṭādau dattvā gaṇeśa ihāgacchetyāvāhya pādyādibhiḥ 
pañcatattvopacāraiśca sampūjya caturdikṣu ete gandhapuṣpe 
gaṇeśāya namaḥ | evaṃ gaṇanāyakāya gaṇanāthāya gaṇakrīḍāya 
ekadantāya vakratuṇḍāya lambodarāya gajānanāya mahādevāya 
vikaṭāya dhūmābhāya vighnanāśanāya sarvatra 
praṇavādinamo'ntena gandhapuṣpābhyāṃ pūjayet | brāhmayai 
māheśvaryai kaumāryai vaiṣṇavyai vārāhyai indrāṇyai nārasiṃhyai 
mahālakṣmyai indrādidikpālebhyo vajrādyastrebhyo nama ityanena 
pūjayitvā tato yathāśakti mūlamantraṃ japtvā japaṃ samarpya 
praṇamya gaṇapate kṣamasveti visarjayet | tato 
dīkṣāpaddhatyuktamadhivāsanaṃ kṛtvā brahmajñakulasādhakān 
pañcatattvairbhojayet | tataḥ paradine snātaḥ kṛtanityakriyaḥ oṃ 
tatsadityucārya oṃ adyetyādi amuke māsi amukarāśisthe 
bhāskare'muke pakṣe'mukatithau amukagotraḥ śrī-amukadevaśarmā 
ājanmajñānājñānakṛtapāpakṣayakāma etat satilakāñcanaṃ 
viṣṇudaivataṃ yathāsambhavagotranāmne brāhmaṇāyāhaṃ 
sampradade || iti tilakāñcanamutsṛjya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | tataḥ 
kaulatṛptyarthaṃ bhojyamekamutsṛjet | tataḥ sūryāyārghyaṃ dattvā 
brahmaviṣṇunavagrahān sampūjya 
gauryādiṣoḍaśamātṛkāpūjāvasudhārāsampātanāyuṣyasūkta-
japābhyudayikaśrāddhāni kṛtvā guroḥ pārśvaṃ gatvā praṇamya 
prārthayet | oṃ ehi nātha ! kulācāra ! nalinīkulavallabha ! ||

p. 973) tvatpādāmbhoruhacchāyāṃ dehi mūrddhni kṛpānidhe ! | 
ājñāṃ dehi mahābhāga ! śubhapūrṇābhiṣecane | nirvighnaṃ 
karmaṇaḥ siddhimupaimi tvatprasādataḥ || tato gururvadet || oṃ 
śivaśaktyājñayā vatsa ! kuru pūrṇābhiṣecanam | manorathamayī 
siddhirjāyatāṃ śivaśāsanāt | iti guroranujñāṃ gṛhītvā adyetyādi 
amukagotraḥ śrī-amukadevaśarmā āyurlakṣmībalārogyaprāptikāmo 
mahānirvāṇatantrīyadaśamaparicchedoktapūrṇābhiṣekamahaṃ 
kariṣye | iti saṅkalpya oṃ yajjāgrata iti oṃ devo va iti vā paṭhet | tataḥ 
oṃ sādhubhavānāstāmityādi karma kṛtvā vastrālaṅkaraṇādinā 
saśuddhikāraṇena ca gurumabhyarcya oṃ 
adyetyādimatsaṅkalpitamahānirvāṇatantrīyadaśamaparicchedokta-
pūrṇābhiṣekakarmaṇi amukagotraṃ śrī-amukānandanāthaṃ 
gurutvena gurukarmakaraṇāya vā bhavantamahaṃ vṛṇe iti śiṣye vadati 
| oṃ vṛto'smīti gururbrūyāt | tataḥ śiṣyaḥ oṃ yathāvihitaṃ 
gurukarma kuru iti śiṣye vadati | oṃ yathājñānataḥ karavāṇīti 
gururbrūyāt | tato gururmanoharagṛhe sucitrite 
citradhvajapatākācandrātapādyuṃpaśobhite dhūpaguggulvādibhiḥ 
suvāsite dhṛtapradīpamālāvigatāndhakāraleśe 
caturaṅgulocchrāyasārddhahastamitamṛṇmayavedikāyāṃ 
sarvatobhadrādimaṇḍalaṃ pañcavarṇarajobhirvidadhyāt ||

tatra kramaḥ || navarātrābhiṣeke sarvatobhadraṃ 
saptarātrābhiṣeke navanābhaṃ pañcarātrābhiṣeke pañcābjaṃ 
trirātraikarātrābhiṣeke aṣṭadalapadmaṃ nirmāya tatra 
ghaṭasthāpanaṃ dīkṣāpaddhatyuktaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ 
pañcatattvādikaṃ saṃśodhya ṭhamiti candravījena ghaṭe 
dadhyakṣatau dadyāt | omiti brahmavījena ghaṭaṃ sthāpayet | śrīmiti 
lakṣmībījena ghaṭe sindūraṃ dadyāt | savindukaiḥ 
kṣakārādyakārāntairvarṇairmūlamantreṇa ṭhakāreṇa ghaṭaṃ 
pūrayet | athavā tīrthatoyena śuddhajalena vā pūrayet | navaratnaṃ

p. 974) svarṇaṃ vā ghaṭamadhye nikṣipet | 
panasoḍumbarāśvatthavakulāmrāṇāṃ pañcapallavāni aimiti 
vāgbhavavījena ghaṭamukhe dadyāt | 
phalākṣatayuktanūtanaśarāvaṃ śrīmiti hrīmiti mantreṇa tadupari 
dadyāt | vastrayugmena ghaṭasya grīvāṃ vadhnīyāt | tatra kramaḥ | 
śākte raktena śaive vaiṣṇave ca śvetena | tataḥ sthāṃ sthīṃ hrīmiti 
śrīmiti mantreṇa ghaṭaṃ sthirīkṛtya ghaṭaśrīpātrayormmadhye 
dakṣiṇādito nava pātrāṇi sthāpayet | tatra kramaḥ | śaktipātraṃ 
rājataṃ gurupātraṃ hiraṇmayaṃ vīrapātraṃ 
mahāśaṅkhamātmapātramapi tathā | athavā 
pāṣāṇadārulauhabhinnāni pātrāṇi kuryāt | tato guruṃ devīñca 
kāraṇena tarpayet | tataḥ  kāraṇapūrṇaghaṭe svasvakalpoktavidhinā 
devatāṃ sampūjya yathāśakti japtvā japaṃ samarpya 
balidānādikaṃ kṛtvā stavakavacādikaṃ paṭhitvāṣṭottaraṃ sahasraṃ 
śataṃ vā hutvā gandhapuṣpavastramālyapātrādibhiḥ 
kumārīśaktisādhakān sampūjya | oṃ anugṛhṇantu kaulā me 
śiṣyaṃ pratikulavratāḥ | pūrṇābhiṣeka saṃskāre 
bhavadbhiranumanyatām | iti cakreśvare paṭhati te brūyuḥ | oṃ 
mahāmāyāprabhāveṇa prabhāvāt paramātmanaḥ | śiṣyo bhavati 
pūrṇaste paratattvaparāyaṇaḥ | tato guruḥ pūrvoktaghaṭe śiṣyeṇa 
devīmarcayitvā śrīmiti hrīmiti japtvā ghaṭaṃ cālayet | oṃ uttiṣṭha 
brahmakalasa ! devatābhīṣṭasiddhidaḥ | sarvatīrthāmbupūrṇena 
pūrayāsya manoratham | iti paṭhitvā | sadāśiva ṛṣiranuṣṭupchandaḥ 
śiṣyeṣṭadevatā praṇavo vījaṃ āyuḥkīrttibalārogyādyavāptyai 
pūrṇābhiṣeke viniyogaḥ | oṃ guravastvābhiṣiñcantu 
brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ | durgālakṣmībhavānyastvāmabhiṣiñcantu 
mātaraḥ || 1 || ṣoḍaśī tāriṇī nityā svāhā mahiṣamardinī | 
etāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 2 || jayadurgā 
viśālākṣī brahmāṇī ca sarasvatī | etastvāmabhiṣiñcantu vagalā

p. 975) varadā śivā || 3 || nārasiṃhī ca vārāhī vaiṣṇavī vanamālinī 
| indrāṇī vāruṇī raudrī tvābhiṣiñcantu śaktayaḥ || 4 || bhairavī 
bhadrakālī ca tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭirumā kṣamā | śraddhā kāntirdayā 
śāntirabhiṣiñcantu te sadā || 5 || mahākālī 
mahālakṣmīrmahānīlasarasvatī | ugracaṇḍā pracaṇḍādyā 
abhiṣiñcantu sarvadā || 6 || matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca nṛsiṃho 
vāmanastathā | rāmo bhārgavarāmastvāmabhiṣiñcantu vāriṇā || 7 || 
asitāṅgo ruruścaṇḍaḥ krodhonmatto bhayaṅkaraḥ | kapālī 
bhīṣaṇaśca tvāmabhiṣiñcantu vāriṇā || 8 || kālī kapālinī kullā 
kurukullā virodhinī | vipracittā mahogrā tvāmabhiṣiñcantu sarvadā || 
9 || indro'gniḥ śamanī rakṣo varuṇaḥ pavanastathā | 
dhanadaśca tatheśānaḥ siñcantu tvāṃ digīśvarāḥ || 10 ||
raviḥ somo maṅgalaśca budho jīvaḥ sitaḥ śaniḥ | rāhuḥ ketuḥ 
sanakṣatrā abhiṣiñcantu te grahāḥ || 11 || nakṣatraṃ karaṇaṃ yogā 
vārāḥ pakṣau dināni ca | ṛturmāso hāyanastvāmabhiṣiñcantu 
sarvadā || 12 || lavaṇekṣusurāsarpirdadhidugdhajalāntakāḥ | 
samudrāstvābhiṣiñcantu mantrapūtena vāriṇā || 13 || anantādyā 
mahānāgāḥ suparṇādyāḥ patatriṇaḥ | taravaḥ kalpavṛkṣādyāḥ 
siñcantu tvāṃ digīśvarāḥ || 14 || pātālabhūtalavyomacāriṇaḥ 
kṣemacāriṇaḥ | pūrṇābhiṣekasantuṣṭāstvābhiṣiñcantu pāthasā || 
15 || daurbhāgyaṃ duryaśo rogā daurmanasyaṃ tathā śucaḥ |
vinaśyantvabhiṣekeṇa kālīvījena tāḍitāḥ || 16 || bhūtāḥ pretāḥ 
piśācāśca grahā ye riṣṭakārakāḥ | vidrutāste vinaśyantu 
ramāvījena tāḍitāḥ || 17 || abhicārakṛtā doṣā vairimantrodbhavāśca 
ye | manovākkāyajā doṣā vinaśyantvabhiṣecanāt || 18 || naśyantu 
vipadaḥ sarvāḥ sampadaḥ santu susthirāḥ | abhiṣekeṇa pūrṇena 
pūrṇāḥ santu manorathāḥ || 19 ||	ityetairmantraiḥ śiṣyamabhiṣicya 

976) paśormukhād yanmantro labdhastaṃ punastaṃ guruḥ śrāvayet | 
pūrvoktanāmnā śiṣyaṃ sambodhya śaktisādhakān vijñāpya 
gururānandanāthāntaṃ nāma śiṣyāya dadyāt | śrutamantro gurau 
iṣṭadevatāṃ sampūjya pañcatattvairgurumarcayet | tato 
gobhūhiraṇyavāsoyānālaṅkaraṇāni gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | 
tataḥ pañcatattvaiḥ kaulaśaktisādhakān abhyarcya guroścaraṇau 
spṛṣṭvā prārthayed yathā | śrīnātha ! jagatāṃ nātha ! mannātha ! 
karuṇānidhe ! | parāmṛtapradānena pūrayāsmanmanoratham || tato 
guruḥ || ājñā me dīyatāṃ kaulāḥ ! pratyakṣaśivarūpiṇaḥ ! | 
sacchiṣyāya vinītāya dadāmi paramāmṛtam || te brūyuḥ || cakreśa ! 
parameśāna ! kaulapaṅkajabhāskara ! | kṛtārthaṃ kuru sacchiṣyaṃ 
dehyamuṣmaikulāmṛtam || iti kaulājñāmādāya śuddhisahitaṃ 
kāraṇapātraṃ śiṣyāya dadyāt | tataḥ sruvalagnabhasmanā 
sarveṣāṃ lalāṭe tilakaṃ dadyāt | tataścakrasampūrṇaṃ kṛtvā 
yatheṣṭaṃ viharediti || iti pūrṇābhiṣekaprayogaḥ ||

pūrṇābhiṣekānantarantu vāmakeśvaratantre 
catuḥpañcaśatpaṭale ||

bhairava uvāca ||

maṇḍapaṃ vidhivat kuryāt pūjopakaraṇāni ca | paramahaṃsena 
guruṇā pūrṇābhiṣecanañcaret | navaratnaṃ jale sthāpya 
jalenāpūrya kumbhakam || dvipañcāśatpaṭale || nātihrasvaṃ 
nātidīrghaṃ kalasāṣṭakamuttamam | abhiṣekātmakamiti 
ratnādibhiralaṅkṛtam | īśāne ca punaḥ kumbhaṃ 
svarṇādibhiralaṅkṛtam | vastreṇa veṣṭayet kumbhān 
mālyacandanapūrvakam | gaṅgādyāḥ saritaḥ sarvāḥ samudrāśca 
sarāṃsi ca | nadā hradāḥ puṇyatamā nadyaḥ pātālavartmagāḥ | 
sarvatīrthāṇi puṇyāni ghaṭe kurvantu sannidhim | ramāvījena japtena 
pallavaṃ pratipādayet | kūrcena phaladānaṃ syāt strīvījena 
sthirākṛtiḥ | sindūraṃ vahnivījena puṣpaṃ pretena vinyaset |

p. 977) mūlena praṇavenāpi dūrvāṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ | hūṃ phaṭ 
svāheti mantreṇa kuryāddarbheṇa tāḍanam | īśakumbhe yajeddevīṃ 
saṅkalpoktavidhānataḥ | saṅkalpoktena vidhinā yajedāvaraṇaṃ tataḥ | 
baliṃ dadyāt prayatnena homayettantravit sudhīḥ | āgamoktavidhānena 
kuryāttatra kuśaṇḍikām | trimadhvāktena vidhinā vilvapatreṇa 
homayet || upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśaistu punardevīṃ prapūjayet | 
punarāvaraṇaṃ devi ! pūjayed yatnataḥ sudhīḥ | tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ 
datvā prakuryād gurupūjanam | suvedyāḥ pūjayedaṣṭau kumārīśca 
prapūjayet ||

catuḥpañcāśatpaṭale || pūjāvasāne deveśi ! 
kalasairabhiṣecayet | viśeṣataśca tanmantraṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu 
sādarāt | oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hsauḥ putra ! tvaṃ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhava | 
iti pūrvakalasena guruḥ siñcecchiśuṃ śubham | oṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ 
kroṃ hauḥ haṃ saḥ putra ! tvaṃ vetālo bhava | ityagnikalasenaiva guruḥ 
siñcecchiśuṃ śubham | oṃ hrīṃ aiṃ hrīṃ hsauḥ haṃsaḥ putra ! 
tvamaṇimādisiddhīśvaro bhava | iti dakṣiṇakalasena guruḥ 
siñcecchiśuṃ śubham | oṃ hu/ hrīṃ krīṃ śrīṃ putra ! 
tvamañjanāsiddhīśvaro bhava iti nairṛtakalasena guruḥ siñcecchiśuṃ 
śubham | aṃ āṃ aiṃ kroṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ puttra ! tvaṃ 
laghimādisiddhīśvaro bhava | iti vāruṇakalasena guruḥ siñcecchiśuṃ 
śubham | oṃ āṃ aiṃ krīṃ hrīṃ puttra ! tvaṃ kāmyasiddhīśvaro 
bhava | iti marutkalasena guruḥ siñcecchiśuṃ śubham | aiṃ oṃ hrīṃ 
puttra ! tvaṃ stambhanādisiddhiśvaro bhava | iti kuverakalasena guruḥ 
siñcecchiśuṃ śubham | haṃsa aiṃ hrīṃ 
navanavanāmakaraṇābhidhānapūrvakaṃ puttra ! 
tvamākarṣaṇasiddhīśvaro bhava | itīśānakalasena guruḥ siñcet 
śiśuṃ śubham | dakṣiṇāntaṃ kriyākāṇḍaṃ 
kuryātsādhakasattamaḥ || ityabhiṣekaprakaraṇam || 

p. 978)	atha vīrapraśaṃsā || utpattitantre triṣaṣṭipaṭale || yatra 
vīrovaseddevi ! divyo vā parameśvari ! | tatra sarvāṇi tīrthāṇi vasanti 
vīrasādhane | yo vīraḥ sa śivaḥ sākṣāddeva eva na saṃśayaḥ | yatra 
vīro vaseddevi ! tatra kasya bhayaṃ bhavet | nākālamaraṇaṃ tatra na 
durbhiṣabhayaṃ tathā | rājapīḍābhayaṃ devi ! tatra nāsti kadācana || 

atha vīrakṛtyaṃ tatraiva || kulācārarato vīraḥ kulasaṅgī sadā 
bhavet | saṃvidāsevanaṃ kuryāt somapānaṃ maheśvari ! | sarvathā 
kurute devi ! vīraścoddhatamānasaḥ | divyastu 
devatāprāyaścandanāgurulepanaiḥ | raktacandanagandhaiśca sudigdho 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | bhasmāṅgadhūsaro vīra unmattavadviceṣṭitaḥ | 
surāpānarato nityaṃ balipūjāparāyaṇaḥ | naraśchāgaśca mahiṣo 
meṣaḥ śūkara eva ca | śaśakaḥ śallakī godhā khaḍgī kūrmo daśa 
smṛtāḥ | vānaraśca kharaścaiva gajāśvādivihaṅgamāḥ | 
ityādibhirbalerdānaiḥ pūjayet sveṣṭadevatām || tathā | nityaṃ 
naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ prakuryācca dine dine | kulavāre kularkṣe ca tithau 
candanake tathā | bhairavyāḥ kalpitaṃ cakraṃ saṃsthāpya pūrvavat 
priye ! | surāṇāṃ śodhanaṃ kuryād yathāvat parameśvari ! | pravṛtte 
bhairavīcakre sarve varṇā dvijottamāḥ | nivṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve 
varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak | nanu brahmahatyāsurāpānamityādi smṛtyukte 
na mahāpātakajanakasya surāpānasya niṣiddhatvāt surāpānarata iti 
surāṇāṃ śodhanam iti ca kathaṃ saṅgacchata iti cet satyam | 
tattadvacanaṃ 
naimittikavaidhetarapānaniṣedhakamityavagantavyamanyathā 
vedaviruddhatvena tattatsmṛteraprāmāṇyāpattiḥ syāt | tathā ca 
sautrāmaṇīṣṭau vājapeya yāge ca surāgrahān gṛhṇāti 
somagrahāṃśca surāgrahāṃśca gṛhṇāti vājasṛdbhyaḥ 
surāgrahān havantītyādi | iti haṃsaḥ śucisadityādinā

p. 979) surāśodhanañcoktam | tarhi madyamadeyamapeyamagrāhyamiti 
śrutyantarasya kā gatiriti cennaivamarthāparijñānena 
doṣamāśaṅkase | tathā hi devatābhyo'deyaṃ 
devatāsampradānakabhinnaṃ madyamapeyamagrāhyañceti tasyā 
arthaḥ | nimittayāgādītaratra madyadānapānādiniṣedhārthā vā sā 
śrutiriti mantavyam | narāśvamedhau madyañca kaulavarjyaṃ 
dvijātibhirityādivacanaikavākyatayā kalītaraparatvaṃ 
vājapeyādīnāmiti na vācyam | vidyākaravājapeyiprabhṛtīnāṃ 
nāmānusāreṇa kalāvapi tasyāvadhāritatvāt | 
vijayāñcānukalpañca dvijo dadyād yuge yuge || 
ityādivacanādanukalpena yāganirvāha iti tu kudhiyāṃ siddhāntaḥ 
mukhyālābha evānukalpasya uktatvāt ||

tathā ca niruttaratantre pañcamapaṭale || dravyābhāve 
cānukalpaiḥ pūjayet paradevatāmiti | surābhāve ca gokṣīraṃ dvijo 
dadyād yuge yuge | ityādivacanādanukalpena yāganirvāha iti tu 
kudhiyāṃ siddhānto mukhyālābha evānukalpasyoktatvāt || tathā ca 
niruttaratantre pañcamapaṭale || dravyābhāve cānukalpaiḥ pūjayet 
paradevatāmiti | surābhāve ca gokṣīraṃ dvijo dadyād yuge yuge | iti 
coktamata eva | na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na doṣo madyapānataḥ | 
pravṛttireṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttistu mahāphalā | iti 
madyapānadoṣābhāvo manunā ukta iti asaṃskṛtasurāpānaparaṃ vā 
| taduktamutpattitantre || asaṃskṛtāṃ surāṃ pītvā brāhmaṇo 
brahmahā bhavet | saṃskṛtāntu surāṃ pītvā brāhmaṇo jvaladagnivat | 
sautrāmaṇyāṃ kulācāre brāhmaṇaḥ prapibet surām | anyatra 
kāmataḥ pītvā brāhmaṇyādeva hīyate | yadvā 
surāniṣedhakavacanāni anabhiṣiktaparāṇīti jñeyam | tatraiva 
sarveṣāṃ surāpānavidhidarśanāt | tatpramāṇantu kiñcillikhitam | 
lekhitavyañca yattu mātṛkābhedatantre tṛtīyapaṭale || sarvayajñādhipo 

p. 980) vipraḥ saṃśayo nāsti pārvati ! | sautrāmaṇyāṃ mahāyajñe 
catvāro brāhmaṇādayaḥ ! brāhmaṇasya mahāmokṣaṃ madyapāne 
priyaṃvade ! | brāhmaṇaḥ parameśāni ! yadi pānādikañcaret | 
tatkṣaṇācchivarūpo'sau satyaṃ satyaṃ hi śailaje ! | toye toyaṃ yathā 
līnaṃ taijasaṃ tejase yathā | ghaṭe bhagne yathākāśaṃ vāyau 
vāyuryathā priye ! | tathaiva madyapānena brāhmaṇo brahmaṇi priye ! | 
līyate nātra sandehaḥ paramātmani śailaje ! | sāyujyādimahāmokṣaṃ 
niyuktaṃ kṣatriyādiṣu | madyapānaṃ vinā devi ! tattvajñānaṃ na 
labhyate | ata eva hi viprastu madyapānaṃ samācaret | 
vedamātṛjapenaiva brāhmaṇo na hi śailaje ! | brahmajñānaṃ yadā 
devi ! tadā brāhmaṇa ucyate | devānāmamṛtaṃ brahma tadeva laukikī 
surā | suratvaṃ bhogamātreṇa surā tena prakīrttitā | iti brāhmaṇasya 
madyapānavidhāyakavacanaṃ tadapyabhiṣiktabrāhmaṇaparam | nanu 
abhiṣeke svasvavarṇaṃ parityajya śivatvaṃ labhate dhruvamiti 
prāguktavacanādabhiṣiktasya jātivicārābhāvāt 
kathamabhiṣiktaparamiti cet satyam | svajāticihnarakṣakasya 
jñānadurbalasya pānārthametadvacanamityavehi | athavā 
abhiśaptasurāpāṇaniṣedhārthaṃ surāpāṇaniṣiddhavacanam | surātu 
caturyuga eva pavitrakāriṇī | kevalamabhiśāpenaivāpeyā ataḥ 
śāpamocanapūrvarūpatayā peyaiva tadāha tatraiva || mantratrayaṃ 
sadā pāṭhyaṃ brahmaśāpādimocanam | prakuryāttarhi yenaiva tadā 
brahmamayī surā | havirāropamātreṇa vahnirdīpto yathā bhavet | 
śāpamocanamātreṇa surā muktipradāyinī | ata eva hi deveśi ! 
brāhmaṇaḥ pānamācaret | sa brāhmaṇaḥ sa vedajñaḥ so'gnihotrī sa 
dīkṣitaḥ | bahu kiṃ kathyate devi ! sa eva nirguṇātmakaḥ | 
muktimārgamidaṃ devi ! goptavyaṃ paśusaṅkaṭe | prakāśāt 
siddhihāniḥ syānnindanīyo na cānyathā | yadi 
śāpamocanādapyagrāhyatvaṃ tasyā ityucyate tadā 
gāyatrīgrahaṇamapi brāhmaṇānāmasaṅgataṃ syāttatrāpi 
śāpadarśanāditi | utpattitantre | kulastrīsevanaṃ kuryāt sarvathā 
parameśvari ! ramate yuvatiṃ ramyāṃ kāmānmattaviśālinīm | 
naṭīṃ kapālikāṃ veśyāṃ haḍḍipānāṃ varāṅganām | 
śūdrāṇīṃ mleccharamaṇīṃ yavanīṃ parameśvari ! | 
mātṛyonivicāro'sti mātṛyoniṃ vinā priye ! | kulācāraparo vīraḥ 
kulapūjāparāyaṇaḥ | bhagaliṅgasamāyogādākṛṣya japamācaret | 
niścalantu bhaveccittaṃ koṭisūryagraheṇa kim | cale citte 
bhavedvyādhirniścale niścalaṃ yathā || tathā || śavānāṃ sādhanaṃ 
devi ! latāsādhanamuttamam | citāyāḥ sādhanaṃ devi ! siddhirasti 
kalau yuge | mahākālyā mahāpīṭhe yatra kutra maheśvari ! | 
balidānaṃ narasyāpi haṭādbādhā bhaviṣyati | nivṛttaṃ hi tato devi ! 
balidānaṃ narādikam | tataḥ prabhṛti deveśi ! bhāvadvayaṃ vivarjayet 
|| etadvacanaṃ prabalakalītaraparam || taduktaṃ mahānirvāṇatantre || 
divyavīramayo bhāvaḥ kalau nāsti kadācana | kevalaṃ paśubhāvena 
mantrasiddhirbhavennṛṇām ||

utpattitantre catuḥṣaṣṭipaṭale || bhāvaḥ ko vā mahādeva ! vīraḥ 
ko vā maheśvara ! | divya eva kathaṃ deva ! vada me śaṅkara ! svayam | 
iti devīpraśnānantaraṃ mahādeva uvāca || bhāvastu mānaso dharmaḥ 
sa tu sāṅgaḥ kathaṃ bhavet | nirvikalpaṃ maheśāni ! paraṃ 
brahmamayo bhavet | mānasaḥ kāraṇaṃ devi ! śiva eva na saṃśayaḥ | 
siddhamantrī bhavedvīro na vīro madyapānataḥ | kalau tu bhārate varṣe 
lokā bhāratavāsinaḥ | gṛhe gṛhe surāṃ pītvā varṇabhraṣṭā bhavanti 
hi | divye vīrena bhedo'sti jñānī syāt kulasādhanaiḥ | trisūtrajñaḥ 
kulācāro jñānī kulaguruḥ smṛtaḥ | ṣaṭcakraṃ ṣoḍaśadhāraṃ 

p. 982) trailokyaṃ vyomapañcakam | svadehe ye na jānanti kathaṃ 
sidhyanti yoginaḥ || 

	atha pañcamakāraḥ || nirvāṇatantre ekādaśapaṭale ||

śaṅkara uvāca ||

śṛṇu devi ! pravakṣyāmi tattvaṃ paramagopanam | śrutvā gopaya 
yatnena svayonimiva sundari ! | guptasādhanatantre saptamapaṭale || śṛṇu 
devi ! pravakṣyāmi tattvaṃ paramagopanam | mantroddhārakrameṇaiva 
tatsarvaṃ kathayāmi te | bhamaṃ takārasaṃyuktam thāntaṃ 
vāyuyutaṃ kuru | vinduyuktaṃ punarbhāntaṃ sākāraṃ 
vindusaṃyutam | candrabījaṃ samucārya akārasaṃyutaṃ kuru | 
punarbhāntaṃ takārāntaṃ candraṃ vāyusamanvitam | punarbhāntaṃ 
maheśāni ! pañcamasvarabhūṣitam | thāntaṃ 
vahnisamārūḍhamākārasaṃyutaṃ kuru | punarbhāntaṃ maheśāni ! 
sūryasvarasamanvitam | tāntakumārasaṃyuktaṃ 
dhāntamakārasaṃyutam | pañcatattvamidaṃ devi ! sarvatantreṣu 
gopitam | kaivalyatantre prathamapaṭale || pañcatattvāt paraṃ nāsti 
śāktānāṃ mukhamokṣayoḥ | kevalaiḥ pañcamaireva siddho bhavati 
sādhakaḥ | eṣāṃ madhye maheśāni ! paratattvaṃ viśiṣyate || tathā || 
kevalenādyayogena sādhako bhairavo bhavet | dvitīyena ca tattvena 
mahābhairavatāṃ vrajet | tṛtīyena ca tattvena sādhakaḥ śivarūpadhṛk 
| caturthena varārohe ! rudrarūpadharo bhavet | pareṇa paratāṃ yāti 
mama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ | madyaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā matsyaṃmudrā 
maithunameva ca | pañcatattvamidaṃ devi ! nirvāṇamuktihetave | 
makārapañcakaṃ devi ! devānāmapi durlabhamiti || kāmākhyātantre 
pañcamapaṭale || madyairmāṃsaistathā 
matsyairmudrābhirmaithunairapi | strībhiḥ sārddhaṃ 
mahāsādhurarccayed jagadambikām | anyathā ca mahānindā gīyate 
paṇḍitaiḥ suraiḥ | kāyena manasā vācā | tasmāttattvaparo bhavet | 
kālikātāriṇīdīkṣāṃ gṛhītvā madyasevanam | na karoti naro yastu sa 

p. 983) kalau patito bhavet | vaidike tāntrike caiva japahomavahiṣkṛtaḥ | 
abrāhmaṇaḥ sa evoktaḥ sa eva hastimūrkhakaḥ | śunīsūtrasamaṃ 
tasya tarpaṇaṃ yat pitṛṣvapi | kalī tārāmanuṃ prāpya vīrācāraṃ 
karoti na | śūdratvaṃ taccharīreṇa prāpnuyāt sa na cānyathā |

samayācāratantre dvitīyapaṭale | yā surā sarvakāryeṣu kathitā 
bhuvi muktidā | tasyā nāma bhaveddevi ! tīrthaṃ pānaṃ sudurlabham | 
śūdrāṇāṃ bhakṣyayogyānāṃ yanmāṃsaṃ devanirmitam | 
vedamantreṇa vidhivat proktā sā śuddhiruttamā | bhakṣyayogyāśca 
kathitā ye ye matsyā varānane ! | te rahasye mayā proktā mīnāḥ 
siddhipradāyakāḥ | pṛthukā taṇḍulā bhṛṣṭā 
godhūmacaṇakādayaḥ | tasya nāma bhaveddevi ! mudrā 
muktipradāyinī | bhagaliṅgasya yogena maithunaṃ yadbhavet priye ! | 
tasya nāma bhaveddevi ! pañcamaṃ parikīrttitam | prathamantu 
bhavenmadyaṃ māṃsañcaiva dvitīyakam | matsyañcaiva tṛtīyaṃ 
syānmudrā caiva caturthikā | pañcamaṃ pañcamaṃ vidyāt pañcaite 
nāmataḥ smṛtāḥ ||

matsyādiśabdavyutpattistu kulārṇavatantre pañcamakhaṇḍe 
saptadaśollāse || māyāmalādiśamanānmokṣamārganirūpaṇāt | 
aṣṭaduḥkhādivirahānmatsyeti parikīrttitaḥ | māṅgalyajananāddevi ! 
saṃvidānandadānataḥ | sarvadevapriyatvācca māṃsa ityabhidhīyate || 
tathā | pañcamaṃ devi ! sarveṣu mama prāṇapriyaṃ bhavet | 
pañcamena vinā devi ! caṇḍīmantraṃ kathaṃ japet | yadi 
pañcamakāreṣu bhrāntiṃ cet kurute priye ! | tasya siddhiḥ kathaṃ devi 
! caṇḍīmantraṃ kathaṃ japet | ānandaṃ paramaṃ brahma 
makārāstasya sūcakāḥ || athaiṣāṃ phalam nirvāṇatantre 
ekādaśapaṭale || aṣṭaiśvarya paraṃ mokṣaṃ madyapānena śailaje ! | 
māṃsabhakṣaṇamātreṇa sākṣānnārayaṇo bhavet | 
matsyabhakṣaṇamātreṇa kālī pratyakṣatāmiyāt |

p. 984) mudrāsevanamātreṇa bhūsuro viṣṇurūpadhṛk | maithunena 
mahāyogī mama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ || tathā || ekāṃ śaktiṃ 
samānīya eka eva tu sādhakaḥ | pūjayedabahuyatnena pañcatattvena 
kaulikaḥ | evaṃ kṛtvā labhet siddhiṃ nānyasya dṛṣṭigocare | śaive 
śākte gāṇapatye saure cāndre sulocane ! | tattvajñānamidaṃ proktaṃ 
vaiṣṇave śṛṇu yatnataḥ || dvādaśa paṭale || gurutattvaṃ mantratattvaṃ  
manastvattvaṃ sureśvari ! | devatattvaṃ dhyānatattvaṃ pañcatattvaṃ 
varānane ! | tatrādau śrīgurostattvaṃ snehādvakṣyāmi pārvati ! | 
satailaṃ varttikāyuktaṃ dehasthaṃ brhmatejasam | guruṇā 
mantradānena tatsūtraṃ dīpitaṃ bhavet | devatāyāḥ śarīraṃ hi 
mantrādutpadyate dhruvam | ata eva hi tasyātmā devarūpo na 
saṃśayaḥ | īśvarasya tu yadvīryaṃ tadeva akṣayātmakam | tena 
varṇātmakaṃ dehaṃ jantoreva na saṃśayaḥ | mantravarṇe 
sarvavarṇamayāste parameśvari ! | varṇatattvamidaṃ devi ! sarvasvaṃ 
mama yadbhavet | svayaṃ devo na cānyo'smi nirmalo devarūpakaḥ | 
sarvatra devatāṃ dhyāyet tṛṇagulmalatādiṣu | dhyānena labhate 
sarvaṃ dhyānena viṣṇurūpakaḥ | dhyānena siddhimāpnoti vinā 
dhyānaṃ na sidhyati | iti te kathitaṃ tattvaṃ vaiṣṇavasya sureśvari ! | 
yajjñānādamaratvañca viṣṇurūpo bhavennaraḥ | te narā na hi 
gacchanti kadācidyamamandiram ||

tattvaśodhanaprakārastu kaivalyatantre dvitīyapaṭale ||

śrīśiva uvāca ||

śṛṇu pārvati ! vakṣyāmi teṣāṃ vai śodhanakriyām | padmāsanena 
saṃviśya karapuṭaṃ samācaret | vāme gurūn namaskṛtya dakṣiṇe 
gaṇapatiṃ smaret | madhye devīṃ namaskṛtya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ 
caret | śarīre mātṛkāṃ nyasya ṛṣyādinyāsamācaret | 
svakalpoktavidhānena ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācaret | paścādbhūmau 
trikoṇaṃ vā ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vā maheśvari ! | vilikhya maṇḍalaṃ
p. 985) śuddhaṃ tasyopari ghaṭaṃ nyaset | trikoṇaṃ vetyādivādvayaṃ 
sanniyogaśiṣṭacakāravat samuccayasūcakam | tena tanmaṇḍaladvayaṃ 
karttavyamityarthaḥ | 
vakṣyamāṇabhāvacūḍāmaṇivacanaikavākyatvāditi 
svatantratantravacanaikavākyatvāditi tayā ca caturasramapi || bahudhā 
prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā phaṭkāreṇa punaḥ punaḥ | tatastasmin kāraṇantu 
mūlenaiva ca sthāpayet | mātṛkārṇena deveśi ! viparītena caiva hi | 
punaḥ phaṭkāramantreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kārayet sudhīḥ | tato 
viyacchaktivījaṃ caturdaśasvarānvitam | nādavinduyutaṃ kṛtvā 
tasyopari śataṃ japet | tato mūlaṃ japenmantraṃ māyāvījaṃ tataḥ 
param | dhenuṃ yoniṃ gālinīñca trikhaṇḍaṃ mīnasaṃjñakam | 
darśayitvā varārohe ! ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvā paṭhenmanum | oṃ ekameva 
paraṃ brahma sthūlasūkṣmamayaṃ dhruvam | kacodbhavāṃ 
brahmahatyāṃ tena te nāśayāmyaham | oṃ sūryamaṇḍalasambhūte 
! varuṇālayasambhave ! | amāvījamaye ! devi ! 
śukraśāpādvimucyatām | oṃ vedānāṃ praṇavo vījaṃ 
brahmānandamayaṃ yadi | tena satyena deveśi ! brahmahatyāṃ 
vyapohatu | tataḥ ṣaḍaṅgayuktañca vakāraṃ vindusaṃyutam | evaṃ 
sakāraṇañcāpi varṇākṣarasamanvitam | japtvā mantrī tato dhyāyet 
svamantraṃ kulasundari ! | ānandabhairavaṃ dhyāyed yathā 
tantrānusārataḥ | raktavarṇaṃ caturbāhuṃ trinetraṃ varadaṃ śivam | 
jaṭājūṭadharaṃ devaṃ vāsukīkaṇṭhabhūṣitam | ḍamaruñca 
kapālañca mudgaraṃ pāśamuttamam | dhāriṇaṃ taṃ bhajeddevaṃ 
vyāghracarmāmbaraṃ śivam | evaṃ dhyātvā maheśāni ! tasya 
madhye prapūjayet | māyāvījaṃ tato hu/ phaṭ mantreṇānena santyajet | 
tatastasmin maheśāni ! ānandabhairavīṃ smaret | ānandabhairavīṃ 
devīṃ varābhayalasatkarām | ghorarūpāṃ varārohāṃ trinetrāṃ 
raktavāsasam | raktavarṇāṃ mahāraudrīṃ sahasrabhairavānvitām | 
brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyaiḥ stūyamānāṃ śivāṃ bhaje |

p. 986) iti dhyātvā maheśāni ! tenaiva manunā yajet | tayoraikyaṃ 
vibhāvyaiva gāyatrīṃdaśadhā japet | bhairavaṃ ṅe'ntakañcoktvā 
vidmahe tadanantaram | sudhādevyai tataḥ pañcāddhīmahīti tato vadet | 
tanno devi ! tataḥ paścāttadante ca pracodayāt | iti te kathitā devi ! 
gāyatrī paramākṣarī | tasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa dravyaśuddhiśca 
jāyate | tasya prasādādīśo'haṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | tasya 
madhye mūlamantraṃ vilvadaṇḍairlikhet sakṛt | raktavastreṇa sampūjya 
sudhāṃ vastreṇa gopayet || kāraṇaśabdārthastu tatraiva || 
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ viṣayāṇāñca pārvati ! | sarveṣāṃ 
kāraṇaṃ yasmāt kāraṇaṃ parikīrttitam | asmākañca maheśāni ! 
śarīrakāraṇaṃ hi tat | mṛtyuñjayo'haṃ vīreśi ! 
vīrakāryaprasādataḥ | nirvikāreṇa deveśi ! nirvikalpena cetasā | 
sevyamānaṃ kulaṃ bhadre ! bhuktimuktipradāyakam | nirvikalpo 
maheśāni ! muktibhāgī na saṃśayaḥ | savikalpo varārohe ! rauravaṃ 
yāti niścitam ||

atha māṃsādiśodhanaṃ tṛtīyapaṭale ||

śrīśiva uvāca ||

vakṣye'haṃ parameśāni ! māṃsādeḥ śodhanaṃ priye ! | 
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pūjayenmaṇḍalopari | ādhāraśaktiṃ 
kūrmañca anantaṃ pṛthivīṃ tathā | tanmadhye sthāpayenmāṃsaṃ 
matsyaṃ mudrāñca pārvati ! | hu/ vījena ca saṃmantryaphaṭkāraiḥ 
prokṣaṇañcaret | vāruṇena ca dhenvādiṃ darśayet sādhakottamaḥ | 
tato māyāṃ badhūñcaiva śrīvījaṃ kramaśo japet | śuddhimantraṃ 
paṭhedbhaktyā mūlamantraṃ samuccaran | pavitraṃ kuru deveśi ! 
māṃsaṃ matsyaṃ kuleśvari ! | mudrāṃ śasyodbhavāṃ divyāṃ 
pūjārthaṃ kulanāyike ! | tato hu/ phaṭ vāruṇañca tasyopari japet priye 
! | mūlamantraṃ ca tanmadhye daśadhā japanaṃ caret ||

atha śaktiśodhanaṃ tatraiva || idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi 
nārīṇāṃ śodhanaṃ priye ! | agre vā dakṣiṇe vāpi saṃsthāpya
p. 987) maṇḍalopari | bhāle ca maṇḍalaṃ kuryāttraipuraṃ sindureṇa ca 
| nayane kajjalaṃ dadyānmūlamantraṃ japet sudhīḥ | anyaiśca 
vividhairdravyairbhakṣayet śaktimagrataḥ | tāmbūlaṃ vadane 
dadyādiṣṭamūrttiṃ vibhāvya ca | tataḥ ṣaḍaṅgamantraiśca 
ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācaret | mātṛkārṇaṃ tato nyasya 
ṛṣyādinyāsamācaret | mūlena vyāpakaṃ kṛtvā mūrdhni mūlaṃ 
śataṃ japet | hṛdaye kāmavījañca badhvījañca sañjapet | nābhau 
śrīṃ guhyadeśe ca sarvavījañca pārvati ! | maulau ca vāgbhavaṃ 
kāmaṃ kuṇḍalīṃ kulakuṇḍalīm | śaktivījaṃ japenmantrī 
sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet | vāme māyāṃ śrāvayecca karṇe caiva 
maheśvari ! | evaṃ krameṇa deveśi ! nārīśuddhiḥ prajāyate || iti 
śaktiśodhanam ||

śuddhaśaktiphalamāha tatraiva || sādhitā ca jagaddhātrī 
yadyadvadati pārvati ! | tat sarvaṃ satyatāṃ yāti satyaṃ satyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | dīkṣitānāñca deveśi ! śuddhirnāsti kadāpi hi ||

śyāmārahasyadhṛtapañcamakāraśodhanādiryathā || 
svavāme ṣaṭkoṇāntargatatrikoṇavinduṃ vilikhya vāhye 
vṛttacaturasraṃ vidhāya sāmānyārghyodakenābhyukṣya 
tatrādhāraśaktibhyo nama iti pūjayet | nama ityādhāraṃ prakṣālya 
maṇḍalopari saṃsthāpya maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane nama 
iti sampūjya phaḍiti kalasaṃ prakṣālya kāraṇena samparya 
raktavastramālyādinā bhūṣayitvā oṃ ityādhāropari devībuddhyā 
saṃsthāpya maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane nama ityādhāraṃ 
pūjayitvā aṃ arkamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane nama iti kalasaṃ 
sampūjya uṃ somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmane nama iti pūjayet | 
tataḥ phaḍiti darbhairdravyaṃ santāḍya 
humityavaguṇṭhanamudrayāvaguṇṭhya mūlamantreṇa saṃvīkṣya 
nama ityabhyukṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā mūlena tridhā gandhamādāya oṃ iti 
kumbhe puṣpaṃ dattvā dravyamdhye hasauḥ ityanena trikoṇaṃ vilikhet | 

p. 988) hasauḥ hasauḥ nama iti iṣṭvā hrīṃ krīṃ paramasvāmini 
paramākāśaśūnyavāhini candrasūryāgnibhakṣiṇi pātraṃ 
śivaśiva svāhā | iti ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvā daśadhā prajapya aiṃ hrīṃ 
krīṃ ānandeśvarāya vidmahe sudhādevyai dhīmahi 
tannorddhvanārīśvaraḥ pracodayāt | iti tadupari japtvā śāpamocanaṃ 
kuryāt ||

taduktaṃ svatantre || tataśca kāraṇaṃ divyaṃ samānīya 
ghaṭasthitam | veṣṭitaṃ raktavastreṇa raktamālyena bhūṣitam | 
vāmabhāge maheśāni ! maṇḍalaṃ caturasrakam | tatra 
saṃsthāpayedbhaktyā devībuddhyā varānane ! | maṇḍale kalase 
dravye vahnyarke śaśimaṇḍalam | iti pūjayedityarthaḥ || 
bhāvacūḍāmaṇau ca || vāmabhāge tu ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tanmadhye 
brahmarandhrakam | likhitvā tatra kumbhaṃ vai sauvarṇaṃ rājatañca 
vā | tāmraṃ bhūmimayaṃ vāpi yadvā lauhavivarjitamiti || tantrāntare 
ca || ādhāre sthāpayenmantrī sauvarṇaṃ vātha rājatam | kāṃsyajaṃ 
mṛṇmayaṃ vāti ghaṭamavraṇaśālinam || suvarṇādimayaghaṭasya 
pratyekaphalamāha tatraiva || sauvarṇaṃ bhogadaṃ proktaṃ rājataṃ 
mokṣadaṃ tathā | kāṃsyaṃ śāntikarañcaiva mṛṇmayaṃ puṣṭidaṃ 
bhavet ||

atha śāpamocanamantrāstaduktaṃ kumārītantre || anyacca 
śuṇu deveśi ! yathā pānādikarmaṇi | doṣo na jāyate devi ! tān vai 
mantrān śṛṇuṣva me | ekameva paraṃ brahma sthūlasūkṣmamayaṃ 
dhruvam | kacodbhavāṃ brahmahatyāṃ tena te nāśayāmyaham | 
sūryamaṇḍalasambhūte ! varuṇālayasambhave ! | amāvījamaye ! devi 
! śukraśāpādvimucyatām | vedānāṃ praṇavo vījaṃ 
brahmānandamayaṃ yadi || iti | tena satye na te devi ! brahmahatyāṃ 
vyapohati | evaṃ mantratrayeṇaiva abhimantrya surāṃ śubhām | 
pradadyāt kālikāyai ca tato naivedyabhugbhavet | iti mantratrayaṃ devi ! 
p. 989) ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvā tridhā paṭhet | tataḥ oṃ vā/ vī/ vū/ vai/ vau/ 
vaḥ brahmaśāpavimocitāyai sudhādevyai nama iti tridhā paṭhet | iti 
brahmaśāpavimocanam || tataḥ oṃ śā/ śī/ śū/ śai/ śau/ śaḥ 
śukraśāpādvimocitāyai sudhādevyai nama iti daśadhā japet | iti 
śukraśāpavimocanam || tataḥ ai/ hrī/ śrī/ krā/ krī/ krū/ kra/ krau/ 
kraḥ kṛṣṇaśāpaṃ vimocaya amṛtaṃ śrāvaya śrāvaya svāhā | iti 
daśadhā japet | iti kṛṣṇaśāpavimocanam ||

atha uttaratantre || oṃ haṃsaḥ śucisadvasurantarīkṣaṃ saddhotā 
vedisadatithirduronasat | nṛsadvarasadṛtasadvyomasadabjā gojā ṛtajā 
adrijā ṛtaṃ vṛhat | iti dravyopari vāratrayaṃ paṭhaet | tato dravyamadhye 
ānandabhairavaṃ ānandabharavīñca dhyāyet || yathā || 
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ candrakoṭisuśītalam | aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ 
devaṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam | amṛtārṇavamadhyasthaṃ 
brahmapadmopari sthitam | vṛṣārūḍhaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ 
sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam | kapālakhaṭṭāṅgadharaṃ 
ghaṇṭāḍamaruvādinam | pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ deva 
gadāmuṣaladhāriṇam | khaḍgakheṭakapaṭṭīśamudgaraṃ 
śūladaṇḍadhṛk | vicitraṃ kheṭakaṃ muṇḍaṃ varadābhayapāṇinam 
| lohitaṃ devadeveśaṃ bhāvayet sādhakottamaḥ | evaṃ dhyātvā 
hasakṣamalavarayūṃ ānandabhairavāya vaṣaṭ | ityānandabhairavaṃ 
vāratrayaṃ japet ||

athānandabhairavīṃ dhyāyed yathā | bhāvayecca sudhāṃ 
devīṃ candrakoṭyayutaprabhām | himakundendudhavalāṃ 
pañcavaktrāṃ trilocanām | aṣṭādaśabhujairyuktāṃ 
sarvānandakarodyatām | prahasantīṃ viśālākṣīṃ devadevasya 
sammukhīm | evaṃ dhyātvā hasakṣamalavarayīṃ sudhādevyai vauṣaṭ 
| ityānandabhairavīṃ sampūjya dravyamadhye śakticakraṃ likhet | 
tatastripaṃktyā dakṣiṇāvarttena aṃ 16 kaṃ 16 khaṃ 16 krameṇa 
vilikhya madhye haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ vilikhet | tataḥ śivaśaktyoḥ

p. 990) samāyogād dravyamadhye amṛtatvaṃ vicintya dhenumudrayā 
amṛtīkṛtya vamiti varuṇavījaṃ mūlamantrañcāṣṭadhā japtvā 
devatāmayaṃ bhāvayan ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvā paṭhet | iti dravyaśuddhiḥ || 
taduktaṃ svatantratantre || tataśca bhāvayeddravyaṃ madhye 
raktanibhaṃ priye ! | akathāditripaṃktyā tu halakṣatrayamaṇḍitam | 
pūrvoktayonimudrāyāṃ śivaśaktyāḥ samāgame | amṛtaṃ 
cintayeddravyamaṣṭadhā amṛtaṃ japet | aṣṭadhā mūlamantrañca 
japeddhṛtvā ghaṭaṃ tataḥ | etattu kāraṇaṃ devi ! surasaṅghaniṣevitam 
| ata eva tasyā nāma sureti bhuvanatraye | asyā gandhaḥ keśavastu tena 
gandhena kaulikaḥ | pūjayecca parāṃ devīṃ kālikāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ 
śivām ||

atha māṃsaśodhanam || oṃ pratadviṣṇustava te vīryeṇa mṛgo 
na bhīmaḥ kucaro gariṣṭhā yasyoruṣu triṣu vikrame dhiyanti bhuvanāni 
viśvā | ityanena māṃsaṃ śodhayet | tataḥ oṃ tryambakaṃ yajāmahe 
sugandhiṃ puṣṭivarddhanam | urvārukamiva 
bandhanānmṛtyormukṣīyamāmṛtāt || iti mīnaśuddhiḥ ||

oṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ | divīva 
cakṣurātatam | oṃ tadviprāso vipanyavo jāgṛvāṃsaḥ samindhate | 
viṣṇoryat paramaṃ padam || iti mudrāśuddhiḥ ||

oṃ viṣṇoryoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi piṃsatu | āsiñcatu 
prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te | garbhaṃ dehi sinīvālī garbhaṃ 
dehi sarasvati ! | garbhaṃ te aśvinau devāvādhattāṃ puṣkarasrajau ||

atha svayambhūkusumādi || muṇḍamālātantre dvitīyapaṭale || 
harasamparkahīnāyā latāyāḥ kāmamandire | jātaṃ kusumamādau 
yanmahādevyai nivedayet | svayambhūkusumaṃ devi ! 
raktacandanasaṃjñitam | tathā triśūlapuṣpañca vajrapu'paṃ 
varānane ! | anukalpaṃ lohitākṣacandanaṃ haravallabham ||

p. 991) samāyācāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || strīṇāṃ ṛtuḥ prathamato 
yasmin vayasi jāyate | gṛhṇīyādāśu subhage ! brahmādīnāñca 
durlabham | svayambhūkusumaṃ nāma devatāprītaye sadā | yadi 
bhāgyavaśāddevi ! labhate kusumandvidam | tataḥ svadevatānāñca 
kuryāt snānamanuttamam | vāñchitaṃ labhate mantrī satyaṃ satyaṃ 
varānane ! | idaṃ rahasyaṃ kathitaṃ devatāsnānahetave | na 
kuryādbhakṣaṇaṃ devi ! etayorubhayorapi | etayoḥ 
śaktikusumasvayambhūkusumayorityarthaḥ || tathā || 
jīvadbharttṛkanārīṇāṃ pañcamaṃ kārayet priye ! | tasyā bhagasya 
yadravyaṃ tatkuṇḍodbhavamucyate | mṛtabharttṛkanārīṇāṃ 
pañcamañcaiva kārayet | tasyā bhagasya yaddravyaṃ 
tadgolodbhavamucyate | kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ puṣpaṃ abhāve śṛṇu 
sundari ! | abhāve svayambhūkusumābhāve || vallīpuṣpāṇi sarvāṇi 
taroḥ puṣpāṇi yāni ca | tāni sarvāṇi subhage ! devatāyai nivedayet | 
aparājitāyāḥ puṣpaṃ yadvajrapuṣpañca yadbhavet | karavīrasya yat 
puṣpaṃ yat puṣpaṃ bandhujīvakam | kamalasya ca yat puṣpaṃ yat 
puṣpaṃ campakasya ca | viśeṣatastu yogyāni puṣpāṇi surasundari ! | 
varvarāpatrapuṣpāṇi vilvapatrāṇi sundari ! | pūjārthaṃ 
sarvamādāya sarvasaubhāgyasiddhaye ||

utpattitantre prathamapaṭale || svayambhūkusumairhutvā 
svayambhūpuṣpapūjanam | yaḥ karoti mahādevi ! sa vīraḥ śiva eva tu 
|| gandharvatantre dvitīyapaṭale || ānīya kanyakāṃ divyāṃ pramadāṃ 
yauvanānvitām | prokṣitāṃ mūlamantreṇa suśīlāñcāruhāsinīm | 
sarvadānandahṛdayāṃ ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitām | gurubhaktāṃ 
suveśāñca devatāpūjane ratām | tasya gātre nyasenmantrī 
pañcakāmān śavānapi | pañcakūṭaṃ nyasenmantrī 
pañcāśanmātṛkā nyset | divyamālāṃ samādāya sādhako
p. 992) vijitendriyaḥ sahasraikapramāṇena japet svapnāvatīṃ tataḥ | 
anenaiva vidhānena bhavedākarṣaṇaṃ mahat | tasyāstu madanāgāre 
pūjayet parameśvarīm | sarvasaṅkṣobhiṇīṃ mudrāṃ baddhvā 
yoniṃ pracālayet | kāmeśvarīsvarūpāṃ tāṃ cintayet 
sādhakottamaḥ | kāmeśvarasvarūpañca ātmānamapi bhāvayan | 
svayamakṣobhito bhūtvā sādhakaḥ pañcamaṃ caran | tasyāḥ 
puṣpaṃ svayaṃ yāvadrakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ | 
tāmbūlagandhapuṣpaiśca pratyahaṃ pūjayecca tām | 
nānārasānnapānena tṛptimācarya yatnataḥ | 
vastrālaṅkārabhūṣādyaistasyāḥ santoṣamānayet | yathākāle tathā 
puṣpaṃ svayaṃ tadyogyarūpataḥ | gṛhṇīyāttu prayatnena 
svayambhūkusumaṃ hi tat | svambhūkusumaṃ divyaṃ trailokyeṣvapi 
durlabham | prāpnoti sādhakaśreṣṭho durlabhaṃ sādhayedbhuvi | 
svayambhūpaṣpayogena sākṣāttena sadārcayet | vidyāṃ svapnāvatī 
japtvā kṣipramākarṣaṇaṃ bhavet | devatāśca grahā nāgā dānavā 
rākṣasāśca ye | rājānaśca striyaḥ sarvā nityaṃ vaśyā bhavanti hi | 
svayambhūkusumaiḥ pūjāṃ pratyahaṃ yaḥ samācaret | japtvā vai 
divyamālāyāṃ sarvakāmamupālabhet ||

atha svayambhūkusumādiśuddhiḥ || pluṃ sluṃ mluṃ śluṃ 
svāhā ! amṛte ! amṛtodbhave ! amṛteśvari ! amṛtavarṣiṇi ! amṛtaṃ 
śrāvaya śrāvaya svāhā | iti kuṇḍagolodbhavādiśuddhiḥ || tataḥ 
sarvaṃ huṃ ityavaguṇṭhya dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya tālatrayaṃ 
dattvā daśadigbandhanañca kṛtvā tadupari mūlaṃ saptadhā japediti 
śodhanam || hasakṣamalavarayūṃ ānandabhairavāya vaṣaṭ | 
ityānandabhairavaṃ sampūjya arghyapātraṃ dhṛtvā paṭhet | aiṃ 
krīṃ sauḥ brahmāṇḍakhaṇḍasambhūtamaśeṣarasasambhavam | 
āpūritaṃ mahāpātraṃ pīyūṣarasamāhara | aiṃ 
khaṇḍaikarasānandakakare rayasudhāśuni | svacchandasphuraṇāmatra 
nidhehyakularūpiṇi ! | klīṃ akulasthāmṛtākāre śuddhijñānakare

p. 993) pare | amṛtatvaṃ nidhehyasmin vastuni klinnarūpiṇi ! | sauḥ 
tadrūpeṇaikaramyañca kṛtvāsyaitatsvarūpiṇi | bhūtvā 
parāmṛtākāraṃ mayi visphuraṇaṃ kuru | aiṃ sluṃ vluṃ aṃ amṛte 
amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇi amṛtaṃ śrāvaya śrāvaya svāhā | aiṃ 
vadavadavāgvādini aiṃ klīṃ klinne kledini kledaya mahākṣobhaṃ 
kuru kuru aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ mahāmokṣaṃ kuru hauḥ hasauḥ | anena 
kāraṇamabhimantrya tatra devīmāvāhya tālatrayadigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā 
avaguṇṭhanamudrayā avaguṇṭhya dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya 
yonimudrāṃ pradarśya hasaurnama iti sampūjya tatra śaṅkhamudrāṃ 
pradarśya ṣaḍaṅgena sakalīkṛtya matsyamudrayā pātramācchādya 
mūlaṃ daśadhā japtvā devīsvarūpaṃ pātraṃ bhāvayet | tato 
devyā ājñāṃ gṛhītvā ghaṭaśrīpātrayormadhye pātrāṇi 
sthāpayet | tato ghaṭasamīpe gurupātraṃ tato bhogapātraṃ tataḥ 
śaktipātraṃ yoginīpātraṃ vīrapātraṃ balipātraṃ 
pādyapātramācamanīyapātrañca sthāpayet sāmānyārghyavat | tato 
matsyamāṃsādiyuktakāraṇena tattvamudrayā śrīpātrāt svamūrdhni 
śrī-ānandabhairavaṃ tarpayāmīti tanmantreṇa triḥsantarpya 
tattanmantreṇa amukānandanāthaguruśrīpādukāṃ tarpayāmi iti triḥ 
sakṛdvā santarpya evaṃ paramaparāparaparameṣṭigurūn tarpayet | 
tataḥ śrīpātrāmṛtena devīṃ sāyudhāṃ savāhanāṃ saparivārāṃ 
hṛdi tarpayet || tattvamudrā yathā niruttaratantre caturdaśapaṭale || 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikāyogādvāmahastasya pārvati ! | tarpayet kālikāṃ 
devīṃ sāyudhāṃ saparīkarām | aṅguṣṭho bhairavo devaścānāmā 
śaktirucyate | śivaśaktisamāyogāttarpayeddevi ! devatām | tarpaṇaṃ 
trividhaṃ devi ! śreṣṭhaṃ madhyaṃ kanīyasam | śreṣṭhañca 
divyabhāvasya vīrabhāvasya madhyamam | kanīyasaṃ paśūnāñca 
hṛdi yantre jale kramāt | tatastattvaśuddhiṃ kuryāt | taduktaṃ śrutau |
prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānā me śudhyantāṃ jyotirahaṃ virajā 
p. 994) vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 1 ||
oṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśāni me śudhyantāṃ jyotirahaṃ virajā 
vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 2 ||
oṃ prakṛtyahaṅkārabuddhimanaḥśrotrāṇi me ityādi || 3 || 
oṃ tvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇavacāṃsi me ityādi || 4 ||
oṃ pāṇipādapāyūpasthaśabdā me ityādi || 5 ||
oṃ vāyutejaḥsalilabhūmyātmāno me ityādi || 6 ||
oṃ sparśarūparasagandhaghoṣāṇi me śudhyantāṃ jyotirahamityādi || 
7 || 

iti saptamātrān paṭhitvā kāraṇena karatalaṃ sammārjya dakṣahaste 
trikoṇaṃ likhitvā kalāsadṛśīṃ śuddhiṃ 
trikoṇadakṣiṇottarasammukhamadhye tu nidhāya 
vāmahastāṅguṣṭhamadhyamānāmāyogairekāṃ gṛhītvā 
mūlamantrānte hrīṃ śrīṃ ātmatattvena sthūladehaṃ śodhayāmi 
svāhā | anenādhaḥśuddhiṃ svīkṛtya hrīṃ śrīṃ vidyātattvena 
sūkṣmadehaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā | anena dakṣiṇasthāṃ svīkṛtya 
hrīṃ śrīṃ śivatattvena paradehaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā | 
anenottarasthāṃ svīkṛtya hrīṃ śrīṃ sarvatattvena tattvatrayāśrayaṃ 
jīvaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā | anena vāmamadhyamāñca svīkṛtya 
vastreṇa hastau saṃśodhya hastābhyāṃ sarvāṅgaṃ mārjayediti 
tattvaśuddhiḥ || 

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ saptame nirguṇakāṇḍe 
pūrṇābhiṣekādikathanaṃ nāma dvitīyaḥ paricchedaḥ || 



atha vīrācārapūjā || kṛkalāsadīpikāyāṃ tṛtīyapaṭale || ādau 
dīpanī deveśi ! vaktavyā vīrapūjite ! | yasya vijñānamātreṇa 
jīvanmukto bhavennaraḥ | sarveṣāmeva devānāṃ dīpanīyā 
prakīrttitā | caturvargapradā vidyā siddhividyāsu pārvati ! | anayā tu 
vinā vidyā na sidhyati kadācana | vinā pūjāṃ vinā dhyānaṃ 
vinācāraṃ maheśvari ! | sādhako jñānamātreṇa bhavenmukto 
mahānaghaḥ | tatkule naiva dāridryaṃ tadgotre nāstyapaṇḍitaḥ |

p. 995) prāṇaṃ deyāddhanaṃ deyāt kulaṃ deyāt striyo'pi ca | enāṃ

kūrcayugalaṃ tadanantaram | lajjāvījadvayaṃ devi ! dakṣiṇe kālike 
tathā | punastānyeva vījāni vahnikāntāvadhirmanuḥ | bhairavo'sya 
ṛṣiḥ prokta uṣṇik chanda udāhṛtam | dakṣiṇākālikā proktā devatā 
tantragopitā | vījaṃ śaktiñca deveśi ! kūrcaṃ lajjāṃ kramāt priye ! 
| aṅganyāsakaranyāsau māyayā parikīrttitau | karālavadanāṃ

p. 996) muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitāḥ | tarjanīṃ vāmahastena 
dhārayantyaḥ śucismitāḥ | digambarā hasanmukhyaḥ 
svasvavāhanabhūṣitāḥ | evaṃ dhyātvā prayatnena 
pūjayedaṣṭapatrake | brahmāṇīṃ nārāyaṇīñcaiva tathā 
māheśvarīṃ priye ! | aparājitāñca kaumārīṃ 
vārāhīmarccayedbudhaḥ | nārasiṃhīṃ prapūjyaiva tato dakṣiṇato 
jayet | mahākālaṃ yajeddevi ! viparītaratāntare | digambaraṃ 
muktakeśaṃ caṇḍaveśaṃ prayatnataḥ | evaṃ sampūjya yatnena 
yajenmantramananyadhīḥ | vinā madyaṃ vinā māṃsaṃ yadi devīm 
prapūjayet | devatāśāpamāpnoti mṛte narakamaśnute | vinā 
parastriyā devi ! japed yadi tu sādhakaḥ | śatakoṭijapenaiva tasya 
siddhirna jāyate | striyo gatiḥ striyaḥ prāṇāḥ striyaḥ siddhirna 
saṃśayaḥ | nārīṇāṃ smaraṇe kālī smāritā syānna saṃśayaḥ | 
kaṇṭhe kaṇṭhaṃ mukhe vaktraṃ vakṣojaṃ corasi priye ! | kulāgāre 
tathā daṇḍaṃ dattvā yo japate naraḥ | japārddhenaiva deveśi ! 
sākṣādbhavati kālikā | tasyai kularasaṃ devi ! pāyayitvā yathocitam | 
svayaṃ pītvā japenmantraṃ siddhirbhavati nānyathā | etasya ca 
prayogeṇa glāniryasya prajāyate | kālikāmantravargeṣu nādhikārī sa 
ucyate | lakṣamātrajapenaiva puraścaraṇamucyate | kṣattriyāṇāṃ 
dvilakṣaṃ syādvaiśyānāñca trilakṣakam | śudrāṇāntu 
caturlakṣaṃ puraścraṇamucyate | lakṣamātraṃ japeddevi ! haviṣyāśī 
divā śuciḥ | rātrau niśīthe tāvacca pītvā kularasaṃ priye ! | 
kulanārīgaṇopeto japenmantramananyadhīḥ | evamuktavidhānena 
daśāṃśaṃ homamācaret | taddaśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇañca 
taddāśāṃśābhiṣecanam | taddaśāṃśaviprabhojyaṃ kīrttitaṃ 
parameśvari ! | puṣpiṇīmakarandena homatarpaṇamācaret | evaṃ 
prayogamātreṇa siddho bhavati nanyathā | vāksiddhiṃ labhate devi ! 
kavitvaṃ nirmalaṃ priye ! | dhanenāpi kuveraḥ syādvidyayā

p. 997) syādvṛhaspatiḥ | ākalpajīvano bhūtvā ante muktimavāpnuyāt | 
prayogārambhakāle ca surā dugdhamayī bhavet | lohitaṃ vā bhaveddevi

yasyātra pratyayo devi ! labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ | manoyogaṃ tadarddhe 
syādyatnena varavarṇini! | sauvarṇaṃ rājatañcaiva tathā mauktikameva 
ca | vidrumaṃ padmarāgañca tathaiva varavarṇini ! | proktaṃ 
mālācatuṣkañca samabhāgena mālikām | grathayet paṭṭasūtreṇa 
puṣpiṇī gṛhavarttinī | lohitena varārohe ! sarpākārāṃ suśobhanām | 
snāpayet pañcagavyena makarandena pārvati ! | tāraṃ māyāṃ 
kūrccayugmaṃ māle māle padaṃ tathā | vahnikāntāṃ samuccārya 
śataṃ japtvābhimantrayet | snāpayet pīṭhamadhye tu śūnyāgāre 
varānane ! | tatastāṃ mālikāṃ devi ! gṛhītvā yatnataḥ sudhīḥ | 
jñātvā siddhintu nikaṭe mahotsavamathācaret | ṣoḍaśābdāṃ 
suyuvatīṃ samānīya prayatnataḥ | tāmudvarttya svayaṃ gandhaiḥ 
snāpayeccuddhavāriṇā | divyālaṅkāraśobhābhirdivyapuṣpaiḥ 
sugandhibhiḥ | pūjayitvā ca miṣṭānnairbhojayettāṃ varānanām | 
āsavaṃ pāyayedyatnānniścayaṃ tanmayaṃ pibet | tato mantrī 
ramayettāṃ ratimicchati sā yadā | tasyā haste tato mālāṃ dattvā 
tāṃ yācayedbudhaḥ | nītvā mālāṃ tayā dattāṃ brāhmaṇān 
bhojayettataḥ | tadā japedarddharātrau sākṣādbhavati nānyathā | 
tatrāpi pratyayo no cet kalāmadhye viśedbudhaḥ | paryaṅkasya 
catuṣpārśve paṭṭasūtraṃ manoramam | baddhvā 
dvāviṃśatigranthiṃ ramāpuṭitamūlakaiḥ | niveśyaiva 
svarakṣārthaṃ pāñcālīṃ saindhavīṃ tathā | 
vakṣyamāṇakrameṇaiva vastropari nidhāpayet | ṣoḍaśābdāṃ

p. 998) paralatāṃ gaṇikāñca viśeṣataḥ | samānīya prayatnena 
divyapuṣpairnivedayet | bhojayenmiṣṭabhojyāni kṣaumavastraṃ 
paridhāpayet | lepayeddivyagandhena bhūṣaṇairbhūṣayet svayam | 
ramayet parayā bhaktyā sādhakaḥ siddhihetave | japasyārddhajapenaiva 
siddhirbhavati nānyathā | vinā madyaṃ maheśāni ! na sidhyati 
kadācana | tasmādādau prayatnena pītvā tāṃ pāyayedbudhaḥ | 
tatrāpi pratyayo no ceccāruhomaṃ prakalpayet | niśīthe nirbhayo devi ! 
śmaśāne prāntare tathā gandhaiḥ snānādikaṃ kṛtvā 
pādaśaucādipūrvakam | ghaṭamāropayettatra sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ 
tathā | tāmraṃ vā taṃ maheśāni ! vibhavānukrameṇa tu | kalpayitvā 
niśābhāge pūjayet parameśvarīm | upācārairyathāśakti 
vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet | devīpūjāṃ vidhāyaiva piṣṭantu 
paridāpayet | carau nidhāya yatnena catuṣpiṣṭakavarttulam | tataścaruṃ 
pāyayettu kuṇḍamadhye tu pūjayet | raktāṃ ghanāṃ balākāñca 
nīlāṃ kālīṃ kalāvatīm | dvāreṣu pūjayenmantrī lokapālān 
prayatnataḥ | grahān sampūjayenmantrī catuṣkoṇakrameṇa tu | 
havirdhārāṃ humenmantrī yathāśaktyā tataścarum | 
śrāvayenmūlamantreṇa madhunā siddhihetave | hutā 
sañchādayenmantrī hutāśaṃ pītavāsasā | pūjayed yatnato mantrī 
tato dakṣiṇakālikām | dhūpadīpaiśca naivedyaiḥ 
pradakṣiṇamathācaret | piṣṭavarttulasaṅkhyātaṃ suvarṇādi prajāyate 
| ekenaiva prayogeṇa yadi siddhirbhavet priye ! | tathā homo dvitīyena 
raupyaṃ vāpi sureśvari ! | tṛtīyena bhavettāmraṃ lauhaṃ turyeṇa ca 
smṛtam | eṣāmanyatamāṃ jñātvā sādhayet siddhimuttamām | 
siddhāyāṃ kālikāyāñca naindraṃ durlabhamucyate | 
gurumūlamidaṃ sarvaṃ tasmādādau samarcayet | tasya 
prasādamātreṇa siddho bhavati nānyathā | tatrāpi pratyayo nocet
p. 999) pradakṣiṇamathācaret | amāvasyādine caiva niśīthe 
gatasādhvasaḥ | śmaśāne prāntare vāpi gatvā devīṃ prapūjayet | 
madyamāṃsopacāraiśca dhūpadīpairmanoramaiḥ | naivedyaiḥ 
samiṣānnaiśca tathaiva varavarṇini ! | dravyairlohitavastraiśca 
svarṇābharaṇabhūṣitaiḥ | japanmūlaṃ krodharuddhaṃ 
pradakṣiṇamathācaret | praṇameddaṇḍavadbhūmāvaniśaṃ 
girisambhave ! | niśāyāmuttamaṃ yāvanniśāśeṣaṃ maheśvari ! | 
yadi bhītirbhavettatra tadā dṛdhataro bhavet | dantādanti vidhāyaiva 
manasaiva manuṃ smaret | avaśyaṃ śrūyate śabdaḥ śikhā ca 
dṛśyate sthale | yadi tatra bhaveddevi ! śabdo guṇaguṇo bhavet | tataḥ 
paralatāsaktaḥ punaḥ kāryaṃ tathaiva ca | tadā bhavati cārvaṅgi ! 
devavāṇī suśobhanā | siddhimāvaśyakīṃ jñātvā 
mahotsavamathācaret | tathāpi pratyayo nocet bhagayāgamathācaret | 
kāminīṃ yuvatīṃ yatnāt puṣpitāñca viśeṣataḥ | tāmānīya 
prayatnena svañca bhūṣaṇamācaret | tāmudvarttya svayaṃ 
gandhairbhūṣaṇairvasanaistathā | miṣṭānnairbhojayitvā ca bhaktyā 
paramayā śive ! | tāṃ vivastrāṃ vidhāyaiva 
sthāpayedūrddhvatalpage | tataḥ pūjāṃ vidhāyaiva 
nānāsambhārasaṃyutaiḥ | tatraiva racayedyantraṃ 
raktacandanayāvakaiḥ | bhaganāsāṃ bhagaprāṇāṃ bhagadehāṃ 
bhagastanīm | pūjayedaṣṭapatreṣu madhye devīṃ prapūjayet | 
raktagandhai raktamālyai raktavastrairmanoramaiḥ | pūjayedbhaktito 
mantrī devīdarśanakāmyayā | etasmin samaye devi ! ratimicchati sā 
yadā | latāntu ramayeddevi ! yāvaddhomaṃ karoti na | 
puṣpiṇīmakarandena tato homaṃ samācaret | oṃ namaste 
bhagamālāyai bhagarūpadhare ! śubhe ! | bhagarūpe ! mahābhāge ! 
bhogamokṣaikadāyini ! | bhagavatyāḥ prasādena mama 
siddhirbhaviṣyati | kāli ! kālakalārūpe ! kulīne ! kularūpiṇi ! | 
kulamārga prasādena mama siddhirbhaviṣyati | evaṃ stutvā maheśāni ! 
kūrcavījasahasrakam | japyaṃ deveśi ! vacasā nārasiṃhaṃ

p. 1000) śatāvadhi | unmattā sā svayaṃ brūte yadā 
sākṣādbhaviṣyati | avaśyaṃ kathayet kāntā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā 
| iti te kathitaṃ devi ! guhyādguhyataraṃ param | prakāśāt 
kāryahāniḥ syāttasmādyatnena gopayet | atrāśakto maheśāni ! 
kalāvaṭīṃ samācaret | kuṅkumaṃ candanaṃ candraṃ ekīkṛtya tu 
peṣayet | japet sahasraṃ deveśi ! devīñcaiva prapūjayet | kāminīṃ 
pūjayedbhaktyā tasyā mūrddhvani kārayet | tilakaṃ vaśyamātreṇa 
svayaṃ śirasi dhārayet | ramā vāṇī bhavānī ca sarvasammohinī 
tathā | ṅeyutā parameśāni ! vahnikāntāvadhirmanuḥ | anena 
śatajaptena tilakaṃ mūrdhni kārayet | kalāñca 
pūjayedyatnānnānābharaṇabhūṣitām | pāyayet sā svayaṃ yatnāt 
svayaṃ pītvā ca yatnataḥ | jāyate devavāṇī ca tato devī na saṃśayaḥ 
| evaṃ bhūtvā varārohe ! tato yatnaṃ samācaret | athavā devadeveśi ! 
nagnībhūya vicakṣaṇaḥ | nagnāṃ paralatāṃ paśyan 
japenmantramananyadhīḥ | yāmottaraṃ samārabhya 
yamadvayamatandritaḥ | madhyamāṃsopacāraiśca 
pūjayitveṣṭadevatām | rakṣārthaṃ khaḍgapāṇistu svapārśve'pi 
niyojayet | gaṇanāthaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ vaṭukaṃ yoginīṃ tathā | 
balibhiḥ sāmiṣānnaiśca yajet paramasundari ! | ghṛtapradīpaṃ 
prajvālya tato devīṃ samarcayet | tataḥ sahasraṃ japato 
devatādarśanaṃ bhavet | athavā niyamau bhūtvā 
bhūtalipyādisampuṭam | japet pratidinaṃ devi ! sahasraṃ siddhihetave | 
divārātrau saṃsmaraṇaṃ haviṣyāśanameva ca | sarvadā 
dhautavastrañca paridhāya prayatnataḥ | mlecchasambhāṣaṇañcaiva 
piṣṭakaṃ yatnatastyajet | kevalaṃ dugdhapāyau syādathavā 
yāvakāśanaḥ | śākāhāro jalāhāro nirāhāro'thavā bhavet | 
tīrthamāsādya nivasedbhuvo vā priyameva ca kumārīṃ 
pūjayedyatnānnānābharaṇasaṃyutām | māse pūrṇe varārohe !

p. 1001) niśīthe gatasādhvasaḥ | mahāpūjāṃ prakurvīta 
latāmaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ | madyairmāṃsaiśca vividhairanyaiśca 
vividhaistathā | sampūjya vidhivadbhaktyā sarvadā timirālaye | 
sahasrajapamātreṇa siddhirbhavati nānyathā | sākṣādāyāti sā devī 
satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | sākṣādyāti varārohe ! bhavedindusamo 
naraḥ | añjanaṃ pādukāsiddhiḥ khaḍgasiddhirvarānane ! | 
ajarāmaratā devi ! kāminīsiddhihetave | tathā madhumatīsiddhirjāyate 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | devaceṭīśataṃ tasyā vaśyaṃ bhavati nānyathā | 
svarge marttye ca pātāle sa yatra gantumicchati | tatraiva ceṭikāḥ sarvā 
nayanti nātra saṃśayaḥ | rambhā vāpi ghṛtācī vā yadi japati 
sādhakaḥ | tadaiva yāti sā devī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | 
icchāmṛtyurbhaveddevi ! kimanyat kathayāmi te | eṣa vidhirdakṣiṇāyā 
guhyastantrasahasrake | tava snehādvarārohe ! kathito hi na saṃśayaḥ | 
athavā gaṇikāṃ gatvā pūjayedbhaktibhāvataḥ | tayā saha 
japenmantraṃ pibedaniśamāsavam | nivedya parayā bhaktyā 
pāyayettāṃ prayatnataḥ | evaṃ jñātvā vidhānantu māsamekaṃ 
varānane ! | pratyahaṃ homayedvidvān nityaṃ syādviprabhojanam | 
māse pūrṇe sādhakendro niśīthe tu latāyutaḥ | sākṣāt 
pūjākrameṇaiva pūjayet parameśvarīm | mahātimiramadhyastho 
japenmantramananyadhīḥ | tatkṣaṇājjāyate siddhiḥ satyaṃ devi ! 
vadāmi te | athavā nimbakāṣṭhena pāñcālīṃ racayedbudhaḥ | 
kāntākārāmasiddhāñca snātāmūrddhvakarevatīm | gatvā 
nirjanadeśe tu mahākāntārake priye ! | tatra pūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā 
prayogavidhimācaret | vilikheccandanenaiva bhuvi mantraṃ manorame ! | 
pāñcālīṃ sthāpayettatra raktavastropari priye ! | tatra gatvā caturdikṣu 
kīlakān ropayet priye ! | tatraiva lokapālāṃśca svasvamantreṇa kīlitān 

p. 1002) | gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ kṣetraṃ yoginīṃ candradikṣu ca | 
sampūjya vidhivanmantrī dūrvākṣatasamanvitaḥ | oṃ ye cātra 
saṃsthitā bhūtāḥ śvetāḥ kuṣmāṇḍarūpiṇaḥ | yoginyo 
yātudhānāśca mahāraudrāśca ye tathā | gandharvā vighna 
karttāraḥ kinnarāśca pipīlikāḥ | cipiṭāḥ śaṅkarāścaiva 
ghoradaṃṣṭrā mahotkaṭāḥ | sūcīmukhā mahāpāpāḥ 
pāpakarmaratāśca ye | tāḍitāstu mayā sarve prayāntu 
śaṅkarājñayā | kūrcamantraṃ sasucārya tāḍayedakṣatairbhṛśam | 
mahāpūjāvidhānena devīṃ sampūjya yatnataḥ | tataḥ 
puttalikābhāle mūlamantraṃ likhet sudhīḥ | pūjayeddevatāstasyā 
aṅge devi ! yathocitāḥ | lalāṭe kālikāñcaiva netrayorbhadrakālikām | 
karṇayorguhyakālīñca jihvāyāṃ siddhikālikām | 
śmaśānakālikāṃ mūrdhni mahākālīñca vakṣasi | kaṭyāñca 
kālikāṃ yatnād gale śrīyonikālikām | evaṃ sampūjya yatnena hṛdi 
mūlaṃ śataṃ japet | andhakāre mahāghore nirjane vā surālaye | 
sahasrajapamātreṇa sākṣādbhavati nānyathā | athavāpi citāmadhye 
rambhāmūlaṃ samāśrayet | pūjayet parayā bhaktyā 
nānābharaṇasaṃyutām | madyamāṃsopacāreṇa 
taruṇīgaṇamadhyagaḥ | aniśīthaṃ japenmantraṃ sākṣādbhavati 
nānyathā | athavā karṇikārasya vījena parameśvari ! | 
racayenmālikāṃ divyāṃ raktavastreṇa pārvati ! | puṣpiṇīgṛharaktena 
śobhayet parameśvari ! | tato'pi dhūpayeddevi ! 
kālāgurusamudbhavaiḥ | dhūmairambhojanayane ! devīṃ sampūjya 
yatnataḥ | madyamāṃsopacāraistu sākṣādbhavati nānyathā | iti te 
kathitaṃ devi ! guhyād guhyataraṃ param | prakāśāt siddhihāniḥ 
syāttasmād yatnena gopayet | athavā kākajaṅghāyā latāṃ dagdhvā 
prayatnataḥ | tadbhasmanā varārohe ! bhūṣayet svatanuṃ tathā | 
tataścitāyāṃ deveśi ! nagnībhūya vicakṣaṇaḥ | japedekāntarātmā 

p. 1003) ca sākṣādbhavati nānyathā | athavāpi varāhāṇāṃ 
bhuktānāṃ bhuvi ropayet | tatra devīṃ samabharcya 
madyamāṃsaprayogataḥ | pūjayet parameśāni ! 
nānādravyairmanoramaiḥ | pūjayitvā prayatnena 
japenmantramananyadhīḥ | tataḥ sākṣādvarārohe ! nātra kāryā 
vicāraṇā | athavāpi varārohe ! prayogavidhimācaret | naramuṇḍaṃ 
samānīya mārjārasyāpi pārvati ! | gomuṇḍaṃ sārdramānīya 
bhūmau nikṣipya yatnataḥ | tataḥ pīṭhaṃ samāropya devīṃ dhyātvā 
tu sādhakaḥ | pūjayedarddharātrau  tāmāsavādisamanvitaḥ | japettu 
parayā bhaktyā sahasrāvadhi sādhakaḥ | tataḥ sākṣādbhaveddevi ! 
nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | athavā vanitāṃ ramyāṃ gatvā deveśi ! 
yatnataḥ | pītvā tadadharaṃ samyak karpūreṇa tu pūrayet | tadyonau 
kuṅkumañcaiva tatkarṇe kṣaudrameva ca | tato bhuktvā tu tāṃ 
kāntāṃ tanmantraṃ parameśvari ! | tat kuṅkumañca tat 
kṣaudramekīkṛtya prayatnataḥ | tadeva tilakaṃ kṛtvā niśīthe 
gatasādhvasaḥ | sahasrañca japenmantrī tataḥ sākṣādbhavettadā | 
athavāpi śarīrottharudhireṇa varānane ! | yantraṃ nirmāya yatnena tatra 
devīṃ samarcayet | madyamāṃsopacāraiśca arkapuṣpairvarānane ! | 
sahasrajapamātreṇa siddhirbhavati nānyathā | athavā parameśāni ! 
gaṅgātīre vaset sudhīḥ | upavāsadvayaṃ kṛtvā kuryāt 
snānamatandritaḥ | tato devīṃ samabhyarcya dhūpadīpairmanoramaiḥ | 
haviṣyānnaiśca naivedyaiḥ svayaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ | bhuktvā 
pītvā striyā sārddhaṃ niśīthe gatasādhvasaḥ | japet sahasraṃ deveśi 
! tataḥ siddhirvarānane ! | athavā vaṭamūlastho digvāsā 
muktakeśavān | latābhirveṣṭito bhūtvā japenmantramananyadhīḥ | 
tataḥ sākṣādbhaveddevi ! nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | etenāpi prayogeṇa 
yadi sākṣānna jāyate | tato devi ! pravakṣyāmi upāyaṃ 
paramādbhutam | ekenaiva prayogeṇa yadi sākṣānna jāyate | dvitīyaṃ 
p. 1004) vāpi kurvīta tṛtīyaṃ vāthavā priye ! | tṛtīyena nacet 
siddhistatropāyaṃ vadāmi te | vastre śukle rakte pīte vā nīlavāsasi | 
puttalīṃ racayeddevyāḥ sarvāvayavasundarīm | pūjayet krodharūpeṇa 
raktavastrairmanoharaiḥ | tatra devīṃ japed yantre samabhyarcya 
sahasrakam raktacandanavījena tatra kalpitamālayā | tataḥ 
śālmalikāṣṭhena nimbakāṣṭhena vā priye ! | vahniṃ prajvālya 
yatnena tatra vahniṃ prapūjayet | tataḥ puttalikābhāle likhenmantraṃ 
varānene ! | sindūraputtalīṃ devi ! tato vahnau tu tāpayet | 
tāḍayenmūlamantreṇa mūlamantreṇa rakṣayet | kṣālayet 
śuddhadugdhena athavā dadhivāriṇā | tato hu/kāraṃ prajapet sahasraṃ 
parameśvari ! | tataḥ sākṣādbhaveddevi ! nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | 
athavā tāḍayeddevi ! nārasiṃhena pārvati ! | haviṣyāśī divā 
bhūtvā brahmacārisamo naraḥ | rātrau tāmbūlapūrāsyo 
talāmaṇḍalamadhyagaḥ | nārasiṃhena deveśi ! puṭitantu manuṃ japet | 
tato lakṣajapenaiva sākṣādbhavati nānyathā | avaśyaṃ jāyate 
sākṣānmamaiva vacanaṃ yathā | athavāpi varārohe ! naukālauhena 
pārvati ! | śūlaṃ nirmāya yatnena paṭe devīntu kalpayet | tāṃ 
pūjayet prayatnena raktacandanapuṣpakaiḥ | pūjayitvā prayatnena 
tasyāṅge pīṭhadevatām | āvāhya vidhivadbhaktyā 
japenmantramananyadhīḥ | śūlaṃ sampūjayed yatnāttīkṣṇaṃ 
paramadurlabham | oṃ mahāśūla ! namastubhyaṃ 
sarvadaityāntakāriṇe | astradvayaṃ samuccārya tataḥ śūlena vakṣasi | 
udyamenaiva sā kālī āyāti ca na saṃśayaḥ | avaśyaṃ jāyate 
sākṣānmamaiva vacanaṃ yathā | athavā kālikāvījaṃ śataṃ 
saṃlikhya yatnataḥ | pūrvapatre kuṅkumena mantraṃ svarṇaśalākayā 
| vilikhya bhuvi deveśi ! tatra kāntāṃ samānayet | tadgātre 
pūjayeddevīṃ nānābharaṇasaṃyutām | niśīthe tu 
japenmantramekānte kāntayā saha | japenmantraṃ sahasrantu tataḥ

p. 1005) sākṣādbhaveddhruvam | iti te kathitaṃ devi ! 
guhyādguhyataraṃ param | aprakāśyamidaṃ devi ! gopayet 
mātṛjāravat ||

caturdaśapaṭale || dakṣiṇāyāśca vidyāyā guhyāyāścāpi 
sundari ! | mahākālyā mahāvidyā siddhikālyāśca pārvati ! | 
śmaśānakālikāyāśca nāyikāyāśca śailaje ! | tannāyikānāṃ 
deveśi ! vakṣyate sādhanaṃ tataḥ | uktañca parameśāni ! 
vaktavyaścāpi pārvati ! | kālīvījaṃ maheśāni ! mantraḥ 
sarvottamottamaḥ | triguṇā devi  vikhyātā vidyā paramadurlabhā | 
pūrvoktaṃ sakalaṃ kuryāt phalaṃ tāvadbhavet priye ! | namo'nto vā 
bhavenmantro vaṣaḍanto'pi bhāvini ! | lajjādyo vā bhaveddevi ! 
vāgbhavādyo mahāmanuḥ | etanmantraṃ varārohe ! sarvatantreṣu 
gopitam | bhairavo'sya ṛṣiḥ prokta uṣṇik chanda iti smṛtam | 
pūjādhyānādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavat parikīrttitam | sādhanaṃ 
sakalaṃ devi ! pūrvavat parikīrttitam | yā khyātā tryakṣarī vidyā 
svāhāntā parikīrttitā | pañcākṣarī mahāvidyā sarvatantreṣu gopitā 
| dhyānaṃ pūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvavat parikīrttitam | sādhanaṃ 
sakalaṃ devi ! latāgaṇasamanvitam | ekākṣarī mahāvidyā svāhāntā 
tryakṣarī smṛtā | sādhanaṃ parameśāni ! madyamāṃsopacārataḥ | 
ekākṣarī mahāvidyā svāhāntā parikīrttitā | phaḍantā vā 
maheśāni ! vasaḍantā samīritā | namo'ntā vā maheśāni ! 
sarvasiddhipradāyinī | pūrvavījaṃ varārohe ! kālī cāndraṃ 
varānane ! | kālikāyā mahāvidyā gopitā sarvatantrake | dharāvījaṃ 
varārohe ! kālī lakṣmīśca vāgbhavam | etanmantrākṣaraṃ devi ! 
kālikāyāḥ sudurlabham | eteṣāṃ mantravaryāṇāṃ ṛṣiḥ 
syādugrabhairavaḥ | chando'nuṣṭuviti khyātaṃ devatā dakṣiṇeritā || 

p. 1006) atha vakṣye maheśāni ! guhyakālyāḥ śubhāvahā | 
kālīvījatrayaṃ devi ! guhyakālīyutaṃ tataḥ | svāhāntā kathitā 
vidyā vidyeyaṃ paramā matā | namo'ntā vāpi sā jñeyā 
vaṣaḍantāpi sā smṛtā | kālīvījaṃ samuddhṛtya dharāvījaṃ 
samuddharet | lajjāvījaṃ samuddhṛtya kūrcavījaṃ samuddharet | 
svāhāntā kathitā vidyā caturvargaphalapradā | kūrcalajjāyugaṃ devi 
! tathā kālī prakīrttitā | svāhāntā kathitā vidyā sarvatantreṣu gopitā 
| etāsāmeva vidyānāṃ chanda uṣṇigiti smṛtam | ṛṣiḥ 
syāddakṣiṇāmūrttirdevatā dakṣiṇā smṛtā | kūrcatrayaṃ varārohe 
! svāhāntā hi prakīrttitā | kālīvījaṃ samuddhṛtya kālīkālīpadaṃ 
tathā | mahākālīpadaṃ tasmāt svāhāntaḥ pratimānataḥ | 
sarvatantreṣu vikhyāto mahākālyā mahāmanuḥ | kālīvījaṃ 
samuddhṛtya mahākālī ca ṅeyutā | svāhāntā kathitā vidyā 
caturvargaphalapradā | asitāvījamuddhṛtya mahākālīpadaṃ dvidhā | 
svāhāntā kathitā vidyā mahābhūtivivarddhinī | kālī lakṣmī ramā 
māyā spheṃ paraḥ parikīrttitaḥ | namonto'yaṃ manurdevi ! 
sākṣānmuktiprado mataḥ | kālīvījaṃ samuddhṛtya lajjāvījaṃ 
samuddharet | siddhikālīpadaṃ ṅe'ntamasitāntaṃ varānane ! | 
etāsāmeva vidyānāṃ gāyatrīcchanda īritam | devatāsyā mahākālī 
caturvargaphalapradā | svāhāntā vā varārohe ! phaḍantā vāpi 
kālikā | lajjāvījaṃ samuddhṛtya nijavījaṃ samuddharet | siddhikālī 
tato ṅe'ntā svāhānteyaṃ prakīrttitā | namo'ntā sā mahādevī 
caturvargaphalapradā | asitāmetaduddhṛtya bhavānīvījamuddharet | 
vāgbhavaṃ parameśāni ! parāvījaṃ tataḥ smṛtam | kālīkālīpadaṃ 
tasmāt siddhikāīpadaṃ tataḥ | phaḍantāsau mahāvidyā sarvatantreṣu 
gopitā | ramāvījatrayaṃ devi ! lajjāvījatrayaṃ tathā |

p. 1007) kāmavījatrayaṃ devi ! siddhikālī ca ṅeyutā | svāhāntā 
kathitā vidyā muktimārgapradīpikā | etāsāṃ saumyavidyānāṃ 
gāyatricchanda īritam | ṛṣiḥ syāt kapilo devi ! devatā siddhikālikā | 
kālīvījaṃ samuddhṛtya śmaśānakālikāpadam | ṅeyutaṃ 
parameśāni ! sarvasiddhipradāyinau | kālīvījaṃ samuddhṛtya 
kūrcavījaṃ varānane ! | svāhāntā kathitā vidyā 
sarvasiddhipradāyinī | kūrcadvayaṃ varārohe ! devīvījaṃ maheśvari ! 
| sarvasiddhipradā vidyā sarvaśāstrāvirodhinī | viśvāmitro muniḥ 
prokto gāyatrīcchanda ucyate | śmaśānakālikā devī sarvatantreṣu 
gopitā | etāsāṃ sādhanaṃ devi ! pūrvoktaṃ parameśvari ! | 
sarvāsāṃ vakṣyamāṇantu puraścaraṇamucyate | anenāpi prayogeṇa 
siddhirbhavati nānyathā | śmaśānakālikāyāstu 
kalāyāmupaveśanam | kalāsthāne maheśāni ! kumārīyoga ucyate | 
aṣṭavarṣā tu yā bālā dvādaśādho maheśvari ! | sthāpayettu 
catuṣpārśve miṣṭabhojanabhojitā | pūjayet parayā bhaktyā svayaṃ 
bhuñjīta sādhakaḥ | pāyayedāsavaṃ yatnāt svayañcāpi pibettataḥ | 
sakārañca ghakārañca lakāreṇa samanvitam | japedaṣṭottaraśataṃ 
tāsāṃ karṇe pṛthak pṛthak | tamabhyarcya prayatnena kṛtvā vakṣasi 
sādhakaḥ | aṅganyāsayutaṃ devi ! japenmantramananyadhīḥ | etasmin 
samaye devi ! ratimicchati sā yadā | tadā tāṃ ramayenmantrī pīḍā na 
jāyate yathā | śanairadharapānañca śanairvakṣojamardanam | 
śanairgudaniveśañca śanairāliṅganaṃ priye ! | yadyatra jāyate pīḍā 
tadā siddhivināśinī | evaṃ prayoge tu kālī sākṣādbhavati nānyathā 
| iti te kathitaṃ devi ! guhyādguhyataraṃ param | bhaktihīnaṃ 
kriyāhīnaṃ vidhihīnañca yadbhavet | tadā siddhirvilambena niṣphalaṃ 
naiva jāyate | aviśvāso na karttavya ālasyaṃ naiva pārvati ! |

p. 1008) sarveṣāṃ mantravaryāṇāṃ sāramuddhṛtya pārvati ! | 
dugdhamadhye yathā sarpiḥ kāṣṭhamadhye yathānalaḥ | tathā 
samuddhṛtaḥ sāro devi ! nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ | svayaṃ siddhā hi te 
mantrāḥ sarvatantreṣu gopitāḥ | iti te kathitaṃ devi ! gopanīyaṃ 
prayatnataḥ ||

toḍalatantre caturthapaṭale || śṛṇu cārvaṅgi ! subhage ! 
tārāyāḥ pūjanaṃ mahat | mantreṇācamanaṃ kṛtvā gurudevaṃ 
namettataḥ | jalaṃ saṃśodhya hastau ca pādau ca kṣālayettataḥ | 
mantreṇācamanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ pīṭhaṃ vicintayet | śikhāṃ baddhvā 
tato devi ! vividhaṃ vighnanāśanam | bhūmyāsanañca saṃśodhya 
vastre granthaṃ vidhāya ca | kāyavākśodhanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ 
puṣpaviśodhanam | yantraṃ kṛtvā sādhakendraḥ sāmānyārghyañca 
vinyaset | dvārapālāṃśca sampūjya pīṭhapūjāṃ samārabhet | 
pīṭhaśaktīśca lakṣmyādyāstataḥ pīṭhamanuḥ yajet | bhūtaśuddhiṃ 
pravakṣyāmi viśeṣa iha yadbhavet | kūrcayuktena haṃsena pūrakeṇa 
sureśvari ! | kuṇḍalyā saha cātmānaṃ caturviṃśativījakam | tatra 
līnāni deveśi ! paramātmani sādhakaḥ | māyayā sandahet pāpaṃ 
puruṣaṃ kajjalaprabham | kumbhakena varārohe ! bhasma 
kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ | badhūvījna deveśi ! bhasmanā saha recayet | 
pūrakeṇa tu kūrcena lalāṭe'mṛtasañcayam | cintayet parameśāni ! 
kumbhakenāmṛtāmbudhim | āṃ hrīṃ krīṃ vahnijāyāntaṃ hṛdi 
caikādaśaṃ japet | tatastu cintayeddhīmānāḥkārādraktapaṅkajam | 
tasyopari punarddhyāyet ṭāṅkārādraktapaṅkajam | tasyopari 
punarddhyāyet huṅkāraṃ nīlasannibham | tasyopari 
punarddhyāyedvijabhūṣitakarttṛkām | tasyopari paraṃ vinduṃ 
śivarūpañca avyayam | bālukāyāḥ sahasraikabhāgaṃ vinduṃ 
sudurlabham | vindumadhye maṇidvipaṃ śatayojana vistṛtam |

p. 1009) vindumadhye paraṃ jyotirvudvudākāramaṇḍalam | 
yathaikavudvude devi ! prativimbaṃ prapaśyati | tathā nirīkṣaṇaṃ 
kāryaṃ prakuryājjñānacakṣuṣā | maṇidvīpantu tanmadhye 
suvarṇabālukāmayam | parito bhāvayenmantrī pārijātaṃ manoharam | 
madhye kalpadrumaṃ dhyāyedātmānaṃ tāriṇīmayam | 
udayādityasaṅkāśaṃ sahasrādityavarcasam | caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ 
hemaprākārabhūṣitam | bahucāmaraghaṇṭādidevakanyādiśobhitam | 
mandavāyusamāyuktaṃ gandhadhūpairalaṅkṛtam | tanmadhye 
vedikāṃ dhyāyennānāratnopaśobhitām | suvarṇasūtraracitaṃ 
cintayecchatramuttamam | tadadhaścintayenmantrī ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ 
priye ! | tatra devīṃ cintayecca yathoktadhyānayogataḥ | 
yogasāroktakairdevi ! upacāraiḥ prapūjayet | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ 
kṛtvā ṛṣyādinyāsamācaret | tālatrayaṃ 
choṭikābhirdaśadigbandhanañcaret | ṣoḍhā nyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā 
vyāpakaṃ tadanantaram | viśeṣārghyañca saṃsthāpya pañcatattvaṃ 
viśodhayet | sudhādevīṃ samānīya vipañcīkaramācaret | kumbhe 
puṣpaṃ samādāya trikoṇe triḥprapūjayet | vāmadevaṃ tridhā 
coktvā khecarīṃ daśadhā japet | tathā cānandagāyatrīmṛcañca 
trirjapet sudhīḥ | brahmaśāpaṃ śukraśāpaṃ kṛṣṇaśāpaṃ 
sureśvari ! | digjapāvāśayeddhimān tato mantratrayaṃ paṭhet | 
churikāñca mṛtañcaiva śrītiraskariṇīṃ priye ! | pāvamānañca 
trirjaptvā śodhanādīn samācaret | vāruṇaṃ trirjapeddevi ! cāmṛtaṃ 
saptadhā japet | vidyatattve dhenumudrāṃ pradarśya mūlamaṣṭadhā | 
kuṇḍalinīṃ samutthāpya śivo'haṃ bhāvayettataḥ | māṃsaṃ 
mīnaṃ śodhayitvā mudrāśodhanamācaret | śaktiñca kulapuṣpañca 
tdhoyeśa sādhakottamaḥ | devān pitṝn ṛṣīṃścaiva 
tarpayediṣṭadevatām | śodhitaṃ dravyamādāya viśeṣārghyaṃ 
vinikṣipet | tato brahmamayaṃ dhyātvā pūjādhyānaṃ samācaret | 
nāsāsthānāt samānīya pīṭhe puṣpaṃ nidhāya ca | 

p. 1010) tataścāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā pañcamudrāḥ pradarśayet | 
ṣaḍaṅgena ca sampūjya punarmudrā pradarśayt | jīvanyāsaṃ tataḥ 
kṛtvā upacāraiḥ prapūjayet | ṣaḍaṅgāni ca sampūjya akṣobhyaṃ 
pūjayedadhaḥ | gurupaṃktiṃ pūjayitvā dalamūleṣu pūjayet | 
mahāpūrvāṃśca kālyādīn yajedvairocanādikān | punardevīṃ 
prapūjyātha baliṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā 
mantradhyānaṃ samācaret | japaṃ kṛtvā maheśāni ! devyā haste 
samarpaṇam | prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā tattvasvīkāramācaret | tasyai 
dattvā svayaṃ pītvā prajapet sādhakottamaḥ | pītvā pītvā japitvā ca 
muktaḥ koṭikulaiḥ saha | pratipātre japenmantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ 
sudhīḥ | stutiñca kavacaṃ smṛtvā cāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇamettataḥ 
viśeṣārghyaṃ pradātavyamātmānañca samarpayet | rudrarūpī 
svayaṃ bhūtvā saṃhāreṇa visarjayet | aṅganyāsaṃ maheśāni ! 
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ param | aiśānyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā 
caṇḍālyucchiṣṭapūrvikām | nirmālyavāsinīṃ ṅe'ntāṃ 
caṇḍeśvaryai namo namaḥ | nirmālyaṃ dhārayet śīrṣe candanañca 
lalāṭake | gurusthāne likhedvinduṃ viharedbhairavo yathā | 
saṃkṣepapūjanaṃ devi ! mānasaṃ tattvavarjitam | tatroktamācaredatra 
nānyat sañcārayet sudhīḥ | anyasañcāraṇāddevi ! kruddhā bhavati 
tāriṇī || tatroktaṃ vāhyapūjāprakaraṇoktam || tattvajñānaṃ 
mānasañca śaktimātre hi pārvati ! ||

picchilātantre dvādaśapaṭale || yasmin kṣaṇe mahāmantraḥ 
śrūyate sāvadhānataḥ | taddhīyante śarīrasthapātakāni mahāntyapi | 
prayogaṃ mantraśuddhyādeḥ kathayāmi maheśvari ! | mantraśuddhiṃ 
vidhāyātha kuryānmantraśikhāṃ tataḥ | kallukāṃ mūrdhni 
sañjapya mahāsetuṃ japettataḥ | nābhau japettu nirvāṇaṃ dhyāyet 
kuṇḍalinīṃ tataḥ | prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāyātha 
ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācaret | tata ṛṣyādivinyāso japet saptacchadāṃ sakṛt 
|
p. 1011)  dhruveṇa puṭitaṃ mantraṃ saptadhā prajapet priye ! | 
mantrādau praṇavaṃ dattvā sakṛjjapenmaheśvari ! | praṇavaṃ vātha 
māyāṃ vā vāgbhavaṃ vātha dīpanīm | mantrādau dattvā prajapet 
saptadhā parameśvari ! kālīmantre tu deveśi ! krīṃ mūlaṃ saptadhā 
japet | vidyāmantrañca mantrādau dattvā ca saptadhā japet | svaguruṃ 
mūrdhni sañcintya hṛdi devīṃ tataḥ param | svayaṃ 
kāmakalārūpaṃ bhāvayan japamācaret | japasyānte śivaṃ dhyātvā 
dhyānasyānte punarjapet | aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā śataṃ vā 
prajapenmanum | śyāmāyā dīpanīṃ vidyāratnaṃ mantrāditaḥ śive 
! | dattvā japet saptavāraṃ tato japasamarpaṇam | anyāsāṃ praṇavaṃ 
māyāṃ dīpanī vā japasya ca | setuṃ japtvā saptavāraṃ 
praṇavāntaṃ manuṃ japet | samarpayejjapaṃ paścāt prāṇāyāmān 
samācaret | japasyātra vidhānantu kathitañcānuṣaṅgataḥ ||

atha tārāśatanāmastotram ||

śrīśiva uvāca ||

tāriṇī taralā tanvī tārā taruṇavallarī | tīrarūpā taraśyāmā 
tanukṣīṇapayodharā | turīyā taralā tīvragamanā nīlavāhinī | 
ugratārā jayā caṇḍī śrīmadekajaṭā śivā | taruṇā śāmbhavī 
chinnabhālā ca bhadratāriṇī | ugrā ugraprabhā nīlā kṛṣṇā 
nīlasarasvatī | dvitīyā śobhinī nityā navīnā nityanūtanā | caṇḍikā 
vijayārādhyā devī gaganavāhinī | aṭṭahāsyā karālāsyā 
carasyāditipūjitā | saguṇā saguṇārādhyā harīndradevapūjitā | 
raktapriyā ca raktākṣī rudhirāsavabhūṣitā | balipriyā baliratā durgā 
balavatī valā | balapriyā balaratā balarāmaprapūjitā | 
arddhakeśeśvarī keśā keśaveśavibhūṣitā | padmamālā ca 
padmākṣī kāmākhyā girinandinī | dakṣiṇā caiva dakṣā ca dakṣajā 
dakṣiṇe ratā | vajrapuṣpapriyā raktapriyā sukumabhūṣitā |

p. 1012) māheśvarī mahādevapriyā pañcavibhūṣitā | iḍā ca 
piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā prāṇarūpiṇī | gāndhārī pañcamī 
pañcānanādiparipūjitā | ityetat kathitaṃ devi ! rahasya 
paramādbhutam | śrutvā mokṣamavāpnoti tārādevyāḥ prasādataḥ | 
ya idaṃ paṭhati stotraṃ tārāstutirahasyakam | sarvasiddhiyuto bhūtvā 
viharet kṣitimaṇḍale | tasyaiva mantrasiddhiḥ syānmama siddhiranuttamā 
| bhavatyevaṃ mahāmāye ! satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | mande 
maṅgalavāre ca yaḥ paṭhenniśi saṃyutaḥ | tasyaiva mantrasiddhiḥ 
syādgāṇapatyaṃ labhetta saḥ | śraddhayāśraddhayā vāpi 
paṭhettārārahasyakam | so'cireṇaiva kālena jīvanmuktaḥ śivo bhavet | 
sahasrāvarttanāddevi ! puraścaryaphalaṃ labhet | evaṃ satatayuktā ye 
dhyāyantastvāmupāsate | te kṛtārthā maheśāni ! 
mṛtyusaṃsārabandhanāt || iti muṇḍamālātantre 
tāriṇīśatanāmastotram samāptam ||

atha tāriṇīstotrānantaraṃ mahānīlatantre || ghorarūpe ! 
mahāmāre ! sarvaśatruvaśaṅkari ! | bhaktebhyo varade ! devi ! trāhi 
māṃ śaraṇāgatam | surāsurārcite ! devi ! siddhagandharvasevite ! | 
jāḍyapāpahare ! devi ! trāhi māṃ śaraṇāgatam | 
jaṭājūṭasamāyukte ! lolajihvānukāriṇi ! drutabuddhikare ! devi ! trāhi 
māṃ śaraṇāgatam | saumyarūpe ! ghorarūpe ! caṇḍarūpe ! 
namo'stu te | sṛṣṭirūpe ! namastubhyaṃ trāhi māṃ śaraṇāgatam | 
aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ navamyāñcaikamānasaḥ | ṣaṇmāsaiḥ 
siddhimāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | buddhiṃ dehi yaśo dehi 
kavitvaṃ dehi dehi me | kubuddhiṃ hara me devi ! trāhi māṃ 
śaraṇāgatam | indrādidiviṣadvṛndavandite ! karuṇāmayi ! | tāre ! 
tārādhināthāsye ! trāhi māṃ śaraṇāgatam | mokṣārthī labhate 
mokṣaṃ dhanārthī dhanamāpnuyāt | vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ 
tarkavyākaraṇādikām | idaṃ stotraṃ paṭhedyastu satataṃ labhate

p. 1013) naraḥ | tasya śatruḥ kṣayaṃ yāti mahāprajñā ca jāyate | 
pīḍāyāṃ vāpi saṃgrāme japye dāne tathā bhaye | ya idaṃ paṭhati 
stotraṃ śubhaṃ tasya na saṃśayaḥ | stotreṇānena deveśi ! stutvā 
devīṃ sureśvarīm | sarvān kāmānavāpnoti sarvavidyānidhirbhavet | 
iti te kathitaṃ divyaṃ stotraṃ sārasvatapradam | asmāt parataraṃ nāsti 
stotraṃ tantre maheśvari ! || iti vṛhannīlatantre dvitīyapaṭale 
tāriṇīstotraṃ samāptam ||

	atha vīrahomaḥ || kaivalyatantre pañcamapaṭale || śṛṇu pārvati ! 
vakṣyāmi vāhyahomavidhiṃ priye ! | āmnāyeṣu ca sarvesu vyāsena 
kathitaṃ purā | saṃkṣepāt kathayiṣyāmi yena muktiṃ labhennaraḥ | 
bhūmitrikoṇamālikhya mūlamantreṇa sādhakaḥ | bhūrbhuvaḥ sveti 
mantreṇa vahniṃ saṃsthāpayettataḥ | tatra devīṃ samāvāhya 
parivārasamanvitām | pūjayedvividhairdravyairnirvikalpena cetasā | 
madyena prathamaṃ dadyādāhutiṃ mūlamuccaran | ekaṃ māṃsena 
matsyena mudrayā ca kuleśvari ! | 
rambhāmūlasamudbhūtairmārjanyā galitaiḥ saha | 
juhuyādājyayogena sakṛdvāratrayañca vā | ghṛtena havanañcaikaṃ 
raktapuṣpeṇa caiva hi | kuṇḍodbhavena deveśi ! vāradvayamudāhṛtam | 
golena ghṛtayuktena sakṛddhomo viśiṣyate | 
gopīcandanasindūrairdaśavāraṃ humet śucau | vīrapuṣpeṇa gavyena 
vilvapatreṇa sundari ! | sakṛddhomo bhavedvahnau tato gavyena caikakam | 
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato dadyāt piṇyākairlavaṇānvitaiḥ | tato mūlaṃ 
japenmantrī cāṣṭottaraśataṃ priye ! | japtvā stutvā namaskṛtya 
visṛjedvahnimaṇḍale | iti te kathitaṃ devi ! kaivalyasādhanaṃ priye ! | 
asmāt parataraṃ nāsti satyametadbravīmi te | idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ
