paśubhyo na prakāśayet | prakāśāt siddhihāniḥ syāt satyaṃ satyaṃ 
na saṃśayaḥ || iti vīrahomaḥ || 

p. 1014) iti sampūjya hutvā ca sāmayikaiḥ saha dravyapānādikaṃ 
kuryāt || devyai dravyadānaphalantu muṇḍamālātantre || pūjayitvā 
mahādevīṃ surāmāṃsakalādibhiḥ | annairnānāvidhairdevi ! 
paritoṣya sa tādṛśa iti | surādānaphalenaiva mahāyogeśvaro bhavet ||

surābhedastu tatraiva || surā tu dvividhā proktā sphāṭikī 
ḍākinī tathā | kāñjikīsphāṭikīdāne dhanavṛddhiranuttamā | 
ḍākinīdānamātreṇa sarvavaśyo bhaveddhruvam | kāñjikīmudrayā 
devi ! yo'rcayet parameśvarīm | mahāsiddhiśvaro bhūtvā vaset 
kalpaśatāni ca | annadāturmahādevi ! mahāsiddhiranuttamā ||

atha mahāśaṅkhaḥ || nityātantre dvitīyapaṭale ||

īśvara uvāca ||

mahāśaṅkhasya māhātmyaṃ kathayāmi tavānaghe ! | svayoniriva 
goptavyaṃ sarvadā paśusaṅkaṭe | bhagarūpā mahāmāyā 
śukratarpaṇatatparā | jagadākarṣiṇī vidyā jagadguhyā varānane ! || 
tatraiva gṛhyate sāraṃ jagatāṃ vījarūpakam | tadvījaṃ parameśāni ! 
pañcāśadvarṇarūpakam | garbhaṃ prāpya bhavedasthi 
śiśormajjāsamanvitam | tadasthitārddhamapyasti 
pañcāśadvarṇarūpakam | varṇamālāsu deveśi ! śataṃ sañjapya 
yat phalam | ekenoccāraṇenāsthimālāyāṃ tat prakīrttitam || iti 
mahāśaṅkhamālājapaphalam ||

tanmālākaraṇe varjanīyamāha tatraiva || svecchāmṛtaṃ striyaṃ 
vipraṃ tiryagyonigataṃ tathā | rogiṇaṃ yavanaṃ vṛddhaṃ 
dīkṣitañcāpi varjayet | grāhyamapi tatraiva || vajrāhataṃ sarpadaṣṭaṃ 
patitvā vā jale mṛtam | vyāghrādinihataṃ vāpi sammukhe vā raṇe 
mṛtam | caṇḍālādyāḥ pradhānāste adhamāḥ śūdrajātayaḥ || 
vṛhannīlatantre caturthapaṭale || yuddhe mṛtasya deveśi ! 
caṇḍālasyāsthikena ca | mastakasya viśeṣeṇa mahāśaṅkhaṃ

p. 1015) prakīrttitam || nityātantre || ata eva maheśāni ! asthimālāgale 
mama | prathamapaṭale || samāśritya mahāśaṅkhamālāṃ vīrapadaṃ 
vrajet ||

atha mahāśaṅkhamālākaraṇam || muṇḍamālātantre 
dvitīyapaṭale || narāṅgulyasthibhirmālā grathitā parvabhedataḥ | 
sarvasiddhipradā mokṣadāyinī varavarṇini ! | nāḍyā saṅgrathitā 
mālā kāryā raktena vāsasā | nāḍībhirgrathitā mālā 
mahāsiddhipradā priye ! || svatantratantre'pi || ānīya chinnaśīrṣantu 
narasya patitasya ca | talasīgomayāspṛṣṭaṃ tathā gaṅgodakena ca | 
yavanabrāhmaṇānyasya tat kāryaṃ sādhakairjanaiḥ | aspṛṣṭaṃ tacca 
jānīyāt śālagrāmaśilādibhiḥ ||

atha mahāśaṅkhamālāsaṃskārastatraiva || guruṃ tatra 
praṇamyādau saṃskūryājjapamālikām | kṛtanityakriyo mantrī 
mālāñca kramato yajet | oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ klīṃ phaṭ svāhā | iti 
mantreṇa saptadhābhimantrya caṣakādipātrādau nikṣipet | tata 
āvāhanyādimudrayā devīṃ tatrāvāhya saparivārāmupacāraiḥ 
sampūjya tāṃ vamiti dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya kavacenāvaguṇṭhya 
matsyamudrayācchādya devīsvarūpāṃ tāṃ mālāṃ dhyāyan 
vinduprakṣepaṃ kuryāt | tatra mantraḥ || oṃ hrīṃ svāhā | oṃ hūṃ 
svāhā | oṃ hraḥ svāhā | oṃ phaṭ svāhā | oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ svāhā | 
oṃ hrīṃ hūṃ svāhā | oṃ hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā | iti 
saptārghyavinduprakṣepaṇaṃ kṛtvā kastūrīṃ dhūpayitvā 
caṣakādipātrāduttolya mālāmabhimantrayet | tatra mantraḥ || oṃ 
hrīṃ śrīṃ mahāvajriṇi ! mahāghorarūpe ! 
karkaśamahāsthimaṇḍale ! praviśa sarvasiddhiṃ prayaccha oṃ hrīṃ 
śrīṃ hrīṃ svāhā | oṃ mahākapālini ! mahāghorarūpe ! svāhā | 
ityanenāmantrya dūrvākṣatairabhyarcya kāmavījenoddīpanaṃ kṛtvā 
p. 1016) upacārairabhyarcya mātṛkāmantreṇa sandīpya oṃkāreṇa 
veṣṭayet | tataḥ hūṃ hrīṃ hūṃ oṃ hūṃ śrīṃ hūṃ oṃ hūṃ 
hūṃ oṃ hūṃ phaṭ hūṃ oṃ svāhā | oṃ hūṃ mahāyogini ! 
tribhuvanatāriṇi ! mahāśaṅkhāsthimadhye nivāsaṃ kuru 
sarvasiddhiṃ dehi surāmāṃsopahārān gṛhṇa gṛhṇāpaya hrīṃ 
śrīṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā | ityanena yathoktadravyeṇa baliṃ dattvā 
saṃhāramudrayā devīṃ visṛjya nibhṛtasthāne mālāḥ sthāpayet | 
vinduprakṣepa ityatra vinduḥ surāvindurityarthaḥ | evaṃ śodhayitvā 
paścānmālāsaṃskāravidhānenāpi saṃskāraḥ karaṇīya iti 
tāntrikāḥ || tantre || akasmādīhitā mālā mahāśaṅkhākhyamālayā 
| tathā caivāsthighaṭitā mahāśaṅkhāḥ prakīrttitāḥ | atrāsthipadena 
guṇadeśādigrāhyamiti tāntrikāḥ | iti 
mahāśaṅkhamālāsaṃskāraḥ ||

kubjikātantre saptamapaṭale || aśokaṃ vilvavṛkṣañca 
śivāñcaiva sureśvari ! | dṛṣṭvā praṇāmaṃ kuryācca 
sarvakāmārthasiddhaye | ulūkaṃ śaṅkhacillañca gṛdhrañcaiva 
sureśvari ! | namaskuryānmahākālīṃ yuddhe ca jayamālabhet | 
catuṣpathaṃ śmaśānañca namaskuryādviśeṣataḥ | 
tantrajñañcāvadhūtañca namaskuryādvarānane ! | upāsakaṃ sadā 
dṛṣṭvā madhuraṃ bhāṣaṇañcaret ||

vṛhannīlatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || parvate nirjane caiva vijane 
śūnyamaṇḍape | catuṣpathe kalāmadhye yadi daivānmaheśvari ! | 
kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā manuṃ japtvā natvā gacchedyathāsukham | 
gṛdhraṃ vīkṣya mahākālīṃ namaskuryādatandritaḥ | kṣemaṅkarīṃ 
tathā vīkṣya jambūkīṃ yamadūtikām | kuraraṃ śyenabhūkākau 
kṛṣṇamārjārameva ca ||

eteṣāṃ praṇāmamantrastatraiva || kṛśodari ! mahācaṇḍe ! | 
muktakeśi ! balipriye ! | kulācāraprasannāsye ! namaste śaṅkarapriye ! 
| śmaśāne ca śavaṃ dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇamanuvrajan | praṇamyānena 
mantreṇa mantrī sukhamavāpnuyāt | ghoradaṃṣṭre ! karālāsye !
p. 1017) kiṭiśabdapraṇādini! | gurughoṣavarāsphāle ! namaste 
caṇḍanādini ! | raktavastraṃ raktapuṣpaṃ vilokya tripurāmbikām | 
vilokya daṇḍavadbhūmāvimaṃ mantraṃ paṭhennaraḥ | 
bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśe ! tripure ! bhayanāśini ! | 
bhāgyodayasamutpanne ! namaste varavarṇini ! | kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tathā 
puṣpaṃ rājānaṃ rājaputtrakam | hastyaśvarathaśastrāṇi kālavīraṃ 
tathā śivam | mahiṣaṃ kulanāthañca dṛṣṭvā mahiṣamardinīm | 
praṇamya jayadurgāṃ vā sa tu vighnerna lipyate | jayadevi ! jagaddhātri 
! pūrṇe ! ādye ! tridaivate ! | bhaktebhyo varade ! devi ! 
muṇḍamālāvibhūṣite ! | raktadhārāsamākīrṇe ! varade ! tvāṃ 
namāmyaham | sarvavighnahare ! devi ! namaste haravallabhe ! | eteṣāṃ 
darśanenaiva yadi naivaṃ prakurvate | śaktimantraṃ puraskṛtya tasya 
siddhirna jāyate | eteṣāṃ māraṇoccāṭahiṃsanaṃ vāgurādibhiḥ | 
kriyate yena pāpātmā sa me bhaktaḥ kathaṃ bhavet || iti vīrakṛtyam || 

atha vīrāṇāṃ naiṣkarmyasādhanārthaṃ rasāsvādo 
vāmakeśvaratantre trayaḥpañcāśatpaṭale ||

bhairava uvāca ||

kriyākāṇḍaprakathane tantraṃ bahutaraṃ bhavet | 
kiñcillakṣaṇameteṣāṃ saṅkṣepāt kathayāmi te | kāvyaśāstre 
navarasā yoge cāṣṭau rasāḥ smṛtāḥ | aṣṭādaśaprakārā hi 
vidyāyāṃ parikīrttitāḥ | bhaktiyoge navarasā ṛtavo viṣaye smṛtāḥ | 
pañcamādyā rasā devi ! pañcapañcāśataḥ smṛtāḥ | bhedaṃ 
rasānāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāvahitā priye ! 
śṛṅgāravīrakaruṇāhāsyādbhutabhayānakāḥ | vībhatsaḥ śāntako 
raudro navadhā kāvyaśāstrake | yamaśca niyamaścaiva āsanaṃ 
prāṇasaṃyamaḥ | pratyāhāro dhāraṇā ca dhyānaṃ 
samādhiraṣṭadhā | yoge śāstre mahādevi ! kathitā vistarāt priye ! | 
chandaḥ pādau ca vedasya mukhaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ smṛtam | śikṣā 
ghrāṇaṃ maheśāni ! hastau kalpo'tha kathyate | jyotiṣaṃ devi ! 
tannetraṃ niruktaṃ śrotramucyate | aṅgāni vedāścatvāro

p. 1018) mīmāṃsānyāyavistaraiḥ | dharmaśāstraṃ purāṇañca 
vidyā hyetāścaturdaśa | āyurvedo dhanurvedo gāndharvaśceti te trāḥ 
| arthaśāstrasamāyuktā vidyāṣṭādaśadhā rasāḥ | mananaṃ 
kīrttanaṃ dhyānaṃ smaraṇaṃ pādasevanam | arcanaṃ vandanaṃ 
dāsyaṃ sakhyamātmasamarpaṇam | itthaṃ devi ! navarasā bhaktiyoge 
prakīrttitāḥ | sraggandhavanitāśayyāvastrālaṅkaraṇāni ca | kathitāḥ 
parameśāni ! viṣaye ṛtavo rasāḥ | khādyāḥ pañcarasā gauḍī 
mādhvīkṣuphalaśasyakāḥ | eteṣu rasabhāvajñā ye te vai rasikāḥ 
smṛtāḥ | kṛtyavidhirjapavidhirdravyaśodhaniko vidhiḥ | 
vāhyamānasikīpūjāvidhiśca parameśvari ! | puraścaryāvidhirdevi ! 
karmakāṇḍāni pañcadhā | tridhā kṛtyavidhirdevi ! prātaḥ sāyaṃ 
dinaṃ tathā | anubhūya rasān sarvān kriyākāṇḍaṃ vidhāya ca | 
sādhakaḥ sthiracittena kuryāt pūrṇābhiṣecanam ||

catuṣpañcāśatpaṭale ||

devyuvāca ||

mantraprakathanaṃ nātha ! vedaprakathanaṃ katham | tattadrasānāṃ 
vacanaiścittaṃ mohayasīva me | sāmprataṃ saṃśayacchedakāraṇaṃ 
brūhi me prabho ! | āścaryākhyānamevaṃ hi śravaṇāt kautukaṃ 
mahat ||

śrībhairava uvāca ||

tantroktaṃ vedavākyañca sarvajñānasya kāraṇam | bhedaṃ 
rasānāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāvahitā mayi | śṛṅgāro vīrato 
jñeyastasmin te karuṇādayaḥ | śṛṅgārādirghṛṇādiśca api 
śṛṅgārato bhavet | śṛṅgārastu yadā devi ! paramārthakasūcakaḥ | 
tadaiva paramā śāntīrudratvaṃ samprapadyate | teṣāṃ 
prakaraṇārthantu śṛṇu prāṇādhike ! mayi | pādaṃ vinā śarīrasya 
na gatirvidyate priye ! | chandasā vedamārge tu praviśenna kadācana | 
alaṅkāraṃ vinā chando na śobheta priyaṃvade ! | cakṣuḥ 
parokṣabhedaḥ syājjyotiḥśāstraṃ tathaiva ca | candrasūryasya
p 1019) grahaṇājjyotiḥ pratyakṣatāmiyāt | vedaśākhā niruktaṃ 
syācchākhābhirbrahma jāyate | gānakārye śrūyamāṇe 
bhaktirbhavati brahmaṇi ! | tadaiva gānakṛtyañca mukhanāsikayorbhavet 
| gānaṃ vinā na nṛtyaṃ syāt nṛtyaṃ gānena jāyate | 
śikṣāśāstraṃ nāṭakādiśabdavyākaraṇaṃ smṛtam | 
avinābhāvasambandhād dvayoreva śucismite ! | vedāḥ 
ṣaḍaṅgasahitā brahmavyākhyānatatparāḥ | nyāyena sārddhaṃ 
mīmāṃsā brahma nirṇīyate dhruvam | brahmajñāne tu jīvasya 
paramātmavicāraṇam | dharmaśāstrānusāreṇa 
vyavasthādinirūpataḥ | purāṇākhyāni tenaiva jāyate satataṃ priye ! | 
brahmānandaparo jīva ātmasaṃrakṣaṇotsukaḥ | āyurvedaṃ 
dhanurvedaṃ gāndharvañca samabhyaset | atha sandhānato devi ! 
pūrṇajñānī ca sādhakaḥ | madhunekṣurasenaiva 
dugdhādiphalaśasyakaiḥ | gandhamālyādinā devi ! 
vastrālāṅkaraṇādinā | śayyāyāṃ vanitārūpaṃ 
pūjayejjagadambikām | vanitāpūjane devi ! śṛṅgārarasasādhanam | 
pūjanaṃ karmakāṇḍañca pañcadhā tat prakīrttitam | tatsarvaṃ 
syādhayedvīro devīsamprāptihetave | pūjane navadhā 
bhaktirasollāsaśca jāyate | tadā yogaṃ samabhyasya samādhistho 
bhavedyatiḥ | ata eva maheśāni ! puraiva kathitaṃ mayā | anubhūya 
svarān sarvān pañcapañcāśataḥ priye ! | viṣaye karmakāṇḍeṣu 
niṣkāmī bhavati priye ! | niṣkāme phalamāścaryaṃ tena tṛpyati 
devatā | dehī dehaṃ samāśritya na ca karma parityajet | divyāṃ 
kriyāṃ samāpyaivaṃ devīyātrādikarmaṇā | 
pūrṇajñānarasānandājjīvanmukto bhaved dhruvam ||

atha vīrāṇāṃ ṣaṭkarmasādhanam || yoginītantre pūrvakhaṇḍe 
caturthapaṭale ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

devadeva ! jagadvandya ! surāsuranamaskṛta ! | idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi 
vīraṣaṭkarmasādhanam | dhanyaṃ puṇyavatāṃ rājñāṃ 
bālyādikavrayojuṣām | striyāntu siddhisaṃsthānāṃ 
sarvabhogavilāsinām ||

p. 1020) ṣaṭkarmāsyāha tatraiva ||

īśvara uvāca ||

śāntivaśyastambhanāni vidveṣoccāṭane tathā | māraṇaṃ 
parameśāni ! saṭkarmedaṃ prakīrttitam | tāriṇīṃ kālikāṃ 
chinnāmadhikṛtya jaganmayi ! | kathayāmi tava snehād drutasiddhikaraṃ 
param | ratirvāṇī ramā jyeṣṭhā mātaṅgī kulakāminī | durgā caiva 
bhadrakālī karmādau karmasiddhaye | ṣoḍaśairupacāraiśca 
yajedvīraḥ svaśaktikaḥ ||

ṣaṭkarmaṇāṃ māraṇavyatiriktānāṃ māraṇasya 
sthānamāha tatraiva || śūnyāgāre mahāraṇye devatāyatane'pi vā | 
pañcakarma prakurvīta māraṇantu śavopari | tadabhāve pitṛbhūmau 
vāsāṃsi kathayāmi te | mukhyaṃ digambaraṃ jñeyaṃ dvīpicarma 
dvitīyakam | tadabhāve raktakṣaumaṃ nānyadvastraṃ prakalpayet || 
śāntyādiṣu krameṇaiva pūjāpātramāha || svarṇamādau dvitīye ca 
rājataṃ stambhane śilā | vidveṣoccāṭane tāmraṃ kapālaṃ māraṇe 
śubham | viprānyo'pi naraḥ prokto yuvā ca kṛṣṇavarṇakaḥ | 
adurbhikṣā vyādhimṛtā āsanasya śubhāvahāḥ | abhāve sphāṭikī 
japyā indrākṣairvā japet priye ! | mṛdau vā komale vāpi viṣṭare vā 
sureśvari ! | muṇḍe vā yonike devi ! tvace vyāghrasya vā priye ! | 
ekahaste dvihaste ca caturhaste samantataḥ | sthirāsanaścaret samyak na 
bhayaṃ tatra cintayet | bhaye jāte maheśāni ! bhairavoktamanuṃ smaret | 
viṣañca vajrajālena hanuyugmamataḥ param | sarvabhūtānṛtaḥ 
kūrcamantrānte bhairavo manuḥ | tato bhūtabaliṃ dadyāt sādhako 
dharmasammitam | tacca tailaṃ maheśāni ! udvarttya kālakañcaret | 
kujavārādiyogena pañcakarma samācaret | śanau ca māraṇaṃ devi ! 
niścitaṃ vīravandite ! | rātriyoge na karttavyaṃ sarvakarma śucismite ! | 

p. 1021) prāg vidvān prajapet samyak svamantraṃ saṃyutaṃ śive ! | 
prayogasya phalāvāptyai svasvarakṣākaraṃ mahat | tataḥ śodhanadine 
mantrī yāmamātragate niśi | mādibhiḥ pañcabhirdravyairyaṣṭvā 
kulavilāsinīm | digvāsā galitāśeṣacikuraḥ  kulakaulike ! | śaktiyukto 
japedvidyāṃ sadā tāṃ manasā smaran | hakṣasaṅkhyāṃ maheśāni 
! | śaktipūjāpuraḥsaram | pratyahaṃ bhojayedvidvān kaulikānyān 
dināntime | māṃsaṃ madyaṃ tathā matsyaṃ humed dvau dvau śataṃ 
śatam | dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyād gururūpeṇa śāmbhavi ! | 
evamuktavidhānena divyo vā vīrapuṅgavaḥ | yadi kuryānmaheśāni ! 
devānapi tathā nayet | nāpekṣā jāyate kānte ! avaśyaṃ phalabhāg 
bhavet | mahāprayoge deveśi ! kṛṣṇacchāgaṃ baliṃ haret | pūjānte 
satataṃ devi ! daśāṃśairvahnimarcayet | vidhiḥ sarvatra kathito 
divyavīrapaśukramāt | krameṇa phalamāpnoti vyatyaye pātakī bhavet | 
iti te kathitaṃ tubhyaṃ samyak ṣaṭkarmagocaram | gopanīyaṃ khale 
dṛṣṭe paśupāmarasannidhau ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

surā vā kiṃvidhā deva ! śaktirvā kīdṛśī śubhā | ṣaṭkarmasu 
yathāyogyaṃ vada me karuṇānidhe ! ||

īśvara uvāca ||

mādhvī śāntikarī proktā vaśye ca sphāṭikī śubhā | stambhane 
ḍākinī jñeyā vidveṣe paiṣṭikī matā | uccāṭane tathā gauḍī 
māraṇe bhairavī matā | etāsāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ devi ! kathitaṃ kulamohane 
! || śaktibhedamāha tatraiva || padminī śāntidā proktā vaśye ca 
śaṅkhinī matā | stambhanoccāṭane devi ! praśastā nāgavallabhā | 
māraṇe ca tathā śastā ḍākinī śatrumṛtyudā || 
padminyādilakṣaṇamapi tatraiva || gaurāṅgī dīrghakeśī yā sadā

p. 1022) cāmṛtabhāṣiṇī | raktanetrā suśīlā ca padminī sādhane 
śubhā | mantrasiddhikarī hyeṣā śaṅkhinī sāpi bhāvinī | 
dīrghāṅgī sā śaṅkhinī syājjagadrañjanakāriṇī | śyāmāṅgī 
śubhadehā ca na kharvā nātidīrghikā | dīrghakeśī īṣatpuṣṭā 
mṛdubhāṣā ca nāginī | kṛṣṇāṅgī ca kṛśāṅgī ca danturā 
madatāpitā | hrasvakeśī dīrghaghrāṇā sadā niṣṭhuravādinī | sadā 
kruddhā dīrghadehā mahārāva parāyaṇā | nirlajjā hāsyahīnā ca 
nidrālurbahubhakṣikā | iyaṃ sā ḍākinī proktā mṛtyuyoge praśasyate 
| etāstu śaktayo devi ! sarvajātisamudbhavāḥ | sāpatyāśca 
yuvatyaśca jātaputrādikāḥ śubhāḥ | gṛhyā kularasaiḥ pūjyā 
bhaktibhāvena kāminī | 

śrīdevyuvāca ||

kena kena ca mantreṇa mantrī ṣaṭkarmabhāg bhavet | tattanmantre 
maheśāni ! yadyahaṃ tava vallabhā |

śrī-īśvara uvāca ||

ekākṣaraṃ kālikāyāstārāyāstu trivījakam | vajravairocanīyāyā 
manurekādaśākṣaraḥ | sarvatejo'pahārī ca manurākhyāta eva ca | 
bahunātra kimuktena śṛṇu matprāṇavallabhe ! | kevalaṃ śaktivījena 
japeddevi ! samāhitaḥ | avaśyaṃ phalamāpnoti nānyathā vīravandite ! 
| khalo yadi phalaprāptaḥ saralo yadi niṣphalaḥ | bhavedatra maheśāni ! 
tadā sarvaṃ vṛthā bhavet | ahaṃ dhātā tathā pātā rakṣāsu 
drohaṇaṃ śive ! | tathāpi phalasiddhiḥ syāccitrametannagātmaje ! | 
evantu māraṇaṃ devi ! viśeṣāt kathayāmi te | sāntaṃ 
vahnisamāyuktaṃ vāmanetravibhūṣitam | kūrcayugmaṃ tato devi ! 
amukaṃ māraya dvayam | caturdaśākṣaro mantro svāhāntaḥ 
śatrunāśakaḥ | khadirāṅgāramādāya kujāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣataḥ | 
lekhayet puttalīṃ śatrusvarūpāṃ lauhapatrake | niśāyāṃ mastake 
netre lalāṭe hṛdaye kare | nābhau guhye kaṭau pṛṣṭhe kramoktena 
padadvaye | mantravarṇān samālikhya pratiṣṭhāṃ tatra kārayet |

p. 1023) saṃharamudrāṃ vaddhvā tu dhyāyeddevīṃ jayapradām | 
dīrghākārāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ sadorddhvatanamastakām | 
nṛmuṇḍayugalaṃ haste carvayantīṃ digambarīṃ | śatrunāśakarīṃ 
devīṃ dhyāyet śatrukṣayāya ca | evaṃ 
dhyātveṣṭikācūrṇairvāmahastena śaṅkari ! | oṃ śatrunāśakaryai 
nama iti dattvā maheśvari ! | haridrācūrṇasahitāṃ dhārāṃ 
dadyādanena tu | amukasya śoṇitañca piba pibeti tatparam | māṃsaṃ 
khādaya khādaya hrīṃ nama iti mantram | madhyāhne madhyarātrau tu 
pūjayitvā śatāṣṭakam | japedekādaśāhe ca rogaḥ syānnātra 
saṃśayaḥ | daṇḍādhikaikaviṃśāhe mṛtyureva riporbhavet 
athavānyaprakāreṇa śatrukṣayamahaṃ vade | puṃgośakṛt samādāya 
pūjayeduṣṇavāriṇā | viparītakrameṇaiva japapūjādikañcaret | 
mahādevāya nama iti puṃgośakṛt samāharet | śivāya nama iti 
mantreṇa ghaṭanañca samācaret | paśupataye nama iti prāṇān 
saṃsthāpayettataḥ | lauhapātre maheśāni ! khadirāṅgārayogataḥ | 
śatrupratikṛtiṃ likhya tatra saṃsthāpayecchivam | tato 
dhyāyenmahārudraṃ dhyānantu susamāhitaḥ | śatrorvakṣaḥsthitaṃ 
rudraṃ jvaladagnisamaprabham | vāmahastadharaṃ keśaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ 
prāṇakarṣaṇam | naracarmāmbaraṃ devaṃ mahāvyālādiveṣṭitam | 
pinākadhṛk ihāgacchāgacchetyādināvāhya yatnataḥ | śūlapāṇaye 
nama iti snāpayet sādhakottamaḥ | maheśvarāya nama iti pādyādinā 
prapūjayet | īśānādīstathāmūrtīrvyutkrameṇa prapūjayet | 
agnikoṇādiparyantaṃ pūrvarītyā maheśvari ! | namaḥ śivāyeti 
mūlamaṣṭāviṃśati sa~japet | huṃ kṣamasveti vāmena kareṇa tu 
visarjayet | ajita ! kaiśava ! viṣṇo ! hare ! satya ! janārdana ! | haṃsa ! 
nārāyaṇa ! svāhā mantrameva sakṛjjapet | huṃ namobhagavate 
vāsudevāya svāhā nama ityapi sakṛjjapet | evamekādaśāhena 
śatrucchādanamañjasā | avaśyaṃ jāyate devi ! satyaṃ satyaṃ 
trilocane ! || iti śatrucchādanam || 

p. 2024)  atha stambhanam || kathayāmi mahādevi ! 
vairastambhanamuttamam | kumbhakārasya payanādānayet piṭharaṃ 
punaḥ | ekaḥ svayaṃ pradik sphuṭaṃ yat kṛtaṃ sādhakottamaḥ | ānīya 
ca ḍakhāmadhyādbhasma paryuṣitaṃ tathā | nikṣipya piṭhare 
śuṣkanālikāpatravistaram | bhasmopari ca saṃsthāpya vaṭikāyāḥ 
sureśvari ! | aiśānyāṃ vivaraṃ kṛtvā śanervāre maheśvari ! | 
pūrṇamadhyāhnakāle ca nirjane sati bhāvini ! | caturdikṣu vāṭikāyā 
garttasya nikaṭāt priye ! | tatrāvati mahādevī caurebhyo rakṣayāmi ca | 
praskandamanasā devi ! bhūmau parikramaṃ caret | tāvatīñca tato 
bhūmiṃ vāmāvarttena bhāvini ! parikramya punastatra garttasya 
nikaṭaṃ vrajet | tatraiva nirjane gartta śalākāṃ lauhanirmitām | 
ropayitvā tadupari piṭharaṃ saśarāvakam | saṃsthāpya mṛdbhiḥ 
sampūrya tadgarttaṃ gṛhamāvrajet | gatistambho bhaveddevi ! 
caurādīnāṃ tathā khalu | tvayā yogavaro deva ! durlabho vasudhātale | 
piśācabhūtavetālakuṣmāṇḍabrahmarākṣasāḥ | dānavānāṃ 
tathānyeṣāṃ gatistatra na jāyate | dhanaputrasamṛddhistu 
varddhate'harniśaṃ tathā | dine dine dharmabuddhirjāyate nātra 
saṃśayaḥ ||

vṛhannīlatantre || athānyat sampravakṣyāmi 
vaśīkaraṇamuttamam | yena vijñānamātreṇa mantrāḥ sidhyanti 
tatkṣaṇāt | pratimāṃ kārayeddevi ! palena rajatasya ca | palārddhena 
maheśāni ! sādhyasya pratimāṃ śive ! | haritālaṃ palārddhañca 
haridrācūrṇakaṃ tathā | garttaṃ kṛtvā sārddhahastaṃ tatra nikṣipya 
sundari ! | raktāsanaṃ tatra dattvā viśettadgatamānasaḥ | caturdikṣu 
maheśāni ! patākā viniveśayet | raktāsane copaviśya pūrvāsyo 
japamārabhet | pūjāyā niyamaṃ devi ! jānīhi naganandini ! | 
tilapūrṇaghaṭaṃ tatra sthāpayettatra deśikaḥ |

p. 1025) prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantreṇa prāṇān saṃsthāpayedbudhaḥ | 
adhaḥ kṛtvā pūjayitvā prabālamālayā japet | daśasāhasrajāpena 
prayogārho bhavettataḥ | praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya māyāvījaṃ 
dvitīyakam | kā tvaṃ tvallākinīyuktaṃ vāmakarṇendubhūṣitam | tato 
raktapadaṃ brūyāccāmuṇḍe tadanantaram | sādhyanāma tato nyasya 
vaśamānaya tatparam | vahnijāyāvadhirmantro japeddaśasahasrakam | 
daśāṃśādipramāṇena homādīṃśca samācaret | prātaḥ snātvā 
haviṣyāśī śucirbhūtvā jitendriyaḥ | prātaḥkālaṃ samārabhya 
japenmadhyandināvadhi | jape samāpte deveśi ! humeddine dine śubhe ! | 
jātipuṣpasya homena vaśayennātra saṃśayaḥ | 
karpūramiśritaistoyaistarpayet paradevatām | pūrvaṃ 
praṇavamuddhṛtya cāmuṇḍāṃ pravadettataḥ | 
tarpayāmyagnijāyāntaṃ mantraṃ jānīhi sundari ! | anenaiva 
vidhānena santarpya paradevatām | siddhiprayogāddeveśi ! jāyate nātra 
saṃśayaḥ || abhiṣekaṃ tataḥ kuryādbhairavi ! prāṇavallabhe ! | 
praṇavañca maheśāni ! cāmuṇḍāṃ tadanantaram | abhiṣiñcāmi 
tatpaścāt hṛdantenābhiṣiñcayet | taddaśāṃśena deveśi ! 
brāhmaṇān bhojayettadā | evaṃ kṛte maheśāni ! 
vaśīkaraṇamuttamam | jāyate nātra sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | kāmatulyaśca nārīṇāṃ ripūṇāṃ śamanopamaḥ | 
yāvajīvitaparyantaṃ smaravāṇa iveśvari ! | jāyate nātra sandehaḥ 
satyaṃ suragaṇārcite ! | śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ peṣayedrocanāyutam | 
śatenāmantritaṃ kṛtvā tilakaṃ kārayettataḥ | vaśayennātra sandehaḥ 
satyaṃ satyaṃ maheśvari ! | candrasūryāvapi vṛthā tadā 
niṣphalabhāgbhavet | raktavastreṇa cāmuṇḍāṃ toṣayedbahuyatnataḥ | 
suvarṇaṃ dakṣiṇā deyā vittānusārataḥ priye ! | ādyante mahatīṃ 
pūjāṃ kuryāttatra varānane ! | pañcadinaprayogeṇa rājānaṃ
vaśamānayet | tava prītyai mahādevi ! kathitaṃ bhuvi durlabham || 

p. 1026) atha vidveṣaṇam || tatraiva || vidveṣaṇaṃ maheśāni ! 
śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ priye ! | karavīrarasenaiva tathā dhustūrakeṇa vā | 
kākolūkau samālikhyau bhūrjapatre maheśvari ! | sādhyānāṃ 
nāmasahitaṃ mantraṃ saṃlekhya sādhakaḥ | etayoryādṛśaṃ vairaṃ 
tādṛśañcāmukayorbhavet | vahnijāyāvadhirmantraḥ 
sarvavidveṣakārakaḥ | mahākālīdevatāyā pūjā tasyāḥ 
śubhapradā | pañcasahasramānena japaṃ kuryāt śucismite ! |

atha māraṇam || tatraiva || māraṇañca viśeṣeṇa 
śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ priye ! | nihataṃ kṛṣṇamārjāraṃ mastakaṃ tatra 
taṃ nayet | sindūreṇa samāyuktaṃ kuryāt sādhakasattamaḥ | 
tajjihvāyāṃ mahādevi ! sādhyanāma likhet priye ! | sādhyanāma 
likhitvā tu kālīnāma japenmudā | nijavījena ghaṭitaṃ tryakṣaraṃ 
prajapettataḥ | daśasāhasrajāpena mārayedarimagrataḥ | 
śmaśānakālikāṃ tatra cāvāhya pūjayettataḥ | 
lauhālaṅkārasaṃyuktaṃ vastraṃ devyai nivedayet | dakṣiṇāsyo 
japenmantraṃ tataḥ siddho bhavenmanuḥ � annaṃ pakvā mahādevi ! 
nirjane dāpayet priye ! | arddharātrau baliṃ dattvā khanedvairigṛhe yadi | 
saptāhamadhye deveśi ! śatruryāti yamālayam | iti te kathitaṃ devi ! 
sārātsārataraṃ matam | tava snehādvarārohe ! prakāśitamidaṃ 
punaḥ ||

kāmākhyātantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || pūjayitvā mahādevīṃ 
pañcatattvena śāmbhavi ! | mahānandamayo bhūtvā sādhayet 
sādhanaṃ mahat | svakaṃ mūtraṃ maheśāni ! kūrcavījena śodhayet 
| tarpayedbhairavīṃ ghorāṃ śatrunāmnā pibet svayam | daśadikṣu 
mahāpīṭhe prakṣipedanale'pi ca | nagno bhūtvā bhramettatra 
śatrunāśo bhaveddhruvam | śukraśoṇitamūtreṣu vīro ghṛṇī bhaved 

p. 1027) yadi | bhairavī kupitā tasya satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyaham || 
yathā || sa mṛtpātraṃ samādāya sādhyanāmālikhecchive ! | vāyunā 
puṭitaṃ kṛtvā svamūtraṃ tatra nikṣipet | māyāvījaṃ maheśāni ! 
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japet | bhairavyai tarpayeddevi ! māraṇoccāṭanaṃ 
bhavet | svamūtrañca samādāya vāmahastena pārvati ! | śodhitaṃ 
bhairavīpātre nikṣipet sādhakottamaḥ | unmādo jāyate śatrurmriyate 
vā maheśvari ! | mohitaḥ kṣobhito vāpi vaśyo vāpi bhaved dhruvam ||

eteṣāṃ śāntistu yoginītantre || śāntikaṃ śṛṇu sarvajñe ! 
yena jīveta sādhakḥ | urvaśīṃ prathamaṃ nītvā yugāntakārakaṃ 
param | stanadvayena saṃyuktaṃ dhūminītritayaṃ tataḥ | asya mantrasya 
deveśi ! sahasrāṣṭapramāṇataḥ | japaṃ kuryānmaheśāni ! 
annapūrṇā ca devatā | 
pūjayedvividhairbhakṣyairnānārasasamanvitaiḥ | carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ 
tathā lehyaṃ peyaṃ dadyānmanoharam | daśāṃśādipramāṇena 
homādīṃśca samācaret | padmapuṣpasya homena śāntirbhavati 
sundari ! | madhunā tarpayeddevīṃ cānnapūrṇāṃ parāṃ gatim | 
rathame'hani deveśi ! jalena pūrayedghaṭam | tadghaṭe pūjayeddevīṃ 
tvāvāhya surasundarīm | mantreṇānena deveśi ! yadi kuryāt 
prayogakam | sarvavyādhirvinaśyeta nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ saptame nirguṇakāṇḍe 
vīrācārapūjādiṣaṭkarmakathanaṃ nāma tṛtīyaḥ paricchedaḥ || 



atha kulācāraḥ || nityātantre tṛtīyapaṭale || kulācāravidhiṃ 
vakṣye sāvadhānāvadhāraya | yasya vijñānamātreṇa karttā harttā 
sadāśivaḥ | dikkālaniyamo nāsti tithyādiniyamo na ca | niyamo nāsti 
deveśi ! mahāmantrasya sādhane || kaulalakṣaṇantu tatraiva || kvacit 
śiṣṭaḥ kvacid bhraṣṭaḥ kvacid bhūtapiśācavat |

p. 1028) nānāveśadharāḥ kaulā vicaranti mahītale | kardame 
candane'bhedaḥ puttre śatrau tathā priye ! | śmaśāne bhavane devi ! 
tathaiva kāñcane tṛṇe | na bhedo yasya deveśi sa kaulaḥ parikīrttitaḥ | 
mathitvā jñānadaṇḍena vedāgamamahodadhim | sārametanmahādevi 
! kaulācāraṃ prakalpitam || samayācāratantre || ārdraśuṣkavibhāgena 
dvidhācāraṃ punaḥ śṛṇu | ārdrācārastu vijñeyo makāraiḥ 
pañcabhiryutaḥ | makārapañcarahitaḥ śuṣkācāraḥ prakīrttitaḥ | 
kalau viśeṣato devi ! ārdrācāraḥ phalapradaḥ || guptasādhanatantre 
prathamapaṭale || kulācāraṃ mahājñānaṃ goptavyaṃ paśusaṅkaṭe 
| pragoptavyaṃ mahādevi ! svayoniriva pārvati ! | vedāgamapurāṇāni 
yāni śāstrāṇi pārvati ! | tanmadhye sārabhūtaṃ hi kulācāraṃ 
sudurlabhaṃ | vaktrakoṭisahasraistu jihvākoṭiśatairapi | kulācārasya 
māhātmyaṃ varṇituṃ naiva śakyate | kiñcittathāpi cāpalyāt 
kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva me | śaktimūlaṃ jagat sarvaṃ śaktimūlaṃ 
paraṃ tapaḥ | śaktimāśritya nivased yatra kutrāśrame vasan | 
sādhakasyocitāṃ śaktiṃ sādhakājñānuvarttinīm | iha loke sukhī 
bhūtvā devīdehe pralīyate | sādhakendro mahāsiddhiṃ labdhvā yāti 
hareḥ padam ||

muṇḍamālātantre pañcamapaṭale || kuladharmaṃ samāśritya 
ye vasanti mahītale | te śivāste śivā jīvā bhavanti kuladharmataḥ | 
kulinaḥ śaṅkaro jñeyaḥ kulīnastu hariḥ svayam | kulīno vāsavo devaḥ 
kulīnastu pitāmahaḥ | kulīnā munayaḥ sarve kulīnāḥ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ 
|| tathā || murkho vā paṇḍito vāpi brāhmaṇo vā varānane ! | kṣatriyo 
vaiśyajaḥ śūdraścaṇḍālo varavarṇini ! | sarve tulyāḥ kulīnāśca 
etat sarvārthasādhanam | śṛṇu devi ! varārohe ! mama vākyaṃ 
śucismite ! | śṛṅgārarasalāvaṇyakovidaḥ sarvakarmasu |

p. 1029) śṛṅgārājjāyate sṛṣṭiḥ śṛṅgārājjāyate ratiḥ | 
śṛṅgārācchaṅkarastuṣṭaḥ śṛṅgārādapi pārvatī | 
prahāraśabdaṃ lalitaṃ karkaśaṃ vā sureśvari ! | 
śṛṅgāraśabdamātreṇa janā yāsyanti sadgatim | striyo devāḥ striyaḥ 
puṇyāḥ striya eva vibhūṣaṇam | strīdveṣo naiva karttavyastāsu 
nindāprahārakam | varjayedeva deveśi ! ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitaḥ | 
strīrūpaṃ tāriṇīrūpaṃ yo vetti dharaṇītale | sa śrīpatiśca 
vijñeyaḥ sa jñeyaḥ pārvatīpatiḥ || tathā || kulamārgarato jīvaḥ śiva 
eva na saṃśayaḥ | kuladharmārthago janturviharet  kulamārgake | 
kulapuṣpaṃ samāśritya kulīnāśramamāśrayet || ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || 
kulavāre kulīnantu kuladharmaṃ kulavratam | āśrayet paramānandaṃ 
paramānandameva ca || kulīnānāṃ bhaktirnāstītyādivacanenāpi 
doṣamāha tatraiva || na kulīne parā buddhirna kulīne parā gatiḥ | na 
kulīne parā bhaktirna kulīne parā kriyā | evaṃ vadati yo jantuḥ sa 
muktiṃ naiva yāti vai | ihaiva svargo deveśi ! iha kailāsamandiram | 
ihaiva bhaktirbhuktiśca muktiravyabhicāriṇī | bhogaḥ svargaśca 
mokṣaśca karasthaścaiva śaṅkari ! | śāktānāṃ tripureśāni ! 
satyaṃ vacmi na saṃśayaḥ ||

nirvāṇatantre ekādaśapaṭale || yasmin deśe ya ācāro nirdiṣṭo 
mantrasādhane | tadācāraviśiṣṭo yaḥ kaulikaḥ sa hi kīrttitaḥ | 
mahānirvāṇatantre caturthollāse || kulācāreṇa pūtātmā 
sākṣācchivamayo bhavet | yatrāsti bhogabāhulyaṃ tatra yogasya kā 
kathā | yogo'pi bhogavirahaḥ kaulastūbhayamaśnute ||

atha kaulārcanaphalaṃ tatraiva || ekaścet kulatattvajñaḥ pūjito 
yena suvrate ! | sarve devāśca devyaśca pūjitā nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
pṛthivīṃ hemasampūrṇāṃ dattvā yat phalamāpnuyāt | tasmāt koṭi 

p. 1030) guṇaṃ puṇyaṃ labhate kaulikārcanāt | śvapaco'pi 
kulajñānī brāhmaṇādatiricyate | kulācāravihīnastu brāhmaṇaḥ 
śvapacādhamaḥ | kauladharmāt paro dharmo nāsti jñāne tu māmake | 
yasyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa brahmajñānī naro bhavet | ayantu paramo 
mārgo gupto'sti paśusaṅkaṭe | vyaktībhaviṣyati cirāt saṃvṛtte prabale 
kalau | kalikāle pravṛtte hi satyaṃ satyaṃ mayocyate | na sthāsyanti vinā 
kaulān paśavo mānavā bhuvi ||

atha kulācārapraśaṃsā || kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe 
dvitīyollāse || mathitvā jñānadaṇḍena vedāgamamahārṇavam | 
sārajñena mayā devi ! kuladharmaḥ samuddhṛtaḥ | ekataḥ sakalā 
dharmā yajñatīrthavratādayaḥ | ekataḥ kuladharmaśca tatra 
kaulo'dhikaḥ priye ! | praviśanti yathā nadyaḥ samudramṛjuvakragāḥ | 
tathaiva samayāḥ sarve praviṣṭāḥ kulameva hi | yathā hastipade līnaṃ 
sarvaprāṇipadaṃ bhavet | tathaiva samayāḥ sarve praviṣṭāḥ kulameva 
hi || tathā merusarṣapayoryadvat sūryakhadyotayoryathā | 
tathānyasamayasyāpi kaulasya mahadantaram | darśaneṣu ca sarveṣu 
vinābhyāsena mānavāḥ | mokṣaṃ labhante kaule tu sadya eva na 
saṃśayaḥ || tathā || yogī cennnaiva bhogī syād bhogī cennaiva yogavit | 
bhogayogātmakaṃ kaulamataḥ sarvātmakaṃ priye ! | bhogo yogāyate 
sākṣāt pātakaṃ sukṛtāyate | mokṣāyate ca saṃsāraḥ kuladharme 
kuleśvari ! | brahmendrācyutarudrādidevatā munipuṅgavāḥ | 
kuladharmaparā devi ! manuṣyeṣu ca kā kathā | vihāya 
sarvadharmāṃśca nānāgurumatāya ca | kulameva 
vijānīyādyadīcchecchriyamātmanaḥ | pūrvajanmakṛtābhyāsāt 
kulajñānaṃ prakāśate | suptotthitapratyayavadupadeśādikaṃ vinā ||

divā kulācāraniṣedhastu || vāmakeśvaratantre
p. 1031) ekapañcāśatpaṭale || na divā ca kulācāraḥ pūrṇajñānena 
cānyathā | ācāro dvividho devi ! vāmadakṣiṇabhedataḥ | 
janmamātraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hi abhiṣekena vāmakam | niruttaratantre 
prathamapaṭale || rātrau kulakriyāṃ kuryāddivā kuryācca vaidikīm | 
divā rātrau yajeddevīṃ yogī yogaprabhedataḥ || vāmakeśvaratantre || 
gurorārādhanāddevi ! mantrasādhanataḥ priye ! | 
caturṇāmāśramāṇāṃ hi cāvadhūtāśramo mahān | 
avadhūtāśramaṃ gacchet sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ | 
kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryavarjitaḥ | avadhūtaḥ śivaḥ 
sākṣānmama prāṇasamaḥ priye ! | avadhūtaśca dvividho gṛhasthaśca 
digambaraḥ | sacelaśca sadāraśca bhāvakaḥ sādhakaḥ śuciḥ | 
gurubhaktirato jñānī vāhyābhyantaradharmakṛt | 
aṣṭāṅgābhyāsanirataḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaḥ | niṣkāmī 
jñānaśuddhātmā śivārccanaparāyaṇaḥ | gṛhāvadhūto deveśi ! 
dvitīyaśca sadāśivaḥ | digambarāvadhūto hi pañcācāraparāyaṇaḥ 
| sadānandaḥ sadāśānto digvāsāḥ sarvadāyakaḥ | sarvabhogī 
sarvajātirdharmakarmarataḥ sadā | na kalau 
śodhayenmadyamagamyāgamanaṃ na hi | gṛhāvadhūtaiḥ kāryantu 
karttavyañca digambaraiḥ | saṃvidāsavayormadhye saṃvidaiva garīyasī 
| saṃvidāpānamātreṇa sa vīraḥ sa ca sādhakaḥ | evaṃvidho 
vidhānajñaḥ kulīno bhavati priye ! | tadaiva divyabhāvaśca jāyate 
nātra saṃśayaḥ | ye śāktā brāhmaṇā devi ! kṣatriyā brāhmaṇāḥ 
smṛtāḥ | vaiśyāśca brāhmaṇā devi ! sarve śūdrā dvijottamāḥ | 
antaḥśāktā vahiḥśaivāḥ sabhāyāṃ vaiṣṇavā matāḥ | 
nānārūpadharāḥ kaulā vicaranti mahītale | abhiṣikto gṛhasthaśca 
sadāvadhūtakaśca saḥ | karttā pātā ca saṃharttā śivatulyo na 
saṃśayaḥ | vāhyapūjā prakarttavyā guruvākyānusārataḥ | 
antaryāgātmikā pūjā sarvapūjottamottamā | vahiḥpūjā

p. 1032) vidhātavyā yāvajjñānaṃ na jāyate | jñāne jāte ca deveśi ! 
devatāmūrttibhāvanā | athavā devatāmūrttimālambya sādhayet 
sudhīḥ ||

kāmākhyātantre pañcamapaṭale || sa brāhmaṇo vaiṣṇavaḥ sa 
śākto gāṇapataśca saḥ | sa śaivaḥ paramārthajñaḥ sa eva 
pūrṇadīkṣitaḥ | pāvanānīha tīrthāṇi sarveṣāmiti sammatam | 
tīrthāṇāṃ pāvanaḥ kaulo girije ! bahu kiṃ vacaḥ | tasyaiva jananī 
dhanyā dhanyā hi janakādayaḥ | tasya jñātikuṭumbāśca dhanyā 
ālāpino janāḥ | nandanti pitaraḥ sarve gāthāṃ gāyanti te mudā | api 
naḥ svakule kaścit kulajñānī bhaviṣyati ||

athāntaryāgaḥ || muṇḍamālātantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || dhyāyettu 
pūjayeddevaṃ manasā vācasā hṛdā | tadeva sādhako loke 
cāntaryāgaparāyaṇaḥ | antaryāgaṃ mahāmāye ! 
sādhakānāmagamyakam | brahmāṇḍaṃ vai śarīrantu sarveṣāṃ 
prāṇadhāriṇām | brahmaṇḍe ye guṇāḥ santi te tiṣṭhanti kalevare | 
śarīraṃ tattvaghaṭitaṃ nānārasapariplutam | candravimbasamāyuktaṃ 
nādavinduvibhūṣitam | śarīraṃ śaṅkarasyāpi durlabhaṃ 
muktidāyakam | yāvanmuktirmahāmāye ! tāvadeva hi sādhakaḥ ||

nityātantre caturthapaṭale || athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu 
sarvāṅgasundari ! | na punarjananīgarbhaṃ jajjñātvā yāti sādhakaḥ | 
ādhāre kuṇḍalīṃ devīṃ cintayedbhujagākṛtim | pūrakeṇa 
maheśāni ! tvayā cakrāṇi ṣaṭ tataḥ | sahasrānandasandohamandiraṃ 
prāpayet sudhīḥ | yojayitvā pūrakeṇa sādhakaḥ paramātmani ! | 
śivaśaktisamāyogāt sukhī bhūyānnirantaram | vikāśitaṃ 
bhaveddehaṃ sakalaṃ tatprabhāvataḥ | anāhatādvahiḥ padmaṃ 
ṣoḍaśāraṃ vicintayet | tanmadhye cintayenmantrī 
sudhāsāgaramuttamam | śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ balayākāramujjvalam |

p. 1033) tanmadhye parameśāni ! maṇidvīpaṃ manoharam | 
pārijātādiracitairudyānaiḥ pariśobhitam | kalpavṛkṣaṃ mahādevi ! 
madhyasthāne vicintayet | caturvedānvitaiḥ 
śākhaiścaturbhirupaśobhitam | cintāmaṇigṛhaṃ tasya mūle devi ! 
vicintayet | suvarṇaracitaṃ nānāratnasañcayarañjitam | 
prabālamekhalāyuktaṃ ghaṇṭācāmararājitam | tanmadhye 
parameśāni ! cintayenmaṇivedikām | tasyopari mahādevi ! 
maṇipīṭhaṃ manoharam | svakalpatantramuktaṃ tadyonirūpaṃ 
vicintayet | bhrūmadhye parameśāni ! yaccāndraṃ pātramuttamam | 
tatrasthamamṛtaṃ devi ! tatra yonau vinikṣipet | tenaiva vidyudākāraṃ 
yonimadhye vicintayet | ākāśājjāyate vāyurvāyorutpadyate raviḥ | 
raverutpadyate nīraṃ nīrādutpadyate mahī | anenaiva vidhānena 
pañcabhūtātmakaṃ bhavet | sarvendriyasamāyuktaṃ 
sarvāyudhasamanvitam | sarvālaṅkāraracitaṃ devīdehaṃ vicintayet | 
snātvātha puṣkare tīrthe pūjayet paradevatām | pādyapātratrayaṃ 
bhadre ! arghyapātratrayaṃ tathā | tathā camanapātrañca trayaṃ 
kuryād varānane ! | snānapātrāṣṭakaṃ devi ! niyojyātha dale dale | 
pādyaṃ caraṇayordattvā kuṇḍalinyamṛtāmṛtaiḥ | pātrābhyāṃ 
parameśāni ! pādodakamathāharet | manasārghyaṃ tridhā kṛtvā 
guṇatrayavibhedataḥ | suvarṇadolayā devīmānayet kulabhairavaḥ | 
snāpayenmadhugandhādyaiḥ suvarṇakalasāṣṭakaiḥ | cīnayā 
gātramārjanyā gātranīraṃ samuddharet | paṭṭavastraṃ tato devyai 
nivedya paridhāpayet | kañcukañca nivedyātha śaṅkhaṃ 
bāhucatuṣṭaye | nivedayenmahādevyai gajadantavinirmitam | 
nānālaṅkaraṇaṃ devyai nivedya sādhakottamaḥ | bhaktiyuktena 
manasā svasvasthāne niyojayet | sugandhitailaṃ cikure dattvātha 
kulabhairavaḥ | suvarṇakaṅkati?kayā keśasaṃskāramācaret |

p. 1034) tatastu tribalīṃ baddhvā keśapāśaṃ niyojayet | 
sindūratilakaṃ dadyād bhrūmadhye kajjalaiḥ sudhīḥ | netrañca 
rañjayeddevyāścāmīkaraśalākayā | alaktaṃ caraṇe dattvā 
nakharāgre karāmbjuje | anarghyaratnaghaṭitaṃ dadyācca 
nūpuradvayam | suvarṇaracitaṃ dadyāt pādukāyugalaṃ śuciḥ | 
nivedayet kirīṭañca nānāratnavinirmitam | 
candanāgurukastūrīkusīdakuṅkumādibhiḥ | kuryādālepanaṃ 
devyāḥ kuṇḍagolādibhistathā | raktapadmasahasrāṇi mūladevyai 
nivedayet | samānīya tataḥ pīṭhe punaśca svarṇadolayā | 
caturviṃśatitattvena gandhaṃ devyai nivedayet | tejorūpaṃ maheśāni ! 
dīpaṃ devyai nivedayet | ambaraṃ cāmaraṃ sūryaṃ 
dadyāddarpaṇamuttamam | candraṃ chatraṃ maheśāni ! 
ṣaṭpadmamekhalāṃ tathā | anāhatadhvanimayīṃ ghaṇṭāñca 
vinivedayet | sudhārasāmbudhiṃ dadyāt tatastu māṃ saparvatam | 
brahmāṇḍapūritaṃ devyai pāyasaṃ vinivedayet | ānandaṃ 
vinivedyātha nṣkalaḥ kulabhairavaḥ | 
manonarttakasambhūtaśṛṅgārādirasottamaiḥ | nṛtyairgītaiśca 
vādyaiśca toṣayet parameśvarīm | evameva vidhānena sampūjya 
prajapenmanum ||

śāktānandataraṅgiṇyāṃ ṣaṣṭhollāse || kaścit kaścidviśeṣo 
yathā || ātmasthāṃ devatāṃ tyaktvā vahirdevaṃ hi mṛgyate | 
karasthaṃ kaustubhaṃ tyaktvā bhramate kācatṛṣṇayā | pratyakṣīkṛtya 
hṛdaye hṛdisthāṃ pūjayecchivām | yasya yasya hi devasya yathā 
bhūṣaṇavāhanam | tadeva pūjane tasya cintayet parameśvari ! | 
athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye yena devamayo bhavet | sukhāsane samāsīnaḥ 
prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ | svakīyahṛdaye dhyāyet 
sudhāsāgaramuttamam | ratnadvīpantu tanmadhye 
suvarṇabālukāmayam | mandārapārijātādyaiḥ kalpavṛkṣaiḥ 
supuṣpitaiḥ | sarvato'laṅkṛtairdivyairnityapuṣpaphaladrumaiḥ |

p. 1035) nānāsugandhikusumagandhāmoditadiṅmukham | 
utphullakusumāmodaprahṛṣṭabhṛṅgasaṅkulam | kūjatkokilasaṅghena 
vācālitadigantaram | sarvato'laṅkṛtaṃ divyaṃ 
lasatkāñcanapaṅghajam | mauktikaiḥ kusumaiḥ sragbhirmukulaiḥ 
svarṇatoraṇaiḥ | tanmadhye saṃsmareddevi ! kalpavṛkṣaṃ manoharam | 
catuḥśākhācaturvedaṃ guṇatrayasamanvitam | pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā 
śvetaṃ raktaṃ puṣpañca sundari ! | haritañca vicitrañca 
nānāpuṣpavirājitam | kokilairbhramarairdevi ! śobhitaṃ bahupakṣibhiḥ 
| evaṃ kalpadrumaṃ dhyātvā tadadho ratnavedikām | tatropari 
mahadvyāptaṃ cintayedratnamaṇḍapam | udyadādityasaṅkāśaṃ 
ratnasopānamaṇḍitam | dhvajāvalīsamākīrṇaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam 
| nānāratnaviśobhāḍhyaṃ ratnaprākāramaṇḍitam | 
svasvasthānasthitāvasthairlokapālairadhiṣṭhitam | 
siddhacāraṇagandharvairvidyādharamahoragaiḥ | kinnarairapsarobhiśca 
krīḍadbhiḥ paridiṅmukham | nṛtyavāditraniratairamarastrīgaṇairyutam | 
kiṅkiṇījālasambandhapatākābhiralaṅkṛtam | 
mahāmāṇikyavaidūryaratnacāmarabhūṣitam | 
sthūlamuktāphaloddāmalambamānairalaṅkṛtam | 
candanāgurukastūrīkuṅkumādivilepitam | tanmadhye saṃsmareddevi ! 
mahāmāṇikyavedikām | udyadarkendukiraṇāṃ caturasropaśobhitām | 
dhyāyet siṃhāsanaṃ tatra brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam | siṃhāsane 
maheśāni ! prasūnatūlikāṃ nyaset | pīṭhapūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā 
svakalpoktakrameṇa tu | pretapadmāsane tatra cintayet parameśvarīm | 
ātmanyabhīṣṭadevānāṃ dhyānaṃ dhyānamihocyate | 
śrīratnapāduke dattvā nītvā tāṃ snānamandire | 
siṃhāsanopaviṣṭāyamudvarttanamathācaret | karpūrāgurukastūryā 
tathā mṛgamadena ca | 
rocanākuṅkumairmiśrairnānāgandhasamanvitaiḥ | devyā udvarttanaṃ 
kṛtvā gandhatailaṃ vilepayet | divyavārisahastusrai

p. 1036) svarṇakumbhasahasrakaiḥ | ānīya vāriṇā snātāṃ cintayet 
paradevatām | dukūlairmārjitaṃ gātraṃ dukūle paridhe tathā | 
kaṅkatyā keśaṃ saṃskuryādvidhivad bandhanaṃ tathā | 
paṭṭagucchaṃ keśapāśairnānāratnopaśobhitam | pādayornūpure 
dadyānnāsāgre gajamauktikam | nivedayed yathāśaktyā 
puṣpamālāñca bhūṣaṇam | sarvāṅgalepanaṃ kāryaṃ 
gandhacandanasiktakaiḥ | kāñcanāñcitakañcūlī śobhitā hṛdayopari 
| samādhau cintayeddevīṃ bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ diśet | nyāsajālaṃ 
vidhāyātha samādhau pūjayet sadā | 
ṣoḍaśānāmupacārairhṛdisthāṃ pūjayecchivām | ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ 
dadyāt svāgataṃ kuśalaṃ vadet | pādyañca pādayordevi ! 
śirasyarghyaṃ nivedayet | parāmṛtamācamanaṃ 
pradadyānmukhapaṅkaje | madhuparkaṃ mukhe dadyāt tridhā 
ācamanaṃ mukhe | hemapātragataṃ divyaṃ paramānnaṃ pariṣkṛtam | 
kapilāghṛtasaṃyuktamannañca vyañjanānvitam | sudhāmbudhiṃ 
māṃsaśailaṃ matsyarāśiṃ phalāti ca | bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā 
lehyaṃ carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ tathaiva ca | sakarpūrañca tāmbūlaṃ 
mānasaṃ parikalpayet | āvaraṇān tato devyāḥ pūjanaṃ manasaiva hi | 
itthamantaḥ samārādhya mānasaiva japenmanum | sahasrādijapaṃ 
kṛtvā devyai sodakamarpayet | brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca 
sadāśivaḥ | ata eva mahādevyāḥ paryaṅkaṃ samudāhṛtam | 
payaḥphenanibhāṃ śayyāṃ nānāpuṣpopaśobhitām | 
puṣpaśayyāñca saṃskuryāttatra devīṃ sureśvarīm | cintayet 
sādhako yogī nānāsukhavilāsinīm | nṛtyagītaiḥ savādyaiśca toṣayet 
parameśvarīm | tato homaṃ prakurvīta pūjāsāphalyahetave | atha 
homaṃ pravakṣyāmi yena cinmayatāṃ vrajet | athādharāmaye kuṇḍe 
cidagnau homayettataḥ ||

mahānirvāṇatantre pañcamapaṭale || hṛtpadmamāsanaṃ 
dadyāt sahasrāracyutāmṛtaiḥ | pādyaṃ

p. 1037) caraṇayordadyānmanasārghyaṃ nivedayet | 
tenāmṛtenācamanaṃ snānīyamapi kalpayet | ākāśatattvaṃ vasanaṃ 
gandhantu gandhatattvakam | cittaṃ prakalpayet puṣpam dhūpaṃ 
prāṇān prakalpayet | tejastattvañca dīpārthe naivedyañca 
sudhāmbudhim | anāhatadhvaniṃ ghaṇṭāṃ vāyutattvañca cāmaram | 
nṛtyamindriyakarmāṇi cāñcalyaṃ manasastathā | puṣpaṃ 
nānāvidhaṃ dadyādātmano bhāvasiddhaye | 
amāyāmanahaṅkāramarāgamamadaṃ tathā | amohakamadambhañca 
adveṣākṣobhake tathā | amātsaryamalobhañca daśapuṣpaṃ 
prakīrttitam | ahiṃsāṃ paramaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigraham | 
dayākṣamājñānapuṣpaṃ pañcapuṣpaṃ tataḥ param | iti 
pañcadaśaiḥ puṣpairbhāvapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet | sudhāmbudhiṃ 
māṃsaśailaṃ bharjitaṃ mīnaparvatam | mudrārāśiṃ subhaktañca 
ghṛtāktaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā | kulāmṛtañca tat puṣpaṃ 
pīṭhakṣālanavāri ca | kāmakrodhau tu vikṛtau baliṃ dattvā japaṃ 
caret | mālā varṇamayī proktā kuṇḍalīsūtrayantritā | savinduṃ 
varṇamuccārya mūlamantraṃ samuccaret | 
akārādikṣakārāntamanulomavilomataḥ | punarlakāramārabhya 
śrīkaṇṭhāntamanuṃ japet | viloma iti vikhyātaḥ kṣakāro merurucyate | 
aṣṭavargāntimairvarṇaiḥ saha mūlamathāṣṭakam | 
evamaṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā tena samarpayet | sarvāntarātmanilaye 
svāhā jyotiḥsvarūpiṇī | gṛhāṇāntarjapaṃ mātarādye ! kāli ! 
namo'stu te | samarpya japametena sāṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇameddhiyā | 
ityantaryajanaṃ kṛtvā vahiḥpūjāṃ samārabhet ||

athāntarhomaḥ || nityātantre saptamapaṭale || jñānahomavidhiṃ 
vakṣye śṛṇu sarvāṅgasundari ! | yasya vijñānamātreṇa karttā harttā 
sadāśivaḥ | ātmeti caturasrantu vicintya vīravandite ! | ātmāntarātmā 
paramajñānātmā parameśvari ! | caturbhiretairdeveśi ! kuryāttu

p. 1038) caturasrakam | arddhamātrāṃ yonirūpāṃ kuṇḍamadhye 
vicintayet | ānandaṃ mekhalāṃ kuryātrirekhāṃ balayaṃ tathā | 
jñānāgniṃ tatra deveśi ! yojayet kulabhairavaḥ | śabdākhyaṃ 
mātṛkārūpaṃ saṃvidagnau tato humet | akṣarāṇīha me devi ! 
niḥśabdaṃ brahma jāyate | puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vikalpañca saṅkalpaṃ 
vīravandite ! | kṛtyañcākṛtyamīśāni ! havīṃṣyetāni pārvati ! | 
cintayenmūlavidyāñca juhuyānmanasā srucā | tadā saṃvinmayaḥ 
sākṣāt paraṃ brahmapadaṃ vrajet ||

atha pañcāhutayaḥ || śyāmārahasye mūlānte || 
nābhicaitanyarūpāgnau haviṣā manasā srucā | jñānapradīpite 
nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham | svāhā | anena prathamāhutiṃ dadyāt || 
tato mūlānte || dharmādharmahavirjñāpta ātmāgnau manasā srucā | 
suṣumnāvartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham | svāhā iti 
dvitīyāhutiṃ dadyāt || tato mūlānte || 
prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīsrucā | 
dharmādharmakalāsnehapūrṇamagnau juhomyaham | 
vahnijāyāntamantreṇa tṛtīyāhutimādiśet || anena tṛtīyāhutiṃ 
dadyāt mūlānte || antarnirantaranirindhanamedhamāne 
māyāndhakāraparipanthini saṃvidagnau | 
kasmiṃścidadbhutamarīcivikāśabhūmau viśvaṃ juhomi 
vasudhādiśi vāvasānam ||

atha śrīcakrakramaḥ || kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe 
pañcamollāse || ādhāreṇa vinā bhraṃśo na ca tṛpyanti mātaraḥ | 
tasmādvidhivadādhāraṃ kalpayet kulanāyike ! | ādhāraṃ tripadaṃ 
prāhuḥ ṣaṭpadaṃ vā catuṣpadam | athavā varttulākāraṃ kuryāddevi 
! manoharam | iti pātrādhārakathanam ||

svarṇarūpyaśilākūrmakapālālāvumṛṇmayam | nārikelañca 
p. 1039) śaṅkhañca muktāśuktisamudbhavam || 
puṇyavṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ pātraṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ | 
atisūkṣmamatisthūlaṃ chinnaṃ bhinnañca varjayet | iti pātrakathanam 
|| suvarṇaraupyatāmrāṇi sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca | śāntike ca 
śilāpātraṃ stambhane caiva mṛṇmayam | nārikelañca 
vaśyādāvabhicāre ca kūrmajam | śaṅkhaṃ jñānapradaṃ muktā 
śuktividyāpradāyinī | kapālālāvupātrāṇi yogasiddhikarāṇi ca | 
puṇyavṛkṣajapātrāṇi sarvapāpaharāṇi ca | ukteṣveteṣu deveśi ! 
pātramekaṃ prakalpayet || iti karmaviśeṣe pātraphalam ||

etadanantaraṃ kuladravyakaraṇamuktvā ityādi madye hetūni 
madyānyanyāni kārayet | pānasaṃ drākṣamādhūkaṃ khārjūraṃ 
tālamaikṣavam | madhūtthaṃ sīdhumādhvīkamaireyaṃ nārikelajam | 
madyānyekādaśaitāni bhuktimuktikarāṇi ca | dvādaśantu 
surāmadyaṃ sarveṣāmuttamaṃ priye ! | gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca 
vijñeyā trividhā surā | sarvasiddhikarī paiṣṭī gauḍī bhogapradāyinī | 
mādhvī muktikarī proktā surā syāddevatā priye ! | iti 
trividhasurākathanam ||

vidyāpradaikṣavī mālā mādhvī rājyapradā bhavet | tālajā 
stambhane śastā khārjūrī ripunāśinī | nārikelabhavā śrīdā 
pānasī ca śubhapradā | madhūkajā jñānakarī mādhvīkā 
roganāśinī | maireyākhyā maheśāni ! sarvapāpāpahāriṇī | 
ityekādaśamadyaphalam || kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ madyaṃ 
valkalasambhavam | madhupuṣpasamudbhūtamāsavaṃ taṇḍulodbhavam 
| tadgandhaghrāṇamātreṇa śatakratuphalaṃ labhet | tasya 
samparkamātreṇa tīrthakoṭiphalaṃ labhet | devi ! tatpānataḥ 
sākṣāllabhenmuktiṃ caturvidhām || madyābhāve vaṭikoktā tatraiva || 
madyamāṃsādivijayāṃ cāṣṭagandhaiḥ sumiśritam | sammardya 
vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā saṅgṛhyātha vicakṣaṇaḥ | madyābhāve tu vaṭikāṃ 

p. 1040) jale saṃyojya tarpayet | vaṭikābhāve dravyāntareṇa 
tarpaṇamuktaṃ tatraiva || guḍamiśreṇa takreṇa tarpayenmadhubhājinā | 
sauvīreṇāthavākuryādetat karma na lopayet | pramādād yati lupyeta 
devatāśāpamāpnuyāt | māṃsañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ 
khabhūjalacaraṃ priye ! | yathāsambhavameṣvekaṃ tarpaṇārthaṃ 
prakalpayet | māṃsasandarśanenāpi surādarśanavat phalam | 
pitṛdevādiyajñeṣu vaivahiṃsā vidhīyate | ātmārthaṃ prāṇināṃ 
hiṃsā kadācinnoditā priye ! | animittaṃ tṛṇaṃ vāpi chedayenna 
kadācana | devatārthaṃ dvijārthaṃ vā hatvā pāpairna lipyate || 
māṃsābhāve māṃsapratinidhiruktastatraiva || māṃsābhāve tu 
lasunaṃ sārdrakaṃ nāgarantu vā | ādāya pūjayeddevīṃ nānyathā 
niṣphalaṃ bhavet || matsyamāṃsatatpratinidhyabhāve 
kevalakāraṇenāpi tarpaṇamuktam tatraiva || matsyamāṃsādihīnena 
madyenāpi ca tarpayet | na kuryānmatsyamāṃsābhyāṃ vinā 
dravyeṇa pūjanam || tarpaṇaphalamuktaṃ tatraiva || piśitaṃ 
tilamātrantu tilārddhamapi vindunā | sakṛttarpitamātreṇa 
koṭiyajñaphalaṃ labhet | kulapūjāsamaṃ nāsti puṇyamanyajjagattraye 
| tasmād yaḥ pūjayedbhaktyā bhuktimuktyoḥ sa bhājanam | 
anadhītaśca śāstrajño gurubhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ | kulapūjārato yastu 
sa me priyatamo bhavet | caturṇāmapi 
varṇānāmāśramāṇāmadhīśvari ! | puṃstrīnapuṃsakānāñca 
pūjitā tvaṃ sadāśive ! | ihāmutra phalaṃ dadyāt pūjyā 
surabadhūriva | kulapūjādikaṃ vinā yaścaitat karoti tasya doṣamāha 
tatraiva || kulapūjāṃ vinā yastu karotyetat sudurmatiḥ | sa yāti narakaṃ 
ghoramekaviṃśatibhiḥ kulaiḥ || ārādhane tvaśaktānāṃ 
kṛtyamuktaṃ tatraiva || tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kulapūjārato bhavet | 
labhate sarvasiddhiñca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |

p. 1041) ārādhanāsamarthaśceddadyādarcanasādhanam | yo dātuṃ 
naiva śaknoti kuryādarcanadarśanam || śaivādīnāmapi kuladravyeṇa 
pūjanamuktaṃ tatraiva || śaive ca vaiṣṇave śākte saure ca 
gaṇadarśane | bauddhe pāśupate sāṅkhye vrate kalāmukhe tathā 
sadakṣavāmasiddhānte vainikādiṣu pārvati ! | ninālipiśitābhyāñca 
pūjanaṃ viphalaṃ bhavet | kuladravyairvinā 
kuryājjapayajñatapovratam | niṣphalañca bhaveddevi ! bhasmanīva 
yathāhutiḥ | etena vaiṣṇave kulācārābhāva iti yenoktaṃ 
tenetyādivacanaṃ na śrutamiti bhāti ||

kulācārakaraṇaphalamāha tatraiva || yathaivānucarā rājñaḥ 
priyāḥ syurna vahiścarāḥ | tathāntaryāganiṣṭhā ye te priyā devi ! 
nāpare | samarpayanti ye bhaktyā karābhyāṃ piśitāsavam | utpādayati 
cānandaṃ matpriyāḥ kaulikāśca te | āvayoḥ paramākāraṃ 
saccidānandalakṣaṇam | kuladravyopabhogeṇa parisphurati nānyathā | 
antaḥsthātmabhavollāso manovācāmagocaraḥ | kuladravyopabhogeṇa 
jāyate nānyathā priye ! | sevite ca kuladravye kulatattvārthadarśinaḥ | 
jāyate bhairavāveśaḥ sarvatra samadarśinaḥ | tamaḥparivṛtaṃ veśma 
yathā dīpena dṛśyate | tathā māyāvṛto hyātmā dravyapānena 
dṛśyate | mantrapūtaṃ kuladravyaṃ gurudevārpitaṃ priye ! | ye pibanti 
janāsteṣāṃ stanyapānaṃ na vidyate | surā śaktiḥ śivo māṃsaṃ 
tadbhakto bhairavaḥ svayam | tayoraikyaṃ samutpannamānando mokṣa 
ucyate | ānandaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ tattu deheṣvavasthitam | 
asyābhivyañjakaṃ madyaṃ yogibhistena pīyate | niḥsaṅgo nirbhayo 
vīro nirdvandvo niṣkutūhalaḥ | nirṇītavedaśāstrārtho varadāṃ 
vāruṇīṃ pībet ||

saṃskṛtadravyadānaphalantu tatraiva || 
mantrasaṃskārasaṃśuddhāmṛtapānena pārvati ! | jāyate 
devatābhāvo bhavabandhavimocakaḥ | brāhmaṇasya sadā peyaṃ 
kṣatriyasya raṇāgame | golambhane ca vaiśyasya 
śūdrasyāntyeṣṭikarmaṇi | devān pitṝn samabhyarcya devi ! 
śāstroktavartmanā || arthalobhādinā kuladravyapāne doṣastatraiva || 
guruṃ smaran pibanmadyaṃ khādanmāṃsaṃ na doṣabhāk | 
tṛptyarthaṃ sarvadevānāṃ brahmajñānārthameva ca | seveta 
madhumāṃsāni tṛṣṇayā cet saṃ pātakī | 
mantrārthasphuraṇārthāya manasaḥ sthairyahetave | 
bhavapāśanivṛttyarthaṃ madhupānaṃ samācaret | sevate yaḥ 
sukhārthāya madyādīni sa pātakī | prāśayeddevatāprītyai 
hyabhilāsavivarjitaḥ | matsyamāṃsāsavādīnāṃ mādanānāṃ 
niṣevaṇam | yāgakālaṃ vinānyatra dūṣaṇaṃ kathitaṃ priye ! | yathā 
kratuṣu viprāṇāṃ somapānaṃ vidhīyate | madhupānaṃ tathā 
kāryaṃ samaye bhogamokṣadam | śrīguroḥ kulaśāstrebhyaḥ 
samyagvijñāya vāsanām || pañcamudrāṃ niṣeveta cānyathā 
pātakī bhavet | ayaṣṭvā bhairavaṃ devamakṛtvā mantratarpaṇam | 
paśupānavidhau pītvā kaulo'pi narakaṃ vrajet | kaulajñāne hyasiddho 
yastaddravyaṃ bhoktumicchati | sa mahāpātakī devi ! 
sarvakarmavahiṣkṛtaḥ | samayācārahīnasya svairavṛtterdurātmanaḥ | 
na siddhayaḥ kulabhraṃśastatsaṃsargaṃ na kārayet | yaḥ 
śāstravidhimutsṛjya varttate kāmacārataḥ | na sa siddhimavāpnoti 
paratra na parāṃ gatim | svecchayā varttamāno yo 
dīkṣāsaṃskāravarjitaḥ | na tasya sadgatiḥ kvāpi 
tapastīrthavratādibhiḥ | asaṃskṛtaṃ pibeddravyaṃ balātkāreṇa 
maithunam | svapriyeṇa hataṃ māṃsaṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 
kaulāḥ paśuvratāścaiva pakṣadvayaviḍambakāḥ | keśasaṅkhyā 
sthitā yāvattāvattiṣṭhati raurave | kuladravyāṇi sevante 
ye'nyadarśanamāśritāḥ | tadaṅgaromasaṅkhyātaṃ palāśī sa 
nigadyate | svalakṣyādindriyagaṇaṃ sampādyātmani yojayet | 
māṃsāśī sa bhaveddevi ! śeṣāḥ syuḥ prāṇihiṃsakāḥ |

p. 1043) aprabuddhvā paśoḥ śaktiṃ prabuddhvā kaulikasya ca | 
śaktiṃ tāṃ sevayed yastu sa bhavecchaktisevakaḥ | 
paraśaktyātmamithunasaṃyogānandanirbharaḥ | ya āste maithunaṃ yat 
syāditare strīniṣevakāḥ | ityādipañcamudrāṇāṃ vāsanāṃ 
kulanāyike ! jñātvā gurumukhāddevi ! yaḥ seveta sa mucyate ||

ṣaṣṭhollāse || pūjakalakṣaṇādi || pūjābhiṣekasahito devi ! 
śāstrārthatattvavit | devatāgurubhaktaśca niyataṃ yo'rcayet priye ! | 
kulāgamarahasyajño devatārādhanotsukaḥ | gurupadeśasaṃyuktaḥ 
pūjayet kulanāyike ! | śuddhātmā cātisaṃhṛṣṭaḥ 
krodhalaulyavivarjitaḥ | paśuvratādivimukhaḥ sammukhastarpayet priye ! 
| mantrayogena deveśi ! kuryāt śrīcakrapūjanam | tadahantu tvayā 
sārddhaṃ gṛhṇāmi svayamādarāt | bhairavo'hamiti jñānāt 
sarvajñādiguṇānvitaḥ | iti sañcintya yogīśaḥ kulapūjārato bhavet | 
ityādilakṣaṇopetaḥ kauliko niyatavrataḥ | yastvāṃ samarcayeddevi ! 
bhuktimuktyoḥ sa bhājanam | ekānte vijane'raṇye deśe bādhāvivarjite 
| sukhāsane samāsīnaḥ prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ | 
ātmasthānamanudravyadevaśuddhistu pañcadhā | yāvanna kurute devi 
! tāvaddevārcanaṃ kutaḥ | pañcaśuddhiṃ vidhāyetthaṃ paścād 
yajanamācaret | sā pūjā saphalā jñeyā cānyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet | 
maṇḍalena vinā pūjā viphalā kathitā priye ! | 
tasmānmaṇḍalamālikhya vidhivat tatra pūjayet ||

maṇḍalalakṣaṇaṃ tatraiva || akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ viśvaṃ 
vyāpya vyavasthitam | trailokyaṃ maṇḍitaṃ yena maṇḍalaṃ tat 
sadāśivam | udyānacaturasraṃ syāt kāmarūpañca varttulam | 
jālandharorddhvacandrābhaṃ tryasraṃ pūrṇagirirbhavet | abhyarcya 
maṇḍalaṃ paścādādhārān sthāpayet kramāt | sāmānyaṃ 
śrīgurorbhogabalipātrāṇi pañcadhā | dvipātraṃ vā tripātraṃ

p. 1044) ekapātraṃ na kārayet | svadakṣiṇādivāmāntaṃ 
saṃsthāpyārcyāsavena tu | sampūjya mūlamantreṇa kuleśvari ! 
vidhānavit | tatra māṣapramāṇantu matsyaṃ māṃsañca nikṣipet ||

tarpaṇe niṣiddhasurāmāha tatraiva || neṣṭaiḥ 
paryuṣitocchiṣṭairdurgandhairgandhavarjitaiḥ | hetubhiḥ 
parapātrasthaistarpaṇaṃ viphalaṃ bhavet || asaṃskṛtā surā 
pāpakalahavyādhiduḥkhadā | 
āyuḥśrīkīrttisaubhāgyadhanadhānyavināśinī | tasmāt saṃskṛtya 
vidhivat kuladravyaṃ tato'rccayet | anyathā yāti narakaṃ bhoktā dātā 
na saṃśayaḥ ||

surāśodhanaprakārastu tatraiva || 
vīkṣaṇaprokṣaṇadhyānamantramudrāviśodhitam | dravyaṃ 
tarpaṇayogyaṃ syāddevatāprītikāraṇam || pratyekaṃ 
pañcamakāraśodhanamantro'pi tatraiva || prathamaṃ haṃsaḥ sadasat 
pratadviṣṇuranantaram | tryambakantu tṛtīyaṃ syāccaturthaṃ 
tatpadādikam | viṣṇuryonikamityādi pañcamaṃ kalpanāmanuḥ | 
ekadvitricatuḥpañcadvicaturvāramambike ! | saṃskṛtyābhyarcya 
pātrantu pūjayeddhenumudrayā | 
brahmāṇḍakhaṇḍasambhūtamaśeṣarasasambhavam | āpūritaṃ 
mahāpātraṃ pīyūṣarasameva ha | śuddhadravyeṇa tenāpi 
śuddhapuṣpākṣatairapi | nyāsapūrvoktamantrestu ātmānaṃ pūjayet 
priye ! | mūrdhni śrīgurupaṃktiñca mūlādhāre ca pādukām ||

gurupaṃktikramamāha tatraiva || divyaughe cādināthāśca 
tacchaktīśca sadāśivaḥ | tatpatnī ceśvarastasya bhāryā rudraśca 
tadbudhaḥ | viṣṇuśca tatpriyo brahmā tatkāntā dvādaśeritā | 
siddhaughe sanakaścaiva sanandaśca sanātanaḥ | sanatkumāraśca 
sanatsujātaśca ribhukṣajāḥ | dattātreyo daivatako vāmadevastataḥ 
param | tato vyāsaḥ śukaścaiva ekādaśa samīritāḥ | mānavaughe 
nṛsiṃhaśca maheśo bhārgavastathā | mahendro mādhavo viṣṇuḥ

p. 1045) ṣaḍete parikīrttitaḥ | namo yo yojayeddevi ! divyaughe paramaṃ 
śivam | mahāśivañca siddhaughe mānavaughe sadāśivam | tataḥ 
pīṭhaṃ samabhyarcya devīmāvāhayet priye ! | 
mahāpadmavanāntaḥsthe ! kāraṇānandavigrahe ! | sarvabhūtahite ! 
mātarehyehi parameśvari ! | deveśi ! bhaktisulabhe ! 
sarvābharaṇasaṃyute ! | yāvattvāṃ pūjayāmīha tāvattvaṃ susthirā 
bhava | mantreṇānena cāvāhya yajeddevīmananyadhīḥ | dhyātvā 
mudrāḥ pradarśyārcyedgandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ ||

atha kulācāracakram || kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe 
ekādaśollāse || yadi svāddīkṣito jyeṣṭhaḥ kulapūjādivarjitaḥ | 
tatkaniṣṭhastatkramaścet kulapūjāṃ samācaret || tatsamīpaṃ tato 
gatvā namaskṛtya guruṃ yathā | tasmai nivedya tatsarvaṃ śeṣaṃ 
bhuñjīta pārvati ! | pūjāmadhye gurau jyeṣṭhe pūjye vāpi samāgate | 
natvā brūyāt sthitiṃ śiṣyamācarettadanujñayā | jyeṣṭhasya ca 
kaniṣṭhasya śiṣyāvekatra saṃsthitau | tatra pūjyavadācāraḥ kathitaḥ 
kulanāyike ! | ajñātakālaprāptañcet paurvāparyantu cintayet | smṛtvā 
tasya guruṃ devi ! samaveśena tarpayet | nityārccanaṃ divā 
kuryādrātrau naimittikārccanam | ubhayoḥ kāmyakarmāṇi ceti 
śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ | na snātvā nāsanastho vā bhuktvā ca 
pralapannapi | gandhapuṣpākṣatākalpavastrādyairanalaṅkṛtaḥ | 
avinyastaśarīro vā kulapūjāṃ na cācaret | vinā mantreṇa yā pūjā 
vinā māṃsena tarpaṇam | vinā śaktyā ca yat pānaṃ niṣphalaṃ 
kathitaṃ priye ! | śrīcakrameko vā kuryādekapātrañca nārccayet | 
nārcayedekahastena pibedekapāṇinā | matsyamāṃsāsavairddevi ! 
nārccayet paśusannidhau | praṇamya praviśeccakraṃ nirgacchecca 
praṇamya ca | śrīcakradarśanaṃ devi ! netrayoḥ pāpanāśanam | 
anācārān sadācārān cakrasthān śaktikaulikān |

p. 1046) śivagaurīdhiyā devi ! bhāvayennāvamānayet | 
kulācāryagṛhaṃ gatvā bhaktyā pāpaviśuddhaye | 
yācedamṛtagandhañca tadabhāve jalaṃ pibet | kulācāryeṇa 
yacchaktyā dattaṃ pātrantu bhaktitaḥ | namaskṛtya tu 
gṛhṇiyādanyathā narakaṃ brajet | asnātvā vāpi bhuktvā vā 
lobhādvāpi kuleśvari ! | yaḥ sevate kuladravyaṃ sa 
dāridryamavāpnuyāt | uṣṇīṣī kañcukī nagno muktakeśo 
gaṇāvṛtaḥ | parāṅmukho vivādī ca na pibettu kulāmṛtam | 
yogāmṛtena niṣṭhivya madyabhāṇḍaparibhramāt | ūrddhvanālena 
pānācca devatāśāpamāpnuyāt | ekāsane niviṣṭā ye bhuñjate 
caikabhājane | ekapātre pibantyambu te yānti narakaṃ priye ! | yaḥ 
sevate kuladravyamekagrāme sthite gurau | tatkulajñe ca tatputre 
sajyeṣṭhe kuladeśike | vinānujñāṃ kuleśāni ! so'kṣayaṃ narakaṃ 
brajet | ucchiṣṭo na spṛśeccakre kuladravyāṇi pārvati ! | vahiḥ 
prakṣālya ca karau kuladravyāṇi dāpayet | madyabhāṇḍaṃ 
samuddhṛtya na pātraṃ pūrayet priye ! | bhogapātraṃ surākumbhe 
nikṣipenna kadācana | cakramadhye śucidhiyā karaprakṣālanādikam | 
yaḥ karoti vimūḍhātmā sa bhavedāpadāṃ padam | niṣṭhīvanaṃ 
malaṃ mūtramadhovāyuvisarjanam | śrīcakramadhye yaḥ kuryāt sa 
bhavet yoginīpaśuḥ | cakramadhye ghaṭe bhagne pātre ca patite bhuvi | 
dīpanāśe ca śāntyarthaṃ śrīcakraṃ kārayet priye ! |

prauḍhollāse sati jñānimattayorvijñānaṃ yathā tatraiva || 
mantrān japanti dhyāyanti stuvanti praṇamanti ca | bodhayanti ca 
pṛcchanti nandanti jñāninaḥ priye ! | mattā hasanti gacchanti vivadante 
parasparam | rudanti śriyamicchanti nindanti jñāninaḥ priye ! | 
parihāsaṃ pralāpañca vitaṇḍāṃ bahubhāṣaṇam | audāsīnyaṃ

p. 1047) bhayaṃ krodhaṃ cakramadhye vivarjayet | pātrahasto mahādevi 
! na bhrameccakramadhyataḥ | pūrṇapātraṃ kare kṛtvā na tiṣṭheta 
ciraṃ priye ! | nālapet pātrahastaḥ san na nindyāt pātramambike ! | na 
padbhyāṃ saṃspṛśet pātraṃ na vinduḥ pātayedadhaḥ | naikahastena 
dātavyaṃ na mudrāvarjitaṃ priye ! | pātraṃ na cālayet sthānāt na 
kuryāt pātrasaṅkulam | saśabdaṃ na pibenmadyaṃ tathaiva na ca 
pūrayet | nānyonyaṃ tāḍayet pātraṃ na pātraṃ pātayedadhaḥ | 
sādhāraṃ noddharet pātramanādhāre ca nikṣipet | riktapātraṃ na 
kurvīta na pātraṃ bhrāmayet priye ! | na pātraṃ laṅghayeddhīmān 
pātraṃ notpātayet kvacit | prakṣālya gopayet pātramityājñā 
parameśvari ! | yadasandīpitollāsaḥ kaulikaḥ paśumīkṣate | paṭhitvā 
paśuśāstrāṇi saṅgacchedvā paśustriyam | kuryāt paśuprasaṅgaṃ 
vā paśukāryāṇi vā caret | śrīcakrasthaṃ kuladravyaṃ yaḥ 
paśubhyaḥ prayacchati | lehāllobhādbhayādvāpi sa 
bhavedyoginīpaśuḥ | ripuṇāpi na karttavyo vāgvādaścakramadhyataḥ 
| pitṛmātṛsamaṃ paśyet tenoktaṃ puruṣaṃ sahet | tathā 
strīputramitrādi dṛṣṭvā cetaḥ prahṛṣyati | tathā cet kaulikān dṛṣṭvā 
sa bhaved yoginīpriyaḥ ||

tathā || nityaṃ samarcayedbhaktyā paśuhaste na nikṣipet | 
svadāravanniṣeveta kulaśāstrāṇi pārvati ! | paśuśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi 
varjayet paradāravat | śvacarmasthaṃ yathā kṣīraṃ na peyaṃ 
syāddvijātibhiḥ | tathā paśumukhāddharmo na śrotavyaśca kaulikaiḥ 
| yaḥ śṛṇoti kulācāraṃ yathāśāstrañca yo vadet | tāvubhau 
macchataḥ sākṣād yoginīvīramelanam | aśraddaddhānā ye cātra 
kuladharme kuleśvari ! | narakānna nivarttante yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam 
|| tathā || guruśaktiṃ vīrabhāryāṃ kumārīṃ vratadhāriṇīm | 
vyaṅgāṅgīṃ vikṛtāṅgīñca kubjāmapi na kārayet | sutāñca 
bhaginīṃ pautrīṃ snuṣāṃ vāpi priyāmapi | na kāmayedguroragre

p. 1048) kuryānnānyonyagūhanam | kṛṣṇāṃ śuklāṃ 
kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ kumārīñca kṛśodarīm | manoharāṃ 
yauvanasthāmarcayeddevatādhiyā | ekadāpi na seveta balena 
kulayoginīm | cakramadhye svayaṃ kṣubdhāṃ kāmayet kulasundari ! | 
āmamāṃsaṃ surākumbhaṃ mattebhaṃ siddhaliṅginam | 
sahakāramaśokañca krīḍālīnāḥ kumārikāḥ | ekavṛkṣaṃ 
śmaśānañca samūhaṃ yoṣitāmapi | nārīñca raktavasanāṃ 
dṛṣṭvā vandeta bhaktitaḥ | guruśaktiṃ sutaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ kaniṣṭhaṃ 
kuladeśikam | kuladarśanaśāstrāṇi kuladravyāṇi kaulikān | prerakān 
sūcakān vāpi vācakān darśakāṃstathā | śikṣakān bodhakān 
yogiyoginīsiddharūpakān | kanyāṃ kumārikāṃ 
nagnāmunmattāmapi yoṣitam | na nindenna jugupseta sahāsaṃ 
nāvamānayet | nāpriyaṃ nānṛtaṃ brūyāt kasyāpi kulayoginaḥ | 
kurūpā ceti kṛṣṇeti na vadet kulayoṣitam | parīkṣayenna bhaktānāṃ 
vīrāṇāñca kṛtākṛte | na paśyet patitāṃ nagnāmunmattāṃ 
prakaṭastanīm | divase na ramet kāntāṃ tadyoniṃ naiva vīkṣayet | yā 
kācidaṅganā loke sā mātṛkulasambhavā | kupyanti kulayoginyo 
vanitānāmatikramāt | striyaṃ śatāparādhena puṣpeṇāpi na tāḍayet | 
doṣānna gaṇayet strīṇāṃ guṇāneva prakāśayet | tiṣṭhanti 
kulayoginyaḥ kulavṛkṣeṣu sarvadā | tatpatreṣu na bhoktavyamarcayettu 
viśeṣataḥ | na svapyāt kulavṛkṣādho na copadravamācaret | dṛṣṭvā 
bhaktyā namaskuryācchedayenna kadācana | śleṣmātakakarañjāni 
nimbāśvatthakadambakāḥ | vilvo vaṭoḍumbaraśca tintiḍirdaśamāḥ 
smṛtāḥ | prāyaścittaṃ bhṛgoḥ pātaṃ sannyāsaṃ vratadhāraṇam | 
tīrthayātrābhigamanaṃ kaulaḥ pañca vivarjayet | vīrahatyā 
vṛthāpānaṃ vīradravyāpahāritā | vīrastrīgamanañcaiva 
tatsaṃsargaśca pañcamaḥ | mahāpātakamityuktaṃ kaulikānāṃ
p. 1049) kulānvaye | śaive tattvaparijñānaṃ gāruḍe viṣabhakṣaṇam | 
jyotiṣe grahaṇaṃ sāraṃ kaule'nugrahanigrahau || 

athānandasvarūpakathanam || samayācāratantre prathamapaṭale || 
ānandaṃ paramaṃ brahma paraśaktiḥ sadāśivaḥ | ānandena vinā 
bhraṃśo na ca tṛpyanti devatāḥ | 
dravyaśuddhyādisakalamānandārthañca bhairavi ! | ānande 
jāyamāne tu bhakṣayenna kadācana || atipānādbhavenmatto 
japapūjādi niṣphalam | buddhināśo bhaveddevi | ata eva mitaṃ caret | 
sukhārthaṃ bhakṣayedyastvāpadastasya pade pade | 
atisaṅkṣobhato'tyarthalokanindā ca jāyate || tathā || sarvaṃ 
brahmamayaṃ paśyan sa ca doṣairna lipyate | mohādvākāmato vāpi 
yaḥ kaścidiha varttate | so'dhamaḥ sādhakānāñca nārakī bhavati 
dhruvam | iha loke ninditaḥ syāt pare ca narakaṃ vrajet || 

athānukalpaḥ || samayācāratantre || gavyādīnāmabhāve tu 
nityaṃ karma na lopayet | pramādāllopayed yastu narakaṃ labhate 
dhruvam | dravyaṃ caturdaśaṃ proktaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva pṛthak pṛthak | 
teṣāṃ madhye dvayaṃ dravyaṃ na grāhyañca kadācana | kṣīraṃ 
dadhi tathā takraṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ guḍaṃ tathā | śarkarā śītalaṃ 
toyamaikṣavaṃ nārikelakam | āsphenaṃ vijayāñcaivamunmattañca 
viṣaṃ tathā | dhūstūrañca viṣaṃ devi ! dravyaṃ prāṇaharātmakam 
| anyāni yāni dravyāṇi ānandārthaṃ pibennaraḥ || tathā || 
sarvadravyādyabhāve tu ārdrakaṃ parikīrtitam | jalaṃ vā kṣīratakraṃ 
vā nityaṃ karma samāpayet | makārapañcakābhāve pañcamaṃ 
parikīrtitam | abhāve pañcame devi ! manasā pañcamaṃ vrajet || 

atha pāramparyopadeśādiṃ vinā pañcatattvasevananindā || 
p.1050) kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe dvitīyollāse || gurūpedaśarahitā 
mohayantīha kecana | mohayanti janān kecit svayaṃ pūrvavimohitāḥ | 
durācāraparāḥ kecidvācayanti ca pāmarāḥ | bahavaḥ kaulikaṃ 
dharmaṃ mithyājñānaviḍambakāḥ | svabuddhyā balayantīśaṃ 
pāramparyavivarjitāḥ | madyapānena manujo yadi siddhiṃ labheta vai | 
madyapānaratāḥ sarve siddhiṃ gacchantu pāmarāḥ | 
māṃsabhakṣaṇamātreṇa yadi puṇyagatirbhavet | loke māṃsāśinaḥ 
sarve puṇyabhājo bhavantviha | strīsambhogena deveśi ! yadi mokṣaṃ 
vrajanti vai sarve'pi jantavo loke muktāḥ syuḥ strīniṣevaṇāt | 
kulamārgo mahān devi ! na mayā ninditaḥ kvacit | ācararahitā ye'tra 
ninditāste ca sarvadā | anyathā kaulike dharme ācāraḥ kathito mayā | 
vicarantyanyathā devi ! mūḍhāḥ paṇḍitamāninaḥ | kṛpāṇadhārā 
gamanādvyāghrakaṇṭhāvalambanāt | 
bhujaṅgadhāraṇānnyūnamaśaṅkyaṃ kulavarṇanam | vṛthāpānantu 
deveśi ! surāpānaṃ taducyate | yanmahāpātakaṃ jñeyaṃ vedādiṣu 
nirūpitam | anāghreyamanāsevyamaspṛśyañcāpyapeyakam | madyaṃ 
māṃsaṃ paśūnāntu kaulikānāṃ mahāphalam | yattu | amedhyāni 
dvijātīnāṃ madyānyekādaśaiva tu | dvādaśantu surāmadyaṃ 
sarveṣāmadhamaṃ smṛtam | tasmād brāhmaṇarājanyau baiśyaśca na 
surāṃ pibet | iti dvijātipānaniṣedhakavacanaṃ tadapi 
pūrvavacanaikavākyatayā paśuparamanabhiṣiktaparaṃ vā || 

cakrānuṣṭhānaṃ yathā śyāmārahasyadhṛtam || cakrākāreṇa 
paṃktyākāreṇa vā bhinnabhinnāsane svaśaktiyuktaścet 
yugmayugmakrameṇa padmāsanenopaviśya sāmayikalalāṭe candanaṃ 
dattvā śivaśaktibuddhyā puṣpañca dadyāt | tato yadi gurustatra 
tiṣṭhati tadādau gandhādinā taṃ pūjayitvā tatpātre puṣpaṃ dattvā 
śuddhisahitaṃ tasmai samarpya praṇamet | gurorabhāve tatpātraṃ jale

p. 1051) kṣipet | tataḥ śaktipātraṃ śuddhisahitaṃ śaktyai dattvā 
sāmayikebhyo'pi jyeṣṭhānukrameṇa vīrapātrāt parāmṛtaṃ 
śuddhisahitaṃ dadyāt | tataḥ sāmayiko'pi bhaktyā hastadvayena 
saṅgṛhya tadupari mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japtvā 
pūrvavadānandabhairavaṃ bhairavīñca santarpya gurūn devatāñca 
santarpayet | svasvakalpoktavidhinā bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt | 
tataścakranāyakastaiḥ saha pātravandanaṃ kuryāt yathā | 
śrīmadbhairavaśekharapraviṃlasaccandrāmṛtaplāvitaṃ 
kṣetrādhīśvarayoginīsuragaṇaiḥ siddhaiḥ samārādhitam | 
ānandārṇavakaṃ mahātmakamidaṃ sākṣāttrikhaṇḍāmṛtaṃ vande 
śrīprathamaṃ karāmbujagataṃ devaṃ viśuddhipradam || 
ityabhimantrya vāmahastena pātramuttolya anyonyavandanaṃ kṛtvā 
gṛhṇāmīti guruśaktisādhakeśvarāṇāmājñāṃ gṛhṇīyāt | te 
juṣasveti brūyuḥ || tato mūlādhārāt kuṇḍalinīṃ 
iṣṭadevatāsvarūpāṃ ājihvāntāṃ vibhāvya gurupādukāṃ 
smṛtvā śivo'hamiti vicintya hastābhyāṃ pātraṃ dhṛtvā 
mūlamuccaran kuṇḍalinīmukhe devīṃ tarpayet || 
etaduktamudayākarapaddhatyām || kṛtvā mantratanuṃ smaret 
gurupadaṃ devīṃ kalāṃ cinmayīṃ paścād vā tritayaṃ 
hṛdāsanavṛtaṃ dīpairyutaṃ kajjalaiḥ | puṣpādiṣvabhimantritañca 
niviḍaṃ manmohakadhvaṃsakaṃ ye sañcintya pibanti yānti khalu te 
bhuktiñca muktiṃ parām || 

tantrāntare ca || sindūratilakaṃ bhāle pāṇau ca madirāsavam | 
kṛtvā pibedguruṃ dhyāyaṃstathā devīñca cinmayīm | tataḥ 
pātramādhāropari saṃsthāpya punastena krameṇa parāmṛtaṃ 
gṛhītvā pātravandanaṃ kuryāt | yathā || madyaṃ mīnarasāvahaṃ 
yadi tayā dātuñca peyādibhiḥ kiñciccañcalaraktapaṅkajadṛśā 
tasyai samāveditam | vāme svātmaviśuddhikamalaṃ pāṇau 
vidhāyātmake vande pātramahaṃ dvitīyamadhunā 
nandaikasaṃvarddhanam || tataḥ pūrvavat pātraṃ svīkṛtya anyat

p. 1052) pātravandanaṃ kuryāt || yathā || 
sarvāmnāyakalākalāpakalitaṃ kautūhaladyotanaṃ 
candropendramahendraśambhuvaruṇabrahmādibhiḥ sevitam | dhyātaṃ 
devagaṇaiḥ paraṃ munigaṇairmokṣārthibhiḥ sarvadā vande 
pātramahaṃ tṛtīyamadhunā cātmāvabodhakṣamaḥ || 
tṛtīyapātrābhivandanaṃ kṛtvānyat pātravandanaṃ kuryāt || yathā || 
haimaṃ mīnarasāvahaṃ hariharabrahmādibhiḥ sevitaṃ 
mudrāmaithunadharmakarmanirataṃ kṣārāmlatiktāśrayam | 
ācāryāṣṭakasiddhibhairavakalāmāṃsena saṃśodhitaṃ pāyāt 
pañcamakāratattvasahitaṃ pātraṃ caturthaṃ namaḥ || iti 
caturthapātravandanaṃ kṛtvānyat pātravandanaṃ kuryāt yathā | 
ādhāre bhujagādhirājabalayepātraṃ mahīmaṇḍalaṃ madyaṃ 
saptasamudravāripiśitaṃ cāṣṭau ca digdantinaḥ | so'hañcaiva 
vibhāvayan pratidinaṃ tārāgaṇairakṣito'pyādityapramukhaiḥ 
surāsuragaṇairājñākaraiḥ || iti pañcamaṃ pātraṃ svīkuryāt | tato 
yāvaddṛṣṭyādikaṃ na calati tāvat pānādikaṃ kāryam | 

athāsya pramāṇaṃ yathā || rudrajāmale || sādhakebhyaśca 
śaktibhyo dadyānnirmālyacandanam | sāmayikaiḥ samaṃ kuryāddevi ! 
pānādibhakṣaṇam || anyatrāpi | niviśeccakrarūpeṇa paṃktyākāreṇa 
vā yathā | śaktiyukto vasedvāpi yugmayugmavidhānataḥ | 
śivaśaktidhiyā sarvaṃ cakramadhye samarpayet || tantrāntare ca || tataḥ 
puṣpaṃ samādāya guroḥ pātre nivedayet | gurave ca nivedyātha 
śaktyai dattvā svayaṃ haret || bhāvacūḍāmaṇau ca || sākṣād yadi 
gururna syāttadā toye visarjayet || 

atha pānapātraparimāṇaṃ yathā kulasāre || 
nayanāgnivāṇasaṅkhyakarṣaistu parameśvari ! | hetupātraṃ 
prakartavyamityuktaṃ kulasādhane | ito'pyadhikapātrantu na 
kartavyañca sādhakaiḥ | karṣaṃ laukikatolakamityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ
p. 1053) kulottame || guñjādvādaśamāṣaḥ syāttadaṣṭau karṣa ucyate 
|| athottaratantre || anujñāṃ purato labdhvā gṛhṇāmīti svayaṃ vadet | 
juṣasvetyabhyanujñāto guruṇā vā kulīnakaiḥ | gṛhṇiyācca svayaṃ 
siddho baddhapadmāsanaḥ sudhīḥ || 

kulārṇave || ekāsane niviṣṭā ye bhuñjīraṃścaiva bhājane | 
ekapātre pibantyevaṃ te yānti narakādhame | ekapātramiti 
sarvairmilitvā naikapātre pibet | na tu prativāraṃ dravyapāne 
bhinnabhinnapātraṃ kāryam anuṣṭhānalakṣaṇāpatteḥ 
sampradāyavirodhācca vacanāntaradarśanācca || svaśaktiṃ 
vīraśaktiṃ vā dīkṣitāṃ gurumagraṇīm | pāyayitvā pibeddravyamiti 
śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ | guruśaktiyutānāñca gurujyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhayoḥ | 
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhakṣayet strīṇāṃ tābhyo nocchiṣṭamarpayet | 
cakramadhye ca niyataṃ nānyathā patanaṃ bhavet | kaniṣṭhānāṃ 
svaśiṣyāṇāṃ dadyāt svocchiṣṭameva hi | dadyāt snehena 
yo'nyebhyaḥ sa bhavedāpadāṃ padam || anyatrāpi || śaktyucchiṣṭaṃ 
pibeddravyaṃ vīrocchiṣṭañca carvaṇam | śaktivīraprasādena kiṃ na 
sidhyati bhūtale ||

cakreśvareṇa svaśaktyucchiṣṭamapi peyam | tatpramāṇantu || 
śaktyucchiṣṭamavicārya pibeccakreśvaro yadi | ākalpaṃ narakaṃ 
bhuktvā viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate krimiriti | avicāryeti iyamabhiṣiktā 
nābhiṣiktā veti vicāramakṛtveti tu kaulāvalīkāraḥ || pītvā pītvā 
punaḥ pītvā punaḥ patati bhūtale | utthāya ca punaḥ pītvā punarjanma 
na vidyate | idañca vacanaṃ dhyānajapādyavasaraviṣayam | na tu 
paśusaṃsargaviṣayakañca | prakaṭe siddhihāniḥ syādityādiśravaṇāt 
| yāvanna calate dṛṣṭiryāvanna calate manaḥ | tāvat pānaṃ
p. 1054) prakartavyaṃ paśupānamataḥ param | iti vacanena 
dhyānādyapekṣitatvācca ||

tataḥ śāntistotraṃ paṭhet || taduktaṃ ḍāmare || pītvā 
svāmijanaiḥ sārdhaṃ śāntistotraṃ tataḥ paṭhet | naśyantu 
pretakuṣmāṇḍā naśyantu dūṣakā narāḥ | sādhakānāṃ śivāḥ 
santu āmnāyaparipālinām | jayanti mātaraḥ sarvā jayanti 
yoginīgaṇāḥ | jayanti siddhiḍākinyo jayanti gurupaṃktayaḥ | jayanti 
siddhiḍākinyo jayanti guravaḥ sadā | jayanti sādhakāḥ sarve 
viśuddhāḥ kaulikāśca ye | samayācārasampannā jayanti pūjakā 
narāḥ | nandantu aṇimāsiddhā nandantu kulapālakāḥ | indrādyā 
devatāḥ santu tṛpyantu vāstudevatāḥ | candrasūryādayo 
devāstṛpyantu mama bhaktitaḥ | nakṣatrāṇi grahā yogāḥ karaṇā 
rāśayeśca ye | sarve te sukhino yāntu sarpādanyāstu pakṣiṇaḥ | 
paśavasturagāścaiva parvatāḥ kandarā guhāḥ | ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāḥ 
sarve śāntiṃ kurvantu me sadā | stutā me viditāḥ santu 
siddhāstiṣṭhantu pūjakāḥ | ye ye pāpadhiyaḥ sudūṣaṇaratā 
mannindakāḥ pūjane vedācāravimardanaṣṭahṛdayā bhraṣṭāśca ye 
sādhakāḥ | dṛṣṭvā cakramapūrvamandahṛdayā ye kaulikā 
dūṣakāste te yāntu vināśamatra samaye śrībhairavasyājñayā || 
dveṣṭāraḥ sādhakānāñca sadaivāmnāyadūṣakāḥ | ḍākinīnāṃ 
mukhe yāntu tṛptāstatpiśitaistu te || ye vā śaktiparāyaṇāḥ śivaparā 
ye vaiṣṇavāḥ sādhavaḥ sarvasmādakhile surādhipamajaṃ sevyaṃ 
suraiḥ santatam | śaktiṃ viṣṇudhiyā śivañca sudhiyaḥ 
śrīkṛṣṇabuddhyā tu ye sevante tripurantvabhedamatayo gacchantu 
mokṣantu te || śatravo nāśamāyāntu mama nindākarāśca ye | 
dveṣṭāraḥ sādhakānāñca te naśyantu śivājñayā | tato 
yathāśakti vidhinā śivaśaktiyogaṃ kṛtvā devīpāde svamātmānaṃ 
p. 1055) samarpya śrīpātramuttolya devyā upari trirbhrāmayitvā punaḥ 
saṃsthāpya saṃhāramudrayā svahṛdi samānīya śrīdakṣiṇakālike ! 
kṣamasveti visṛjet || oṃ kālikāyaiḥ namaḥ ||

athānandastotram || manobhuvo maṅgalamūlamudrāṃ saundarya 
lakṣmīṃ purivejayantīm | śrīsundarīmindukalāvataṃsāṃ 
sānandamānandamayīṃ smarāmi || 1 || 
śrīsundarīpūjanatatparāṇāṃ hālābhirālohitalocanānām | 
asmākamānanditamānasānāṃ māheśvarāṇāṃ divasāḥ prayāntu 
|| 2 || vidhāya dhārāṃ vadane surāyāḥ śrīkṛṣṇamabhyarcya 
kulakrameṇa | āsvādayantaḥ piśitaṃ mṛgākṣīmāliṅgya mokṣaṃ 
sudhiyo labhante || 3 || dine dine siddhighaṭo'stu pūrṇo dine dine 
tarpaṇamastu devyāḥ | dine dine saṅghaṭatāṃ dvitīyāṃ dine dine 
sādhakasaṅgamo'stu || 4 || āsvādayantaḥ piśitasya 
khaṇḍamākaṇṭhapūrṇaṃ madirāṃ pibāmaḥ | 
vāmekṣaṇāsaṅgamamādadhānā bhuktiñca muktiñca parāṃ 
vrajāmaḥ || 5 || na svādulābhaḥ piśitasya yasmin pañcākṣarī 
pūrṇaratirna yatra | na yatra saṅgo mṛgalocanāyāstattaddinaṃ 
durdinameva manye || 6 || pravartakānāṃ sahasātha lakṣmīḥ prayāti 
dehaṃ supadakrameṇa | śrīsundarīsādhakanindakānāṃ 
prāṇādhamānīha layaṃ prayānti || 7 || aṅgeṣu kuṣṭhādi 
gṛheṣvalakṣmīrmūkatvamāsye bhavanaṃ śmaśānam | 
śrīsundarīsādhakanindakānāmāyuḥ kṣayaṃ gacchati tatkṣaṇena || 8 
|| śrīsundarīsādhakapuṅgavānāṃ yathā yathā ninditamātanoti | 
janaḥ samaṃ putrakalatramitraistathā tathā nāśamupaiti śīghram || 9 ||
hālāṃ piban dīkṣitamandireṣu svapanniśāyāṃ gaṇikāgṛheṣu | 
gṛhe gṛhe bhojanameva kurvan bambhramyate sādhakacakravartī || 10 ||
anantaraṃ kālavaśācca yo'haṃ so'haṃ bhaviṣyāmi na me vivādaḥ 
| śrīsundarīṃ janmani janmani tvāṃ yadbhairavo'haṃ jananīṃ 
smarāmi || 11 || vikalpavāṭītaṭasanniviṣṭe pravartamānāḥ paśavo

p. 1056) varākāḥ | praviśya mohāmbunidhāvagādhe 
bhramantyanāpāditakaulamārgāḥ || 12 || nānyaṃ viloketa ca 
vānyamīhe nānyaṃ smarannāparamāśrayāmi | kadāpi nāhaṃ 
paramātmarūpāṃ śrīsundarīṃ cetasi vismarāmi || 13 || 
vilokya sindūramayaṃ sudhābhiḥ śrīcakrarājaṃ niśi kḷptavantaḥ | 
śrīsundarīṃ cetasi cintayanto helāvilokairvaśayanti lokān || 14 || 
unmūlanaṃ pātakabhūruhāṇāmunmādanaṃ cittakutūhalānām | 
ākarṣaṇaṃ paṅkaruhekṣaṇānāmaireyapānaṃ vayamācarāmaḥ || 
15 || vārāṇasījahnusutāprayāgakedāratīrthāṇi vilokitāni | tenaiva 
manye jagato vṛtāni śrīsundarīcintanameva yasya || 16 || vāme 
candramukhī mukhe ca madhuraṃ pātraṃ karāmbhoruhe mūrdhni 
śrīgurucintanaṃ bhagavatīdhyānāspadaṃ mānase | jihvāyāṃ 
japasādhanaṃ pariṇatiḥ kaulāṅganācintanaṃ yat pātraṃ niyataṃ 
pibettadamṛtaṃ bhaktiñca muktipradam || 17 || vāme 
rāmāramaṇakuśalā dakṣiṇe pānapātraṃ cāgre nyastaṃ 
maricasahitaṃ śūkarasyoṣṇamāṃsam | skandhe vīṇā lalitasubhagā 
sadgurūṇāṃ prapañcaḥ kaulo dharmaḥ paramagahano 
yogināmapyagamyaḥ || 18 || yatrāsti bhogo na ca tatra mokṣo yatrāsti 
mokṣo na ca tatra bhogaḥ | śrīsundarīpūjanatatparāṇāṃ bhogaśca 
mokṣaśca karastha eva || 19 || ekena śuṣkacaṇakena ghaṭaṃ pibāmi 
kumbhaṃ pibāmi sahasā lavaṇādrakeṇa | āsvādayāmi yadi 
rohitamuṇḍakhaṇḍaṃ gaṅgāṃ pibāmi yamunāṃ saha sāgareṇa || 
20 || kare pātraṃ mukhe stotramānando hṛdayāmbuje | mūrdhni 
śrīnāthapādasya smaraṇaṃ kimataḥ param || 21 || pītvāṃ madyaṃ 
paṭhet stotraṃ sādhakaḥ kulabhairavaḥ | kulastrīsaṅganirataḥ 
kulakāryaṃ samācaret || 22 || alipiśitapurandhrīyāgapūjārato'haṃ 
bahuvidhakulamārgārambhasambhāvito'ham |

p. 1057) gurucaraṇarato'haṃ bhairavīmāśrito'haṃ 
paśujanavirato'haṃ bhairavo'haṃ śivo'ham || 23 || iti kulārṇave 
ānandastotraṃ samāptam ||

athānandollāsaḥ || kulārṇave pañcamakhaṇḍe saptamollāse || 
ārambhastaruṇaścaiva yauvanaṃ | prauḍha eva ca | tadante 
conmanāścaiva tattvollāsastataḥ param | samādhiriti 
vijñeyastattvollāsastu saptamaḥ | tattvatrayaṃ syādārambhaḥ kathitaṃ 
kulanāyike ! | kathitastaruṇollāso hyaruṇaṃ mukhamambike ! | 
yauvanaṃ manasaḥ samyagullāsaḥ kathitaḥ priye ! | skhalanaṃ 
dṛṅmanovācāṃ prauḍhamityabhidhīyate | samullāsapare cakreya 
icchet pātramelanam | ādau prauḍhasamullāsaṃ naiva kuryāt kadācana 
|| tathā || prauḍhollāsamadhikṛtya || tadārūḍheṣu vīreṣu 
kāryākāryaṃ na vidyate | icchaiva śāstrasampattirityājñā 
parameśvari ! | tatra yadyat kṛtaṃ karma śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham | 
tat sarvaṃ devatāprityai jāyate surasundari ! | jalpo japaphalaṃ tandrā 
samādhirabhidhīyate | vikriyā pūjanaṃ devi ! chardanaṃ bhairavo baliḥ 
| muktiḥ syācchaktisaṃyogāt stotraṃ tatkālabhāṣaṇam | 
nyāso'vayavasaṃsparśaḥ kaṇḍūtirhavanakriyā | vīkṣaṇaṃ 
dhyānamīśāni ! śayanaṃ vandanaṃ bhavet | tattvanyāse kṛtā nānā 
yā ceṣṭā sā ca tatkriyā | kāryākāryavicārantu yaḥ karoti sa pātakī | 
etaccakragatā vīrā vijñeyāḥ parayoginaḥ | yenāplāvanti manujāḥ 
sākṣādbhairavarūpatām | sammodakaparānandatarpaṇaṃ 
jñānavartanam | veṇuvīṇādivādyañca kavitāracanādikam | rodanaṃ 
bhāṣasampātaḥ samutthānaṃ vijṛmbhaṇam | gamanaṃ vikriyā devi ! 
yoga ityabhidhīyate | cakre'smin yoginīvīrayoginyo madamantharāḥ | 
samācaranti deveśi !  yathollāsaṃ manogatam || śanaiḥ pṛcchanti 
pārśvasthānāvismṛtyātmavīkṣitam | nidhāya vadane pātraṃ

p. 1058) nirvāṇā nivasanti ca | mattā svapuruṣaṃ matvā 
kāntānyamavalambate || tathaiva puruṣasyāpi 
prauḍho'ntollāsasaṃyutaḥ | puruṣaḥ puruṣaṃ 
mohādāliṅgatyaṅganājanam | pṛcchanti svapatiṃ mugdhā kastvaṃ 
kā tvamihāgatā | kiṃ kāryaṃ vayamāyātāḥ kimarthamiha 
saṃsthitāḥ | udyānaṃ kimidaṃ hantaḥ ! gṛhaṃ kiṃ vāgataṃ kimu ? 
| mukhe sampūrya madirāṃ pāyayanti striyo narān | upadaṃśamukhe 
kṣiptvā nikṣipanti priyānane | gṛhṇantyanyasya pātrāṇi vyañjanāni 
ca śāmbhavi ! | dhṛtvā śirasi nṛtyanti madyabhāṇḍāni yoginaḥ | 
ajñānāt karatālāntamaspaṣṭākṣaragītakam | 
praskhalatpadavinyāsaṃ tṛpyanti kulaśaktayaḥ | yogino madamattāśca 
patanti pramadorasi | madākulāśca yoginyaḥ patanti puruṣopari | 
manorathasukhaṃ pūrṇaṃ kurvanti ca parasparam | 
ityādivividhāśceṣṭāḥ kurvanti kulanāyike ! | vikṛtiṃ manaso hitvā 
yadullāsaḥ pravartate | madā tu devatābhāvaṃ bhajante 
yogipuṅgavāḥ | kaulikān bhairavāveśān yo vā nindati mūḍhadhīḥ | 
tattvāśayasya sandeho yoginyaḥ kulanāyikāḥ | na nindenna hasedvāpi 
cakramadhye madākulān | etaccakragatāṃ vārtāṃ vahirnaiva 
prakāśayet | tebhyaśca bhojanaṃ kuryāt nāhitañca samācaret | 
bhaktyā saṃrakṣayedetān gopayecca prayatnataḥ | cakre madākulān 
dṛṣṭvā cintayeddevatādhiyā | modate vandate bhaktyā sa gacched 
yoginīpadam | paśyedevaṃ vidhaṃ cakraṃ yo bhaktyā kaulikaḥ priye ! | 
vratatīrthatapodānayajñakoṭiphalaṃ labhet | unmādāt patanotthānāt 
mūrcchanā ca muhurmuhuḥ | unmattakṣetra ullāse ṣaṣṭhe 
vīrasamanvite | ciraṃ sannidadhāte tau yau vittaḥ parākṣarau | paraṃ 
brahmātmasaṃsthānakāṅkṣiṇaṃ kulanāyike ! | dehendriyāṇāṃ 
manasaścānavasthānnigadyate | samavasthābhidhā tasmin tattvollāse 
samaṃ priye ! | paramantrasvarūpo'sau jāyate mūrcchanā parā |
p. 1059) mūrcchanāsannikarṣo'pi nyūnaṃ mukteḥ paraṃ viduḥ | 
antarllakṣyo bahirdṛṣṭirnimeṣonmeṣavarjitaḥ | eṣā ca śāmbhavī 
mudrā sarvatantreṣu gopitā | eṣā ca khecarī mudrā śivasya 
samavāhinī | sarvottīrṇaḥ sadā hantā sāgarasya samākṛtiḥ | 
anayollāsinī vīrā śiva eva na saṃśayaḥ | na kiñcidapi jānanti 
ātmadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ | tadā matparamaṃ saukhyamiti vaktuṃ na 
śakyate | svayamevānubhavati śarkarākṣīrapānavat | īdṛśaṃ 
tādṛśaṃ saukhyamiti vaktuṃ na śakyate | dṛśyate kulakādeśca 
tadbrahmadhyānamuttamam | yat sukhaṃ vidyate dhyāne 
dehādeśakaraṃ param | kathituṃ naiva śaknomi prabuddhastu 
samāhitaḥ | brahmadhyānāmṛtānandaparāḥ sukṛtino narāḥ | 
kṣaṇe'pyantahite tasmin mocayanti hataprabhāḥ | 
saptamollāsayuktānāṃ tadbhaktānāṃ mahāphalam | aṣṭau 
trikālajñānotthāḥ pratyayāśca kuleśvari ! | aṣṭāvasthāśca 
kampādyā jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ | bahunātra kimuktena 
aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhayaḥ | pratīhārīpadaṃ prāptāḥ sevante munitāṃ 
ciram | ye guṇāḥ parameśasya pañcatattvatanoḥ śubhāḥ | te guṇāḥ 
kulatattvajñe ! tattvajñānāṃ samāhṛtāḥ | ārambhaḥ stavanollāso 
yauvanaṃ prauḍhameva ca | nandanti yogādityuktaśconmanāḥ svapna 
ucyate | anavasthā suṣuptiḥ syādavasthātrayasaṃyutāt | saptollāsaṃ 
yo hi vetti sa muktaḥ sa ca kaulikaḥ | pravṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve varṇā 
dvijātayaḥ | nivṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak | strī 
vātha puruṣaḥ ṣaṇḍaścāṇḍālo vā dvijātayaḥ | cakramadhye na 
bhedo'sti sarve devasamāḥ priye ! | nagarīnirjharādyambu gaṅgāṃ 
prāpya yathaikatām | yāti śrīcakramadhyeṣu caikatvaṃ 
mānavāstathā | kṣīreṇa sahitaṃ toyaṃ kṣīrameva yathā bhavet

p. 1060) | tathā śrīcakramadhye tu jātibhedo na vidyate | 
svargādipuṇyalokeṣu devādanyo yathā na hi | tathaiva cakramadhye tu 
devatāḥ sarvamānavāḥ | jātibhedo na cakra'smin sarve śivasamāḥ 
smṛtāḥ | vedopasthitamevaṃ hi sarvaṃ brahmeti cābravīt | bahunātra 
kimuktena cakramadhye kuleśvari ! | madrūpāḥ puruṣāḥ sarve 
tvadrūpā yoṣitaḥ priye ! | cakramadhye tu mūḍhātmā jātibhedaṃ 
karoti yaḥ | taṃ bhakṣayanti yoginyaḥ śapanti kulanāyike ! | 
strīṇāmanyatamaṃ sthānaṃ puṃsāmanyatamaṃ pṛthak | athavā 
mithunaṃ kṛtvā kramāttamupaveśayet | puṃstvākāreṇa vā samyak 
cakrākāreṇa vā priye ! | śivaśaktidhiyā sarvāścakramadhye 
samarcayet | avibhaktau yathācārau lakṣmīnārāyaṇau priye ! | yathā 
vāṇīsadānandau tathā vīraḥ saśaktikaḥ | madyakumbhasahasraiśca 
māṃsabhāraśatairapi | na tuṣyāmi varārohe ! bhagaliṅgāmṛtaṃ 
vinā na cakrāṅkaṃ na padmāṅkaṃ na vajrāṅkamidaṃ jagat | 
liṅgāṅkañca bhagāṅkañca tasmācchaktiśivātmakam | 
śivaśaktyātmasaṃyogo yasmin kāle prajāyate | sa 
sandhyākulaniṣṭhānāṃ samādhiḥ sa vidhīyate | kāmuko na striyaṃ 
gacchedanicchantīmadīkṣitām | sadyaḥ saṃskārasaṃśuddhāṃ 
vihitāṃ tāṃ vrajet striyam || iti tattvatrayollāsapānabhedābhicoditam | 
samāsena kuleśāni ! kimbhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi || ityānandollāsaḥ || 

atha pañcacakrānuṣṭhānam || yathā niruttaratantre 
daśamapaṭale ||

śrīśiva uvāca ||

sarvajātyudbhavā śaktiḥ yogibhiḥ pūjyate sadā | yāṃ yāṃ paśyati 
yogīndrastāṃ tāmeva prapūjayet | vīraśaktirviśeṣeṇa śṛṇuṣva | 
varavarṇini ! | puraścaryā kṛtā vīrā praśastā vīrasādhane | 
puraścaryāvihīnāścenna yojyāḥ kulasādhane ! | yojyāścet

p. 1061) siddhihāniḥ śyādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | vīraśaktiṃ vinā 
devi ! na kuryāt kulasādhanam | tadabhāve hīnajātau praśastā 
vīrasādhane | pañcacakre praśastā yāstāḥ śṛṇuṣva varānane ! | 
cakraṃ pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ tatra śaktiṃ prapūjayet | rājacakraṃ 
mahācakraṃ devacakraṃ tṛtīyakam | vīracakraṃ caturthañca 
paśucakrañca pañcamam | pañcacakre yajeddivye vīraśca kulasundari ! 
| brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca pañcacakre prapūjayet | balīyasi ca deveśi ! 
śivacakre prapūjayet | brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vīracakreṇa pūjayet | 
yogibhiḥ pūjyate devi ! pūrvacakreṣu kāminī || mātā ca bhaginī caiva 
duhitā ca snuṣā tathā | gurupatnī ca pañcaitā rājacakre prapūjayet | 
gauḍī vāpyathavā mādhvī surā śastā kuleśvari ! | 
śuddhicchāgodbhavā śastā tṛtīyoddālakastathā | mudrā 
godhūmajā śastā svayambhū kusumaṃ tathā | kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ 
dravyamanukalpaṃ niyojayet | raktacandanaṃ tathā śvetamanukalpañca 
candanam | vastrālaṅkārabhūṣādyairgandhamālyānulepanaiḥ | 
pūjayet parayā bhaktyā devatābhyo nivedayet | bhakṣyaṃ 
nānāvidhaṃ dravyaṃ nānāvastrasamanvitam | āsanaṃ 
śuddhisaṃyuktaṃ tābhyo dadyāt punaḥ punaḥ | praṇamya 
prajapenmantraṃ dṛṣṭvā tāśca sahasrakam | aṅgaṃ naiva 
spṛśettāsāṃ spṛśecca narakaṃ vrajet | madhumattā yadā tāstu na 
svapanti susampadaḥ | tattadevaṃ bhavet sarvaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi brahmaloke mahīyate | mātā bhaginī 
snuṣā kanyā vīrapatnī kuleśvari ! | mahācakre yajedetāḥ 
pañcaśaktīḥ punaḥ punaḥ | dravya dāne tu sampūjyā na śaktau 
śivayojanam | yojane siddhihāniḥ syādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 
mahāvyādhirbhaveddevi ! dhanahāniḥ prajāyate | sadaiva 
duḥkhamāpnoti sarvaṃ tasya vinaśyati | ādyañca gauḍikaṃ proktaṃ 
dvitīyaṃ kukkuṭodbhavaṃ | tṛtīyaṃ rohitaṃ proktaṃ caturthaṃ

p. 1062) śaśasambhavam | karavīrodbhavaṃ puṣpaṃ candanaṃ 
raktacandanam | pūjayet parayā bhaktyā śivaloke mahīyate | 
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi tatra devīṃ prapūjayet | aṣṭamyāñca 
caturdaśyāmamāyāñca kuje'hani | rājacakre mahācakre bhaktyā 
śaktiṃ prapūjayet | śuklapakṣe gurorvāre caturthīsaptamītithau | 
mahācakre yajedbhaktyā sarvakāmārthasiddhaye | devacakraṃ 
pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva varavarṇini ! | vidagdhāḥ sarvajātīnāṃ 
pañca kanyāḥ prapūjayet | gauḍikaṃ phaladaṃ ramyaṃ dvitīyaṃ 
pakṣisambhavam | tṛtīyaṃ śālamatsyantu caturthaṃ dhānyasambhavam 
| sugandhi sarvapuṣpañca devacakre niyojayet | devacakre yajecchaktiṃ 
devacakre mahīyate | ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi devakanyāṃ prapūjayet | 
pañcakanyā yajeccakre nātiriktāḥ kadācana | lobhādvā kāmato vāpi 
chalādvā varavarṇini ! | yadi syāt saṅgamastāsāṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ 
vrajet | aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi | pitṛbhūmīṃ 
samāgamya vīracakre prapūjayet | divyavīrānvito mantrī yajet śaktiṃ 
valīyasīm ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

mātrādayaḥ pañcakanyā yatīnāñca kathaṃ prabho ! ||

śrīśiva uvāca ||

mātrādayaḥ pañcakanyā hīnajātā yataḥ priye ! | 
caturvarṇodbhavāṃ veśyāṃ viśeṣeṇa balīyasīm | 
bhūmīndrakanyakā mātā duhitā rajakīsutā | śvapacī ca svasā 
jñeyā kāpālī ca snuṣā smṛtā | yoginī nijaśaktiḥ syāt 
pañcakanyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | guroḥ samīpe kartavyamathavā 
bhrātṛbhiḥ saha | siddhamantro bhavedvīro na vīro madyapānataḥ | 
abhiṣikto bhavedvīraḥ abhiṣiktā ca kaulikī | evañca vīraśaktiñca 
vīracakre niyojayet | kramasaṅketakañcaiva pūjāsaṅketameva ca | 
mantrasaṅketakañcaiva yantrasaṅketakantathā | likhanaṃ 
mantrayantrāṇāṃ saṅketaṃ gurumārgataḥ | saṅketajaṃ vinā vīraṃ 
yadi cakre niyojayet | niṣphalaṃ pūjanaṃ devi ! dukhaṃ tasya pade pade | 

p. 1063) saṅketahīno yo vīro nābhiṣekī gurukramāt | kūpe bhraṣṭaḥ 
sa pāpiṣṭhastaṃ tyajedvīracakrake | nābhiṣiktaṃ vāmacakre 
nābhiṣiktāṃ ca kaulikīm | niveśya rauravaṃ yāti satyaṃ satyaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ | evaṃ kramaṃ vinā devi ! vīracakre vasedyadi | 
siddhihāniḥ siddhihānī rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | sarvamadyaṃ 
sarvaśuddhiṃ sarvamīnaṃ kuleśvari ! | sarvamudrāṃ sarvapuṣpaṃ 
svayambhūkusumaṃ tathā || kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ dravyaṃ 
nānārasasamanvitam | pradadyāt sādhakaśreṣṭho vīracakre punaḥ 
punaḥ | svaśaktiṃ pūjayettatra taducchiṣṭaṃ pibet priye ! | carvyaṃ
