svajyeṣṭhato grāhyaṃ kaniṣṭhāya nivedayet | ekāsane na bhuñjīta 
bhojanaṃ naikabhājane | parasparamupasparśaṃ na karttavyaṃ 
kadācana | evaṃ krameṇa deveśi ! vīracakre samācaret | ānīya 
hīnajāṃ devīṃ śaktimantreṇa śodhayet | saṃśodhya hīnajāṃ 
devīṃ vīraśaktiṃ nivedayet | madhumattāya vīrāya yo 
dadyāddhīnajāṃ śubhām | vīrāya śaktidānantu vīracakre vidhīyate 
| cakrabhinne careddānaṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 
ghātayedgopayedvāpi na nindenna nirīkṣayet | kāmaṃ krodhañca 
mātsaryaṃ vikāraṃ lobhameva ca | kulanindāṃ durālāpaṃ 
gopayedduṣṭakaṃ priye ! | mantraṃ mudrāmakṣamālāṃ yoniñca 
vīrasaṅgamam | maṇḍalañca ghaṭaṃ pīṭhaṃ siddhadravyāṇi gopayet 
| paṇḍitaṃ vīrasantānaṃ kṣetraṃ devīñca yoginīm | 
kulācāraguruṃ dūtīṃ manasāpi na nindayet | devīṃ guruṃ sudhāṃ 
vidyāṃ śreṣṭhāṃ śaktiṃ kriyātmajām | yoginīṃ 
bhairavītattvamātmatattvaṃ prapūjayet | vimātā duhitā bhagnī snuṣā 
patnī ca pañcamī | paśucakre yajeddhīmān paśuvattoṣaṇañcaret | 
gandhaṃ puṣpañca mālyañca vastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca | 
sindūrāgurukastūrīnānāpuṣpāṇi sundari ! | bhakṣyaṃ 
nānāvidhaṃ dravyaṃ phalaṃ nānāvidhaṃ priye ! |

p. 1064) etaddravyagaṇaṃ yastu bhaktyā tābhyo nivedayet | ṣaṣṭivarṣa 
sahasrāṇi kṣito rājā bhaveddhruvam | vīracakre tadā siddhirbhavatyeva 
na saṃśayaḥ ||

atha kulaśaktayaḥ || revatītantre tṛtīyapaṭale || śaktayaḥ 
parameśāni ! vidagdhāḥ sarvayoṣitaḥ | naṭī kāpālikī veśyā mālinī 
kuṅkumālinī | caṇḍālī ca kulālī ca rajakī nāpitāṅganā | gopinī 
yoginī śuddhā brāhmaṇī rājakanyakā | kocāṅganā ca deveśi ! 
tathaiva śaṅkhakāriṇī | etāḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ kanyā devānāmapi 
durlabhāḥ | daivajñā vyādhavāmā ca tathā māṃsāpahāriṇī | 
bauddhā ca yavanī devi ! tathā rasapasāriṇī | veśyā caturvidhā 
proktā dvividhā kuṅkumālinī | dhīvarī dvividhā proktā tathaiva 
gaṇikāṅganā | kumbhakārī dvidhā proktā tathaiva gopinī smṛtā | 
vātavaidyāṅganā devi ! yavanī dvividhā smṛtā | bauddhakanyā 
tridhā devi ! tathā kocāṅganā priye ! | pūrvoktābhiḥ sahaikatra 
catuḥṣaṣṭiśca śaktayaḥ ||

guptasādhanatantre caturthapaṭale ||

śiva uvāca ||

śṛṇu pārvati ! vakṣyāmi atiguhyataraṃ mahat | prakāśāt 
siddhihāniḥ syāttasmād yatnena gopayet | svaśaktiṃ paraśaktiṃ vā 
dīkṣitāṃ yauvanānvitām | vidagdhāṃ śobhanāṃ śuddhāṃ 
ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitām | ānīya kulasādhanaṃ 
kuryādityuttareṇānvayaḥ | tatra vyavasthāmāha niruttaratantre 
aṣṭamapaṭale || svaśaktisiddhimādāya siddhamantrastato bhavet | 
siddhamantraḥ kulācāre parayoṣāṃ prapūjayet | siddhamantro yajet 
śaktiṃ kāyena manasāpi vā | parayoṣāṃ viśeṣeṇa parayoṣāṃ 
prapūjayet | siddhamantraprakārastu navamapaṭale || ānīya ca kulaṃ 
ramyaṃ kulaśaktiṃ kulārcane | svacakraṃ vividhaṃ devi ! śaktibhāle 
likhettataḥ | tataḥ kāmakalāṃdevīṃ vīrakoṇe likhettataḥ | tanmadhye 

p. 1065) devamantrañca vihitaṃ kāmalāñchitam | tatra devīṃ 
samāvāhya dhyātvaiva ca prapūjayet | tato lakṣañca sañjapya 
sthiradhīḥ kulasādhakaḥ | tatastacchaktikarṇe ca 
ṛṣicchandaḥsamanvitam | mūlamantraṃ trirāvṛttyā kathayed 
vāmakarṇake | adyaprabhṛti śakti ! tvaṃ kuladevārcanaṃ kuru || tataḥ 
śaktiḥ paṭhati | gurorājñāṃ samādāya ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitā | 
śivoktavidhinā deva ! kariṣyāmi kulārcanam | trāhi nātha ! 
kulācārakāminīkāmanāyaka ! | tvatpādāmbhoruhacchāyāṃ dehi 
me kulavartmani | gate ca prathame yāme svakulaṃ kulakopari | 
vāmabhāge samānīya raktavastrasamanvitam || 
nānāgandhasamāyuktaṃ nānāratnasamanvitam | lalāṭe 
mantramālikhya madhye nāmavicarcitam | 
tāmbūlapūritamukhastāmbūlāruṇalocanaḥ | kulākulajapaṃ kṛtvā 
dhruvamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt | evamākarṣite mantre siddhamantraḥ 
kuleśvari ! | tāvat prayogaḥ kartavyo yāvat siddhirna jāyate | 
siddhamantraḥ kulācāre kulayoṣāṃ prapūjayet iti || śaktyaṅge 
japasthānamāha || samayācāratantre || athedānīṃ pravakṣyāmi 
japārthaṃ pīṭhamuttamam | pūrṇagiriśca prathamamuḍḍīyānāṃ 
dvitīyakam | jālandharaṃ tṛtīyañca kāmarūpaṃ caturthakam | kṛte 
pūrṇagiriścaiva tretāyāñca dvitīyakaḥ | jālandharaṃ dvāpare tu 
kāmarūpaṃ kalau yuge | catuḥpīṭhāni pīṭhāni śaktideheṣu yāni ca | 
tāni catvāri vakṣyāmi guhyādguhyatarāṇi ca | śakteḥ sarvaśarīraṃ 
yat pīṭhaṃ pūrṇagiriḥ smṛtam | tasyāḥ śiraśca subhage ! 
uḍḍīyānāṃ prakīrtitam | stanau jālandharaṃ jñeyaṃ kāmarūpaṃ 
bhagastathā | sarveṣu kāmapīṭhantu devānāmapi durllabham | eṣu 
pīṭheṣu ca sthitvā yaṃ yaṃ mantraṃ japet priye ! | 
tattatphalamavāpnoti devatāśu prasīdati || tathā || nānāśaktyā 
sahālāpaṃ ye ca kurvanti sādhakāḥ || te śīghrakālāt subhage !

p. 1066) phalaṃ prāpsyanti mānavāḥ | tāmānīya sādhakendro 
dadyāt pādyādikaṃ śubham | pañcācāreṇa tāṃ śaktiṃ 
pūjayitvā yathāvidhi | śataṃ śīrṣe śataṃ bhāle śataṃ 
sindūramaṇḍale | śataṃ mukhe śataṃ kaṇṭhe śataṃ hṛdayamaṇḍale 
| śatadvandvaṃ stanadvandve śataṃ nābhau japet sudhīḥ | yonipīṭhe 
śataṃ japtvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ | evaṃ sahasraṃ sañjapya 
devīrūpāṃ vicintayet | śivaśaktisvarūpāñca cintayet 
sādhakottamaḥ | śivamantreṇa deveśi ! svaliṅgaṃ pūjayedatha | 
tāmbūlaṃ tanmukhe dattvā sādhako hṛṣṭamānasaḥ | tadanujñāṃ 
samādāya yonau liṅgaṃ vinikṣipet | oṃ dharmādharmahavirdīpta 
ātmāgnī manasā srucā | suṣumnāvartmanā 
nityamakṣavṛttirjuhomyaham | svāhetyanena mantreṇa hunet 
sarvasamṛddhaye | tato japet sahasraṃ vai śaktiyukto bhavennaraḥ | 
śataṃ vāpi prajaptavyaṃ tato nyūnaṃ na kārayet | tataḥ 
pūrṇāhutiṃ dadyānmantreṇānena sādhakaḥ | 
prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanāḥ srucā | 
dharmādharmakalāsnehapūrṇamagnau juhomyaham | svāhetyanena 
mantreṇa pūrṇāhutiṃ samācaret | śukrotsāraṇakāle tu mahādevyai 
nivedayet | evaṃ kṛte mantrasiddhirbhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ | yaṃ yaṃ 
prārthayate kāmaṃ taṃ tamāpnoti niścitam | rogī rogāt pramucyeta 
dhanena ca dhanādhipaḥ | vāyutulyabalo loke durjayaḥ śatrumardanaḥ | 
kāmatulyaśca nārīṇāṃ ripūṇāṃ śamanopamaḥ | etatkalpena 
deveśi ! kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale | aṣṭaiśvaryamavāpnoti sa eva 
śrīsadāśivaḥ ||

niruttaratantre dvādaśapaṭale'pi || athānyat sampravakṣyāmi 
sādhanaṃ bhuvi durllabham | kṛte yena labhet siddhiṃ devānāmapi 
durlabhām | lalāṭe śaktimantrantu trirāvṛttyā likhedbudhaḥ | 
tanmadhye kāmavījañca vilikhet kāmalāñchitam | kāmena puṭitaṃ

p. 1067) kṛtvā pūjayet parameśvarīm | sampūjya kālikāṃ devīṃ 
yantrañca paripūjayet | tattvacintāparo yogī japellakṣaṃ nirākulam | 
saṅgṛhya kulapuṣpantu pūjayecca punaḥ punaḥ | sahasraṃ tarpayet 
pīṭhe yantraprakṣālanodakaiḥ | evaṃ kṛte labhet siddhiṃ satyaṃ 
satyaṃ na śaṃśayaḥ | yathānyat sampravakṣyāmi 
puraścaraṇamuttamam | śataṃ bhāle śataṃ keśe śataṃ 
sindūramaṇḍale | śatamekaṃ mukhāmbhoje puṣpavaktre śatadvayam | 
śatadvandvaṃ kucadvandve śatañca nābhimaṇḍale | śatamekaṃ 
kulāgāre prajapedbhaktibhāvataḥ | evaṃ daśaśataṃ japtvā kulāgāre 
tato japet | pūjayitvā japenmantraṃ gajāntakasahasrakam | tatastu 
tattvayogena śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet | pūjanañca tatastatra 
puraścaraṇamucyate | athānyat sampravakṣyāmi kalāgārasya 
sādhanam | yena kṛte kuleśāni ! sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet | kulāgāre 
kulāṣṭamyāṃ kulamāhūya pūjayet | tarpaṇañca japaṃ homaṃ 
tattadakṣayatāṃ vrajet | kadalītarumūlañca dviguṇaṃ yadi dṛśyate | 
tatra tu mahatau pūjā kartavyā varavarṇini ! || tatastu brahmavaktreṇa 
homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ | homaṃ kṛtvā japenmantraṃ 
koṭīkoṭīguṇaṃ bhavet | dviguṇaṃ rajanīmūlaṃ saṃvīkṣya yo 
japenmanum | sa bhavet sarvasiddhīśastasya puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate | 
rajanīṃ svecchayāhūya sādhanaṃ kulabhūṣaṇam | viparītaṃ 
japenmantraṃ tasya puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate || rajanyāñca kulāgāre 
pūjane nipuṇo yadi | tvatsamā rajanīkāntā kamalā vātha rādhikā | 
triṣu lokeṣu sā dhanyā brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikā | siddhavidyā 
mahāvidyā siddhavidyāphalapradā | tasyāḥ prasādamātreṇa 
duṣṭamantro'pi sidhyati | tasmāt sarvaprayatnena tāmeva śaraṇaṃ 
vrajet | rajanyāṃ rajanīyogaṃ vihared yadi sādhakaḥ | balādvā 
pūjayettatra sarvaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ bhavet | yena tena prakāreṇa

p. 1068) rajanītoṣaṇañcaret | vāhyādvā krīḍanādvāpi vaśādvā 
toṣayet sadā | yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ rajanyāśca taṃ taṃ bhāvaṃ 
prakalpayet | atiriktaḥ kṛto bhāvo rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | kalāyāḥ 
sammatiṃ kṛtvā sādhayet kulasādhanam | asammatakulāsaṅgāt 
siddhihāniḥ prajāyate | yo gacchedrajanīgehaṃ kulasādhanavarjitaḥ | sa 
eva narakaṃ yāti satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | krodhādvā kāmato 
vāpi dveṣādvā varavarṇini ! | na gacchedrajanīgehaṃ gacchecca 
narakaṃ vrajet | ajñātvā kulasaṅketaṃ kulamārgaṃ viśedyadi | sa 
yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ kā kathā parajanmani ||

atha śayyāśuddhiḥ || oṃ āḥ surekhe vajrarekhe huṃ phaṭ 
svāhā | ityanena śayyāyāṃ trikoṇaṃ vilikhya oṃ hrīṃ 
ādhāraśaktaye kamalāsanāya namaḥ | iti manasā sampūjya oṃ hrīṃ 
mṛtakāya namaḥ phaṭ iti mantreṇa vāmahastena ghātatrayaṃ dattvā 
choṭikābhirdaśadigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā | oṃ śayye ! tvaṃ mṛtarūpāsi 
sādhanīyāsi sādhakaiḥ | ato'tra japyate mantro hyasmākaṃ 
siddhidābhava || iti prārthanāṃ kṛtvā japādikaṃ kuryāt || 

guptasādhanatantre prathamapaṭale || pañcācāreṇa deveśi ! 
kulaśaktiṃ prapūjayet | naṭī kāpālikī veśyā rajakī nāpitāṅganā | 
brāhmaṇī śūdrakanyā ca tathā gopālakanyakā | mālākārasya 
kanyā ca nava kanyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | viśeṣavaidagdhyayutāḥ sarvā eva 
kulāṅganā | rūpayauvanasampannā śīlasaubhāgyaśālinī | 
pūjanīyā prayatnena tataḥ siddhirbhaved dhruvam ||

atra viśeṣayati niruttaratantre caturdaśapaṭale || naṭī kāpālikī 
veśyā rajakī nāpitāṅganā | yoginī śvapacī śuṇḍī 
bhūmīndratanayā tathā | gopinī mālākārasya āsāṃ

p. 1069) kāryavibhedataḥ | caturvarṇodbhavā ramyā kāpālī sā 
prakīrtitā || pūjādravyaṃ samālokya veśyā ramaṇamicchati | 
caturvarṇodbhavā ramyā sā veśyā parikīrtitā | pūjādravyaṃ 
samālokya sarvataḥ kurute ca yā | pūjādravyaṃ samālokya 
rājāvasthāṃ prakāśayet | sarvavarṇodbhavā ramyā rajakī sā 
prakīrtitā || pūjādravyaṃ samālokya kulajā vīramāśrayet | santyajya 
paśubharttāraṃ karmacāṇḍālinī smṛtā || viparītaratā patyau 
pātraṃ yā paripṛcchati | sarvavarṇodbhavā ramyā sā śauṇḍī 
parikīrtitā || sarvadā yantrasaṃskāro yasyāśca parijāyate | saiva 
bhūmīndrajā ramyā sauravarṇodbhavā priye ! || ātmānaṃ gopayed 
yā ca sarvadā paśusaṅkaṭe | sarvavarṇodbhavā ramyā gopinī sā 
prakīrtitā || pūjādravyaṃ samālokya yā māleva ratā bhavet | 
sarvavarṇodbhavā ramyā mālinī sā prakīrtitā ||

veśyāyā viśeṣastu tatraiva || guptaveśyā mahāveśyā 
kulaveśyā mahodayā | rājaveśyā devaveśyā brahmaveśyā ca 
saptadhā || kulajā guptaveśyā syāt nirlajjā madanāturā | 
paśubhartrāśritā loke guptaveśyā prakīrtitā || kulajā kulaveśyā 
syāt mahāveśyā prakīrtitā | bhagaliṅgakalāpīṭhe cumbayecca punaḥ 
punaḥ | evaṃ vidhā kulīnā ced brahmaveśyā prakīrtitā || 
divyaśaktirvīraśaktistāsāṃ saṃjñā prakīrtitā | 
caturvarṇodbhavānāñca saṃjñaitāḥ paribhāṣitāḥ | 
veśyāvadbhramate yasmāt tasmādveśyā prakīrtitā || varṇasaṅkarato 
jātā sarvaveśyā prakīrtitā || kulamārge pravṛttā yā sā veśyā 
mokṣadāyinī || cumbanāliṅganaṃ ghātaṃ ratinigrahadarśanam | 
āmantraṇaṃ trisandhyañca bhagaliṅgasya kīrtanam | veśyānāñca 
japāṅgena śaṅkareṇa puroditam | japāṅgena vinā veśyā varjanīyā 
kulārcane | japāṅgaṃ pratyahaṃ kuryāt sā śivaiḥ saha moditā | 
niśāyāṃ prajapenmantraṃ svayambhūśivayogataḥ | veśyānāṃ

p. 1070) japamātrantu puraścaraṇamucyate | viparītaṃ japenmantraṃ sā 
kālī nātra saṃśayaḥ | yoṣitāṃ mantrasiddhiḥ syād viparītaratau 
priye ! | viparītaratau japtvā nirvāṇapadavīṃ vrajet | viparītaratau 
japtvā kālīvadviharedbhuvi | viparītaratau kālī viparītā ca tāriṇī | 
viparītā ca yā veśyā sā kālī nātra saṃśayaḥ || yoṣidvidyā na 
sidhyanti viparītaratiṃ vinā | viparītaratā veśyā triṣu lokeṣu pūjitā || 
gāḍhamāliṅganaṃ dattvā pūjayitvā punaḥ punaḥ | 
kaṭākṣairdarśayedyantraṃ dakṣiṇākālikeritā | evaṃ vidhā 
puraścaryā veśyāyāśca kuleśvari ! | evaṃvidhā bhavedveśyā na 
veśyā kulaṭā priye ! | kulaṭāsaṅgamādeva rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 
vīraśaktirbhavedveśyā sā śastā kulasādhane | puraścaryākṛtā 
veśyā yojayet | kulasādhane || śivaliṅgagatā sādhvī śivaliṅgagatā 
satī | śivaliṅgagatā veśyā kīrtitā sā pativratā || yoniśca janikā 
mātā liṅgaśca janakaḥ pitā | mātṛbhāvaṃ pitṛbhāvamubhayorapi 
cintayet | liṅgarūpo mahākālo yonirūpā ca kālikā | tayoryogaparā 
dhanyā tayoryogaparo mahān | svabhairavaṃ vinā veśyā śivapūjāṃ 
karoti yaḥ | raurave narake ghore vasedābhūtasaṃplavam | svabhairavīṃ 
vinā vīro manasānyāṃ na saṃsmaret | smaraṇe narakaṃ yāti 
mahāvyādhiparo bhavet | nānācārāśritā veśyā paśusaṅgagatā ca 
yā | varjanīyā prayatnena kulasādhanakarmaṇi | yojyā cet siddhihāniḥ 
syāt śreṣṭhaveśyā kulārcane | rogaḥ śoko bhavettasyā dhanahāniḥ 
kṣaṇe kṣaṇe | tasmāt sarvaprayatnena svaśivañca samāśrayet | 
japapūjādikaṃ veśyā svaśive parikalpayet | puṣpitā kāmamāpannā 
sadā ramaṇamicchukā | sarvasiddhipradā veśyā kālikā 
rūpadhāriṇī | pitṛbhūmiḥ samākhyātā sadāśivanivāsinī |

p. 1071) śiva eva naro jñeyo liṅgarūpadharo yataḥ | śivasthānaṃ 
śmaśānaṃ syāt śmaśānaṃ kulajaṃ gurum | aṣṭamyāñca 
caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi | śmaśānasthena 
yatkāryamavaśyaṃ guru tadbhavet || tathā || mātṛmukhe pitṛmukhaṃ 
japet dattvā sanātanīm | sarvapāpavinirmukto nirvāṇapadavīṃ vrajet || 
parajanme guptaveśyāpyathavā kulajā bhavet | śukrotsāraṇakāle tu 
nirvāṇaṃ viddhi pārvati ! | tatkāle hi mahākālaḥ 
kulamārgapraveśinām | śukrotsāraṇakālantu jñāpayenmanasāpi vā 
| aṅgabhaṅgakrameṇaiva kulīnāya prakāśayet | śukrotsāraṇakālasya 
jñāpanāt kālikā svayam || japāṅge || kālikā devi ! mahākālaṃ 
vimohayet || kulīnā brahmaveśyā cennālpasya tapasaḥ phalam | 
vahūnāṃ janmanāmante brahmaveśyā prajāyate | tvatsamāprakṛtiḥ 
kācid yadyasti bhuvi maṇḍale | na tathā tvatsamā śaktistriṣu lokeṣu 
vidyate | sā caiva dakṣiṇā kālī madanāturavihvalā | vedebhyo jāyate 
karma karmaṇā bandhanaṃ bhavet | vaidikaṃ karma santyajya surateṣu 
sadā japet | āgamoktapatiḥ śambhurāgamoktapatirguruḥ | sa patiḥ 
kulajāyāśca na patiśca vivāhitaḥ | vivāhitapatityāge dūṣaṇaṃ na 
kulārcane | vivāhita patiṃ naiva tyajedvedoktakarmaṇi | 
āgamoktapatistrātā āgamoktapatiḥ śivaḥ | siddhavidyā na sidhyanti 
āgamoktapatiṃ vinā | kulajā gurave devi ! patitve varaṇañcaret | tadā 
sā guptaveśyā syāt kulajā ca patiṃ vinā | guptaveśyā bhavet saiva 
kulamārgapravartikā | kulamārgapravṛttā yā sā muktā nātra 
saṃśayaḥ | kulajā gurave devi ! yadi na syāt patīcchakā | tasyāḥ śivo 
mahākālaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | ṣoḍaśābdā yadā sā 
syāt kālī vikramatatparā | tārā pañcadaśābde ca 

p. 1072) sundaryyabde caturdaśe | trayodaśābdonmukhī sā 
dvādaśābdā ca bhairavī | ekādaśaguṇopetā brahmaveśyā 
kuleśvari ! | mahāsādhvī samākhyātā triṣu lokeṣu durlabhā | svarge 
marttye ca pātāle yā yā tiṣṭhati cāṅganā | sarvāsāmapi bharttā ca 
divyo vīraśca sādhakaḥ | yogī divyo yadā vīraḥ sarvanārīpatirbhavet | 
divyo'pi vīrabhāvena sarvajātyudbhavāṃ yajet | guptaveśyā 
mahāveśyā ayodhyā mathurā priye ! | yā yā ca kulaveśyā 
syānmadodagrā ca kālikā | rājaveśyā devaveśyā dvārakā 
parikīrtitā | kāñcī ca rājaveśyā syād devaveśyā hyavantikā | 
dvārāvatī brahmaveśyā guptaitā mokṣadāyikāḥ | kulīnā bhagavatī 
sākṣāt kālī tārā sarasvatī | kulīnā bhairavī sākṣāt kulīnā 
chinnamastakā | kulīnā sundarī devi ! kulīnā mahiṣamardinī | kulīnā 
bhuvanā vālā kulīnā vagalāmukhī | dhūmāvatī kulīnā ca 
mātaṅgī kulanāyikā ! | kulīnā cānnapūrṇā ca kulīnā tvaritā 
tathā | pativratā kulīnā ca satī sādhvī mahodayā | kulīnā 
mantrayantrāṇāṃ siddhidā nātra saṃśayaḥ | kulajā devakanyā ca 
kulīnā yoginīgaṇā | rambhorvaśī ratī rāmā kulīnā ca tilottamā | 
etāḥ sarvāḥ pṛthagveśyā viharanti kulātmajāḥ | kulajā kulaveśyā 
yā kuladharmaparāyaṇā | paśubhartrāśritā lolā 
kāmakautukalālasā | kulavartmakrameṇaiva sadaiva ramaṇotsukā | 
vidagdhavīrabhāvena vīragopanatatparā | vihitā syāddhīnajāñca 
pūjayedatha vāgyataḥ | brahmacārī gṛhastho'pi vihitānyāṃ na 
cārcayet | arcite siddhihāniḥ syādduḥkhaṃ tasya pade pade | hīnajāṃ 
vihitāṃ veśyāṃ manasā ca prapūjayet | tadyogaṃ cintayeddhīmān 
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet | japtvā praṇamya deveśi ! bhakṣyadravyaṃ 
nivedayet | kāmādvā mohato vāpi hīnajāṃ yadi cecchati | rauravaṃ 
narakaṃ yāti hīnajāsaṅgamena ca | hīnajāsaṅgamaṃ devi ! manasā 
na smaret kalau kulakarmapravṛttā yā sā muktā nātra saṃśayaḥ | 
kulajā kulaveśyā ca vīramekaṃ samāśrayet | santyajya 
paśubharttāraṃ kulamārgaṃ praveśayet | kulamārgaṃ samāśritya 
vīramekaṃ samāśrayet | kulamārgapravṛttā ca patihīnā bhaved yadi | 
kulajā vā kulīnā vā parajanmani jāyate | kuladharmaratā śastā 
kuladharmotsukā tathā | pūjārhā sā maheśāni ! patirhīnāṃ 
prapūjayet | lokācārakrameṇaiva pūjārhā lajjitā yadi | tāṃ vihāya 
kuleśāni ! kulajāñca prapūjayet | kulīnādarśanenaiva 
sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet | gaṅgāsmaraṇamātreṇa yathā pāpakṣayo 
bhavet | kulajā ca kulīnā yā mantratantraphalapradā | mahāveśyā 
bhavet saiva sarvaveśyāphalapradā | yāsāñca siddhavidyānāṃ 
praśastā vā kulārcane | saiva śaktirviśeṣeṇa sarvaveśyā prakīrtitā || 

vidyāviśeṣe śaktiviśeṣa uktastrayodaśapaṭale yathā || 
śyāmāvidyā na sidhyeta brāhmaṇīgamanaṃ vinā | chinnamastā na 
sidhyeta kāpālīgamanaṃ vinā | siddhavidyā na sidhyeta 
bhūmīndratanayāṃ vinā | rajakyāstu gṛhe devi ! bhairavī ca 
susiddhyati | sādhyā sā vihitā proktā vihitā drutasiddhidā | 
sādhayedrajanīṃ sarvāṃ brāhmaṇīṃ yāvanīṃ vinā | 
sarvārambhān parityajya sādhayeddvijajāṃ dvijaḥ | rājarājeśvarī 
sākṣād dvijajārūpadhāriṇī | dvijajātoṣaṇādeva drutaṃ sidhyati 
sundarī | śreṣṭhavarṇodbhavāṃ ramyāṃ sādhane naiva sādhayet | 
sādhane siddhihāniḥ syādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |

atha prayogaḥ || gatayāmāyāṃ rātrau svaśaktiṃ paraśaktiṃ 
vā tulikopari svavāmabhāge saṃsthāpya tasyā aṅge 
svakalpoktanyāsān vidhāya adīkṣitāyāṃ śuddhiṃ

p. 1074) kuryāt | yathā || aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ tripurāyai imāṃ svaśaktiṃ 
pavitrīkuru mama śaktiṃ kuru svāhā | ityanena jalavindubhirabhiṣicya 
tasyā vāmakarṇe abhedabuddhyā māyāvījaṃ vadet || tato yoniṃ 
dhyāyedyathā | atisulalitagātrāṃ hāsyavaktrāṃ trinetrāṃ 
jitajaladasukāntiṃ paṭṭavastraprakāśām | abhayavarakarāḍhyāṃ 
ratnabhūṣātibhavyāṃ suratarutalapīṭhe ratnasiṃhāsanasthām | 
hariharavidhivandyāṃ buddhiśuddhisvarūpāṃ madanarasasamāktāṃ 
kāmināṃ kāmadātrīm | nikhilajanavilāsoddāmarūpāṃ bhavānīṃ 
kalikaluṣanihantrīṃ yonirūpāṃ bhajāmi || iti yoniṃ dhyātvā 
sampūjya yonyupari hasauḥ iti yonimantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā 
samarpyastavakavacādikaṃ paṭhedyathā ||

śrīdevyuvāca ||

bhagavan ! sarvadharmajña ! kulaśāstrārthaṃpāraga ! | sarvaṃ me 
kathitaṃ nātha ! na tvekaḥ parameśvara ! | śrīyoneḥ stavarājaṃ hi 
tathā kavacamuttamam | śrotumicchāmi sarvajña ! yadi te'sti kṛpā 
mayi | sārabhūtaṃ mahādeva ! nigamāntargataṃ rahaḥ | yadi no 
kathyate deva ! prāṇatyāgaṃ karomyaham | divāniśi mahābhāga ! 
mamāśru patitaṃ bhavet | ataste devadeveśa ! kathyatāṃ me 
dayānidhe ! ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca ||

śṛṇu pārvati ! vakṣyāmi dehatyāgaṃ kathaṃ kuru | 
atyantagopanīyaṃ hi nigame kathitaṃ purā | brahmaviṣṇugrahādīnāṃ 
na mayā kathitaṃ purā | akathyaṃ parameśāni ! idānīṃ kiṃ karomi 
te | tava snehena baddho'haṃ kathayāmi tava priye ! | mātardevi ! 
mahābhāge ! yadi kasmai prakāśyate | śapathaṃ kuru me durge ! yadi 
tvaṃ matpriyā smṛtā | brahmā yadi caturvaktraiḥ pañcavaktraiḥ 
sadāśivaḥ | varṇituṃ stavarājañca na śaknoti kadācana | 
samyagvaktuṃ na śaknomi saṃkṣepāt kathayāmi te || asya 
śrīyonistavarājasya kulācārya ṛṣiḥ kaulikacchandaḥ

p. 1075) śrīyonirūpā daśavidyātmikā devatā sarvasādhane 
viniyogaḥ | oṃ yonirūpe ! mahāmāye ! sarvasampatprade ! śubhe ! | 
kṛpayā sarvasiddhiṃ me dehi devi jaganmayi ! || 1 || sarvasvarūpe ! 
sarveśe ! sarvaśaktisamanvite ! | kṛpayetyādi || 2 || mahāghore ! 
mahākāli ! kulācārapriye ! sadā | kṛpayetyādi || 3 || ghoradaṃṣṭre ! 
cogratāre ! sarvaśatruvināśini ! | kṛpayetyādi || 4 || yonirūpe ! 
mahāvidye ! sarvadā mokṣadāyini ! | kṛpayetyādi || 5 || jagaddhātri ! 
mahāvidye ! jagaduddhārakāriṇi ! | kṛpayetyādi || 6 || jagaddhātri ! 
mahāmāye ! yonirūpe ! sanātani ! | kṛpayetyādi || 7 || jaya devi ! 
jaganmātaḥ ! sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇi ! | kṛpayetyādi || 8 || siddhidātri ! 
mahāmāye ! sarvasiddhipradāyini ! | kṛpayetyādi || 9 || mahālakṣmi ! 
mahādevi ! mahāmokṣapradāyini ! | kṛpayetyādi || 10 || gaurī 
lakṣmīśca mātaṅgī durgā ca navacaṇḍikā | vagalāmukhī 
bhuvaneśī bhairavī ca tathā priye ! | chinnamastā ca kālī ca yonirūpā 
sanātanī | kṛpayetyādi || 11 || kālī kapālinī kullā kurukullā virodhinī | 
nāyikā vipracittādyā anyā yā nāyikāḥ smṛtāḥ | vasanti 
yonimāśritya tābhyo'pīha namo namaḥ || 12 || aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhiśca 
vasatyasyāḥ samīpataḥ | namaste'stu namaste'stu yogamokṣapradāyini 
! || 13 || sarvaśaktimayi ! devi ! sarvakalmaṣanāśini ! | he yone ! hara 
vighnaṃ me sarvasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 14 || ādhārabhūte ! 
sarveṣāṃ pūjakānāṃ priyaṃvade ! | svargapātālavāsinyai yonaye 
ca namo namaḥ || 15 || viṣṇusiddhiprade ! devi ! viṣṇusiddhipradāyini ! | 
brahmasiddhiprade ! devi ! rāmacandrasya siddhide ! | śakrādīnāñca 
sarveṣāṃ siddhidāyai namo namaḥ || 16 || iti te kathitaṃ devi !

p. 1076) sarvasiddhipradāyakam | stotraṃ yonermaheśāni ! 
prakāśitamidaṃ tvayi ! | sarvasiddhipradaṃ stotraṃ yaḥ paṭhet kaulikaḥ 
priye ! | likhitvā pustake devi ! raktadravyaiśca sundari ! | tasyāsādhyāni 
karmāṇi susiddhāni kuleśvari ! | nāsti nāsti punarnāsti nāstyeva 
bhavanatraye | yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāya gāṇapatyaṃ labhennaraḥ | 
rātrau kāntāsamāyoge yaḥ paṭhet sādhakottamaḥ | stavenānena 
saṃstutya sādhakaḥ kiṃ na sādhayet | svalaṅkṛtāṃ svakāntāñca 
līlāhāvavibhūṣitām | raktavastraparīdhānāṃ kṛtvā sampūjya 
sādhakaḥ | bhojayitvā tato devi ! svayaṃ bhuñjīta tatparaḥ | 
matsyamāṃsādikān bhuktvā kroḍe kṛtvā svayoṣitam | rātrau yadi 
japenmantraṃ sā durgā sa sadāśivaḥ | bhavatyeva na sandeho mama 
vaktrādvinirgatam | yena dattaṃ mayi stotraṃ sa eva madguruḥ smṛtaḥ | 
tasyaiva yadi bhaktiḥ syāt sa bhavejjagadīśvaraḥ | namo'stu 
stavarājāya namaḥ stavaprakāśine | yatrāste stavarājo'yaṃ tatrāste 
śrīsadāśivaḥ | iti śaktikāgamasarvasve harapārvatīsaṃvāde 
śrīyonistavarājaḥ samāptaḥ ||

atha yonikavacam ||

devyuvāca ||

bhagavan ! śrotumicchāmi kavacaṃ paramādbhutam | idānīṃ 
devadeveśa ! kavacaṃ sarvasiddhidam |

mahādeva uvāca ||

śṛṇu pārvati ! vakṣyāmi atiguhyatamaṃ priye ! | yasmai kasmai na 
dātavyaṃ dātavyaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet || asya śrīyonikavacasya 
guptaṛṣiḥ kulaṭācchando rājavighnotpātavināśe viniyogaḥ | hrīṃ 
yonirme sadā pātu svāhā vighnavināśinī | śatrunāśātmikā yoniḥ 
sadā māṃ pātu sāgare | brahmātmikā mahāyoniḥ sarvān kāmān 
prarakṣatu | rājadvāre mahāghore klīṃ yoniḥ sarvadāvatu | 
hūmātmikā sadā devī yonirūpā jaganmayī | sarvāṅgaṃ pātu 
māṃ nityaṃ sabhāyāṃ rājaveśmani | vedātmikā sadā

p. 1077) yonirvedarūpā sarasvatī | kīrtiṃ śrīkāntimārogyaṃ 
putrapautrādikaṃ tathā | rakṣa rakṣa mahāyone ! sarvasiddhipradāyini ! 
| rajoyogātmikā yoniḥ sarvatra māṃ sadāvatu | iti te kathitaṃ devi ! 
kavacaṃ sarvasiddhidam | trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennityaṃ 
rājopadravanāśakṛt | sabhāyāṃ vākpatiścaiva rājaveśmani rājavat 
| sarvatra jayamāpnoti kavacasya japena hi | śrīyonyāḥ saṅgame devi ! 
paṭhedevamananyadhīḥ | sa eva sarvasiddhīśo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | 
mātṛkākṣarasampuṭaṃ kṛtvā yadi paṭhennaraḥ | bhuñjate vipulān 
bhogān durgayā saha modate | iti guhyatamaṃ devi ! 
sarvadharmottamottamam | bhūrje vā tāḍipatre vā likhitvā dhārayed 
yadi | haricandanamiśreṇa rocanā kuṅkumena ca | śikhāyāmathavā 
kaṇṭhe śivaḥ so'pi na saṃśayaḥ | śaratkāle mahāṣṭamyāṃ 
navamyāṃ kulasundari ! | pūjākāle paṭhedetat jayī nityaṃ na 
saṃśayaḥ || iti śaktikāgamasarvasve haragaurīsaṃvāde 
śrīyonikavacaṃ samāptam ||

iti kavacaṃ paṭhitvā asyā aṅge ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā yonau 
mātṛkānyāsaṃ yonivījanyāsañca kṛtvā tadanujñāṃ labdhvā 
tāmbūlādikaṃ dattvā liṅgopari aiṃ iti liṅgamantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ 
japtvā iyaṃ gaurī ahaṃ śiva iti vibhāvya mātṛmukhe pitṛmukhaṃ 
dattvā mūlamuccārya oṃ dharmādharmahavirdīpte ātmāgnau 
manasā srucā | suṣumnāvartmanā nityamakṣavṛttirjuhomyaham | iti 
mantraṃ paṭhitvā liṅgaṃ yonau praveśya 
nidhuvanāsaktaśivaśaktyorabhedaṃ vibhāvya akṣubdhaḥ 
sthiramānasaḥ san varṇamālayā aṣṭottarasahasraṃ śataṃ vā 
prajapya mūlānte | oṃ 
prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīsrucā | 
dharmādharmakalāsnehapūrṇamagnau juhomyaham | svāhā iti 
mantreṇa śukraṃ tyajet || ekadā tāḍane devi ! ekadā yadi uccaret |

p. 1078) sa kālaścañcalāpāṅgi ! koṭisūryagrahaiḥ samaḥ | 
galaccandraṃ gṛhītvā kuṇḍagolādikaṃ kuryāt | bhartṛmatyāṃ 
kuṇḍaṃ vidhavāyāṃ golamiti | pūrvoktakrameṇa saṃśodhya tena 
pūjayettarpayecca | tato yonipīṭhaṃ prakṣālya tenodakena 
mūlamuccārya sāyudhāṃ saparivārāṃ savāhanāṃ 
mahākālasahitāṃ amukadevīṃ tarpayāmi svāhā | iti yonau 
tristarpayet pūjayecca ||

ārambhakramaḥ || āliṅganaṃ cumbanañca stanayormardanaṃ 
tataḥ | darśanaṃ sparśanaṃ yonīvikāśo liṅgagharṣaṇam | praveśaḥ 
svāpanaṃ śakternava puṣpāṇi pūjane | śivaśaktisamāyogo yoga eva 
na saṃśayaḥ | śītkāro mantrajāpastu vacanaṃ stavanaṃ bhavet | 
āliṅganañca kastūrī karpūraṃ cumbanaṃ bhavet | 
nakhadantakṣatādīni  puṣpāṇi vividhāni ca | maithunaṃ tarpaṇaṃ 
viddhi vīryapāto visarjanam | aṣṭottaraśatenāpi yonimārakṣya 
dharmavit | maithunaṃ yaḥ pratyātyeva sa tu sarvaṃ phalaṃ labhet | 
latārateṣu japtavyaṃ mahāpātakamuktaye | rajo'vasthāṃ samādāya 
tanmūleṣviṣṭadevatām | pūjayitvā mahārātrau tridinaṃ japamācaret 
| lakṣapīṭhasahasrāṇi labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ | pṛthvīmṛtumatīṃ 
vīkṣya sahasraṃ yadi nityaśaḥ | tadā vādī susiddhāntarhataṃ 
kṣititalaṃ viśet || nityaśa iti ṣoḍaśadinaṃ yāvat | kulāgāraṃ 
puṣpitāyā dṛṣṭvā japati yo naraḥ | ayutaikapramāṇena sa bhavet 
kalpapādapaḥ ||

atha śaktipraśaṃsādi || pañcamapaṭale || śaktiṃ vinā 
maheśāni ! sadāhaṃ śavarūpakaḥ | śaktiyukto yadā devi ! 
śivo'haṃ sarvakāmadaḥ | śaktiyuktaṃ japenmantraṃ na mantraṃ 
kevalaṃ japet | sāvitrīsahitobrahmā siddho'bhūnnaganandini ! | 
dvārāvatyāṃ kṛṣṇadevaḥ siddho'bhūt satyayā saha | tathā 
kocabadhūsaṅgānmama siddhirvarānane ! | īśvaro'haṃ mahādevi ! 
kevalaṃ śaktiyogataḥ | śaktiyogena deveśi ! yadi siddhirna jāyate |

p. 1079) tadaiva parameśāni ! mama vākyaṃ vṛthā bhavet | gaṅgā 
kāśī prayāgādi puṣkaraṃ naimiṣaṃ tathā | vadarī ca tathā revā 
utkalaṃ gaṇḍakau tathā | sindhuḥ sarasvatī caiva pīṭhāni vividhāni ca 
| sarvaṃ tyaktvā maheśāni ! strīsaṅgaṃ yatnataścaret | strīsaṅge 
siddhimāpnoti mama vākyaṃ na cānyathā | yaddattaṃ 
jalagaṇḍūṣaṃ śaktivaktre sureśvari ! | sindhurūpaṃ maheśāni ! 
tajjalaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ | svīyeṣṭadevībhāvena bhojayettāñca 
yatnataḥ || śaktiśca kathyate devi ! śṛṇuṣva surasundari ! | 
trayodaśābdādūrddhvaṃ yā pañcaviṃśativārṣikī | aprasūtā 
viśeṣeṇa pañcame'pi bhavet priye ! | sundarī tu viśeṣeṇa prasūtā 
vāprasūtikā | avaśyaṃ pañcamaṃ kuryāt śaktimātre maheśvari ! | 
pañcapañcīkṛte devi ! yaḥ kuryāt pūjanādikam | tadakṣayaṃ labhet 
puṇyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā | guruṃ santoṣayedbhaktyā tatpatnīṃ 
tatsutāṃ latām | nānābhakṣaṇavastrādyaistathā dravyeṇa sundari ! | 
pañcamārthe ca yā śaktistasyāṃ kuryācca pañcamam | tasyāṃ 
stotraṃ prakūrvita viśeṣeṇa śṛṇu priye | dravyādibhūṣaṇaiścaiva 
bhakṣyabhojyairviśeṣataḥ | nānābhakṣaṇavastrādyaistathaiva 
cumbanādibhiḥ | snānārthaṃ devatānāñca rahasyaṃ paramaṃ 
śṛṇu | yena snāne kṛte devi ! devatā suprasīdati | rātrau 
pañcamakāreṣu kṛtvā pañcamakārakam | bhagamadhye ca 
yaddravyaṃ gṛhṇīyācca prayatnataḥ | tacca kuṇḍodbhavaṃ toyaṃ 
jñeyaṃ devi ! varānane ! | labhete bhāgyayogena devānāmapi 
durlabham | tasmin kiñcijjalaṃ kṣiptvā tāmrapātre vinikṣipet | 
taddravyasnānamātreṇa devatā suprasīdati ||

uttaratantre dvitīyapaṭale || sarvapīṭhamayī śaktiḥ 
sarvadevasvarūpiṇī | sarvamantramayī śaktiścaturvargapradāyinī | 
śāktau manuṣyabuddhintu yaḥ karoti varānane ! | na tasya

p. 1080) mantrasiddhiḥ syādviparītaphalaṃ labhet | śakteḥ 
sampūjanāddevi ! sarvadevārcanaṃ priye ! | śaktau yaddīyate devi ! 
vastrādibhūṣaṇaṃ priye ! | devyaṅge dattametattu 
mahāsaukhyapradāyakam | devyai yadyadvarārohe ! dattaṃ 
vastravibhūṣaṇam | ihaiva śatasaṃkhyena dīyate ca tadā punaḥ | pāne 
bhrāntirbhaved yena ghṛṇā syādraktaretasoḥ | tamālokya varārohe ! 
kulīnaḥ paśutāmiyāt ||

puraścaraṇarasollāsatantre caturdaśapaṭale || kalau tu bhārate 
varṣe strīsaṅgaṃ paramaṃ padam | na dhūpaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi ! na 
dīpaṃ varavarṇini ! | naivedyaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi ! tantroktaṃ 
ṣoḍaśaṃ priye ! | dravyaṃ nāsti maheśāni ! kathaṃ siddhirbhaviṣyati 
| ata eva maheśāni ! sadānandamayaṃ yataḥ | pūjanaṃ 
cañcalāpāṅgi ! priyāṅgeṣu vyavasthitam | sadānandamayī nārī 
svakīyā paragocarā | yasyā aṅge maheśāni ! sarvatīrthāṇi santi vai | 
tāṃ vihāya maheśāni ! anyasthāne japedyadi | tasya sarvaṃ vṛthā 
devi ! unmatto bhavati dhruvam ||

nigamakalpadrume tṛtīyapaṭale || saptalakṣā mahāvidyāḥ 
kathitāstava suvrata ! | romakūpe vasantyetā mantrarūpāḥ pṛthak 
pṛthak | yāvanti santi romāṇi śarīre prāṇavallabha ! | tāvaddevāśca 
devyaśca santi tasyāṃ kalevare | tisraḥ koṭyastadabdena tīrthāṇi 
jāhnavījale | jāhnavī yonimūle ca satataṃ parisaṃsthitā | 
mūtrasthāne sadā muktiḥ sthāpitā tvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | prakṛtiśca 
pumāṃścaiva paraṃ brahma prakīrtitam || yonitantre'pi || kālī tārā 
yonigartte kuntale chinnamastikā | bagalāmukhī ca mātaṅgī vased 
yonisamīpataḥ trikoṇe tripurā devī tatpārśve bhairavī svayam || 
ityādipuraścaraṇarasollāse || pīṭhāni cañcalāpāṅgi ! kalau guhyaṃ 

p. 1081) bhaviṣyati | pañcāśatpīṭhasaṃyuktaṃ strīṇāmaṅgaṃ 
śubhapradam | tatkathaṃ mūḍhalokaśca vihāya strīpadaṃ mahat | 
anyapīṭheṣu tīrtheṣu mantrantu priye ! || etasmin rūpe devānāṃ 
tṛptirbhavati pārvati ! | nārītyāgaṃ tadā kṛtvā anyapīṭhaṃ vrajet 
priye ! | sā strī tu parameśāni ! sākṣānmūrtirna cānyathā | 
nārīprasaṅge cārvaṅgi ! siddhayo'ṣṭau bhavanti hi || iti 
śaktiprasaṃsā ||

atha prakārāntara latāsādhanam || uttaratantre prathamapaṭale || 
vallīmūle varārohe ! lekhanyaṅgasamutthayā | yo likhenmantra 
varṇāṃśca tasya siddho bhavenmanuḥ | tasya daśanamātreṇa muktāḥ 
santi ca pāpinaḥ || vallīmūle yonau || aṅgasamutthayā śephasā || 
śailakanyā kalānāthe kalānāthasamanvite | yaddravyaṃ jāyate devi ! 
taddravyaṃ mama durlabham | māhātmyaṃ tasya vaktuṃ vai samarthaḥ 
suravandite ! | jihvākoṭisahasraistu vaktrakoṭiśatairapi | 
puraścaraṇakarmādi duḥsādhyaṃ parameśvari ! | 
śaṅketamidamevātra sugopyaṃ paśusannidhau || aparantu dvitīyapaṭale 
|| śaṅkaropari saṃsthāpya kalāṃ kāmaśarārditām || yastu cintayate 
bhaktyā sa eva kalpapādapaḥ || śaṅkaropari viparītaratau || 
vallīmūlasamudbhūtaṃ pāśaṃ kareṇa sannibham | samūlaṃ 
sundarāraktaṃ patatrīva sa lobhitam | svādvannaṃ vyañjanairyuktaṃ 
drākṣārasasamanvitam | navanītaṃ kalājātaṃ 
kadalīnāthasambhavam | rambhārambheśayorāsyaṃ jāntavaṃ 
paramaṃ priye ! || ekatra sakalaṃ kṛtvā muktakeśo digambaraḥ | catvare 
vā śmaśāne vā baliṃ dadyādvarānane ! | strīrūpeṇa ca sā devī 
gṛhṇāti parameśvarī | evaṃ kartuṃ samartho yaḥ sa eva bhagavān 
svayam | yāminītimire bhānau mukhe hāṭakaparvate | āvarte 
kāmarūpe ca khañjane ca sureśvari ! | muktā dvandve ca cāpe ca

p. 1082) vimbe ca tilapuṣpake | padmanāle dharāyāñca rambhāyāñca 
varānane ! | yo japet parameśāni ! kimasādhyaṃ bhavet priye ! | 
yāminītimire keśe | bhānau sindūramaṇḍale | hāṭakaparvate 
stanayugale | āvarte nābhau | kāmarūpe yonau | khañjane cakṣurdvaye | 
muktādvandve dante | cāpe bhruvi | vimbe oṣṭhe | tilapuṣpe nāsikāyām 
| padmanāle bāhudvaye | dharāyāñca urūdvaye | rambhāyāñca 
nitambe ||

devyuvāca ||

kathameva mahādeva ! paraśaktyā kriyāṃ caret | paradārakṛte doṣaḥ 
kathaṃ naiva prajāyate | kathaṃ parastriyāṃ kāryaḥ pareṇa saha 
saṅgamaḥ | sādhvītvaṃ naśyati kṣipraṃ tasmānnaitatsamācaret || 

vṛhannīlatantre caturthapaṭale'pi || paradāravidhau devi ! 
nindāvādaḥ pravartate | tāsāṃ saṅgānmaheśāni ! tāmisraṃ 
narakaṃ vrajet | vedārthamiti vijñāya kathaṃ kuryācca sādhakaḥ | 
siddhāntayati tatraiva || paradārānna gaccheran gacchecca prajaped yadi | 
śrutidvayavirodhācca gaccheran parayoṣitaḥ | uttaratantre'pi || 
pūjākālaṃ vinā nānyaṃ puruṣaṃ manasā spṛśet | pūjākāle ca 
deveśi ! veśyāñca paritoṣayet | sādhvītvaṃ na hataṃ 
tasyāścumbanāliṅganaiḥ priye ! | kāśyādi sarvatīrthāni 
sarvapuṇyāni suvrate ! | tasyā dehe vasantyeva sarve mantrāśca sundari ! 
| vikalpo nātra kartavyastvayā girivarātmaje ! ||

atha yonipūjā || niruttaratantre prathamapaṭale || yonirūpā 
mahākālī śavaḥ śayyā prakīrtitaḥ | śmaśānaṃ dvividhaṃ devi ! 
citā yonirmaheśvari ! || tathā || kalāpūjāṃ vinā devi ! yā kācit 
kriyate kriyā | sā kriyā tvabhicārāya satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | 
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kalāpūjāṃ samācaret | yonirūpā kalā devi ! 
dakṣiṇaiva na saṃśayaḥ | divyavīro varārohe ! kalāpūjāṃ

p. 1083) samācaret | paśubhāvāśrito mantrī kalāṃ naiva prapūjayet | 
kalāyā dvividhā pūjā guptā vyaktā kuleśvari ! | guptā ca 
sādhakaiḥ kāryā nirjane ca mahāniśi | vyaktā divā prakartavyā 
lokācārakrameṇa tu | lokācāraṃ vinā devi ! gopanaṃ naiva jāyate | 
gopanaṃ siddhimūlañca satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ | brāhmaṇaḥ 
kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraśca kulasundari ! | kalayāsavayogena 
kalāpūjāṃ samācaret | dravyābhāve dvijo dadyāccānukalpaṃ yuge 
yuge | kalāyāścānukalpaśca kalāyāścaiva cintanam | kalāpūjāṃ 
vinā devi ! dakṣiṇā na phalapradā ||

kalātra svīyeṣṭadevatopalalakṣaṇam || tathāca 
samayācāratantre dvitīyapaṭale || athedānīṃ pravakṣyāmi 
bhagapūjāṃ samāsataḥ | guroḥ prasādād yanmantraṃ nityaṃ japati 
sādhakaḥ | tenaiva manunā mantrī bhagapūjāṃ karoti ca | tadāpi 
bhagamadhye vai cintayedbhagarūpiṇīm | śivo bhūtvā smareddevīṃ 
tadeva melanañcaret | śaktiṃ śaktimayīṃ cintettadā vai maithunaṃ 
caret | maithunāsaktayogena mānasaṃ japamācaret | yathoktaṃ kurute 
devi ! prāpyate paramaṃ padam | yāni kāni ca mantrāṇi kathitāni 
varānane ! | japtavyāni ca deveśi ! sarvatantreṣu niścitam || tathā || 
bhagasya smaraṇe puṇyaṃ bhagasya darśane tathā | bhagasya 
sparśanāddevi ! yat puṇyaṃ samprajāyate | tat sukhaṃ na 
japairhomairna dhyānaistapasā tathā | pañca pañca kṛte devi ! yat 
puṇyaṃ bhuvi jāyate | na tat puṇyañca subhage ! koṭitīrthasya darśane | 
japo homastathā dānaṃ dīkṣā yogādikalpanā | 
kṛtapañcamabhaktasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||

nigamakalpadrume ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || āvāhanādirahitāṃ yoni 
pūjāṃ vidhāya ca | mūlamantreṇa tadyonyāṃ pūjayediṣṭadevatām 
p. 1084) | māyābījaṃ yonimantraṃ tena tāṃ paripūjayet | svaśaktyā 
bhaktiyogena pūjayedvibhavāvadhi | tato yonau śataṃ mantraṃ japet 
sādhakasattamaḥ | bhage liṅgaṃ tato dadyādiṣṭamūrtiṃ vicintayan | 
gāḍhaṃ nipīḍayettāṃ tu svaśaktyā kulabhairavaḥ | ghāte 
brahmahatyāśataṃ hanyāt sudhākaraḥ | kāmaśāstraprasaṅgena 
yathā santoṣaṇaṃ bhavet | punaḥ punaḥ kāraṇādi mukhe dadyāt 
susādhakaḥ | vīryapātādisamaye japenmantramudāradhīḥ | tatastu 
dravyamādāya mahādevyai nivedayet | evaṃ kṛtvā mahādevi ! jaṭhare 
na punarbhavet | satyaṃ satyaṃ mahādevi ! mamaitadyogamuttamam | 
bahunā kimihoktena śṛṇu matprāṇavallabhe ! ||

vṛhannīlatantre ṣaṣṭhapaṭale || athavā kāmarūpasya darśanaṃ 
yadi jāyate | tadā bhagādidevīnāṃ pūjāṃ tatra vidhīyate | yadi 
bhāgyavaśenaiva kuladṛṣṭiḥ prajāyate | tadeva mānasīṃ pūjāṃ 
tatra tāsāṃ prakalpayet | bhaginīṃ bhagajihvāñca bhagāsyāṃ 
bhagamālinīm | bhagāṅgāñca bhagākṣīñca bhagavarṇāṃ 
bhagatvacām | bhagastanīṃ bhaganāśāṃ bhagasthaṃ bhagasarpiṇīm | 
tatra sampūjya gandhādyairmānasairgurumeva ca | namaskṛtya 
vidhānena svayamakṣobhitaḥ sudhīḥ || nigamakalpadrume || yonirvā 
stanapadmaṃ vā bhānu kṛṣṇasaroruham | cāmaraṃ vā nitambaṃ vā 
darśanaṃ yadi jāyate | tadā japenmantrarājaṃ namaskṛtya punaḥ 
punaḥ | teṣāñcāpi mahādevi ! muktirasti karasthitā | śaktyāḥ 
pādodakaṃ yastu pibedbhaktiparāyaṇaḥ | ucchiṣṭaṃ vāpi bhuñjīta 
tasya siddhirakhaṇḍitā | madvākye yadi viśvāso jāyate śivaṅkari ! | 
tadā vākyaṃ mayā proktaṃ anyathā cenna cācaret | yātrākāle 
yonitattvamāghrāya yadi gacchati | kuntalaṃ śirasā dhṛtvā tasya 
śatrurvinaśyati | sabhāyāṃ vijayī bhūyādrājakārye tathaiva ca | 
satyametanmahābhāge ! mama vākya vṛthā na hi | yena tena

p. 1085) prakāreṇa yonipūjāparāyaṇaḥ | sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ 
devairapi sudurlabham | atyutkaṭe ca yogādā tathaiva rājaveśmani | 
mahopadravamutpāte yadi yoniṃ prapūjayet | tadā yonirmahādevī 
coddharet | sarvaśaṅkaṭāt | ito'dhikaṃ na kiñcittu vidyate mama 
vākyataḥ | satyametanna sandeho yadyahaṃ tava vallabhaḥ | 
sarvadharmaṃ parityajya yonipūjārato bhavet | tadaiva paramā 
prītirlajjākaivalyamāpnuyāt | toyānāñca yathā gāṅgaṃ 
gavyānāñca yathā ghṛtam | tathā devi ! maithunaṃ me bhavet 
priyatamaṃ priye ! || nigamakalpadrume || ūrūdvayañca kāntāyā 
ūrvorupari saṃnyaset | grīvāmākramya pāṇibhyāṃ stanadvandvaṃ 
kvacit kvacit | cumbanaṃ bahudhā vaktre bandho'yaṃ kāmasundaraḥ | 
yonau liṅgaṃ samādāya hastopari padadvayam | keśānākramya 
pāṇibhyāṃ stanadvandvaṃ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe | cumbanaṃ sarvagātreṣu 
pibedoṣṭhādharaṃ muhuḥ | dṛḍhaghātena ca ramet bandho'yaṃ 
ratipaṇḍitaḥ | skandhayorvinyaset pādau yonau liṅgaṃ tathā kṣipet | 
śiraścākramya hastābhyāṃ dantāghātakramāt kramam | cumbanaṃ 
cakṣurvadane stanayomardanaṃ muhuḥ | ramet svaśaktitaḥ kāmī 
bandho'yaṃ kāmamardanaḥ | urasyāsanamāsthāya 
corasyaparamūrukam | skandhāvākramya pāṇibhyāṃ gaṇḍe 
cumbanapūrvakam | ramecca sudṛḍhāghātairvandho'yaṃ 
kāmakautukaḥ | evaṃ krameṇa deveśi ! bahubandho'sti bhairavi ! | 
kāmaratnāditantreṣu niśeṣeṇa prakāśitam | viśeṣabandhaṃ 
saṅkṣepāt prasaṅgāttvayi coditam ||

atha ṣoḍaśabandhāḥ || ratirahasye || bandhāścaturaśītiḥ 
syurmuninā parikīrtitāḥ | tasmādākṛṣya likhitāḥ ṣoḍaśa 
premahetavaḥ | pādau skandhayuge nāryāḥ kṣiptvā liṅgaṃ bhage 
laghu | kāmayet kāmuko nārīṃ bandhaḥ kākapado hi saḥ || 
pādamekaṃ kare dhṛtvā dvitīyaṃ skandhasaṃsthitam | nārīñca 

p. 1086) kāmayet kāmī bandho veṇuvidārakaḥ || ūrūmūlopari nyasya 
yoṣiduruyugaṃ yadi | grīvāṃ dhṛtvā karābhyāntu bandho nāgarako 
mataḥ || pīḍayeduruyugmena kāmuka yadi sundarī | ratipāśaṃ 
samākhyātaṃ kāminīnāṃ sukhāvaham || strījaṅghāntarito 
dorbhyāṃ gāḍhamāliṅgya sundarīm | bandhayedbandhanam | kāmī 
bandho māyūrasaṃjñakaḥ | nārīpādau svahastena dhārayed galake 
yadi | stanārpitakaraḥ kāmī bandhaḥ syāddhi vilāsavān | yoṣitpādaṃ 
hṛdi nyasya dvitīyaṃ skandhamāśritaḥ | stanau dhṛtvā rametkāmau 
bandhastanabharaḥ smṛtaḥ | nārīpādadvayaṃ kāmī dhārayet 
stanamaṇḍale | dhṛtakaṇṭho ramennārīṃ bandho'yaṃ ratisundaraḥ | 
nāryā ūrūdvayaṃ dhṛtvā karābhyāṃ pīḍayet punaḥ | 
kāmayennirbharaṃ kāmī bandho'yaṃ sukhasaṅgamaḥ | dhṛtvā 
vāmakareṇoruṃ pādañca sirasi sthitam | sudṛḍhaṃ ramayet kāmī 
smaracakraḥ prakīrtitaḥ | svajānudvayamadhyābhyāṃ 
dhārayennirbharaṃ striyam | vāme niḥśaṅkitaḥ kāmī bandho'yaṃ 
nāgapāśakaḥ | strīpadadvayamākṛṣya vimukhaḥ kṣiptaliṅgakaḥ | 
yonimāpīḍayet kāmī bandhaḥ kuliśanāmakaḥ | ityādibandhavinyāse 
gāḍhamutpādyate sukhaṃ | tasmādvidagdhasaṃghātairbandhāḥ 
kāryāḥ prayatnataḥ | bandhaḥ sarvātmanā kāryo nāyake sarvathā 
sukhe | tena mañjāyate kāmaḥ puruṣe maithune khalu || 16 || striya anīya 
pūrvārddha civukaiḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ kramāt | dhṛtvodare ramet paścāt 
bandho'yaṃ ratisundaraḥ | śayyāsīnastanī nārī utkṣiptajaghanī yadi | 
kāntaṃ kāmayate paścāt bandho'yaṃ kavargasaṅgamaḥ || 
ityadhomukhau dvau kartavyau || uddhṛtya pādamekantu saṃsthāpya 
cāparaṃ bhuvi | kuḍyāśritāṃ rametkāmī bandho dolāyitaḥ smṛtaḥ | 
pādābhyāṃ madhyamāveṣṭya samāliṅgyāśu sundarīm | cumbya 
mukhaṃ priyāyāstu bandho nāgeśvaro mataḥ || striyaṃ sukhaṃ

p. 1087) samāliṅgyānucumbya ca mukhaṃ muhuḥ | priyāyāḥ 
kaṭimākṛṣya svakaṇṭhe dhārayet priyām | kāmacālanamevāśu 
pradadyāt puruṣo dṛḍhaḥ || nārīpādadvayaṃ kṛtvā 
kāntasyorudvayopari | kaṭimālokayedyastu bandho'yaṃ sapadaḥ 
smṛtaḥ | kāntakrīḍe sthitā nārī bhūmau dattapadadvayā | hṛdaye 
dattahastā ca bandho līlānalottamaḥ || vīparītaratiḥ || bhaganiḥkṣipta 
liṅgāyāḥ sundaryā leḍhitaṃ priyam | tathā saṃpuṭakañceti 
nārīliṅgapravartanam || iti sukharatam || iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyāṃ 
pañcapuṣpastavakānvitaṃ saptamaṃ nirguṇajñānakāṇḍa 
samāptam ||

athauṣadham || matsyasūkte ekaviṃśati paṭale || dīpaṃ kṛtvā 
tāmrapātre kajjalaṃ pātayedatha | gavāṃ ghṛtena cāloḍya cakṣuṣī 
tvatha rañjayet | janmāntare yathā rūpaṃ tādṛśañca prapaśyati || 1 || 
kākamācī dakṣamūlaṃ śikhāyāṃ dhārayettu yaḥ | nidrāṃ karoti 
deveśi ! arddhaśūlaṃ haratyapi || 2 || kṛṣṇatulasyāḥ patrarasaṃ

tāpayettataḥ | karṇe tu tāpayeddevi ! karṇakauṭvādikaṃ haret || 3 || 
bhṛṅgarājaṃ sāranālaṃ arddhaśūlaṃ haredatha || 4 || jambu

śuṇṭhīnimbadalarasaṃ piṣṭvā cakṣuśchade priye ! | vilepayati 
pañcamyāṃ cakṣuśūlaṃ vinaśyati || 8 || apāmārgasya mūlañca 
rocanā saralaṃ tathā | gavyamustakasaṃyuktaṃ tāmrapātre tu peṣayet

kṛṣṇapatrasya rasena kaṭutailakam | lavaṇaṃ vāranālañca

p. 1088) bhaumavāre pradānataḥ | cakṣuśchade maheśāni ! paṭalaṃ

p. 1089) madhusaindhavasaṃyuktaṃ kolamatsya samanvitam | cakaśāṃ 
mūlakaṃ paktvā śāsullena varānane ! | vātaghnaṃ paramaṃ devi !

pārvati ! | tathā krauñcasya tailena nāgatailena vā priye ! || 25 || 
śuśutailena dugdhena ajākṣīreṇa bhāvayet | śrīpadaṃ vātikaṃ hanti 
kaṭivātaṃ tathaiva ca || 26 || unmattaṃ kumbharūpañca 
viśāḍhakīyapatrakam | samūlamaṭasārañca vāsakaṃ

samuddhṛtya varānane ! | snuhīmūlaṃ vilvamūlasamarīcaṃ

kalivṛkṣatvacaṃ devi ! gale baddhvā viśeṣataḥ | okasya nāśanaṃ 
proktaṃ pāmarīśūlakaṃ tathā || 30 || tintiḍauvījakaṃ piṣṭvā 
takreṇa sahayogataḥ | pānādīkaṃ haratyāśu vastāvāmasya vījakam 
|| 31 || dantamāsaṃ haredāśu vārtakībījakaistathā | pakvavārtākunā 
svedāt karoṣāgnī tathā haret || 32 || śvetāparājitāmūla tathā 
vāsyudakena ca | mukhamadhye pralepācca jāḍyaṃ nāśayati dhruvam 
|| 33 || śvetaguñjā samutpāṭya nakṣatre puṣpake tathā | 
pralepāttatkṣaṇāddevi ! viṣarogo vinaśyati || 34 || nīlakaṇṭhaṃ 
madhuyutaṃ tathā rogavināśanam || 35 || śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ tathā 
śvetajayantikām | śvetacitrakametena kārayedgulikāṃ tataḥ | 
svātyāṃ candrodaye devi ! samutpāṭya vidhānataḥ | bhāle ca tilakaṃ 
kṛtvā vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 36 || palāśabījaṃ samadhu ghṛtena saha 

p. 1090) peṣayet | bhakṣaṇānmāsamātrantu valīpalitanāśanam || 27 || 
vṛhatīnīlakaṇṭhānāṃ triphalālavaṇaiḥ saha | dante vilepanāddevi ! 
dantaśūlaṃ haratyapi || 38 || chāgamāṃsamajākṣīraṃ bhuktvā 
śālyudakaiḥ saha | yakṣmakuṣṭhādika hanti tālamūlī tathaiva ca || 39 
|| nāgakeśaramūlañca madhunā ca tathā haret | 
yaṣṭomadhusamantvāsye tathā hanyānna saṃśayaḥ || 40 || tasya 
patrapakvapānāt sarvajvaranivāraṇam || 41 || madhunā saha pānācca 
śātakāle jvaraṃ haret || 42 || anuditārke coddhṛtya savajvaraharaṃ priye 
! śvetāparājitāmūla puṣyārke dhāraṇāttathā | apāmārgasya ca 
tathā kanyakāsūtrabandhane || 43 || jvaraṃ hanyānmaheśāni ! 
svātyāṃ vā viṣṇubhe priye ! | raktavāṭyālamūlañca arkavāre 
viśeṣataḥ | nirveṣṭya kadalīsūtraiḥ śayyāyāṃ dāpayettataḥ | 
sarvajvaraṃ tato hanyāttathā tāpajvarāpanut || kuddālasya tvacañcaiva 
arjunasya tvacaṃ tathā | ghṛtena saha pānācca sarvakāsaṃ haratyapi || 
45 || nāgeśvarasya mūlañca madhunā saha śaṅkari ! | pānācca 
codaraṃ hanti viṣṇukrāntā tathaiva ca || 46 || pippalīṃ saindhavayutāṃ 
pāyayenmadhunā samam | mūtrasrāvaṃ tato hanyāt saptāhānnātra 
saṃśayaḥ || 47 || vilvamūlaṃ saguḍūcīṃ madhunā pāyayettataḥ | 
vāntaṃ hanti na sandeho mātuluṅgasya vījakam || 48 || palāśabijaṃ 
siddhārthaṃ madhutailasamanvitam | bhakṣayat samabhāgena jvarāṃ 
hanti na saṃśayaḥ || 49 || tilakalke samaṃ yuktaṃ tathā bhallātakaṃ 
priya ! | atyagnirjāyate devi ! trirātreṇa na saṃśayaḥ || 50 || 
candramūlīṃpippalīṃ ca śuṇṭhīñca maricāni ca | 
purātanaguḍenāpi gulikāṃ kārayettataḥ | atyagnirjāyate śīghraṃ 
bhakṣaṇāt śaivayogataḥ || 51 || kākolūkasya pakṣeṇa 
dadyāddhūpaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ | vināśayati tatbhītiṃ tathā

p. 1091) jaṅgamavāraṇe || 52 || nīlakaṇṭhasya pakṣañca bhujagasya ca 
kañcukam | samabhāge kṛtaṃ dhūpaṃ pūrvavadbhavati dhruvam || 53 
|| hayamārasya raktasya gṛhītvā kusumaṃ budhaḥ | amlaṃ 
savetasañcaiva sanmārjyaṃ tena darpaṇam | 
aśvoṣṭragarddhabhānāñca rūpaṃ paśyedananyadhīḥ || 54 || 
śūkarāśvagarddhabhasya vānarasya tathaiva ca | gṛhṇīyāttu nakhaṃ 
tāvat puṭāgnau dāhayettataḥ | paścānmaṇḍūkavaśayā darpaṇaṃ 
mārjayedatha | teṣāṃ rūpāṇi saṃpaśyennātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 

ṛtumatyā durbhagāyāstaddināvadhi saptake | vāsare śarṣapān 
śvetān yonau pakṣepayedbudhaḥ | bhakṣyaistaiḥ śarṣapairyo'yaṃ 
spṛśatyeteva taṃ vrajet || aṅkoṭhavṛkṣavījasya gṛhnīyāttailameva tu | 
mocike tu tato vṛścattasya dohadameva ca | balapuṣṭikarañcaiva 
āyurvṛddhikaraṃ param || jamboraphalapatrañca saindhavena samanvitam 
| dantalepaṃ bhavettīkṣṇaṃ māṣapuṣpaṃ ghṛtaudanam | 
āyurvṛddhikarañcaiva cirakāle balaṃ bhavet || kūrmamāṃse balaṃ 
hīnaṃ māṃsañca korakasya ca | alpāyuryāti vai śīghraṃ 
haṃsamāṃsena pārvati ! || pūgasya śīkarañcaiva 
snuhīkardamasaṃyutam | māṃsayukte arkadugdhaṃ 
mandāraphalasaṃyutam | vāte rajanisaṃyuktaṃ tālamūlī sabhaktakam 
| machaktī ca chade dadyājjihvāroge kuleśvari ! | parpaṭaṃ 
guḍasaṃyuktaṃ dadhibhaktaṃ nivedayet | uṣṇaṃ kṛtvā pāyayecca 
nābhau saṃlepayettataḥ | aśokamūlaṃ pinyākaṃ kapharoge galagrahe 
| aṇḍaroge parṇaśirāṃ māṇamūlantathaiva ca | tālamūlañca 
kuṣmāṇḍaṃ vātasāre śirauṣakam || trayoviṃśati paṭale || 
dantaśūle mārutañca saindhavaṃ pippalīyakam | samūlaṃ 
dantisārañca dantarogopaśāntaye | kṣate kauṭe prajāte ca 
mandārāgraṃ sacūrṇakam | snuhīkṣīrasametañca | kīṭaḥ patati 
tatkṣaṇāt || iti gavādīnāṃ kṣate pratyakṣaṃ draṣṭavyam ||

p. 1092) atha vakṣye maheśāni ! turagāṇāṃ cikitsitam | vānte dadyāt 
palalatāṃ śṛṅgaveraṃ sapippalīm | yamānīṃ nārikelañca 
muṇḍavāsakamūlakam | koṣalañca guḍañcaiva piṇḍaṃ tāpaharaṃ 
param ||

jīraṃ sarjarasaṃ kolaṃ dūrvādhanyākasaṃyutam | yogapatraṃ 
gimāśākaṃ marīcaṃ lavaṇānvitam | vāsantīmūlasahitaṃ 
tripuṇḍrantu śramāpaham | vṛhatpānīyamūlapatraṃ saṅkasya 
bījapatrakam | śṛṅgaveraṃ yavañcaiva haridrādvayameva ca | 
etatpiṇḍa vātahare balapuṣṭikaraṃ param | araṇyavadarīṃ sūktiṃ 
kānane rāmavāsakam | kharjūrasya ca bījena saguḍena jvaraṃ haret | 
dhānyamūlaṃ ravimūlaṃ kālañjarasamanvitam | somadhānyena 
taddadyācchardikāroganāśanam || tathā || apāmargaṃ viḍaṅgañca 
saindhavaṃ nāgakeśaram | saindhavena samāyuktaṃ pakṣirogo 
vinaśyati | carcikāyāntu bhekasya tailaṃ bheruṇḍakasya ca | 
niśāyogena dātavyaṃ sarvakuṣṭhavināśanam || nāgeśvarasya tailena 
madhuvṛkṣasya cūrṇitam | dagdhaṃ kṛtvā dadellepaṃ naśyeddevi ! 
vicarcikā ||

ṣaḍviṃśati paṭale || dānavasya ca puṣpāṇi dhṛtasiktāni bhakṣayet 
raktasrāvaṃ ṛtusnānaṃ jalandhārañca naśyati || uttarāśāmukho 
bhūtvā kanyāsūtreṇa veṣṭayet | viṣṇukrāntāmapāmārgaṃ tathā 
jīramarīcakam | ekakena pibet pītvā sukhaṃ sūyeta garbhinī | 
māhīṣanavanītena tathā kavikalākṣatam | vilipya tābhyāṃ deveśi ! 
liṅgaṃ sthalaṃ bhavedanu | raktabheraṇḍamūlañca tathā | 
caladalapadmakam | marīcañca priyaṅguñca piṣṭvā liṅgaṃ 
vivardhayet ||

guvākapuṣpamekantu gokṣīreṇa samaṃ pibet || svātyāṃ vā 
janmadivase apuṣpā piṣpitā bhavet || hastiśūṇḍyāstato mūlaṃ 
kalaviṅkasya nāsikām | ghṛtena bharjitaṃ bhakṣedindriyaḥ prabalo 
bhavet ||

p. 1093) nāgārjunakṛtakakṣapuṭe dvitīyapaṭale || 
bhūdāravaṭamūlañca jalena saha gharṣayet | vibhūtyā saṃyutaṃ 
devi ! tilakaṃ lokavaśyakṛt || 1 || puṣye punarnavāmūlaṃ rudantīṃ 
mūlikāṃ tathā | yavamūlaṃ tathā badhvā kaṇṭhe 
saptābhimantritam | pūjyo bhavati sarvatra mantramatraiva kathyate || aiṃ 
puraṃ kṣobhaya bhagavati gambhīre lkau svāhā | amuṃ 
mantramayutadvayaṃ japet tataḥ siddhiḥ || 1 || ugrātapatramañjiṣṭhā 
kuṅkumaṃ tagaraṃ samam | snāne pāne tathā sparśe dattaṃ vaśyaṃ 
bhavatyalam || 2 || siṃhīmūlaṃ haret puṣye kaṭyāṃ baddhvā 
jagatpriyaḥ || 3 || niśi kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ mahānīlīṃ śmaśānataḥ | 
uddhṛtya naratailena cāñjanenaiva vaśyakṛt || 4 || tanmūlaṃ 
bandhayeddhaste sarvalokapriyo bhavet || 5 || candraṃ puṣye samuddhṛtya 
brahmadaṇḍīya mūlakam | bhojane sarvasatvānāṃ 
vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || ulūkahṛdayaṃ tulyaṃ kumāryā rocanaṃ 
sudhīḥ | añjane rocane vaśyamānayedbhuvanatrayam || 6 || oṃ namo 
yakṣiṇi amukaṃ vaśamānaya svāhā oṃ | asya mantrasya 
pūrvamevāyutaṃ japtvā tata ugrātapatrādi sarvaprayogāḥ | 
śatavāramabhimantritā siddhā bhavati coṣadhiḥ | sarveṣāmeva 
mantrāṇāṃ mantradhyānaṃ pṛthak pṛthak | tattatsthāne 
yathāsaṅkhyamanukte tvayutaṃ japet | mṛgaśīrṣe tu saṅgrāhyaṃ 
suraktakaravīrakam | navāṅgulaṃ kīlakaṃ tat saptavārābhimantritam | 
yasya nāmnā khanedbhūmau sa vaśyo bhavati dhruvam | oṃ hu/ hu/ 
svāhā | śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā pūrvamevārabhennaraḥ || 7 ||

tṛtīya paṭale || kuṅkumaṃ candanañcaiva rocanaṃ 
saśimiśritaṃ | gavāṃ kṣīreṇa tilakaṃ rājavaśyakaraṃ param | oṃ 
hrīṃ saḥ amukaṃ vaśaṃ kuru kuru svāhā | pūrvameva sahasraṃ 
japtvā tato'nena mantreṇa pūrvavat tilakaṃ kuryāt | oṃ hrīṃ 
raktacāmuṇḍai kuru kuru amukaṃ vaśamānaya svāhā | iti 
cāmuṇḍāmantraiḥ sarvasiddho bhavati || mañjiṣṭhā

p. 1094) kuṅkumañcaiva bālā modā kumārikā | citābhasma 
svaraktañca niyamaireva bhāvayet | puṣyeṇa guṭikāṃ kṛtvā bhakṣye 
pāne ca dāpayet | pṛṣṭo vā rājavaśyaḥ syāt 
caṇḍamantraprabhāvataḥ || 8 ||

atha vivādajayaḥ || gojihvā śikhimūlā vā mukhe śirasi 
saṃsthitā | kurute savavādeṣu jayaṃ puṣye samuddhṛtā | mārgaśīrṣe 
tu pūrṇāyāṃ śikhimūlaṃ samuddharet | vāhau śire śikhe 
dhāryaṃ vivāde vijayo bhavet | bandhanānmucyate jantuḥ 
śikhāmantrānna saṃśayaḥ || 2 || meghanādasya mūlañca 
cakrasyāntaraveṣṭitam | paradārī bhavenmūko'thavā yāti digantaram | 
niśi kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ mahānīlīṃ śmaśānataḥ | ādāya 
bandhayeddhaste vivāde vijayo bhavet | śvetaguñjānvitaṃ mūlaṃ 
mukhasthaṃ duṣṭatuṇḍajit | ukta yogānāṃ caṇḍamantreṇa siddhiḥ || 
mantrastu oṃ sudarśanāya huṃ phaṭ svāhā | pūrvamevāyutaṃ 
japtvā caṇḍamantrasya siddhaye | tato hyauṣadhiyogaśca 
saptābhimantrito bhavet || 4 || śuklapakṣayute puṣye guñjāmūlaṃ 
samuddharet | baddhaṃ śirasi tatsarvaṃ cauravādhāharaṃ param || 

yogasāre prathamaparicchede || devīvākyam || 
vyādhināśamahaṃ vakṣye palamātraṃ ghṛtaṃ pibet | paṭhitvānena 
manunā yaḥ pibet sādhakottamaḥ | sarvavyādhivinirmukto bhavatyeva na 
saṃśayaḥ | mantraṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu madbhairavottama ! | 
tāraṃ māyā bhṛguḥ śambhuḥ punarbhṛgurudāhṛtaḥ || sṛṣṭiyukto 
guruścaiva tathā candraḥ śivaḥ smṛtaḥ | etanmantraṃ samākhyātaṃ 
ghṛtaśuddhiritīritam || 1 ||

tṛṣṇānāśaṃ pravakṣyāmi raktacandanatolakam | gharṣayitvā 
vāmahaste gṛhītvā sādhakottamaḥ | tolakaṃ jalasaṃmiśrya 
manunāṣṭottaraṃ śatam | tattadupari saṃjapya yaḥ pibet 
sādhakottamaḥ | saptāhājjāyate tasya tṛṣṇānāśastu bhairava ! |

p. 1095) sarveṣāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ saptāhañca kuleśvara ! | 
sarvamaṣṭottaraśataṃ pibejjaptvā ca sādhakaḥ | śabdabījadvayaṃ 
bhadre ! nāśayadvitayaṃ vadet | tṛṣṇeti padamuccārya tataḥ svāhā 
manurmataḥ | anena manunā deva ! tṛṣṇānāśo'bhijāyate || 2 || 
āhāranāśanārthāya kṛṣṇacitrāṃ samāharet | tolārddhaṃ vā 
tadarddhaṃ vā tadarddhaṃ vā sureśvara ! | ānīya manunānena 
vaṭikāṃ kāryedbudhaḥ | ghṛtenāloḍya deveśa ! manunānena 
bhakṣayet || kāmabījaṃ samuccārya kṣunnāśini padaṃ tataḥ | 
nivārayapadaṃ coktvā svāheti ca manurmataḥ || 3 || 
athavānyaprakāreṇa kṣunnivṛttirvidhīyate | haviṣyāśau bhavedādau 
paścājjapati mātṛkām | paścādghṛtaṃ prayoktavyaṃ manunānena 
śaṅkara ! | hasaurvā yena manunā bhakṣayet sādhakottamaḥ || 4 || 
athavā sarpiṣā deva jihvāmāloḍya yatnataḥ | yāvadoṣṭhaṃ 
parivyāptaṃ jihvayā kulabhairavaḥ ! | tāvatpramārjayejjihvāṃ 
yāvadoṣṭhasamāṃ nayet | anenaiva prakāreṇa kṣunnivṛttirbhaviṣyati || 
5 || athavānyaprakāreṇa kṣunnivṛttirvidhīyate | pratyahaṃ 
vijayādhūmaṃ brahmarandhreṇa mantravit | pānaṃ karoti deveśa ! 
kṣunnāśo jāyate dhruvam | brahmamantramahaṃ vakṣye yena 
kṣunnāśanaṃ bhavet | sambartakacandravahnibhautikī bindusaṃyutaḥ | 
evaṃ vījatrayaṃ deva ! samuccārya kuleśvara !  | anena manunā deva ! 
vijayādhūmaśodhanam | śodhayitvā pibeddhūmaṃ na doṣo jāyate 
hara ! || 6 || atha mūtrapranāśārthaṃ vakṣyāmi mantramuttamam | 
aśvagandhāṃ samānīya astramantreṇa mantrayet | tolakaṃ sarpiṣā 
deva ! miśrayitvā pibet sudhīḥ | liṅgamūle kāmabījaṃ japtvā ca 
stambhayeddhiyā | dvātriṃśattolakaṃ dugdhaṃ marīcaṃ 
tolakadvayam | sammiśra prapacedyatnādyāvat śuṣkaṃ samānayet | 
tato vai vāgbhavaṃ japtvā bhakṣayet sādhakottamaḥ anenaiva

p. 1096) vidhānena mūtranāśo'bhijāyate || 7 || purīṣanāśanārthāya 
viḍaṅgaṃ tolakadvayam | pippalītolakaṃ grāhyaṃ śuṇṭhīñca 
tolakatrayām | tejapatrasya kṣāreṇa miśrayitvā japenmanum | 
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā miśrayedbahu yatnataḥ | mantraṃ tasya 
pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvānanabhairava ! | śrīrmāyā vāgbhavañcaiva 
śaktivījatrayaṃ tathā | śivavāyujalaṃ pṛthvī vahnijāyā tataḥ param 
| mantrayitvā pibeddravyaṃ purīṣaṃ nāśayed dhruvam || 8 || 
śukrastambhaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu madbhairavottama ! | piṣṭvā 
cairaṇḍakaṃ mūlaṃ nābhimūle vilepayet | rohitamatsyapittena 
tanmūlaṃ lepayecchiva ! | śabdavījairmantrayitvā stambhayet 
śukraśoṇitam | śabdabījaṃ samuddhṛtya mardayedastramuccaran | 
stambhayenniyataṃ śukraṃ tathoktaṃ kulasundara ! | mantreṇānena 
śukrasya stambhanaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ || 9 || śukrastambhanakaṃ 
proktamālasyanāśanaṃ śṛṇu huṅkāradhvanimāhūya 
karatāladvayaṃ dvayam | kṛtvā vetālamūlañca piṣṭvā yo bhakṣayet 
sudhīḥ || phaṭkārairmantrayitvā ca tālamūlaṃ pibet sudhīḥ | tato 
madhu pibeddhīmānālasyanāśanaṃ bhavet || 10 || nidrānāśaṃ 
pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvānandabhairava ! | alaktakaṃ tolakārddhaṃ 
tolārddhaṃ kaṭutailakam | śvetadhūpaṃ miśrayitvā manunā 
mantrayet sudhīḥ | manuṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi yena nidrākṣayo bhavet | 
nidrā yamasamādeva ! nidrā vyādhikarī matā | nidrāyāṃ 
sarvadoṣaḥ syānnidrayā jñānanāśanam | nidrayā līyate sarvaṃ 
nidrayā narakaṃ vrajet | tasmādyatnena karttavyaṃ nidrānāśantu 
bhairava ! | sambartaṃ vahnisaṃyuktaṃ candrakhaṇḍasamanvitam | 
kramāttrayaṃ samuddhṛtya vahnijāyāṃ pare vadet | śatamaṣṭottaraṃ 
japtvā mardayedbahuyatnataḥ | pratyahaṃ cakṣuṣordadyānnidrānāśa 
itīritaḥ || tato japedvāgbhavantu cakṣuṣormadhyabhāgayoḥ | tato 
dhyāyet kulaguruṃ kuladevīṃ prayatnataḥ |

p. 1097) prārthayedbahuyatnena nidrāyā nāśanaṃ drutam | oṃ 
nidredevi ! mahāmāye ! jagannirvāṇakāriṇi ! māṃ jahi tvaṃ 
jagaddhātri ! māṃ jahi parameśvari ! | praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya 
nidrāpatipadaṃ tataḥ | nidrāpatipadaṃ brūyāt tato brūyāt sanātanī | 
vahnijāyāṃ tato brūyāt nidrākṣayamanurmataḥ | etanmantrajapādeva 
nidrānāśo bhaved dhruvam | śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vāpi sahasraṃ vā 
kuleśvara ! | japtvā nidrā kṣayaṃ yāti krimanyat kathayāmi te | atra 
sthitavṛ?tyadhyāhārājjaptetyanenaikakarttṛkatā || 11 || athānyat 
sampravakṣyāmi cendriyāṇāñca nigraham | yonitantroktamārgeṇa 
kulākāraṃ karoti hi || kāntāṃ manoramāṃ deva ! ānīya 
bahuyatnataḥ | jayettena mārgeṇa kāmayedbahuyatnataḥ || evaṃ 
satkārayogena śukrastambho bhavennṛṇām || yasya yaddevatābhāvo 
yadrūpā devatā smṛtā | tattadrūpajagatsarvaṃ || dhyātvā ca 
madirāṃ pibet || tataḥ prāṇāyāmayoge vāyuñcāpi vaśaṃ nayet | 
tato ghṛtaṃ samāloḍya nālamūlaṃ tathā śiva ! | piṣṭvā 
pūrvoktamanunā japtvā ca sādhakaḥ pibet | sarvavyādhivinirmukto 
naro bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || gurupādarajo dhyātvā 
madhumāṃsasamanvitam | surāyuktaṃ mahādeva ! 
mardayedbahuyatnataḥ | tataḥ pūrvoktamantreṇa sammantryāṣṭadinaṃ 
pibet | evaṃ saṃskārayogena tṛṣṇānāśo bhaved dhruvam || 3 || 
kuleśvarīṃ tato dhyātvā kṛṣṇacitrāṃ tataḥ pibet | tataḥ 
pibenmadyamāṃsaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā kālabhairava ! | evaṃ 
saṃskārayogena kṣunnāśo jāyate dhruvam || 
vākcintāmaṇinirmitātivitatā sarvairguṇairanvitā | 
sākṣātkalpalatāśritārthasahitā viśvopakāre hitā ||
dhyātvā vijñasabhājitātiphalitā śrīprāṇatoṣiṇyasau ||
draṣṭavyā bhavatā satā matimatā śubhrātiśubhrā latā || 

iti śrīprāṇatoṣiṇī samāptā
